vaiyākaraṇabhūṣaṇasāraḥ |
śrīharibhaṭṭakṛtā kāśikā ṭīkā 
vande gaṇapaticaraṇau vighnadhvāntālivāraṇo(ṇau)dyotau |
vandyau suragaṇasaṅavaiḥ sarvābhīṣṭapradātārau(phaladātārau) || 1 ||
bhairavaṃ śaṃkarīṃ natvā siddheśāṃ lokamātaram |
vyākhyāṃ bhūṣaṇasārasya kāśikāṃ tanute hariḥ || 2 ||
munitrayaṃ sarvajanopakārakaṃ praṇaumi vidyārṇavapātrabhūtān |
gurūṃśca tātaṃ vibadhendravandyaṃ yaśaḥprakāśaṃ jananīṃ ca devīm || 3 ||
prāripsitagranthasya nirvighnasamāptaye kṛtaṃ tāvat sakalajagartṛṃbhūtaśrīlakṣmīramaṇastutyā-
tmakaṃ maṅgalaṃ śiṣyāśikṣāyai nibaghnāti, śrīlakṣmīraṇamiti | śrīrlokottaraśobhā, tadyuktā yā (śrī)mahā-
lakṣmīrnikhilaprakṛtistasyā ramaṇaṃ - kayitaṃ viṣṇuṃ, naumi - staumotyarthaḥ | yadvā, śrīḥ pūrvoktā, nityatvādidharmopetā vilakṣaṇā vuddhirvā, tadyukto lakṣmīramaṇa ityarthaṃḥ | athavā ; śrīḥ - sarasvato, sā ca lakṣmīśca, tayo ramaṇa ityarthaḥ, 'śrīśca te lakṣmośca patnyau' iti śruteḥ, tatra 'śrī' śabdena sarasvatī, na tu lakṣmīḥ, lakṣmīśabdasya pṛthugupādānāt, 'patnyau' iti dvivacanāsaṃgatyāpatteśceti spaṣṭameva | nanu 'kuryāddhariharārcanam' iti vacanād, 'yathā viṣṇustathā śivaḥ |'
" śivasya hṛdayaṃ viṣṇurviṣṇośca hṛdayaṃ śivaḥ |
viṣṇurudrāntaraṃ yañca yo brūte mūḍhadhīstu saḥ || "
rauravādiṣu ghoreṣu narakeṣu patatyadhaḥ || "
ityādivacanebhyaścābhedenaivopāsanāyā vihitatvādviparītāyāśca niṣedhāt, kevala- viṣṇustutikaraṇe ca bhedabhāvanā pratīyeteti tadapanuttaye(tadanutpattaye) lakṣmī- ramaṇaviśeṣaṇamāha, gaurīti | gaurī - pārvatī, sā ca kālikā, 
'tasyāṃ vinirgatāryā tu kṛṣṇābhūt sāpi pārvatī | kāliketi samākhyātā ' 
iti mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇāt,'umā kātyāyanī gaurī kālī' iti kośāñca | itthaṃ ca mahākālyāditrayasyāpi stutiḥ kṛtā bhavati | tasyā ramaṇaḥ śivastasya rūpaṃ tadasyā-
stītyarthaḥ | gaurīramaṇena rūpayati tadabhinnatayā gṛhṇātīti vārthaḥ , karmakartari ṇiniḥ | gaurīramaṇaṃ svābhinnatayā rūpayati grāhyatīti vārthaḥ(tyarthaḥ) | pūrvakalpe(pakṣe) taddhita iniḥ, uttarapakṣe ' supyajātau 'iti ṇiniḥ |
sa kaḥ? etad dṛśyamānaṃ sarva jagat yataḥ,-yasya | advayāditvāttasiḥ(ādyāritvā- diti bhavet ) | vivarttate - viṣayasattākaṃ bhavati, atāttvikānyathābhāvāśrayo bhavatīti vārthaḥ | taduktam, 'tāttviko'nyathābhāvaḥ pariṇāmo'tāttviko'nyathābhāvo vivartaḥ 'iti | anyathābhāvaśca bhrama eva | tadāśrayatvaṃ tu viṣayatayā jagati(viṣaye jagaditi) bodhyam | etena jagato vivartādhiṣṭhānatvarūpaṃ sarvajagadupādānakāraṇatvamuktaṃ, tena sarvo- tkṛṣṭatayā stutyatvamāveditam | tadupādānatvaṃ ca 'yato vā imāni bhūtāni jāyante yena jātāni jīvanti yatprayantyabhisaṃviśanti ' ityādiśrutisiddham | etacchutisiddhe jagat-
sthitilayakartutve'pi bodhye | jagato viśeṣaṇaṃ sphoṭarūpaṃ, sphuṭatyasmāditi sphoṭaḥ, sa ca siddhānte brahmasvarūpa iti spaṣṭaṃ haryādigranthe | tadrūpaṃ tadabhinnabhityarthaḥ | 'sarvaṃ khalu idaṃ brahma' ityādiśrutisiddho brahmābhedaḥ | lakṣmīramaṇaviśeṣaṇaṃ vaitat(caitat), sphoṭasya vaiyākaraṇopāsyatvena samucitadevatāstutisaṃpattyarthaṃ tadupādānam || 1 ||
pāṇinyādimunitrayamadhye idānīmatiprāmāṇikatvotkṛṣṭatvādinābhimato yaḥ śeṣastadviśiṣṭa (tadiṣṭa) devasaṃkīrtanātmakaṃ maṅgalaṃ nibadhnāti aśeṣaphaleti | śeṣāśeṣārthalābhāya, śeṣasya -śeṣanāgasya mahābhāṣyakṛto'śeṣāḥ - kṛtsnāḥ vācyādayaḥ, sphuṭā asphuṭāśca ye'rthā mahābhāṣyasthāsteṣāṃ lābhāya - jñānāya | śeṣabhūṣaṇaṃ, śeṣo bhūṣaṇamalaṃkāratvena, paryaṃkatvena (vā) bhūṣaṇaṃ yasya taṃ śivaṃ, śeṣaśāyinaṃ viṣṇuṃ ca prārthaya iti saṃbandhaḥ |
nanu śeṣārthalābhaḥ śeṣādeva saṃbhavati, na tu śivādeḥ, na hi devadattīyārthalābho mitradattādeḥ | ataḥ śeṣa eva prārthanīyo na tu śivādirityāśaṃkāṃ nirācikīrṣusta- dviśeṣaṇamāha, aśeṣaphaleti | aśeṣāṇyaihikāmuṣmikāṇyapavargaparyantāni, phalāni - tattatkarmaphalāni, teṣāṃ dātā iti tṛnnantena śeṣaṣaṣṭhyantasya samāsaḥ | yadvā tṛjantena śeṣaṣaṣṭhya(..ntena tasya)ntasya samāsaḥ, etena tṛjantatve ' tṛjakābhyām- 'itisamāsa- pratiṣedhāpattistṛnnantatve ' na loka-- ' iti ṣaṣṭhīniṣedhāpattiriti dūṣaṇaṃ pratyuktaṃ , kārakaṣaṣṭhyā
eva 'na loka ' iti niṣedhāt , tasyā eva samāsaniṣedhāñca | phaladātṛtvaṃ ceśvarasya 
'yādṛgiva vaidevebhyaḥ karoti tādṛgivāsmai daivāḥ kurvanti ',
'yo yo yāṃ yāṃ tanuṃ bhaktaḥ śraddhayārcitumicchati |
tasya tasyācalāṃ śraddhāṃ tāmeva vidadhāmyaham ||
sa tayā śraddhayā yuktastarayārādhanamīhate |
labhate ca tataḥ kāmān mayaiva vihitān hitān || "
ityādi śrutismṛtipurāṇeṣu prasiddhaṃ 'vaiṣamyanairghṛṇye na, sāpekṣatvāt, tathā hi darśayati iti vyāsasūtre dvitīyādhyāyasya prathamapādasthe, tṛtīyasya dvitīyapādasyānte 'pūrvaṃ tu bādarāyaṇo hetuvyapadeśāt 'iti vyāsasūtre ca bhagavacchaṃkaṃrācāryaiḥ śārīrabhāṣye nipuṇataramupapāditam | tattajjīvagatadharmādharmasāpekṣasyeśvarasyaiva phaladātṛtvaṃ, na kevala- karmaṇaḥ | tatra hi prathame, yathā parjanyo vrīhiyavādisṛṣṭau sādhāraṇaṃ kāraṇaṃ, vrīhiya- vādivaiṣamye tu tattadvījagatāni sāmarthyāni śaktyaparaparyāyāṇi kāraṇāni bhavantyevamīśvaro devādisṛṣṭau sādhāraṇaṃ kāraṇaṃ bhavati, devamanuṣyādivaiṣamye tu tattajjīvagatādṛṣṭāni vilakṣaṇāni (asādhāraṇāni) kāraṇāni bhavanti, evamīśvaraḥ karmasāpekṣatvā- dvaiṣamyanairghṛṇyābhayāṃ na duṣyati, yathā sevānurūpaphaladātā rājā | kathaṃ punaravagamyate sāpekṣa īśvaro nīcamadhyamottamasaṃsāraṃ nirmimīta iti | tathāhi darśayati śrutiḥ 'eṣa hyeva sādhu karma kārayati taṃ, yamebhyo lekebhya unninīṃṣate, eṣa u evāsādhu karma kārayati taṃ, yamadho ninīṣate ' 'puṇyo vai puṇyena karmaṇā bhavati, pāpaḥ pāpena ' iti ca | smṛtirapi, 'ye yathā māṃ prapadyante tāṃstathaiva bhajāmyaham |' ityādyabhihitaṃ |
tṛtiye tu 'dharma jaiminirata eva' iti jaiminisūtrita(sūtramataṃ) dharmādeva phalamiti pakṣaḥ 'tu' śabdena vyāvartyate, tathā ca karmasāpekṣādapūrvasāpekṣādveśvarādeva phalamiti siddhāntaḥ, kutaḥ?, 'hetuvyapadeśāt' | dharmādharmayorapi kārayitṛtveneśvaro heturvyapadiśyate phalasya dātṛtvenetyabhidhāya 'eṣa hyave(brahmaiveti)'ti pūrvoktaśrutiḥ pradarśitetyādyanyatra vistaraḥ |
nanu pratibandhakānekaduritasattve kathaṃ roṣāroṣaphalalābhaḥ, satyekasminnapi bādhake sādhakasahasratyāpyakiṃcitkaratvādityāśaṃkāmapanetuṃ viśinaṣṭi bhavābdhīti | bhavaḥ - saṃsāraḥ, sa evābdhiriti mayūravyaṃsakāditvāt samāsaḥ, taraṇyāmānuguṇyāt, yadvā 'bhavo'bdhirive 'tyupamitasamāsaḥ , nāśarūpatya tāraṇasya tatrāpyanvayasaṃbhavāt , 'bhāṣyābdhiḥ kvātigambhīraḥ 'ityatreva prakṛte sādhāraṇadharmavācakopādanirūpavādhakābhāvāñca | bhavābdhe-
staraṇaṃ tatra tariṃ naukāṃ(tariḥ naukā) sādhanamiti yāvat, 'tarati śokamātmavit' iti śruteḥ | 'striyāṃ naustaraṇistariḥ' ityamaraḥ | tathā cānanyasādhyānekaduḥkhasamūhasaṃsāra- nivartakatve pratibandhakaduritanivṛttiḥ sutarāṃ tato bhaviṣyati, kaimutikanyāyāditi bhāvaḥ | śeṣālaṃkāratvena tanniyojakatvaṃ, tena tadviśiṣṭaprārthanayāvaśyaṃ śeṣoktāśeṣārthalābhaḥ, vighneśajanakatvena vighnapracayanivṛttirapyāvaśyakīti sūcitam | etena vighnanivāraṇasaṃpa- ttaye 'ādau pūjyo gaṇādhipaḥ' ityādivākyanicayādādau(vākyānicapādādo) gaṇeśaprārthanaivociteti tadadūṣaṇaṃ nirastam || 2 ||
nikhilapaṇḍitamānyaṃ svagurubhūtaṃ pitṛvyaṃ tatkīrtyanuvṛttaye tannāmata nibadhnaṃ(mani daivataṃ) stauti, vāgdevīti | siddhaye - prāripsitagranthasamāptaye, tatpracārāya ca | narīnarti - punaḥ punaratiśayena vā nṛtyaṃ karoti, vāgdevyā jihvāgre nṛtyaṃ ca, jhaṭityanavarataṃ dhārārūpeṇa prakāśarūpam | etena lokottaraṃ pāṇḍityaṃ vaktṛtvaṃ cāveditam | 'nṛtī gātravikṣepa' ityato dhātoryaṅlugantāllaṭ, tip, śapo luk, 'carkarītaṃ ca' iti yaṅlugantasyādādau pāṭhāt , 'rīgṛdupadhasya ca' ityabhyāsasya rīgāgamaḥ | chandaso'pi yaṅluk ' huśnuvoḥ sārvadhātuke' iti sūtre huśnugrahaṇāt, 'babhūthātatantha ' iti sūtre nigamagrahaṇāñca jñāpakād bhāṣāyāmapi na virūddha iti bhāvaḥ | atraitādṛśavilakṣaṇa- sāmarthyavatto(vato)panyāsena dīkṣitasya devāṃśatvasūcanadvārā tadīyastuteramīpsitārthasādhakatvaṃ, samucitatvaṃ ca sūcitam | etādṛśasya devāṃśatvañca , 
'yadyadvibhūtimat sattvaṃ śrīmadūrjitameva vā, tattejo māmakaṃ viddhi ' iti bhagavadgītāyāṃ spaṣṭamuktam || 3 ||
vyākaraṇapravartakācāryapāṇinyādimunitrayādinamaskārātmakaṃ cikīrṣitaṃ maṅgalaṃ nibadhnan, prekṣāvatpravṛttaye'nubandhacatuṣṭayaṃ bodhayan śiṣyāvadhānāya cikīrṣitaṃ(nirūpaṇārthaṃ) pratijānīte, pāṇinyādīti | pāṇinyādimunīn , raṅgojibhaṭṭābhidhaṃ pitaraṃ , ḍhurṇḍhi - gaṇapatiṃ, praṇamya - prakṛṣṭaṃ sāṣṭāṅgatvaguṇa(sāṣṭāṅgatvena)viśiṣṭanamaskāraṃ kṛtvā, gautamajaiminivacanavyākhyātṛbhirdū- ṣitān siddhāntān - vaiyākaraṇa siddhāntān | upapattibhiḥ - yuktibhiḥ | prakaṭaye - prakāśaye | teṣāṃ - gautamajaiminīyavacovyākhyātṛṇāmādhunikatārkikamīmāṃsakānāṃ vaco dūṣaye iti yojanā | 'vyākhyātṛbhirdūṣitā' niti 'vyākhyātṛ'grahaṇena-gautamādimunīnāṃ pāṇi- nyādibhiḥ sahāviredha iti sūcitaṃ, sa ca mañjūṃṣāyāṃ tatra tatra spaṣṭīkṛtaḥ, ihāpi(iha) kvacit
pratipādayiṣyate | pāṇinirādiryeṣāṃ te pāṇinyādayaḥ, te ca te munayaśceti bahuvrīhigarbhaḥ karmadhārayaḥ | ayaṃ bahuvrīhistadguṇasaṃvijñānaḥ, 'sarvādīni' itivat | tena pāṇinerapi namaskārakarmatvalābhaḥ | ādipadagrāhyau-kātyāyani-patañjalī | 'raṅgojibhaṭṭe'tyabhidhā nāma yasyeti bahuvrīhiḥ | gautamaśca jaiminiśca gautamajaiminī - nyāyamīmāṃsāsūtrapraṇetārau, tayorimāni-gautamajaiminīyāni, idamarthe 'vṛddhācchaḥ' iti chaḥ, tāni ca tāni vacanāni nyāsūtrāṇi, mīmāṃsāsūtrāṇi ceti karmadhārayaḥ, teṣāṃ vyākhyātāra iti aśeṣaphaladātṛpadavat ṣaṣṭhīsamāsaḥ |
namaskāryatāprayojakaṃ pitṛviśeṣaṇamāha ddhaitadhvāntetiṃ | dvau(dve) itaḥ - āśra- yatvena prāpto, dvīto - bhedaḥ, tasyedaṃ jñānaṃ dvaitam | yadvā ' dvayekayoḥ' itivad dviśabdo dvitvaparaḥ, tathā ca dvitvaviśiṣṭau dvītapadapratipādyau, tayorbhavaḥ dvaito bhedaḥ, tadrūpaṃ dhvāntaṃ tamo moha iti yāvat | tasya nivāraṇaṃ tat ādi phalaṃ yasyāḥ sā etādṛśīṃ-pūṃbhāvavādevatāṃ, pumān puruṣaḥ tasya bhāvaḥ - ākārarūpo yasyāḥ sā, puṃbhāvā - puruṣākṛtiketyartha | sā cāsau vāgdevatā - sarasvatī, tadrūpa ityarthaḥ | ajahalliṅgatvāt ' strī prādhāna ' mitivat vibhinnaliṅgatve'pi samānādhikāraṇaviśeṣaṇatvamaviruddham | anena viśeṣaṇenādvaitapratipādakottaramīmāṃsāyāmatiśayitanaipuṇyaṃ pratipāditaṃ, tena vilakṣaṇotkarṣaḥ sūcitaḥ || 4 ||
pracuravidhnaśaṃkayā punarapi gaṇeśādinamaskārātmakaṃ maṅgalamācaran svakīrtyanuvṛttaye svasya cikīrṣitagranthasya ca nāma nibadhnāti, natveti | śrīkauṇḍabhaṭṭaḥ , -śrīrnavanavonmeṣaśālinīsphūrtyākhyā matistadyuktaḥ kauṇḍabhaṭṭo'haṃ,- gaṇeśapādāmbujaṃ,- gaṇeśasya-gaṇānāṃ śivagaṇānāmīśo niyantā tasyetyarthaḥ | athavā-gaṇānāṃ śatrugaṇānāmīrlakṣmīstāṃ śyati - tanūkarotītyarthaḥ, śo tanūkaraṇa iti dhātoranaimittika ātve kṛte ' āto'nupasarge kaḥ ' iti kaḥ | yadvā gaṇānāmiḥ - kāmastaṃ kāmodvegaṃ, ' kāme ' tyupalakṣaṇaṃ krodhādīnāmapi, tathā ca kāmādiṣaḍvargaṃ sadupadeśādidvārā śyati - khaṇḍayatītyarthaḥ | etādṛśasya devasya pādāmbujaṃ - pādāvevāmbujaṃ kamalam, atiśayitasaṃśleṣaviśiṣṭatvena dhyātatvasūcanāyāmbujamityekavacanam | gurūn - nigūḍhamarthaṃ gṛṇātīti guruḥ, ' kṛgroruñca ' ityuṇādisūtreṇa ' gṛ śabda ' iti kryādergṛdhātoḥ kupratyayaḥ | sarasvatīṃ saro'mṛtasarastadvatī sarasvatī, ' sara ityudakanāma, sartestadvatī ' iti niruktāt , sarovaraviśeṣo vā yasyā āśrayo vartata iti vārthaḥ | vaiyākaraṇabhūṣaṇa ' mityanvarthaṃ
granthanāma, tena vaiyākaraṇānāmavaśyopādeyatā dhvanitā || 5 ||
prāripsiteti | prārabdhumiṣṭo yo granthastasya pratibandhakāni teṣāṃ vyūhaḥ - samuhastasyopaśamanāya - nāśāyetyarthaḥ | etena ' samāptiṃ prati maṅgalaṃ na kāraṇamanyathāsiddhatvāt , kiṃ tu vighnadhvaṃsaṃ prati, samāptistu svasāmagrīta ' ityādi bodhitamityādyanyatra vistaraḥ | nanu tathāpi granthādvahireva maṅgalācaraṇamastu, granthamadhye tannibandhanaṃ niṣprayojanamata āha, śiṣyaśikṣārthamiti | granthārambhasamaye śiṣyairapi tādṛśanibaddhaśloke paṭhite sati te'pi maṅgalabhājo bhavanti tatprayuktaphalabhājaśvaityedarthamityarthaḥ | cikīrṣitaṃ - kārikāvalīrupaṃ grantham | asmābhiriti śeṣa iti | anena vākyena kārikāvalīrūpagranthakarturatiśāyitasāmarthyaprakāśanadvārā tatkṛtaprakṛtagranthasyāvaśyopādeyatā dhvanitā | ādhunikotprekṣitanirāsaprayojanamāha anyatheti | anyathā - kaustubhoktīnāmādhunikītprekṣitatvanirāsākaraṇe | ādhunikotprekṣiteti | ādhunikairutprekṣito yaḥ kaustubhastatsāratvāpattau - tatsāratvani(tanni)ścayāpattāvityarthaḥ | pāṇinīyānāṃ - pāṇininā proktaṃ śāstramadhīyānānām | mūle, - devatāntaraṃ vihāya phaṇina evopādānaṃ tu vyākaraṇaśāstrapraṇetṛ. munitrayābhyarhitatveneṣṭatamatvasūcanāya, itarabhāṣyavad vyākaraṇabhāṣyaṃ nādhunikapraṇītamapi tu viṣṇurūpimahāmunipraṇītamatastaduktiṣvapramāṇyaśaṃkā naiva vidheyetyasyārthasya lābhāya cetyāhuḥ(cce) mūle prathamato dhātvarthanirūpaṇaṃ tu 'sarvāṇyākhyātajāni ' iti śākaṭāyananiruktāt , ' sarvaṃ nāma dhātujaṃ niruktaṃ vyākaraṇe śakaṭasya ca tokam ' iti bhāṣyañca sakalaprayogamūlatvena dhātoranyārhitatvena, prāyo vākyajanyabodhe dhātvarthasya prādhānyācca tasyābhyarhitatamatvāditi bodhyam | 'ākhyātajānī' tyatratyākhyātaśabdastiḍantasamudāyātmakākhyātaghaṭakadhātuparaḥ | ' sarvaṃ nāma dhātujam ' iti bhāṣyāt , nāmnastiḍantajatvāsaṃbhavācca || 2 ||
' smṛtā ' iti bahuvacanāntasya ' dhātu ' rityantrānvayona saṃbhavati, viśeṣyavācakapadasamānavacana tvābhāvādityata āha smṛta iti | vacanavipariṇāmeti | asya ' vācakatvene'ti śeṣaḥ |
' phalavyāparayo 'ritidvivacanāntanirdeśena phale, vyāpāre ca pṛthak śaktirna tu phalaviśiṣṭavyāpāre prācīnatārkikābhimataikā śaktiḥ, tathā sati phalasya padārthaikadeśatayā dvitīyādipadārthāntarānvayo na syāditi sūcitam | phalavyāpārayoḥ sādhyasādhana-
bhāvenānvayaḥ, saṃsargasyāpadārthatve'pi kṣativirahāt , sāṃsargikaviṣayatātiriktaviṣayatāśā- liśābdabodhaṃ pratyeva vṛtyā padajanyapadārthepasthiteḥ prayojakatvāt, tathā ca, janyatāvacchedakakoṭau-sāṃsargikaviṣayatātiriktaviṣayatātvena viṣayatāyā anupraveśa iti kecit |
vastutastu sāṃsargikaviṣayatātiriktaviṣayatātvena [na] tadanupraveśaḥ,tathā niveśe'pi vṛtyā ghaṭapadād ghaṭopasthitau satyāṃ paṭādiviṣayaka(paṭāderviṣayakārya)śābdabodhāpattiḥ, atastadviśeṣyakatatprakārakāśābdatvāvacchinnaṃ prati vṛtyā tattatpadajatyatadarthepasthitirheturiti viśiṣyaiva hetuhetumadbhāvo vaktavyaḥ, evaṃ ca sāṃsargikaviṣayatāmantarbhāvya kāryakāraṇa(karaṇa)bhāvavirahāt śaktyānupasthitasyāpi saṃsargasya bhānaṃ niṣpratyūhameva |
kiṃ ca śaktayā, lakṣaṇayā vopasthitasya tattadarthasya śābdabodhe bhānād dvividhopasthiterapi śābdabodhaprayojakatvamupeyaṃ, tatrānugatānatiprasaktāguroḥ kāraṇatāvacchedaka- dharmasyāsaṃbhavāt pṛthak pṛthak hetutāvaśyaṃ svīkāryā, tathā caikavidhopasthitivirahe'ṣyanya- vidhopasthityā tattadarthaviṣayakaśābdabodhajananād vyābhicāra ityataḥ ' śaktiprayojyatatadartho- pasthitiviśiṣṭatattadarthaviṣayakaśābdatvaṃ ', 'lakṣaṇāprayojyatattadarthopasthitiviśiṣṭatattadarthaviṣaya- kaśābdatvaṃ ' ca kāryatāvacchedakamabhyupeyam, upasthitivaiśiṣṭyaṃ ca svasāmānādhikaraṇya, svāvyavahitottaratvābhyām, evaṃ ca saṃsargaviṣayakaśābdabodhasya kāryatāvacchedakānākrānta- tayā'śakyasyāpi tasya bhānaṃ niṣpratyūhamiti pare |
' saṃbandho vācya ' iti vaiyākaraṇāḥ,' lakṣya ' bhaṭṭā iti dik |
nanu tathāpi 'vibhinnapadopasthitayoreva parasparānvayabodha' iti vyutpatteḥkathamekadhātū- pasthitayo[rvyāpāra-phalayoḥ parasparānvayabodhaḥ, na ca tādṛśavyutpattirasiddhā, niṣprayojanatvāditi vācyaṃ, haripadādindrāśvayo]rupasthitayorādhārādheyabhāvā- vagāhiśabdabodhāpatterkāraṇasya, vāśabdādita upasthitayorvikalpādyorarthayoḥ paraspaparānvaya- bodhāpatte(rdhe)rvāraṇasya ca prayojanatvāditi cenna | paramate liṅādyarthayoḥ[kṛtīṣṭasā- dhanatvayorākhyātarthakṛtivarttamānatvayośca] passparamanvayabodhadarśanena tādṛśavyutpatteḥ saṃkoca- nīyatayā phalavyāpārayorapi parasparamanvayabodhasaṃbhavāt, navyanaiyāyikairapi svīkṛtatvācca | phalavyāpārayorevādāvantaraṅgatvādanvayastadantaramevārthāntareṇānvayo'nubhūyate, ata eva ' gamanaṃ na spanda ' iti vākyānna spanda(bhedā)nvayānubhavaḥ, viśeṣyabhūtavyāpāre tadbodhāt , phalasya tadvī(itara)śeṣaṇatvāpannatvāditi bhāvaḥ | abhedena stokāderanvayo bhavatyeva, vyutpattivai- citryāt , evaṃ karmakārakasya(dhāradyatya) bhedenāpīti na ' stokaṃ pacatī'tyāderasaṃgatiḥ |
tatra bhavanto mañjūṣākārāstu "phalaviśiṣṭavyāpāre, vyāpāraviśiṣṭaphale ca śaktiḥ, phale vyāpāre ca pṛthakśaktisvīkāre vyāpāroddeśyakaphalavidheyakabodhasya, phaloddeśyakavyā- pārāvidheyakabodhasya cāpattiḥ, tādṛśabodhaprayojikāyāḥ pṛthagupasthiteḥ sattvāt, viśi- ṣṭaśaktimate tu na tādṛśabodhāpattiḥ , tatprayojikāyāḥ pṛthagupasthiterabhāvāt, na ca pṛthagupasthitestādṛśabodhaprayojakatvamasiddhamiti vācyaṃ, ghaṭapaṭādijanyaikopasthiti- viṣayayorghaṭatvaparatvādyo(ghaṭapaṭetyādyoḥ)stathābodhānanubhavabalena [tathāvidho[pasthitestathātva- kalpanāt, na ca dhātujanyavyāpārādyupasthitestathāvidhabodhapratibandhakatvakalpanānna] tathā- vidhabodhāpattiriti vācyaṃ, tādṛśapratibadhyapratibandhakabhāvakalpane gauravāt , katṛṃbhāvāpratyayasamabhivyāhāraḥ phalaviśiṣṭavyāpārabodhaniyāmakaḥ, karmapratyayasamabhivyāhāro vyāpāraviśiṣṭaphalabodhaniyāmakaḥ, na ca viśiṣṭadvaye [śaktidvayaṃ kalpanīyaṃ,] śaktijñānadvayasya dhātujanyaviśiṣṭaviṣayopasthitidvayaśya ca śābdabodhaṃ prati haitutvaṃ kalpanīyamiti gauravamiti vācyaṃ, prāmāṇikatvena tasyādoṣatvāt , pṛthak śaktivāde'pi bhinnapadopasthitayorevā(sthitya evā)nvayabodha iti vyutpattestyāgaśya, vyutpattyantarasya ca kalpanīyatayā gauravam, | api ca kartṛpratyayasamabhivyāhṛtadhātujopasthiteḥ phalaprakārakavyāpārāviśeṣyakabodhaṃ prati, karmapratyayasamabhivyāhṛtadhātujopasthitervyāpāraprakārakaphalaviśeṣyakabodhaṃ prati hetutāyāḥ kalpanīyatayā kāryakāraṇabhāvadvayakalpanāgauravaṃ, dhātorarthadvaye śaktidvayasya kalpanīyatayā ca | na ca bhavanmate'pi tādṛśāviśiṣṭaviṣayakopasthitidvayasya kāraṇatāyāstathāvidhe'rthadvaye śaktidvayasya ca kalpanīyatayā sāmyamiti vācyaṃ, bodhajanakatvasya viṣayāsaṃbaddhe'nupapattyā tattatpratyayasamabhivyāhṛtadhātau viśiṣṭadvayanirūpitasaṃbandhadvayasya kalpanīyataya['dhika]gauravasya jāgarūkatvāt , anyathā śakterucchedaḥ , evamanyatrāpi pṛthakśaktikalpanā nirasanīye'ti procuḥ |
phalavyāpārayori'ti mūlamapi tadubhayasamudāye śaktirityetāvanmātratātparya- kaṃ, na tu pṛthakśaktitātparyakaṃ, evaṃ ca śābdabodhe ekaviśiṣṭasyaivāparasya bhānādu- ktaviśiṣṭadvaye'pi śaktirmūlalabhyaivetyetadabhiprāyo'vagantavyaḥ |
mūloktakramānurodhād vyāpārāt prākaphalāṃśaṃvyācaṣṭe phalaṃ viklityā- dīti | viklittiḥ - śithilībhāvo'vayavavibhāgaviśeṣastatsaṃyogaviśeṣo vā, tasya ca viklittitvādyātmakaviśeṣarūpeṇa vācyatā, na tu phalatvena,viśeṣarūpeṇaiva śābdabosyānubhavāt |
viklittyādiniṣṭhaphalatvaṃ tu 'taddhātvarthajanyatve sati kartṛpratyayasamabhivyā- hāre taddhātvarthaniṣṭhaviśeṣyatānirūpitaprakāratāvattvaṃ' ,janyatvamāropitānāropitasā- dhāraṇaṃ,tena 'vārayatī'tyādau[saṃyogānukūlavyāpārābhāvānukūlavyāpāraṃ prati]saṃyogānu- kūlavyāpārābhāvādeḥ phalatvānirvāhaḥ,vibhāgajanyasaṃyogādirūpe patatyādyarthe [vibhāgasya phalatvavāraṇāya satyantaṃ, janyatvamātraniveśe'tiprasaṅgatādavasthāta taddhātvarthasya janyatvanirūpakatayopādānaṃ,] vibhāgasyāpi gamyādirūpaya- tkiñciddhātvarthajanyatvāttadavastha evoktadeṣa iti tatpadopādānam , | na ca vibhāgajanyasaṃyogasya(janyatvanirūpakasaṃyogasya) svajanyākriyādvārā vibhāgajana- katayā taddoṣatādavasthyamiti vācyam , upadhāyakatārūpajanakatā- nirūpitajanyatāyā, kriyādvārikāyā vā janyatāyā niveśanīyatvāt | tatraiva saṃyoge phalatvavāraṇāya 'prakāratāvattvānta(tvasya)niveśaḥ | 'patati bhavatī'ti vākyajanyabodhīyabhavatikriyāniṣṭhaviśeṣyatānirūpitāvattvāt- 'taddhātvarthaniṣṭhe'ti viśeṣyatāviśeṣaṇaṃ, (|) karmapratyayasamabhivyāhāre 'taṇḍulaḥ pacyate' 'taṇḍulaḥ pakva' ityādau viklityāderviśeṣyatvāttatra tasya phalatvaṃ na syādataḥ 'kartṛpratyayasamabhivyāhāre'ti | yadvā, tadanupādāne śābdatvāvacchedena tādṛśapra- kāratāvattvaṃ labhyeta(labhyate), tathā cāsaṃbhavaḥ syādatastadupādānaṃ, tathā ca tādṛśaprakāratā(prakāravataṃ)vattvamupalakṣaṇaṃ, na viśeṣaṇam ,ato na karmapratyayasthale viklittyāderatattvāpattiriti bhāvaḥ | atra 'yatkiñcitpratyayasamabhivyāhāre' ityapahāya tathoktistādṛśabodhasthalarpadarśanāya |
vyāpāraṃ nirūpayati vyāpārastviti | bhāvaneti | utpattyanukūlo vyā- pāra ityarthaḥ, utpattyarthakabhavaterṇyantāt 'ṇyāsaśrantha 'iti bhāve yuc | abhi- dheti | abhidadhāti - janayati phalamityabhidhā, sādhyatvena dhātunābhidhīyata
iti vābhidhā, tatra prathame 'ātaścopasarge' iti kartari kaḥ | dvitīye-tenaiva sūtreṇa karmaṇyaṅpratyayaḥ, tathā ca bhāvanābhidhāparanāmikā sādhyatvenābhidhīyamānā kriyā vyāpāra ityarthaḥ | 'bhāvanābhidhe'tyuktistu-vyāpārasya dhātvarthatve'pi bhāva- nādināmakasyārthaviśeṣasya ākhyātārthatvameveti bhramanivāraṇārthā | 'kriye'tyukti- rṇyantasya bhavateḥ śuddhena karotinā tulyārthatvapradarśanārthā ca | yāvatsidvamiti | 'pacati' 'pakṣyatī'tyādāvasiddhama'apākṣī'dityādau siddhaṃ vā 'sādhyatvena' tatprakāreṇābhidhīyamānaṃ vastu, 'kriye'tyabhidhīyate | kuta ityākāṅkṣāyāṃ yogasya(yogyasya) hetutvaṃ pradarśayati āśritetyādinā | kriyate'vayavānāṃ krameṇotpattyāta evāśritakramarūpetyarthaḥ | āśritaṃ kramarūpaṃ yayāsau [āśrita- kramarūpeti bahuvrīhiḥ, tatastvaḥ, sāmānyavivakṣāyāṃ napuṃsakatvaṃ |
darśanasyaiva mānatvāditi | yadi sādhyatvena pratītirnāṅgīkriyeta tarhi] kṛtirityādito'vilakṣaṇapratītyā tatrevātrāpi kriyāntarākāṅkṣāpadyeta, tadabhyupagame ca siddhasyaiva kriyāntaranivartakatvasya darśanāt, sādhyatvena pratītau kriyāntarānākāṅkṣānirvāhaḥ | anutthāpakatāvacchedakamiti | tadutthitiprati- bandhakatāvacchedakatayā siddhajātiviśeṣarūpamityarthaḥ | tadrūpamiti | dhātūpa- sthitakriyāmātravṛttivaijātyasiddhau lāghavāttasyaivāsattvarūpatvaṃ svīkartuṃ yuktaṃ, na tu liṅgādyananvayitvāderiti bhāvaḥ | parantu tadvaijātyaṃ liṅgādyananvayitvasamā- nādhikaraṇaṃ pūrvaparībhūtāvayavatvasamānādhikaraṇameva | 'etadeve'-tyasyokta evābhiprāyaḥ | tiṅpadairiti tiṅntapadairityarthaḥ | etatpratidvandvitayā kriyākāṅkṣotthāpakatāvacchedakajātiviśeṣaḥ siddhatvamiti bodhyam |
na caivaṃ hirugādyavyayānāṃ tādṛśasādhyatvena rūpeṇa tattadarthānupasthāpakatayā kriyāpradhānatvavyavahāro nirudhyeteti vācyaṃ, tadarthasya kriyāmātra- viśeṣaṇa(tva)nibandhanatvāt , ata eva hirugādyarthe na kārakāṇāmanvayaḥ, kārakānvayayogyatāvacchedakavaijātyenābhānāt |
nanvevaṃ ' kṛṣṇaṃ nameccetsukhaṃ yāya 'dityatra namanasukhaprāptirūpakriyayorhetuhe- tumadbhāvenānvayo na syāditi cenna | cecchabdasamabhivyāhārasācivyena kriyākā- ṅkṣotthānā(tpādana)dityāhuḥ | vastutaḥ sādhyatvaṃ niṣpādyatvameva, tenaiva rūpeṇa bodhasyānubhavasiddhatvāt , spaṣṭaṃ cedam 'upapadamatiṅ,' 'suṭkātpūrvaḥ', ["gatigatau"] ityatra ca bhāṣye, tatra 'upapadamatiṅ' iti sūtre 'dhātūpapadayoḥ
samāsamāśaṅkya, 'pūrvaṃ dhātuḥ sādhanena yujyate' iti pūrvaṃ tiṅi viśiṣṭonaiva sāmarthyaṃ syāt , tatra ' atiṅ ' iti niṣedhānna tiḍntenaikārthībhāva iti samāhi- tam | 'suṭ kāt pūrvaḥ' 'gatirgatau' ityatra, dhātupasargayoḥ kāryaṃ yattadanta- raṅgamityādyuktvā 'naitat sāraṃ pūrvaṃ hi dhātuḥ sādhanena yujyate paścādupasargeṇa, sādhanaṃ hi kriyāṃnirvartayati, tāmupasargoviśinaṣṭi' ityuktam | sādhanaṃ - tadbodhakapratyayaḥ,kriyāṃ nirvartayati - sādhyātvavaiśiṣṭyena bodhayatyatastatprayukta- kāryaṃ pūrvamiti vṛddhāstadarthamāhuḥ | 'upapadamatiṅ' ityatra bhāṣye'pyetadāśayenaiva pūrvaṃ sādhanasaṃbandhābhidhānam , [etadbhāṣyaprāmāṇyena sādhyatvabhānamaṅgīkṛtam |] etena śabdaśaktisvabhāvādeva tiṅnate kriyānākāṅkṣāsiddhau na sādhyatvabhānamiti dūṣaṇaṃ pratyuktamiti bodhyam | anubhavasiddhatvāditi | ata eva 'pacatī'tyāditaḥ śābda(lakṣaṇādeva)bodhe sati phūtkāratvādirūpeṇa na tatsaṃśayaḥ | nānā- rthatāpattiriti | śakyatāvacchedakānāṃ phūtkāratvādīnāṃ nānātvādekā- śaktervaktumaśakyatvāt , ata eva haryādipadasya nānārthatvābhyupagamaḥ | tadā- dīti | tadādipadasya yathā buddhiviṣayatvarūpa-buddhisthatvena [buddhiviśeṣeṇo- palakṣaṇībhūtena] ghaṭatvādīnanugamayya tadavacchinne śaktiḥ svīkriyate, ghaṭatvādiviśeṣarūpeṇānubhavasiddhabodhanirvāhārthaṃ, tathā prakṛte'pi saṃbhavānna nānārthatvāpattiḥ, śakyatāvacchedakanānātve'pi śakyatāvacchedakasyaikasyaiva saṃbhavādityarthaḥ | tajjanyaśābdabodhe bhānaṃ mā bhūdityetadarthaṃ 'tasyopalakṣaṇa- tvopagamaḥ | upalakṣaṇatvaṃ ca tattatpadajanyabodhaviṣayatvena tatpadaśaktigrahāviṣa- yatvaṃ | buddhiviśeṣāderiti | phūtkārādiviṣayakabuddherityarthaḥ | buddheḥ śakyatā- vacchedakatāvacchedakatvaṃ viṣayatāsaṃbandhena | tasya vṛtyaniyāmakatayāvacchedakatā- ghaṭakatvānaṅgīkāre tvāha āderiti | ādinā buddhiviśeṣaviṣayatvatadava- cchedakatvayoḥ parigrahaḥ |
pare tu-haryādivat pacādīnāṃ nānārthatve iṣṭāpattiḥ, tadādiśabdānāṃ tu buddhi- sthatvādyātmakasāmānyarūpeṇaiva bodhakatvaṃ, taduktaṃ 'śeṣe'iti sūtrabhāṣye 'idaṃviśe- ṣā hyete'patyaṃ, nivāsaḥ, samūhaḥ iti vadanti | nanvavayavarūpatyāpārāṇāṃ nānātvā- dāśutaravināśināṃ kramikāṇāṃ teṣāṃ yugapadavasthānāsaṃbhavena samūhagataikatvā (kakriyā)bhiprāyatvāsaṃbhavācca' ākhyātenaikā kriyā pratyāyyata' iti vyavasthānupapattiḥ, na ca tādṛśavyasthā'siddhaiva, mānābhāvādi[ti vācyaṃ, na vā
tiṅntānyekaśeṣārambhaṃ prayojayantīti svarūpasūtrasthabhāṣyasyaiva mānatvādi]tyā- śaṅkāṃ nirācaṣṭe ākhyāte iti| vyavasthāpi - kriyaikaiva pratyāyyata iti niyamo'pi | avacchedaketi | avacchedakaḥ śakyatāvacchedakatāvacchedako [yo buddhiviśeṣastadaikyamādāyaivetyarthaḥ,sā ca] buddhiḥ[-samūhālambanarūpā ekaiva, tathā ca tādṛśabuddhigataikyāropanibandhanā tādṛśī] vyavastheti bhāvaḥ,athavā tādṛśabuddhistha- samūhaikyamādāyaivetyarthaḥ | uktārthe harisaṃmatimāha uktaṃ cetyādinā |
guṇabhūtairiti | kramajanmanāmeṣāṃ vyāpārāṇāṃ samūhaṃ prati guṇībhūtaista- ttadrūpeṇa bhāsamānairavayavairyuktaḥ saṃkalanātmikayaikatvaviṣayakabuddhayā prakalpitā- bhedaḥ - prakalpito yo bhedaḥ tadrūpo yaḥ samūhaḥ sa kriyeti vyavahriyata ityarthaḥ | abhedaścāvayavabhūtavyāpārāṇām | atrāvayavāśrayaṃ paurvāparthe, samūhagatamekatvaṃ ca spaṣṭameca labhyate | ekaikāvayave'pi samūhatvarūpāropādadhiśrayaṇādye- kāvayavadaśāyāmapi 'pacatī'tyādivyavahāropapattiḥ, ārope ca pratītirevāva- lambanam , adhyāsagṛhītayaiva kriyayā dhātorākhyātasya(ātma) śaktirūpasaṃbandhagraho bodhyaḥ, tadatiriktamukhyārthasadbhāvānna dhātvarthasya gauṇatvamevaṃ satyāśaṅkyaṃ, yasya hi mukhyo'rtho'tirikto'sti tasyaiva gauṇatvaṃ yathā 'siṃho māṇavaka'ityādau |
yadvā-tādṛśānekavyaktigatā pacitvādijātireva kriyā, svataḥsādhyatvavirahe'- pi tasyā vyaktidvārakaṃ tadabadhitameva, sphoṭavadasyāḥ krabhavadbhirapi kṣaṇairabhivyā- ktistatsādhakaścānugatavyavahāraḥ |
uktaṃ ca vākyapadīye 
'jātimanye kriyāmāhuranekavyāktivartinīm |
asādhyā vyaktirūpeṇa sā sādhyevopalakṣyate ||' iti |
phalasyāpi svahetuvyāpāravartipaurvāparyāropeṇa sādhyatvenaiva bhānam , ata eva 'pacyate taṇḍulaḥ svayameve'tyādo 'phalamātravācakatā pacyāde'riti mate'pi tasya dhātusaṃjñānirvāhaḥ, kārakāṇāṃ bhāvanayaivānvaya'iti niyamaśca saṃgacchate | anyathā dvitīyādyarthakarmakārakasya phalenaivānvayāttadasaṃgatiḥ spaṣṭaiva | atra śaktatā- vacchedakaṃ pacitvādikaṃ tattadānupūrvīviśeṣarūpaṃ,na tu dhātutvam , atiprasaktatvāt , tadajānato'pi bodhācca |
phalāśraya iti | nanu kārakacakrapravartakatvād , vyāpārāṃśasya prādhānyācca vyāpārāśraya iti prathamato vaktumucitaṃ, na tu phalāśraya iti iti cenna |
śaktigrāhaka 'laḥ karmaṇi' iti sūtre,mūle ca phalāśrayasya karmaṇaḥ prāṅnirdeśāttasyaivādāvabhidhānasya yuktataratvāt , vyāpārasya sarvaprādhānyamiti niyamābhāvācca, karmapratyayasamabhivyāhāre vyabhicārāt | nanu karmakartroḥ śakti- grāhakasūtrasattve'pi phalāśrayavyāpārāśrayayorna kiṃcidākhyātasya śaktigrāhakaṃ, karmakartṛpadayorloke spandakṛtimatoreva prasiddherityāśaṅkyāha phalāśrayaḥ karmeti | kṛtrimākṛtrimanyāyenaitayoreva karmādipadena grahaṇaṃ yuktamiti bhāvaḥ | 'svatantraḥ kartāṃ' iti sūtreṇa dhātūpāttapradhānībhūtavyāpārāśrayasyaiva svatantrasya kartṛsaṃjñāvidhānāt , kṛtimata eva tadvidhāne 'ghaṭo bhavati' 'kāṣṭhāni pacantī'tyādau dhaṭādeḥ sā na syāditi dik |
nanu phalavyāpārayorapyākhyātārthatvāpattiḥ, na ceṣṭāpattiḥ, 'devādattaḥ pacatī'- tyādāvākhyātena, dhātunā ca phūtkārādyupasthitau nirākāṅkṣatvenānvayānupapatteḥ, phūtkārādiviśiṣṭasya phūtkārādāvanvaye ākāṅkṣāvirahāt , ata eva 'karma gacchatī'tī na prayogaḥ, evaṃ 'pacyate taṇḍula' ityādāvapītyāśaṅkāṃ nirasitumāha tatretyādinā | dhātulabhyatvāditi | ananyalabhyasyaiva śabdaśakyatvāditi bhāvaḥ | nanvāśraye śaktisvīkāre āśrayatvaṃ śakyatāvacchedakatvamabhyupeyam , āśra- yatvaṃ cādhāratvaṃ, tacca nirūpakādibhedād bhinnaṃ, tadānantyācchatayānantyāpattirata āha tattacchaktīti | kartṛtvādiśaktisvarūpamityarthaḥ | yadyapi śakterapi nirūpakabhedānnānātvamabhyupeyamanyathā''dya bhuktvā'yaṃ dvayahe dvayahādvā bhokte'tyatra saptamīpañcamyauḥ 'saptamīpañcamyau kārakamadhye' ityatra kārakapadasya śaktiparatvepyanupaparttitādavasthyaṃ syāttathāpyāśrayatātvādinānugatasya tasya śakyatāvacchedakatve doṣānavakāśāditi bhāvaḥ, ata eva kṛtiviśiṣṭavācitṛcaḥ 'kṛtyarthakakṛdhātusamabhivyāhāre āśrayaparatva'miti naiyāyikā manyate | evaṃ kṛtpratyayasthale bhāvānāṃśasyākṣepalabhyatvāt 'āśrayamātramartha' iti mīmāṃsakāḥ |
nanvāśrayasyākhyātavācyatve mānābhāvaḥ, na ca 'pacatī'tyukte āśraya- pratītirjāyamānā''khyātasya śaktikalpanāyāṃ mānaṃ gavādyarthapratītiriva gavādipade tattadarthaśaktikalpanāyāmiti vācyam ,anyathānupapannapratītereva mānatvāt anyathā gaṅgādipadasya tīrādāvapi śaktikalpanāpatteḥ, tatra lakṣaṇayaivopattiriti cet , prakṛte'pyanyathopapattervakṣyamāṇatvāt | na ca 'laḥ karmaṇi' ityādi sūtrameva mānaṃ, tatratya'karma'padasyānuvṛtta'kartṛ'padasya ca lāghavena dharmamātraparatvāt ,
tatsūtrasya 'karmādibodhakā lakārā' ityarthapratipādakatvādvā, evaṃ ca bodhasya vakṣyamāṇarītyāpi nirvāhe na tasya tacchaktigrāhakatvamityāśayena pṛcchati, nanva- nayorityādinā |
pratīteriti | phalāśrayavyāpārāśrayapratīterityarthaḥ | lakṣaṇāsvīkāre pada- vṛttikalpanāgauravādāha ākṣepāditi | ākṣepo nāmārthāpattirmīmāṃsakamate, tā- rkikamate'numānaṃ, yena vinā yadanupapannaṃ tena tat kalpyate, yathā 'pīno devadatto divā na bhuṅkte' ityatra bhojanaṃ vinānupapannena yogabhyāsādyajanyapuṣṭatvena bhojanaṃ kalpyate, tacca dine bādhe rātribhojanamādāya paryavasyati, tathā prakṛte bhāvanāyāḥ kartāraṃ vinānupapattyā kartṛrūpāśrayaṃ sā kalpayati, tathā cānupapattijñānamarthāpattya- paranāmākṣepaḥ, tajjanyaṃ jñānamapi tannāmakaṃ pramityantaram | tārkikamate tu 'bhāvanā kvaccidāśritā, guṇatvāt, sakartṛkā vā kāryatvādghaṭatvava'- dityanumitijanakaṃ vyāptijñānādikamākṣepaḥ | evaṃ cākṣepeṇa bhāvanājñānotta- ramarthāpattyānumityā vā kartā viṣayīkriyata iti paryavasannam | mīmāṃ- sakairākṣepalabhyasyāpi śābdabodhaviṣayatvābhyupagamādākhyātajanyaśābdabodhe bhānamavi- ruddham , evaṃ phalasyāpi svāśrayaṃ vinānupapattyā tena sākṣipyata ityarthaḥ |
nanu vṛttyā padajanyapadārthopasthiteḥ śābdhabodhahetutvenākṣepalabhyasya śābdabo- dhaviṣayatvaṃ na saṃbhavati, kiṃ cānupapattyādi[jñāna]vidhurāṇāmapyanubhavasiddhatadbodhānu- pattiḥ, ākṣipte tatrākhyātārthasaṃkhyānvayānupapattiśca, vṛttyā padānupasthitatvāt , tathā cocyate 'śābdī hyākāṅkṣa śabdenaiva prapūryate' iti | śābdī - śābdabo- dhaviṣayiṇī, ākāṅkṣā - jijñāsā, śabdenaiva prapūryate - vṛttyā padārthopasthiti- dvārā viṣayasiddhisaṃpādanena nivāryate'iti tadartha ityata āha prathamānteti | uttarayati atrocyata ityādinā |
nanvidaṃ sūtraṃ karmaṇi śaktigrāhakaṃ, na kartari, tatra kartṛpadāsattvādityāśaṅkyāha atra hīti | cakārāditi | bhāṣye'nukarṣaṇārthasarvacakārapratyākhyāne sati kathaṃ tadanuvṛttiriti cet , svapitatvapratijñānādapekṣāvaśāccānyasyānyatrevā- trāpi tadanuvṛttisaṃbhava ityavehi | bhāve lakārastadanuvādako [na vācako] nāpi dyotako, dhātuta eva tasya raphuṭapratīteḥ, saṃkhyākālārthakaśca saḥ, tadarthe saṃkhyāyā ekatvarūpāyā kriyāyāmanvayasaṃbhāvāt , ata eva bhāṣye'vyayebhya ivaikavacanānutpa- ttirnāśaṅkiteti spaṣṭaṃ mañjūṣā-śekharādau | nanvanena sūtreṇa lakāre karmādibodhakatārūpā śaktirgṛhyate, na tu tivādau, na ca sthānyartha [evādeśārtha] iti tatrāpi tatsiddhiriti vācyaṃ, sthānino'prayogeṇa bodhājanakatvādityāśaṅkāṃ nirācikīrṣu- rāha bodhakatārūpāmityādinā | atiriktaśaktipakṣe'pi tatsamanaiyatyameva tasyā iti bodhyam |
tibādiśaktimiti | tanniṣṭhāṃ śaktimityarthaḥ, vyavahāradaśāyāṃ tatprayogā- devārthabodhāt | tatsthānitvena - tibādisthānitvena | kalpite - lāghavena prakriyānirvāhārtha kalpite | nanu'lasye'tyanuttavā'tiptasbhī'tyādinā svatantrāṇāṃ tiptasādīnāmeva vidhānamastu, evaṃ 'laḥ karmaṇi 'iti sūtre'tiṅka- rmaṇī'tyastu,'vartamāne tiṅ'tiṅaḥ śatṛśānacāraprathamāsamānādhikaraṇe'ityādinyasai- stivādīnanudya tattatkālavidhirastviti cenna, 'dita ātmanepadānām 'ityādera- saṃgatatvāpatteḥ | na ca 'vartamāne tiṅṭ' 'anadyatane tiṅṭ' ityādinyāsaistatta- tkālārthakativādestattadanuvandhāsaṅgaḥ kriyatāṃ, tathā ca 'ṭita ātmanepadānām ' ityādernāsaṃgatiriti vācyaṃ,'liṭastabhyoreśirec' 'luṭaḥ prathamasya ḍāraurasaḥ' ityā- destathāpyasaṃgatatvāpatteḥ,tatra tatra nyāsāntarakaraṇe gauravāpatteścatyāstāṃ vistaraḥ | prakavpya - āropya | 'arthavatyeva sthānyādeśabhāva' iti niyamānurodhāttadārepa iti bhāvaḥ | [tatrānurūpaṃ dṛṣṭāntamāha nakāreti | 'rāmān' 'ghadaiḥ ityādau nakārādita eva tattadarthapratītyā tanniṣṭhaiva śaktiriti bhāvaḥ | karttṛtvakarmatvapare iti | lāvavānurodhāt | dharmiparatve gurūbhūtadharmadharmiṇorūbhayatra śaktiḥ kalpayā, anantānāṃ kṛtīnāṃ, phalānāṃ ca śakyatāvacchedakatvamupeyaṃ, tathā cātigauravam | rṛteḥ śakyatve tu, kṛtitvasya jātirūpasya śakyatāvacchedakatayā lāghavamiti bhāvaḥ |
darśanāntarīyarītyā - bhāvārthādhikaraṇoktamīmāṃsakarītyā | dhātvarthatvābhā- vāditi | viklittyādiphalameva dhātvarthe, na vyāpāraḥ, vahūnāṃ dhātūnāṃ tatra śakti-kalpanāpekṣayā tadapekṣayā'lpānāṃ pratyayānāṃ tatkalpane lāghavāt, 'pacatotya-trākhyātasya bhāvanārthakakṛdhātunā vivaraṇācca tasyaiva bhāvanārthakatvaṃ yuktaṃ, vivriyamāṇavivaraṇayoḥ samānārthakatvasya klṛptatvāditi bhāvaḥ | tatphala- syāpi 'karmavat karmaṇā 'ityatra kriyāśabdavyavahartavyatayā dṛṣṭatvena tanmātravācino'pi dhātusaṃjñāsiddhiḥ, evameva'pacyate taṇḍulaḥ svayameve'tyatra bhavanma- te'pi pacyāderdhātutvamiti bhāvaḥ |
tatra - vyāpāre,vyāpārattvaṃ phūtkāratvādikaṃ vā śakyatāvacchedakaṃ[ṇvulādīnā- m] evaṃ 'phalasya dhātulabhyatve'pi tadāśrayatvarūpe karmatve eva lakāravidhiḥ syā"diti pūraṇīyam, ata eva 'kartṛkarmādivācitvaṃ ne'tyuttaragranthe karmādigrahaṇaṃ saṃgacchate, pūrvapakṣe karmavācakatvānaṅgīkāre tadasaṃgatiḥ spaṣṭaiva | kṛtāmapīti | kartrādyarthe vihitānāṃ ṇavultavyadādīnāmapi | na sidhyediti | uktayuktisāgyā- diti bhāvaḥ | nanu 'kartari kṛt' 'tayoreva kṛtya ' ityādyanuśāsanavalāt kṛtāṃ kartrādivācitvasiddhiḥ,tatratyakartrādipadasya dharmiparatyādityata āha 'kartari kṛta' iti ceti | cena 'tayoreva 'ityādīnāṃ parigrahaḥ | tulyayogakṣemamiti | tulyo yogakṣemo'rthapratipādakatā yasyetyarthaḥ | ayaṃ bhāvaḥ, 'yadi lāghavamātrapakṣapātena 'laḥ karmaṇi ' iti sūtrasthakarmādipadasya dharmaparatvāṅgīkārastarhi tatrāpi tathaiva svīkāra ucito, vaiṣamye bījābhāvāt |
nu devadattādinā'bhedānvayānubhavabalāt kṛtāṃ dharmivācakatvapuraskāraḥ, bhāva- nāyāstvākṣepā[llābhaḥ] setsyatītyāśaṅkya prakṛte'pi abhedānvayānubhavabalādeva tathātvamurīkartumucitamityāha api ceti | mīmāṃsakānāmityasya'mate'iti śeṣaḥ tathā sati - ākṣepalabhyatve sati | na syāditi | ākhyātāvācyatvāt, vācya- syaiva prādhābhyamiti niyamāt | anyathā'gnimānaye'tyādāvākṣiptapātrāderapi prādhā- myāpatteḥ, ki ca 'mayūraḥ parvatetarasminna nṛtyati' 'nṛtyati ce'tyatra nṛtyasyādhikaraṇaṃ vinānupapattyā kalpyamānamadhikaraṇamitarabhādhāt parmatarūpama dhikaraṇaṃ sidhyatīti mīmāṃsakasiddhāntaḥ, tatra tasyādhikaraṇasya prādhānyāpatteḥ | ata eva viparītaṃ na, tiṅajñivava kṛtsvapi bhāvanāyāḥ pratyayavācyatvādīkṣiptakartrapekṣayā prādhānyāpatteḥ | [tathā cā'nubhavavirodhaḥ,] tathā toktaṃ bhaṭṭaiḥ yadi kartrā dhātvarthena vā bhāvanāvaga- myate,tataḥ pācakapāṭhakādipade iva tirohitasvaravarūpāvagamyeta, prādhānyāttu śabdā- rthatvādhyavasānamiti bhāvaḥ |
ghaṭamānayetyādāviveti | jāterānayanakarmatvānvayānupapattyā tadanvayopapattaye jātyākṣiptāyāḥ svāśrayavyakterthathā prādhānyamanubhavasiddhaṃ, tadvadākhvātavācyakartrākṣi- ptabhāvanāyā anubhavasiddhaprādhānyamupapādayituṃ śakyamityarthaḥ, uktaniyamasya vyābhi- cārāditi bhāvaḥ | na ca pratyayavācyatvāt kartureva prādhānyāpattiḥ, pratyayārthākṣipta- syāprādhānyaniyamāt, ata evākṣiptamātrasyāprādhānyaniyame ākṛtyadhikaraṇoccheda iti dūṣaṇasyāpi nāvasara iti vācyaṃ, tādṛśaniyame mānābhāvāt, 'gomantamānaye'-
tyatra matuvarthasaṃvandhākṣiptadharmiṇaḥ prādhānyanubhavena vyābhicārācca |
vivaraṇadarśanāt - pākaṃ karotīti bhāvanārthakakṛdhātunā vivaraṇadarśanāt | vācyatvaṃ - pratyayavācyatvam | vivaraṇaviṣayatvāviśeṣāditi | 'karotī'tyāthyātena karturvivaraṇaṃ, tataḥ karturapi bodhād vivriyamāṇasyāpi tadarthakatvamāvaśyakamiti bhāvaḥ | tātvaryārtheti | 'pacitī'tyataḥ karturbodho jāyatāmiti yattātpartha tadvi- ṣayībhūtākṣepalabhyakarturvivaraṇaṃ, na śabdārthavivaraṇamityarthaḥ |
nanu śabdārthavivaraṇasyaiva dṛṣṭatvenedamaśabdārtha[vivaraṇamityasaṅkāmanurūpadṛ- ṣṭātapradarśanena nirākaroti pākaṃ karotīti | dvitīyayā'śabdārthakarmatvādi]- vivaraṇādaśabdārthasyāpi saṃsargasya vivaraṇadarśane'pi tadatiriktāśabdārthavivaraṇama- dṛṣṭacaramevetyanuśayāno dṛṣṭantāntaramāha itaretaradvandva iti | samuñcayāṃśeti | cadvayena samuccayāṃśo vivriyate | tulyatvāditi | bhāvanāyāḥ śabdārthatva- virahe'pyuktarītyā vivaraṇopapattau bhāvanāyā apyākhyātārthatvaṃ na sidhyedityarthaḥ |
nanu lāghavādbhāvanaiva pratyayavācyā, na tu kartā, gauravāt, 'tathā sati kṛtā- mapi tathātvaṃ syā'titi na śaṅkanīyaṃ, 'devadattaḥ pakte'tyādau devadattādinābhedānva- yabodhasyānubhavasiddhatvādityata āha, kiñceti | abhedānvayadarśanāt - abhe- dānvayasyānubhavasiddhatvāt | samānavibhaktimattvamiti | tāddaganvayapratiyogya- nuyogivācakaviśeṣaṇaviśeṣyapadayoḥ samānavibhaktimattvaṃ, tacca viśeṣaṇavācakapade viśeṣyavācakapadottaravibhaktisajātīyavibhaktimattvaṃ,[viśeṣyavācakapade viśeṣaṇavāca- kapadottaravibhaktisajātīyavimaktimattvaṃ vā] | sājātyaṃ ca vibhaktivibhājakatā- vacchedakaprathamātvādinā,nātaḥ prathamādvitīyādīnāṃ sarvāsāṃ vibhaktitvādinā sājā- tyā'nnīlo ghaṭa'mityādivākyādabhedānvayabodhāpattiḥ | nayāmakam - prayojakam | nāstīti | prathamātiṅavibhattayorekarūpeṇa sājātyābhāvāt, evaṃcābhedānvayabodha- prayojakābhāvādabhedānvayabodhāsaṃbhavānna dharmiṇi śaktisvīkāro yukta iti bhāvaḥ |
vyabhicārāt samānivabhaktimattvasya niyāmakatvaṃ na saṃbhavatītyāha somena yajeteti | somapadasya somavati lakṣaṇā, somavato yāgenābhedānvayaḥ, abhedānva- yānurodhādeva cātra matvarthalakṣaṇābhyupagama iti nyāyaprakāśādau vistaraḥ |
stokaṃ pacatīti | atra sotokapadārthasya dhātvarthaphale'bhedenānvayaḥ | rāja- puruṣa iti| atra rājasvatvavato laśraṇayā rājapadārthasya puruṣeṇa tathānvayaḥ|atrā- dyasthaladvaye vibhaktyoḥ sajātīyatvābhāvayukto viśiṣṭābhāvaḥ,carame tu viśeṣyābhāva-
prayuktaḥ sa iti vivekaḥ | abhedānvayabodhānāpatteriti | aktasamānavibhaktimattva- virahāditi bhāvaḥ | tathā coktasthale'bhedānvayabodho niṣpratyūhastadupapattaye kartrādau śaktirāvaśyakīti tātparyārthaḥ |
śaṅkate na ceti | sāmānādhikaraṇyamekadharmibodhakatvaṃ, tathā ca lakṣaṇayai- vābhedānvayabodhanirvāhe tatra śaktisvīkāro nirarthaka iti bhāvaḥ | dravyavācitvā- nāpatteriti | lakṣaṇayaiva dravyabodhanirvāhāt | ityāditaddhitānāmapi | ityādi- samudāyaghaṭakataddhitānāmapītyarthaḥ | atrāmi 'dravyavācitvānupapatte'rityanuṣaṅgeṇā- nvayaḥ | nanu samasyamānapadārthasaṃpandhinyeva bahuvrīheranekamanyapadārthe'nuśāsanādvi- grahavākyādyadupasarjanako vartipadārthasthaḥ pratīyate sa svāmyādirevānyapadārthaḥ, vigra- havākyavṛttivākyayorviśeṣaṇaviśeṣyabhāvavyatyāsarūpameva vailakṣaṇyamityanyat | tathā ca kuto yaugikānāṃ dravyavācitvānuśāsanena [iti vigrahapradarśanāt ] pradhānaṣaṣṭharthe evānuśāsanalābhāditi yojanā | ityanuśāsanena | etadrūpeṇānuśāsanena - śāstreṇa | 'anuśāsanene'ti karaṇe lyuṭ | iti vigrahapradarśanāt - etādṛśavigra- hapradarśanahetoḥ, pradhānībhūtaṣaṣṭhyarthe evānuśāsanasya - sādhutvānvākhyānasya lābhā- dityarthaḥ | ayamanuśāsanaśabdo bhāvalyuḍantaḥ | vigrahāt ṣaṣṭhyantārthe tallābhaḥ | ṣaṣṭhyantena ca prakṛtyaropasartanako vibhattayarthaḥ saṃbandha eva pratīyate, atastatraiva tasya sādhutvaṃ labhyate iti samuditatātparyārthaḥ | uktaṃ cāruṇādhikaraṇavārtike 
'bahuvrīhiḥ samāso'yaṃ matubarthe vidhīyate |
asyātreti ca saṃbandhe matvarthīyaḥ pravartate ||' iti |
[evaṃ vaiśvadevādiśabde'pi bodhyaṃ, na ceṣṭāpattirityāha tathā ceti | dravyā- nukteriti |] ākṛtyādhikaraṇanyāyenāruṇādipadasyāruṇyādiguṇe eva śaktikalpa- nāt ,piṅgākṣyādipadasyoktarītyā saṃbandhe eva śaktikalpanācceti bhāvaḥ | svavā- kyeti | svaṃ - krayaṇabhāvanā,tatpratipādakavākyaghaṭakapiṅgākṣyādipadopāttetyarthaḥ| atrāruṇyasyāmūrtatvāt krayaṇabhāvanāṃ prati karaṇatvāsaṃbhavāt tāmuddiśya tadvidhānā- saṃbhavād guṇavācakasyāpyasya lakṣaṇayā dravyabodhakatvasaṃbhavena krayātpṛthagbhūtaḥ- prākaraṇikadravyāṇyuddiśya tatparicchedakatvenāruṇyarūpo guṇo vidhīyate, 'yena dravyeṇa kicit kriyate tadaruṇaguṇakene'ti |
nanu lakṣitadravyasya kraye eva karaṇatvasaṃbhavāt kimiti vākyabhedamurīkaro- ṣīti cenna, ekahāyanyavaruddhakraye lakṣitadravyāntaravidhyasaṃbhavāt , [ekahāyanyāsta- duguṇaviśiṣṭāyāvidhānaṃ sukaramiti cenna,svaśabdena vidhānena punarvidhānā'sambhavāt] na caikahāyanīmevānūdya guṇavidhirastu, naivaṃ sati vākyabheda iti vācyam | tathā sati tasyāḥ krayaṇaṃ prati guṇībhāvaḥ svārthatvaṃ ca syāt, taccaikoccāraṇena na saṃbhavati, guṇībhūtatvapradhānatvayovirodhāt , tasmādāvṛttiprasaṅgaḥ, ato vākyabhedaṃ parikalpya prākaraṇikadravyamātre āruṇyaniveśa iti pūrvapakṣayitvā, tṛtoyayā guṇasyaiva karaṇatvaṃ krayaṃ prati viśpaṣṭaṃ gamyate, tacca dravyadvāreṇa yujyata iti siddhānta uktaḥ | yadyapi guṇasyāmūrtatvāt krayaṃ prati sādhanatvaṃ na saṃbhavati,tathāpi dravyaparicchedadvārā saṃbhavatyeva, tathā cāyamarthe bhavati 'aruṇaparicchinnena dravyeṇa krayaṃ kuryā'titi| evaṃ śrautasyaiva guṇasyaikavākyatve saṃbhavati lakṣaṇā,vākyabhedaśca na yujyete, kriyārthatve ca tadīyadravye eva niveśa ityabhihitaṃ pārthasārathimi- śrairityalaṃ vistareṇa |
evaṃ vaiśvadevyādipadaghaṭakataddhitasyoktarītyā dravyavācakatvābhāve, 
'āmikṣāṃ devatāyuktāṃ vadatyavaiṣa taddhitaḥ |
āmikṣāpadasāṃnidhyāt svasyaiva viṣayārpaṇam ||' 
iti bhāṭṭasiddhāntavirodhastanmūlakaguṇādhikaraṇocchedaśca draṣṭavyaḥ | tathāhi 'tapte payasi dadhyānayati sā vaiśvadevyāmikṣā vājibhyo vājinam' iti cāturmāsyāntargatavaiśvadevaparvaṇi śrūyate, tatra 'vājibhyo vājina'miti vākyena prakāntavaiśvadevayāge 'vāja āmikṣārūpamannaṃyeṣāmastī'ti vyutpatyā vājipadalabdha- veśvedevānuddiśya vājinarūpo guṇo vidhīyate iti pūrvapakṣamukatvotpattiśiṣṭaśrautā-
mikṣārūpadravyāvaruddhatvena nirākāṅkṣatayā prakṛtayāge vājinasya niveśāsaṃbhavena tadguṇakamapūrvadevatākaṃ karmāntaraṃ vidhīyate, prakṛtayāge vājinasaṃbandho bhavanavākyena syāt,tataḥ pūrvameva taddhitaśrutyā prabalayāmikṣāsaṃbandhasaṃpādanaṃ kṛtamiti nairākāṅkṣa- yam, āmikṣetipadāṃtarāpekṣāsatve'pi svabhinnārthakapadāntarāpekṣaṇāt śrautasvameva viniyogasya, yatra bhinnārthakapadāntarāpekṣā tatra vākyapramāṇakatvam,tathā coktaguṇa- karmāntaravidhānamiti siddhānta uktaḥ, uktarītyā tadasaṃgatiḥspaṣṭaiveti | mūlokta- yukteḥ sāmānādhikaraṇyasyeti | [dravyavācakatvasādhikā mūlayuktiḥ, - asādhāraṇa- yuktiḥ,] sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ - 'piṅgākṣī gauḥ' vaiśvadevyāmikṣetyatrānubhavasidvaṃ sāmā- nādhikaraṇyaṃ, tasyoktarītyā - ākṣepādinopapatterityarthaḥ | evaṃ ca yaugikānāṃ gomatpiṅgākṣyādipadānāṃ, taddhaṭakasya pratyayasya ca na kicid dravyavācakatva- sādhakaṃ, tatpratīterākṣepādinopapatta'riti teṣāṃ tadvācitvaṃ na syāditi bhāvaḥ |
nanvevaṃ gomattvamityataḥ saṃsargapratyayo na syāt, padārthatāvacchedakasyaiva tvā- dyarthatvāditi cenna, gotvādipadebhyaḥ śakyajātibhānavatadbhānopapattīḥ , nanu prakṛti- janyabodhe ākṣiptavyattayaṃśe prakāratayā tadbhānopapattiriti cenna , prakṛte'pi sāmyat, | yattu mīmāṃsāvārtikakārairmatubārdravyavācakatāsādhakayuktirupanyastā,- "matubāderna saṃbandhasāmānyaṃ vācyaṃ, [gomaddaṇḍyādipadānāṃ] paryāyatāpattīḥ, na saṃba- ndhaviśeṣaḥ, tatra saṃbandhirūpa eva hi viśeṣo'taḥso'vasyaṃ vācyo'nabhihitasya vyāvartakatvāsaṃbhavāt, prātipadikenaikasaṃbandhyabhidhāne'pi saṃbandhasyobhayanirūpyatvena dvitīyābhidhānāvaśyakatve [ubhayavācakatve] pratyuta gauravaṃ syāt, ato lāghavād dravyameva matuvādivācyaṃ, tenākṣepāllābhe na saṃbandhe śaktiḥ | uktaṃ ca 
'sarvatra yaugikaiḥ śabdairdravyanevābhidhīyate |
na hi saṃbandhavācyatvaṃ saṃbhavatyatigauravāt ||'
ki ca 
'saṃbandhinaiva saṃbandhaḥ pratyetuṃ yadi śakyate |
punastasyābhidhāśakti kaḥ śruteḥ parikavpayet ||' iti
bhāvartatyayenaiva saṃbandhābhidhānaṃ na syāditi cenna |
'yadā svasamaveto'tra vācyo nāsti guṇo'paraḥ |
tadā gantantarābhāvāt saṃbandho vācya āśritaḥ ||'
ityanena tadabhidhāne yukteruktatvāt | matubarthaviśeṣaṇībhūtānāṃ gavāṃ
puruṣāsamavetatvāt samavetaguṇābhidhāyinā tvapratyayena grahaṇāsaṃbhavenāgatyārthākṣiptaḥ saṃbandharatadartha ityāśrīyate iti tadarthaḥ | yadapyekaṃ hāyanamasyā iti vigrahadarśanāt [ṣaṣṭhyartha]saṃbandhārthakatā tādṛśasamāsasya syāditi dūṣaṇaṃ, tadapi na | anubhūya[māna]matubarthānusāreṇa citrāṇāṃ gavāmayamityādivākyayasyaiva vigraha- tvasvīkārāt | taduktam 
'abhidheyo bahuvrīheryadyapyasyeti kathyate |
tathāpi prathamāntena tulyo'sau saṃpratīyate ||' iti |
atrāhurbṛhadbhūṣaṇakṛtaḥ matubādīnāṃ dravyasāmānyavācitve paryāyatāpatteḥ tulyatayā viśeṣata eva tadvodhakatvāṅgīkāra āvaśyakaḥ | tatra viśeṣaḥ saṃsargo gavādiprātipadikārtho vā, ādye'pratītasyānabhihitasya viśeṣakatvāsaṃbhavādvācyatvaṃ tasyāṅgīkāryamevaṃ ca gauravaṃ spaṣṭameva| antye saṃsargavācitāmate'pi prātipadikā- rthasya gavādervyārktakatayā viśeṣaṇatvasaṃbhave tatpakṣopekṣā nocitā | saṃbandhaptatyakṣe eva yāvatsaṃbandhiprasyakṣaṃ kāraṇamanyathā ghadākāśasaṃyogapratyaśrāpatetīḥ | evaṃ cobha- yanirūpyaḥ saṃbandha iti dvitīyasaṃbandhijñānaṃ vinā tadvodhānirvāhāttadvācakatāvaśya- kīti dūṣaṇaṃ nirastam | tathā ca gomatpadādgoviśiṣṭa itibad gosaṃbandha iti bodho niṣpratyuha eva | śabdaśaktisvabhāvādeva, bhāvanāpadopāttabhāvānāyā āśrayānākṣepakatvamākhyātopāttāyāstasyāstadākṣepakatvamitivat saṃbandhapado- pāttasaṃbandhasyāśrayānākṣepakatve'pi matuvādyupāttasya tasya tadākṣepakatvaṃ saṃbhavati | 'yadvā svasamaveta' ityādyapi , tvādeḥ svasamavetaguṇavācakatvaniyamāsiddherjāteḥ padārthatvamate prakṛtyarthavācakatvasīkāreṇa matubādeḥ saṃbandhāvācakatve gomattvamitya- tastatpratyayānāpattaḥ, prakṛtyarthasamavetavācakatve'tigauravāpattaḥ | ghaṭatvatvamityato ghaṭatvatvasyaiva bodhāpatteśca | ghaṭādipadena ghaṭatvābhighāne'pi prādhānyena bodhārtha tvādiśakteśavaśyakateti na vaiyarthyaśaṅketi bodhyam | tasmāt saṃbandhavācyatve doṣābhāvādanuśāsanalabhyatvācca tasyaiva vācyatvaṃ syānna tu dravyasya | sāmānādhi- karaṇyānurodhena dravyaṃ matubādivācyamiti cet prakṛte'pi tulyamityādi vestareṇa bṛhadbhūṣaṇe nirūpitamityāśayenāha vistareṇeti | nanu 'laḥ karmaṇi ' tipta- sabhi-', dvacekayodvivacalaikatvādau lakārasya sthāne ekavacanā disaṃjñakāstibādayo bhavantīti vākyārthaparyavasānādekatvādisaṃkhyāyā eva vācyatvamanuśāsanallabhyate, na kartṛ karmaṇoḥ, śavarasāmibhiḥ puraskṛto'yamartha iti cenna, ekavacanādisaṃjñāyā
ādeśaniṣṭhatayā tivādividhinaiva 'dvacekayo-' ityāderekavākyatā, na tu 'laḥ karmaṇi-' ityanenāpi, tatra ca na kartarītyādikamasti | ata uktarthakatvāsaṃbhavā- llavidhitivādividhyostu na padaikavākyatāpi tu vākyaikavākyatā, lavidhilabhyala- kārānuvādenādeśāvidheḥ pravṛtteḥ, sa caikavacanādisaṃjñāvinirmukta eva, tasmāt-'laḥ karmaṇi-' ityanenādau vākyārthabodhane kṛte satyevānyenaikavākyayā tena ca kartradi- kamarthamupādāya tatra lakārasya vidhānāttasya vācyatvaṃ duṣpariharameva | nanu 'varta- māne laṭ' ityādinā vihitalakāramuddiśyoktarītyā padaikavākyatāsaṃbhava iti cede- vamapi supsviva tiṅkṣavapi kartrādīnāṃ pratyayārthatvaṃ saṃkhyāyāyāstadviśeṣaṇatvaṃ ca durvāram | api ca karmāśe'pyevamevāpattau karmaṇo'pyavācyatvāpattyā 'pacyate taṇaḍula' ityādau bhāvanāyāḥ karmavyabhicāritayākṣepāsaṃbhavena kamaṇo bodhānāpattiḥ | ākhyātārthasaṃkhyayāśrayākṣepasaṃbhave'pi karmatvaśaktimattvena bodhānupapattirdṛdaiva | nanu karturvācyatve nānṛtaṃ vadediti niṣedhaseya prakaraṇāpekṣayā śrutervalavattvāt puruṣārthatva- meva syānna kratvarthatvaṃ tathā ca kratau tādṛśāpabhāṣaṇe kṛte'pi kratubhraṃśaprāyaścittā- nāpattiḥ, na ceṣṭāpattiḥ, yāājñikasamācāravirodhāt | 'parāvārajñā kecidṛṣayo yadvā nastadvā na iti prayoktavye varvāṇastarvāṇa iti prayuñjate, yājñe karmaṇi puna- rnābhāṣante, yarvāṇastarvāṇo nāma ṛṣayo babhūvuḥ' iti paspaśāhnikasthabhāṣyasvarasa- virodhācceti cenna | prakāraṇasacivaśrutyā kratuyuktapuruṣārthatvasaṃbhavenādoṣāt, pramā- ṇayorvirodhe eva balāvalacintāpravṛtteḥ, prakṛte virodhābhāvādanyathā 'jañjabhyamāno'- nubrūyānmati dakṣakratū' ityatra kratāvapi jañjabhyamānapuruṣasattvādaviruddhābhyāṃ vākya- prakaraṇābhyāṃ puruṣasaṃskāramukhena kratvarthatvasiddhāntastvadīyo bhajyeteti mañjūṣādau vistara ityalaṃ pallavitena |
nu 'laḥ karmaṇi-'ityanuśāsanena lakārāṇāṃ śaktibodhanāt 'āśraye tu tiṅaḥ' ityayuktamityāśaṅkyāha bodhakateti | tatra - kartrādyarthanāṃ madhye | kartṛkarmaṇī [vyāpāraphalayoriti | karttāvyāpāre, karmaphaleviśeṣaṇamityarthaḥ | karmapratyaye karmaṇītyatra] viśeṣaṇamityanuṣajyate | nanu dhātvarthasya prakṛtyupasthāpitatayā tasyāpi pratyāsannatayā tatra kuto na viśeṣaṇaṃ, ki ca pratyayārthasya prakṛtyartha evānvayasya nyāyyatvāt , subarthasaṃkhyāyāḥ prakṛtyartha evānvayasya dṛṣṭatvācca, tatraiva tadanvaya ucitaḥ ityāśaṅkaṃ nirākartu kartrādau viśeṣaṇatve hetumāha samāneti | tathā ca dhātvarthāpekṣayā pratyāsattyatiśayātatraiva tadanvaya
ucitaḥ | ata eva 'anabhihite' iti sūtrabhādhye 'prātipadikārtha ' iti sūtre etadanuvṛttau satyāṃ saṃkhyāyā evaitadviśeṣaṇaṃ syādanyasyāsaṃbhavāt | tathā ca 'pacatyodanaṃ devadatta' ityādau prathamā na syāt, tiṅoktatvādekatvādīnāmityuktamā- ñjasyena saṃgacchate, kriyāyāṃ tadanvaye tu tadasaṃgātiḥ spaṣṭaiva | ata eva 'pacatīti kartu'rekatvaṃ tiḍā'bhihitamiti prathamā na syāt' iti kaiyaṭopādhyāyai- ruktam | rūpapsūtre 'sādhanāpekṣaṃ pacata ityadau dvilacanam' iti bhāṣyapītyameva vyākhyātaṃ taiḥ | kriyāyāmanāropitadvitvāsaṃbhavaśca, sarvatrāropa mānābhāvaśca | 'tibantaṃ cāpi kicidvibhattarythapradhānaṃ kicit kriyāpradhānam , kāṇḍe kuḍye iti vibhattayarthapradhānaṃ ramate brāhmaṇakulamiti kriyāpradhānaṃ, na caitayorarthayorliṅga- saṃkhyābhyāṃ yogaḥ' iti 'hasvo napuṃsake ' iti sutrasthabhāṣyavirodhaśca (na) ? atra prāya iti rośaḥ | tena hataśāyikā ityādau tadyoge'pi nā'saṃgatiḥ | pacatorupamityādāvapyautsargikamekavacanaṃ pacatirūpamitivat |
vastutastu hataśāyikā ityādāvapi nāsattvabhūtakriyāyāṃ saṃkhyānvayaḥ, paraṃ tu bhāṣyakāravacanādeva tatra bahuvacanamiti prāmāṇikā iti vṛhadbhūṣaṇādau spaṣṭam | anye tu bhāvatiṅante ekavacanamautsargikamasattvabhūtatvāt kriyāyāḥ | anyathā 'brāhmaṇābhyāmāsyate' ityādau dvivacanādyāpatteḥ, kriyābhedena kartṛbhedasyāśyakatvāditi vadanti | mīmāṃsakanaiyāyikādīnāmapi sādhama eva saṃkhyānvayo'bhimato na kriyāyāmiyāneva paraṃ bhedo yatanmate padāntaropasthāpitādau tadanvayaḥ, svamate tiṅupasthāpite iti | na cānekaḥ pacati, dārāḥ pacanti ityadau sādhane'pyāropitasaṃkhyānvaya āvaśyaka iti vācyam | tatrāgatyā tadaṅgīkāre mānābhāvāditi bhāvaḥ | subvibhattarythasaṃkhyāyāstu samānapratyayopātte'nvayāsaṃbhavāt prakṛtyarthe evānvayaḥ, karmādīnāṃ vibhaktivācyatāmate tatsaṃbhave'pi dyotyatāmate tadasaṃbhava eva | tadhā coktaṃ 'bahuṣu bahuvacanam' iti sūtrabhāṣye 'evaṃ tarhi ekavacanaṃ, dvivacanaṃ, bahuvacanamiti śabdasaṃjñā etāḥ, yeṣvartheṣu svādayo vidhīyante teṣu bahuṣu, keṣvartheṣu svādayo vidhīyante, karmādiṣu, na vai karmādayo vibhattarythāḥ, ke tarhi ? ekatvādayaḥ, ekatvādiṣvapi vai vibhattarythaṣvavaśyaṃ karmādayo nimittatvenopādeyāḥ, karmaṇa ekatve, karmaṇo dvitve, karmaṇo bahutve iti' iti | atrānabhihitādhikārārambhasāmarthyāt saṃkhyā 'vibhaktivācyā, catuṣṭayaṃ prātipadikārtha ityāśayako 'na vai karmādayaḥ' ityādiḥ 'ke tarhi ekatvādayaḥ'
ityanto grantha ityāhuḥ | pare tu pañcakaṃ prātipadikārtha ityāśayako'yaṃ granthaḥ, ardhajaratīyasyāyuktatvāt, atrāpi pakṣe ekatvādīnāmāśrayākāṅkṣāyāṃ 'karmaṇi dvitīyā'ityādibhirekavākyatve sati pratyāsannatayā saṃbhavena ca nimittatvamityāśayena 'ekatvādīnāṃ prādhānyābhiprāyikā ityāhuḥ | tathā ca samānapratyayopāttatvapratyāsatyākhyātārthasaṃkhyāyāstadarthāntare āśraye evānvayaḥ | evaṃ 'laḥ karmaṇi ' 'tipatas ' 'dvayekayoḥ ' ityādīnāmekavākyatayaikatvā- diviśiṣṭakartrādau tadvidhānādityapi bodhyam | tatra bhavantaḥ pare tu teṣāṃ pūrvoktarītyā vākyaikavākyatā, na padaikavākyatā, sā ca lakārāḥ karmādau bhavati, teṣāṃ ca sthāne ekatvādau tibādayo bhavantītyevaṃrūpā | tatra hi dvitoyasya karmaṇītyādipadasyāsattvāt karmādāvekatvāderviśeṣaṇatvaṃ na saṃbhavati | āāvṛttau ca mānābhāvaḥ, tasmāddhātvartha eva saṃkhyānvayaḥ | kasyaikatvādāvityākāṅkṣāyā- mupasthita[prakṛtyarthasyaiva sambandhaucityā]tprakṛtyarthagataikatvādāvityevārthaḥ | evaṃ hi supatiṅviṣaye 'dvayekayoḥ ' ityādīnāmaikarūpyaṃ bhavatiḥ ata eva samānapratyayo- pāttatvamapyakicitkaram | karmaṇītyāderapi vidheyaṃ prati guṇatvādguṇānāṃ ca parārthatvāditi nyāyena saṃkhyayā tatsaṃbandhāsaṃbhavāt | svaprakṛtikapratyayavācya- sādhanagatasaṃkhyāroṣeṇa ca na dvivacanādyanupapattiḥ | [āropa]mūlabhūtasādhanadharmika- tatsaṃkhyāprakārakamānasaniścayasattvānna kartrādau tatsaṃśayaḥ | devadattayajñadattābhyāṃ bhūyate, supyata ityādau kartṛgatadvitvādyāropanibanthanadvi vacanādikaṃ tu nāpā- danīyam | śaktisvabhāvāt, svaprakṛtikapratyayavācyasādhanagatasaṃkhyāyā eva kriyāyāmāropo na tu bhinnapadopāttasādhanagatasaṃkhyāyā itṅgīkārāt | anekaḥ pacatītyatrāropitāyā eva sādhanagatasaṃkhyāyāḥ pratyāyakatvasya bhāṣyāsiddhānte ??tkṛptatvācca | uttarapadārthapradhāno nañtatpuruṣa iti bhāṣyasiddhāntāt 'pañcakṛtvaḥ pacyata ityādau padāntarasamabhivyāhāreṇa bahutvāgame'pi lakṣyānusāreṇa padasaṃskārapakṣasyaivāṅgīkārānna bahuvacanādyāpattiḥ | dhātunā ca nivṛtibhedaiva kriyāpratipādyate | taduktaṃ hariṇā 
'ekatve'pi kriyākhyāte sādhanāśrayasaṃkhyayā |
bhidyate na tu liṅgākhyo bhedāśrayaḥ ||' iti |
vyākhyātaṃ caitaddhelārājena, ekapadāpāttakartṛkarmalakṣaṇasādhanādhārakadvitvabahutvā- bhyāṃ pacataḥ pacanti pacyete pacyante iti kriyā viśeṣyate, tena hi
dvibahusādhanasādhyatvaṃ pratīyate kriyāyāḥ | evaṃ tadgataliṅgayogo na, śabdārtha ptatīteḥ svābhāvikatvena yuttayagamyatvāditi | tathā 'sambandhājjātabhedo'yam' iti harikārikāpratīkamupādāya 'yathākhyāte pratyayavācyākārabhede sati tatsambandhātdhātvarthakriyāyāmapi bhedaḥ pratīyate yathā pacataḥ pacanti iti puñja- rājaḥ | atra dvivacanāntodāharaṇādekatvaṃ svagatameva pratīyata ityabhipratem | ata eva ṅyāpa sūtre bhāṣye tatsūtrānārambhe pacatītyādau tiṅantāt svāyutpattiśa- ṅkaikatvādīnāmuktatvāt parihṛtā | tava mate sādhanagatāyā uktatve'pi pradhāna- bhūtadhātvarthagatasaṃkhyāyā anuktatvātsuvutpattirdurvārā, supo viśeṣyagatasaṃkhyā'- bhidhāyakatvāt | tathā rūpapasūtrabhāṣye'pi 'pacatirūpaṃ' pacatīrupa'mityādāva- mo'nupapattaliṅgasaṃkhyāvirahādityāśaṅkya kriyāpradhānamākhyātaṃ, kriyā caikā na dvitvādimatīti tiṅntādbhedānavagateḥ | tkībatvaṃ ca svabhāvāt | yadyekā kriyā pacata ityādau kathaṃ dvivacanādītyāśaṅkaya sādhanāpekṣaṃ tadityuktam | atra sādhane dvitvādyanvaya iti vyākhyā na sādhīyasī | ekatvasya kriyāyāmanvaya, dvitvādeḥ sādhane iti vairūpyāpatteḥ | yadyapi kriyāgatamekatvaṃ tiṅoktaṃ tathāpyanena granthena pacatorūpaṃ pacantirūpamityatrānupapattiḥ parihṛteti bhāvaḥ | ata eva tadagre pacatorūpamityādāvapi pacata itivad dvivacanādyāpattestiṅoktatvāt parihāre kṛte sati 'ekavacanamapi tarhi na prāpnoti' ityuktam | pacatītyatra tiṅoktatvādekatva- syaikavacanaṃ na prāpnatīti tadāśayaḥ kaiyaṭopādhyāyairuktaḥ | tadagre ca 'ekavacananamutsargataḥ kariṣyati' iti tatparihāra uktaḥ | na rcautsargikamekavacanaṃ pacatorūpamityādāvapi bhaviṣyati tathā ca kriyā caiketyādyuktirnirarthiketi vā- cyam | 'dvayekayo 'iti yathāśrutatatsūtrābhiprāyeṇa tasya sattavāt | autsargika- mekavacanamiti pakṣe'pi pūrvoktakriyāgataikatvasvākhyaṇḍanācca tasyānabhi- pretatvamiti bodhyam | eko dvau bahava ityādau dvayorekasya bahuṣu trayāṇāmityādi- nirdeśaiḥ prātipadikārthasūtre vacanaśabdena ca saṃkhyāpravṛttinimittakaikādiśabdedhvanuvā- dakavibhattayaiva na kevaleti niyamaparipālane'pyatrautsargikaikavacanenaitatparipālanaṃ lakṣyānurodhāduktabhāṣyaprāmāṇyācca | ato na pacatorūpamityādau dvivacanādyā- pattiḥ| evaṃ bhāve svagataikatvanibandhanamevaikavacanaṃ na tvautsargikam | dhātvarthe tadanvayānupagame pacati bhavati pacato bhavatītyādau bhūdhātūttarākhyātārtha- saṃkhyānanvitaiva syāt, na ceṣṭāpattiḥ | 'bhavatiḥ punarvartamānakālaṃ caikatvaṃ ca' iti
bhūvādisūtrasthabhāṣyeṇa tasya saṃkhyārthakatvapratyāyanādityādi samūcire| vistāro mañjūṣāyāmanusaṃdheyaḥ | ākhyātārthasaṃkhyāprakāraketi | kartṛkarmaviśeṣyaketyapi pūraṇīyam | tathā cākhyātopasthitasaṃkhyāprakārakakartṛbhūtārthaviśeṣyakabuddhitvaṃ, tattvaviśiṣṭakarmabhūtaviśeṣyakabuddhitvaṃ ca kāryatāvacchedakakam, ākhyātajanyakartrupa- sthititvaṃ tatkarmopasthititvaṃ kāraṇatāvacchedakaṃ, samavāyaḥ kāryatvakāraṇatya- yoravacchedakaḥ sambandhaḥ | kartṛpratyayena kartrādiviśeṣyakabodhajananādākhyāta- janyopasthitervyabhicārātkāryatāvacchedakadiśiprakārakāntaniveśaḥ, sūbarthasaṃkhyā- prakārakatādṛśabuddherudayāttaddāṣatādavasthyaṃ syādataḥ ākhyātārtheti saṃkhyāviśeṣa- ṇam | tadarthasaṃkhyāyā api ākhyātaśakyatayā tadavastho doṣa iti tadupasthitiparaṃ, tajjanyopasthitisahakāreṇa saṃkhyāprakārakabodhaprāpakam | anyathā subupasthitāyāstḷḍupasthitāyāśca saṃkhyāyā ekenoktatvāddoṣatādavasthyāpattiḥ | kāṣṭhaḥ taṇḍulaṃ pacatītyatrākhyātārthasaṃkhyāyāḥ karaṇādāvanvayabodhavāraṇāya kartṛ viśeṣyakatvādeḥ kāryatāvacchakakoṭau niveśaḥ | nanu tathāpi kartṛpratyaye karmaṇi karmapratyaye kartari tadāpattiriti cenna | kartṛkarmaṇorākhyātārthatvasya niveśa- nīyatvāt | tasya ca pūrvavadevārtho'to na tadavasthedoṣaḥ | padāntareṇa kartrādyupa- sthititastādṛśabodhavāraṇāyākhyātajanyatvaṃ tattadupasthitau niveśitam | caitrasya tathāvidhasaṃkhyopasthitisattve maitrasya tādṛśavāraṇāya samavāyasyatadghaṭakatayā niveśaḥ | vastutastu kartṛkarmaviśeṣyakatvaṃ kāryātāvacchedake na niveśyaṃ, kartṛtvādya- bacchinnaviśeṣyatāsambandhena kāryakāraṇabhāvāṅgīkārānna pūrvoktadoṣaḥ, tajjanyā kartrādyuṃpasthitirudvodhikā udvodhakakāryatākāraṇatāvacchekakoṭau tatpuruṣīyatvaprave- śānna caitrasyetyādidoṣaḥ | tatpuruṣāyatvaniveśāt kāryakāraṇabhāvabāhulyamiti tu na śaṅkyaṃ, viṣayaniveśāpekṣayā kāryakāraṇabhāvanāmalpatvāt, viṣayāpekṣayā puruṣāṇā- malpatvāt | paramate kāryākāraṇabhāvaprayuktaṃ gauravamāha, naiyāyiketi | vakṣyamāṇa- kāryakāraṇabhāvābhyupagame yuktimāha ākhyātārthetyādinā | tathā ca tadanabhyupagame tathāvidhaniyamānupapattyā sa āvaśyakaḥ | dvitīyādyantapadopasthāpite'pi tadanvayā- patteriti bhāvaḥ || ākhyatārtheti pūrvavadvayākhyeyaṃ, padaprayojanamapi pūrvavadava- gantavyam | janyopasthitiriti | viṣayaniṣṭhapratyāsattyā kāryatvakāraṇatvapakṣe prathamāntatatpadārthaniṣṭhaviṣayatā kāraṇatāvacchedakatayā praveśyā,nātaḥ samūhāla- mbana[prathamāntapadajanyopasthitiviṣayatayāḥ padārthāntare sattvādatiprasaṅgaḥ |]
prathamāntapadajanyetyādinā padāntarajanyopasthiteḥ kāraṇatābhyupagamāt | etanmate svamatāpekṣayā gauravamāveditam | so'pi - uktakāryākāraṇabhāvo'pi | candra iveti | candrāderapi prathamāntarthatayā candra-bhojanādau 'dṛśyate' 'vracatī'tyādyā- khayātārthasaṃkhyāprakārakānvayabodhāpattiḥ, na ceṣṭāpattiḥ, ananubhavāt, 'candra iva dṛśyete mukhe'ityādau 'dṛśyete'iti dvivacanādyanupapattiḥ, candrasyaikatvena dvitvānvayāsaṃbhavāt | na ca kāraṇena kāryajananādviśeṣata āpādakābhāvaḥ, sāmaṭayā evāpādakatvāt, mukhe eva tadanvayabodho na candre iti niyamānirvāha- syodbhāvanīyatvācca | itarāviśeṣaṇatveti | samavāyena kāryakāraṇabhāvapakṣe 'itaraviśeṣaṇatānāpannaprathamāntārthaviśeṣyatvaṃ' kāryatāvacchedakakoṭābantarbhāvanīyaṃ, dvitīyapakṣe itaraviśeṣaṇatānāpannaniṣṭhaviṣayataiva pratyāsattitvena niveśyetyarthaḥ | candrabhojanasya vrajanakriyāyāmivārthasādṛśye viśeṣaṇatvānnoktāpāttiḥ | atigaurava- miti | svamate candrāderatavākhyātārthatvaviraheṇa tatra saṃkhyānvayabodhasyāprasaktatayā netarāviśeṣaṇatvaṃ tatrāntarbhāvanīyamiti bhāvaḥ | idamapi - uktagauravamapi, svamate paramātapekṣayā lāghavamapīti vārthaḥ | nanu vyāpāre viśeṣaṇatvamanyāyyaṃ, samāna- pratyayopāttayoḥ kartṛkarmaṇoreva tadanvayasyocitatvāt | na ca dhātīrityanu- vṛttyā prayoge, dhātorvartamānatvasyāvivakṣaṇāttadarthagatavartamānatva ityarthalābhād vyāpāre eva tadanvayāṅgīkāra iti vācyaṃ, phalasyāpi dhātvarthatvāviśeṣeṇa vyāpāre eva tadanvaye vinigamakābhāvādityata āha tathāhīti | tañca - dhātupadaṃ ca | dhātvartha vadat - uktarītyā dhātvartha bodhayat | prādhānyād prādhānyācca cakāreṇa lakṣyā- nurodhaparigrahastena 'pacyate' 'pacyamāna' ityādau phalasya prādhānye'pi na kṣatiḥ | kartṛkarmaṇoreveti | na ca pūrvoktayuktayā vyāpāre evānvaya iti vācyam, anuvṛttadhātorityasya pañcamyantatayā tathāvākyārthasaṃbhavāt, āvṛttau mānābhāvāt, lakṣaṇaikacakṣuṣkasya lakṣyadarśanābhāvācceti bhāvaḥ | lakṣyaikacakṣuṣkadarśanābhiprāyeṇa samādhatte atīteti | aniṣṭaprasañjanamuktveṣṭāsiddhimapyāha apākṣīditi | nana phale eva tadanvayaḥ svīkriyatāṃ, tatra ca na pūrvoktadoṣāvasara ityāśaṅkya nirācaṣṭe nāpītyādinā-āpatteścetyantena | prayogāpattiriti | phale tadanvayā- ṅgīkārapakṣe tu phalasyāvartamānatayā nāyaṃ doṣaḥ, tathā cāyamapi pakṣo duṣṭaḥ, evaṃ ca sarvapakṣāṇāṃ duṣṭatve tulye etatpakṣasyaivāśrayaṇamanucitamiti bhāvaḥ | ajñānā- daprayogāditi | jñānasyaiva bubodhayiṣayā prayoge nimittatvāt, śukādivat
prayogastviṣṭa eva | nanu paraprayatnāpratyakṣatve'pyānumānikādijñānasaṃbhavāttathāvidha- prayogo durvāra evetyāśaṅkyeṭāpattyā pariharati, kicititi | kicicceṣṭā- dinetyarthaḥ, ādinā tadīyalekhādiḥ |
bṛhadbhūṣaṇakārādayastu-"tadīyayatno notthānātmakaphalānukūlo'pi tu taduddo- śyako'ta eva tatrotthānāya yatata ityeva prayogo nottiṣṭhatīti, anukūlatā- viśeṣasyaiva phalavyāpārayoḥ saṃsargatayābhyupeyatvāt | ata eva taṇḍulakrayaṇādi- daśāyāmayaṃ pacatīti na prayoga" ityāhuḥ |
evaṃñceti | evaṃ - pūrvoktayuktīnāmavaśyāśrayaṇītve | bhāvanaiva - dhātvartha eva | nanu 'bhāvanāyā dhātuvācyatve kartrādestiṅvācyatve tasyāḥ pratyayārthasādhanaṃ prati viśeṣaṇatvāpattiḥ, saṃkhyākālādyatiriktapratyayārthasya prakṛtyartha prati prādhānya- syaiva niyatatvā'diti mīmāṃsakādiproktaṃ dūṣaṇamāśaṅkya nirācaṣṭe yadyapīti | bhāvaṃpradhānamiti | ākhyātaṃ - tiṅntaṃ, tiṅarthaniṣṭhaprakāratānirūpitadhātvartha- niṣṭhaviśeṣyatākabodhajanakam | sattvapradhānāni nāmānīti | sattvaṃ - dravyaṃ, liṅgādyanvayi, tatpradhānabodhajanakam, tanniṣṭhamukhyaviśeṣyatākabodhajanakabodhajana- kamityarthaḥ | mukhyaviśeṣyatā'niveśācca 'pacatyādau tiṅante, āśrayatva- saṃkhyā-niṣṭhaprakāratānirūpitakartrādi sattva-niṣṭhaviśeṣyatākabodhajalakatve'pi nātiprasaṅgaḥ, kriyāniṣṭhaprakāratānirūpitaviśeṣyatāniveśena tatrātiprasaṅgavā- raṇe'pi ['ghaṭa'ityādāvavyāpteḥ] | dhātvarthabhāvanāprādhānyamadhyavasīyata iti | tathā caniruktabhāṣyaprāmāṇyenoktaniyamasaṃkoco'vaśyamaṅgīkāryaḥ, bhavatāmeva pratyuta niruktādivirodha iti bhāvaḥ |
nanu bhavāyatīti bhāva iti vyutpattyā bhāvayaterajantabhāvapadena kṛtirūpā bhāvanocyate, kriyāpadenāpi saiva, tathā ca tiṅarthakālādimadhye kṛtirūpadarthasyaiva [prādhānyaṃ, tathā ca] dhātvarthaniṣṭhaprakāratānirūpitakṛtiniṣṭhaviśeṣyatāśālibodha- janakamākhyātamiti tadarthaḥ | evaṃ ca nipuktādikaṃ bhavadabhimatārthasādhaka- masmatpratikūlaṃ vā, pacatītyāditaḥ kṛtiniṣṭhaviśeṣyatākabodhotpatteriti vācyaṃ, 'ratho gacchati' 'jānātī'tyādāvāvyāpteḥ, (ādye) kṛterapratīteḥ, 'karotī'tyatra tatpratītāvapi tiṅarthatvābhāvāt | evaṃ 'pacyata' iti karmākhyāte 'supyate' iti bhāvākhyāte'vyāptaśca, tatra tiṅpratyayena, kṛterabodhanāt | na caivaṃ tadghaṭitād 'devadattena pacyata' ityādivākyāt kṛtipratītirmasyāditi vācyaṃ, tṛtīyayaiva tadbodhasaṃbhavāt | na cā''dheyatvameva tanmate tṛtīyārthaḥ | ata eva 'devadattena pacyate na yajñadattene'tyato yajñadattavṛttitvasyāśrayatvasaṃbandhāvacchinnapratiyogitākā- bhāva[bodha]saṃbhavaḥ, tasyāḥ kṛtyarthakatve tu kṛterjanyatvasaṃbandhāvacchinnaprati- yogitākābhāva [eva] kriyāyāṃ bodhanīyaḥ, sa ca na saṃbhavati, janyatvasya vṛttya- niyāmakatayā pratiyogitāvacchedakatvāsaṃbhavāt, āśrayatvasaṃbandhāvacchinnaprati- yogitākābhāvabhānopagamane ca yajñadattakartṛ[kara]ke'pi tādṛśābhāvasyābādhitatayā tādṛśaprayogāpattiḥ, tathā ca tatra tiṅpratyayasyaiva tadarthakatvamabhyupagamyate iti na tatrāvyāptiriti vācyaṃ, 'devadattena pakkastaṇḍula'ityataḥ kṛtibodhānāpattyā tṛtīyāyāstadarthakatvāvaśyakatvāt, ata eva 'kartṛkaraṇayostṛtīyā' ityānuśā- sanamapi sādhu saṃgacchate | prātīnanaiyāyikairvṛttyaniyāmakasaṃbandhasyābhāva- pratiyogitāvacchedakatvāṅgīkārācca na nañsamabhivyāhāre doṣaḥ, navyamatekati- janyatvasya tṛtīyārthatvānnoktadoṣaḥ | evaṃ ca-tṛtīyayaiva tadvodhanasaṃbhave karmākhyā- tasya tadvodhakatvābhāvāt 'pacyata' ityādāvavyāptirduṣpatiharaiva | 'tṛtī- yāyā ādheyatvamevārthaḥ, karmākhyātenaiva kṛtirbodhyate, kṛtpratyayasthale tṛtīyāyāḥ kṛtau, kṛtijanyatve vā lakṣaṇā' ityabhyupagame'pi karmākhyāte kṛteḥ prādhānyā- bhāvādavyāptistadavasthaiva | na ca 'kṛti'padaṃ saṃkhyākālādyatiriktatiṅartha- mātraparaṃ, tathā ca na 'ratho gacchati' 'jānāti' 'naśyatī'tyādau 'pacyate' ityatra cāvyāptiḥ, tatrāpya[śrayatvādestadarthasya prādhānyāditi vācyam, evamapi bhāvākhyate'vyāpteraparihārāt, na ca tatrāpi taṅarthakṛtyā]śrayatvāviśeṣyakabodhā- ṅgīkārānnāvyāptisaṃbhavaḥ, 'caitreṇa supyate' 'gaganena sthīyata' ityādita[ścaitravṛttiḥ
svāpānukūlā kṛtiḥ, gagamavṛttiḥ sthityāśrayatā iti bodhasambhavāditi vācyaṃ, tārkikaistādṛśabodhonānubhāvasiddho'pi tu] caitravṛttikṛtijanyaḥsvāpaḥ, gaganavṛttiḥ sthitirityeva bodho'nubhavasiddha ityuktatvāt | tattva ? janitabodhake 'pavatī'tyā- dāvavyāptivāraṇāya tathāvidhabodhajanakatāvacchedakānupūrvyadikatvameva vaktavyamevaṃ ca na bāvākhyāte'vyāptirbhūyate ityādito'pi karmatvatātparyagrahadaśāyāṃ pratyayārtha- viśeṣyakabodhajananena tādṛśānupūrvyā api tathāvidhabodhajanakatvākṣateḥ | athavā 'caitreṇa supyata' ityāditaḥ pūrvoktabodho'ṅgīkriyate'ta eva bhāvanā- viśeṣya- virahādananvitaiva saṃkhyetyākhyātavādasthadīdhitigranthaḥ saṃgacchate | tanmukhya- viśeṣyatākabodhānubhavenetaraviśeṣyatākabodhavirahādityarthakatvāt | dhātvarthe nirūpa- katāsaṃbandhena bhāvanāprakārakabodhasaṃbhavena yathāśrute'saṃgateḥ, tiṅarthabhāvanāyā dhātvarthaviśeṣyatākabodhasyānyatra klṛptasyā[palāpā]pekṣayā etādṛśaniruktādyanurodhena tādṛśabodhasvīkārasyocitatvācca | mīmāṃsakanaye pratyayārthavyāpārarūpabhāvanāyāḥ prādhānyasyaiva saṃbhavāt, tathā ca 'pacatī'tyādāvākhyātapratyayārthaḥ kṛtyādirūpā bhāvanā, saiva ca pradhānamiti matameva samyak, prakṛtipratyayayoriti niyamasyā- saṃkocenaiva nirvāhādityata āha api ceti | abhedānvayāditi | idaṃ kartṛḥ pratyayārthatvāpakṣe | kṛtestadarthatvapakṣe tvāśrayatvasaṃbandhenāvayāditi bodhyam | prādhānyāpattiriti | pratyayārthakṛtyāderitaraviśeṣaṇatānāpannatayā prathamāntārthe prakāratayā bodhajanakasāmagrīsattvānnaiyāyikamatasiddhatathāvidhabodhasvīkārāpattiriti bhāvaḥ |
nanu prathamāntārthamukhyaviśeṣyakabodhe iṣṭāpattiḥ, yatastadaṅgīkāreṇaiva prathamāntārthoddeśyakabhāvanāvidheyānvayabodhasya sarvānubhavasiddhasya nirvāho bhavati, na kriyāviśeṣyakabodhasvīkāre 'daivadattaḥ pacatī'ti vākyajanyabodhadaśāyāṃ devadattaḥ pākānukūlakṛtimān na veti saṃśayānutpattinirvāhaśca kriyāprādhānyamate na syāt | samānaviśeṣyakaikakoṭiprakārakaniścayasyaiva tattadabhāvakoṭikasaṃśayanivartakatvā- dityāśaṅkāṃ parihartumāha tathā caiti | bhāṣyeti | 'kā tarhīyaṃ vācoyuktiḥ' pacati bhavati, pacasi bhavati, pakṣyati bhavati' iti-praśne-uttaraṃ 'pacādayaḥ kriyā bhavatikriyāyāḥ karvyo bhavanti' iti bhūvādisūtrasthabhāṣyetyarthaḥ | atra 'pakṣyati bhavatī'tyasya-bhaviṣyantī pākakriyā sūkṣmarūpeṇa bhavatītyarthaḥ | etena bhaviṣyatpāka- kriyākartṛkavartamānabhavanasya vādhāt tādṛśaprayogānirvāha iti dūṣaṇaṃ pratyuktam |
atra kriyayoḥ kriyākārakabhāvenānvayāṅgīkārāttulyanyāyena'paśya mṛga' ityatrāpi kriyayostathā bodhastadabhyupagata iti bhāvaḥ | evaṃ 'karmaṇā yamabhipraiti ' iti sūtre kriyāgrahaṇaṃ kartavyamiti vārttikakhaṇḍanārthe 'kriyāpi kṛtrimaṃ karma, kriyayā hi kriyepsitā bhavati, kayā kriyayā ? saṃdarśanādikriyayā' iti bhāṣye uktam | tadrītyā dhāvanakriyāyā darśanakriyāṃ pratyapi karmatvamabhimatameva | evaṃ ca 'mṛgakartṛkaṃ dhāvanakarmakaṃ darśana'mityarthe tadvākyaṃbhāṣyakṛtāmabimatamiti bhāvaḥ | hariṇāpyuktam 
'subantaṃ hi yathānekaṃ tiṅntasya viśeṣaṇam |
tathā tiṅntamapyāhustiṅantasya viśeṣaṇam ||' iti |
'purīmavaskanda lunīhi nandanam' iti māghapadye 'samuccaye'nyatarasyām' iti sūtre kaumudyudāhṛte spaṣṭa eva tiṅantārthakriyāṇāṃ viśeṣyaviśeṣaṇabhāvaḥ |
paramate ekavākyatānupapattau bījamāha prathamānteti | prathamāntamṛgapadopa- sthāpitasyetyarthaḥ | viśeṣyasyetyanena dhāvanaṃ prati viśeṣyatvāddarśane sākṣādviśe- ṣaṇatvādantaraṅgādapi pradhānakāryasya balavattāyā 'hetumiti ca' iti sūtrabhāṣyasaṃmatatvenāntaraṅgāmapi prathamāṃ bādhitvā dvitīyā syāt, mama tu nāyaṃ doṣaḥ, mṛgasya dhāvanaṃ prati viśeṣaṇatvenāntaraṅgājjātasya prathamārūpasaṃskārasyā- nivṛttiriti sūcitamiti vadanti | dvitīyāpatteriti | tathā ca dhāvanāśraya- mṛgakarmakadarśanāśrayastvamityarthe 'paśya mṛgaṃ dhāvatī'ti vākyasyaiva saṃbhavena tādṛśavākyasyaivāsiddhiriti bhāvaḥ | svamate tu na dvitīyāpattiḥ, dhāvanasya dhātvarthatayā karmabhūtatadvācakadhātoraprātipadikatvāt, na ca dvitīyāvirahe karmatva- bhānānāpattirvṛttyā padānupasthitatvāditi vācyaṃ, mīmāṃsakanaye'rthopasthiti- mātrasyaiva śābdabodhe hetutayā padajanyopasthiti vināpi karmatvaprakārakabodhasaṃbha- vāt, tādṛśopasthitihetutāvādimate tu tatsaṃsargakabodhasyaivābhyupagamāt | na ca tārkikamate'pi na dvitīyāpattiḥ, dhāvanakriyāviśiṣṭamṛgasyaiva karmatāsaṃbandhena dṛśikriyāyāmanvayo na tu kevalamṛgasya | viśiṣṭasya śuddhādanatireke'pi mṛgatvāvacchinnaviṣayatvāpekṣayā dhāvanaviśiṣṭamṛgatvāvacchinnaviṣayatāyā bhinnatvāt | nāmārthadhātvarthayoḥ sākṣād bhedhenānvaya iti vyutpattivirodho'pi na | tādṛśavyutpatteḥ kevalanāmārthadhātvarthānvayaviṣayatvāt | ataḥ 'śrutvā mamaitanmāhātmyaṃ tathā cotpattayaḥ śubhāḥ 'jānāmi sītā janakaprasūtā' ityādi saṃgacchate | bhavanmate
utpattipadādito dvitīyāpatterasaṃgatiḥ spaṣṭaiva | evaṃ ca prathamāntārthamukhya- viśeṣyakabodhāṅgīkāre na ko'pi doṣa iti vācyam | nīlādiviśiṣṭaghaṭādeḥ karmatvabodhe 'nīlaṃ ghaṭa'mityatra dvitīyānāpatteḥ, prathamāpatteśca | na ceṣṭāpattiḥ | anabhihitasūtrasthabhāṣyavirodhāt | tatra hi 'bhīṣmamudāraṃ kaṭaṃ ku'rvityatra viśi- ṣṭasya karmatvapakṣe kaṭapadādutpannayā dvitīyayā abhidhāne bhīṣmādipadāttadutpattirna syāditi tiṅādiparigaṇanamityuktam | na ca karmatvasya prātipadikārthaviśeṣyatayā vivakṣāyāmeva dvitīyā, na tu saṃsargatayā vivakṣāyāmapi, prakṛtabhāṣyagranthaśca karmatvasya viśeṣyatāpara iti na tadvirodha iti vācyam | bhāṣyakāramate sarvatra vibhattayarthasya saṃsargatayā bhānāt tathā vaktumaśakyatvāt | nīlādiviśiṣṭa- ghaṭādikriyāyāmāharaṇāderakāraṇatvādisaṃbanedhena bodhatātparthe nīlo ghaṭa āhara vidvān brāhmaṇo dehītyādiprayogāṇāmuktārthe kāvyapurāṇādāvanupalambhāt | nīlo ghaṭa ānaya śuklastaṇḍulaḥ pacatītyādiṣu 'tiṅasāmānādhikaraṇe prathamā' 'abhihite prathamā'ityanuśāsanāt prathamāyā apyanutpattyā tādṛśaprayogāsiddhiśca | evaṃ 'tathā cotpattayaḥ śubhāḥ' ityādiprayogā āṣatvādeva samādheyāḥ | anārṣestta- vasādhureva | dṛśadhātorutkaṭathāvanakartṛkamṛgakarmakadarśane, mṛgakartṛkotkaṭadhāvana- karmakadarśane vā lakṣaṇā, itarapadāni tātparyagrāhakāṇi, ato na vyutpattivi- rodhaleśo'pi | mṛgakartṛkotkaṭadhāvanakarmakadarśanasya pratipipādayiṣitasya bodha- nirvāho'pi bhavatīti tu na yuktam | uktarītyā lakṣaṇāṃ vinaiva nirvāhe tadāśrayaṇasyānucitatvāt | anupapattipratisaṃdhānaṃ vināpi bodhodayācca | nanu tathāpi kriyāmukhyaviśeṣyako bodho na yukto'pi tu prathamāntārthamukhyaviśeṣyakaeva yuktastathā hi 'anubhihite kartari tṛtīyā' ityasya samabhivyāhṛtatiṅādinā karturavācyatve tṛtīyoti nārthaḥ śābdikaiḥ svīkartu śakyastiṅsāmānyasyaiva kartṛniṣṭhaśaktinirūpakatvavācakatayā 'caitreṇa pacyate taṇḍula' ityatrāpi tṛtīyānāpatteḥ | kartari yako'sādhutvādeva caitraḥ pacyata ityādiprayogakāraṇasaṃbhave yagādyanu taṅantatvādergauraveṇa vācakatāvacchedakakoṭāvanupraveśābhāvāt | nāpyabodhye kartari tṛtīyetyarthaḥ | caitreṇa pacyata iti vākyasya kartṛviṣayakabodhajanakatve tadghaṭakasakalapadānāmapi tadvodhajanakatayā kartṛtiṅbodhyatvāttaddoṣatādavasthyāt | na ca tacchābdaupayikākāṅkṣāśūnyatvameva tadvodhakatvaṃ niveśyam' pacyata ityatra tadvodhaprayojakākāṅkṣāśūnyatvamakṣatameva | caitrādipadottaratṛtīyayaiva tadvodhasaṃbha-
vāttatra tādṛśākāṅkṣāyā akalpanaditi vācyam | caitreṇa pakṣyata ityadau tṛtīyānupapatteḥ | caitraḥ pakṣyata ityataścaitraprakārakakartṛviśeṣyakakriyāmukhyaviśeṣya- kasya, kevalāt pakṣyata ityaśca kartṛprakārakakriyāviśeṣyakasya bodhasyodayāttatrā- nupūrvorūpākāṅkṣāyāstacchābdaupayikatvāvaśyakatvāt | na ca samabhivyāhārarūpai- vākāṅkṣā vivaśraṇīyā, prakṛte tvānupūrvorūpaiva sā tathāvidheti noktadoṣa iti vācyam | tathā sati caitreṇa pacatītyādāvapi tṛtīyāpatteḥ | tādṛśasama- bhivyāhārasya tadvoghaupayikatvābhāvāt | tasmāt tattatprātipādikārthaviśeṣyatayā kartṛvivakṣāyāṃ tattatprātityatrāpi caitrapadāt tṛtīyāpattiścaitreṇa pacyata ityādā- viva kartṛścaitrapadārtha prati viśeṣyatvāviśeṣāt | tasmāt prathamāntārthamukhya- viśeṣyaka eva bodho yukto'ṅgīkartumiti cedatra vadanti, prātipadikārthaviśeṣya- katayetyādyuktarītyanusaraṇe'pi na caitraiṇa pacatīti prayogāpattiḥ | ekapadopāttatva- pratyāsattyā pūrvameva tiṅarthānvayabodhasya jātatvāt | tiṅādeḥ kartranabhidhāyakatvaṃ tattātparyasūnyatvaṃ, tadatātparye caitreṇa pacatītyatra tṛtīyāyāḥ sādhutve'pi bhāvakarmaṇorityātmanepadena bādhānna parasmaipadaṃ sādhviti na tādṛśaprayogāpattiḥ | caitreṇa pacatīti prayogastatra syādityapi na, kartṛtātparyābhāvena śapo'viṣayatayā yako durvāratvāt | tathā ca tādṛśakartrananvitatattatprātipadikārthaviśeṣyatvena kartṛvivakṣayā tṛtīyeti paryavasitārtha iti na ko'pi doṣaḥ | yaddhātvarthakartari tṛtīyādikaṃ vidhitsitaṃ samabhivyāhṛtatiḍādyupasthāpyataddhātvarthakartrananvitatva- mupādeyamato bhūñjanena caitreṇa pacyata ityatra śānajarthakartranvitatve'pi caitrasya na caitrapadāt tṛtīyānupapattiḥ | pacikriyākartrananvayāt | tattaddhātvarthakriyāvyaktī tatadvayaktitvenopādāya tathāvidhatattatkriyākartrādyananvitatvaṃ niveśyam | tena caitreṇa dṛśyamānaṃ maitra devadattaḥ paśyatītyādau na maitrādipadād dvitīyānupapattiḥ | vastutaḥ kartṛḥ samabhivyāhṛtatiṅādestātparyaśūnyatve kartṛparaprātipadikāt tṛtīyeti 'anabhihite kartari tṛtīyā' ityasyārthaḥ | caitreṇa pacyata ityatra tiṅpratyayasya kartratātparyakatvāccaitraḥ pacatītyatra tasya tattātparyakatvāttṛtīyātacchūnyaprayoga- nirvāhaḥ | evameva 'anabhihite karmaṇi dvitīyā' ityāderarthe'vaseyaḥ | nanu ekkāni bhuṅkte caitraḥ, pattavā bhujyata odana ityādau krameṇa kṛtā kartṛkarmānabhidhānāccaitrapadātṛtīyāyā odanapadād dvitīyāyā āpattiḥ | na ca samabhivyāhṛtatiṅagadyabhihitatvasāmānyābhāvasyaiva vivakṣaṇāt prathame kartuḥ kṛtāna-
bhidhāne'pi tiṅābhidhānād, dvitīye kṛtā karmaṇo'nabhidhāne'pi tiṅābhidhā- nānnoktadoṣa iti vācyam | caitreṇa pakkamodanaṃ maitro bhuḍkte, pācakena caitreṇa bhujyate odana ityādau odanacaitrapadābhyāṃ dvitīyātṛtīyayoranutpattyāpatteḥ, kṛtā karmaṇaḥ kartṛścābhidhānāditi cenna | pradhānakriyānirūpitaśakteranabhidhāne guṇa- kriyānirūpitaśakterabhidhāne'pi tattadvibhakteḥ sādhutvānaṅgīkārāt | taduktaṃ bhartṛharibhiḥ 
'pradhānetarayoryatra dravyasya kriyayoḥ pṛthak |
śaktirguṇāśrayā tatra pradhānamanurudhyate ||
pradhānaviṣayā śaktiḥ pratyayenābhidhīyate |
yadā guṇe tadā tadvadanuktāpi pratīyate ||' iti |
prathamakārikoktaṃ pradhānakriyānirūpitaśattayanurodhaṃ prapañcayati pradhānaviṣa- yeti | guṇe guṇanirūpitā anuktāpi tadvaduktavāt pratīyata iti yojanā | tulyanyāyād guṇanirūpitāyā uktatve'pi pradhānanirūpitaśakteranuktatve guṇanirūpitāpyanuktavāt prakāśata ityapi labhya iti bodhyam | ata eva 'prāsāde āste' ityatra sadinirūpitādhikasṇatvasyabhidhāne'pyāsinirūpitādhikaraṇatva- nimittikā saptamo sidhyati | anabhihita iti paryudāsāṅgikārāt | idaṃ ca 'svādumi ṇamul' ityatra bhāṣye spaṣṭam | tatra hi ā ca tumunaḥ samānādhi- karaṇe'nuprayogeṇa pratyayā vaktavyāḥ | anyathā 'svāduṃkāraṃ bhajyate yavāgurde- vadattene'tyatra ṇamul 'karttarikṛ'diti kartari syāt | tenābhidhānād devadattaśabdāt tṛtīyāna syādityādikamuktvā''vyayakṛto bhāva' ityāśrityokta- vacanaṃ khaṇiḍatam | tatra kaiyaṭena yadyevaṃ paktvaudanaṃ bhuṅkte devadattaḥ paktvaudana bhujyate devadattoneti katvāpratyayena kartṛkarmaṇoranabhidhānāt pākāpekṣayā tṛtīyā- dvitīye kasmānna bhavataḥ ityāśaṅkaya pradhānakriyānirūpitaśattayanupodhena pūrvavat samāhitam | evaṃ caitadbhāṣyāśayaprakāśike ime harikārike iti spaṣṭameva tadvidām | samānākārajñānabhedādviṣayatābhedemānābhāve'pi tattajjñānakriyā- nirūpitakarmatvaśattayorbhedāccaitreṇa dṛśyamānaṃ ghaṭaṃ maitraḥ paśyatītyatra tiṅprakṛti- bhūtadṛśyarthanirūpitānabhihitakarmatvaśaktiprayuktadvitīyāsiddhiḥ | viṣayatāyā dvitī- yādyarthatvaṃ tu nānuśāsanasiddham | āvaraṇabhaṅgānukūlavyāpārasya viṣayatāsaṃbandhā- vacchinnajñānānukūlātmamanoyogarūpavyāpārasya vā dṛśyādyajānātyarthatvena phalasya
kriyādibhedena bhinnatayā phalarūpatve'pi karmatvaśakterna kṣatirityalaṃ prasaṅgāgata- vicāraprapañcena |
'ānaya dhāvati mṛga ityekaṃ vākyaṃ na syāt | kriyāyāḥ prādhānyavādimate kriyāyā ānayanakarmatvāsaṃbhavāt | manmate tu dhāvanaviśiṣṭamṛgasya tatsaṃbhavā- nnānupapattirekavākyatāyā' iti tu na śaṅkyam | 'śikhīdhvasta' iti- vadupapatteḥ | evameva brāhmaṇānāṃ śataṃ bhojyatāmityādiprayogasaṃgatiḥ | tādṛśaikavākyatve mānā- bhāvācceti dik | etena 'bāvapradhānamākhyātam' iti niruktādyanurodhāt tiṅarthasya kriyāṃ prati viśeṣaṇatvopagame'pi 'pacati devadatta' ityatraikakartṛkapacikriyāśrayo devadatta iti prathamāntārthamukhyaviṣeṣyakabodhasaṃbhavāt sa evāṅgīkriyatāmiti pratyuktam | kuto vākyabheda ityata āha | adhyāhāre iti | ananvayāpatteśceti | co hetvarthaḥ | tathā ca parasparamananvayāpattayā bhāṣyakṛdabhimataikavākyatvahā- nirityarthaḥ | paramatadūṣaṇamupasaṃharati evaṃ ceti | evam | uktarītyā karturākhyātārthatve dhātvarthaprādhānye ca | bhāvanāprakāraketi | saṃkhyākālādyati- riktastiṅartho bhāvanā | ata eva ratho gacchati caitro jānātītyādijanyabodha- sāṃdhāraṇyam |
kāraṇamiti | viśeṣyatāsaṃbandhena bhāvanāprakārakāśābdabodhaṃ prati tattatprathamāntārthaniṣṭhaviśeṣyatāsaṃbandhena tattatprathamāntapadajanyopasthitiḥ kāraṇa- mityarthaḥ | tena samūhālambanopasthitiviṣayatāmādāya nātiprasaṅgaḥ | bhāvanātvā- vacchinneti | idaṃ dhātunā bhāvanātvaprakārakabodha ityabhyupetya | yadi tu viśeṣarūpāvacchinnaviṣayatayeti bodhyam | kāryatāvacchedakastu viśeṣyatāsaṃbandho bodhyaḥ | tiṅarthakarmaprakārakabodhe tattatphalaniṣṭhaviśeṣyatāsaṃbandhena dhātujanyo- pasthitiḥ kāraṇamityapi kāryakāraṇabhāvo draṣṭavyaḥ | nanu dhātvarthavyāpārasya kṛdarthaṃ pratyeva prakaritvaṃ sa tiṅādyarthaṃ pratīti niyamanirvāhāya bhāvanāprakārakaśābdabodhe kṛjjanyopasthitiḥ kāraṇamiti kāryakāraṇabhāva āvaśyakaḥ | tathā ca pacatyā- ditaḥ kriyāprakārakakriyāviśeṣyakabodho na saṃbhavati, taddhetubhūtakṛjjanyopasthitera- bhāvādityāśaṅkāṃ nirākartumāha bhāvaneti | kṛjjanyopasthitivaditi | yadyapi yatra kṛjjanyopasthitau śabdāvijñānāt tadartho'pi viṣayastādṛśasamūhālamba- nopasthitiviṣayatā ghaṭādyarthe'pyastītyatiprasāṅgastadavasthastathāpi svajanaka- jñānīyakṛdavṛttiprakāratānirūpitaviśeṣyatāsaṃbandhena kṛjjanyopasthiterhetutvānnāti-
prasaṅgaḥ | svamupasthitiḥ samuhālambanopasthitijanakaṃ śaktisaṃbandhena yatkṛtpra- kārakaṃ tadarthaviśeṣyakaṃ jñānaṃ tādṛśajñānīyakṛnniṣṭhaprakāratānirūpitaviśeṣya- tāyāstadarthakartrādāveva sattavāt | dhātvarthabhāvaneti | dhātujanyabhāvanā- niṣṭhaviśeṣyatāsaṃṣandhenopasthitirityarthaḥ | anyathā sattādipadopasthitasyāpi sattāderdhātvarthānapāyādatiprasaṅgāpatteḥ | bhāvanāpadaṃ ca tanniṣṭhaviśeṣyatālā- bhārthameva | digiti | tadarthastu pūrve vistareṇa nirūpitaḥ | yadvā nanu tiṅanta- samudāyasyāpyekavākyatve 'tiḍṅatiṅaḥ' iti sūtre 'atiṅaḥ' iti vyartha tiṅantātparaṃ tiṅantasya samānavākyasthatvavirahānnidhātāprāpteriti bhāṣyaviredha iti bhāva ityanyatra vistaraḥ | itthaṃ ceti | pratyayārthasya kartrāderdhātvarthaviśeṣaṇatve tadarthasaṃkhyāyāstadarthakartrādiviśeṣaṇatve ca | ekāśrayiketi | vartamānakālikī- tyapi bodhyam | navyamate tvekakartṛketi bodhākāro draṣṭavyaḥ | pākānukūleti | viklittyanukūletyarthaḥ | ekāśrayikā yā viklittistadanukūlā bhāvaneti | 'bhāva- pradhānamākhyātam' iti niruktabhāṣyānurodhāt karmapratyayāntajavodhe'pi bhāvanāyāḥ prādhānyamiti bhāvaḥ | vastutastu karmakṛdante phalasya vyāpāraṃ prati prādhānyaṃ dṛṣṭa- miti karmatiṅante'pi tasyaiva mukhyaviśeṣyatvaṃ yuktam | ata eva pratyayārthe sākṣāt prakṛtyarthaviśeṣyānvayitvasya sarvatra kṛptasya na tyāgaḥ | ata eva 'iṣyate putraḥ' 'iṣṭaḥ putraḥ' ityarthe na putrīyatīti prayogo bhinnārthatvādapi tu putramicchatītyarthe eva | kyajantāt karmaṇi pratyayastu durlabho'karmakatvādityuktaṃ 'supa ātmanaḥ ityatra bhāṣye | anyathā ubhayatrāpīcchānukūlavyāpārasyātmamanaḥsaṃyogādyā- tmakasya prādhānyāviśeṣāt tadasaṃgatiḥ | manmate tvekatra vyāpāre viśeṣyo'paratra phalamiti bhinnārthatvaṃ spaṣṭameva | tathā cīktaṃ kaiyaṭena 'yadā kriyāphalasya (karmaṇaḥ) prādhānyaṃ tadā 'iṣṭaḥ putra' 'iṣyate putra' iti vākyameva, na tu kyajantaṃ; tasyākarmakatvāt, karmaṇi pratyayānutpādāt' iti pare prāhuḥ |
yadyapi karmapratyaye bhāvanāyāḥ prādhānye'pi tadarthasaṃkhyāyāḥ karmaṇi viśeṣaṇatvaṃ kartṛ pratyaye kartari, tāvatā bhinnārthatvopapattirāśrayānvayamādāyāpi tadupapattistathāpi bhinnamukhyaviśeṣyakabodhajanakatvarūpaṃ bhinnārthatvaṃ vivakṣitaṃ tada- saṃgatirdṛḍhaiva | yatkicidaśe vibhinnaprakārakabodhajanakatvameva bhinnārthakatvaṃ vivakṣitamiti cedicchāpadanirdiṣṭapratyayārthāśe vṛttivākyayobhinnārthatvavirahe pratyayā- vāraṇena tasyākicitkaratvāditi bhāvaḥ | nanu sarmadhātūnāṃ vyāpārārthakatvaṃ na
