saṃbhavati naśyatītyādau tadananubhavādata āha naśyatītyatreti | pratiyogitva- viśiṣṭeti | anyapratiyogikanāśasāmagrīsamavadhāne'pi ghaṭo naśyatītyaprayogāt pratiyogitvaviśiṣṭeti svapratiyogitvaviśiṣṭārthakam | ata eva - naśadhātostā- dṛśasāmagrayarthakatvādeva | iti prayoga iti | tadarthakatvābhāve naśyati naḍkṣyatīti prayogīpapattāvapi nāśasyātītatvavirahānnaṣṭa iti prayogo na syāt | cira- vinaṣṭe'pi naśyatītyeva prayogaḥ syāt | sāmagryarthakatve tu sāmagryāmeva pratyayārthakālānvayānnoktāpattyanupapattī iti bhāvaḥ | idaṃ ca dūṣaṇaṃ vartamānardhvasa- pratiyogitvarūpamatītatvaṃ pratyayārtha iti mate | vartamānadhvaṃsapratiyogikāla- vṛttitvasya tadarthatve tu noktaṃ dūṣaṇamiti bodhyam | antata āśrayataiveti | vastuta ātmamanaḥsaṃyogo'pi jñānānukūlaḥ saṃbhavatīti bhāvaḥ | atra phalatāvacchedakaḥ saṃbandho viṣayatā tena ghaṭādeḥ karmatvamiti bodhyam | jñānameva phalaṃ, tadeva tadanukūlo vyāpāraḥ, phalatāvacchedakaḥ saṃbandho viṣayatā, vyāpāratāvacchedakastu samavāya iti matāntaraṃ bodhyam | ūhyamiti | icchatītyādāvūhyā- mityarthaḥ || 2 ||
ubhayabodhāpattiḥ - kartṛkarmobhayabodhāpattiḥ | ubhayatrāpi śakteḥ sattvā- dityarthaḥ | nanu yatra tātparyagrahastasyaiva bodho na tvaparasya, ekadobhayabodhe tātparya- grahasattve'pyekena pratyayenaikadobhayabodhasyāvyutpannatvānnaikadobhayabodhāpattirityato dūṣaṇāntaramāha kartṛmātrabodhavaditi | bodhāpattiriti | haryādipadāt kadā- cidindramātrasya kadācidviṣṇavādimātrasya bodhotpādavaditi bhāvaḥ | tātparyagrāhaka- prakāraṇādeḥ samānatvāditi bhāvaḥ | tātparyagrāhakamiti | anāditā- tparyagrāhakamityarthaḥ | tathā ca pacyata ityatra kasyācit kartṛbodhe tātparyasattve- 'pyanāditātparyābhāvānna kartṛ bodhe iti bhāvaḥ |
kārikāyāṃ tatreti tayorityarthakam | tathā ca tayoḥ phalavyāpārayormadhye ityarthaḥ | nanvevaṃ taṅādayaḥ karmadyotakā ityādivyavahāraḥ kathamupapādanīya ityata āha phalānvayetyādinā samudāyārya ityantena | śaktyudbodhakatvarūpadyota- katvāsaṃbhavādāha tātparyaṃ grāhayantīti | tatpratīticchāṃ grāhayanti | taṅ yagādisamabhivyāhṛto bodhyaḥ, tena dhatte ityādau nātiprasaṅgaḥ || 3 ||
evam - taṅātīnāṃ phale āśrayānvaye tātparyagrāhakatve | nanu 'bhāvakarmaṇoḥ' ityādisūtravihitataṅādīnāṃ kathaṃ kartari sādhutvamityāśaṅkyāha 'karmavat karmaṇā' ityādinā sādhanāt ityantena | mūle-utsargo'yamiti | ayaṃ - taṅādiḥ, karmadyotaka ityādiḥ, pūrvokto'rthaḥ | utsargaḥ - prāyikaḥ, na sārvatrika ityarthaḥ | kuta ityāśaṅkāyāmāha karmeti | viparyayāt - vaiparītyāt | yathāgamaṃ - yathāśāstram, yathābodhaṃ vā | tacca vyākhyāne spaṣṭam | 'pacyate odanaḥ svayameve'tyādau vyāpāre evāśrayānvaye iti spaṣṭayituṃ śābdabodhaṃ varṇayati atra hīti |
ekanāradeti | eko nāradastadviṣayakaṃ jñānamityarthaḥ | atra nipātenetiśabdena karmaṇo'bhidhānānna nāradaśabdottaraṃ dvitīyeti bhāvaḥ | navyamate tu nārada ityākārakaṃ yajjñānaṃ tadanukūla ityādiśābdabodho'vagantavyaḥ, taiḥ kkacinnipatonābhidhānamiti nānumanyate, tiṅātiharigaṇanasyaiva bhāṣye uktatvāt | 'sakarmakathātvi'ti samabhivyāhṛtāntopādānaṃ taṇḍulaḥ pacyate svayamevetyādo karmavadityanena 'bhāvakarmaṇo'riti śāstramatidiśyate iti pakṣe'tiprasaṅgavā- raṇāya | 'abodhiśrīkṛṣṇa' ityādāvatiprasaṅgavāraṇāya bhāvasādhāraṇeti | yagādīti | ādinā ciṇvadiṭ | tattadvidhāyakaśāstrajñānaśūnyasya tu prakaraṇādikameva tātparyagrāhakam | evaṃ liṅādāvapi | anyathā 'pece' ityatra kartṛkarmaṇorubhayorapi samānatvāt tātparyagrāhakadurbhikṣameva syāt || 4 ||
bhāvanāyā avācyatvamiti | tasyā dhātuvācyatve prādhānyānupapattyā tada- vācyatvamityarthaḥ | vyāpārākhyabhāvanāyā avācyatvamevetyartha iti vā | vācyatāṃ dhvanayitumiti | tathā ca yatnabhinno vyāpāro na dhātuvācyo nāpi tiṅvācyo'pi tu phalamātraṃ dhātvarthaḥ, kṛtiḥ pratyayārtha iti prācīnanaiyāyikādimataṃ na samyagiti sūcitam | kasyacit kaścicchabdaḥ prasiddha ityanekaparyāyopādānam | athavā cetanācetanasādhāraṇaphūtkārayatnādīnāṃ dhātuvācyatvakriyātvayorbodhanārtha tat | ata eva - tena tena rūpeṇa vyāpārasya vācyatvādeva | sarvasiddha iti | sarvānubhavasiddha ityarthaḥ | sarvānubhavasiddhasyāpalāpo'nucita iti bhāvaḥ | evaṃ 'devadattaḥ pacatī'ti
vākyajanyabodhadaśāyāṃ devadattaḥ phūtkārādimānna veti saṃśayo'pyata eva netyapi bodhyam | evam - uktaprakāreṇa phūtkārādivyāpārāṇāṃ vācyatve | gauravā- pattyeti | phūtkāratvādīnāmupādhirūpatvāt |
tadavacchedakamiti | pravṛttitvarūpakṛtitvamiṣṭasādhanatājñānakāryatāvaccheda- katayā siddho jātiviśeṣo'tastadevākhyātaśakyatāvacchedakaṃ lāghavāditi bhāvaḥ | tathā ca phalaṃ dhātvartho yatna ākhyātārtha iti prācīnanaiyāyikamataṃ paryavasannam | yatnatvaṃ dhātuśakyatāvacchadakamiti tu nārthaḥ | tathā sati pratyayasyā- śrayārthakatāpatteḥ | tacca na naiyāyikābhimatam | ata eva kālasaṃkhyayoreva pratyayavācyatetyapi na, naiyāyikānabhimatatvāt | dhātuvācyayatnasya prathamāntarthe- 'nvayānāpatteśca, nāmārthadhātvartheti vyutpattivirodhāt | 'laḥ karmaṇi' iti sūtra- virodhācca | nanu 'ratho gacchatī'tyādau yatnasya bādhād vyāpārādipratītiḥ sarvānubhavasiddhā kathaṃ nirvāhyetyata āha ratho gacchatīti | 'jānāti' ityasya 'devadatta' ityādiḥ śeṣo bodhyaḥ | ādipadāt icchati-naśyatītyādiparigrahaḥ | vyāpāratvādityādināśrayatvatvapratiyogitvatvaparigrahaḥ | ghaṭo naśyatītyato nāśā- nukūlavyāpārasya nāśāśrayatvasya ca pratiyogini bādhāt pratiyogitvameva latra bhāsata iti bodhyam | evaṃ 'ratho gacchatī'tyatrāpi vyāpārabhānamiti na sādhu, gamanānukūlanodanādimatyakatra sthite'pi caitrādau caitro gacchatīti prasaṅgāt | guruṇi saṃbhavāditi | śaktilakṣaṇayorubhayorapi padatadarthasaṃbandhatvāviśeṣād gurudharmāvacchinne lakṣaṇāyā iva śakterapi saṃbhavādityarthaḥ | vaiṣamye bījābhāvā- diti | nanu kāraṇatāvacchedakatvapratiyogitāvacchadakatvavacchakyatāvacchedakatvaṃ svarūpaṃsaṃbandhaviśeṣarūpaṃ saṃbhavati lavudharme guruṇi na kavpayet | ata eva laghudharmavat kāraṇatāvacchedakatā pratiyogitāvacchedakatā ca kalpyate | ata eva prameyadhūme prameyatvaviśiṣṭābhāvapratiyogitāvacchedakatvaṃ nāstīti ppatīti pratītiḥ siddhā dhūmatve nāstīti tārkikapravādaḥ saṃgacchate | ataḥ saṃbhavatītyasya saṃbhavatta- davacchedakatāke ityarthaḥ | tathā ca saṃbavattadavacchedakatākadharmāpekṣayā yo guruḥ sa tadanavacchedatāka iti vyāptiḥ paryavasitā | saṃbhavattadavacchedakatākatvaṃ ca tadavacchedakatvabādhakaśūnyatvaṃ, bādhakaṃ cāvacchedyapratiyogitvādyasamanaiyatyam | tathā ca tatsamanaiyatyameva saṃbhavattadavacchedakatātvam | samanaiyatyaghaṭakadaladvayaniveśād ghaṭapaṭānyatarābhāvamahānasīyavahrayabhāvapratiyogitāvacchedakatvaṃ dhaṭatvavahnitvādya- pekṣayā gurorapi tādṛśānyataratvamahānasīyavahnitvādenirvahati | pratiyogitāyāḥ samanaiyatyaṃ ca tadanyūnavṛttitve sati tannirūpakābhāvavirodhitāvacchedakatvam | ato guṇakarmānyatvādiviśiṣṭasattodyabhāvapratiyogitāvacchedakatvasya na sattātva-
samaniyatatādṛśaviśiṣṭasattātve'nupapattiḥ, śakyatāvacchedakatvameva svarūpasaṃbandha- viśeṣo na lakṣyatāvacchedakatvamiti cenna | svarūpasaṃbandhasya tattatsvarūparūpatve gurudharmasvarūpāṇāmapi sattvāt tadvaramavacchedakatvasvīkāra eva | bhāvakalpanāyāṃ lāghavāt | anyatra tkṛptābhāvasya saṃbandhamātra kalpyate dhāghavāditi cedanyatra kalpyamānāvacchedakatvasya saṃbandhakalpanaiva yuktā | gurudharme anyūnā- natiriktavṛttitvarūpāvacchedakatvasyānapāyācceti bṛhadbhūṇādito'vagantavyam | yatnārthakakarotineti | yatnajanyatvapratisaṃdhānadaśāyāṃ paṭāṅkurayoḥ kṛtākṛtavyava- hārāttasya yatnārthakatvamāvaśyakamiti bhāvaḥ | ratho gamanamiti | rathapadānuktau gamanānukūlakṛteścaitrādisamavetāyāḥ sattvānna tādṛśaprayogānupapattirato 'ratha' iti | 'aṅkuraḥ kṛta' ityetāvanmātrasyeśvarakṛtijanyatvamādāyopapatterāha bījādineti | vivaraṇāditi | idamupalakṣaṇaṃ, 'ki karotī'ti yatne praśre pṛṣṭe pacatītyuttarasya yatnārthakatvaṃ vinānupapatteścetyapi draṣṭavyam | yatnārthakatāyā asiddheriti | na ca ratho gamanaṃ karotītyādau karotervyāpārasāmānye lakṣaṇeti vācyaṃ, mukhyārthatve saṃbhavati lakṣaṇākalpanasyā- nyāyyatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
nanu vyāpāraśaktatve yatnajanyatvāpratisaṃdhānadaśāyāṃ 'paṭaḥ kṛtaḥ' 'aṅkuraḥ kṛta' iti vyavahārānupapattiḥ, vyāpārajanyatvapratisaṃdhānāviśeṣāt, īśvarīyakṛti- janyatve'pi caitrādikṛtijanyatvābhāvapratisaṃdhānadaśāyāṃ tādṛśavyavahārasyeṣṭatvāt, taduktamācāryaiḥ 
'kṛtākṛtavibhāgena kartṛrūpavyavasthayā |
yatna eva kṛtiḥ pūrvā parasmin saiva bhāvanā ||' iti |
tatra - kṛño (kṛtau-yatne ?) parasmin mate - mīmāsakamate ityarthaḥ | nirūḍhalakṣamā eva kartṛpadasthakṛño'pīti cellādhavāt kṛtitvāvacchinne eva śaktirastu, vyāpāre eva lakṣaṇāstu, laghudharmāvacchinne eva śaktemīmāṃsakādimata- siddhatvāt, lakṣaṇayā bodhāt tatpadajanyabodhaviṣayatvaprakārakabhāvavadicchāyāḥ sanmātraviṣayiṇyāstatrāvaśyakatve'pi laghudharmāvacchinne eva saṃbandhatvamaṅgīkriyate, na gurudharmāvacchinne, anubhavabalena gauravajñānasya pratibandhakatvakalpanāt, śakyasaṃbandharūpalakṣamāyāstu tatrāpi saṃbandhatvaṃ tkṛptaṃ, na kalpyamiti vaiṣamyamata eva na lakṣaṇoccheda iti sakalatāntrikasiddhabhityata āha ki ca bhāvanāyā iti | avācyatve - vyāpārarūpabhāvanāyā dhātostiṅaścāvācyatve | dvitīyā
syāditi | tasyā avācyatve svajanakavyāpāravyadhikaraṇadhātvarthaphalāśrayatvaṃ karmatvaṃ vaktavyaṃ, dhātvarthaphalaśrayatvameva vā, evaṃ ca 'ghaṭaṃ bhāvayatī'tyatreva 'ghaṭo bhavatī'tyatrāpi dhaṭapadād dvitīyāpattiḥ, | ubhayatrāpi ghaṭasya dhātbarthotpatti- rūpaphalāśrayatvāviśeṣādityarthaḥ | karmasaṃjñāyā bādhāditi | ghaṭasya phalāśrayatve- 'pi dvitīyāprayojakakarmasaṃjñāyā virahānna dvitīyāpattirityarthaḥ | anugateti | cetanācetanasādhāraṇetyarthaḥ | ghaṭasyākartṛtvamupapādayati-kṛtyāśrayatvasyeti | taddhātvarthaphalānukūlakṛtyāśrayatvasya | kāraketi | prakṛtadhātvarthakāraka- cakretyarthaḥ | abhāvāt - acetanatvāt, kārakacakraprayoktṛtvābhāva iti bhāvaḥ | tathā ca kṛtyādimata eva kartṛsaṃjñāvidhānāt kṛtyādyabhāve kartṛsaṃjñāprāptyabhāvena karmasaṃjñāyā anapavādād dvitīyāpattirdurvāreti bhāvaḥ | nanu dhātvarthānukūlavyāpā- rāśrayatvaṃ svatantrapadena vivakṣaṇīyaṃ, tacca ghaṭe'bādhitamevetyata āha dhātvartheti | kārakamātreti | kārakamātre prakṛtadhātvarthānukūlavyāpāravattvāt sarvakārakeṣvati- prasaṅgaḥ | nanu kārakacakraprayoktṛtvaṃ kartṛtvaṃ, tacca vaivakṣikamato nātiprasaṅgaḥ, ata eva 'sthālī pacatī'tyapi bhavati | spaṣṭaṃ cedaṃ 'kārake' iti sūtrabhāṣye ityata āha tathā ceti | bhūprabhṛtāvatipraśaṅgavāraṇāya-svārthaphalavyadhikaraṇeti | bhūprabhṛterapi pacyādidhātvarthaphalavyādhikaraṇavyāpāravācitvādatiprasaṅgatādavasthyam, ataḥ svārthatvaṃ phale niveśitam | phalavyadhikaraṇatvaṃ ca phalatāvacchedakasaṃbhandhena yatkicittatphalādhikaraṇabhinnavṛttitvaṃ, nāto gamyādarasaṃgrahaḥ | phalatāvacchedaka- saṃbandhaniveśād bhūprabhṛtyarthaphalasya kālikādisaṃbandhena yadadhikaraṇaṃ kālādi, tadbhedasya svajanakavyāpārādhikaraṇe sattve'pi nātiprasaṅgaḥ | caitragrāmobhayaniṣṭha- saṃyogavyaktayadhikaraṇatvāvacchinnapratiyogitākabhadasya caitre'sattvādgamyāderasaṃgraha- stadavastha iti 'yatkicit' tatphalavatyādhikaraṇe niveśitam | yadvā phalādhi- karaṇayatkicidavṛttitvaṃ phalavyadhikaraṇatvaṃ, vinigamanāvihahādāsvārthavyāpāreti | vyāpāravyādhikaraṇatvaṃ - vyāpārānadhikaraṇavṛttitvam | saṃbandhaniveśanaṃ pūrvavadvo- dhyam | athavā svānadhikaraṇavṛttitattatphalakatvaṃ-phalavyadhikaraṇatvaṃ, svaśabdārtho vyāpāraḥ, svādhikaraṇatvaṃ vyāpāratāvacchadakasambandhena bodhyaṃ, nāto grāmasyāpi kālikasaṃbandhena caitravṛttivyāpāravattve'pi kṣatiḥ | tadvṛttitvaṃ ca phalatāvacchedaka- saṃbandhena, tena nākarmake'tiprasaṅgaḥ | vācyatvam - dhātuvācyatvam | anyatamatvamiti | sakarmakatvenābhimatā yāvanto dhātavastadvṛttyanyata- matvamityarthaḥ,
tacca tattaddhātvānupūrvyavacchinnapratiyogitākabhedasamudāyavadbhinnatvām | ekasyai- veti | ekānupūrvīkasyaivetyarthaḥ | tena 'bhāraṃ vahati' 'nadī vahatī'ti vākyadvayaghaṭakavahadhātuvyaktayoruccāraṇabhadenārthabhedena parasparabhedāvaśyakatvenaika- syaivetyasaṃgatamiti nirastam | yadvārthabhedādisattve'pi śabdābhedamate idamiti bodhyam | evaṃ ca sakarmakatvenābhimatadhātūnāṃ tattadvayaktitvāvacchinnaprati- yogitākabhedānāmanyatamatvarūpabhedapratiyogitāvacchedakakoṭau praveśe'pi nistāra iti bodhyam |
nanu sakarmatvaṃ svajanakavyāpāravyadhikaraṇaphalavācakatvaṃ, tañca karoterakṣatam, yatnasya phalatāvacchedakaviṣayatāsaṃbandhena svajanakātmamanoyogādirūpavyāpārā- nadhikaraṇavṛttitvāt, tadbhinnatvamakarmakatvaṃ, tacca karotau na saṃbhavatīti 'kṛño'- karmakatāpatte'rityasaṃgatamityāśaṅkayāha ayaṃ bhāva iti | artha iti | asya 'dhāto'rityādiḥ | yatī prayatne itivaditi | tathā ca noktarūpaṃ sakarmakatvaṃ vaktumucitaṃ, yatadhātāvatiprasaṅgāt, api tu 'svārthaphale'tyādhuktamevāsmadrītyā, pararītyā phalaviśiṣṭavyāpāravācakatvameva, tacca karoteryatnamātravācakatve na saṃbhavatītyakarmakatāpattirdurvāraiti bhāvaḥ |
nanu dhātumātrasya vyāpārāvācakatve dhātumātrasyākarmakatāpattisaṃbhavāt 'kṛña' iti viśeṣoktiranucitetyāśaṅkyāha kṛña itīti | phalavācakatvaṃ sakarmakatva- mityabhipretyāha sakarmakateti | tathā ca sakarmakākarmakavibhāgoccheda iti tātparyam | tat - sakaladhātuvācyatvam | etena vyāpāro bhāvaneti kārikayā vyāpārasya dhātuvācyatvasādhane'pi phalāṃśasya tadasādhanena nyūnateti dūṣaṇaṃ pratyuktam | kṛña itīti | 'yatī prayatna' itivad yatnārthakatāyāma- karmakatāpattirityuktam, anyathā 'vāyurvikurute'saindhavā vikurvate' ityādiprayogadarśanādyathāśrute'saṃgatyāpattīḥ | nanu phalāvācakatve'pi phalavyadhi- karaṇavyāpāravācakatvaṃ sakarmakatvaṃ kṛñāderapyakṣatameva, utpattyādirūpa- phalavyadhikaraṇayatnādyātmakavyāpāravācakatvadityāśaṅkyāha ayaṃ bhāva iti | dhātvartha iti | sakaladhātvartha ityarthaḥ | sakarmakatvocchedāpattiriti | samanantaroktaṃ na sakarmakatvamāśrayitumarha, yatisyandyativyāpakatvāditi bhāvaḥ | nanu sakarmakatvavyavahārānurodhādastu pacyādeḥ phalāṃśo'pi vācyaḥ, ata eva dhātorvyāpāramātravācitve phalaṃ dvitīyādyartho vācyaḥ, tathā ca prayāgāt kāśīṃ
dacchati caitre 'prayāgaṃ gacchati caitraḥ' 'kāśīṃ cyajatī'tyapi prayogaḥ syāt, evaṃ 'grāmaṃ syandate iti prayogāpattiśca, ekasyaiva vyāpārasya gamyādivācyasya pūrvāparadeśaniṣṭhavibhāgasaṃyogajanakatvāt-tattatphalaviśeṣānvayabodhe taddhātujanyo- pasthiterhetutvakalpanuyoktaprayogāpattorvāraṇe tu gauravam | tyajigamyoḥ paryāyatā- bhramadaśāyāṃ 'gacchati grāmaṃ'tyajati grāma'miti vākyadvayādapyavilakṣaṇabodhotpattyā tathāvidhahetuhetumadbhāvakalpanamaśakyaṃ ca, vyabhicārāt, tatpuruṣīyatathāvidhabhrama- śūnyakālikatatpuruṣīyatattatphalānvayabuddhitvasya kāryatāvacchedakatvaṃ svīkṛtya vyabhicāravāraṇe tvatigauravam | tathāvidhabhramadaśāyāmapi tādṛganvayabodho nābhyupeta iti tu na yuktamanubhavasiddhasyāpalāpālārhatvāt | evaṃ tyajati gacchati tyāgo gamanamityato'pyavikṣaṇabodhāpattiḥ | na ca tatra phalaviśeṣāvacchinnavyāpārelakṣaṇā- svīkārānnoktāpattiriti vācyam | anekakarmapratyayānāṃ tattatphalaviśeṣe śakti- kalpanāṃ, karmapratyayāsamabhivyāhṛtagamyādestattatphalāvacchinne lakṣaṇākalpanāṃ, cāpekṣya gamyādereva viśiṣṭavācakatvasyocitatvāt | evaṃ 'pacyatetaṇḍulaḥ svaya- meve'tyādiprayogānupapattiśca, phalasya dhātvavācyatve karmasthakriyakatvābhāvena karmavādbhāvāprāpterityādidoṣāṇāmanavatāra eva, tathā pacyāderuktarītyā phalā- vacchinnavyāpārārthakatve'pi kṛñāderyatnādirūpavyāpāramātrārthakatve bādhakābhāvaḥ, tatra sakarmakatvavyavahāraḥ karmānvitārthakatvanimittako gauṇaḥ | evaṃ tatra dhātvarthatā- vacchedakaphalaśālitvarūpamukhyakarmatvavirahe'pi viṣayatvarūpaṃ karmatvaṃ saṃbhavatītyāśaṅkyāha na ca kṛñādāviti | lakārāsaṃbhavāditi | gauṇamukhyo- riti nyāyāditi bhāvaḥ | tadeva dṛṣṭāntena darśayati-
na hīti | utpattyanukūlayatnārthakatve kṛño noktadoṣasaṃbhava ityata āha-evaṃ ceti | yatnamātramitīti | mātrapadenotpattirūpaphalavyavacchedaḥ, tathā ca parābhimatayatnamātrārthakatvaṃ tu svābhimatameveti bhāvaḥ || 5 ||
ata eva - yatnapadasya tanmātraparatvādeva | anyathotpādanapadasyotpattya- nukūlayatnādyarthakatayā'saṃgatiḥ spaṣṭaiveti bhavatyupaṣṭambhakatā | yatnādītyādi- padenānyavyāpārasyāpi parigrahaḥ, 'bījādināṅkuraḥ kṛta' ityādyanurodhāt, tatra yatnabādhāt tattyage'pi śaktyaivetaravyāpārabhānamiti bhāvaḥ | nanu
pratyayārthaphalavyadhikaraṇavyāpāravācitvaṃ sakarmakatvamastu, pratyayārthaphalāśrayatavameva karmatvamato na kaścid doṣa iti cenna, 'ghaṭaṃ bhāvayatī'tyādau phalasyātvayatirekābhyāṃ prakṛtibhūtadhātuvācyatve sati gauravānmānābhāvācca tasya pratyayārthatvābhāvena tatra lakṣaṇadvayasyāpyavyāptyāpatteḥ | kiṃva phalasya pratyayārthatve 'pacatī'tyādau phalaviṣayakabodhānāpattiriti dik | yuktyantaramāheti | mukhyabhāktasādhāraṇakarmatvanvitasvārthabodhakatvameva sakarmakatvam, karmatvānāmananu- game'pi karmatvapadavattvena taiṣāmanugamaḥ | gauṇe'pi karmatve ghaṭaṃ jānāti karotīyādiprāmāṇikaprayogānurodhād dvitīyādayaḥ, yathā śābdikamate āropitakartṛtvādikamādāya tṛtīyādayaḥ, ata eva 'śṛṇota grāvāṇa' ityādi saṃgacchate | ata eva ca kārakacakraprayoktṛvaṃ kartṛtvamiti bhāṣyamate ghaṭo bhavatītyādiprayoganirvāhaḥ, na ca kārakādhikāre gauṇamukhyanyāyāpravṛtteḥ siddhānta- siddhatayā dvitīyādisiddhāvapi tatra lakārādyasiddhau 'jñāyate kriyate ghaṭa' ityādi- prayogāsiddhireveti vācyam | svaritatvapratijñayā adhikaḥ kāro'dhikāro'dhikaṃ kāryamityarthaka 'svaritena ' iti sūtreṇa teṣāmapi siddheḥ | dhātuviśeṣajanya- yatnādyupasthiterdvitīyādyarthaviṣayatvānvayabodhe niyāmakatvānna ghaṭāya yatata iti vāktavye 'ghaṭaṃ yatata' ityādayaḥ prayogāḥ | astu vā tatra viṣayataiva phalaṃ, tasyā api jñānādiprayojyatvarūpaphalatvasattvāt | evaṃ ca sukhyameva karmatvamiti noktanyāvyarthakatvameva sakarmakatvam, etadavaitacchāstrīyakarmasaṃjñākārthānvayyarthakatva- mucyate, etacca bhavaco'pyāvaśyakamata karmaṇi pratyayānupapatterityato yuktyantaramā- hetyarthaḥ | nanu vacanābhāvāt karmakartari karmavat syādyagādyapīti mūlamanupa- pannabhityata āha karmavat syāditi padeneti | sūtraṃ lakṣyata iti | sūtravihita- mityarthaḥ | anyathā yagādītyanenānvayāpatteḥ | kāryātideśapakṣe sākṣādeva tatsūtra- vihitatvaṃ yagādeḥ, śāstrātideśapakṣe tu paramparayeti bodhyam | tanna syāditi | karmasthakriyayā tulyā kriyā phalarūpā, phalasamānādhikaraṇavyāpārarūpā vā yatra tādṛśakartā karmavadityarthakasya 'karmavat karmaṇā 'iti sūtrasyāprāpteḥ, kṛñaḥ phalavācakatvābhāvāt, tadarthayatnasya tu ghaṭādisamavetatvābhāvāt | yatra karma- vadbhāvābhāve iṣṭāpattistu na, prakṛtasūtrabhāṣyavirodhāt | nanu karmaṇi samavāyena yatnasyāsattve'pi viṣayatayā tanniṣṭhatvamakṣatameva, ata eva ghaṭaṃ jānātītyādivad ghaṭaṃ karotīti prayoga upapadyate, phalatāvacchedakaviṣayatāsaṃbandhena jñānādivad
yatnasya ghaṭaniṣṭhatayā tasya karmatvāt | na caivaṃ ghaṭaṃ yatata ityapi syāditi vācyam | tadupasthitayatnasya viṣayatayā phalatvavyavahārābhāvena viṣayatāyāḥ phalatāvacchedakatvānaṅgīkārāt | evaṃ ca 'kriyate ghaṭaḥ svayameve'tyupapannamevetyā- śaṅkya naivaṃ vaktuṃ śakyaṃ 'dṛśyate ghaṭaḥ svayameve'tyādiprayāgāpatterityāśayena mūloktaṃ dṛṭhṭāntamupapādayati yathā dṛśyate ghaṭa iti || 6 ||
evam - phalavyadhikaraṇetyādereva sakarmakatvarūpatve | viṣayāvacchinneti | avacchedakatāsaṃbandhena viṣayaniṣṭha āvaraṇabhaṅga ityarthaḥ | bhaṅgadītyādipadena viṣayatāparigrahaḥ | kiṃ na syāditi | tathā ca phalavyadhikaraṇetyādirūpaṃ sakarma- katvamapi svīkārayogyaṃ na bhavatīti tātparyam | kiṃ ca 'gamyate grāmaḥ svaya- meve'tyādiprayogāpattirūpo'pi doṣa ityāha evaṃ grāma iti | nirvartyādisvarūpajñānārthamāha īpsitamiti | tena tadbhinnakarmaṇa ādhikye'pina kṣatīḥ |
kriyākṛtaviśeṣeti | kriyākṛtāviśeṣeṇa tadvaiśiṣṭyenānupalabhyamāna ityarthaḥ | yadvānupalabhyamānaḥ kriyākṛtaviśeṣo yatreti bahuvrīhau rājadantāditvā- dinā viśeṣaṇasyāpi parinapātaḥ | viśeṣadṛṣṭisarpadṛṣṭyoryadyapi mūrcchādirūpo viśeṣo dṛśyate, tathāpi na sarvatra, tathā yaddhātukarmaṇi prāyaḥ kriyākṛtaviśeṣo- palambhābhāvastaddhātukarma prāpyaṃ karmeti paryavasannam | vakṣyata iti |
'kriyākṛtaviśeṣāṇāṃ siddhiryatra na gamyate |
darśamādanumānādvā tat prāpyamiti kathyate ||' iti |
iti vākyapadīyāditi vakṣyata ityasyārthaḥ | noktātiprasaṅga iti | tadbījaṃ tu 'karmavat karmaṇā ' iti sūtre karmaṇetyupādānāt karmasthakriyāṇāṃ karmasthabhāvakānāmeva dhātūnāṃ grahaṇaṃ vināpyantaraṅgatvāt prakṛtadhātvarthāśraya- kārakeṇaiva tulyakriyatvalābhena 'sādhvasiścchinattī'tyatra vāraṇāt, kartṛbhinnaṃ ca prakṛtadhātvarthāśrayībhūtaṃ karmaiveti bhāvaḥ | kriyāvyavasthā cetthamuktā bhartṛharibhiḥ 
'viśeṣadarśanaṃ yatra kriyā tatra vyavasthitā |
kriyāvyavasthā tvanyeṣāṃ śabdaireva prakalpitā ||' iti |
pūrvārdhasya tūkta evārthaḥ | "nanvevaṃ cintayati paśyatyādīnāṃ kartari kriyā-
kṛtaviśeānupalambhāt kartṛsthabhāvakatvānupapattirataḥ siddhāntimatamāha anyeṣā- miti | mata iti śeṣaḥ | yatra kartṛkarmobhayasādhāraṇaṃ phalaṃ śabdena pratipādyate sa kartṛsthabhāvakaḥ, yathā 'paśyati ghaṭaṃ' 'grāmaṃ gacchatī'tyādau, jñānarūpaphalasya samavāyaviṣayatāsaṃbandhābhyāmubhayaniṣṭhatvāt, saṃyogasya ca samavāyenobhayaniṣṭhatvam, evaṃ hāsasyāpi cittavṛttiviśeṣasyobhayaniṣṭhatvam | ata eva jānāterā- varaṇabhaṅgādyanukūlavyāpārarthakatve kartṛsthakriyakatvānāpattiḥ, karmasthakriyakatvā- pattiśceti jñānānukūlavyāpārārthakatvameva svīkriyate | yatra tu kartṛdṛttiphalaṃ sa karmasthabhāvako yathā 'bhinattī'tyādau, na hi dvidhābhavanādikaṃ kathamapi kartṛgataṃ bhavatīti" helārāja ityanyatra vistaraḥ |
dhātāḥ phalavācakatāsādhikāmanyeṣāṃ yuktimāha dhātunāmiti | kriyāvāca- katvāviśeṣāditi | ekajātīyakriyāvācakatvāviśeṣādityarthaḥ | ata eva dhātumātrasya paryāyatāpattirna kṛtā | api vadantītyanenāsvarasaḥ sūcitaḥ, tadvabījaṃ tu dvitīyayā tattatphalabodhe tattaddhātujanyopasthiterhetutvakalpanayā prayāgaṃ tyajatīti prayoktavye prayāgaṃ gacchatītyādiprayogavāraṇaṃ sukaramiti |
nirvivādamiti bhavatītyāditaḥ sattādibodhasya sarvamatasiddhatvādityarthaḥ | pacatītyādita eva phalabodhe vivādaḥ | tatra phalabodho dvitīyādita eveti parābhyu- pagatatvāt | ata eva - dhātoḥ phalavācakatvādeva | saṃgacchata iti | taṇḍulānodanaṃ pacatītyatra taṇḍulānāṃ vikāryakarmatvamodanasya nirtartyakarmatva- mabhimataṃ tannirvāhārthamutpattivikṛtirūpaphaladvayārthakatvapara eva sa bhāṣyagranthaḥ | ekasyaiva vyāpārasyoktaphaladvayajanakatayā vyāpāradvayaparatve asaṃgatiḥ spaṣṭaiveti bhāvaḥ | nanvevaṃ jānātyādeḥ sakarmakatvāya jñānādyanukūlavyāpāravācitvaṃ, tathā ca cakṣupādikaṃ jānātīti syāccakṣurādavapi jñānānukūlacakṣurmanaḥsaṃyāgādeḥ sattavāditi cenna, 'sthālī pacatī'tivadiṣṭāpatterityāśayenāha digiti || 7 ||
pūrvoktayuktibhiḥ phalavyāpārayorvācyatve sādhite 'tiṅpratyayavācyaiva bhāvane'ti maṇḍanamiśrānuyāyimatamāśaṅkya nirākuste evaṃ sidhyatvityādinā | tadāgame hīti | 'śabdavattūpalabhyate tadāgame hi dṛśyate tasya jñānaṃ yathānyeṣm' iti jaiminisūtram | 'paśunā yajete'tyatraikavacanopāttaukatvasya karaṇatvena na
kriyāyāmanvayaḥ, kiṃ tu prakṛtyarthe, 'pratyayānāṃ prakṛtyarthānvite'ti vyutpatteriti svākāre ekatvasya yāgāṅgatvābhāvāttadabhāve'pi yāgāvaiguṇyāttadavivakṣitameva syāditi, pūrvapakṣe; idanuttarasūtram | tadarthastu-śabdavat - śabdabodhyaṃ yathā bhavati tathopalabhyate ekatvaṃ, hi - yataḥ tadāgame - ekavacanaśravaṇe, dṛśyate'tastasyāṅgatvajñānaṃ, yathānyeṣāṃ paśutva-lauhityādīnāmiti | tathā ca viśaṣṭaṃsya śrutyāṅgatvabodhanena paśuvācitvavacchabdabodhyasyāsya viśeṣaṇasyāṅga- tvopapattiriti bhāvaḥ | evaṃ ca tatra yathā eka vacanāgame ekatvaṃ dṛśyate'tastadarthatvamevaṃ pacatītyādāvākhyātaśravaṇe sati vyāpārabodhāt tasya tadarthatvamevāṅgīkāryamanyathoktanyāyavirodhāpatteriti tātparyam | tathā coktaṃ bhaṭṭapādaiḥ 
'tathā kramavato nityaṃ prakṛtipratyayāṃśayoḥ |
pratyayaśrutivelāyāṃ bhāvanātmāvagamyate ||' iti |
nanvevaṃ pūrvoktasakarmakatvākarmakatvavibhāgocchedaḥ, vyāpārasya dhātvarthatvā- bhāvāt, evaṃ 'devadattaḥ pacatī'tyādau dhātūpāttavyāpārāśrayatvarūpakartṛtvasya durvacatvena devadattādirūpe kartari pratyayo na syādityataḥ sakarmakatvādi- kamanyādṛśamāha evaṃ ca svayukteti | svaṃ - prakṛtadhātuḥ | bhavatyādeḥ pacyādi- yuktākhyātārthavyāpāravyadhikaraṇaphalavācakatvāt sakarmakatvāpattirataḥ 'syukte'- tyākhyātaviśeṣaṇam | yogaśca svāvyavahitottaravartitvaṃ, tena 'devadattaḥ pacati' 'bhavati ghaṭa' ityatra pacyuttarākhyātasya bhūdhātuyuktatve'pi nātiprasaṅgatādava- sthyam | 'svottaravṛttitva'mātraniveśe, 'ghaṭo bhavati' 'pacati devadatta' ityatra bhūdhātāvatiprasaṅgaḥ 'avyavahitottaravartitva'mātraniveśe 'atti' 'papāce'tyetadghaṭa- kāttipacyoḥ saṃgrahe'pi pacati atsyatītyetatsamudāyaghaṭavayostayorasaṃgrahaḥ, ato dvitīyadalapraveśaḥ | tena 'ghaṭaṃ bhāvayati,' 'grāmaṃ gamayatī'tyādau bhavatyāderṇajarthavyāpāravyādhikaṇotpattyādiphalavācakatvasattvānnasaṃgrahaḥ | utpattyā- divācakatvaṃ, prakṛtibhūtabhūprabhṛtāveva na ṇyantasyoti ṇyantasya sakarmakatvābhāve 'bhāṣyate ghaṭa' iti ṇyantāt karmaṇi lakārānupapattiriti na vācyaṃ, svasvaghaṭakānyataravṛttyā tadbodhakatvarūpasya tadvācakatvasya niveśenādoṣāt | 'tadghaṭakabhūdhātāvatiprasaṅga' iti tu na śhakyam, iṣṭāpatteḥ | evaṃ 'svayuktapratyayārthavyāpārasamānādhikaraṇaphalavācakatva'makarmakatvaṃ, 'svayuktapratya-
yārthavyāpārajanyaphalāśrayatvaṃ karmatva'mityapi bodhyam | kartṛtvaṃ vācyamiti | yathā devadattaḥ pacatītyādau devadattādeḥ caitro maitreṇa pācayatī'tyādau prayojyaprayojakayorapi ṇijarthākhyātārthāśrayatvāt kartṛtvaṃ, devadattena pacyate, devadattena sthīyata ityatrāpyākhyātārthavyāpārāśrayatvād devadattasya kartṛtvamiti | ityādītyādipadāduktākarmakatvalakṣaṇādisaṃgrahaḥ | mīmāṃsakammanyamiti | 'ātma- māne khaśca' iti khaś | anena bhāvanāmukhyaviśeṣyako bodha iti sūcitam | gamanavāniti pratītyāpattiriti | phalavyāpārayordhātvarthatvamate'pi tadārattiriti tu na śaṅkyam, antaraṅgatvāt phalasya vyāpāre evānvayāṅgīkārāt | evaṃ 'gamanaṃ na syanda' iti prayogārattiśca bodhyā | saṃyogāśrayatvāditi | asya 'grāgasye'tyādiḥ | nanu karmaṇaḥ kartṛtvavivakṣāyāṃ tādṛśaprayogāpattiḥ siddhānte'pīti cenna | karmadevadattādereva kartṛtvavivakṣāyāmapi tadāpattirityāśayāt, phalamātrārtha- kāt tatsamānādhikaraṇavyāpārārthakācca lyuḍāderanabhidhānādveti bhāvaḥ | vyāpāra- vigame - vyāpāranāśe | ityāpattiśceti | dhātorvyāpārāvācakatve pākapadārtha- phalameva vācyaṃ, tathā coktānāpattiranuktāpattiśceti bhāvaḥ | phalānutpādadaśāyāṃ phalakartṛkasattāyā vartamānatvābhāvāt, vyāpāravigame'pi phalakartṛkasattāyā vartamānatvācceti bhāvaḥ | pūrvoktasthale pacatītyānāpattistadāpattiśca na saṃbhavati, pratyayārtha vyāpāre eva samānābhidhānaśrutyā tadarthakālānvayopagamāt | ukta- dūṣaṇoddhāraṃ taduktaṃ nirākurūte yattu bhāveti | nānupapattiriti | uktadoṣaṇāmanavakāśādestanmatānupapattirnetyarthaḥ |
kartrākhyātavaditi | yathā 'laḥ karmaṇi ' iti sūtre'nuvṛtta 'kartari' ityasya kartṛtvaparatvamabhyupagamya tatsūtreṇa vyāpāre lakāravidhānaṃ mīmāṃsakairaṅgī- kriyate, tathā ghañādināmapi 'kartari kṛt' iti sūtreṇa vyāpāre vidhisaṃbhava ityarthaḥ | na kevalaṃ tadvaiyarthyamapi tu tadbhāṣyavirodho'pityāha tadbhāṣyaviro- dheti | dhañāderapyasattvabhūtakārakānvayayogyavyāpārārthakatve 'bhāve' iti sūtrabhāṣya- virodhāpatterityarthaḥ | tatra hi 'sattvabhūtabhāvo ghañāderartha'iti pratipāditamityagre vakṣyate | nanu dvividhabhāvo'pi ghañādyartho'stvato na tadvirodha ityatastadbhāṣyavirodhāpatteśceti cenānyeṣāṃ dūṣaṇānāṃ saṃgrahaḥ sūcitaḥ | tathā hi ākhyātapratyayasya karturavācyatve'bhidhānānabhidhānavyavasthocchedaḥ, kartṛkarma- kṛtāṃ vyāpārāvācakatve taṇḍulānāṃ pācakaḥ devadattena pakka ityādau
ṣaṣṭhyādyanupapattiḥ, karmatvādyasaṃbhavāt | teṣāmapi bhāvārthakatve gauravam, anuśāsanena tadarthakatvālābhaśca | na ca 'kartari kṛt' iti sūtrasthakartṛpadasyā- vṛttyā dharmadharmyubhayabodhakatayā bhāvanāyāṃ tadvidhilābha iti vācyam, āvṛttau pramāṇābhāvāt, evamapi karmakṛtāṃ tadarthakatvānupapattiḥ sthiraiva | na ca 'tayoreva kṛtya ' ityādāvapi kartṛgrahaṇamanuvartya bhāvanāyāmapi teṣāṃ vidhānamiti vācyaṃ, gauravāpatteḥ | evaṃ 'guruḥ śiṣyābhyāṃ pācayatī'tyatra 'hetumati ca' iti sūtravalāt pyojakavyāpārasya ṇijarthatve sthite prayojyavyāpāra evākhyātārtho vācyaḥ, evaṃ ca ṇijarthavyāpārasya prakṛtyarthatayā ākhyātārthavyāpārānvayinyeva tadarthasaṃkhyānvayā- dgurugatasaṃkhyānabhidhāne gurupadāt prathamānāpattiḥ, śiṣyapadāt tadāpattiśṭetyādyanyatra vistaraḥ | etyabhyupeyamiti | pūrvoktadūṣaṇagaṇanirāsāya yatmātirikto vyāpāro'pi dhātvarthaḥ, yatna ākhyātārtha itbhyupeyata iti bhāvaḥ | sakalavyāpāralābhasaṃbhaveneti | vinigamanāvirahādyatno'pi dhātuvācya ityarthaḥ | gauravamiti | saṃkhyāyāṃ kālādau tacchaktirastu, na tu yatne'pi, anyalabhyatayā tatra śaktikalpanānaucityādityarthaḥ na puna'vyāpāratvena dhātuta eva lābhe pratyayasya yatnatvāvacchinne śaktikalpānāyāṃ gauravādityarthaḥ', tathā satyanubhavasidvaphūtkāra- tvādinā bodhāsyānirvāhāt, tattatphalānukūlayatnalābhe'pi tadanukūlavyāpā- rānukūlayatnālābhāt | 'tallābhārthamākhyātasya yatne śakti'riti navyamīmāṃsa- koktamapi na yuktaṃ, tathānanubhavāt, anyathā vyāpārānukūlavyāpārālābhāt tatrāpi śaktikalpanāpatteḥ, pākaṃ karotīti dhātvarthāt pṛdhak karotinā vivriyamāṇatayā viklittyādiphalānukūlavyāpārānukūlayatnādirūpā bhāvanā ākhyātārthaḥ, vyāpāravigame phalasattvadaśāyāṃ 'pāko vidyate' ityaprayogāt, ghañantapākādipadasya phalāvacchinnavyāpārārthatāyā āvaśyakatvāditi tvavācyaṃ, pākaṃ karotītyatratya- pākapadasya phalamātrārthakatvāt, phalānutpādasamaye phūtkārādivyāpārasattve'pi'- pāko bhaviṣyatī'tyādivyavahārāt kkacittasya phalamātraparatvasyāpi darśanāt, anyatra phalāvacchinnavyāpāraparatvānnoktadoṣa iti bhāvaḥ | 'kathaṃ punarjñāyate kriyāvacanāḥ pacādayaḥ ? iti, yadeṣāṃ karotinā sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ, kiṃ karoti pacatīti, dhātureva kriyāmāha' iti bhūvādisūtre bhāṣyamapyata eva saṃgacchate | atra gauravamityantena tasmādityanena sūcito buddhisthahetusamudāyaḥ pratipāditaḥ | uktahetutaḥ karotiḥ pacādidhātoreva vyākhyānaṃ vivaraṇamiti
mūlārthamāha pacatītyasyetyādinā | bādhakam - prakṛtipratyayayoḥ pratyayārtheti nyāyavirodhātmakaṃ bādhakam | tanmatam - ākhyātavācyaiva bhāvanā, karotinā vivaraṇāt, 'dhātvarthasyaiva vivaraṇa'miti tu na, vinigamakābhāvāt, asmanmate ca 'vyāpāraviśeṣyakabodhānupapattireva mānam, taduktam 
'pratyayārtha saha brūtaḥ prakṛtipratyayau sadā |
prādhānyād bhāvanā tena pratyayārtho'vadhārthate ||' iti 
māmāsakamatamityarthaḥ | nanu padapadārthopasthityādighaṭitasāmagrīsattve kuto'nvayabodhābhāvaḥ, na ca kārakāṇāṃ bhāvanāyāmevānvaya iti niyamātra parasparamanvaya iti vācyaṃ, kārakarūpeṇānvayāsaṃbhavo'pi mīmāṃsakarītyā saṃbandhasāmānyenānvayasambhavādityata āha ayaṃ bhāva iti | sahartha brūta iti | ekārthānvitasvārtha bodhayataḥ | arthadvayamadhye kasya viśeṣyatvaṃ, kasya viśeṣaṇatvamityākāṅkṣāyāmāha tayoriti | phalitārthamāha ityasya hīti | prakṛtipratyayārthayoḥ parasparānanvayaprayojakamubhayoḥ kārakatvamāha pākaḥ karma- kārakamiti | pākaḥ - phalaṃśastasya-kriyānirvarttyatayā, vyapadeśivadbhāvena svāśrayatayā ca karmatvamityarthaḥ | aruṇādhikaraṇe hi kārakāṇāṃ kriyāyāmevānvaya ityuktam | vakṣyamāṇeti | kriyātvasyaiva tadanvayitāvacchedakatayā tadviśiṣṭakriyā- yāmevānvaya iti vakṣyamāṇetyarthaḥ | tatra iyān paraṃ bhedo yanmīmāṃsakamate 'aruṇayā piṅgākṣyā ekahāyanyā somaṃ krīṇātī'tyādau viśeṣyaviśeṣaṇānāṃ sarveṣāṃ kriyāyāmanvayaḥ, parasparamanvastu pārṣṭhikaḥ, svamate tu viśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭaviśeṣya- syeti | ata eva nīlotpalamityādau sāmarthyāt samāsa upapadyate | niyamasyaivā- bhāve iti | bhavanmate etādṛśasthale vyābhicārāduktaniyamasyaivāsaṃbhave ityarthaḥ | na ca 'saṃbandha'-iti | yajetetyatra śrutyā - dhātvādiśrutyā, dhātvarthabhāvayoḥ - dhātvarthabhāvanayoḥ, saṃbandhamātraṃ - saṃbandhasāmānyamuktam, tatra tṛtīyādivibhaktaya- bhāvena karaṇatvādiviśeṣarūpaṇānvayāsaṃbhavāt saṃbandhasāmānyenānvayaḥ pratipādyate, tadekāṃśaniveśe - saṃbandhaviśeṣabodhane, asyāḥ - śrutervyāpāraḥ - sāmarthya neti tadarthaḥ | arthāvādharūpayogyatāsattvāt tadvirahoktissaṃgatetyata āha-anvayeti | tanmate'pi tasyaiva yodyatātvāt . ata eva sthūlajavābhiprāyakaṃ 'payasā sicatī'ti vākyaṃ na yogyaṃ, dravadravyatvasyaivānvayitāvacchedakatvāt, anvayaprayojakapade- nānvayitāvacchedakasyaivābhidhānāt, yadavacchinne'nvayastasyaiva hyanvayitāvaccheda- katvaṃ, na ca sthūlajale dravadravyatvajñānadaśāyāṃ 'seko naitajjalakaraṇaka' iti bādhakapramāsattve'pi śābdabodhāpattiriti vācyaṃ,svarūpasadyogyatāyā hetutvāt | yadvā-bādhakapramāviraho'pyapekṣaṇīya iti bhāvaḥ | niyamadvayo'pi dūṣaṇamāha- ajāśvetyādinā | strītvasyaiveti | yaḥ pradhānaṃ sa pratyayārtha eveti niyame|ḍajādeḥ pratyayārthatvaviraheṇa prādhānyānāpattiḥ, kiṃ tu strītvasyaiveti bhāvaḥ | dvitīyaniyame tu spaṣṭamevedaṃ dūṣaṇam | nanu viśeṣyatvādinā bodhe niyāmakābhāve'niyata eva
bodhaḥ syādityata āha-viśeṣyatvādineti | vyutpattyānviti | tathā ca vyutpattireva tatra niyāmiketi bhāvanāyā dhātvarthatve'pi na prādhānyānupapattiriti bhāvaḥ | vyutpattyanusāritvaṃ bodhasya darśayati-ata evetyādinā | śakyatāvacchedakaprakāraka eveti | ata eva 'gaṅagāyāṃ ghoṣaḥ', 
'jātā latā hi śaile jātu latāyāṃ na jāyate śailaḥ |
saṃprati tadviparitaṃ kanakalatāyāṃ giridvayaṃ jātam ||'
ityādau gaṅgātva latātvādinaiva bodhaḥ | ata eva vilakṣaṇaśaityapāva- natvādipratīticamatkāro'pi | anyathā gaṅgātore ghoṣa ityataḥ ko viśeṣaḥ syāt, viparītapadapratipādyaṃ vaiparītyaṃ ca na bhavodityāhurālaṅkārikāḥ |
'kacatastrasyati vadanaṃ vadanāt kucamaṇḍalaṃ trasati |
madhyādvibheti nayanaṃ nayanādadharaḥ samudvijati ||'
ityatra kacatvādinā bodho na trāsakaro'pi tu rāhutvacandratvādinaiva, ata eva 'gaṅgāyāṃ ghoṣa' ityādau tīratvādinā bodhaḥ | kacapadaṃ rāhau, vadanapadaṃ candre, kucamaṇḍalapadaṃ cakravākadampatyormadhyapadaṃ sihe, nayanapadaṃ hariṇe'dharapadaṃ pravāle lākṣaṇikam | gaṅgāpadena svasṛttyā pratipādanādeva vilakṣaṇaśaitya- pāvanatvādipratītinirvāha iti naiyāyikādaya āhuḥ | viparyayeṇeti | vaiparītyena | vyutpannānām - tādṛśavākye ādhārādheyabhāvasaṃbandhena ghaṭaprakārakakarmatvaviśeṣyaka- bodhajanakatāgrahavatām | naiyāyiketi | karmadhārayasamāse naiyāyikaśabdasya kriyāśabdatayā pāṭhakapācakādau pāṭhakādivat pūrvanipātaḥ | bodhaḥ - uktabodhaḥ | na taditi | atra bodha ityanuṣajanyate | tathāvyutpattirahitānāṃ ghaṭavṛttikarmatva- mityākārako bodha ityarthaḥ, na tu bodhasāmānyānudayo'rthaḥ, ayogyatāniścayaśūnya- samaye tathāvyutpattirahitānāmapi tādātmyasaṃbandhena ghaṭaprakārakakarmatvaviśeṣyaka- bodhodayāt | ata eva - bodhasya vyutpattyanusāritvādeva | vacanameveti | eva- kāreṇa prakṛtipratyayayorityasya vyuvṛttistasyautsargikatvāt sāmānyavacanatvāccau- tasya viśeṣavacanatvādidameva grāhyamiti bhāvaḥ | tatrākhyātapadena dhāturucyate, ākhyāyate sarvapradhānabhūto'rtho'neneti vyutpatteḥ | nāmādiprakṛtīnāmevoddeśāt | tatra 'nāmānyākhyātajāni' iti niruktācca | na hyākhyātapratyayajaṃ tadantajaṃ vā nāma saṃbhāvyate | ata eva 'sarva nāma dhātujamāha vyākaraṇe śakaṭasya ca tekam' iti pūrvoktaniruktasamānārthakaṃ vākyaṃ mahābhāṣye paṭhāte | tathā ca vākye
sarvāpekṣayā pradhānībhūtabhāvārthakatvaṃ dhātulakṣaṇaṃ paryavasannam | prādhānyoktistu tanmukhyaviśeṣyaka eva bodho jāyata iti sphoraṇāya, na tu tasya lakṣaṇe niveśa iti bhūṣaṇakārānumatā saraṇiḥ |
mañjūṣākārāstu tatrākhyātaśabdena tiṅntam, 'ākhyātamākhyātena' ityādau tathaiva pratīteḥ, 'catvāri padajātāni nāmākhyāte copasarganipātāśca' ityupa- kramācca, 'bhāvakālakārakasaṃkhyāścatvāro'rthā ākhyātasya, tatra bhāvaḥ pradhānam' iti tadbhāṣyāccetyādikamāhuḥ | vistarastu tata evāvagantavyaḥ | etanmate'pi eta'nniruktaṃ bhāvasya prāguktayuktibhirdhātvarthātve sthite tatprādhānyaniyāmakaṃ bhavatīti dhyeyam | ityatrāpīti | apinā pakkavānityasya samuccayaḥ | ubhayasāvāraṇyena - tiṅntakṛdantobayasādha raṇyena | dhātureva vācaka iti | ubhayabhāpi pacidhātorekatvena lāghavāt, pratyayānāṃ bhinnatayā tadvācakatyāṃ gauravāditi bhāvaḥ | prādhānyāpattiśceti | kṛtāmapi bhāvanāvācakatve bhāvanayaivākṣepāt kartṛkarmalābhasaṃbhave tiṅkṣivavātrāpi tasyā eva prādhānyāpattiriti bhāvaḥ || 8 ||
liṅgasaṃkhyānvayeti | pakavān pakkavatī pakkavāt ityādau puṃstatvastre- tvādiliṅgaikatvādisaṃkhyānvayāyetyarthaḥ | karturvācyatvamiti | idaṃ 'pakkastaṇḍula' ityādau karmādesapyupalakṣaṇam | samāsānupapattau hetumāha purupa iti | rājasaṃbandhī puruṣo devadattasaṃbandhinī bhāryetyanvaye vivakṣite rājño bhāryā ityanena yathā samāso na bhavati, asāmarthyādananvayādevaṃ nakhairbhinna ityādāvapi sa na syādityarthaḥ | asāmarthyāditi | kārakāṇāṃ bhāvanāyāmevānvayena parasparamananvayāt, paramate tiṅagaderbhāvanānācakatvāt | sākṣāddhātvarthatvānva- yeneti | kṛdhātostanmate'pi kṛtirūpabhāvanārthakatvāditi bhāvaḥ | parampareti | svakartṛkādikriyājanyatvarūpaparamparetyarthaḥ | śaṅkate na ceti | tathā ca noktaparamparāsaṃbandhena tayoranvayaḥ, api tvekasyāṃ kriyāyāmanvayitvarūpameva sāmarthya nīlotpalamityādisamāsasiddhaye tairaṅgīkartavyaṃ, taccātrāpyastīti na samāsānupapattiriti bhāvaḥ | asamartheti | 'asūryapaśyā rājadārā'ityatra darśana- kriyāyā evābhāvabodhena sūryapadārthena nañarthasyānvayavirahādasamarthasamāsatvena bhāṣyakārāderabhimataḥ, tasyā'samarthasamāsatvaṃ tava mate bhajyeta, na sūrya paśyantī-
tyarthe nañasūryapadārthayorekakriyānvayitvarūpasāmarthyasattvāditi bhāvaḥ | ādi- padā'daśrāddhabhojī brāhmaṇa' 'amunargeyāḥ ślokā' ityādeḥ saṃgrahaḥ | ityādyā- patteriti | na cātrāpīṣṭāpattiḥ, tatra samāsābhāvasyaiva sarvānumatatvāt, ata eva 'sarvacarmaṇaḥ kṛtaḥ ' iti sūtre sarvaścarmaṇā kṛta ityarthe 'sarvacamaṇa' iti nipātanāt samāsa ityuktam | nanu kṛtaḥ sarva ityādāvanabhidhānānna samāsaḥ, kiṃ ca 'saha supā' ityasārvatrikaṃ | tenaiva sarvatra samāsasiddhau 'dvitīyā śritā ' ityādinā samāsavadhānāt, anyathā tattatsūtraistatpuruṣādisaṃjñā eva vidhīyeran | ata evānena samāsasyāgatitvaṃ tatra tatroktam | kiṃ caivaṃ 'kartṛkaraṇe kṛtā ' ityādinā kṛprakṛtikakṛteti vā vaktavye sāmānyasūtrapraṇayanasāmarthyāt 'devatrāto galo grāha'iti nirddaśācca kriyādvārakasāmarthyamādāya kṛdantena kārakasya samāsa ityabhyupagamānna doṣaḥ | kṛdgrahaṇasāmarthyādapyayamarthaḥ sidhyati | 'kartṛkaraṇe'iti nyāsenaiva 'supā' ityanuvṛttyā kṛdantenaiva samāsasya siddheḥ, 'kāṣṭhaiḥ pacatitarā'mityatra samāsasya bahulagrahaṇenāpi vārayituṃ śakyatvād, bahulagrahaṇenaiva subutpatteḥ prāgeva samāsasya siddhatvādityabhipretya dūṣaṇāntaramāha kiṃ ceti |
dhātvarthaphaleti | utpattirūpaphaletyarthaḥ | nanu bhavanmate'pīyamāpattistulyā, kartṛsaṃjñayā karmasaṃjñāyā bādhānneti samādhānamapi tulyamityāśaṅkāṃ nirācaṣṭe na cākhyātārtheti | tathā ca dṛṭāntārṣṭāntikayorveṣamyam, dṛṣṭānte prayojakavyāpāro ṇitarthaḥ, dārṣṭāntike tu prayojyavyāpāra ākhyātārthaḥ | evaṃ ca ghaṭaṃ bhāvayatītyatra ghaṭasya kartṛtvābhāvena ṇijarthabhāvanāṃ prati karmatvaṃ nirapavādameva | ghaṭo bhavatītyatra ākhyātārthavyāpārāśrayatayā kartṛsaṃjñayā vādhitatvānna ghaṭasya karmatvamityarthaḥ | akartṛtvāpattāviti | sarvasyaiva yatkicidākhyātārthavyāpārāśrayatvāt samabhivyā- hṛtetyāvaśyakam | prakṛte cātyātasya na prayojyavyāpāro'rthaḥ, api tu ṇijarthavyāpāra eva tenānūdyate | ata eva prayojakasya kartṛtvanirvāhaḥ, ata evākhyātārthavyāpārānvayinyeva tadarthasaṃkhyānvayaniyamād 'guruḥ siṣyābhyāṃ pācayatī'tyatra gurupadāt pradhamotpattiḥ, pācayatītyekavacanopapattiśca | evaṃ cākhyātārthavyāpārānāśrayatvāt prayojyasya kartṛtvānupapattiriti bhāvaḥ | kāraṇa- tṛtīyaiveyamiti tu na śaṅkyaṃ, tathā sati kartuḥ prayojakasyaiva hetutayā devadattasya hetutāpattau ṇijutpatterevāsaṃbhavāpatteḥ | 'tatpayojaka 'ityatra tatpadena dhātvarthānukūlavyāpārāśrayatvaditi na vācyam, 'svatantraḥ kartā' iti pūrvasūtreṇa
kartṛsaṃjñāvidhānāt tatpadena kartṛsaṃjñakasyaiva parāmarśārhatvāt, kārakamātrasyaiva dhātvarthānukūlavyāpārāśrayatayā kārakamātraprayojakavyāpāre ṇijutpattyāpatteḥ | na ceṣṭāpattiḥ 'kāṣṭhaiḥ pācayati' 'darśayati bhavaṃ bhakta' ityādiprayogāṇāṃ darśanāditi vācyaṃ, kāṣṭhāni pacanti paśyati bhava ityarthe kāṣṭhādīnāṃ kartṛvivakṣāyāṃ tatpreraṇāyāmeva tādṛśaprayogāṇāmiṣṭatvāditi bhāvaḥ | gamikarmatānāpatteriti | kartṛrīpsitasyaiva karmatvāt | nanu tvatsiddhānte'pi karmasaṃjñāvidhāyake 'karturīpsitatamam-' iti sūtre kartṛpadaṃ svatantramātraparaṃ, na tu kartṛsaṃjñakaparaṃ, 'devadato viṣṇumitraṃ grāmaṃ gamayatī'tyatra grāmasya karmatvānāpatteḥ, prayojyasya gatyādisūtreṇa karmasaṃjñāyāṃ kartṛsaṃjñāvirahāt | evaṃ ca mamāpi mate na doṣaḥ, prayojyasya dhātvarthasaṃyogānukūlavyāpārāśrayatvarūpasvātantryasattvāditi cenna | etanmate ākhyātārthavyāpārāśrayatvasyaivaṃ, na tūktasya, kārakāmātre'tivyāpteḥ, tasya ca prayojye'sattvāt | prakṛtadhātusaṃbandhikārakacakraprayoktṛtgharūtaṃ taddhātukartṛtvaṃ tvacetane na saṃbhavatītyuktameveti bhāvaḥ | grāmasya ṇijantakarmatvaṃ saṃbhavatyeva tata eva dvitīyānirvāha ityata āha gamikarmateti | nanu tadabhāve kā kṣatirityā- śaṅkāmāha tathā ceti | ityapi na syādityatra hetumāha gatyarthakarmaṇi ' iti | ādhvabhinne gatyarthakarmaṇi dvitīyācaturthyau staśceṣṭāyāṃ satyāmiti tatsūtrārthaḥ | tanmate gatyarthakarmatvaṃ saṃyogarūpagatyarthakadhātusamabhivyāhṛtākhyārtha- vyāpārāśrayarūpaṃ karturūpsitatamatvaṃ tādṛśakartṛniṣṭhākhyātārthakriyājanyaphalāśraya- tayecchāviṣayatvarūpaṃ, taccoktarūtyā grāmasya na saṃbhavatīti ṇijantakarmatvanibandha- nadvitīyāsiddhāvapi caturthī na sidhyediti bhāvaḥ | etena - uktaprayogānirvāheṇa | ubhayam - prayojyaprayojakavyāpārobhayam | apāstamiti | uktākhyātasya ṇijantadhātuyuktatve'pi gatyarthakadhātuyuktatvābhavena prayojyasya svayuktākhyātārtha- vyāpārāśrayatvarūpagatyarthakartṛtvābhāvād gatyarthakadhātukarmatvānāpattau caturthyanāpatti- tādavasthāditi bhāvaḥ | nanu gatyarthakarmapadena tadarthasaṃyogāśrayatve sati tadanukūlavyāpārāśrayatvena vivakṣito yastadabhinno'rtho vivakṣitaḥ, lakṣyānurodhāt | kiṃ ca 'gatyarthakarmaṇi-' iti sūtraṃ bhāṣye pratyāthyātam, tathā ca gatyarthakarmatvā- bhāve'pi na kṣatiḥ, 'patye śete' itivaccaturthī siddheti spaṣṭaṃ bhāṣyādāvata āha-kiṃ ceti | nanvākhyātaśūnyasthale'pi prathamānurodhena tiṅtādhyāhāra āvaśyakaḥ, anyathā tiṅsamānādhikaraṇe prathametyanuśiṣṭaprathamāyāḥ sādhutvaṃ na syāt, evaṃ ca
devadattaḥ paktetyatrāpi astītyādyadhyāhārād devadattasya kartṛtvopapattiriti cenna, evaṃ satyapyasdhātukartṛtvopapattāvapi pacikartṛtvānupapattitādavasthyena tataḥ kartari tṛjanāpatteḥ | nanu yaddhātuprakṛtikākhyātārthavyāpārāśrayatvaṃ yasya gṛhītaṃ tatra taddhātoḥ kṛd bhavatītyeva 'kartari kṛt' ityasyārtho'bhyupeyate, sāmarthyāt, ākṣiptabhāvanāśrayatvena yadā yo vivakṣitastadā tasmin vācye iti ca | yadvā kṛtpratyayasya vyāpāro'pyartho'ṅgīkriyate, evaṃ ca pratyayārthāśrayatvameva kartṛtvamabhyupeyate iti cenna, kliṣṭatvāpatteḥ | ita eva vāsvarasādāha-digiti | tadarthastu bhavatā dhātvarthaphalaprakārakānukūlatvasaṃsargakabhāvanāviroṣyakabodhe dhātvā- khyātānupūrvīdhātukṛdānupūrvyoḥ kāraṇatāvacchedakatvamaṅgīkāraṇīyam, mayā tu dhātvānupūrvyā eva tathātvamaṅgīkartavyamiti lāghavamityādi-bṛhadbhūṣaṇato- 'vagantavyam |
nanu 'bhūvādayaḥ' ityasya bhūrādiryeṣāmiti bahuvrīhiṇā bhūprabhṛtayo dhātusaṃjñā ityevārtho na tu bhvādigaṇapaṭhitāḥ kriyāvācakā dhātusaṃjñā iti, tadvodhakaśabdā- bhāvāt, kathaṃ tarhi 'bhūvādayaḥ' iti nirdeśopapattiḥ, nipātanādvakārāgama iti gṛhāṇa | nanu tathāpi tadānarthakyaṃ duṣpariharameveti cenna |
'bhūvādīnāṃ vakāro'yaṃ maṅgalārthaḥ prayujyate' |
iti vārtikakāraireva parihṛtatvāt | maṅgalārthaḥ - maṅgalasūcakaḥ | yathā'pūrvasya dadhyāderlābho loke maṅgalasūcakastathetyarthaḥ | athavā sati vakāre- 'vikṛtabhūśabdoccāraṇena mahāvyāhṛtirephāntabhūśabdasmaraṇasaṃpādanadvārā tasya maṅgalasūcakatvamiti kaiyaṭiye spaṣṭam | evaṃ ca na sūtrānupattiḥ paramate ityāśaṅkāṃ nirācikīrṣuḥ sūtrārthamāha-tatra bhūścetyādinā | vyavastheti | niyamenāvadhisākāṅkṣaprathamarūpo'rthaḥ | kriyāvācakatveneti | śabdatvādinā sādṛśyābhyupagame vādaya ityasyāvyāvartakatāpattiḥ | adāditvagatyarthakatvena tatsvīkāre sakalabhvādīnāmasaṃgraha ityato vyākhyānācca kriyāvācakatvenaiva tadabhyupagama iti bhāvaḥ | hirūgādīnāṃ kriyānātraṃ prati viśeṣaṇatve'pi teṣāṃ kriyāvācakatvābhāva itbhipratyāha-ityādāviti | ādinā śiśye iti bhāvataḍaḥ, āṇavayatyādeścasaṃgrahaḥ || 6 || ākāralopāpattiriti | nanvetasyākārasya lākṣaṇikatayāna lopa iti cenna | 'paśuṣā' ityasiddhayāpatteḥ | paśuṃ salotītyarthe 'janasana-' iti viṭ | 'viḍvanoḥ-' ityātvam | lākṣaṇikatvāditi |
pratipadoktasya 'yā' ityasyaiva bhvādipadena grahaṇaṃ na sarvanāmna iti bhāvaḥ | yadi tu 'nakārajāvanusvārapaccamau jhali dhātuṣu | sakārajaḥ śakāraścerṣāṭṭavargastavargajaḥ ||'
-ityabhiyuktokterdhātusaṃjñāsūtre lakṣaṇapratipadoktaparibhāṣāpravṛttirvaktumaśakyā, tatparibhāṣāpravṛttau hi dhātuviśiṣṭaghaṭakatvaṃ nakārāderasaṃbhavīti tathābhiyuktoktayā dhātusaṃjñāyāmuktaparibhāṣā na pravartata ityaṅgīkāryamityucyate, tarhyastu dhātusaṃjñā, tathāpi, 'āto'nāpaḥ' iti vārtikānna prakṛte ākāralopa ityata āhetyava- taraṇikā bodhyā | nanu nipātasya vetyasya dhātutve'pi na kṣatiḥ, vātīti prayogasyeṣṭatvāt, ata āha-vikalpārthaka iti | tathā ca gatyādyarthakasya vātītiprayogasyeṣṭatve'pi vikalpārthakoneṣṭa iti bhāvaḥ | kiṃ ca tasya dhātutve 'adhātuḥ' iti paryudāsāt pratipadikatvānāpattau sūbantatvābhāve padatvānāpattiḥ | etena hantergatyarthatve'pi hantītyataḥ prāyastadarthānavagativat vātītyato vikalpānavagatinirvāha iti tasya dhātutvepi na kṣatiriti doṣānavākāśaḥ | yadyāpi nipātasyānarthakasyetyavaśyamanarthakanipātānāṃ prātipadikasaṃjñārthamārabdhavyaṃ tathā ca tatrānarthakasya nipātasya saṃjñāvidhānena caritārthatayā sarvato balīyaso niṣedhabādhe mānābhāva ityāśayaḥ, ita evāsvarasādvā-yāvādīnāmityādipadam | arthānādeśanāditi bhāṣyeti | muṇḍamiśretyādisūtrasthetyādiḥ | ata eva prakṛtasūtre 'parimāṇagrahaṇaṃ kartavyamiyānavadhirdhātusaṃjño bhavatīti vaktavyam, kuto hmetudbhūśabdo dhātusaṃjño bhavati ca pūnarbhvedhaśabda iti' vārtikaṃ tadbhāṣyaṃ ca saṃgacchate | arthānādeśanādityasya sarvatrāthāṃnādeśanādityarthaḥ | ata eva 'evamartha' svalvapyācāryaścitrayati, kkacidarthānādiśati kkacinna iti 'namovarivaścatra-' iti sūtre bhāṣye uktam | ata eva ca 'kurda khurda krīḍāyāmeva' ityevakārasya dhātunāmanekārthatve jñāpakatoktā saṃgacchate | arthanirdeśasyopalakṣaṇātvāccetyapi bodhyam || 10 ||
grahapadapūraṇaprayojanagāha-yathāśruteti | utpattau grahe veti | devadattasya yajñadattād yajñadattasya ca devadattādutpattirna saṃbhavati | evamitpadārthajñāne halpadārthajñānasya kāraṇatvaṃ halpadārthajñāne cetpadārthajñānasya kāraṇatvaṃ na saṃbhavati,
kāryāvyavahitaniyatapūrvavartina eva kāraṇatvaṃ, taccobhayatra na saṃbhavatīti tatraiva tasya pratibandhakatvamityarthaḥ | na cānyatamatvamiti | yāvanto dhātupadavyayahāryā- stadanyatamatvarūpaṃ 'dhātutvaṃ, dhātvarthatvaṃ kriyātva'mityeddhaṭakamato nānyonyā- śrayāvakāśa iti bhāvaḥ | vaiyarthyāpatteriti | 'dhātoḥ karmaṇaḥ-' ityādau tādṛśānyatamatvavata eva grahaṇeneṣṭasiddhestatparicayārthameva sūtramiti cedeta- dvākyārthabodhātmakaparicayāsaṃbhave tādavanyatamatvenopādānāsaṃbhava iti bhāvaḥ | lakṣaṇamiti | sūtrajavākyārtharūpamityarthaḥ | phalāṃśānāmanyatamatvenopādāne sūtravaiyarthya śaṅkāpi neti bhāvaḥ | nanvevaṃ kathaṃ punarjñāyate kriyāvacanāḥ pacādayo yadeṣāṃ karotinā sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ kriyāvācano dhāturiti bhūvādisūtre bhāṣyavyavahāraḥ kathaṃ saṃgamanīyaḥ, phalāṃśasya kriyātvābhāvādityata āha-dhātvartha- tvāditi | tathā ca gauṇo vyavahāra ityāśayaḥ | atra vadanti laḍādyutpattau bhvādigaṇapaṭhitatve sati kriyāvācakatvaṃ na niyāmakaṃ, kriyātvasyānugatasyābhāvāt, kiṃ tu saṃjñāśabdatvāt saṃjñāviśeṣaḥ, saṃjñāśabdānāṃ jātivācitvapakṣe jātiviśeṣa eva, sūtraṃ tu tatparicāyakameva, kārikāyāṃ kriyāśabdasya sādhyatvenābhidhīyamāna- vyāpāre saṃketikaśakteḥ sa eva gṛhyate, sūtre vāśabdaḥ sūcanāyati na sūtraṃ dhātoḥ kriyāvācakatvasādhanāyābhihitaṃ kiṃ tūktayuktibhiḥ sādhitārthasya svotprekṣitatva- nirāsāyaiveti tattvam | etena bhvādyanyatamatvamevāstu dhātutvaṃ, tacca na kriyātva- garbhamathavā gaṇapaṭhitatve sati sattādyarthakatvameva bhūdhātordhātutvam, evaṃ tattadarthamupādāya tattaddhātutvamūhyam | arthasya viśeṣyopādānācca nānyonyā- śrayo'pi | athavā sarvaphalānyanyatamatvenopādāya gaṇapaṭhitaviśeṣitatadvācakatvameva sūtrārthaḥ kalpyatāmityapāstam | uktarītyaiva nirvāhe'nyatamatvādigarbhabhavadukta- pakṣāṇāṃ gurūtvāt | caramapakṣe vikalpasyāpyanyatamamadhye niveśāvaśyakatvāditi | etena yasmāddhātoryo yo'rthaḥ pratīyate loke tattadarthaviśiṣṭasya tasyaiva gaṇe pāṭhena, bhvādigaṇapaṭhitatvameva nirdiṣṭamiti parāstam, durjñetvācca || 11 ||
asyaiva | bhvādigaṇapaṭhitatve sati kriyāvācakatvasyaiva | pratītyabhāvā- diti | asya 'kriyāyā' ityādiḥ | tattatkālasaṃbandharūpasattāyā eva tataḥ pratīteḥ | ata eva kiṃ karotīti praśre pacatītivadastītyuttaraṃ na | dharmibhāge -
sattādirūpe dharmibhāge | vastusato bhāvyasya bhāvanāpratītyupayogitvaṃ na saṃbhavatītyata āha bhāvyatveneti | sutarāmiti | utpattyanukūlabhāvanāpratīteḥ sakalānubhavasiddhatvāt sattārthakatve kkavideva tatpratītiḥ | utpattyādyarthakatve tu sarvatreti bodhanāya tarappratyayaḥ | utpattyādītyādipadāt parābhavatītyādau parābhavādisaṃgrahaḥ | utpattyarthakatve rāmāyaṇaprayogaṃ pramāṇayati 'rohita' iti | sattārthakatve'nubhavavirodhaḥ, vaṃśāvalyāmutpatterevānubhavāt, pañcamyarthahetutvānva- yānupapatteḥ | pañcamyanupapatteśceti bhāvaḥ | astyādeḥ sattātulyabhāvanārthakatve sādhakāntaramapyāha kiñceti | tasyā eveti | bhāvanāyā evetyarthaḥ | evena phalāṃśavyavacchedaḥ | tatra mānamāha kriyābhedāyeti | svaviśiṣṭakriyāyā itara- kriyāto vyāvartanāya | kriyāyāṃ kālānvaye satyevetaravyāvṛttiriti kriyāyāmeva kālānvayaḥ, 'kriyābhedāye'tyasya sāvadhāraṇatvāt, saṃkhyā sarvasyettyuttaravākyāt | tathā ca bhāṣyakārādyanubhava eva mānamiti bhāvaḥ | tathā coktaṃ bhūvādisūtre bhāṣye 'nānreṇa kriyāṃ bhūtabhaviṣyadvartamānāḥ kālā vyajyante | astyādibhiścāpi bhūtabhaviṣyadvartamānakālā vyajyante' iti | ata eva ca 'vartamānakriyāvṛtte'riti vyākhyātam | ata evāstītyādau kriyātvanivāhaḥ | astibhavatividyatī- nāmātmabharaṇamarthaḥ | ātmā sattā, sattā cāṣaṣṭhādbhāvavikārādanusyūtaṃ yadrūpaṃ saditi pratyayavedyaṃ tat, tadviṣayavyāpāraścātmabharaṇasvarūpadhāraṇa-padābhyāmucyate | spaṣṭaṃ cedaṃ bhūvādisūtre bhāṣye | nirūkte'pi 'astītyutpannasya sattvasyāva- dhāraṇam' iti | 'astīti cotpannasya sattvasya sattāvato'vavāraṇamācaṣṭo iti tadbhāṣyakṛtaḥ | aśeṣabhāvapadārtha vyācaṣṭo phalasamānādhikaraṇatvāditi | doṣa iti | tadanupapattiprayojakaḥ | tathā ca dūratvādikamiva tadbhāvanāyāḥ spaṣṭapratītau pratibandhakamityarthaḥ | susthatayā niścite iti | sattānukūlavyāpāraniścaye kiṃ karotīti praśno'nyaviṣayaka evetyavadhāraṇādastī- tyuttaraṃ tatra netyarthaḥ | bṛhadbhūṣaṇakārāstu vastuto bhūvādaya iti sūtrānu- rodhāddhātūnāṃ kriyāvācakatvāvaśyakatve'pi phalavācakatvaṃ nāvaśyakam, ato- 'styādau sattādireva kriyā, ata eva 'paśyati bhavaḥ' 'pacyate taṇḍulaḥ svayameve'tyādau viṣayatādirūpaphalasyaiva kriyātvāt tadāśrayabhāvādeḥ kartṛtvopa- pattirityuktam |
'ekadeśe samūpe vā vyāpārāṇāṃ pacādayaḥ |
svabhāvavat pravartante tulyarūpaṃ samāśritāḥ ||' iti |
vākyapadīyavyākhyāvasare helarājīye'pi taddhātvarthakriyātvaṃ ca taddhā- tvarthāviśeṣaṇatve sati taddhātujanyaśābdabodhaviṣayatāviśiṣṭatvam, ato na 'taṇḍulaṃ pacati' 'ghaṭaṃ bhāvayati' 'paśyati bhava'mityādau viklittyādirūpakriyāśrayatvāt kartṛ tāpattyā kartṛsaṃjñayā karmasaṃjñābādhāt dvitīyānāpattiḥ | tasmim prayoge phalasya taddhātvarthaviśeṣaṇatvenīktakriyātvābhāvāt | karmakartṛ prayoge tu phalasyoktakriyātvasattvādupapadyante 'taṇḍulaḥ pacyate svayameve'tyādayaḥ prayogāḥ | satyantaghaṭakatatpadānupādāne pacati bhavatītyādau pacyādyarthasya kriyātvānāpattira- ratastadupādānam | na ca yathāśrute'pi ghaṭaṃ bhāvayatītyādau karmasaṃjñā 'pacati devadatta' ityādau sāvakāśāṃ kartṛsaṃjñāṃ bādhata iti nānupapattiriti vācyam | 'caitro grāmaṃ gacchati' 'paśyati bhava'ityādāvapi dhātūpāttaphalāśrayatvāccaitrāderapi karmatyāpattā dvitīyāpatteḥ | ghaṭaṃ bhāvayatītyatra bhavanasya ṇijantadhātvartha- viśeṣaṇatvo'pibhvarthvāviśeṣaṇatvenabhvarthakriyātvāpattyā tadāśrayasya kartṛtvāpattistu na, iṣṭāpatteḥ | ata eva kriyābhedādubhayamapyastīti 'ṇyante kartṛśca karmaṇaḥ' iti bhāṣye ubhayathāpi vyavahāraḥ kṛtaḥ | nanvevaṃ kriyātvasya dhātutvaghaṭita- tayānyonyāśrayatādavasthyamiti cenna, dhātutvasya jātyādirūpatvāt, bhūvādisūtraṃ tu taricāyakamiti prāgevoktam | taddhātvarthaphalatvamapi - taddhātvarthaviśeṣaṇatva- viśiṣṭataddhātvarthatvam | 'paśya mṛgo dhāvatī'tyatra dhāvanakriyāyā dhāvato dṛśi- phalatvavāraṇāya prathamadale tatpadam, anyathā mṛgapadād dvitīyāpattiḥ | evaṃ ca 'pacyate odanaḥ svayameva' 'ghaṭo bhavatī'tyādau āśrayatvaṃ vyāpāraṃ prakalpya phalavyāpārobhayārthatvakalpanaṃ tatra tatroktamanādeyam | bhāvayatītyādau bhavanā- śrayatvānukūlavyāpārapratīteranubhavavirahācca, bhavanānukūlavyāpārasyaiva tataḥ pratīteḥ | tathā caitanmate kevalavyāpārārthakatvamakarmakatvam | phalavyāpāro- bhayavācakatvaṃ sakarmakatvaṃ bodhyam | jīva prāṇadhāraṇe, nṛtī gātravikṣepe, saṃsudhvaṃsu adhaḥ patane ityādyanuśāsanenaitādṛśānāṃ prāṇādiviśiṣṭadhārakasaṃyoga-gātraviśiṣṭa- vikṣepādikriyāmātravācakatvenobhayavācakatvābhāvāt, viśiṣṭasyaiva kriyātvāt | dadhātyādestu saṃyogadhāraṇakriyobhayavācakatvāt sakarmakatvopapattiḥ | gātraṃ dhatta ityādiprayogopapattiśca | patadhātorapi gamivadubhayārthakatvādīdhvaṃ patati narakaṃ patita ityādiprayoganirvāha ityāhuḥ || 12 ||
phalaniyatatvāditi | phalānukūlavyāpārāsyaiva bhāvanāpadārthatvāditi bhāvaḥ | tadvyaktibhinnāniṣṭhatve sati tanniṣṭhatvamekamātraniṣṭatvam | phalavyāpārayoranyata- rādhikaraṇabhedādhikaraṇavṛttitvamityarthaḥ | māvāntarbhāvasya prayojanamāha teneti | tatra viśeṣyadalasyavaiyarthyādāha bhinnādhikaraṇeti | ātmānaṃ - sattāmātmanā - svena bibhrat - tadanukūlavyāpāravān bhavan, mniyatau sattātyāgānukūlavyāpārasye- vātra tadanukūlavyāpārasya pratātiriti bhāvaḥ | nanvevamātmānaṃ jānātītyādau jñānādirūpaphalasya kartṛmātraniṣṭhatayā sakarmakatvānupapattirityāśaṅkayāha ātmānamiti | bhāṣyokteti | tathā cāntaḥkaraṇāvacchinna ātmā kartā, śarīrā- vacchinnaḥ karma, tathā caupādhiko bheda iti bhāvaḥ | evaṃrītyā jīvatyāderna sakarmakatvam, phalāśrayāvācakatve satīti viśeṣaṇānupraveśāt | ata evāsterātma- dhāraṇānukūlavyāpārārthakatve'pyātmarūpāśrayavācakatvānna sakarmakatvamityāhurbhūṣaṇa- kṛdādayaḥ | mañjūṣākārāstu etacchāstrīyakarmasaṃjñakārthānvayyarthakatvaṃ sakarma- katvam | tena ādhyāsitā bhūmaya ityādhupapadyate | atra tu 'adhiśīṅsthā- sām ' ityādhārasya karmatvam, anvayaśca pṛthagupasthitayoḥ saṃsargarūpaḥ, anvaya- padasya tatraiva vyutpattestena jīvatyādiṣu na doṣastatra prāṇādirūpakarmaṇo dhātvarthāt pṛthuganupasthiteḥ | evaṃ cāśravācakatvarūpaviśeṣaṇānanupraveśāllāvavamapīti prāhuḥ || 13 ||
bhāṣyavirodhāditi | tādṛśabhāṣyāt kṛdantāt sattvenaiva pratīterlābhādityā- śayaḥ | mṛgo dhāvatītyādāviti | mṛgo dhāvati paśyeti sādhyasādhanarūpa- tetyanupadameva vākyapadīye'bhidhānāt | ākhyātaśabde ityasya tiṅsamudāye ityarthaḥ | bhāgadvayasattvāditi | tathā ca tatrāpi siddhāvasthāpannakriyāpratītyā- pattiḥ | sādhyatvasādhanatvābhyāṃ pratītimātre'yaṃ dṛṣṭāntaḥ | yathā pacatīti tiṅante dhāturūpaprakṛtyā sādhyatvena svārtho bodhyate, sādhanatvarūpasvārthastiṅā, tathā 'pāka' ityatra dhātunā sādhyatvena kriyā pratyāyyate, pratyayena siddhatvarūpeṇetyarthaḥ | ata evākhyātabhabde ityekavacanaṃ svarasataḥ saṃgacchate | idameva vyākhyānaṃ helā- rājīye dṛśyata iti pare | kārakatvenānvayitvamiti | kartṛtvakarmatvetarakārakatva-
vyāpyarūpeṇānvayitvamityarthaḥ | tena pacati bhavatītyādau kartṛtvakarmatvarūpeṇānvayi- tvasattve'pi nāsattvabhaṅga ityuktaṃ prāk | naivamiti | tathā ca dṛṣṭāntadārṣṭānti- kayorvaiṣamyād dṛṣṭāntatvaṃ nopapadyata iti bhāvaḥ | dṛṣṭāntadārṣṭāntikabhāve sarvāśe sāmyaṃ na niyāmakam, tathā iti candramukhādyorapi sa ma bhavet, api tu kicidaṃśe eva, tacca prakṛte'stītyāha tathāpīti | mānatvāditi | sādhyatvena pratīyamānārthe eva kārakānvayāt, prakṛte tathānupasthitau karmakatvānupapattau 'kartṛkarmaṇoḥ--' iti ṣaṣṭhyanupapattiriti bhāvaḥ | ṣaṣṭhīti | tathā ca ghañante tathā'pratītāvapyupapadya- mānayā ṣaṣṭhyā tatkalpanā na saṃbhavatīti bhāvaḥ | nanvadhyāhṛtatiṅantopasthita- kriyādvārako yogaḥ kṛdante nāstyato na ṣaṣṭhyanupapattirityābhipretyāha-na loketi | na ca kartṛkarmaṇoḥ--'iti vidhivaiyarthyam, na ca kriyāntaramādāyaiva ṣaṣṭhyupa- pādanīyā, sā ca na loketi niṣedhena na sambhatīti vācpaṃ, karttari kṛtiti sūtrabhāṣyaprāmāṇyāttumunādīnāmivaktino'pi sādhyāvasthāpannadhātvarthānuvādakatva- meveti--vastrasya kṛtirityādau caritārthatvāt | evamodanapāka ityādau samāso'pi na bhavet, asāmarthyāt | 'kṛdyogā ca ṣaṣṭhī samasyate' iti vacanasya vastrakṛtirityatra caritārthyāt | na ca śeṣaṣaṣṭhyaiva vākyaṃ samāsaśceti vācyam, nalapāka ityādau nalakartatvādiprakārakabodhasyānubhavasiddhasyānāpatteḥ, 'āścartho gāvāṃ doho'gopene't.siddhyāpatteśca | 'ibhayaprāptau--'ityasyānantarasyeti nyāyena 'kartṛkarmaṇoḥ--' ityasyaiva niyāmakatayā agopapadāt śeṣaṣaṣṭhyā durvāratvādityā- dyahyamiti vadanti | nanvevaṃ sati 'kāṣṭhaiḥ pāka' ityāpadyeta, ākhyātenaiva bhāvanā sādhyatayocyate iti mīmāsakamate tasyāmiṣṭatyādityāśaṅkāmiṣṭāpattyā pari- harati-evaṃ rītyeti | tathā ca 'nanvasattvabhūtakriyāyā' ityādipūrvapakṣe iṣṭapatti- reveti tātparyam | nanvekasyaiva dhātvarthasya viruddhasādhyatvasiddhatvābhyāṃ bhānaṃ na saṃbhavatīti cenna | yathā saṃbandhibhadādekasyaivācāryatvaṃ mātulatvaṃ ca tathaikatraiva kārakaliṅgādirūpasaṃbandhibhedādubhayasaṃbhavena tayovirodhasyaivābhāvāt | yathā vā 'paśya mṛgo dhāvatī'tyatraikasyaiva dhāvanasya mṛgaṃ prati sādhyatvaṃ, darśanaṃ prati sādhanatvaṃ tathaikasyaiva dhātvarthasya sādhyatvena prakāratā, siddhatvena viśeṣyatā | ekaniṣṭhatvācca na tayoḥ sphuṭaṃ graho bodhe | ata eva 'ghañvācyo bhāvaḥ pradhāna'miti granthāḥ saṃgacchante | 'kartari kṛt' iti sūtre bhāṣye cektaṃ 'ghañvācyo bhāvo bāhyaḥ prakṛtyarthāt' iti | ityupapadyata iti | anyathā kārakāṇāṃ
sādhyatvenopasthite evānvaya iti niyamādabhedenānvaye'pi sādhyatvenopasthiti- rapekṣiteti stokaṃ pāka iti nopadyeteti bhāvaḥ | vyāpārānvaye tu śobhanaḥ pāka ityeva | vyāpārasyākarmatayā tadviśeṣaṇe dvitīyāyā aprāpteḥ | phalānvaye dvitīyāṃ bādhitvā 'kartṛkarmaṇoḥ-- iti ṣaṣṭhī na | kartṛ sāhacaryeṇa dhātvarthe'bhedenānvayi- karmaṇa eva grahaṇāt | spaṣṭaṃ cedaṃ 'pūjanāt pūjitam--' ityatra bhāṣye ityanyatra vistaraḥ || 14 ||
mūle sādhyatveneti | tatra - ghañādyante | atra'pratīyata' iti śeṣaḥ | tadbodhakamāha-dhāturūpeti | dhāturūpaṃ nibandhanaṃ-nimittaṃ yasyā ityarthaḥ | pratītiniṣṭhaṃ nimittatvaṃ pratīyamāneupacaritamiti bahuvrīhau kriyāyāḥ prādhānyopa- pattiḥ | siddhabhāvaḥ - siddhatvena pratītiḥ |
ityanāpatteriti |dhātūpasthapyakriyāyā liṅgānanvayitayā tatra stokaradārthā- nvaye tatrāpi pūṃstvānupapattyā sāmānye napuṃsakasyaivāpattoriti bhāvaḥ | dhātvarthā- nvaye - dhātvarthaphalānvaye | prathameti | uktarītyā dvitīyāyā aprāptayā prātipadikārthamātre prathametyarthaḥ | ghaño na śaktirabhyupeyate'pi tu ghañantasya, tadantaśaktayopasthitānvaye 'stokaḥ pāka' ityupapadyata eveti 'kātantrapari- śiṣṭa'kārādayo'numanyate tanmatamāśaṅkya nirākurute na ca ghañanteti | na ca bhavatsiddhānte'pi samudāyaśaktirvṛttimātre'bhyupeyate eva, anyathārthavattvābhāvena prātipadikasaṃjñā na syāt, 'kṛttāddhita'-iti sūtrepyarthavatpadānuvṛttoruktatvāt, pratyayārthasya prakṛtyarthe evānvayādvibhaktyarthasaṃkhyādyanvayo'pi na syāditi vācyaṃ, samudāyaśakterāvaśyakatvena anuśāsanād ghañopi śaktirāvaśyakyevānyathā ṇvulāderapi śaktirna sidhyat, na ceṣṭāpattiḥ, samudāyaśaktyaiva tatrāpi kartrādibodhasaṃbhavāditi vācyaṃ, 'kartari kṛt' ityādyanuśāsanavirodhāt, na ca bhvādyantarbhāveṇa samudāyaśaktikalpanāyāṃ tanniyāmakamiti vācyaṃ, kliṣṭatvāt, sakalatāntrikasiddhāntavirodhāt, khaśādau śabanutpattyā janamejayādyasiddhayā- pattośca, khaśo ṅittvena guṇaniṣedhādityāśayenāha-digiti || 15 ||
saṃbodhanāntasyeti | saṃbhodhanavibhaktyantārthasyetyarthaḥ, arthadvārā tasyaiva vā |
nighātānurodhāditi | 'devadatto'tyasya bravaṇe'nvaye satyevaikavākyatvopapattau sattyām-'āmantritasya ca' iti nighāto 'nudāttaḥ sidhyati, nānyatheti bhāvaḥ | nanvekavākyatvābhāve'pi nighātaḥ kuto na ityata āha samānavākye iti | nimittanimittinorekavākyasthatve satītyarthaḥ | saṃbodhanapadamiti | atra puñja- rājaḥ-"ākhyātaṃ saviśeṣaṇaṃ vākyamiti samarthasūtrabhāṣyoktavākyalakṣaṇasya saṃbodhanapadārthe kriyāviśeṣaṇatvābhāve nighāto na syāt' iti | saṃbodhana- vibhakteranuvādyaviṣayatayā vidheyakriyāyāmevānvayasyaucityena nyāsiddha evāyamarthaḥ | taduktaṃ hariṇā 
'siddhasyābhimukhībhāvamātraṃ saṃbodhanaṃ viduḥ |
prāptābhimukhyo hyarthātmā kriyāsu viniyujyate ||' iti |
abhimukhībhāvaḥ - paroktavacanārthagraraṇe sādaratvābhyupagamaḥ, evaṃ ca 'rāma māṃ pāhi' ityasyābhimukhībhavadrāmoddeśyakaṃ pravartanāviṣayamatkarmakaṃ rakṣaṇamiti bodhaḥ, atra 'saviśeṣaṇa'mityanena sākṣātparāsādhāraṇaṃ viśeṣaṇatvamiṣṭaṃ, na tu sākṣādeva, tena 'nadyāstiṣṭhati kūle' ityarthe 'nadyāstiṣṭatī'tyatra samarthasūtrabhāṣyasaṃmatodā- haraṇe mighātāsiddhiḥ | vrajāni devadatto'tyatra 'jānīhī'tyadhyāhāraḥ, ata eva 'kriyāsu viniyujyate' ityanena na virodhaḥ, jñānakriyāyāṃ devadattasyoddośyatayā vrajanasya karmatayānvayena samānavākyatvopapattiḥ | vākyaikadeśanyāyena śrūyamāṇādeva jñānabodhaḥ, ayameva cādhyāhārapadārthaḥ, ato na tatra 'ekatiṅ vākyam' iti lakṣaṇasyāvyāptiriti vadanti |
anye tu saṃbodhanaṃ jñānoddośyakecchā, jñāne, icchāyāṃ ca viśakalitaśakti- dvayam, jñāne prakṛtyarthasya samavetatvasaṃbandhenānvayaḥ, prakṛtyarthānvitajñāne uddeśyatā- saṃbandhenecchāyāḥ, icchānvitajñānasya viśiṣṭaviṣayatāsaṃbandhena vākyarthe'nvayaḥ, evaṃ ca prātipadikārthasamavetajñānasyoddeśyatayā prātipadikārthasya jñānasamavāyita- yoddeśyatvarūpaṃ saṃbodhyatvaṃ prātipadikārthasya pratīyate; etadeva saṃbodhyatvamābhi- mukhyapadena 'kāśikā'dāvuktam | evaṃ ca 'rāma māṃ pāhī'tyādā'vicchāviṣaya- rāmasamavetajñānaviṣayo matkarmakatvatkartṛkāśaṃsāviṣayo rakṣaṇa'mityādyākāro bodhaḥ ityāhuḥ |
ekavākyatāsattvāditi | 'ākhyātaṃ saviśeṣaṇa'miti vākyalakṣaṇasattvā- dityarthaḥ | tatraikavākyatāyaṃ sūtrakārasaṃmatimāha 'tiṅṅatiṅaḥ' iti | ati-
ṅpadaghaṭitaṃ sūtraṃ kurvatetyarthaḥ | 'pacati bhavatī'tyādau tiṅantāt parasya nighātavāraṇaprayojanakamatiṅgrahaṇamuktasthale vākyatvābhāve'narthakaṃ sattiṅanta- samudāye'pyekavākyatvaṃ sūtrakṛdabhimatamiti sūcayatīti bhāvaḥ | ekatiṅiti | ekaṃ tiṅ - tiṅantaṃ yatreti bahuvrīhiḥ, tathā caikatiṅantaghaṭitasamudāyo vākya- mityarthaḥ | atrāpi 'viśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭakriyārthakatvaṃ' viśeṣaṇaṃ niveśanīyaṃ, tena 'tiṣṭhati devadattaḥ' 'pacati maitra' ityatra devadattaḥ pacatītyamanayorekavākyaṃ na, tathā sati 'pacatī'tyasya nighātāpattiḥ, na ceṣṭāpattiḥ, 'āśyātaṃ saviśeṣaṇa'miti vākyalakṣaṇamiti patre'niṣṭatvāt, ata eva ca 'tiṅṅatiṅaḥ' iti sūtre 'pacati karotīti pratīkamupādāya 'vināpi caśabdena samuccayāvahamāt saṃbandhasadbhāvaḥ' ityuktaṃ 'kaiyaṭīye', 'kriyākarmabhāvenāpyanvayaḥ suvacaḥ' ityuktaṃ 'pradīpoddayote' | mate param - mate eva, netyatra 'nighāta' ityanuṣajyate, saṃbhavati mataikye matabhedo na nyāyya ityāśayenāha-vastutastviti | ekatiṅviśeṣyakamiti | ekatiṅ - tiḍantaṃ-viśeṣyaṃ - mukhyaviśeṣyārthapratipādakaṃ yatretyarthaḥ, tathā ca mukhyaviśeṣyī- bhūtārthabodhakaikatiḍantaghaṭitasamudāyatvamiti paryavasannam | pare tu 'ekatiṅ vākyam iti svaśāstrīyanighātādikāryamātropayogi, tena 'paśya nṛgo dhāvatī'tyādāvetadasattve'pi na kṣatiḥ, ata evaitadāśritya 'tiṅṅatiṅaḥ' iti sūtrasthamatiṅagrahaṇaṃ pratyākhyātamaṣṭame bhāṣye, evaṃ coktasthale 'devadatto'tyasya nidhātābhāva eva labhyate, yathottaraṃ mūnīnāṃ prāmāṇyāditi prāhuḥ | tadarthāditi | 'pañcakṛtvo bhūṅkte' ityādau pañcādiśabdāstatsaṃkhyotpattiparāḥ, tadadyotaka eva pratyayaḥ, tādṛśotpatteśca kriyāyāmevānvaya ucitaḥ,-'saṃkhyāyāḥ kriyābhyāvṛttigaṇane kṛtvasuc' iti sūtrasvārasyāditi bhāvaḥ | tatrābhyāvṛttipadaṃ punaḥ punarjanmārthakaṃ bhujikriye'ti bodhaḥ | atra sūtre'kriyā'padaṃ spaṣṭārtha, dravyāderupatterekatvena tasya gaṇanayogyajanmāprasiddheḥ, anekasyā api kriyāyāstu nivṛttabhedāyā dhātuvācya- tāyāstādṛśajanmasaṃbhavaḥ, saṃkhyāyāstattasaṃkhyāvattā bodhanarūpagaṇane hetutayā gaṇanalābhe 'gaṇana'padaṃ gaṇanāyāṃ ye prasiddhā ekādayasteṣāmeva parigrahārtha, tena yugmobhādiśabdā neti spaṣṭamākare iti dik |
kriyānvayināmeveti | 'dhruvamapāye ' ityatra 'kriyāyā'mityanuvṛttau kriyāyāṃ janakākāṅkṣayā kriyāyāṃ yadapāye dhruvamityanena kriyājanakamityarthasaṃpattyā janakasya janyākāṅkṣayā tatraivānvaya ucita iti
tadanvayināmevāpādānādisaṃjñetyarthaḥ | ityuktatvāt | yathā'brāhmaṇavadadhīte' ityatra brāhmaṇaśabdastatkartṛkādhyayanaparaḥ, 'devadattavanmaitraḥ suṃdara' ityatra 'bhavatī'tyadhyā- hāro bodhyaḥ, anyathā kriyāgrahaṇasya nirarthakattāpatteḥ, evaṃ ca tadarthasya kriyaivāśraya iti spaṣṭameva | 'mathurāvat pāṭalipūtre prākāra' ityatra vatyarthasya prākāre evānvayena kriyāyāmananvayāt 'prathamo vati'rityabhidadhau | tatra mathurāśabdasya tatsaṃbandhiprākāre vṛttiḥ, ṣaṣṭhyarthasaptamyantādeva pratyayo na tvadhikaraṇasaptamyantā- divārthenāsāmarthyāditi spaṣṭaṃ śekharādau, vistarastata evāvagantavyaḥ |
teṣāṃ vidhānāditi | 'yāhi yāhīti yāti', atra loḍantārthasya 'yātī'ti- padopasthitakriyāyāṃ sāmānyaviśeṣabhāvenānvayaḥ | bṛhadbhūṣaṇe tu 'bhoktuṃ pacatī'- tyudāhṛtaṃ, tatra yadyapi 'tumunṇghalau ' iti vihitastumun na dhātusaṃbandhe ityadhikāre vihitastathāpi kriyāvācinyupapade eva vihitatvāt kriyāsaṃbandha- nimittakatvamakṣatamevetyudāharaṇasaṃgatiḥ | nanu 'ghaṭo na paṭa'ityatrā'samastanañaḥ kriyāyāmananvayena niyamāsaṃgatirityāśaṅkamāno'samastanañityasya vivakṣitārtha- māha samāsāyogya iti | prasajyeti | prasājya-pratiṣedhapadāyoḥ 'supsupe'ti samāse tamarhatītyarthe 'tadarhati' iti sūtreṇa chaḥ, kriyānvayayogya ityarthaḥ | tadanvayāyogyaḥ - uttarapadārthānanvitaḥ | tathā coktam 
'prasajyapratiṣadhastu kriyayā saha yatra nañ |
paryudāsaḥ sa vijñeyo yatrottarapadena nañ ||' iti |
vyākhyāprayojanaṃ darśayati uttarapadārtheti | uttarapadārthānvaye'pi asamastatvāditi yojanā | asamastatve hetumāha samāsavikalpeneti | nanu 'ghaṭo nāstī'tyatra ghaṭabhāva eva pratīyate na tu kriyābhāvaḥ, na ca bhūtalādhikaraṇaghaṭakartṛkasattāyā evābhāvo nañā bodhyata iti vācyam, anubhava- virodhāt, yatra bhūtale vartamānakālāvacchedena ghaṭasaṃbandhābhāvastatra tadadhikaraṇa- kavartamānakālikasattayā aprasiddhyātadabhāvabodhanāsaṃbhavāccetyāśayenāśaṅkate-na cāsamastetyādinā | samādhatte-na tvamityādinā | kriyāyā eva niṣedha iti | ata eva yuṣmadādisāmānādhikaraṇyena madhyamapurūṣādyupapattiḥ, anyathā 'tvadabhāvo' 'madabhāvo'stī'tivat 'tvaṃ na pacasī'tyādāvapi yuṣmadasmatsāmānādhikaraṇya- virahānmadhyamottamayoranupapattiḥ, prathamasyāpattiśca syāditi bhāvaḥ | carame kriyāniṣedhapratītau yuktimāha ata eveti | jananakriyāyā abhāvapratītereva |
vidyamāne'pi ghaṭe - ghaṭasya vidyamānatādaśāyāmapi | ghaṭasya vidyamānatve ghaṭābhāvabodhanasyā'śakyatayā tatra kriyāniṣedhabodho'vaśyamabhyupagantavya iti bhāvaḥ | yadyapyavidyamānattādaśāyāmapi ghaṭātyantābhāve ni ye jananāśrayatvasya bādhād ghaṭābhāvabodhanaṃ na saṃbhavati, atastatrāpi kriyāyā eva niṣedhabodho'bhyupagantavyaḥ, evaṃ ca vidyamānatvoktinirathikā, tathāpyutpādavināśa- yogyabhāvābhyupagamapakṣe ghaṭābhāve jananāśrayatvabodhaḥ saṃbhavatīti tasya vidyamāna- tvānusaraṇam | tathā ceti | 'tvaṃ na pacasī'tyādāvuktakrameṇa kriyāniṣedhāvaśya- katve cetyarthaḥ | astitvabhāva eveti | ghaṭakartṛkasattāyā evetyarthaḥ | evena ghaṭābhāvavyāvṛttiḥ | nanu pūrvoktasthale anupapattyā kriyāniṣedhapratītisvīkāre'pi 'ghaṭo nāstī'tyādau dhaṭādyabhāvapratītireva svīkriyatāṃ, vādhakābhāvāt, tathā ca tatraivoktaniyamabhaṅgha ityāśaṅkyāha na hīti | tathā ca tathābhyupagame vairūpyaṃ syāt, tacca na yuktamiti bhāvaḥ | yadadhikaraṇakaghaṭādikartṛkavartamānasatā aprasiddhā tatrāpyaropitādirūpā sāstyeveti noktadoṣa iti bhāvaḥ |
nanu 'bhūtale na ghaṭa' iti kriyāvācakapadarahitavākye ghaṭe bhūtalavṛttitvābhāva eva bodhanīyastathā ca tatraiva niyamabhaṅga ityata āha tathā ca bhūtala iti | adhyāhāre hetumāha prakārateti | prakāratāsaṃbandhena nañarthaviśeṣyakaśābdabuddhitvā- vacchinnaṃ prati bhāvanāniṣṭhaviṣayatāsaṃbandhena dhātujanyopasthitirheturiti hetuhetu- madbhāvaḥ paryavasannaḥ, nañartha viṣayatāsaṃbandhena tādṛśabuddherjananād vyabhicāra- stadvāraṇāya prakāratātvena viṣayatāyā niveśaḥ | prakāratā cābhāvatvāvacchinnaviṣaya- tānirūpitā bodhyā, nāto 'ghaṭo nāstī'ti vākyajanyabodhīyaprakāratāyā dhaṭādau sattve'pi vyabhicāratādavasthyam | klṛptatvāditi | etādṛśakāryakāraṇa- bhāvānaṅgīkāre uktaniyammirvāha iti tasyāvaśyakatvādityarthaḥ | śeṣam - āropitatvaṃ nañdyotyamityādi || 16 ||
'tathā yasya ca bhāvena ṣṭī ce'tyatra 'yasya ca bhāvene'tyanena tadghaṭitaṃ, 'ṣaṣṭhī ce'tyanena ca tacchabdaghaṭitaṃ sūtraṃ lakṣyata ityāśayena vyācaṣṭe 'yasya ca bhāvena bhāvalakṣaṇamityatreti 'ṣaṣṭī cānādare' itīti ca | tathā ca 'goṣu duhyamānāsu gata' ityatrādyasūtreṇa saptamī, 'rudati rudato vā prāvrājī'dityatra
dvitīyena ṣaṣṭīsaptamyau ca kriyāyoge eva sādhutvenānvākhyāyete iti samudi- tārthaḥ | 'goṣu duhyamānā'svityatra goradottarasaptamyā jñāpyajñāpakabhāvātmakaḥ saṃbandho'rthaḥ | duhyamānānāṃ gavāṃ jñāpakatvaṃ jñāpyatvaṃ ca gamanakriyāyāṃ śabdataḥ pratīyate, paryavasānaṃ tu tadgatadohanakriyāyāṃ, nirjñātakālakriyāyā evānirjñāta- kālakriyāyāṃ svasahacaritakālāvaccinnatvajñāpatvāt | evamaparatrāpi rudatītyādau anādaraḥ putrādiviṣayako'dhiko bhāsata iti bodhyam |
vakṣyata iti | 'asādhuranumānene'tyetadvyākhyāvasare 'anapabhnaṣṭatā- nādi'rityādinā vakṣyataṃ ityarthaḥ | nanu kriyāśabdasya dhātvarthamātre prasiddhatayā phalānvayenaivoktāṣṭakasya satdhutvalābhe sati na bhāvanānvayaniyama iti na tadanurodhena 'pāka'ityādau bhāvanāyā vācyatāvaśyakatetyāśaṅkāṃ nirākurute ayaṃ bhāva ityādinā | saṃjñāśabdasyānapekṣapravṛttakatvena balavattve nāvamikādhikaraṇaṃ pramāṇayati ata eveti | mirṇītamiti | navamādhyāye 'yadyonyāṃ gāyati taduttarayoḥ' iti vacanasthottarāśabdena sthantarayonyuttarayoruttarāgranthapaṭhitayoścotta- rayorgrahaṇamutottarārganthapaṭhitayoreveti saṃdehe,-uttaraśabdasya 'praśastataramuttaraṃ barhiṣaḥ sādayati' ityādau saṃbandhiśabdatvadarśanādyaiva yoneḥ parataḥ paṭhitā saivottarā, tataśca yonigranthe rathantarayoneḥ parato bṛhadyoneḥ pāṭhāt tasyāmṛci sthantarasya gānam, uttarāgranthe ca rathantarayoneḥ parato 'na tvā vāmanyo divyo na pārthivaḥ' ityasyāmapi vikalpeneti prāpte-uttarāśabdasya uttarāgranthapaṭhitottarāsveva vaidikaprayogādasaṃbandhikatayā rūḍho'yaṃ śabda iti niścayaḥ, tataścottarāśabde- nātrottarāgranthe paṭhitayoreva jhaṭityupasthitiḥ kṛteti tatraiva sthantaraṃ geyaṃ na bṛhadyonau iti siddhāntitam | evaṃ ca tatra yathā saṃbandhiśabdasya tattatsaṃbandhipratītisāpekṣapravṛttikṛtaṃ durbalatvaṃ, saṃjñāśabdasya nirapekṣapravṛttikṛtaṃ prabalatvaṃ, tathā prakṛte, yogārthasāpekṣapravṛttikayaugikāpekṣayā saṃjñāśabdasya tadanapekṣapravṛttikatayā prabalatvam | vilambelopasthāpakatvaśīghropasthāpakatva- yordaubalyaprāvalye prayojake iti paryavasānāditi bhāvaḥ |
nanu phalasyāpi 'karmavat karmaṇā ' iti sūtre kriyāśabdena vyavahārāt, sādhyatvena pratīyamānatvādastyādau sattādaireva kriyātvopapādanācca tasyāpi sarvatra kriyāśabdena grahaṇe bādhakabhāva ityāśayenāha kiṃ ca phalāṃśeti | kvacit tathā- bhūtāni - devadattastaṇḍulaṃ pacatī'tyādau kārakāṇi bhāvanāṃ prati
viśeṣaṇatāpannānītyarthaḥ | kṛtandate kārakāṇāṃ bhāvanāṃ prati viśeṣyatvāt kkaciditi | bhāvanāyāṃ kārakānvaye viśeṣātideśādhyāyādhikaraṇanyāyaṃ sādhakatayopanyasyati-na hi sikṣuka iti | tathā ca kārakāṇāṃ nityaparatantrāṇāṃ viśeṣyākāṅkṣāyāṃ phalāṃśasya tathānvayayogyatve'pi viśeṣyakāṅkṣāyāṃ tasyāpi na hi bhikṣuka iti nyāyena bhāvanāyāmevānvayamaṅgīkurvanti nyāyavidaḥ, tattulyatayā kārakāṇāmapi bhāvanāyāmevānya ucita iti tātparyārthaḥ | evaṃ ca - bhāvanāyāmeva kārakānvayaniyame ca | kārakādītyādipadena dhātvarthaphalāṃśaparigrahaḥ | tasyā- meva - viśeṣaṇībhūtabhāvanāyāmeva | evena phalāṃśavyavacchedaḥ |
nanu viśeṣaṇatvāviśeṣāt phale evaudanasya paktetyādau kārakānvayo'stu, evaṃ ca tadanurodhāt phalopasthiterevaṃ kārakānvayabodhaṃ prati hetutāyāḥ tkṛptatvādodanasya pāka ityādāvapi phale'nvayo nānupapanna iti cenna, kārakībhūtadhātvarthasya bhāvanāyāmananvayāpatteḥ, na hi svayameva svakārakaṃ, phalāṃśasya bhāvanāṃ prati karaṇatvenānvaye tatra kārakānvaye vājapeyādhikaraṇabhaṅgāpattyā karaṇāntaropa- saṃgraheṇa prayogavidherabodhakatāpatteścetyādikaṃ bṛhadbhūṣaṇe'nusaṃdheyamityāśayenāha bhūṣaṇe prapañcitamiti | tasmāt pāka ityādāvodanasyetyādikārakānvayasiddhaye bhāvanāyā vācyatvamāvaśyakamiti siddham |
prakārāntareṇa kārakāṇāṃ bhāvanānvayaniyamaṃ sādhayatāmanyeṣāṃ vaiyākaraṇānāṃ matamāha kecitviti | anvayabodhākāṅkṣānivṛttyoriti | anvayabodhasyā- kāṅkṣānivṛtteścetyarthaḥ | 'bhūtale ghaṭa' ityatra bhūtalanirūpitamādheyatvamekatva- viśiṣṭo ghaṭa ityanvayabodhasya paramate, svamate ca bhūtalābhinnamadhikaraṇa- mityādyanvabodhasya, devadatto ghaṭamityatra ghaṭavṛttikarmatvamekatvaviśiṣṭo devadatta ityanvayabodhasya paramate, svamate ca ghaṭābhinnaṃ kamaṃtyādyanvayabodhasyodaye'pi vākyaghaṭakayāvatpadārthānāṃ parasparamanvayabodhānudaya iti bodhyam | na ca kriyā- rūpārthādhyāhāreṇaiva śābdabodhādeḥ saṃbhavādalaṃ tadvācakapadādhyāhāreṇeti vācyam, padajanyapadārthopasthitereva śāhdopayogitvāt padānāṃ saṃbhūyānvayabodhakatva- vyutpatteśca ityāhurityasvarasasūcanam, tadvījaṃ tu phalānvayaniyamenāpyupapattau bhāvanānvayaniyamālābha iti || 17 ||
svayamupapattimiti | 'bhūtale na ghaṭa'ityato bhūtalavṛttitvābhāvavān ghaṭa iti
bodhodayānna kriyāpadāpekṣā, evaṃ 'parvato vahnimān bhavitumarhati dhūmānmahānasavat' iti vedāntyuktanyāyāntargatapratijñāvākye kriyāpadaprayogo vṛtheti kkacit tarkagranthe sthitaṃ taddūṣayannupapattimāhetyarthaḥ | nañādiṣvityādipadāgrāhyamāha bhūtale ghaṭa ityādipadāditi | caturthyāḥ spṛhīti | 'puṣpebhya' iti caturthyantamātraprayoge 'spṛhayatī'ti śabdādhyāhāra eva caturthyāḥ sādhutvārthamanusriyate, na tu tatsamānārtha- kadhātvantaraghaṭipadādhyāhāro, nāpyarthādhyāhāro laiyāyikaistat tyajyatāmityarthaḥ |
ardhajaratīyamiti | jaratyā ardhamityardhajaratī tatsadṛśamarghajaratīyam | 'samāsācca tadviṣayāt' iti chaḥ | yathā jaratyā ardha kāmayate'rdhana tathetyarthaḥ || 18 ||
vivicya - pṛthagbhūya | itarāviśeṣaṇatveneti yāvat | ityatrāpi | guṇabhūteti ktapratyayaḥ karmasāmānye iti pakṣe | 'tatra bhāva' iti pakṣe'pi tatraiva viśeṣaṇatāmāpannetyarthaḥ | kathaṃ tatrānvaya itīti | padārthaḥ padārthenaiveti vyutpattestatrānvayasyāsaṃbhavāditi bhāvaḥ | anvayābhyupagamāditi | idaṃ ca samarthasūtre bhāṣye spaṣṭam | harirapyāha 
'saṃbandhiśabdaḥ sāpekṣā nityaṃ sarvaḥ samasyate |
vākyavat sā vyapekṣā hi vṛttāvapi na hīyate ||' iti |
samāse tadghaṭakapūrvapade vā prācīnatākikamate śakterlakṣaṇāyāścābhāvena gururna padārthaikadeśa iti sthalāntaramanusarati caitrasya napteti | janyaśarīra- janyaśarīraṃ naptṛpadārthaḥ | tadekadeśajanyatve caitranirūpitatvādirūpaṣaṣṭhya- ntārthānvayaḥ | na ca ṣaṣṭhyarthasaṃbandhasya viśeṣye evānvayo'stu kimekadaśe'nvaya- svīkāreṇeti vācyam, caitrasya putre'pi tathāvidhaprayogāpatteḥ, tasyāpi pitāmahajanyajanyatvāt, caitranirūpitajanyatvātmakasaṃbandhāśrayatvācca, na ca prayojyatvaṃ paramparājanyatvameva saṃbandhastatra ṣaṣṭhyartha ityabhyupagamānna prayogāti- prasaṅga ityata āha ityādāviveti | ādinā 'ghaṭādanyaḥ' 'ghaṭāt pṛthagi'tyādiparigrahaḥ | anyapadārthaikadeśe bhede pañcamyantārthaghaṭapratiyogitvādeḥ, pṛthakpadārthaikadeśe pṛthaktve pañcamyantārthaghaṭāvadhitvāderanvaya iṭhṭa iti bhāvaḥ | yadyapi sarvatraikadeśaghaṭitaparamparāsaṃbandhenānvaya iti pakṣāntaramapi samarthasūtrabhāṣye
ukta, harirapyāhaḥ 
'samudāyena saṃbandho yeṣāṃ gurukulādinā |
saṃspṛśyāvayavāṃste tu yujyante saha ||' iti |
tathāpyuktasthale ekadeśe evākāṅkṣāsattvādanubhavānurodhācca prathamapakṣa evātrorarīkṛtaḥ | ata eva devadattasya gurukulamityādivākyajanyaśābdabodhānantaraṃ gururdevadattīyo na vā, janyatvaṃ caitrīyaṃ na vetyādisaṃśayānutpattiścāñjasyena saṃgacchate | viśeṣye evaikadeśaghaṭitaparamparāsaṃbandhenānvaya iti pakṣe tu 'bhāva- nāyāmevānvaya' ityasya bhāvanāyāmeva sākṣāt saṃbandha ityarthaḥ | sa ca viśeṣyabhūtabhāvanāyāṃ saṃbandhaghaṭitāyāṃ vā, evaṃ ca tādṛśaniyamena bhāvanāghaṭitasaṃbandhaṃ vinānyatrānanvaya eva vyāvartyata iti bodhyam | uktamarthamanyatrāpyatidiśati evamiti | bhoktu pāka iti | 'tumunṇvulau ' iti sūtreṇa vihitasya tumunaḥ kriyāyoge eva vidhānādasattvabhūtadhāūpasthāpitakriyāyā- manvayāt sādhutvaṃ bodhyamityarthaḥ | evaṃ 'bhuktvā pāka' ityatrāpi ktvāpratyaya- syāpīti bhāvaḥ | bhāṣyapradīpoddayotakṛtastu 'upapadāmatiṅ' 'gatikārakopa- padāt ' iti sūtrabhāṣyād bhoktuṃ pāka ityāderasādhutvaṃ labhyate tathā hi 'upapadam ' iti bhāṣye hi tatrātiṅgrahaṇasya 'kārako vrajatī'tyādike pratyudāharaṇa supsupeti vartata ityuktvā svaṇḍite tiṅityanena tiṅantārtho lakṣaṇayā gṛhyate, tathā ca pradhānabhūtatadarthakriyāvācakaśabdasya samāsapratiṣedho- 'tiṅityanena tiṅantārtho lakṣaṇayā gṛhyate tathā ca pradhānabhūtatadarthakriyāvācaka- śabdasya samāsapratiṣedho'tiṅityanena kriyate | evaṃ ca 'supsupe'tyasyānuvṛttāvapi 'kārako gata' ityātadvyāvartya bhaviṣyatīti tātparyakam 'atiṅiti tadarthapratiṣedhaḥ' ityuktam, tadantaraṃ co'papadamakriye'ti spaṣṭapratipattaye 'vaktavyam' ityaktaṃ, tasyāpyukta evārthaḥ | 'atha 'akriyā' ityasya kiṃ pratyudāharaṇam' iti praśnottaraṃ pratyudāharaṇe pradarśite 'naitat kriyāvāci' iti tatkhaṇḍanaṃ kṛtaṃ, tasyāyamāśayaḥ,- aya 'tamunṇvulau iti sūtre ṇvuleva na bhavati, tatra kriyārthāyām iti strīliṅganirdeśena tatphalakriyāyā eva lābhasaṃbhave kriyāgrahaṇasāmarthyāt pratyāsattyā ca kriyāyoḥ sajātīyatvalābhe pratyayārthaviśeṣaṇatānāśrayasādhyatā- mātrāpannakriyāvācakapade eva tumunādividhānāt ata eva siddhānte tatra na samāsāpattiḥ | kiṃ ca kriyāvācakatvābhāvāt samāsapratiṣedho na, tumunṇghulau tu bhavata ityasya vaiṣamyasyānucitatvaṃ ca | na ca gataśabdasyānupapadatvānna samāsaḥ, tasya prādhānyena kriyāvācakatvābhāvāt, dhātostu kṛdarthasaṃsṛṣṭārthakatayā niṣkṛṣya padārthāntareṇaikārthībhāvāsaṃbhānna sa iti vācyaṃ 'yena vidhistadantasya'
'anupasarjanāt' 'samarthaḥ padavidhiḥ' iti sūtrabhāṣyavirodhāt, tatra hi niṣkṛṣye- kārthībhāvavirahe'pi taddhitasamāsavṛtterabhyupagamāt, tathā 'yena vidhiḥ ' iti sūtrabhāṣye paramagārgyasyāpatyamityarthe gārgyaśabdāt pratyayagrahaṇaparibhāṣayā yañantatvaprayukto 'yañiñośca' iti phagityuktam |
'anupasarjanāt' ityatraitadadhikārābhāve tadantavidhyabhāvena kumbhakāraikadeśa- kāraśabdasyaivāṇantatayā tata eva ṅīpi anupasarjanastrīpratyaye tadādiniyama- pratipedhai'pi kadācitkārīśabdād ḍhak syādityuktam |
'gatikārakopapadāt ' ityatra bhāṣye tatra śūtre kṛdagrahaṇābhāve tiṅantena samāsābhāvādyatkriyāyuktā ityena niṣkauśāmbyādervāraṇād, gatyādibhyaḥ parasya kriyāvācakṛdantasyaivottarapadasya lāsaṃbhave kṛdagrahaṇaṃ vyarthamityāśaṅkāyāṃ yatkiyetyasya prabhavanamityatraiva syāt, kriyāyāḥ prādhānyāt, na tu 'praṇāyakaḥ' 'praṇī'rityādau kriyāviśeṣaṇabodhajanake'taḥ kṛdagrahaṇaṃ kṛdantamātrasaṃgrahārtha- mityuktam | tadupādanasāmarthyādviśeṣaṇībhūtakriyake'pi pravṛttiriti bhāvaḥ | kriyāvācakaśabdagrahaṇe pradhānībhūtasādhyamātrāvasthakriyāgrahaṇaṃ kṛdagrahaṇaṃ jñāpayati, kriyārūpārthagrahaṇe tu atādṛśyā api tasyā grahaṇamataḥ kārakavibhakti- kṛtvasujādayaḥ siddhāvasthāpannaguṇībhūtakriyāyāmapi bhavanti, nanvevaṃ prapacatitarā- mityādāvuttarapadaprakṛtisvarānāpattiḥ, kṛdagrahaṇāsattve tu sādhyāvasthāpannakriyāmātra- bodhakottarapadasyāsaṃbhavādadhikārthe praṇāyakādāvivātrāpi svarapravṛttirniṣpratyūhaiva, prabhavanamityatrāpi na sādhyāvasyaiva kriyā pratīyate | taduktaṃ bhāṣye 'yadi kṛdagrahaṇaṃ kriyate āmante svaro na prāpnoti, ityādīti cenna | 'tiṅi codāttavatī'ti sūtrasthatiṅgrahaṇapratyākhāyādanyayapūrvapadaprakṛtisvaraṃ paratvādvādhitvā 'pre'tyasya nidhātapravṛttau nārthastatrottarapadaprakṛtisvarapravṛttyā, tasmāt kṛdagrahaṇaṃ kartavyam' ityanantaraṃ 'yadi kṛdagrahaṇaṃ kriyate āmante svaro na prāpnoti' ityādiḥ sarvo'pi granthaḥ śaṅkānta eva, samādhistvayameva nigūḍhaḥ | evaṃ ca bhoktuṃ pāka ityadyapyaniṣṭameveti prāhuḥ | pūrvottarakāle iti | kriyārthakriyāvācake upapade, arthāt phalībhūtakriyāvācakadhātostumunaṇbulau vidhīyete, phalībhūtā ca kriyā uttarakālavṛttireveti bhāvaḥ |
dvirvacanamityāditi | ādipadād 'dviraṇivadhirdvirāvṛṣprayoga' ityādeḥ parigrahaḥ | atiprasaṃṅgaḥ - dviḥpākāvityādiprayogātiprasaṅgaḥ | anabhidhānā-
diti | tādṛśaprayogāt kriyājanmagaṇanāpratīterityarthaḥ | taduktaṃ bhāṣye dvau pākāvityatra kasmānna bhavati, netat kriyāgaṇanam, abhimukhī pravṛttirabhyā- vṛtti'riti, sā ca janmarūpā, evaṃ naitat kriyājanmagaṇanamiti tadarthaḥ | kriyājanmagaṇanārthakatve tu dvau pākāviti dvivacanāpattiḥ | ata eva dviṣprayoga ityatra dvivacanaṃ na |
dviṣprayogo dvirvacanamiti prayujyata iti prayogaḥ, evañca sādhyamātrasvabhāvā dhātūpasthāpyā kriyā tatreti tatsamānārthakadvirvacanaśabde'pi dhātūpāttā tādṛśyeva kriyā, pratyayastu bāhulakāt karmaṇi | atra āhurityanenāsvarasaḥ sūcitaḥ, tadvījaṃ tu bhāvārthakadvirāvṛttirityādestathāpyanirvāhaḥ, 'dvirvacane'ci' ityādiprayoga- darśanāt kriyāmātragrahaṇasyaiva nyāyyatvam | kriyāgrahaṇaṃ tu spaṣṭārthamuttarārthaveti spaṣṭamanyatreti || 20 ||
ākhyātaśaktigrahavatāmiti | 'tibādipratyaye' bhāvanāśaktā iti śakrigrahavatām | bodhāt - tibādipratyayād bhāvanābodhāt | tannibandhanam | viśeṣyaviśeṣaṇabhāvasaṃbandhanimittakamityarthaḥ | yathāśvatvajātipravṛttinimittako dantyasakāraghaṭito'svaśabdasturage'sādhurdhanarāhityapravṛttinimittakastu sādhuḥ, tāla- vyaśakāraghaṭitastu tatraiva vaiparītyena sādhurasādhuśca bhavati | nanu vyākaraṇaṃ sādhutvānvākhyāyakameva na tu śakrigrāhakamata eva 'atha śabdānuśāsanam' ityeva mahābhāṣye uktam, 'siddhe śabdārthasaṃbandhe lokato'rthaprayukte śabdaprayoge śāstreṇa dharmaniyamaḥ' iti ca, na tvarthabodhakamityādi, 
'sādhutvajñānaviṣayā saiṣā vyākaraṇasmṛtiḥ |'
