iti vākyapadīye'pi 'sādhutvajñānaviṣayā' ityevoktamityāśaṅkāṃ nirākurvastātparyārthamāha ayamartha iti | ayam - vakṣyamāṇaḥ | tatraiva - sādhuśabde eva avacchedakatayā | bodhakatāvacchedakatayā | śaktitvamiti | bodhakatā - bodhajanakatā | janakatāyāścāvacchinnatvaniyamena tadavacchedakatayā dharmakalpanāvaśyakīti tasyaiva śaktitvaṃ yuktam | punaḥ śabdastvarthe | śaktatvaṃ sādhutvamityekamevetīti | sādhūśabdebhya eva bodhotpattyaṅgīkāreṇa śaktaḥ sādhumātravṛttitayā śaktatvameva sādhutvamiti bhāvaḥ | tadrītyā - mīmāṃsakarītyā |
sādhutvanirṇaya eveti | tathā ca vyākaraṇasya śaktinirṇāyakatvasya niṣpratyūhatayā 'laḥ karmaṇi ' iti vyākaraṇavirodhād bhāvanābhiprāyeṇa tiṅprayogo'sādhureveti tātparyam | atirikteti | sādhutvāpekṣayā'tiriktetyarthaḥ | sādhutvamanapabhraṣṭate- tyādirūpaṃ, śaktiricchāviśeṣaḥ, padārthāntaraṃ vā | ata eva sādhuḥ śakta ityādi- vyavahāra upapadyata iti bhāvaḥ | syādeveti | vyākaraṇasmṛteḥ sādhutvajñāpanārthaṃ pravṛttatve'pi sādhutvasya pūrvoktarītyārthaviśeṣanibandhanatvenārthavaśasaṃpannameva śakti- nirṇāyakatvamiti vyākaraṇavirodhāccaturthyarthe tṛtīyāprayogavad bhāvanāyāṃ tivādi- pratyayasyāsādhutvaṃ syādeveti tātparyārthaḥ |
nanvevaṃ mahāmunijaiminisūtravirodhena pāṇinismṛtirbādhyeti cenna, sādhutvāṃśe pāṇinismṛterbalavattvāt, sādhutvānuśāsanārthameva praṇītatvāt | vastutastu jaiminīyavacanavyākhyātṛgranthavirodhe'pi tatsūtravirodhaleśo'pi nāvarati, tathā hi 'bhāvārthāḥ karmaśabdāstebhyaḥ kriyā pratīyetaiṣa hyartho vidhīyate'iti hi bhāvārthādhikaraṇe jaiminisūtram | "dadyājjuhuyādityādayaḥ karmaśabdā bhāvārthāḥ - prādhānyena kriyārthakāḥ, 'bhāvapradhānamākhyātam' iti niruktāt, atastebhyaḥ kriyā pratīyeta prādhānyena pratīyeta, hī - prādhānyāddhetoḥ | eṣa eva - kriyārūpa evārtho vidhīyate na tu dravyarūpaḥ kriyāyāścāśutaravināśitvāt kālāntarabhāvi- phalajanakatā na saṃbhavatīti dvāramapūrva kalpya"miti tadarthaḥ | etatsūtrād bhāvanā pratyayārtho na kartetyādikaṃ tu na labhyate iti vibhāvanīyamiti dik | prakāraṇāt - darśapūrṇamāsaprakaraṇāt | tatprakaraṇe nānṛtamityasya pāṭhāt | tacca - prakaraṇaprāptaṃ kratvarthatvaṃ ca | bādhyeteti | tathā ca kratāvapabhāṣaṇe kratubhreṣa- prāyaścitācaraṇarūpasakalayājñikamīmāṃsakaśiṣṭācāravirodhaḥ syāt, ato neṣṭāpattiḥ kartu śakyeti bhāvanāvācakatvameva vibādīnāmabhyupeyam | na caivaṃ 'laḥ karmaṇi ' iti vyākaraṇasyāprāmāṇyamiti vācyam, vṛddhayādipadasya loke ākārādibodhakatvenādṛṣṭasyāpi svaśāstre tatra vṛddhayādipadaṃ paribhāṣitam, evaṃ ca vyākaraṇasya sarvaśaktinirṇāyakatvābhāvenādoṣāt, kiṃ cāsanneva lakāraḥ kalpita- stathaiva kartṛkarmavācakatvasyāpi kālpanikasyāpi saṃbhavāt, yatra tu nyāyavirodha- statra lokavedayorapi na taduktārthābhyupagamaḥ, na caitāvatāpi śāśtrasyāprāmāṇyam, arthavādānāmiva tātparyārthe prāmāṇyanirvāhāt, 'jañjabhyamāno brūyānmayi dakṣakratū' ityādau vākyāt puruṣārthatvasiddhaye karturvācyatvamāvaśyakamityuktā pratibandirapi
neti mīmāṃsakoktamevāśrayituṃ yuktamiti bhāvaḥ | tiṅarthasya viśeṣaṇatve'pi śrutyarthatvānapāyāt kathaṃ na niṣedhasya kratvarthatvamatastadupapādayati na hi guṇeti | viśeṣye evaṅgitvasya nyāyyatvād viśeṣaṇe'ṅgasaṃbandhe prādhānyamaprādhānyaṃ ca viruddhadvayamāpadyeta, ata eva 'somena yajete'tyatra somasya yāge karaṇatvenānvayo nirākṛtaḥ somasādhyena yāgeneti mīmāṃsakaiḥ . nanu dhātvarthabhāvanāpi kathaṃ niṣedhenānvayaṃ labhate, tasyā api pratyayārthakartṛsambandhatvādityāśaṅkyāha bhāvanātviti | pradhānamiti prathamāntaṃ bhāvanāviśeṣaṇaṃ, tathā ca viśeṣaṇatve- netarānvitasyānyatra viśeṣaṇatayānvayāsaṃve'pi tiṅartha prati viśeṣyāyāstasyā nañarthe viśeṣaṇatayānvayo nānupapanna iti sā grahītuṃ samartheveti bhāvaḥ | evaṃ ca niṣedhasya tadbhāvanāyā vā kaimarthakyāt prakaraṇāt kratvarthakyāt prakaraṇāt kratvarthataiveti bhāvaḥ | śrutiprakaraṇayoraṅgatvagrahakatvasaṃbhave eva śrutireva grāhikā valīyastvāt, prakṛte tu naivamiti tātparyam | nanvevaṃ 'na hisyāt sarvābhūtāni' 'na kalañjaṃ bhakṣaye'dityādiniṣedhānāmapi śrutyā puruṣārthatvaṃ na syāt, śrutyupasthita- kartuḥ kriyāviśeṣaṇatvāt | yadi ca tatrānyasyāṅagino'saṃbhavāt viśe,ṇībhūta- karturapi niṣedho'ṅgamiti ceduktaniyamavyabhicāreṇa 'nānṛtaṃ vade'dityatrāpi niṣedhaḥ karturaṅgaṃ syādata āha astu veti | yadvā śrutyapekṣayā vilambena viniyojakatayā prakāraṇasya daurvalyaṃ kluptaṃ, śruteritaranairapekṣyeṇa prakāraṇāt pūrvameva viniyojakatvena prābalyāt, ataḥ prakṛte'pi prabalāyāḥ śruteḥ prakaraṇabādhakatvamabhyupetyetthaṃ parihāraḥ kṛtaḥ, vastutastu pramāṇayovirodhe eva balābalacintā, anyathā vakṣyamāṇarītyā jañjabhyamānādhikaraṇavirodhaḥ, prakṛte tu śrutiprakāraṇābhyāmavirodha evetyāha astu veti | kratuyuktapuruṣadharma iti | asya 'śrutiprakaraṇābhyā'mityādiḥ | tatrānurūpaṃ dṛṣṭāntamāha jañjabhyamāna iti | tatra yathā'viruddhābhyāṃ vākyaprakāraṇābhyāmuktamantrajapasya kratuyuktapuruṣadharmatvaṃ, tathā śrutiprakaraṇayoranṛtaniṣedhasyāpītyarthaḥ | taduktaṃ bhaṭṭapādairapi 
?R 'strayupāyamāṃsabhakṣādipuruṣārthamapi śritaḥ |
pratiṣedhaḥ katoraṅgamiṣṭaḥ prakaraṇāśrayāt ||' iti |
'bhakṣādī'ti naruṃsakadvitīyāntaṃ, tadviṣayīkṛtya yaḥ pratiṣedha ityarthaḥ | ata eva 'yadarthā prakṛtistadarthaḥ pratiṣedha' iti niyamasya 'nānṛtaṃ vade'dityatra bhaṅgo na | vastuta uktabhaṭṭoktyā tādṛśaniyamo na sārtrika iti bodhyam | anārabhyādhīto
nānṛtaṃ vadediti niṣedhastu 'parāvapajñā ṛṣayo yadvā nastadvā na iti prayoktavye yarvāṇastarvāṇa iti prayuñjānā yarvāṇastarvāṇo nāma babhūvuryājñe karmaṇi punarnāpabhāṣante' iti paspaśāhnikasthabhāṣyasvarasādarthānṛtapara eva ata eva bhāṣyāt, 'te 'asurā helayo helaya iti kurvantaḥ parābabhūvuḥ' iti kratuprakāraṇapaṭhitabhāṣyalikhitaśrutyantarācca darśapūrṇamāsaprakāraṇapaṭhito nānṛtamiti niṣedha ubhayavidhasyāpyanṛtasyeti dhyeyam | tadetad dhvanayannāha bhūṣaṇe prapāñcita- miti | apabhraṃśavaditi | yathā'pabhraṃśānāṃ sādhutvabhramādbodhakatveṣapyasādhutvaṃ tathā bhāvanābodhakatve'pi tasyāṃ tibāderasādhutvaṃ syādevetyarthaḥ | asādhutvaṃsyāditi | na caitadanuśāsanasya nirmūlatvena tibāderbhāvānābodhakatve'pi sādhutvaṃ nirābādhameveti vācyam | 'brāhmaṇena niṣkāraṇaḥ ṣaḍaṅgo vedo'dhyeyo jñeyaśca' iti śruteḥ 
'pāṇinīyaṃ mahāśāstraṃ padasādhutvalakṣaṇam |
sarvopakārakaṃ grāhyaṃ kṛtsnaṃ, tyājyaṃ na kicana ||'
-iti parāśaropapurāṇādeśca mūlatvāt | na ca lakāravat kartrādyarthakatvamapi kalpitamiti vācyam, sarvatra prakṛtipratyayavibhāgaṃ kalpitaṃ tattadartha cādāyaiva sādhutvabodhanāt | jñānaṃ na heturiti tadvayatireke'pi śābdabodhānubhavāt | tasya hetutvābhāve'pi asādhutvajñānasattve śābdabodhānudayāt tasya pratibandhattvaṃ kalpanīyam | evaṃ ca bhāvanāyāmasādhustibādiriti jñānasattve kathaṃ tatastadvodhaḥ, na ca tadā tadbodho na jāyata eva, api tvasādhutvajñānavirahadaśāyāmeva tadbodha iti vācyaṃ, tadāpi bodhasyānubhavasiddhatvādityāśaṅkāṃ nirākaroti tavdyati- reketi | sādhutvavyatirekanirṇaya ityarthaḥ | vakṣyāma iti | sādhutvajñānarahitānāma- sādhutvajñānavatāmapi gagaryādiśabdebhyo bodhasya sarvānubhavasiddhatayā sādhutvajñānaṃ na hetustadvayatirekajñānaṃ ca na pratibandhakamiti vakṣyata iti naḥ sarveṣṭasiddhiriti bhāva iti śivam || 21 ||
keśavāṅgajaviprasya harirāmasya mirmitau |
pūrṇo bhūṣaṇaṭīkāyāṃ dhātvarthākhyātanirṇayaḥ ||
iti śrīmadvaiyākaraṇabhūṣaṇaṭīkāyāṃ kālopanāmakaharirāmaviracitāyāṃ
dhātvākhyātasāmānyārthanirūpaṇaṃ samāptam ||
<2>
adha lakārārthanirṇayaḥ
pratyekamiti | ekaikasyetyarthaḥ, vīpsāyāmavyayībhāvāt | daśalakārāṇāmiti | 'diksaṃkhye saṃjñāyām' iti samāsaḥ, saptarṣivadiyaṃ laḍādīnāṃ saṃjñeti bhāvaḥ | avayavāvavibhāvasaṃbandhe ṣaṣṭhī, pratyekamityatrānvayaḥ | arthamiti sāmānyā- bhiprāyeṇaikavacanam | nirūpayatīti | lakārasāmānyārthajñāne sati lakāraviśeṣārthajijñāsādeyāt | tathā ca prasaṅgasaṃgatyā tannirūpaṇamiti bodhyam | vartamānatvaṃ lakṣayati-prārabdheti | prārabdhastattatphalānukūlavyāpāradhvaṃsāsamāna- kālikatattphalānukūlakṛtiviṣayībhūto yo'parisamāptastattatphalānukūlacaramakriyā- dhvaṃsaprāgabhāvādhikaraṇakālavṛttiḥ, prāgabhāvānaṅgīkāre tu tādṛśadhvaṃsā- nadhikaraṇakālavṛttirityarthe'vaseyaḥ, etādṛśo yaḥ kriyātmako'rthastattatphalānukūla- vyāpārasaṃtānātmakakriyārūpapatadadhikaraṇatvopalakṣitasamayatvamityarthaḥ | vartamānatvasya kālagatatatvena sarvānubhavasiddhatvāt, ata evākhyātārthaḥ kāla iti pravāda'pi saṃgacchate, bhūṣaṇe'pyata evetthamevoktam | "bhāṣye nyāyyā tveṣā vartamānakālatā''rambhānapavargādavaśyaṃ bhuñjāno hasati jalpati pibati nimiṣati ce"tyuktam | nāntarīyakatvāt tāsāṃ na vicchedakatvamiti hasanādidaśayāmapi 'bhūṅkte' iti prayogopapattiḥ, yadvā tāsāmapi tattaddhātvarthaphalānukūlatvena bhujyādidhātvarthatvavivakṣeti tadbhāva iti kaiyaṭaḥ | anekadinādisādhyagranthādhyayanasamaye bhojanagamanādinānāvidhakriyāṇāmapi tathāvidhitvamiti bhāvaḥ, ata eva saṃhitādyadhyayanasamaptiparthantaṃ 'devadattaḥ saṃhitāṃ paṭhatī'tyādiprayogopapattiḥ, paraṃ tu paṭhadhātvādestathāvidhavahutaravyāpāravācakatva- kalpanāyāṃ gauravāt kaṇṭhādyabhidhātādivyāpāreṣvalpeṣveva śakriruciteti pūrvoktameva samyagiti pratibhāti, jatatāḍanādyanekakriyāṇāṃ tadanukūlatve mānābhāvācca saṃhitādyadhyayanambhakṣaṇatatsamāptikṣaṇāntarālikasamaye niruktavartamānatvasattvādukta- prayoganirvāhaḥ, uktabhāṣyasyāpyetādṛvartamānatvaparigrahe tātparyam, prārabdhā- parisamāptatvañca viśeṣaṇaṃ na tūpalakṣaṇaṃ tenātītādikriyāyāmapi tattatkālavacchedena prārabdhāparisamāptatvasattvena pacatīti prayogāpattirna, anyathā tu syāt tadāprāpteḥ, prārabdhāparisamāptakriyopalakṣitatvamiti bhūṣaṇagranthaparyālocanayedaṃ tattatkālopalakṣakamātraṃ na tu tasya śābdabodhe bhānam, śābdabodhe tu
vāstavametīdṛśavartamānatvaṃ yatra sa kālastatkālatvena bhāsate, atastatkālatvenaiva laḍvācyatā, paraṃ tu evaṃrītyā kālagatabhūtatvānadyatanatvāderapi liḍādiprayoge bhānānāpattiḥ | teṣāmanubhavabalācced bhānamiti teṣāṃ liḍādivācyateti cet prakṛte'pi tatheti pratibhāti | ata eva vartamānakālikī pacikriyeti bodhaṃ varṇayanti prāmāṇikāḥ | papācetyādijanyabodhe sati bhūtatvānadyatanatvādi- saṃśayānudayāt tasya vācyate prakṛte'pi tulyam | atītādisamaye pākakartari vartamānasamaye tatkartṛtvaśūnye devadattādāvapākṣīt, pakṣayati devadatto na pacatīti prayogānupapattiśca | caitrakartṛkapākasāmānyābhāvasya bādhitatvāt, uktavidhavartamānasyāvāctve tu anvayitāvacchedakacaitrakartṛkavartamānapākatvāvacchinna- pratiyogitākābhāvasyābādhitatvānnānupapattiruktaprayogasya, pratiyogiprasiddhiścāntata āhāryāroparūpaiveti dhyeyam | agnirdahatītyādau niruktaprārabdhatvasyāropāt, 'śrṛṇota grāvāṇaḥ' iti śrutibalāt sarvasyaiva caitanyādvopapattirbodhyā, tattatphalānukūlakriyādhvaṃsānādhikaraṇatadanukūlakriyādhikaraṇasamayarūpādyakṣaṇa- sambandharūpaṃ vā prārabdhatvaṃ niveśyam | atra pakṣe gauravāllaghuvartamānatvamāha-bhūteti | tathā ca bhūtabhaviṣyadbhinnakālātvaṃ vartanāmatvamityarthaḥ | tatra bhūtatvaṃ vartamānadhvaṃsapratiyogitvam | bhaviṣyattvaṃ vartamānaprāgabhāvapratiyogitvam, vartamānatvaṃ ca dhvaṃsādau prakṛtaprayogādhikaraṇakālavṛttitvam, prakṛtaprayogādhikaraṇakālaśca praharadinādyātmako buddhisthastatra tatra viśiṣya niveśyaḥ, tena tadadhikaraṇapraharādyātmakakālāntargatāsu kāsucid ghaṭikāsvapi tadadhikaraṇakiñcitkālavṛttidhvaṃpratiyogitvādisattve'pi bhūtabhaviṣyadbhinnatvasya nāsaṃbhavaḥ, atra būtatvādikaṃ paricāyakameva bhedapratiyogikālaviśeṣasya, tattatkāla- bhedaviśiṣṭakālatvāvacchinne eva tu śaktiḥ, tādṛśabhedasyāpi śābdabodhe bhānānaṅgīkāra tu bhedāntaṃ paricāyakaṃ, tattatkālatvavatyeva śakririti bodhyam | bhinnatvaṃ vetyatra vāśabdenānāsthaveditā | tanmūlaṃ tu vartamānabhaviṣyadbhinnatvaṃ bhūtatvamityādyapi vaktuṃ śakyaṃ, vinigamanāvirahāt, tathā ca na bhūtādibhedaghaṭitaṃ vartamānatvaṃ vaktavyaṃ, kiṃ tu bhūtatvādivadvartamānatvamapyuktabhedāghaṭitameveti | adhiśrayaṇāditi | adhiśrayaṇamādiryasyāghaḥśrayaṇamanto yasyeti bahuvrīhidvaye sati karmadhārayaḥ, anyapadārthaḥ samūhaḥ | karmadhāraye'pi sa eva viśeṣyaḥ | tādṛśasamūhāntargatamadhye ityarthaḥ | tat - uktapūpavartamānatvam | na'nvātmāsti'
'parvatāḥ santī'tyādiprayogānupapattiḥ, tatrātmadhāraṇānukūlavyāpārasya nityatayā- sādhyatvābhāvāt kriyātvābhāvāt, taddharmasya prārabdhāparisamāptatvarūpavaptamānatvā- bhāvadityāśaṅgāṃ nirācaṣṭe ātmetyādinā |
tadviśiṣṭasya - tādṛśajakriyāviśiṣṭasyātmādikartṛkoktavyāpārasyetyarthaḥ | utpattyādikamityatra ādipadāt samāptestattatkālabhedasya ca parigraha | tathā cātmādikartṛkoktavyāpārasya nityatayotpattyādivirahe'pi tattatkālikarājakriyā- viśiṣṭasya tasyotpattyādimattayā niruktavartamānatvaṃ sūpapadamiti bhavāḥ | tatra 'vartamāne laṭ' sūtrasthabhāṣyaṃ pramāṇayati uktaṃ ca bhāṣye iti | tiṣṭhateradhikaraṇamiti | tatra kaiyaṭopādhyāyo'pītthaṃ vyācaṣṭa 'tatra śajñāṃ sthiti- rbhūtādibhedena bhinnā parvatādisthityāderbhediketi kriyārūpatvaṃ kālatrayayogaścopa- padyate' iti | anyathā parvatādisthitīnāṃ sadṛśāvayavatvāt pūrvāparībhūtāvayavaka- samūhatvarūpakriyātvānupapattiḥ, evaṃ tāsāṃ durabadhārabhedatayā kālabhedānupapattiśca syāditi bhāvaḥ | kriyāyāḥ kālarūpatve, kriyāḥ tiṣṭhateradhikaraṇan - kālikasaṃbandhenādhikaraṇamityarthakatayā yathāśrutameva sādhu | 'prakṛtipariṇāmabhūto- 'tibhaṅguro'vibhuḥkṣaṇarataddhārātmaka eva kāla' iti mate tu kriyāpadasya tatparicchedakakālaparatvena tadīyakālasya parvatādisthitāvātmasattāyāṃ cādheyatāsaṃbandhenāropa ityāśayakaṃ bodhyam | parato sidyata iti | sarva - ātmāsattādikaṃ sarva, parato sidyate -parata eva sidyate, bhedasādhakaṃ svātiriktameva | ātmā tu - svasvarūpaṃ tu, na vikalpate - svato bhedakaṃ bhavati | pararūpeṇa bhidyata ityasyokta evārtho helārājīye spaṣṭaḥ |
tama āsīditi | tamo'jñānaṃ tasya sarvadā sattvena bhūtakālārthakapratyayaprayogo'pītthameva nirvahati | evameva 'pihitamajñānenāvṛtamāsī' dityādāvapi bodhyam | vartamānatvādīti | vartamānakālādītyarthaḥ | nanu laḍāderdyotakatvaṃ śattayudbodhakatvarūpaṃ vācyaṃ, tacca dhātorvartamānatvādau śakti vinā na saṃbhavati, evaṃ ca bahūnāṃ dhātūnāṃ tatra śaktikalpanāyāṃ gauravādalpatarapratyayānā- meva vācakatvameva syānna tu dyotakatvamityata āha kriyāsāmānyeti | vācakasyetyanantaraṃ 'dhāto'riti śeṣaḥ |
tadviśiṣṭe iti | vartamānatvādiviśiṣṭakriyāyāmityarthaḥ | tātparyagrahakatveneti | viśiṣṭalakṣaṇāyāṃ tātparyagrahakatvameva prakṛte dyotakatvamiti
bhāvaḥ | lakṣaṇāsvīkārācca na śaktikalpanāprayuktaṃ gauravaṃ, vṛttidvayenaikaśabdajanya- bodhānabhyupagamādviśiṣṭalakṣaṇānusaraṇaṃ, 'gaṅgāyāṃ mīnaghoṣau sta' iti vākya- ghaṭakagaṅgāpadācchaktilakṣaṇābhyāmupasthitapravāhatīrayorbodhānubhavādvṛttidvayavirodhe mānābhāva' iti matābhyupagame tu vartamānatvādāveva latraṇeti bodhyam | laḍādervartamānatvādivāvakatvapakṣe tu kartrāderlaḍādivācyatvaṃ na syāt, viśeṣeṇa sāmānyabādhāt, tathā ca 'caitramaitrau pacata' ityādau karturabhānāt tadgatadvitvādyapratītau dvivacanabahucanānāpattiḥ, śabādyanupapattiḥ, tṛtīyāpattiśca caitrādipadāt syāt, karturamabhidhānāt, ato dyotakatāpakṣa eva yuktaḥ | yadyapi niravakāśatvamevaṃ sati kartrādīnāṃ syāt, loḍādisthale'pi vidhyādyarthairbādhāt, kiṃ ca takrakauṇḍinyanyāsya vidheyaviṣayatayā'rthasya samarthasūtrasthabhāṣyaprāmāṇyadavidheya- tayā bādhaprasaktireva na, spṭaṃ cedaṃ 'nityaṃ kauṭilye gatau' ityādi sūtre bhāṣyapradipoddyotādāviti sa pakṣe'pi sūpapadastathāpi vācakatāpakṣe pratyayārthatayā prādhānyāpattiḥ, tadartha 'prakṛtipratyayayo'riti niyamasaṃkocaḥ kalpanīyaḥ, dyotakatāpakṣe tu 'hetumati ca' iti sūtrabhāṣyaprāmāṇyena dyotyārthasya viśeṣaṇatvaniyamasiddhau, pratyayavācyaviṣayasya 'prakṛtipratyayayo'rityasyāvatāra evātra neti na saṃkocakalpanāprasaktiḥ, spaṣṭaṃ cedaṃ 'mañjūṣādā'vityayaṃ pakṣa eva sādhīyāniti bodhyam | ata evātrāpyagre 'ityapi' pakṣāntara'miti 'api' padaghaṭitamasvarasasūcakamuktam | tadrūpam - vartamānatvādirūpam | laḍādivācya- miti | vācakatāvacchedakaṃ laṭtvaliṭtvādikaṃ, tadapi laḍādeśe tibādāvanubhavasiddhameva, tacca tatrāropitam, ata eva 'yajete'tyatrāstyaṃśadvayam, ākhyātatvaṃ, liṅtvaṃ ca, tatra liṅtvena śābdī bhāvanābhidhīyata"iti māmāṃsakagranthaḥ saṃgacchate | anye tu tibādikameva tathā, laṭtvādijñānavidhurasyāpi śābdabodhotpatteḥ, tattadānupūrvīviroṣasya tadbodhe tātparyagrāhakatvācca na 'pakṣyatī'tyādau 'vartamānatvādibodhāpattirityāhuḥ |
anadyatane bhūte iti . adya bhavo'dyatanastadbhinnakālavṛttiḥ parokṣā yā kriyā tadarthakāddhātorliḍiti sūtrārthaḥ | evameva 'anadyatane luṭ' ityādau vyākhyānaṃ bodhyam, evaṃ caitanmate tadarthabodhako liḍityarthaḥ, pakṣāntare tu tadvācaka ityarthaḥ | krameṇa 'anadyatana' ityādiviśeṣaṇaprayojanamāha tenetyādinā | tena anadyatana ityādiviśeṣaṇopādānena | 'adyatane bhūte' ityādisaptamyantayugalatrikasya 'na
liṭprayogaḥ' ityanenānvayaḥ | etāddaśaviṣayateti | etādṛśabdābhilapyamānaviṣaya- tetyarthaḥ, jñāne vaktṛvṛttitvaṃ niveśyaṃ, tenānyavṛttitādṛśajñānaviṣayatve'pi 'ayaṃ papāce'tyādiprayogasya nānupapattiḥ | avyāvartakamiti | kasyāścit kriyāyāḥ pratyakṣaviṣayatve tathāvidhābhiprāyeṇa tādṛśaprayogavyāvartanāya 'parokṣe' iti sārthakaṃ bhavet, kriyāmātrasya pārokṣye tu tannirarthakamityāśaṅkate ityarthaḥ | 'apariddaṣṭe'ti pāṭhamāśritya, pūrvāparībhūtā avayavā yasyāḥ, ata eva piṇaḍībhūtā nidarśayituṃ na śakyā, pūrvāparībhūtāvayavakatvena piṇḍībhāvasyaivābhāvāditi bhāṣyasya vyākhyānamanusṛtya samādhatte piṇḍībhūtāyā iti | avayavaśaḥ - ekaikasyāvayavasyetyarthaḥ, saṃkhyaikavacanācca' iti śas | pratyakṣatvasaṃbhavāditi | tathā ca kriyāvayavānāṃ vaktraparokṣatvadaśāyāṃ liṭprayogasya sādhutvavyāvartanāya tadāvayakameveti bhāvaḥ | avayavanāmapi parokṣatve bādhakamāha anyatheti | darśanakarmatā na syāditīti | tathā ca prāmāṇikasya tādṛśaprayogasyānupapattiriti bhāvaḥ
prakārāntareṇa parokṣapadaṃ sārthakayatāmanyeṣāṃ matamāha vyāpārāviṣṭānāmiti | vyāpāraviśiṣṭānāmityarthaḥ | noktadoṣa iti | vyāpārāviṣṭasādhanānāṃ vaktuḥ pratyakṣadaśāyām 'ayaṃ papāce'ti prayogavāraṇārthatayā sārthakye satyavyāvartakatvarūpo doṣo netyarthaḥ | vyāpārasya viśeṣaṇavidhayā niveśaprayojanamāha ayaṃ papāceti | kartuḥ pratyakṣadaśāyāṃ tādṛśapyogānupapattiriti 'vyāpārāviśiṣṭa'niveśaḥ | vaktuḥ- pratyakṣāviṣaya kartṛkasthale 'caitraḥ papāce'tyādāveva liṭprayogaḥ syāditi bhāvaḥ | ata evedaṃśabdadhaṭitānudhāvanam | api vadantītyanenārucisūcanaṃ kṛtaṃ, tadbījaṃ tu kriyāyāmeva parokṣetiviśeṣaṇānvaye'pyuktarītyā sārthakatve saṃbhavati tathāvidhasādhanānvayānaucityaṃ, 'dhātoḥ' ityadhikārāt kriyāyāḥ śīghropasthiterityādikamavaseyam | bhāṣyapradīpoddyotādikṛtastu , "śatapatrabhedanakarttṛratisanikṛṣṭasyāpi svāṅgulyādigatakriyābhedāderanavagamena, padārthasaukṣmyavat sūkṣmakālāvacchinnatvasyāpi pratyakṣapratibandhakatayā, kriyāvayā- nāmapi pratyakṣatvaṃ na saṃbhavati, āśrayanāśājanyaguṇanāśasya virodhiguṇaprayuktatva- miti niyame mānābhāvena vibhāgānabhyupagamena kriyāṇāṃ trikṣaṇāvasthāyitvaṃ manyante, tathā hi prathame kṣaṇe kriyotpattistato dvitīye pūrvasaṃyoganāśastṛtīye uttaradeśa- saṃyogotpattiścaturthe kriyāyā nāśaḥ, svajanyasaṃyoganāśahetoḥ pūrvakṣaṇe sattvāt, evaṃ sūkṣmakālāvacchinnatvam ; yaistūttaradeśasaṃyogotpattirdvitīyakṣaṇa evāṅgīkriyate, pūrvadeśasaṃyoganāśātmakatvasvīkārāt tasya, tanmate tasyāḥ kṣaṇāvasthāyitvena sutarāṃ sūkṣmakālāvacchinnatvam | ata eva 'kriyā nāmeyam ' ityādi bhāṣyam, 'aparadṛṣṭe' itipāṭhābhiprāyeṇa "pareṇa - pradhānena pratyakṣātmakapramāṇenātyanta- madṛṣṭa-aavayavarūpeṇa samūharūpeṇa cetyartha" iti vyākhyātaṃ 'niṣṭhā' iti sūtre kaiyaṭopādhyāyaiḥ | 'piṇḍībhūta'iti tvāśutaravināśināṃ kramikāṇāṃ melānāsaṃbhavāt piṇḍībhāvāsaṃbhavatātparyakameva, tasmāduttaradeśasaṃyogādirūpaphalānumeyaiva sarvā'pi kriyeti bhūvādisūtrabhāṣyādau spaṣṭaṃ, 'paśya mṛgo dhāvatī'tyasya-dṛśerjñāna- sāmānyārthakatvena saṃyogātmakaphalaviṣayakadarśanavivakṣayā vopapattiḥ | evaṃ ca vyarthameva parokṣapadopādānamityāśaṅkya vyāpārāviṣṭānāmiti pakṣamevāṅgīcakruḥ, manoramāyāmapyayameva pakṣaḥ sthitaḥ | yadyapi kriyāyā apratyakṣatve tadviśiṣṭasādhanapratyakṣaṃ durupapādamatastadvaiyarthya tadavasthameva, tathāpi vyāpāravaiśiṣṭyasamaye sādhanāpārokṣyasya vivakṣitatvānnoktadoṣa iti bhāvaḥ |
kathaṃtāyā hetumāha svakriyāyā iti | pratyakṣatvāt - svātmakavakturavaśyaṃ
pratyakṣaviṣayatvāt | pakṣāntare kiyāviṣṭasādhanasya svapratyakṣatvāditi heturunneyaḥ | nanu viṣayāntarasaṃcārarūpavyāsaṅgādinā svavyāpārasya, vyāpāraviśiṣṭasya ca svapratyakṣaviṣayatvābhāvarūpapārokṣyasaṃbhavānna liṭprayogāsaṃgatirityāśaṅkya bhūtatvādyasaṃbhavanibandhanāṃ tāmāha vyāsaṅgādineti | ata eva 'bahu jagada purastāttasya mattā kilāham' iti prayoga upapannaḥ | anadyataneti | kiraṇāvalīkarmakavistārānukūlavyāpārāvacchedenādyatanabhinnatvābhāvāt, atītakāla- saṃbandhābhāvāccetyarthaḥ | idaṃ cā'nadyatanatvamatītatvaṃ ca dvayaṃ kālāghaṭitaṃ kriyāviśeṣaṇa'miti matamāśrityābhihitam | 'adyatanabhinnātītakālasya kriyāviśeṣaṇatva'miti niṣkṛṣṭhabhāṣyamate tu tādṛśakriyāyāmadyatanabhinnātītakāla- saṃbandhābhāvāditi heturbodhyaḥ | anadyatanakālavṛttitvātītakālavṛttitvayoriti vā bhūtānadyatanātītatvayorityasyārtho bodhyaḥ | atra kriyāyāmanadyatanabhūtakāla- saṃbandhasya, parokṣatvasya cāropadupapadyata eva liṭprayogaḥ, āropaprayojanaṃ tu sukaratvaśīghranirvartyatvāṃdibodhanam, tatphalaṃ tvadhyetaṇāṃ padārthatattvaniścayaphala- kaitadgranthaṃ jñātuṃ sotkaṇṭhānāmāhlādaḥ, parokṣatvāropo'pyetatphalakaḥ, anāyāsena granthanirmāṇamapyatra vyaṅgyam | evameva 'idaṃ nu kadā gantā yaivaṃ pādau nidadhāti', deve vṛṣṭe 'devaścedvṛṣṭaḥ saṃpannāḥ śālyaḥ' ityādiprayogopapattiḥ | ādye adyatane'pyanadyatanatvāropaḥ, ativilambagamanapratyāyanaṃ prayojanam, antye hetubhūta- meghotpattigatamatītatvaṃ vṛṣṭau, tadgatamatītatvaṃ ca śālisaṃpattāvāropyata iti bhūte ktaḥ | ata eva medhotpattyanantarakālepyuktaprayoganirvāhaḥ | etena kāryasaṃpattitādātmyasya kāraṇībhūtāpūśālisaṃyoge'dhyāsāt tasyātītatvena vṛṣṭyanantaraṃ tathāvidhaprayogopapādane'pi medhotpattyānantaraṃ tadupapādanāsaṃbhava iti śaṅkā nirastā | evaṃ grāmāntaraṃ jigamiṣubhiḥ sthalaviśeṣe kūpasattānirṇaya- vadbhirbhāvini tallābhe'kūpo bhaviṣyati'. atīte 'kūpo'bhū'diti prayujyate, tatrāpi lābhagatabhāvitvāderāropa eva tatprayoganirvāhaka iti 'kṣipravacane lṛṭ', 'luṅ' ityādisūtrabhāṣye iti pare pratipannāḥ |
nanu śvo bhāvinītyayuktam, tṛtīyādidinabhāvinyapi pākādau paktetyādi- prayogasyeṣṭatvāt | śva itsya nirmūlatvāccetyāśaṅkamāna āha anadyatana iti | tathā ca tadupalakṣaṇaṃ vṛttyanurodhena tu taduktiriti bhāvaḥ | bhāvitvaṃ -
bhaviṣyattvaṃ, tacca vartamānaprāgabhāvapratiyogisamayatvam | tatra pramāṇamāha 'anadyatana-' iti | tatra 'bhaviṣyati gamyādayaḥ' ityato bhaviṣyatītyanuvartate, evaṃ ca 'pakte'tyatrānadyatanabhaviṣyatkālikī ekakartṛkā pacikriyeti bodhaḥ,śvaḥpadādyupa- pasthāpitakālaviśeṣasya kriyāyāmanvayo, na pratyayārthaghaṭakaprāgabhāve, na vā tadarthakartrādau, prāgabhāvasya padārthaikadeśatvena 'padārthaḥ padārthene'ti vyutpattivirodhāt, paraśve bhāvini, paraśvaḥ paktari ca-'śvo bhavitā'. 'śvaḥ pakte'tyādiprasaṅgācca | paraśva ādyakṣaṇaprārabdhapākādisthale paraśvaḥ paktā, paraśvo bhavitetyāderanupapatteśca, paraśvastanasattādeḥ prāgabhāve śvastanatvabādhāt | antye tathāvidhapākādiprāgabhāve paraśvastanatvabādhāt, caramapakṣe śvaḥpaktari 'paraśvaḥ pakte'tyādyāpattestasyādhikaraṇakārakatvena kriyānvayasyaiva nyāyyatvācca | nanu kriyāyāmanvayābhyupagame'pi śvobāvini 'paraśvo bhavite'ti prayogāpattiḥ, dhātvartha- sattāyāṃ paraśvastanatvasyābādhāt, na ca tatkartṛkadhātvarthatvāvacchedena tadanvayābhyupagamānneyamāpattiriti vācyam, śvobhāvinyapi 'śvo bhavite'ti prayogānāpatteḥ, paraśvastanatadīyasattāyāḥ śvastanatvābhāvaditi cenna | tatrotpattyarthaḥ eva dhātutaḥ pratīyata ityubhavāt, śvaḥpadādyupasthāpitakālavṛttitvasya svanirūpakakālotpattikatvaviśiṣṭāśrayatvasaṃbandhena vā kriyāyāmanvayopagamādukta- doṣo nirasanīyaḥ | anadyatanabhaviṣyatyapi bhaviṣyattvamātravivakṣāyāṃ lṛḍeva, 'sāvaṇako nāma manurbhavān bhaviṣyati' ityādau yathā, evamanadhyatanabhūte'pi bhūtattvamātravivakṣāyāṃ luṅ, yathā 'suratho nāma rājābhūt' ityādau, ata evānadyanatvādervācyatvāṅgīkāraḥ | vastutastasya prayogasādhutvanimittattvābhyupagame tu 'iyaṃ nu kadāgantā' 'bhavān bhaviṣyati' ityādiprayogānirvāhāt | evaṃ praharānantaramadyaiva gamanakartari devadatte 'gamiṣyati devadatto na gantā' ityādi- prayogānupapatteśca bhaviṣyakālikadevadattakartṛkagamanābhāvasya bodhanīyasya vādhāt | anadyatanatvasya vācyatve tu tadviśiṣṭibhaviṣyatkālikatādṛśakriyābhāvasyā- bādhāduktaprayogopapattiriti bodhyam | evamādau bhaviṣyattvamātravivakṣāyāṃ lṛṭ | tataḥ śvaḥśabdayoge'nadyatanatvapratītāvapi na tasya nivṛttirantaraṅhasaṃskārasyānivartanāt | ata eva 'śvo yakṣye' ityādiprayogopapattiḥ | 'lṛṭ śeṣe ca' itīti | atra 'bhaviṣyati gamyādayaḥ' iti sūtrād 'bhaviṣyati' ityanuvartate | kriyārthakriyāvācakapadāsattvaṃ śeṣapadārthaḥ, 'tumunṇvulau kriyāyām-' iti
sūtrottarametasya pāṭhāt | cakārāt tatsattve'pi lṛṭ | samayotpattimattvamiti | utpattau śvaḥpadādipratipādyakālaviśeṣasaṃbadhānvayena śvobhāvini 'paraśvo bhavite'tyādyatiprasaṅgo na, etadarthamevotpattyanantarabhāvaḥ, anyathotpattimattvasthāne vṛttitvameva niveśanīyaṃ syāt | tadanantarbhāve tu pūrvoktā rītirevānusartavyā |
vidhyādāviti | ādinā nimantraṇādīnāṃ tatsūtroktānāṃ parigrahaḥ |
prārthanetīti | prārthanasyādiḥ prārthanamādiryasyeti tatpuruṣavahuvrīhyostantreṇa nirdeśa ityāśayenāha ādinā vidhyādyāśiṣa iti | tatrāśiṣyudāharati yathā bhavatviti | āśīḥ prayoktṛniṣṭhā hitaviṣayiṇīcchā, tasyā viṣayatāsaṃbandhena kārakaviśiṣṭakriyāyāmanvayaḥ, tathā bhāvāttadudāharaṇānupanyāsaḥ | nanu vidhyādyarthānirṇaye kathaṃ leṭloṭorarthanirṇaya ityata āha etayoriti | tannirṇayena - liṅarthanirṇayena vidhyādīnāṃ liṅavidhāyake evopādānāt tatraiva tadartho nirṇeṣyata iti tātparyam || 22 ||
laṅādikrameṇeti | laṅādīnāṃ kramo laṅādikramastena | teṣāṃ kramastu lokaprasiddhaḥ | hyobhūtatvavivakṣaṇe dinādivyavahitabhūtakāle na syādatastadupalakṣaṇamityāha anadyatane bhūta iti | tatra pramāṇamāha 'anadyatane laṅ' itīti |
bhṛtyāderiti nikṛṣṭoktiḥ svasamānottamayoḥ pravartanāyāmativyāptivāraṇāya | ājñapyatvena nikṛṣṭatvaṃ vivakṣitamiti bodhayituṃ-bhṛtyāderiti | ata eva svāpekṣayādhikavayaskabhṛtyādipreraṇāyāḥ saṃgrahaḥ | pravartanaṃ ca prerayitrabhilaṣita- viṣayakaṃ bodhyam | iyamevājñetyucyate | apravṛttaprayojyasya tadabhilaṣitājñātopāyapravartanamupadeśaḥ | yathā 'svargakāmo yajeta' yathā vā 'anena pathā yāhi' iti | ādye svargo'bhilaṣitaḥ | anyte kāśyādiprāptiḥ | āvaśyake ityasya 'śrāddhabhojanādā'viti śeṣaḥ | kāmacāreti | svābhilaṣite kāmacāreṇa pravartyapravartanam, kāmacāraśca pravartyasyaiva' | pravṛttasya prayojyasya nivṛttipratibandhadvārā taddhite pravṛttipyojikoktiranujñā, ārabdhaṃ kurvevetyevamātmikā | 'tathā kuruṣva yathā hitam' ityuktirapyanujñaiva | etat sarvamāmantraṇapadenopalakṣyate |
satkārapūrvako vyāpāra iti | yathā 'māṃ pāṭhaya' iti | eṣaivābhyarthaneti gīyate | prārthanā tu svābhilaṣitavastudānādipravatikoktiḥ | etaccatuṣṭayeti | adhīṣṭāntacatuṣṭayānugataṃ yat pravartanātvaṃ tenetyarthaḥ | svābhilaṣitadānādi- viṣayakecchā prārthanaṃ, tacca na pravṛttijanakamanyathāsiddhatvādityabhipretya catuṣyetyuktam | vakṣyamāṇaharikārikāyāṃ caturṇāmityapyevameva, pūrvoktasvarūpatve tu pañcānugateti bodhyam | evaṃ kārikāsthacaturṇāmityapi prārthanasyopalakṣaṇaṃ bodhyam |
pravartaneti | pravartanāyā rūpaṃ-pravartanātmityarthaḥ | tatraiva - pravartanātvavatyeva | pravartanātvamakhaṇḍopādhiḥ | ākṛtyadhikaraṇanyāyena dharmamātraśaktiriti mate tu yathāśrutameva sādhu | athavā tatraiva - tasmin satyeva | evaṃ ca tadviṣiṣṭa vidhānavābhaḥ | nyāyeti | vidhipadārthavivaraṇārthamityarthaḥ | yadvā vidhyādyanugatapravartanātvarūpeṇa vācyateti bodhanārthamityarthaḥ | nanu lokavyutpattyaiva tadavagamo bhaviṣyatītyata āha prapañcārthamiti | ādita ādāvityarthaḥ | ādyāditvāttasiḥ | tathā ca 'pravartanāsaṃpraśrayoliṅ' iti sūtraṃ kartavyamiti bhāvaḥ | pravṛttijanaketi | pravṛttijanakaṃ yajjñānaṃ tadviṣayatāvacchedakamityarthaḥ | bhavati hi yāgo madiṣṭasādhanamiti jñānaṃ pravartakaṃ,-tadīyayāganiṣṭhaviśeṣyatātmaka- viṣayatānirūpakatvarūpaṃ tadavacchedakatvamiṣṭasādhanatvasyetyarthaḥ | tādṛśajñānīyaviśeṣyatānirūpitaprakāratālābhārtha viṣayatvamapahāya viṣaya- tāvacchedakatvānusaraṇam | eteneṣṭasādhanatve tādṛśajñānaviṣayatāyāḥ svarūpa- saṃbandharūpāvacchedakatvaṃ na saṃbhavati, yāgaviśeṣyatāyā iṣṭasādhanatvaprakāratā- nirūpitāyā iṣṭasādhanatvānavacchedyatvāditi dūṣaṇaṃ nirastam | tacca - uktavidhipravartanātvaṃ ca | tadeva - iṣṭasādhanatvameva, tādṛśapravartanātvaprakāreṇa bodhānanubhave tūpalakṣaṇībhūya vācyatāvacchedakatvaṃ tasyeti bodhyam | anyathā punārviśeṣaṇībhūya tadavacchedakatvaṃ bodhyam | mīmāṃsakānāṃ punarviśeṣaṇatvapakṣe nirbharo dṛśyate | nanu kṛtyasādhyatāniścayaviṣayasumervānayānādau pravṛttivāraṇāya kṛtisādhyatājñānasyāpi pravṛttihetutvabhabhyupeyam | na ca kṛtyasādhyatāniścayasya pratibandhakatvakalpanayaivopapattāvalaṃ kṛtisādhyatājñānasya hetutayeti vācyam | tādṛśaniścayābhāvatvena kāraṇatvāpekṣayā kṛtisādhyatājñānatvena kāraṇatve lāghavāt tatkalpanasyaivocitatvādityāśaṅkya nirācaṣṭe yadyapītyādinā | etat -
pravartanātvam | lokata evāvagamyata iti | yāgo matkṛtisādhyaḥ, matkṛtisādhyatvavirodhidharmānadhikaraṇatvādityādyanumānenetyarthaḥ | svargādīṣṭa- sādhanatvaṃ tu na lokato'vagantuṃ śakyate api tu vedenaiveti tatra śaktikalpanāvaśyakīti bhāvaḥ | anye tu kṛtisādhyatājñānaṃ na pravartakaṃ, tadasādhyatvena niścite pravṛttistu na, tata iṣṭābhāvena vṛthā śramajanakatvena dveṣāt | 'cikīrṣānurodhena tatra śaktikalpanamāvaśyakamanyathā kṛtisādhyatājñānaviraheṇa kṛtisādhyatvaprakārakecchātmakacikīrṣānupapatte'riti tu na śaṅkyam, lokata eva tadanugamena cikīrṣopapatteḥ | na cāniṣṭajanake udāsīne ca 'kaṣṭa karme'ti nyāyena vṛthā śramajanakatvena dveṣādevāpravṛttyupapattāviṣṭa- sādhanatājñānasyāpi pravṛttihetutānāpattirii vācyam, iṣṭājanake āntarālikaśrame dveṣābhāvadaśāyāmapravṛtteḥ sarvasiddhatayā tadanurodhena tajjñanasya taddhetutvādityāhuḥ | nanu madhuviṣasaṃpṛktānnabhojanādau pravṛttikaraṇāya balavadaniṣṭājanakatvajñānasyāpi pravṛttihetutvaṃ kalpanīyaṃ, tathā ca tatrāpi śaktirāvaśyakītyāśaṅkāṃ nirākurute balavaditi | balavaduttayā yāgapākāderapi nāntarīyakālpaduḥkhajanakatayā yāgādau pravṛttyanāpattinirastā | dveṣābhāveneti | vahṇāyāsasādhye bahutaraduḥkhajanakatayā pravṛttyanāpattyā balavadaniṣṭājanakatvajñānaṃ na pravṛttihetuḥ, api tu tajjanyaduḥkhaviṣayakadveṣaprayuktastadviṣayakadveṣastatra pravṛttau pratibandhakastadabhāvo heturityavaśyamāśrayaṇīyam | yāgādijanyasvargādiphale rāgautkaṭyena yāgādijanyaduḥkhe dveṣānutpādena tatra pravṛttiniṣpratyūhaiva, evaṃ ca tadabhāvādeva bhadhuviṣasaṃpṛktānnabhojane pravṛttinirvāhe tenānyathāsiddhaṃ balavadaniṣṭā- janakatvajñānaṃ na pravṛttiheturityarthaḥ | nanu nāntarīyakaduḥkhādhikaduḥkhājanakatvaṃ balavadaniṣṭājanakatvaṃ, tajjñānasya hetutayā na bahṇāyāsasādhye pravṛttyanāpattistajjanyabahutaraduḥkhāsyāpi balavattvābhāvāt, na cāvaśyakadveṣābhāvena tasyānyathāsiddhiriti vācyam, svatantrānvayavyatirekaśālitayoktajñānasya hetutvāvaśyakatvāt | dveṣābhāvadaśāyāṃ tathāvidhajñānaṃ vinā pravṛttyāpatteścetyā- śaṅkya vbhicārānna hetutvaṃ tasyetyāha āstiketyādinā | āstikatvotkīrtanaṃ parakīyasundarīgamane narakasādhanatājñānasattvāvaśyakatvasūcanāya, tena tathāvidhajñānavidhuranāstikasya parakīyakāminīpravṛttau na vyabhicārastasya balavadaniṣṭājanakatvajñānasyāpi saṃbhavāditi śaṅkāvyudāsaḥ |
'daśāyā'mityantena tadabhāvadaśāyāṃ pravṛttau narakājanakatvajñānasya bhramarūpasya saṃbhavena tasya vbhicārābhāve'pi na kṣatiriti sūcitam | vyabhicārā - vyatirekavyabhicārāt | tathā ca niyatānvayavtirekāvasiddhau, kkācitkānvaya- vyatirekau tvakicitkarau, tādṛśajñānaṃ vinā dveṣābhāvadaśāyāṃ pravṛttāviṣṭāpattiśceti bhāvaḥ | upasaṃharati tasmāditi | kṛtisādhyatvasya lokāvagatatvāt, balavadaniṣṭānanuvandhitvasya ca pravṛttyanupayogādityarthaḥ | iṣṭeti | iṣṭasādhanatvamevātra pravartanātvena vācyamityarthaḥ, yathāśrutaṃ tu na saṃgacchate, kṛtisādhyatvasyāpi pravartanātvāt | kṛtisādhyatājñānasya hetutvābhāvābhiprāyako vāyaṃ granthaḥ | puṃsāmiti | puṃsāṃ - prāṇinām | iṣṭābhyupāyatvāt iṣṭābhyupāyatvam - iṣṭasādhanatvaṃ tasmāt | anyaḥ pravartako na - pravartakajñānaviṣayo na, api tviṣṭasādhanatvameva tathetyarthaḥ | kṛtisādhyatājñānantvanyetumatarītyā pravṛttihetureva neti bāvaḥ | nanu kathaṃ tasya pravartanāpadavyavahāryatvamata āha pravṛttīti | propasṛṣṭavṛterhetumaṇṇijantād bhāve yuci niṣpannapravartanāpadaṃ pravṛttiprayojakavyāpāraparamiti bhāvaḥ | ata eva 'sarva ime svabhūtyartha yatante' iti 'hetumati ca' iti sūtre bhāṣye uktam | atradaṃ bodhyam 'jyotiṣṭomena svargakāmo yajete'tyādau devatoddeśena dravyatyāgo yajyarthaḥ phalaṃ' tasya tadarthakṛtyādirūpabhāvanāṃ prati karaṇatvenānvayaḥ, tasyāmeveṣṭasādhanatvānvayaḥ, evaṃ ca bhāvanādatasyeṣṭakaraṇatvasya liṅābhidhāne'pi bhāvanābhāvyanivartakatvarūpabhāvanānirūpitayāganiṣṭhakaraṇatvasyānabhihitatayā tatsamānādhikaraṇajyotiṣṭomādipadāt tṛtīyāsiddhiḥ, jyotiṣṭomādināmadheyamapi yāgasyaiva, tadghaṭitakarmakalāpe tathā vyavahārastu gauṇastasya svargakaraṇatvavyavahāro'pyevameveti | ayaṃ bhāvaḥ iṣṭasādhanatvajñānasya pravṛttisāmānye hetutvāt tasyaiva vidhipratyayārthatvamucitaṃ, na tu liṅādighaṭitaprayogādirūpapravartānāyāḥ, tasyā vācyatvapakṣe'pi āptoktayā tayā iṣṭasādhanatvamanumāyaiva pravṛttirityavaśyamabhyupeyam, tathā ca yajetetyādivākyajanyabodhottaraṃ sarvatra tadanumititatsādhanasāmagrīkalpanāyāmati- gauravaṃ, vyāptyādimatividhurāṇāmanubhavasiddhapravṛttyanirvāhaśca,
na caitanmate bahuvittāyāsasādhyajyotiṣṭomādau 'jyotiṣṭomene'tyādivākyāt pravartakajñānānirvāhaḥ, viśiṣya svargādirūpaphalasādhanatājñānasyaiva tatrāpekṣitatvāt,
yajetetyāditaśceṣṭasādhanaṃ yāgo yāgāviṣayiṇī pravartanetyādibodhasyaivotpatteḥ, svargatvādyavacchinnasādhanatvaśaktau śattayānantyamiti vācyaṃ, tadādinyāyenecchā- viṣayatvāvacchedakatvenopalakṣaṇībhūtena svargatvādīnanugamayya tadavaccinne sādhanatve śaktisvīkāreṇādoṣāt, cikīrṣādinānyathāsiddhistu na, vyāpāreṇa vyāpāriṇo- 'nyathāsiddhatvābhāvāt, anyathā saṃnikarṣeṇendriyasyāpi pratyakṣaṃ pratyanyadhāsiddhayā- 'kāraṇātvāpatteḥ, svatantrānvayavyatirekitayendriyaṃ nānyathāsiddhamiti cet, prakṛte'pi tulyam, nanvidaṃ 'neṣṭasādhanaṃ tasmāt tvaṃ kurvi'ti prayogānupapattiḥ, paunaruttayāpatteḥ | na cedaṃ paunaruttayamekāntata iṣṭasādhanatvabodhanāya, kiṃ ca pravartanātveneṣṭasādhanatvasya liṅādivācyatayā na paunaruttayameveti vācyaṃ, tasmādityasyānanvayāpatteḥ, abhede hetuhetumadbhāvāsaṃbhavāditi cenna, kurvityasyāvaśyakartavyārthakatayā'doṣāt, paramate'pya'tra mayā pravartito'tastvamidaṃ kurvi'tyatretthameva vācyatvācca, na ca 'śajñā pravartito'ha'mityādivyavahārāt pravartanā pravartakapurūpaniṣṭhā saiva vidhipratyayenocyate, ata eva 'vidhiniyoga'iti vātsyāyano'pyāha, iṣṭasādhanatvantu na pravartakapuruṣaniṣṭhamiti na talliṅādivācyamiti vācyam, iṣṭasādhanatvasyāpi svajñāpanavattvasaṃbhandhena pravartakaniṣṭhatvādiṣṭasādhanatājñānasyāpi tanniṣṭhatvāt, pravartyeṣṭasādhanatvaṃ jñātvaiva vākyaprayogakaraṇāt, liṅādyantaprayogarūpavyāpārasyāpi 'jyotiṣṭomene'tyādi- pravartakavedaniṣṭhatvāttadabhāvācca, tadbodhānanubhāvācca, lāghavātpravṛttyanukūladharmasyaiva pravarttanāpadārthatvaṃ, na tu pravarttakaniṣṭhatvasya tatrāntarbhāva ityasyāpi vaktuṃ śakyatvācca, na ca tathā 'hetumati ca' iti sūtre bhāṣye 'pṛcchatu mā bhavānityatra ṇic kasmānna bhavati' iti praśne, uttaram 'akartṛtvāt' iti | kriyāviśiṣṭapreraṇāyāṃ ṇic, 'hetumati ca' iti sūtreṇa hetuvyāpāre ṇijvidhānāt, kartuḥ prayojakasya hetutvānniṣkriyadravyamātrapreraṇāyāṃ loṅvidhānāditi tadāśayaḥ, taduktam 'dravyamātrasya tu praiṣe pṛcchayāderloḍ vidhīyate |
sakriyasya prayogastu yadā sa viṣayo ṇicaḥ ||' iti |
prayojanaṃ - prayogaḥ, preraṇamiti yāvat,na ca sakriyasya preraṇā nirarthaketi vācyam, ālasyādinā virāmābhāvaśaṅkayā tatsārthakyāt, nanu saṃbhāvitatattatkriyākartṛtvavāneva preṣyate, tatra ṇic pratyaya iṣyate, 'tatprayojaka' ityatra tatpadena vyāpārāviṣṭa eva gṛhyate ityatra nāgraha ityaśayena 'athāpi
kathaṃcit kartā syādevamapi na doṣaḥ, loṭoktatvādasminna bhaviṣyati' iti dvitoyamuttaramuktaṃ tadvirodhaḥ, iṣṭasādhanatvasya loṭoktatve'pi tasya ṇijarthatvābhāvena tadasaṃgateḥ spaṣṭatvāditi vācyaṃ, loṭā pravartanātvena rūpeṇa pravartanāsāmānyasyokterityāśayāt, yadvā loṭoktatvaṃ gamyamānaṃ, tathā ca loḍantaprayogeṇa śābdabodhottaraṃ preraṇāyāḥ pratīyamānatvānna ṇijiti tātparyādityāhurmaṃṇḍanamiśrānuyāyinaḥ |
anye tu mīmāṃsakāḥ śābdakāścoktabhāṣyādibalāt pravartaneṣṭasādhanatvātiriktaiva viṅādyartha iti pratipannāḥ, sa ca dyotyaiva, pacetetyādito vināpi pratyayaṃ tatpratīteḥ, ata eva tasyā dhātvarthaviśeṣaṇatvaṃ, dyotyatve'pi tasyā viśeṣyatvamiti tu na, dyotyārthānāṃ viśeṣaṇatāyā eva 'hetumati ca' iti sūtrabhāṣyasiddhatvena yuktatvāt, kiṃ ca tasyā viśeṣyatve pravartyasya pradhāna- dhātvarthavyāpārāśrayatayā'kartṛtve tatra pacetetyādyanupapattiḥ, kiṃ ca 'svādhyāyo'dhyetavya' ityādivākyāt pravartanāviśeṣaṇakasyaiva bodhasyotpādena sarvatra tasyā viśeṣaṇatvasvīkārasyaivocitatvācceti prāhuḥ |
yattu prābhākarāḥ- āśutaravināśino yāgādeḥ kālāntarabhāvisvargādi- sādhanatvasya grahītumaśakyatvānneṣṭasādhanatvaṃ vidhyarthaḥ, dvārānupasthityā ca na paraṃparayā sādhanatvasyāpi grahaḥ saṃbhavati, yogyatājñānasya śābdabuddhihetutvāt, sā-cānvayaprayojakarūpavattvaṃ, yathā 'ghaṭena jalamāhare'tyatra sacchidretaraghaṭatvaṃ, na caivamapi sādhanatvasāmānyena bodha upapadyata eveti vācyaṃ, ekāviśeṣabādhe sāmānyajñānasya taditaraprakārakatvaniyamāt, tasmāt kāryatvarūpeṇāpūrva liḍhādiśakyaṃ, kāryatvarūpeṇa ghaṭādau śaktigrahāt yogyatāvaśādapūrvavyaktilābhaḥ, na hi yāgaviṣayakaṃ kāryamapūrvādanyat saṃbhavati, na ca jñānādikaṃ tādṛśaṃ saṃbhavatyeveti vācyaṃ, kārye svargakāmasya niyojyaniyojakabhāvalakṣaṇānvayāṅgīkāreṇādoṣāt, tathā hi kāmī niyojyo, niyojakaṃ kārya, niyojakatvaṃ tatkāmikāryatayā pratīyamānatvam, atra cānvayaprayojakaṃ kāmyasādhanatvaṃ, na hi kāmī kāmyādanyat kāmyāsādhanatvena jñātaṃ kāryatayāvaiti | yāgaviṣayakajñānādikaṃ na kāmyasvargādisādhanamato yogyatābalādyāgaviṣayakaṃ kārya svargakāmīyaṃ, svargakāpro yāgaviṣayakakāryavāniti vā śābdānubhavo jāyamāno'pūrvālambano bhavati, evaṃ ca kāmye klṛptaśaktirliṅagadirnimittikayorniṣedhe cā'pūrvavācakaḥ, phalāśravaṇācca tatra
phalālābhaḥ, vaidikakarmaṇyuddeśyatvena bodhitāpūrvamuddiśyaiva hi pravartate, evaṃ ca kāmye phalāvāptirānuṣaṅgikī, nitye'pyapūrvasyoddeśyasya sattvā pravṛttiḥ, tasmāt kṛtyuddeśyarūpaṃ kāryameva śakyamiti procuḥ |
atra tārkikādayaḥ pratyavatiṣṭhante, apūrvasya vācyatve mānābhāvaḥ, na coktānupapattireva mānām, ekaviśeṣabodhe'pi sāmānyapūpeṇaiva taditarabodhaḥ, 'ghaṭena jalamāhare'tyatra hi yogyatayā vastuto yaśchidrarahito ghaṭaḥ sa ghaṭatvenaiva bodhaviṣayo bhavati, na tu sacchidretaratvaviśiṣṭaghaṭatvena, tadupasthāpakābhāvāt, na ca lakṣaṇayā ghaṭapadenaiva tena rūpeṇopasthitirmānābhāvāt, na cānvayaprayojaka- rūpattvapūpayogyatājñānasya hetutayā tadupasthitirāvaśyakīti vācyaṃ, vṛttyā padajanyapadārthopasthitereva śābadbodhahetutayā tena rūpeṇa bhānāsaṃbhavāt, upasthitābādhitapadārthāntarabhānaprasaṅgācca, evaṃ ca sādhanatvasāmānya- bodhānupapattiḥ, nanu tathāpyaśutaravināśinaḥ kālāntarabhāviphalajanakatve phalasamayasyāpi vyāpārajanakatvasya yogyatayā'pūrvānupasthitau tādṛgayogyatā- jñānāsaṃbhavādyagādāvapīṣṭasādhanatvaṃ grahītumaśakyameveti cenna, dravadravyatvasya karakātmakajale'pi sattvena tadabhiprāyaka'payasā sicatī'tivākyasyāpi yogyatvāpattyā tajjñānadaśāyāṃ 'seko naitajjalakaraṇaka' iti bādhakapramāsamaye'pi tajjalakaraṇakaśābdabodhāpattyā cānvayaprayojakarūpavattvasya yogyatātvānabhyu- pagamāt, api tu tatpadārthe tatpadārthavattvasyaiva tattvābhyupagamāt, saṃśayaniścaya- sādhāraṇatajjñānasyaiva kāraṇatayā tanniścayāsaṃbhave'pi saṃśayātmakatajjñānasya saṃbhavāt, kāryatājñānasya loke pravatakatvadarśanāt, kāryatvenāpūrvameva vidhyartha ityapi na yuktaṃ, kṛtijanyatvarūpasya dhātvarthaniṣṭhasyaiva pravartakatvādapūrvasya tathātvasaṃbhāvanāpi na, tādṛśasya lokata eva saṃbhavāccetyādyuktameveti | yatu niṣphale sandhyāvandanādau bādhādiṣṭasādhanatvaṃ na vidhyartha iti, tanna, pravṛttimātre iṣṭasādhanatājñālasya hetutayā tatra pravṛttyanupapatteḥ | na ca vedabodhitāpūrvoddeśenaiva pravṛttiriti vācyaṃ, tadrūpameveṣṭamādāyoṣṭasādhanatvasya vidhyarthatvopapatteḥ | vastutaḥ sukhaduḥkhābhāvānyatararūpatvābhāveneṣṭasādhanatvābhāvena ca taduddeśena pravṛttirna saṃbhavatyena, na ca vedabodhitatvānniṣphave'pi pravṛttiḥ, niṣphalatvajñānadaśāyāṃ vedabodhitatvajñāne'pyapravṛttaḥ, taduktaṃ 'prayojanamanuddiśya na mando'pi pravartate' iti, tasmāt pratyavāyaparihāra phalaṃ nitye'pi bhavati | na ca 
'aphalākāṅakṣibhiryajño vidhidṛṣṭo ijyate |
yaṣṭavyameveti manaḥ samādhāya sa sāttvikaḥ ||'
iti bhagavadragītāvirodha iti vācyam |
'yajño dānaṃ tapaḥ karma pāvanāni manīṣiṇām |
etānyapi tu karmāṇi saṅgaṃ tyaktvā phalāni ca ||
kartavyānīti me pārya ! niścitaṃ matamuttamam ||'
ityaṣṭādaśādhyāye pāvanatvokteḥ | saṅgaḥ - kartṛtvābhimānaḥ, tathā cānuddiṣṭasyāpi pratyavāyaparihārasya phalatvena phalāmusaṃdhānaṃ vināpi kriyāmāṇasya pāvatvaṃ bhavatīti bhāvaḥ | ata eva nityajyotiṣṭomādeḥ svargādisiddhaye karaṇe'pi nityaprayogasiddhiriti siddhāntaḥ saṃgacchate | nityasya sarvathā niṣphalatvaniyame tu tadasaṃgatiḥ spaṣṭaiva, kiṃ cārthavādikamapi phalaṃ rātrisatranyāyena tatra vaktuṃ śakyaṃ, rātrisatre hi 'jyotirjyoti'rityādikaphalaśūnyavākyabodhite 'pratitiṣṭhanti ha vai ya etā rātrīrupayantī'tyarthavādoktaṃ pratiṣṭhātmakaṃ phalaṃ svīkṛtaṃ, tadvadatrāpi, 
'sandhyāmupāsate ye tu satataṃ saṃśitavratāḥ |
vidhūtapāpāste yānti brahmalokaṃ samātanam ||' 
ityarthavādoktaphalasaṃbhavaḥ, anyathā etasya 
'etat sandhyātrayaṃ proktaṃ brābmaṇyaṃ yadadhiṣṭhitam |
yasya nāstyādarastatra na sa brāhmaṇa ucyate ||' 
ityasya vacanasya ca vaiyarthyāpattiḥ | na ca stāvakatvamātreṇopayoga iti vācyaṃ, 'sarvebhyaḥ kāmebhyo darśabūrṇamāsau, sarvebhyaḥ kāmebhyo jyotiṣṭoma' ityetayo- rapi tathātvāpatteḥ, na ca caturthīsācivyena phalabodhakatvam, 'ūrjo'varudhyai' ityatrevārthavādatvenaivopapattoḥ, kiṃ ca stāvakatvenopayoge'pi stutivaiyarthya durvārameva, prarocanārthā seti na śaṅkayaṃ, prarocanāyā eva niṣphalatvāpatteḥ, 'bahudugdheyaṃ dhenu'riti vākyāprāmāṇyajñānasattve tadvākyādiva, arthavādānāmapramāṇyaṃ jānataḥ pravṛttyasamavetapravṛttiphalakatāyā svīkartumaśakyatvāt, nanu svargādyarthakatāyāmaprāmāṇye'pi tattātparyaviṣayībhūtaprāśastye prāmāṇyaṃ saṃbhavatyeveti cenna, tattatphalasādhanatvātiriktaprāśastyasya kutrāpyakalpanāt, tajjñānasya pravṛttyanupayogācca, tasmāt svārthe evaiṣāṃ prāmāṇyamabhyupagantavyam, evaṃ ca 'sandhyāmupāsīte'ti vidhita iṣṭāvagame'pi avāntararūpeṇa bodhanadvārā
śīghretpattipratyāyanadvārā vā pravṛttiviśeṣasaṃpattiphalakā arthavādā iti dhyeyam | ata eva 'sarvaśaktau pravṛttiḥ syāttathābhūtopadeśāt' iti sarvaśaktyadhikaraṇa- pūrvapakṣasūtre 'kāmyaṃ yathā phalāyopadiṣṭaṃ tathā nityamapi' ityuktaṃ bhaṭṭācāryaiḥ | na ca nitye'pi phalajanakatvasvīkāre nityatvahāniriti vācyaṃ, pratyavāya- parihārātiriktaphalājanakatvarūpasya, pratyavāyakāraṇībhūtābhāvapratiyogitvarūpasya vā nityatvasyākṣatatvāt | kāmyatvamapi syādityapi na, iṣṭāpatteḥ, pratyavāyaparihīrātiriktaphalajanakatvam, uktanityabhinnatvaviśiṣṭaphalajanakatvaṃ kāmyatvamityabhyupagamādvā | nanu yatrārthavādikaṃ phalaṃ nāsti tatra pratyavāyaparihārasya phalatvaṃ na yuktaṃ, duritadhvaṃsarūpasya tasya sukhaduḥkhābāvānyatvādiṣṭājanakatvāccecchāviṣayatvābhāvāt, na ca pratyavāya- parihīrasya duḥkhānutpādaphalakatvādiṣṭatvamiti vācyaṃ, prāgabhāvarūpasya tasyoktakrameṇecchāviṣayatvarūpaphalātvābhāvāt | na ca duḥkhātyantābhāva eva phalatāmāpnotviti vācyam, prāgabhāvātyantābhāvayovirodhena tadā tasya tatrāsattvāditi cenna, tayovirodhasya dīdhitikāreṇaiva tiraskṛtatvāt, na ca tathāpi tasya nityatayā tajjanakatvāsaṃbhavaḥ svasvavyāpyetarayāvatkāraṇasattve yatsattve yadavyavahitottarakṣaṇe yatsattvaṃ yadasattve yadasattvamityevaṃrūpasya yogakṣemasādhāraṇa- janyajanakabhāvasya saṃbhavādityalaṃ pallavitena | [ata evāntarā śyāmadaśāyāmatyantābhāvaviṣayako 'raktaṃ rupaṃ nāstī'ti pratyaya upapadyate, dhvaṃsādyavalambana evāsāviti cenna, madhye rakte'pi tathāpratyayaprasaṅgāditi dik |]
'na kalañjaṃ bhakṣaye'dityādau nañsamabhivyāhṛtaliṅāderaniṣṭasādhanatvamartho, nañ tātparyagrāhako, na tvabhāvārthakaḥ, kalañjabhakṣaṇādāvapi tṛptyādirūpeṣṭa- sādhanatvasattvena tadabāvasya bodhayitumaśakyatvāt, nāpi kalañjabhakṣaṇābhāva i,ṭasādhanamiti bodhaḥ svīkartu yuktaḥ, pratyayārthaprakṛtyarthayorantarā padārthāntara- praveśasyāvyutpannatvāt, 'nānupamṛdya prādurbhāvā'dityādāvapyāropitaprādurbhāvahetukatva- bodhasyaiva śabdato'ṅgīkāreṇa na prakṛtipratyayārthamadhyaniveśo nañarthābhāvasyeti bodhyam | prādurbhāvābhāvahetukatvabodhastu mānasaḥ, kiṃ ca tathābodhāṅgīkāre kalañjabhakṣaṇādenivṛttijanakajñānānutpādanena nañghaṭitatādaॉsavākyasya nivarta- katvānupapattiḥ, na ca yadabhāvo yasyeṣṭasādhasatayā bodhyate tattasyāniṣṭasādhanamiti
vyāptyā kalañjabhakṣeṇe'niṣṭasādhanatvānumānānnoktadoṣa iti vācyam, aprayojakatvāt, grīṣmasamaye sūkṣmakambalādyabhāvasya sukaviśeṣajanakatvabodhe'pi tasyāniṣṭasādhamatvābhāvāt, sūkṣmakambalādeḥ śītasamaye'niṣṭajanakatvābhave'pi grīṣmakāle duḥkhātmakāniṣṭajanakatvasyānubhūyamānatayoktavyāpteḥ susthatvamiti cetathāpi vākyasya sākṣānnivartakajñānajanakatvānirvāhaḥ, ttathāvidhavyāptiprati- saṃdhānaśūnyasyānumityanupapattiśca, kiṃ ca vidhiniṣedhavākyayostathā satyekārthatva- meva syāt, tacca na yuktam 
'antaraṃ yādṛśaṃ loke brahmāhatyāśvamedhayoḥ |
dṛśyate tādṛgevedaṃ vidhānapratiṣadhyoḥ || iti |'
yanmate iṣṭasādhanatvātiriktapravartanā liṅādyarthaḥ, tanmate nivṛtyanukūla- vyāpārarūpanivartanāṃ liṅādiḥ pratipādayati, nañ dyotakaḥ, nañaḥ svasaṃbandhi- pratipakṣabodhakatvasvabhāvāt, ata evāstītyanena saṃbadhyamāno nañ sattvapratipakṣa- bhūtamasattvaṃ gamayati |
pare tu 'na kalañja'mityādāvāropitaṃ kalañjabhakṣaṇaṃ pravartanāviṣaya iti vācyo'rthaḥ, kalañjabhakṣaṇābhāvaḥ pravartanāviṣaya ityudīcyo mānasabodhaḥ, 'tattu samanvayāt' iti sūtrabhāṣye śaṅkarācāryā api 'na brāhmaṇaṃ hanyā'dityādau hantyarthānurāgyeṣa nañ, eṣa eva ca nañsvabhāvo yat svasaṃbandhino'bhāvabodhanaṃ, niṣedhyābhāvabuddhiśca niṣedhyaviṣayaudāsīnye kāraṇamityāhuḥ | audāsīnyaṃ - nivṛttiḥ | pravṛttyabhāvasamānādhikaraṇo 'nivartate' iti padagamyaḥ kriyāviśeṣo nivṛttirna tu pravṛttyabhāvarūpā, nivṛttyabhāvasya pravṛttirūpatāpatteḥ, vinigamanāvirahāt, tasmāt pravṛttivannivṛttirapi padārthāntarameveti prāhuḥ |
anye tu pūrvamupavaṇitamudīcyabodhameva vākyārthabodhamaṅgīcakruḥ, etanmate nānupamṛdya prādurbhāvāt'paṭo na daṇḍa'dityādāviva vyutpattivaicitryeṇa prakṛtipratyayayorantarā nañarthābhāvasya praveśāṅgīkārānnānupapattiritidhyeyaṃ | nanu gaṅgāsrānādito'pi svargātpatteḥ purāṇaprasiddhatayā 'darśapūrṇamāsābhyāṃ svargakāmo yajete'tyādiśrutyā darśapūrṇamāsāderapi svargasādhanatvabodhanena tadvyabhicāreṇa jyotiṣṭomādau tatsādhanatvabādhāt kathaṃ tadbodhanīyamiti cedatrāhurbhūṣaṇa- kārādayaḥ, vijātīyasvargasādhanatve eva śattayā tādṛśasvargasādhanatvameva tena vākyena bodhyate, tacca tatrābādhitameva, yadyapi 'svargakāme'ti samudāyāntargata 
'svargapadasamutthāpitasvargasādhanatvameva pratyāsattinyāyena liṅśakhyamityanumanyante nibandhakṛta, svargapadaṃ ca svargatvenaiva bodhakaṃ na tu vijātīyasvargatveneti noktarītyā vijātīyasvargasādhanatve śaktisaṃbhavastathāpi vākyāntaraparyālocanasācivyena vijātīyasvargasādhanatve manasopasthite śaktigrahasaṃbhavena nānupapattiḥ, haryādipadavannānārthatvamiṣṭameveti | rāmakṛṣṇabhaṭṭācāryāstu na vaijātyāntarbhāveṇa śaktirapi tu svargasamānādhikaraṇābhāvapratiyogitāvacchedakatve'bāve ca khaṇḍaśaḥ śaktistādṛśāvacchedakatvasya vijātīyasvargasamānādhikaraṇābhāvapratiyogitāvaccheda- katvāvacchinnapratiyogitākatvasaṃbandhena liṅpādarthe'bhāve'nvayaḥ, tena vaijātyasya pūrvamanupasthitāvapi na kṣatiḥ, uktasaṃbandhena svargasamānādhikaraṇābhāva- pratiyogitāvacchedakatvābhāvaśca tādṛśāvacchedakatvaviśeṣābhāvo na tu tatsāmānyābhāvaḥ, sa ca jyotiṣṭomatvādāvabādhita eva, tādṛśāvacchedakatvā- bhāvasya svāśrayajyotiṣṭomatvavattvasaṃbandhena jyotiṣṭomādāvanvaya iti na 'padārthaḥ, padārthene'tyādivyutpattivirodhaḥ | gurudharmasya pratiyogitāvacchedakatvapakṣe 'kamvugrīvādimānnāstī'ti vākyād ghaṭatvāvacchinnapratiyogitāvakatvasaṃbandhena nañarthābhāve'pyanvayabodhasyābhyupagamāt, ata eva yatkiṃcidghaṭavati na tādṛśavākyaprayogaḥ prāmāṇikaḥ, api cānvayitāvacchedakāvacchinnapratiyogitā- katvavyutpattirnañsamabhivyāhāraviṣayeti na tadvirodha iti samādadhire |
navyatārkikāstvanyathāsiddhinirūpakatānavacchedakaniyatottaravartitāvacchedaka- dharmavatsvargakatvameva svargakāraṇatvaṃ, tādṛśadharmaścottarakāle kalpyo jātiviroṣaḥ, śābdabuddhau niyatottaravartitāvacchedakatvātmakasāmānyadharmeṇa bhāsate, tādṛśaṃ svargakāraṇatvaṃ ca yoge'bādhitameva, niyottaravartitāvacchedakatvaṃ ca hetu- tāvacchedakadharmāvacchinnatadvayāpārānyatarābhāvādhikaraṇatīviśiṣṭotpattikṣaṇāvacchinnādhikaraṇatānirūpakatānavacchedakatvam, adhikaraṇatāvaiśiṣṭyaṃ ca 'svāvacchedaka- kṣaṇāvyavahitottarakṣaṇāvacchinnasvāśrayaniṣṭhatva'saṃbandhena | bhavati hyetādṛśaṃ daṇḍaniyatottaravartitāvacchedakatvaṃ ghaṭatve utpattikṣaṇāvacchinnakapālaniṣṭha- ghaṭādhikaraṇatāyāṃ daṇḍatvāvacchinnatadvyāpārānyatarābhāvādhikaraṇatāvaiśiṣṭyenokta- saṃbandhenāpi tūtpattikṣaṇāvacchinnaghaṭādyadhikaraṇatāyāmeva tannirūpakatānava- cchedakatvasya ghaṭatve susthatvāt | athavā svarganiṣṭhadharmāvacchinnaniyata- pūrvavartitāvacchedakadharmavattvameva svargakāraṇatvam, niyatapūrvavartitāvacchedakatvaṃ ca
svarganiṣṭhadharmāvacchinnāvyavahitapūrvakālāvacchedena tadadhikaraṇaniṣṭhābhāvapratiyogitā- navacchedakadharmavattvarūpadaiśikavyāpakatāyāḥ svāśrayatva svāśrayanirūpitavyāpāra- tvānyatarasaṃbandhenāvacchedakatvam, anyatarasaṃbandhena tathāvidhavyāpakatāvacchedakatvaṃ ca tena saṃbandhena tathāvidhavyāpakatānatiriktavṛttittvādirūpaṃ bodhyam | jyotiṣṭomatvādeḥ svāyanirūpitavyāpāratvasaṃbandhenādṛṣṭātmakavyāpāragatatādṛśa- vyāpakatāvacchedakatvasattvāt svarganiṣṭhadharmāvacchinnanirūpitaniyatapūrvavartitāvaccheda- katvaṃ nirābādhameva, etena niyatottaratvādyuktakāraṇatvasya vidhyarthatāmate yāgadharmikatādṛśakāraṇatāgrahasya pravṛttihetutābhyupagantavyā | tadapekṣayā tadghaṭakaniyatottaravartitāvacchedakadharmavattvātmakayāgasādhyatvaprakārakasvargadharmikajñānasya hetutve tādṛśasādhyatvasya vidhyarthatve ca lādhavamitoṣṭasādhanatvasya vidhyarthatvaṃ, pravṛttiprayojakatvaṃ ca bhajyeteti dūṣaṇaṃ nirastam, vyāpakatāghaṭutakāraṇatāyāḥ sādhyatā'ghaṭutatayā lāghavena pravartakatvādisaṃbhavāt kādṛśasādhanatāśarīre vyāpakatāvacchedakatvenaiva kāraṇatāvacchedakasya praveśāt, yatra tasya viśiṣyānupasthitiḥ kāraṇaṃ ca nyūnavṛttinātiprasaktena vā dharmeṇopalakṣitaṃ, tatra śabdāt, kāraṇatvagrahānupapattirna, nanu tathāpi taddharmaprakāra- kecchādhīnā pravṛttistaddharmāvacchinnanirūpitasādhanatādhīprayojyeti 'jyotiṣṭome' tyādivākyāt svargatvopalakṣitavaijātyāvacchinnanirūpitasādhanatājñānasattve'pi svargatvāvacchinnanirūpitasādhanatājñānaviraheṇa svargatvaprakārakecchādhīnapravṛttyanupa- pattiriti cenna, vahnitvādiprakārakecchātopi tṛṇādisamavadhāne'bhrāntapravṛtterānu- bhavikatvād bhūyaḥ sukhārthināmapi bhrāntirahitānāṃ kriyāviśeṣe pravṛtteśca, taddharma- prakārakaphalecchādhīnapravṛttau taddharmaprakāreṇa bhāsamānanirūpitasādhanatājñāna- syaivāpekṣitvāt, na hi vahlitvādikaṃ tṛṇādijanyatāvacchedakaṃ, tṛṇādikaṃ vināpi jāyamāne vahnau sattvāt, bhūyastvādikamarthavaśasaṃpannatayā na kasyāpi janyatāvacchedakaṃ, ghaṭatvāvacchinnārthi tantvādāvapi pravarteta, tantvāderapi ghaṭatvādyupalakṣitajanyasattvādiviśiṣṭanirūpitadravyatvāvacchinnasādhanatāvattvāditi na śaṅkyaṃ, phalānupadhānaniścayasya svātantryeṇa pratibandhakatvāt, phalopadhāyakatātmakasādhanatvasya liṅādiśakyatayā na ghaṭakāmastantumupādadīteti prayoga iti prāhuriti kṛtaṃ pallavaiḥ |
'hetuhetumatorliṅ' ityasyodaharaṇamāha yo brāhmaṇāyeti | avagoraṇaṃ -
hananārtha daṇḍādyudyamanam | 'brāhmaṇāye'ti 'patye reti' itivat saṃpradānatvāccaturthī, tathā ca yatkartṛkaṃ brāhmaṇoddeśyakaṃ daṇḍādyudyamanaṃ taddetukatādṛśapuruṣakarmakayamadūtakartṛkaśatayātanā iti vākyārthabodhaḥ | kāraka- viśiṣṭatādṛśakriyāyā yātanārūpakiyāyāṃ hetuhetumadbhāvasaṃbandhenānvayo na tu hetvādyarthakatvaṃ liṅaḥ, hetuhetumadbhūtakriyārthakadhātorliṅiti sūtrārthāt, tena saṃbandhenānvayabodhe tātparyagrāhakatvenaiva sūtrasya sārthakye tatra śaktikalpanā- yogāt |
bhūtamātre iti | mātraśabdo'vadhāraṇe ityabhipretyāha bhūtasāmānya iti | bhūtatvarūpasāmānyadharmeṇa vivakṣāyāṃ, na tu viśeṣarūpeṇa vivakṣāyāmityarthaḥ | tatra mānamāha bhūte ityadhikṛtyeti | kriyāyā'mityupalakṣaṇaṃ, kriyādhikaraṇāsamaye ityapi bodhyam | bhūtatvasya pratyayārthatvapakṣe tu pūrvoktasthale'abūd ghaṭa'ityutpattyarthakaprayoganirvāhe'pi vidyamānatve sattārthakatādṛśaprayogo'pi syāt, vartamānatvaṃ sapratiyogisamayavṛttitvasya tadānī ghaṭe sattvāt, kriyāyāṃ tadanvaye tu na doṣaḥ, ghaṭakartṛkātmadhāraṇānukūlayāvaddravyavyāpārasamūhe tasyāsattvāt, tādṛśabhūtatvasya buddhisthadhātuvācyayāvadavayavasamūhe evādhāradheyabhāvasaṃbandhenānvayā- bhyupagamāt | utpattestātparyaviṣayatve'pyutpattyavyavahitottakṣaṇe 'ghaṭo'bhū'diti prayogānupapattirdraṣṭavyā kartrādāvanvayābhyupagame |
iti bodha iti | tādṛśasamayavṛttitvaṃ bhūtatvamiti pakṣe vidyamānadhvaṃpratiyogisamayavṛttiratyādyākārako bodho'vaseyaḥ | yamatreti | ayaṃ - buddhistho vakṣyamāṇaḥ | 'saṃgraha' ityasya 'drṭavya' ityanena saṃbandhaḥ | ādya iti | adyatana ityarthaḥ | bhaidāditi | tathā cādye 'apākṣīd devadattaḥ' 'pacati' 'pakṣyatī'ti trividhaprayogo'pi dṛśyate | antyo - anadyatanaḥ |
anayoḥ - bhūtabhaviṣyatkālayoḥ | laṅiti | 'liṅninitte 'iti sūtreṇa vivakṣite ityartha iti kecid bruvate | vastutastu bhūtatvasyevānadyatanatvasyāpi bodhasyānubhavasiddhatayānabhūtatvena vivakṣita ityeva vyākhyocitā, anyathā bhūtatve'pi śaktina sidhyet, vastugatyā tādṛśabūtakālavṛttiryā kriyā tadarthakadhātorlṛṅ ityarthasvīkāreṇaivātiprasaṅgābhāvāt yuktayantaramiti prāguktamanusaṃdheyam | tatraiva - anadyanabhūtatve vivakṣite satyeva | tāddaśe bhaviṣyatīti | anadyatana- bhaviṣyatītyarthaḥ |
bhūte bhāvinīti | hetuhetumadbhāve - hetuhetumadbhāvādau sati, bhūtakāle bhaviṣyatkāle ityarthaḥ | tatra pramāṇamāha 'liṅnimitte' ityādinā | suvṛṣṭiriti | suvṛṣṭibhavanaṃ subhikṣabavanasya hetuḥ,subhikṣabhavanaṃ hetumaditi tatsamarpakābhyāmubhābhyamāpi dhātuvyaktibhyāṃ lṛṅ | hetuhetumadbhāvaḥ svarūpasannimittaṃ, na tu tatrāpi śaktiḥ, śābdabodhe'bhānāt | evaṃ kriyāniṣpattirapi mānāntaragatya, na tu vācyā | nanu suvṛṣṭibhavanasubhikṣabhāvanayoḥ sādhanavaikalyānna- yatānutpattiḥ svayamavagatya pratipādyate tatra bhaviṣyatvādaivānutpattiḥ, utpanna eva sattvādikrameṇa bhūta ityucyate, sāmagrīsamavadhāne saṃbhāvitotpattikaśca bhaviṣyannityucyate, ata ca sādhanābhāvāt saṃbhāvitāpi na tadutpattiriti cenna, abhūte abhaviṣyati ityapi bhūtatvabhaviṣyattve, āropyete niṣedhapratiyogitvāyeti doṣābhāvāt ; subhikṣabhavane hetu suvṛṣṭibhavanaṃ saṃbhāvayata eva hi taduktaḥ, evaṃ hi kriyāniṣpattiḥ pratipāditā bhavati | atra bhūtatvādyabhāvaniścayasattve'pi bhūtatvādiprakārakabodha āhāryāroṣātmaka evāvaśyamabhyupetavyaḥ | anyathā gate vādhaniścayasya bhasabdabodhaṃ pratyapratibandhakatve tvanāhāryāropātmako'pi saṃbhavati, yuktaṃ caitat, anyathā 'mukhaṃ candra' ityājyabhedarūpakādau, prahasanādau ca bādhaniścayasattve'pyanubhavasiddhaśābdabodhāpalāpāpattiḥ, kalahādo mithyābhilāpā- vacanācchābdabodhānupapattiśca, tatra śābdabodhānugame krodhānupapattiḥ, evam-'eṣa vandhyāsuto yātī'tyādivākyācchābdabodhānupagame sahṛdayāhlādānupapattiḥ, nanu 'mukhaṃ candra' ityādau candrādipadaṃ candradisadṛśe lākṣaṇikaṃ, tasya cābhedo'bādhita eva mukhe budhyate, evaṃ mithyābhiśāpasthale 'eṣa vandhyāsuta' ityādau ca śabdāt padārthopasthitau mānaso viśiṣṭabodhastena krodhādernānupapattiriti cenna, evaṃ hi 'candrasadṛśaṃ mukhaṃ' 'candro mukha'mita vākyayoranubhavasiddhavilakṣaṇabodhajanakatvānupa- pattiḥ, abhiśāpavacanādisthalemānasabodhāṅgīkāre sarvatraiva tathātvāpattau śabdaprāmāṇyocchedāpatteśca, ata eva khaṇḍanakṛtāpyuktam 
'atyantāsatyapi hyarthe jñānaṃ śabdaḥ karoti ca |' iti |
na caivaṃ yogyatājñānasya śābdabodhahetutvānupapattiḥ, iṣṭāpatteḥ, | na caivaṃ 'seko vahnikaraṇakaḥ' iti vādhaniścayasattve'pi śābdabodhāpattiḥ, iṣṭāpatteḥ, śābdabodhaṃ pri vādhaniścayasya pratibandhakatvamanaṅgīkurvadbhiryogyatājñānasya hetutāṃ svīkurvadbhiḥ parairapyāhāryayogyatājñānāt śābdabodhāṅgīkārācca, na caivaṃ sati
pravṛttirāpādanārhā vādhaniścayena tatra bodhe'prāmāṇyajñānajananenāgṛhītā- prāmāṇyakajñānasyaiva pravṛttijanakatvādityalaṃ pallavitena | lṛṅsamabhivyāhāre dhātorlṛṅo vābhāvo'pyarthaḥ, abhāve dhātvarthavyāpārasāyānvayaḥ, abhāvayośca prayojyaprayojakabhāvasaṃbandhenānvaya ityabhipretyāha atra vahnyabhinneti | arthāntare- 'pīti | yathā 'laṭ sme' ityādinā bhūtādāvapi laḍāderdarśanādityarthaḥ | darśanāntararītyeti | svamate tu sarvasminnukte'rthe śaktireva, na tu lakṣaṇeti bodhayituṃ 'darśanāntararītye'tyuktam | prasaṅgāllaḍādikramaniyāmakamāha eteṣāmiti | eteṣāṃ - lakārāṇām | anubandhakrama eveti | anubandhānm - akārādyanubandhānām | ukte'rthe mīmāṃsakavyavahāramapi pramāṇayati ata eveti | ḍita evādau tu na gaṇitāḥ, 'vartamāne laṭ' ityādinā pāṇininā prāk ṭitāmeva nirdeśāt | lakāreti | lakāraviśeṣāṇāṃ laḍādīnāmartha ityarthaḥ || 23 ||
iti kṣivaiyākaraṇabhūṣaṇasāravyākhyāyāṃ lakāraviśeṣārthanirūpaṇaṃ samāptam ||
<3>
subarthamāheti | padatvaprayojakatiṅarthanirūpaṇānantaraṃ tatparayojakasubartha- nirūpaṇamiti bhāvaḥ | nanu dvitīyādīnāṃ 'karmaṇi dvitīyā' ityādyanuśāsanabalāt karmādīnāṃ tadarthatvalābho nākṣaye vidhyabha vādityāśaṅkāmapaninīṣurāha-tathā hītyādinā | tacceti | tat - karma | cakārastvarthe |
karturīpsitatamamiti | 'karturīpsitatamam-' iti sūtreṇatasya karmasaṃjñāvidhānāt | nanvabhipretarūḍhepsitaśabdāttamapyatiśayitābhipretarūpārtha eva labhyate, nāśraya ityāśaṅkyāha-kriyājanyeti | tathā cāpnīteḥ saṃbandhārthakāt sannantāt karmaṇi vartamānakāle 'mativuddhipūjārthebhyaśve'ti ktā ta īpsitaśabdaḥ kriyāśabda upātto nābhipretārthako rūḍhaśabdaḥ tathā mati bhūḍkte' ityādau payaāde- rapi karmasaṃjñāpattiḥ, viśiṣṭabhojanasyābhipretatvena tasyānabhipretatvasya vaktumaśakyatvāt, ata eva kṛtabhojano'pi payovābhecchayā bhojane pravartata iti lokasiddham, evaṃ kriyāśabdādare tu prakṛṣṭopakārakatvamātravivakṣāyāṃ kriyājanyaphalāśrayatve'pi tattvenānuddeśyatayā na karmatvāpattitādavasthyam, spaṣṭaṃ cedaṃ prakṛtasūtrabhāṣyādau | 'vāraṇārthānām-' iti sūtrabhāṣye cāyaṃ kriyāśabda iti spaṣṭam | 'karturīpsitatamam-' ityatra 'kartu'riti 'ktasya ca vartamāne' iti kartari
ṣaṣṭhī, tathā ca kartuḥ samabhivyāhṛtadhātvarthavyāpārāśrayasyāptuṃ - saṃbaddhumiṣṭatamaṃ kārakaṃ karmasaṃjñāmiti sūtrārthaḥ, kartuḥ saṃbandhaśca kartṛpadārthaviśeṣaṇībhṛtakriyādvāraka eva grahītumucitaḥ, upasthitatvāt, sa ca tajjanyaphalāśrayatvarūpaḥ, tathā cepsitatamatvaṃ - tādṛśavyāpāraprayojyasamabhivyāhṛtadhātvarthaphalāśrayatvenoddeśyatvaṃ paryavasannam, 'prayojyatva'niveśo duhyādevibhāgādyanukūlavyāpārānukūlavyāpārārthakatve payaādeḥ karmatvasiddhiphalaka ityanyatra vistaraḥ |
nanu kriyāghaṭitakriyāprayojakatvādisaṃbandhasya kārakāntare'pi sattve kathaṃ tathāvidhaphalalābho'ta āha-kriyājanyeti | saṃjñāntaravidhānādiṣṭānurodhāccokta saṃbandha eva gṛhyate iti bhāvaḥ | athavā-'nanu sanā icchāyā bodhanādīpsitatama- padena tādṛśaphalavattvenoddeśyasyaiva lābhāt kriyājanyaphalāśrayamātroktirasaṃgatetyā- śaṅkyāha kriyājanyeti | karmaṇa eva - tādṛśaphalākṣayasyaiva | tathā ca tādṛśaphalavattvena karturuddeśyatvaṃ tādṛśasaṃjñāyāmevopayogi, na tu vācyakoṭau tasyāntarbhāva iti bāvaḥ | tatra yuktimāha 'tathā yuktaṃ ca ' iti | ityādī-tyādipadād 'gatibuddhi-' ityādeḥ parigrahaḥ, etatsūtravihitakarmasaṃjñā- sādhāraṇyārtha tādṛśaphalākṣaya eva dvitīyādeḥ śaktiriti bhāvaḥ | saṃgrahāditi | phalasyāpi hetutvena vivakṣāyāṃ paḍhcamī | evameva - dvitīyāderuktārthakatva- kalpanameva | īpsādeḥ śābdabodhe bhānenaivameva yuktamityayuktamityāśaṅkyāha īpsiteti | īpsitatvānīpsitatvayorityarthaḥ | nanu tathāpi kriyājanyaphalaviśiṣṭa evāśraye śaktirlabhyate, na tu kevalāśraya ityāśahkāṃ nirākaroti-tathā cetyādinā | tathā ca - kriyājanyaphalāśrayasya karmatve ca | pare tu tādṛ- phalāśrayatveneccheddośyatvasyāpi vācyatvamāvaśyakamata eva 'payasaudanaṃ bhuṅkte' ityādau tathāvidhaphalāśrayasyāpi payaādestādṛśoddośyatvavirahānna karmatvamiti prāhuḥ | nanvāśrayatvasya nirūpakādibhedād bhedena śaktyānantyaṃ syādityata āha tattvaṃ ceti | āśrayatvaṃ cetyarthaḥ | 'śakterbheda eve'ti bhāṣyasiddhānte tvāśrayatātvenāśrayatānāmanugamo bodhyaḥ | navyamate tu karmatvarūpaśaktimattvenāśrayaḥ śakya iti dhyeyam | utpattyāśrayatvādityādau 'karmate'tyasyānuṣaṅgeṇānvayaḥ | utpatterdhātvarthatvāditi | nirūpitametat prāka | jānātyāderjñānādimātrārthakatayā ghaṭādeḥ kriyājanyaphalāśrayatvābhāvāt karmatvānutpattirityāśaṅkāṃ pūrvoktāvaraṇa- bhaṅgānukūlavyāpārārthakatāmāśritya pariharati-jānātītyatreti | 'ghaṭaṃ
jānātī'tyādāvityarthaḥ, 'ghaṭāde'riti śeṣaḥ | atītānāgatayoḥ phalāśrayatvanupa- pattiḥ, asata āśrayatvābhāt, na ca jñānasamaye tajjanyāvaraṇabhaṅgasyātītādi- ghaṭe'sattvepi taddhaṭādisamayāvacchedena tadāśrayatvamakṣatameveti vācyam | vyāpārasya svaśūnyottarakālikaphalāranbhakatvāsaṃbhavāt, evaṃ svapūrvakālikaphalārambhakatvā- saṃbhavācca, kāryāvyavahitapūrvakṣaṇavartina eva phalārambhakatvādityāśaṅkāṃ pariharati atītetyādinā | kālāntare phalārambhakatvāsaṃbhave'pi vyāpāreṇa svāvyavahitottarakāla evotpāditāvaraṇabhaṅgātmakaphalāśrayatā svīkriyate, naiyāyikairatītānāgatayoviṣayatayā jñānāśrayatā yathāṅgīkriyate tatheti bhāvaḥ | anyathā 'yathāpūrvaṃ na jānāmī'tyasya matadvaye'pyāpattiḥ, tadānīṃ tatrāvaraṇabhaṅgasya jñānasya ca svarūpaviṣayatāsaṃbandhāvacchinnapratiyogitākābhāvasattvāt | āvaraṇa- bhaṅge jñāne cādheyatvaviṣayitāsaṃbandhāvacchinnasyātītaghaṭoderbhāvasya sattvācca | nañā kārakaviśiṣṭakriyābhāvapratipādanamiti svamate āvaraṇabhaṅgādyabhāvabodhā- saṃbhave'pi tadabhāvaprayuktakārakaviśiṣṭakriyāyā abhāvasya āropitakriyāyā bodhasaṃbhavāt tādṛśaprayogāpattiḥ | tatra viṣayatāsattve'pi viṣayatayā jñānāśrayatve vivādādāha satkāryeti | ata eva 'ghaṭo jāyate' ityatra ghaṭasya kriyājanakatvarūpaṃ kārakatvaṃ, kartṛtvaṃ copapadyate, anyathāsatastadanupapattiḥ | tatra bharttṛharisaṃmatimāha-uktaṃ ceti | saiva - tirobhāvarūpaiva | nāstitā - nāṣṭatā | labdheti | labdhaḥ kramo yena tādṛśe tirobhāve satītyarthaḥ tadutpattigatavarttamānatvanibandhano naśyatīti vyavahāraḥ, tadanantaram-anaṣṭeti tadarthaḥ | labdhakrama' ityanena vyāpārarūpatāveditā | pratīyata ityanena pratītiḥ pramāṇatvenoktā | tirobhāvo nāma sūkṣmarūpeṇāvasthānamevaṃ ca tatrāvaraṇabhaṅgāśrayatvasya nānupapattiḥ, evamanāgate'pi draṣṭavyam |
phalāśrayatvāditi | tadāśrayatvenoddeśyatvādityapi bodhyam | vibhāgasya prayoge'pīti | phale dhātūpāttatvāniveśād vibhāgarūpaphalaśālitvāt prayogasyāpi karmatvāpattiriti bhāvaḥ | karmasaṃjñāyā bādheneti | paratvād bādhenetyarthaḥ | ākaḍārīyatvāt saṃjñayorapi bādhyabādhakabāva iti bhāvaḥ | nanu phalāśrayatvakarmasaṃjñayormadhye saṃjñāyā eva niyāmakatvamityatra kiṃ bījaṃ, kiṃ ca saṃjñāniyāmakatvenāvaśyakatādṛśaphalāśrayatvasyaiva dvitīyāniyāmakatvamastvityā- śaṅkyāha dvitīyotpattāviti | phalāśrayatvasya niyāmakatve dūṣaṇamāha 
anyatheti | kṛṣṇapadād dvitīyāpatteriti | kṛṣṇasya pācayatidhātvarthavyāpāra- prayojyaviklittyanukūlavyāpārātmakaphalāśrayatvād dvitīyāntasya sādhutṃ syādityarthaḥ | aṇijantadhātvarthavyāpāraprayojyaphalāśrayatvasyaiva dvitīyāniyāma- katvābhyupagamānneyamāpattiriti śaṅkānirākaraṇāya 'gamayati kṛṣṇā'mityādi- dṛṣṭāntābhidhānam | tathā ca pūrvoktarītyabhyupagame 'gamayati kṛṣṇā'mityatrāpi dvitīyāntasya sādhutvaṃ na syāditi bhāvaḥ | nanu noktasthale dvitīyāntasya sādhutvamāpādayāmi, kiṃ tu tādṛśaprayogācchābdabodhamityāśaṅkāmiṣṭāpattyāpaharati śābdabodha iti | śābdabodhaḥ - caitraktṛkaṃ caitrakarmakaṃ gamanamityādyākārakaḥ śābdabodhaḥ | caitraścaitramityatreti | caitraścaitraṃ gacchatītyatretyarthaḥ | tathāvyutpannānāmimi | tasmin vākye tādṛśabodhajanakatvagrahavatāmityarthaḥ | tādṛśavākyāduktaḥ śābdabodho nānubhavasiddha ityata iṣṭāpattirayuktetyāśaṅkya pariharati-ucyatāṃ veti | svīkriyatāmityarthaḥ | prakāratāsaṃbandheneti | prakāratāsaṃbandhaḥ kāryatāvacchedakaḥ, kāraṇatāvacchedakasaṃbandhastu dhātvarthavyāpārā- nadhikaraṇāśrayaniṣṭhaviśeṣyatā | tathā ca 'prakāraratāsaṃbandhena tādṛśaphalabodhe tādṛśaviśeṣyatāsaṃbandhena vibhaktijanyopasthitiḥ kāraṇa'miti kāryakāraṇabhāvaḥ paryavasannaḥ, 'dhātvarthavyāpārānadhikaraṇe'tyādigranthaścoktasaṃbandhapradarśaka eva, caitro grāmaṃ gacchatī'tyatra grāmarūpa āśraye tādṛśaviśeṣyatāsaṃbandhena dvitīyājanyopa- sthitiriti, atastatra dhātvarthasaṃyogarūpapaphalasya prakāratāsaṃbandhena bodhanirvāhaḥ, upasthitau vibhaktijanyatvaniveśād 'gamyamāno grāma' ityatra nānvayavyabhicāraḥ, 'caitraścaitra'mityatrātiprasaṅgavyāvṛtti darśayati-prakṛte caitrasyeti |
uktaprāyatvāt kriyājanyaphalāśrayasyetyanenoktaprāyatvāt | tatra 'dhātvartha- phale'tyanuktatvāt 'prāyaḥ'padopādānam, ata eva yadā puṣṭhārtha māṣabhakṣaṇāya māṣakṣetreśvo badhyate tadā 'māṣeṣvaśvaṃ badhnātī'tyatra bandhanaprayojyabhakṣaṇarūpa- phalāśrayatve'pi māṣāṇāṃ na karmatvam | prayāgasya karmatvasaṃbhāvanā'saṃbhavokti- rvibhāgo gacchatyādyartho na bhavatītyabhipretya | apādānaprakaraṇe vakṣyamāṇarītyā tasya tadarthatve tvavadhiniṣṭhasaṃbandhasya phalatānavacchedakatayoktadoṣaparihāro'vaseyaḥ |
prakārāntareṇa sthaladvaye doṣaṃ vārayatāṃ naiyāyikānāṃ mataṃ dūṣayitumupanyasyati- naiyāyakāstviti | parasamavetatvamiti | ādyadoṣavāraṇāya parasamavetatvaṃ dvitīyāvācyamupādadate - svīkurvantītyanvayo, dvitīyadoṣavāraṇāya dhātvartha- tāvacchedakatvaṃ phale viśeṣaṇamupādadata ityaparo'nvayaḥ | vibhāgasya gamadhātvarthatāvacchedakatvābhāvānna 'prayāgāt kāśī gacchatī'tyatra prayoge'ti- prasaṅgaḥ | dhātvarthatāvacchekaṃ ca dhātuśakyāṃśe viśeṣaṇatāpannatvam, dvitīyāvācya- parasamavetatvasya phale tadāśrayādāvanvaye'pi noktadoṣavāraṇasaṃbhavaḥ, saṃyogagrāmādau caitrabhinnasamavetatvasattvādityata āha-parasamavetatvamiti | dhātvarthakriyāyā- manvetīti | phalajanakasamabhivyāhṛtadhātvarthakriyāyāmanvetītyarthaḥ | svapadaṃ yatphalavyaktimattvena karmatvamabhimataṃ tadvayaktiparam, tena 'pacantaṃ caitraṃ maitro gacchati'tyatra ca pākakriyāyāṃ caitrasamavetāyāṃ saṃyogānukūlakriyāyāṃ ca caitrabhinnasamavetatvābhāve'pi na caitrapadād dvitīyānupapattiḥ | dvitīyasthale samabhivyāhṛtagamyupāttasaṃyogajanakakriyāyāṃ svasamavetāyāṃ svabhinnasamavetatvā- bhāve'pi maitrapratiyogikacaitrānuyogikasaṃyogavyaktijanakakriyāyāṃ tasyākṣatatvānnānu- papattiḥ | paro - bhedavān, tatsamavetatvamityarthaḥ, 'caitro grāmaṃ gacchatī'tyatra vyutpattivaicivyeṇa bhedavatsamavetatvaikadeśe bhede dvitīyāprakṛtyarthagāmasya pritayogitānirūpitānuyogitāsaṃbandhenānvayaḥ, sasaṃbandhikatvācca padārthaikadeśe'pi bhede 'devadattasya pite'tyādau devadattanirūpitatvasya pitṛpadārthaikadeśe janakatve ivānvayo nānupapannaḥ, tadanvitabhedavatsamavetatvasya kriyāyāmāśrayatvasaṃbandhenānvayaḥ, 'caitreṇa ganyate grāma' ityatrākhyātārthaḥ parasamavetatvaṃ, yatra phalāśraye karmaṇi pratyayastasya tadghaṭakabhede grāmādeḥ pratiyogitayānvayaḥ, na ca caitre'pi dvitvādinācaitrabhedasattvā'ccaitraścaitraṃ gacchatī'ti durvārameva,
prakṛtyarthatāvacchedakāvacchinnapratiyogitākatvasaṃbandhena prakṛtyarthasya dvitīyārthabhede- 'nvayāṅgīkāre 'devadatto dravyaṃ gacchati' 'mallo mallaṃ gacchatī'tyādyanupapattiḥ, devadatte dravyatvāvacchinnapratiyogitākabhedasya, malle mallatvāvacchinnapratiyogitāka- bhedasya cāsattvāt, na ca vaiśiṣṭyavyāsajyavṛttidharmānavacchinnapriyogitātvasyaiva saṃbandhatāṅgīkārānna 'caitraścaitraṃ gacchatī'tyasyāpattiriti vācyaṃ, tādṛśī pratiyogitā prakṛtyarthatāvacchedakāvacchitraiva saṃbhavatīti devadatto dravyamityādāvanupapattitādavasthyāditi cenna, tattadvayaktitvāvacchinnapratiyogitākatva- saṃbandhena prakṛtyarthasyānvayāṅgīkārāduktadoṣābhāvāt | evaṃ caitreṇa grāmo gamyate' ityatra parasamavetatvamāśrayaśca karmākhyātārthaḥ, tārkikamate āśrayatvaṃ ca karmākhyātārtha iti bodhyam, parasamavetatvaghaṭakabhede grāmādikarmaṇastadvayaktitvā- vacchinnapratiyogitākatvasaṃbandhenānvayaḥ, tena 'caitraścaitreṇa gamyata' ityasya nāpattiḥ, na vā 'caitreṇa dravyaṃ gamyate' 'mallo mallena gamyata' ityasyānupapattiḥ, anvayitāvacchedakāvacchinnapratiyogitākatvasyaiva saṃsargatvamiti vyutpattistu nañupasthāpyābhāvaśābdabodhaviṣayiṇīti bhāvaḥ | na cobhayakarmajasaṃyogasthale caitrānuyogikasaṃyogajanakamaitravṛttikriyāyāṃ caitrabhinnasamavetatvasya nirāvādhatayā taddoṣastadavastha iti vācyam, anyaniṣṭhakriyāyāṃ caitrabhinnasamavetatvasattve'pi caitraniṣṭhasaṃyogajanakacaitrabhinnasamavetā yā'nyaniṣṭhakriyā tadāśrayatvasya caitre'saṃttvāt 'caitraścaitraṃ gacchatī'tyasya caitrakartṛkakriyāyāṃ caitrānyasamavetatvā- bhāvāccaitrāyasamavetakriyājanyasaṃyogavattvasya caitre'sattvā'ccaitreṇa gamyata' ityasyāpatterayogādityanyatra vistaraḥ | yadvā-bheda ādheyatvaṃ cārthaḥ, tatra bhede prakṛtyarthasya pūrvavadevānvayaḥ, bhedasya sāmānādhikaraṇyasaṃbandhena vyāpāre, ādheyatve ca prakṛtyarthasya, evaṃ ca 'grāmaṃ gacchati caitra' ityato grāmavṛttisaṃyogajanako grāmapratiyogikabhedasamānādhikaraṇo yo vyāpārastadāśrayaścaita iti bodhaḥ | 'caitreṇa grāmo gamyata' ityaścaitrāśrayakagrāmabhedasamānādhikaraṇavyāpāraprayojyasaṃyogāśrayo grāma iti bodhaḥ |
nanu parasamavetatvasya phalādāvevānvayaḥ syāt, tathā cātiprasaṅgatādavasthyamata āha tathaiva kāryeti | viśeṣyatāsaṃbandhena dvitīyādyarthaparasamavetākriyāniṣṭhabodhaṃ prati svaviśeṣyatāsaṃbandhena dhātujanyopa- sthitiḥ kāraṇamiti kāryakāraṇabhāva ityarthaḥ | dvitīyayetyupakṣaṇaṃ subvibhakteḥ, tena
'caitro gāmāya gacchatī'tivaccaitraścaitrāya gacchati' 'caitreṇa grāmasya gamana'mitibaccaitreṇa caitrasya gamana'mityādina prayogaḥ, tathā ca parasamavetakriyājanyaphalaśālitvaṃ karmatvametanmate paryavasannamityāśayena tadupādayati tathā ca caitrastaṇḍulamiti | 'dhātvarthatāvacchedake'tyuktirviklitterdhātvartha- tāvacchedakatvasattvaprakaṭanāya, na tu tena rūpeṇa śābdabodhe bhānaṃ, tadavacchinne śakyabhāvāttena rūpeṇa bodhānanubhavācceti bodhyam | nanvatiprasaṅganirāsāya gurubhūtaparasamavetatve śaktiḥ kalpanīyaivetyata āha atiprasaṅga iti | tattādavasthyāt - aniṣṭasthale dvitīyātipraṅgatādavasthyāt | 'caitraścaitraṃ gacchatī'tyatiprasaṅgasyoktarītyā'saṃbhavādanyatrātiprasaṅgamāha pācayati kṛṣṇa- miti | ityāpatteriti | prayojye kṛṣṇe svabhinnaprayojakasamavetakriyājanyaṇijanta- dhātvarthatāvacchedakībhūtaprakṛtyarthakriyātmakaphalaśālitvarūpakarmatvasattvād dvitīyā- pattirityarthaḥ, dṛṣṭāntopādānaṃ pūrvavat | karmakartari dvitīyātiprasaṅgamupapādayati viklittyanukūleti | viklittyanukūlastaṇḍulānyasamaveto'grisaṃyogarūpo dhātvarthaḥ pacadhātvarthastajjanyaphaletyarthaḥ, atrāgrisaṃyogo dhātvarthatayā vivikṣata ityabhipretyedaṃ dūṣaṇam | 'karmakartari phalamātrasya dhātvarthatvaṃ, iklityanukūlataṇḍusamātravṛttivyapārasya dhātvarthatva'miti pakṣe nedaṃ dūṣaṇamiti bodhyam | iṣṭānvayeti | paratvaṃ dvitīyādiprakṛtyarthapratiyogikameva budhyate,nānyapratiyogikaṃ, parasamavetatvasya tādṛśakriyāyāmevānvayo na tvanyatreti dvividhaniyamanivārhāya | tattatkāryakāraṇabhāvasvīkāre gauravādhikyādityarthaḥ |
sāmānyaprakārakajñānasya viśeṣarūpeṇa jijñāsāhetutvādviśeṣarūpeṇa jñānecchāyāṃ satyāṃ prasaṅgasaṅgatyā karma vibhajate etacca saptavidhamiti | nirvartyamiti | tadīpsitatamaṃ karma nirvartya vikārya prāpyaṃ ceti tridhā matamiti yojanā | anyattu - īpsitatamabhinnaṃ tu | catuvidhatvamāha audāsīnyeneti- īpsitatvabhāve dveṣyatvābhāve ca sati yat prāpyaṃ kriyājanyaphalāśraya ityarthaḥ | anīpsitam - dveṣyam, saṃjñāntaraiḥ - apādanādisaṃjñābhiḥ, anākhyātam - avivakṣitam, tathā cākathitamitisūtreṇa karmasaṃjñakamiti | anyapūrvakamiti | anyasaṃjñāpūrvakaṃ, yathā 'krūramabhikrudhyatī'tyatra 'krudhadruherṣyā ' iti saṃpdānasaṃjñā prāptā | nirvartyāditrayasya sakṣaṇamāha yadasaditi |
prakṛtyucchedeti | prakṛtibhūtasyātmana ucchedaṃ saṃbhūtaṃ - prāptam, bhū
prāptavityasmāt kartariktaḥ, prakṛteḥ - kāṣṭhādeḥ | krameṇodāharaṇānyāha ghaṭaṃ karotīti | ādyam - nirvartyam - ghaṭasyāsata evotpattirnaiyāyikamate | sāṃkhyādimate svamate ca sata eva, taduktaṃ hariṇā 
'utpatteḥ prāgasadbhāvo buddhayavasthānibandhanaḥ |
aviśiṣṭaḥ satā'nyena kartā bhavati janmanaḥ ||
kāraṇaṃ kāryabhāvena yadā vā vyavatiṣṭhate |
kāryaśabdaṃ tadā labdhvā kāryatvenopajāyate ||
yathā'heḥ kuṭilībhovo vyagrāṇāṃ vā samagratā |
tathaiva janmarūpatvaṃ satāmeke pracakṣate ||' iti |
prāpyalakṣaṇamāha-kriyākṛteti | darśanāditi | pratyakṣāt | nirvartthe svarūpalābhātmako viśeṣaḥ pratyakṣagamyaḥ, vikārye'pi kkacit svarūpalābhaḥ, kkacidguṇāntarātmakaḥ, anumānādyathā putraḥ sukhamanubhavati, atra hi mukhaprasādena sukhānumānaṃ bhavati, spaṣṭaṃ cedaṃ 'karmaṇyaṇa' kaiyaṭādau | dvitīyam - vikāryam | atra kāṣṭhasuvarṇayoreva vikāryatā, bhasmakuṇḍalayostukāṣṭhasuvarṇayoḥ pariṇamitvavivakṣā'yorapi nirvartyataiveti bodhyam | kecittu 
'satī vā vidyamānā vā prakṛtiḥ pariṇāminī |
yasya nāśrīyate tasya nirvartyatvaṃ pracakṣate ||'
'prakṛtestu vivakṣāyāṃ vikārya kaiścidanyathā |
nirvartya ca vikārya ca karma śāstrī pradarśitam ||'
iti vākyapadīyād 'ghaṭaṃ karotī'tyatra ghaṭasya prakṛtiḥ satyapi pariṇāmitvena na vivakṣiteti ghaṭasya nirvartyataiva, 'bhasma karotī'tyatra bhasma prakṛtiḥ kāṣṭharūpā-bhasmadaśāyāmavidyamānā vivakṣiteti bhasmano virvartyataiva, 'evaṃ mṛdaṃ ghaṭaṃ karotītya'tra prakṛteḥpariṇāmitvāvivakṣāyām | [evaṃ vikāryateveti svīcakruḥ] | 'kāṣṭhaṃ bhasma karoti' 'suvarṇa kuṇḍalaṃ karotī'tyādau kṛdhātorvikārānukūlavyāpārapūrvakotpattyanukūlavyapāro'rthaḥ, 'kāṣṭhaṃ vikurvan bhasmotpādayatī'ti pratīteḥ, ata ubhayorapi karmatvanirvāhaḥ, ādye ucchedarūpo vikāraḥ, anye ākāraviśeṣātmakaḥ sa ghaṭasya | svātantryasyetarānadhona- tvarūpasyākṣayaṇe 'devadattena pācayati mitradatta' ityādau prayojakādīnatayā katṛtvānāpattirityāśaṅkāpanodārtha vivakṣitārthamāha svātantryaṃ ceti |
dhātūpātteti | prakṛta dhātūpāttetyarthaḥ,yatkiñciddhātūpāttarayāvyāvartakatvāt | vyāpāraśca pradhānībhūto grāhyaḥ, tena nayateḥ, saṃyogānukūlavyāpārānukūla- vyāpārārthakatve 'devadatto grāmamajāṃ nayatī'tyatrājāyā na kartṛtvam | na ca karmasaṃjñayā bādhānna tadāpattiriti vācyaṃ, 'karturīpsitatamam ' iti karmasaṃjñāyā'ścaitrastaṇḍulaṃ pacatī'tyādau taṇḍulādau labdhāvakāśatayā'pavādatvā- bhāvāt, paratvābhāvācca, pratyuta paratvāt kartṛsaṃjñaiva vādhikā syāt, kartṛtvavivakṣāyāṃ ca na 'akathitam ' ityasya prāptiḥ |
'duhiyācirudhipracchibhikṣiciñāmupayoganimittamapūrvavidhau |
bruviśāsiguṇena ca yat sacate tadakītitamācaritaṃ kavinā ||'
iti parigaṇanavārtike nayatyāderapāṭhācca, prādhānyaṃ ca kartṛpratyayasamabhi- vyāhṛtatattaddhātujanyabodhīyatattaddhātvarthaniṣṭhavaśeṣyatānirūpitaprakāratānāśrayatvam, 'pakkastaṇḍulo devadattene'tyatra vyāpārasya phalaṃ prati viśeṣaṇatvāddevadattasya kartṛtvānāpattiriti 'kartṛpratyayasamabhivyāhāre' iti | svatantrapadasya niruktārthakatve mānamāha dhātunokteti | 'dhātunoktakriye kārake kartṛtā nityamaṣyate' iti yojanā | nanvetādṛśasvātantryāśrayaṇe 'sthālyāṃ pacatī'tyādāvapi sthālyādeḥ kartṛtvāpattiḥ, pacadhātūpāttadhāraṇādyātmaka- vyāpārāśrayatvādityata āha ata eva yadeti | ata eva uktasthale sthālyādāvatiprasaṅgādeva | naca niravakāśābhiradhikaraṇādirsajñābhiḥ kartṛsaṃjñāyā bādha iti vācyam, kartṛsaṃjñāviṣaye'pyadhikaraṇasaṃjñā prāptā tasyā api niravakāśatayā paryāyatāpattirityā śayāt | yadā yo dhātūpāttavyāpārāśrayaḥ sa tadā karteti svīkāre ekasya kartṛtvakarmatvayoḥ saṃbhave 'kartṛkarmavyapadeśā'dityadhikaraṇavirodhamāśaṅkya pariharati nanvevamiti | evam - akasya kartṛtvakarmatvobhayāṅgīkāre | tasyeti | tasya - manomayatvādiguṇayuktasya, etamitaityādiśrutau kartṛkarmavyapadeśo viruddha iti saṃbandhaḥ | etamiti | prakṛtaṃ manomayatvādiguṇakamupāsyamānamātmānaṃ karmatvena prāptikarmatvena vyapadiśati aṅisaṃbhavitāsmīti | śārīramupāsakaṃ kartṛtvena - prāptakartṛtaḥ, 'abhisaṃbhavitāsmī'tyasya 'prāptāsmī'tyarthāt | 'na ca satyāṃ gatāvekasya karmakartṛvyapadeśo yukta' iti śaṅkarabhāṣyam, atrābhisaṃbhavitāsmīti 'kartṛtvene'tyatra vyapadiśatī'tyasyānuṣaṅgeṇānvayaḥ, satyāṃ
gatāvityādinātmānamā'tmanā hantī'tyādividaupādhikabhedakalparanāgatikarūpā na yukteti sūcitam, 'saṃbhavitāsmī'ti samupasṛṣṭāt prāptyarthakādbhavaterātmanepadino vyatyayena luṭaḥ sthāne parasmaipadam, ekasya samayabhedena saṃjñādvayasaṃbhave'pi na yugapat, prakṛtaśrutau tu yugapadeva saṃjñādvayaprayuktakāryadvayopalambho viruddha ityaśayena samādhatte ucyata ityādinā | jñeyatve - 'manomaya' ityādinā jñeyatvena bodhitatve | karmatvaṃ vācyamiti | 'eta'mityanena karmatvaṃ vācyametacchabdasyopa- krāntaparāmarśakatvāditi bhāvaḥ | bādhāditi | paratvāditi bhāvaḥ | karmaṇaḥ kartṛtve dūṣaṇāntaramaṃpyāha karmakartṛtāyāmiti | 'saṃbhavitāsmī'ti na suddhe kartari lakāraḥ, api tu karmakartarīti bhāvaḥ | tathā ca etamitaḥ pretyeti pūrvavākye karmatayā nirdiṣṭasya kartṛtvamiti na yugapatsaṃjñādvayamiti yagādyāpattiriti | yagādyāpattiriti | 'ādi'padagrāhyātmanepadāpattiriti bhāvaḥ | vyatyayaṃ vinaiva nirvāhe tadabhyupagamo na yukta ityaśayaḥ | bhaidaheturiti | jīvaparamātmanore- bhedaheturityarthaḥ | tathā ca 'manomaya' ityādipadena paramātmaiva nirdiṣṭa iti siddhamiti noktādhikaraṇavirodha iti tātparyārthaḥ | evaṃ ca - dhātūpāttavyāpārāśrayatvasya kartṛtve ca | ata eva 'ghaṭo bhavatī'tyatra ghaṭīyavyāpārāśrayaḥ karteti kartṛlakṣaṇayogād ghaṭa eva karteti kalpatarukārairuktaṃ ca saṃgacchate | ata eva bādhalakṣaṇe'sthiyajñe 'kratvartha tu kriyate guṇabhūtatvāt' iti kratvarthābhyuccayādhikaraṇe na caitanyāpekṣyaṃ kartṛtvaṃ kāṣṭhādīnāmapi jvalanādau kartṛtvād, yasyaivānyāpekṣayākhyātopāttavyāpārasamavāyaḥ sa kartā, tasmācchukrānva- rambhaṇādikarmāsthibhiḥ kartavyamiti siddhāntitam | 'śukaṃ yajamono'nvarabhate'tyādau yatra yajamāno guṇabhūtastatkarma satriṇāṃ madhye kaścinmṛtastadasthibhiḥ sahetarairjīvaddhirārabdhasatraiḥ kartavyamuktasvātantryarūpakartṛtvasya tatrāpi saṃbhavāditi tatratyasiddhāntāśāyaḥ | pūrvamīmāṃsāyāṃ daśamādhyāye prathamacaraṇe spaṣṭamidam | kārakacakreti | samabhivyāhṛtadhātvarthavyāpārakasamudāyetyarthaḥ, tacca 'devadattaḥ kāṣṭhaistaṇḍulaṃ sthālyāṃ pacatī'tyādau devadattasyaiveti tasminneva lakāraḥ |
uktāpakṣayā lāghavamabhipretyāha kṛtīti | prakṛtadhātvarthavyāpārānukūlakṛtyā- śrayatvaṃ taddhātvarthakartṛtvamityarthaḥ | tenānyavyāpārānukūlakṛtimādāya nātipraṅgaḥ, āśrayatvaṃ samavāyena grāhyam, tena pākānukūlakṛteḥ kālikasaṃbandhenāśrayatvasya ghaṭe sattve'pi na 'ghaṭaḥ pacati' 'ghaṭena pacyata' ityāderāpattiḥ | avyāptamiti |
kārakacakraprayojakatvarūpaprayoktṛtvasya, kṛtimattvasya cācetane daṇḍādau bādhādityarthaḥ | atra 'kṛtimattvaṃ kartṛtva'miti mate āśrayāṃśasya devadattādiśabdalabhyatayā kṛtimātraṃ vibhaktayartha ii svīkāre kṛterdhātuta eva ābhānna tatra vibhaktiśaktisaṃbhava iti dūṣaṇaṃ saṃbhavadapi loṭṭaṅkitam, phalānukūlakṛterdhātuto lābhe'pi vyāpārānukūlāyā alābhāt tatra vibhaktiśaktisaṃbhavāt, 'tadanukūlakṛtirna bhāsata' ityasya śapathanirṇetvāt | athavā kṛterdhātulabhyatayā āśrayatvameva vā tṛtīyādyartha iti svīkāre uktadūṣaṇāsaṃbhavenedameva dūṣaṇamabhihitam | pare tu kārakacakraprayoktṛtvameva svātantryaṃ bhāṣyasaṃmataṃ, tacca devadattādāvanāropitaṃ, ghaṭādāvāropitaṃ, prayojakatvasyāropādeva'bhikṣā vāsayatī'tyādiprayoganirvāhaḥ, taduktaṃ 'kārake' iti sūtrabhāṣye 'pradhānena samavāye sthālī paratantrā,kiṃ punaḥ pradhānam ? kartā, kathaṃ jñāyate kartā pradhānamati ?, yat sarveṣu sādhaneṣu saṃnihiteṣu kartā pravartayitā bhavati' iti | evaṃ svecchādhīnapravṛttinivṛttikatvamapi svātantryam, tacca prayojakasaṃnidhye'pi svārthasattvāsattvayoḥ pravṛttinivṛttibhyā prayojyasya saṃbhavati, etacca 'svatantraḥ kartā' iti sūtre bhāṣye spaṣṭam | idamapi dhaṭādāvāropitaṃ, devadattādāvanāropitam, pradhānadhātvarthavyāpārāśrayatvameva na tat, tatra svatantra- padasyāparibhāṣaṇālloke uktārthayoreva tatpadapravṛtteśca, ata eva prayojyasyāsvātantryāt kartṛtvānupapattiśaṅkāyāṃ svecchādhīnāpravṛttinivṛttikatvarupaṃ sādhitam, etādṛśasvātantrayāśrayaniṣṭho vyāpāro dhātunocyate, tadabhipretyaiva dhātunoktakriyeityādikaṃ hariṇoktamiti na tadvirodhaḥ, ata eva 
'prāganyataḥ śaktilābhānnyagbhāvāpādanādapi |
tadadhīnapravṛttitvāt pravṛttānāṃ nivartanāt ||
adṛṣṭatvāt pratinidheḥ praviveke cadarśanāt |
ārādapyupakāritvāt svātantryaṃ karturucyate ||'
iti vākyapadīye uktam | tadarthastu karaṇāditaḥ prāgevānyato'rthitādereva nimittāt kartā pravartate, karaṇādikaṃ tadadhīnapravṛttinivṛttikam, kiṃ ca karaṇāderabhāve pratinidhirdṛśyate vrīhyabhāve'pi yāgakaraṇāt, kartuḥ sa nāsti, tadbhede kriyāntarameveti prasiddheḥ, api ca kārakāntarānupādāne'pyasau dṛśyata ityetadgranthasyātraiva tātparyamiti prāhuḥ | atredaṃ bodhyam sarveṣāṃ kārakāṇāṃ
svasvāvāntarakriyādvārā pradhānakriyānirvartakatvamata eva teṣāṃ bhāvanāyāmevānvayaḥ kriyājanakamiti jñāne ca janyakāṅkṣāniyamena tatraivānvayaucityāt, na ca kārakaśabdakartṛśabdayoraikārthyāt kārakaṃ karaṇamiti vyavahāraḥ kartā kāramitivadanupapannaḥ, kartā kārakamiti ca paunaruttayānna syāditi vācyam, 
'niṣpattimātre kartṛtvaṃ sarvatraivāsti kārake |
vyāpārabhedāpekṣāyāṃ karaṇāditvasaṃbhavaḥ ||'
