iti harigranthenaiva samādhānāt, sarveṣāṃ svātantryeṇaiva svasvavyāpāradvārā pradhānakriyāniṣpādakatayā kartṛtvāt kārakatvam, tena 'karaṇaṃ kāraka'miti vyavahāra upapannaḥ, viśeṣe kārakatvarūpasāmānyadharmasya sattvāt, evamīdṛśe sthale karmādīnāmivāpādānasya kartṛtve'pi 'grāma āgacchatī'ti prayogo nānabhidhānāt, kāraṇatvādyavasthāyāṃ kartṛsaṃjñāyā asattvānna karaṇaṃ kartrityādivyavahārāpattiḥ, kārakapadānuvadattyaiva svātantrye labdhe punaḥ svatantragrahaṇaṃ yasya svātantryameva vivakṣitaṃ na tu pāratantryayuktasvātantryaṃ tasyaivetyarthalābhārthamato na karaṇatvādyavasthāyāṃ sā, kārakasaṃjñā tu vastusatsvātantryasya sthitimātreṇa karaṇādīnāṃ vidhānasāmarthyāt pravartate, yadvā jvalanādirūpasvasvavyāpāre svātantryāt teṣāṃ vyāpārāṇāṃ dhātuvācyatve 'edhāḥ pacantī'tyādiprayogadarśanāt tātkālikaṃ kartṛtvamādāya kārakatvam, ssvavyāpāradvārā pradhānakriyāniṣpādakatvena ca karaṇatvādi, saṃpradānāpādānayorapi svavyāpāre svātantryamādāyaiva kārakatvaṃ 'vipro dadāti grāma āgacchatī'ti prayogābāve'pi saṃpadyate, taduktaṃ bhāṣye 'paryāptaṃ karaṇādhikaraṇakarmam̐ṇāṃ kartṛtvaṃ nidarśitamapādānakartṛtvanidarśanāya' iti, 'vṛkṣovadhibhāvamupagacchati vipro'numanyata'ityādo svasvavyāpārapravṛttestayopari kartṛtvaṃ dṛśyate, tadvayāpārasya patidādhātuvācyatā nānabidhānāditi dhyeyam | kārakatvaṃ copalakṣaṇaṃ sadeba tattatsaṃjñopayogi, spaṣṭaṃ cedaṃ bhāṣye | athavā dhātūpāttavyāpāratvarūpasvātantryaṃ na karaṇatvādyavasthāyāmiti nātiprasaṅgaḥ, kārakatvaṃ pūrvavadbhavatyevetyalaṃ vistareṇa |
kartāraṃ vibhajati-ayaṃ ceti | trividhaḥ - dharmatrayavān | krameṇodā- haraṇānyāha-mayeti | prayojakatvakarmatvāsamānādhikaraṇarūpaśuddhakartṛtvavānayam, prayojakatvakarmatve tattaddhātvarthanirūpitegrāhye, nāto'prasiddhiḥ, athavā karmatva- prayojakatvāvivakṣāsamānakālikavivāviṣayakartṛtvaṃ śuddhakartṛtvam | kāryata iti |
karoteṇijantāt karmaṇisaṭ, yak, atra hariḥ prayojakakartā | gamayati, kṛṣṇamiti | atra kṛṣṇāsyā'ṇau karturṇyante 'gatibuddhi-' iti sūtreṇa karmasaṃjñāvidhānāt kṛṣṇaḥ karmakartā, tathā ca karma cāsau karteti karmadhārayaḥ, karmasaṃjñāyā kartṛsaṃjñāyā bādhāt tadabhāve'pi prakṛtabhūtadhātūpātta- vyāparāśrayatvarūpakartṛtvasya na kṣatiḥ | sevanānukūlo vyāpāra iti | sevā - pādasaṃvāhanādikaṃ, prītirvā, navyamate tu madabhinnāśrayakavyāpārajanyā harikarmasevā ityādiḥ śābdabodho'vaseyaḥ | evaṃ 'kāryate hariṇe'tyatrāpi tādṛśa- vyāpārajanyotpādaneti bodho'nusaṃdheyaḥ |
nanvāśrayyāpārayoḥ karaṇatṛtīyāvācyatve pramāṇābhāvaḥ, sādhakatamasyaiva karaṇasaṃjñāvidhānādityata āha-tathā hīti | avyavadhāneneti | avyavadhānena phalājanako yo vyapārastadvatetyarthaḥ | 'avyavadhānene'ti tṛtīyāyā vaiśiṣṭyamarthaḥ, tathā ca tadviśiṣṭakāla ityarthaḥ, tasya ca janane'nvayaḥ, kriyāyāḥ - phalasya, dhātvarthaphalasya sādhyatayā pratīyamānatvāt kriyātvena vyavahāraḥ, 'karmavat karmaṇā tulyakriya' ityatra yathā, tathā cāśrayavyāpārayoḥ śakyatve 'sādhakatama'miti sūtraṃ pramāṇamiti bhāvaḥ | evaṃ ca mūle 'āśraya' iti vyāpārasyāpyupalakṣaṇamiti tātparyam, 'bāṇena hato vālī'tyatra bāṇasya prakṛtyarthasyābhedena tṛtīyārthāśraye'nvayaḥ, tasya ca vyāpāre'paratṛtīyārthe ādheyatayā, tasya ca janyatvasaṃbandhena dhātvarthavyāpāre phale vā prāṇaviyogātmani | anye tu-'vyāpāre na śaktirākṣayasyaiva tṛtīyārthasya svavṛttivyāpārajanyatvādisaṃbandhenānvaya' ityāhuḥ | pare tu 'karaṇatvaśaktimānarthaḥ, anvayaḥ pūrva'vadityāhuḥ | 'vivakṣyata' ityasya prayojanaṃ kārikayā spaṣṭayati-vastuta iti | tat - karaṇatvaṃ, vastuto - vastuviśeṣaniṣṭhatvenānirdeśyaṃ - nirdeṣṭumaśakyam,
tatra hetumāha-na hīti | hi - yasmāddhetostanna vastuvyavasthitaṃ gotvādivad vastuviṣeṣamātraniṣṭham | vastuviśeṣamātrāniṣṭhatve hetumāha-sthālyeti | adhikaraṇatvaniścayaviṣayībhūtāpi sthālī tanutarakapālatayā vaivakṣikaṃ karaṇatvaṃ bhajati, uktarītyā sarveṣāṃ karaṇatve'pi na yugapat sarveṣāṃ karaṇatvavivakṣeti na sarvatra tṛtīyāpattiriti dhyeyam | sarveṣāṃ vivakṣayā karaṇatvābhyupagame'dhikaraṇa- virodhamudbhāvya pariharati-na caivamityādinā | mīmāṃsādhikaraṇa iti | dvitīyādhyāyasya tṛtīyapādasthādhikaraṇe 'jīvaḥ kartā śāstrārthavattvāt', evaṃ hi
yajeta juhuyādityādiśāstraṃ sārthakaṃ bhavati, kartṛtve satyeva yāgādikamupadeṣṭuṃ śakyate nānyatheti sūtrārthaḥ | 'śaktiviparyayāt' itīti | vijñānavyatirikto jīvaḥ kartā bhavitumarhati, yadi punarvijñānaśabdavācyā buddhireva kartrī syāt tataḥ śaktiviparyayaḥ syāt, karaṇaśaktirbuddhehīyeta kartṛśaktiścāpadyeta, satyāṃ ca buddheḥ kartṛśaktau tasyā evāhaṃpratyayaviṣayatvaṃ syādityādi-śaṃkarabhāṣye pratipāditam |
antaḥkaraṇasyeti | antaḥkaraṇaṃ - buddhipariṇāmaḥ, pariṇānapariṇāminora- bhedānna bhāṣyavirodhaḥ |
karaṇatayeti | tathā ca karaṇatvaṃ sarveṣāṃ na vaivakṣikamityabhiprāyakaṃ na sūtrabhāṣyadikam, api tvantaḥkaraṇe karaṇatvasya klṛptatve'pyanyatra kartṛtvavikṣāyāṃ na kicid bādhakaṃ yugapadubhayavivakṣāyā eva viruddhatvādityāśaṅkyāha- vastutastviti | bhāṣya eveti | śāstrārthavattvādihetubhiḥ sādhitaṃ jīvasya kartṛtvaṃ na svābhāvikamapi tvaupādhikam, tasya svābhāvikatve muktirna syāt, kartṛtvasya duḥkhasvarūpatvāt, tasya svabhāvetve hi agrerivauṣṇyādātmanaḥ kartṛtvādviyogā- saṃbhavāt | tadetadāhācāryaḥ-'yathā ca takṣobhayathā' iti | ca śabdastvarthaḥ, yathā-takṣā vāsyādikaraṇahasto duḥkhībhavati tadrahitastu sukhī sabapadyate, anena dṛṣṭāntena kartṛtvamaupādhikameveti spaṣṭamiti śaṅkarabhāṣye uktam | evaṃ ca pūrvoktaśaktiviparyayasyābhyuccayarūpatvaṃ spaṣṭameveti bhāvaḥ | tacca - adhikaraṇasaṃjña ca | nanvādhārasya saptamyarthatve'pyāśrayaḥ kathaṃ saptamīśakyaḥ, ādhāratvāśrayatvayorbhedhādityāśaṅkyāha-tatvaṃ ceti | ādhāratvaṃ cetyarthaḥ | āśrayatvamiti | ādhāratvasyātiriktatve mānābhāvāt, tathā ca nānni bhedo na vastunīti bhāvaḥ | āśrayatvamātreṇeti | āyatvamātrapuraskāreṇa vihitetyartha- | ādhārasaṃjñeti | ādhārasya saṃjñā - adhikaraṇasaṃjñetyarthaḥ | ādhārasaṃjñāyā avidhānādyathāśrute'saṃgateḥ, na cādhārasaṃjñaivādhikaraṇasya vidhīyata iti vācyam, tasya prāṅnirdiṣṭatvāt paścānniṣṭhasyaiva saṃjñātvāt, spaṣṭaṃ cedaṃ vṛddhisūtre bhāṣye | 'stamyadhikaraṇe' ityāderasaṃgatyāpatteśca | tābhiḥ - karmādisaṃjñābhiḥ | bādhaḥ syāditi | tathā ca niravakāśābhistābhiradhikaraṇasaṃjñāyā bādha ucita eveta na karmādīnāmadhikaraṇasaṃjñāpattiriti bhāvaḥ | kriyākṣaya ityeveti | sākṣāt kriyākṣaya ityarthaḥ, evena caitrādyāśrayakaṭādervyāvṛttiḥ, tathā ca tādṛśasya kartureva saṃjñā syāt, phalāśrayakarmaṇo'pi vā syāt, na tu kaṭāderiti tātparyārthaḥ | sākṣāt
kriyāśrayasya kartṛkarmasaṃjñāvidhānāt | pariśeṣāt sākṣāt kriyāśrayadvārā kriyāśrayasya grahaṇamityāśayenottarayati-tathāpyatreti | atra kārakādhikārāt kriyā labdhā sā ca phalapūpāpītyāśayenāha-kartṛkarmadvāreti | tadāśrayatvamiti | kartṛkarmagatavyāpāraphalātmakakriyāśrayatvamityarthaḥ | ukte'rthe harisaṃmatimāha- kartṛkarmavyavahitāmiti | svāśritakartrādyāśritāmityarthaḥ | upakurvāditi | svāyayā kriyayā pradhānakriyānirvartakam | idaṃ ca 'kārake' ityadhikāralabdham |
aupaśleṣikamiti | upa - samīpe śleṣaḥ - saṃbandhastatkṛtamaupa- śleṣikam | ekadeśāvacchedena śleṣe'pyauśleṣikam | tatrāpi śleṣasya samīpamupaśleṣaṃ tatkṛtamiti vyutpattisaṃbhavāt, tatrādyam 'iko yaṇaci' ityādāviti 'saṃhitāyām' iti sūtrabhāṣye spaṣṭam | yanmāse'tikrānte dīyate tasya dānasya sa māsa auṣasleṣikamadhikaraṇamiti 'tatra ca dīyate ' ityatra bhāṣye uktam | 'tadasmannadhikam'-iti sūtre ca bhāṣye spaṣṭamidam | vaiṣayika- miti | aupaśleṣikābhivyāpakabhinnādhikaraṇamityarthaḥ | abhivyāpakamiti | sarvāvayavāvacchedenādheyasaṃbandhāśraya ityarthaḥ | yathā 'tileṣu tailaṃ' 'dadhni sarpi'riti | atra yāvattiladadhyavayavāvacchedena tailasarpiḥsaṃbandhasya sattvenābhi- vyāpakādhāratānirvāhaḥ | idameva mukhyamamadhikaraṇaṃ, tadbhinnaṃ sarva gauṇamiti 'svaritena-' iti sūtrabhāṣye spaṣṭam | gurau vasatīti | gurvadhīnatvavivakṣāyāṃ gurau vasatīti | pare tu tādṛśāvadhitvasamānādhikaraṇāpādānatvaśaktistadvān pañcamyartha ityāhuḥ | idaṃ, 'mīkṣa icchāstī'ti ca vaiṣayikodāharaṇam |
avadheḥ pañcamyarthatve sūtraṃ pramāṇayati-'apādāne pañcamī' iti | nanu sūtreṇāpādānasya pañcamyarthatvalābhe'pyavadheḥ kathaṃ tadarthatvalābha ityata āha-tacceti | tacca - apādānaṃ cetyarthaḥ | 'dhruvamapāye'pādānam' iti sūtrādavadhibhūtamiti yojanā | tathā coktasūtreṇāvadheḥ saṃjñāvidhānādarthatvamiti tātparyam | pare tu tādṛśāvadhitvasamānādhikaraṇāpādānatvaśaktimān pañcamyartha ityāhuḥ | 'bhītrārthānām-' ityādisūtre hetvādīnāmapādānasaṃjñāvidhāne'pi vācyatvamitaratra lakṣaṇeti bhāvaḥ | vastutastu 'bhūtrārthānām-' ityādisūtrāṇāṃ bhāṣyakāreṇa pratyākhyānādapādānasaṃjñāvadhereva, tathā ca kārakapañcamyartho- 'vadhirevetyabhiprāyakamidam, tenānyādiśabdayoge vihitāyā 'vibhāṣā guṇe'- iti vihitāyāśca tasyāḥ saṃbandhahetvarthakatve'pi na kṣatiḥ | apāyo viśleṣa iti | viśleṣo vibhāgaḥ | apāyapadamapāyajanakakriyāparamityabhipretyāha-tajjanaka- kriyāyāmiti | avadhibhūto'yaṃ dhruvapadārtha uktaḥ | dhruvaṃ - sthiraṃ, tacca kriyānāśrayaḥ, sā ca pratyāsattyā prakṛtadhātūpāttā, evaṃ cedamavadhibhūtameveti bhāvaḥ | vākyapadīyakārikāyāmubhayakarmaka iti pāṭhe ubhe karmaṇī janake yasyeti bahuvrīhirbodhyaḥ, ubhayakarmajanya ityarthaḥ | ubhayakarmaja iti pāṭhastu ṛjureva |
apāye yadudāsīnamityatrāpāye iti vibhāgajanakakriyāparaṃ
lakṣaṇayetyāśayena vyācaṣṭaviśleṣahetviti | dhruvapadārtha udāsīnapadenāktaḥ | audāsīnyaṃ - tadanāśrayatvamityabhipretyāha adāsīnamanāśraya iti | phalitārthamāha-evaṃ ceti | apāye dhruvamityanena viśleṣajanakakriyaudāsīnyavivakṣaṇe cetyarthaḥ | viśleṣāśrayatvamiti | dhātvarthavibhāgāśrayatvamityarthaḥ | etadapyarthāllabdham | etanniveśanaṃ tu 'devadatto grāmāt kāśī yātī'tyādau kāśyādāvativyāptivāraṇāya | yadyapi karmādisaṃjñābhiḥ paratvena bādhādapādanasaṃjñātiprasaṅge neti cet tathāpi vibhāgasamānādhikaraṇaivāpādāna- śaktiriti bodhanāya taditi bhāvaḥ | tadvāraṇāya - apādānasaṃjñāvāraṇāya | satyantamita | idaṃ ca dhruvatvapadārthaḥ | tadanupādāne kartṛsaṃjñāpavāda eva syāt, apāyaviṣaye yat kārakamuktāpāyarūpakriyāśrayarūpamapādānamityarthasaṃbhavāt | kartṛ- saṃñjñāyāstu 'pacati caitra' ityādiravakāśa iti bhāvaḥ | etena paratvāt kartṛsaṃjñayā bādhādidamasaṃgatamityapāstam | nanvapāyaśabdo vibhāhārthaka evāstu, sati saptamī-apāya iti, kārakādhikārāddhātulābhaḥ | tathā ca prakṛtadhātvarthavibhāgāśrayatve sati yat kārakaṃ tadapādānamiti sūtrārthāṅgīkāre neyaṃ saṃjñā kartṛsaṃjñāpavādikā, grāmādau caritārthatvāt, itthaṃ ca parayā kartṛsaṃjñayā bādhānna kartaryatiprasaṅga iti cet, pūrvoktārthasyāpi saṃbhavāt spaṣṭārthameva tadityāhuḥ | nanu prakṛtadhātūpāttakriyānāśrayatvavivakṣaṇena kartaryatiprasaṅgavāraṇe alaṃ viśleṣahetutvaniveśanenetyata āha dhāvata iti . 'aśvāt patatī'tyukte kriyānāśrayatvadaśāyāmeva tādṛśaprayogopapattirityāśaṅkyeta, atastadvayāvṛttaye- dhāvata iti viśeṣaṇopādānam | tathā coktavivakṣāyaṃ prakṛtadhātūpāttadhāvana- rūpakriyāśrayatvenaiva tādṛśaprayogānupapattirdṛḍhaiveti bhāvaḥ | dhāvanaṃ tu vyabhicārādanyathāsiddhitvācca na vibhāgaheturiti noktadoṣatādavasthyam | etadarthameva ca apāye iti sūtre upāttam | anyathā dhruvapadena sthirasyaiva lābhāpattyokta prayogānupapattiprasaṅgaḥ | viśleṣajanaketi | aśvakuḍyayoviśleṣastajjanakapatanāśrayatve'pītyarthaḥ | viśleṣasāmānyopādāne uktasthale'śvasyāpādānatvānupapattisattve'pi viśleṣaviśeṣopādāne na doṣaḥ | aśvaniṣṭhapatanasyāśvavadeva tadvibāgājanakatvāditi samādhānāśayamāha-tadviśleṣa iti | yadviśleṣāvadhitvaṃ tadviśleṣavyaktītyarthaḥ | aśvaniṣṭeti | aśvakuḍya- vibhāgajanakāśvaniṣṭhakriyetyarthaḥ | kriyānāśrayatvābhāvāditi | meṣadbayasyāpi
tadviśleṣajanakakriyāśrayatvāditi bhāvaḥ | anyatarakarmajavibhāgasthale tadvibhāga- janakakriyānāśrayatvasyānyatarasmin saṃbhavānmeṣo meṣādapasaratītyasya nānupapattirityetena sūcitam | ubhāvapyadhruvāvityādanā vibhāga ityantena śaṅkā, pravibhakte tu kriye tatra vyavasthite iti samādhānam | tadeva meṣayorityādinā spaṣṭīkṛtamiti bodhyam | tatrānyatarakarmajavibhāgasthalīyamanurūpaṃ dṛṣṭāntamāha yathā niścaleti | niścalo vibhāgajanakakriyānāśrayo yo meṣastasmādityarthaḥ | apasaraditi | apasaraṃstādṛśakriyāśrayo yo dvitīyameṣa ityarthaḥ | tattatkriyānāśrayatve'pi tattanmeṣasya tadviśleṣajanakakriyākṣayatvāvacchinna- pratiyogitākābhāvāsattvādanupapattirduṣpariharaivetyāśaṅkāpanuttaye āha-tathā ceti | tatkriyānāśrayatvamiti | tathā ca tadviśleṣajanakakriyāvyakti tadvayaktitvenopādāya tadanāśrayatvaniveśādekaikameṣasya tadviślejanakameṣāntarasamavetakriyānāśrayatvānnā- nupapattiriti bhāvaḥ
pare tu kartṛbhedena kriyābhedasattve'pi sa na pratīyate, tiṅante nivṛttabhedāyā eva kriyāyā dhātunopasthānāt, ata eva sarūpasūtre 'na vai tiṅantānyekaśeṣārambhaṃ prayojayanti' ityuktam | evaṃ ca noktarītyānupapattiparihārasaṃbhavaḥ, api tu śabdabhedena vācyabhedamāśritya meṣaśabdopasthitayostādṛśakriyāśrayatvāt kartṛtvaṃ, parasparaśabdopasthitayostayostādṛśakriyānāśrayatvenāpādānatvam, antaḥkaraṇādi- rūpopādhibhedena bhedavacchabdhabhedenāpi bhedasya svīkartumucitatvāditi saṃpratipannāḥ | nanu vṛkṣakarmaṇaiva vibhāgajananasthale 'vṛkṣādvastraṃ' patatī'i prayogānupapattiḥ, vṛkṣe vṛkṣavastravibhāgajanakatatkriyānāśrayatvābhāvādata āha kriyā cātreti | dātvartha iti | tathā ca saṃyogānukūlyāpārādirūpapatadhātvarthānāśrayatvādvṛkṣasyāpādānatva- siddhiḥ | nanu tādṛśadhātvarthasya vibhāgājanakatayā viśleṣajanakataddhātvarthā- prasiddhayā tadanāśrayatvasya mutarāmasaṃbhava iti cenna, taṃ taṃ dhātvartha tattadvayaktitveno- pādāya tadanāśrayatvamātraṃ niveśyaṃ, na tu dhātvarthe viśleṣahetutvamapi, tāvatāpi 'dhāvato'śvāt patatī'tyatra pūruṣaniṣṭhapatanānāśrayatayāśvasyā'pādānatvopa- patterityāśayāt | athavā viśleṣahetutattaddhātvarthāśrayo yo yastattadbhedavattvamartho bodhyaḥ | 'devadatto vṛkṣāt patatī'ttra devadattādireva niruktadhātvarthāśrayaḥ pratiyogī prasiddha ityāśayo'vaseyaḥ | ubhayakarmajavibhāgatātparyakastu na pūrvapakṣaḥ, 'parasparasmā'dityatrobhayoravadhitvaṃ kartṛtvaṃ ca, iha tvekasyāvadhitvamaparasya
kartṛtvamiti vailakṣaṇyasūcanāyaitadgrandhopanyāsaḥ | nanvanāśrayatvāntadalasyāvācyatve 'devadatto grāmādāyātī'tyatra devadattāditi prayogāpattirviśleṣāśrayatvādityata āha ditīyārthokta iti | yathā 'devadatto devadattaṃ gacchatī'ti prayogasya paramavetatvādvācyatve'pi nāpattiḥ kartṛsaṃjñayā parayā karmasaṃjñāyā bādhāttathā kartṛsaṃjñayāpādānasaṃjñayāvadhitvaśaktimata eva vācyatvaucityāditi tātparyārthaḥ | evamapīti | 'devadatto devadattā'dityādiprayogavāraṇe'pi | vṛkṣāt spandata iti | tatrāpi tādṛśadhātvarthānāśrayatve sati tajjanyavibhāgāśrayatvasya vṛkṣe sattvāt | iyamāpattiḥ satyantārthasya vācyatvapakṣe'pīti bodhyam | iṣṭāpattyoktaprayogā- pattiśaṅkāṃ pariharati-āsanāccalita iti | uktaprayogavaraṇāya keṣāṃcit samādhānaṃ dūṣayitumupanyasyati-eteneti | apādānatvabodhe - nirūpakatāsaṃbandhenā- pādānatvaprakārakaśābdabodhe, tenāpādānatvaprakārakopasthitau na vyabhicāraḥ | hetutvamiti | viśeṣyatāsaṃbandhena hetutvamityarthaḥ | saṃyogānukūlavyapārārthalākṣaṇika śaktibhramaviṣaya spandadhātuyoge tu tādṛśaprayoge iṣṭāpattireveti bhāvaḥ | patadhāturi sakarmakaḥ | adhodeśānuyogikasaṃyogānukūlavyāpārārtha katvāt | ata eva 'dvitīyā śritā-' iti sūtreṇa kūpapatita ityādisamāsaḥ sidhyati | tenāśvāt patatītyādiprayogasya nānupapattiḥ | athavā sakarmakadhātujanyopasthiterityanena vyāpārānadhikaraṇavṛttiphalāvacchinnavyāpāravṛttijñānajanyopasthitirheturiti phala- viśiṣṭavyāpāre śaktiriti mate, tayoḥ pṛthak śaktiriti mate saketīyabodhaniṣṭhaviṣayatāśe tādṛśaphalaviṣayakatvācchinnatvāvagāhijñānajanyopa- sthitirhetustādṛśaphalaviśiṣṭavyāpāraviṣayakalakṣaṇāgrahajanyopasthitisca heturitya- yamartho vivakṣitaḥ | tenārthābhedena śabdabhedābhāve'pi na doṣa iti dik | apāsta iti | vṛkṣāt spandata iti prayogavāraṇāyoktahetuhetudbhāvāṅgīkāre 'āsanāccalita' ityādiprayogasyeṣṭasyānirvāha iti tādṛśahetuhetumadbhāvo nābhyupeya iti 'vṛkṣāt spandat' iti prayoge'pīṣṭāpattiḥ kartumuciteti bhāvaḥ | atrāhuḥ spande kampanamātrārthakatayā vibhāgāpratipādakatayāvidhitvābhāvena 'vṛkṣāt spandata' iti prayogo nocito'pi tu 'vṛkṣe spandata' ityeva | calatestu vibhāgānukūlavyāpārādyarthakatvamāsamāccalita iti prayogānurodhāt, spanderapi tadarthakatvaṃ iṣṭa evoktaprayoga iti | anye tu tasya naiva tadarthakatvamityāhuḥ | calateriva kamperapi tadarthakatvamabhipretya tu prakṛtagrantho'vaseyaḥ | durvāramiti |
vibhāgāvacchinnavyāpārānāśrayatvasya vṛkṣe sattvenāpādānasaṃjñāyāḥ prāpteḥ |
karmasaṃjñayeti | phalatāvacchedakasamavāyena vṛkṣe vibhagarūpaphalāśrayatvasattvena karmasaṃjñā | 'grāmādāgacchatī'tyādāvāgacchatevibhāgajanyasaṃyogānukūlavyāpārārthaka- tayā vibhāgasya phalatvābhāvāt kartṛniṣṭhasyaiva samavāyasya phalatāvacchedakatvācca na grāmādeḥ karmatvaprasaktiḥ | karmasaṃjñayā bādhenāpādānasaṃjñāyā virahāt pañcamyāḥ sādhutvābhāve'pyapādānasaṃjñābhrameṇa tatra tādṛśaprayogādavadhyanvayabodhāpattirityā- śaṅkāṃ pariharati-bhramādityadinā | buddheḥ - upasthiteḥ | hetutvaṃ vācyamiti | na caivaṃ tyajipatyoḥ paryāyatvabhramadaśāyāmapi tathāvidhaprayogādapādānatvabodhānupa- pattiriti vācyam | iṣṭāpatterityāśayāt, patyādiparyāyatvenāgṛhītatyajyādya- janyopasthititvena hetutvamityatra vā prakṛtagranthatātparya kalpyaṃ, tena noktadoṣaḥ | vibhāgānavacchinnavyāpāropasthitehetutvaṃ kalpayitvā bodhavāraṇaṃ tu na samyak, gatijanyasaṃyogārthakapatiyoge tadvodhānāpatteriti bhāvaḥ | atra yadi bodhābhāvo'nubhavasiddha ityatra 'yadi' padena tādṛśabodha iṣṭa eveti sūcitam | nanu 'balāhakādvidyotata' ityatrāpādānatvānupapattiḥ, gateranupasthitestasyā evāpāyatvādata āha valāhakāditi | niḥsṛtyeti | adhyeti | atrādhyāhārastatpūrvakavidyotane dhātūrvṛttiḥ, vakṣayati caitadanupadaṃ grandhakāro'pi | balāhako nāma dhūmajyotiḥsalilamarutāṃ saṃdhātaḥ, tādṛśasamudāyātmaka- meghādavayavabhūtajyotīrūpavidyuto bhedāvivakṣaṇāttasyāvadhitvam | yadā tu tejasa ādhāratvena sa vivakṣyate tadā 'valāhake vidyotate' vidyuditi saptamī | yadā saṃghātāvayavayorabhedāvivakṣā tadā prātipadikārthamātre prathamā bhavatīti bodhyam | spaṣṭaṃ cedaṃ kaiyaṭādau | 'rūpaṃ rasāt pṛtha'gityatra rūpe vibhāgapūrvaka- saṃyogānukūlavyāpārarūpaniḥsaraṇasya bādhādityāśaṅkāṃ nirākartumāha- rūpamiti | buddhiparikalpitamiti | ayaṃ bhāvaḥ 'dhruvamapāye ' ityatrāpāyaśabdena bhedasyāpi grahaṇaṃ 'pañcamī vibhakte' ityatra vibhaktaśabdeneva, pṛthakśabdārtho'pi bheda eva, na tu guṇāntaramuktaprayogādeva | bhādajñānaviṣayatvamapi 'bhidir vidāraṇa iti' vidāraṇaśabdena gṛhyate | ekatvenājñātasya bhedaviṣayatvena jñāne buddhividāraṇasya tatrāpi sattvāt | evaṃ ca 'ghaṭād bhinna' ityādāvivātrāpyanena sūtreṇāpādānatve 'apādāne pañcamī' iti sūtreṇa pañcamī | 'anyārāt ' iti sūtre'nyetyarthagrahaṇaṃ tu na, itaraśabdasya pṛdhagupādānāt | bhedaviṣayabuddhiviṣayatvarūpabuddhiyidāraṇa-
sāpekṣatayā buddhiparikalpitatvamucyata iti | atra pṛthakaśabdasyākriyāvācitvāt kārakatvānupapattiriti na śaṅkyaṃ, bhavatītvadhyāhṛtya dhātūnāmanekārthatayā bhaidātmakapṛthagbhāvasya tadarthatvāṅgīkāreṇādoṣa iti tātparyāt |
imaṃ kleśaṃ parihartu sūtrāntareṇāpyatra pañcamīmāha 'pṛthagvinā ' iti | pare tu apāye - gativiśeṣe sati yad dhruvamavadhibhāvo'pagamāśrate sati tadatiriktasyāvadhitvāpayogivyāpārasyānāśrayo yastadapadānamiti sūtrārthaḥ | trāsādervṛtrāt parṇamityādau vyabhicārānnāvadhitvopayogitā | prakṛte'vādhitvo- payogitve'pi tasyāvivakṣitatvānna doṣaḥ | ata eva bhāṣye adhrauvyasyāvivakṣayā 'trastādaśvā'dityatra śaṅkā samāhitā | 'dhruvamapāye' ' iti tu vṛkṣasya parṇa patatītyādau vastugatyā vṛkṣasya kriyājanakatayā prāptāpādānatvavāraṇāya | tatrāpāye iti kriyājanakatvena vivakṣite ityarthatātparyamāha | dhruvagrahaṇaṃ tvapādānasyāvadhibhāvopagama eva vyāpāro na tu kārakāntarasyevānyo vyāpāra iti jñāpanāyetyāhurityanyatra vistaro'vagantavyaḥ | tadvibhajatenardiṣṭaviṣayamiti | atra viṣayaśabde nimittabhūtagatipara ityāśayenāha-yatra sākṣāditi | uktarūpādhyāhāraṃ vinetyarthaḥ | dhātunā - gatyarthakatvena prasiddhadhātunā | nirdiśyate - upasthāpyate | gatyarthakadhāturyatra nirdiśyata iti yāvat | upāttaviṣayaṃ lakṣayati-yatra dhātvantareti | dhātvantarasya - niḥsaratyāderartho dhātvantarārthaḥ, soṅgaṃ - viśeṣaṇaṃ yatretyarthaḥ | uktamartha lakṣye saṃgamayi-niḥsaraṇāṅga iti | niḥsaraṇaṃ - vibhāgaburvakaṃ gamanaṃ yatreti bahuvrīhiḥ | apekṣiteti | ākāṅakṣitetyarthaḥ | ākāṅakṣā cānupādāne sati bhavatītyāśayenāha-yathā kuta iti | gamyamānāpi kriyā kārakavibhaktiprayodiketi bhāvaḥ | praśnottaravākyedve api apekṣitakriye iti bodhyam || uddeśyasya caturthyarthatve mānamāha-tathā hīti | sūtrāt saṃpradānasya caturthyarthatvasiddhāvapi noddeśyasya tattvasiddhirityāśaṅkāṃ nirācaṣṭe-tacceti | tacca - saṃpradānaṃ ca | 'karmaṇā yamabhipraiti sa saṃpradānam' iti sūtrāduddeśya ityanvayaḥ | saṃpradānasyoddeśyarūpatāgamakatānirvāhakaṃ viśeṣaṇamāha-karmaṇetyādinā ityarthakādityantena | karaṇabhūteneti | anena karmaṇeti sahārthe tṛtīyeti bhramam, asaṃgatā tṛtīyeti bhramaṃ ca nirākaroti | karmaṇaḥ karaṇatvaṃ saṃbandhaviṣayakecchārūpabhiprāyaghaṭakasaṃbandhaghaṭakasattvādidvārā | tathā gavādi- niṣṭhasvatvādirūpaphalanirūpakatvādisaṃbandhavattayoddeśyaḥ saṃpradānamiti paryavasito'rtha
iti bhāvaḥ | idameva - uddeśyatvameva | tatra mīmāṃsakasaṃvādamāha-taduddeśyakeccheti | tathā cecchīyaviṣayatāviśeṣa uddeśyatvamiti bhāvaḥ . ityadhikaraṇa iti | pūrvatantrai caturthādhyādvitīyacaraṇasthabhidamadhikaraṇam | niṇīṃtamiti | tatra hi dīkṣitasya daṇḍapradānaṃ vidhāya punarāmrātaṃ 'krīte some maitrāvaruṇāya daṇḍaṃ prayatthatī'ti, tadetaddhākyavihitaṃ maitrāvaruṇāya daṇḍapradānaṃ pratipattikarma, yato dīkṣitahastadhāraṇe kṛtārtho daṇḍaḥ pratipattyapekṣaḥ | dvitīyayā tasya prādhānyaṃ ca ganyate, dānasyutu na, ato maitrāvaruṇahaste prakṣepo'sya kartavya iti pūrvapakṣayitvā saṃpradānacaturthyā maitrāvaruṇasya uddeśyatvalakṣaṇaprādhānyāvagamānmaitrāvaruṇārtha daṇḍapradānaṃ daṇḍasya prayojakaṃ, sthitvā praiṣānuvacane kurvatastasyālambanatayā dṛṣṭārtha eva hi daṇḍaḥ | ata evāgnīṣomīyapaśuvikāre niruḍhapaśau dīkṣite satyapi maitrāvaruṇārthatvena daṇḍopādānasiddhiriti siddhāntitamatyarthaḥ | tathā ca dvitīyānirdiṣṭadaṇḍāpekṣayā caturthīnirdiṣṭamaitrāvaruṇasya prādhānyāvagamādibhimata- sidhiriti bhāvaḥ | ata evājāṃ grāmaṃ nayatītyatra grāmasyājāṃ prati śeṣitvābhāvānna saṃpradānatvaṃ, grāmasaṃskārarthamajānayane tu grāme tatsattve'pi paratvāt karmasaṃjñayā bādhānna doṣa ityāhuḥ | nanu 'sjakāya vastraṃ dadātī'ti prayogāpattistatra dādhātonirṇejanānukūlecchādirūpo vyāpārastatkarmaṇā vastreṇābhipreyamāṇatvā- drajakasyetyāśaṅakyeṣṭāpattyā pariharati rajakāyeti | tādṛśaprayogasyeṣṭatve mānamāha khaṇḍiketi | bālakopādhyāya ityarthaḥ | bhāṣyodāharaṇāditi | atra dadāteḥ syogaviśeṣānukūlavyāpārārthatayā mukhyadānārthakatadyoge eva saṃpradānatvāṅgīkāre bhāṣyasyoktaprayogānupapattiḥ | capeṭā - prasṛtakaratalam | na ca bhāktaḥ sa prayoga iti vācyam, kriyāgrahaṇaṃ kartavyamiti vacanaṃ khaṇḍayadbhirbhāṣyakāraiḥ saṃpradānasaṃjñāyā anvarthatvāṅgīkāreṇoktaprayogasya, rajakāyetyādiprayogasya ca mukhyatvopagamāt | evaṃ 'na śūdrāya mati dadyā'dityapyata eva saṃgacchate | tatra dadātirbodhanārthaḥ, matiśabdastajjanakavedādiparaḥ | 'viprāya gāṃ dadātī'tyatra viprasaṃpradānakagokarmakaṃ dānamiti bodhaḥ | saṃpradānamityasyānvarthatāṃ svīkurvatāṃ vṛttikāraṇāṃ matamāha vṛttikārāstviti | svasvatvanivṛttiparyantamiti | svasvatvanivṛttayanukūlavyāpārarthantamityartha- | idaṃ ca dānamātreṇa na parasvatvotpattirapi tu pratigraheṇetyabhipretya | anyathā punaḥ svasvatvanivṛttiparamātrasvatvotpattyanukūlavyāpāraparyantamityarthaḥ | saṃsṛṣṭidhane
dadātītyatra prayogāt paramātretyuktam | svatvavati svatvāntarānutpattyā patrāpi svatvavināśo'bhyupetavyaḥ | tatra sādhāraṇasvatvotpāde'pi na paramātrasvatvo- tpattiriti nātiprasaṅgaḥ | svatvaṃ ca dānādināśyaṃ pratigrahādijanyaṃ padārthāntaram | vitṛbhyaḥ śrāddhaṃ dadyādityādau vitṛnirūpitasvatvabādhe'pi dānoddeśyatvasya pitṛṣvakṣatatayā saṃpradānatvānna doṣaḥ | artha varṇayanta iti | uktadānakarmaṇe- tyādyarthamityarthaḥ | āhurityasyarasasūcanam | tadbijaṃ tūktameva | idaṃ ceti | idaṃ ca - saṃpradānaṃ ca, trividham |
traividhye hetumāha anirākaraṇādityādinā vākyapadīyādityantena | kartuḥkarmaṇā īpsitatamanirākaraṇāddānānirākaraṇādapratyākhyānāt preraṇānumatibhyāṃ ca dānaviṣayakapreraṇānumatibhyāṃ ca tyāgāṅgaṃ - tyāganirvāhakam, anena kārakatvamāveditaṃ tathā ca tādṛśaṃ kārakaṃ saṃpradānatāṃ labhata iti yojanā | athavā saṃpradānatāyāmevānirākāraṇādityādi trayaṃ hetutvanānveti | tathā cānirākartṛ prerakamanumantṛ ceti trividhamityarthaḥ | nātra sūrya iti | sūrye prārthanānumatyonirākaraṇaṃ tadrūpānākrāntatvabodhanāya | dvitīyādikārakaprakṛtyartha- vibhattayarthayoranvayabodhaṃ varṇayati atra sarvatreti | 'hari bhajatī'tyādiṣu | prakṛtipratyayārthayoriti | prakṛtyarthasya pratyayārthe'bhedasaṃbandhena viśeṣaṇatvamityarthaḥ | na ca pratyayārthe prakṛtyarthasyābhedasaṃbandhena viśeṣaṇatvaṃ na dṛṣṭamiti vācyam | mīmāṃsakamate devatārthakataddhitādau dṛṣṭatvāt | taduktam 
'āmikṣāṃ devatāyaktāṃ vadatyevaiṣa taddhitaḥ |' iti |
tatra dvitīyārthasyāśrayatvasyādheyatāsaṃbandhena phale'nvayaḥ | kartṛtṛtīyārthattya tasya dhātṛpāttavyapāre, karaṇatṛtīyārthasya tasya vyutpattivaicitryeṇa tṛtīyopātta- vyāpāre, tasya ca janyatāsaṃbandhena dhātvarthavyāpāre tadupāttaphale ca | athavā vyāpāro na tṛtīyārthaḥ | api tvāśrayaḥ karaṇatvaśaktimān vā, tasya svavṛttivyāpārajanyatvasaṃbandhena dhātvarthe saptamyarthāśrayasya kartṛkarmadvārā tannaṣṭhavyāpāre phale ceti yathākramamiti bodhyam | pratyakṣārthakadhātusamabhivyāhāre laukikaviṣayataiva phalatāvacchedakaḥ saṃbandha iti niyamaḥ, anyathā saurabhādyapanītabhāne'pi 'saurabhaṃ paśyatī'tyādiprayogāpatteḥ . evaṃ ca kathamatra tathāvidhaprakāratāyāḥ phalatāvacchedakatvābhyupagama iti na śaṅkyam | tādṛśaniyamasya viṣayānupahitapratyakṣārthasthale eva svīkārāt | 'grandhaṃ
jighratī'tyatra dhrāṇajapratyakṣānukūlo vyāpāro dhātvarthaḥ | na tu gandhasya tadarthe'ntarbhāvaḥ | laukikaviṣayatārupaphalatāvacchedakasaṃbandhena tathāvidhapratyakṣa- rūpaphalāśrayatvād gandhasya karmatvamita bodhyam | 'puṣpaṃ jidhratī'tyatra gandhanirūpitalaukikaviṣayitāśālipratyakṣānukūlo vyāpāro dhātvarthaḥ | ādheyatvoyasāṃsagikaviṣatānirūpitaprakāratārūpaviṣayatāviśeṣātmakaphalatāvacchedakasaṃbandhena tathāvidhapratyakṣātmakaphalāśrayatayā puṣpasya karmatvam | 'tadānanaṃ mṛtsurabhi kṣitīśvara' ityatrānane gandha iti jñānīyatādṛśaprakāratāvatvamānane- 'stītyānanasya karmatvopapattiḥ | saṃbandhaghaṭakaprakāratve gandhaniṣṭhaviśeṣyatā- nirūpitatvaṃ niveśanīyam | tenadānītanapuṣpe gandha iti pratyakṣīyapuṣye viśeṣyatānirūpitādheyatvīyasāṃsargikaviṣayatānirūpitāprakāratāyāḥ kāle samādā- yāpi noktadoṣaḥ | samavāyasaṃbandhāvacchinnatvenādheyatvasya viśeṣaṇatvāditi diktve'pi kālaṃ jidhratīti na prayogaḥ | kāle gandha iti bhramamādaya taddoṣatādavasthyaṃ tu nāpādyam, tādṛśabhramasyāprāmāṇikatvāt | ata eva 'tejo jidhrati' 'rūpaṃ jidhrati'tyādiprayoge na kālikasaṃbandhāvacchinnādheyatvasaṃbandhena kālaprakārakapramā | nanu dvitīyārthāśrayasyādheyatāsaṃbandhena dhātvarthaphalenānvaya- svīkāro na saṃbhavati, tasya haryādirūpanāmārthābhinnasya dhātvarthe bhedasaṃbandhenānvayasyāvyutpannatvāt | anyathā taṇḍulaḥ pacatītyatra karmatādisaṃbandhenāpi tadāpatteriti cenna | nāmārthadhātvarthayovibhattayarthamadvārīkṛtya parasparamanvayo netyeva vyutpatteḥ | prakṛte vibhattayarthadvārā tathānvaye bādhakābhāvāt | nanu prakṛtipratyayārthayorabhedhaḥsaṃsargo na saṃbhavati, vibhaktīnāṃ dharmamātravācaka- tvādityata āha vibhaktīnāṃ dhamīṃti | dvitīyādīnāṃ karmatvādirūpadharmamātre śaktirlādhavāducitā tatra prakṛtyarthasya svarūpasaṃbandhe nādheyatayānvayaḥ | tasya ca nirūpakatāsaṃbandhena dhātvarthaphalādāviti paramataṃ dūṣayati dharmamātreti | mātrapadena dharmivyavacchedaḥ | 'karmaṇi ' iti | 'karmaṇi dvitīyā' 'kartṛkaraṇayostṛtīyā' ityādisūtrasvarasabhaṅgāpatterityarthaḥ | anyathā 'karmatve dvitīyā' ityādi sūtrayetra tu 'karmaṇa' ityādi | karmaṇivācye ityādirnārtho'pi tu karmaṇi bodhye dvitīyetyādirarthaḥ, tannirvāhaśca prakṛtyarthasya pratyayārthe'nvayād bhavati, evaṃ ca na tatsvarasabhaṅgaḥ | na cāśrayatve haryādeḥ prakṛtyarthasyānvaye'pi hariviśiṣṭamāśrayatvamityeva bodho, na tvāśrayatvavān haristatkathaṃ
karmabodhanirvāhakatvaṃ dvitīyāyā iti vācyam, āśrayatve prakṛtyarthaharyāderanvayabodhe harirāśraya iti bhānasabodhasaṃbhavāt | 'rājasakho devadatta' ityato devadatte rājamitratvaśābdabodhe rājani devadattamitratvagrahavat, karmaṇi śaktibodhaka- sūtrasyāśrayamātraśaktigrāhakatvakalpanāpi, bhavanmate'pi sūtrasvarasabhaṅgatādava- sthyācca phalasya dhātulabhyatayāśrayamātre tatkalpanamiti cenmamāpi mate lāghavāddhameṃ tatkalpanamiti tulyamityata āha dvitīyādyarthaketi | dharmivācakateti | 'prāptodaka' ityata udakakartṛkaprāptikarmagrāma ityākārakasya samāsārthasya, grāmasya ca sāmānādhikaraṇyaviṣayakabodhasyānubhavasiddhasya nirvāhāya dvitīyāyāḥ karmaṇi śaktirāvaśyakī | anyathā dvitīyārthabahuvrīherapi karmatvabodhakatvāpattyā sāmānādhikaraṇyānupapatteḥ | dharmivācakatāyāṃ bhāṣyamapi sādhakamityāha supāṃ karmādaya iti | paramate tu etatsvarasabhaṅgāpattiśceti bhāvaḥ | na cātrāpi karmapaddaṃ karmatvaparam, anekasthale lakṣaṇāpatteḥ | nanu karmaṇītyādau karmādipade lakṣaṇāpattāvapi dvitīyādīnāṃ dharmamātravācakatvamucitaṃ lāghavāt, astu ca bahuvrīherapi dharmamātravācakatvaṃ, sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ ca lakṣaṇayopapādyam, mukhyārthe prāptodakādiprayogastu prāptodako'stītyādāvityata āha digiti | tadarthasamavetaṃ sati kṛtastaddhitasaṃjñakasyāpi tabyāderdhanivācakatvaṃ na sidhyet | 'paktavyārataṇḍulā' ityādau taṇḍulādisāmānādhikaraṇyānubhavānurodhāttatheti cenna | uktarītyaiva tadupapatteḥ | 'paktavya'ityāditaḥ karmatvamātrabodho'nubhavaviruddha iti cet, prāptodakādāvapi tulyamiti bhāvaḥ | dvitīyādīnāmāśraye'pi na śaktiḥ, phalavyāpārayordhātulabhyatvavadāśrayasyāpi prakṛtibhūtaprātipadikana labhyatvāt, api tvāśrayatvādimātre'nanyalabhyatvāt | dharmadharmiṇoścābhedāt karmaṇītyādinirdeśo nānupapannaḥ, ata eva dvitīyādeḥ karmatvādiśaktivācakatvimati saddhānte prāptodakādibadavrīherdharmibodhakatānirvāhaḥ, śaktibhedabhadāpannāyā eva śaktevibhakti- vācyatvāt | tiḍkṛdādestu pacati paktā pakka ityādita evāśrayapratīteḥ 'yuṣmadyupapade samānādhikaraṇe-' iti vyavahārānurodhāccāśraye śaktiryuktetyāśayenāha-āśrayasyāpītyādinā | āśrayasyāpi prakṛtyaiva lābhānna vibhaktivācyatetyanvayaḥ | na vibhaktīti | na dvitīyādivibhaktivācyatetyarthaḥ | nanvāśrayatvasya vācyatve āśrayatvātvasya śakyatāvacchedakatvamupeyaṃ, tathā ca gauravaṃ, tasya tadāśrayādyatiriktāvṛttitve sati sakalatadāśrayavṛttitvarūpatvādityata āha 
tadeveti | āśrayatvamevetyarthaḥ | evenāśrayatātvavyāvacchedaḥ | tadvṛttestasya kathaṃ tanniṣṭhavācyatābacchedakatvamityāśaṅkyāha-tādātmyeneti | tādātmyasya vṛttyā- niyāmakatve'pi saṃbandhitvaṃ tena saṃbhavatyevetyāśrayatvasya tādātmyena svasaṃbandhitayā na svaniṣṭhavācyatāvacchedakatvānupapattiriti bhāvaḥ | yajyapyāśrayatātvasyākhaṇḍo- padhitvāṅgīkāre tasyāvacchedakatve'pi na gauravaṃ, tathāpi phalāśrayatvatvādereva dvitīyādivācyatve āśrayatātvasyātīprasaktatayā tadavacchedakatvāsaṃbhavaḥ, phalāśrayatātvasyānekaphalaghaṭitatayā gauravatādavasthyam | kiṃ cāśrayatātvaviśiṣṭe śaktikalpanāyāṃ dharmadharmiṇorubhayatra tatkalpanayā na pūrvakalpāpekṣayā lāghavamiti bhāvaḥ |
vyāpāro'rīti | karaṇatāghaṭakavyāpāro'pītyarthaḥ | apināśrayatvaparigrahaḥ | ṣaṇṇāmiti | ṣaṇṇāṃ kārakavibhaktīnāmityarthaḥ | kārakāṇāṃ ṣaṭatvena tatprayuktavibhaktitvenopādānāt ṣaṣṭhīrahitavibhaktīnāṃ vivakṣaṇe tāsāṃ paṭtve'pi kārakaṣaṣṭhyā asaṃrgahaḥ, tatsahitānāṃ vibhaktīnāṃ vivakṣaṇe tu tāsāṃ saptatvaprakārakavibhaktisaṃgrahāpattiśceti ṣaṇṇāmityuktirasaṃgataiveti dūṣaṇānavakāśaḥ | anena caipapadavibhaktīnāṃ saṃbandhārthakatvamiti sūcitam |
atha keṣucit prayogeṣu vibhattayartho nirupyate tatropapadavibhaktīnāmatrā'rthe tu viśeṣānuktestatsthale tatsaṃbandhapratīśca tadarthakatvam | tatra yāsāṃ viśeṣasaṃbandhe vidhānaṃ, yatra ca tatsaṃbandhena saṃbandhini niyatākāṅkṣā-tatra tāsāṃ viśeṣarūpeṇaiva saṃbandhārthakatvam yathā 'anurlakṣaṇe' ityādau 'antarā tvāṃ māṃ hari'rityādau ca | 'japamanu prāvarṣa'dityatra lakṣyalakṣaṇabhāvasaṃbandho dvitīyāvācyo na dyotyaḥ, karmapravacanīyatvāt | 'karma proktavantaḥ karmapravacanīyāḥ' iti tadvayutpattipradarśanena kriyāgataviśeṣaṃ dyotitavatāṃ saṃprati nāmārthakriyayoḥ saṃbandhadyotakānāmeva tatsaṃjñāvidhānāt | yatra tu tadvādhastatra vidhānasāmarthyāt saṃjñā, yathā 'adhiparī anarthakau' ityādinādhyādīnām | spaṣṭaṃ cedaṃ 'karmapravacanīyāḥ' ityatra bhāṣyādau | japamityatra vibhakterhetutṛtīyābādhakatvāddhetuvācakatvamapi, lakṣyatvaṃ ca prakṛte jñānajanyajñānaviṣayatvarūpajñāpyatvasvarūpam | japasya hi kālaviśeṣādikaraṇa- katvena jñātasya svādhikaraṇakālottarakālavaiśiṣṭyena jñāpakatvam | etena japasya vṛṣṭivyāpyatvābhāvādavṛṣṭerjapajñāpyatvāsaṃbhava iti śaṅkā nirastā | nirjñātakāla- kriyāyā anirjñātakālakriyāyāḥ kālaviśeṣavaiśiṣṭyena jñāpakatvāt | ata eva 'kadā vṛṣṭa' iti praśne etasyottaratvaṃ saṃgacchate | kālaviśeṣavaiśiṣṭyajñānaṃ ca tadvākyajanyaṃ, mānāntarajanyaṃ vetyanyadetat | japābhinnahetujñānajanyajñānaviṣayo varṣaṇamiti bodhaḥ | uktaṃ ca hariṇā 
'kriyāyā dyotako nāyaṃ, saṃbandhasya na vācakaḥ |
nāpi kriyāpadākṣepī, saṃbandhasya tu bhedakaḥ ||' iti |
tadarthastu 'prajayatī'tyādāvupasargavanna kriyāgataprakārṣādirūpaviśeṣadyotakaḥ anuḥ, prādeśaṃ viparilikhatītyādau viśabda iva na kriyāpadākṣeī, viśabdo hi vimānakriyāmākṣipati, ata eva prādeśasya karmatvam, prādeśaṃ vimāya parilikhatītyarthāt, tathā satyākṣiptakriyānibandhanadvitīyāsiddhau
'karmapravacamīyayukte-' ityasya vaiyarthyāpatteḥ, kiṃ tu viśiṣṭakriyajanitoktasaṃbandhasya vibhaktivācyasya dyotakaḥ, prakṛte tu niśamanakriyākṛta uktasaṃbandha iti | anye tu lakṣaṇaṃ - vyāvartanaṃ, prakṛte ca pravarṣaṇakriyā japottarakālikatayā bodhyatvena vivakṣitā, vyāvartakaśca japa iti kālottarakālikatvarūpasaṃbandhahetuśca na dyotyo vibhattayarthaḥ | japābhinnahetvadhikaraṇakālottarakālikaṃ varṣaṇamiti bodha ityāhuḥ | viśeṣasaṃbandhe tattadupapadavibhaktevidhānaṃ, viśeṣarūpeṇa saṃbandhavivakṣaṇe ṣaṣṭyeveti 'caturthī-' iti sūtrabhāṣyasiddham, tatra hi guroradaṃ gurvarthamityatra tādarthyasya saṃbandhatvena vivakṣāyāṃ ṣaṣṭhītyuktam, tena 'tava mama cāntarā kamaṇḍalu'rityatra yuṣamdasmadbhyāṃ na dvitīyā, avadhitvasya saṃbandhatvena vivakṣaṇānna tvavadhitvatvena | antarāśabdavācyamadhyādhikaraṇaghaṭakamadhyatvasyāvadhitvasaṃbandhena saṃbandhiniyatā- kāṅkṣatvāt, avadhitvatvavacchinnasyaiva dvitīyārthatvād dvitīyā'prāpteḥ | ata eva svasaṃbandhikamaṇḍalutātparyeṇoccarite kamaṇḍalau yuṣmadasmadarthasya svasvāmibhāvarūpasaṃbandhavivakṣayā ṣaṣṭyantaprayogopapattāvapi tadasvāmikakamaṇḍalu- tātparyakatādṛśaprayogānupapattiriti dūṣaṇānavakāśaḥ | antarāśabdasyā- bhāvavānapyarthaḥ, antarā hari na sukhamityādau | hariśabdastātparyagrāhakaḥ | yadvā pratiyogitātvena tadviśeṣabhānam | etena śuddhapratiyogitvasya vācyatve haribhaktimatyapi dvitvādinā tatpratiyogikābhāvasattvādantarā hari sukhamiti prayogāpattiriti dūṣaṇaṃ nirastam . astītyadhyāhārasabhyasukhakartṛkasattābhāve haribhaktayabhāvata ādheyatvasaṃbandhenānvayaḥ | na ca nāmārthayoriti vyutpattivirodhāt tathānvayāsaṃbhava iti vācyam | nipātātirikteti viśeṣaṇāt | yadvābhāva evāntarāśabdārthaḥ | sāmānādhikaraṇasaṃbandhena tathāvidhābhāve tasyānvayaḥ, pakṣadvaye'pi sukhādyabhāve haribhattayabhāvaprayojyatvaṃ paryavasyati | dvitīyayakṣe tenaiva vā saṃbandhenānvayaḥ | yadyapi nipātātirikteti viśeṣaṇāt pratiyogitvādisaṃbandhenānvayaḥ prakṛtyarthasya saṃbhavati, tathāpi tasyāḥ sārthakatve saṃbhabhavati sādhutvamātraprayojanakatvasvīkāro'nucitaḥ | ṣaṣṭhyā iva tasyā uktārthakatvāvaśyakatvācca | mañjūṣākṛnmate nipātānāṃ nāmatvābhāvātanmate nipātātiriktatvaviśeṣaṇapraleśo nāsti | evamantareṇaśabdo'pyanena samānārthakaḥ | tadyoge'pi 'antarāntareṇa' iti sūtreṇa antareṇa tvāṃ māṃ hariḥ, antareṇa hari na sukhamityatra dvitīyā | tatrāpyuktaprakāro'nusartavyaḥ | evaṃ
vināśabdo'pyabhāvavadarthako'bhāvārthako vā tatrāpyeṣaiva dik | 'vṛkṣaṃ prati vidyotate vidyu'dityādau pratiparyanūnāṃ dyotyo lakṣyalakṣaṇabhāvo vibhaktivācyaḥ, niviḍāndhakāre vṛkṣadarśanena hi tatsamīpadeśaviśeṣe vidyudvidyotanamanumīyate, jñānajanyajñānaviṣayatvaṃ vibhattayarthaḥ | va-kṣaviṣayakajñānajanyajñānaviṣayo vidyudvidyotanamiti bodhaḥ | bhakto viṣṇuṃ pratītyādau bhaktatvākhyaḥ saṃbandhaḥ kaṃcitprakāraṃ prāptasyākhyāne pratyādiḥ karmapravacanīyasaṃjña ityarthakana 'itthaṃbhūtākhyāne' ityanena karmapravacanīyasaṃjñāka- pratyādidyotyo vibhaktivācyaḥ, na tu viṣayaviṣayibhāvastathā, sūtropāttasya bhaktatvasyaiva tathātvaucityāt | na ca tasya bhaktapadopāttatayā na dvitīyāśakti- saṃbhavo'nanyalabhyasyaiva śabdārthatvādita vācyam | prakāratayā bodhe siddhe'pi saṃsargatayā bodhanirvāhāya vibhaktiśakterāvaśyakatvāt | padāntaravācyasyāpi vibhattayarthasya saṃsargatayā bhānasya caitrasya putra ityadau dṛṣṭatvāt | janyatsasyaiva putrapadaśaktiviśeṣye saṃbandhatāsaṃbhavāt | padārthaḥ padārtheneta vyutpattivirodhena padārthaikadeśajanyatve nirūpitatsasya saṃbandhatāyā anucitatvāditi bhāvaḥ | 'lakṣmīrhari pratī'tyādau svasvāmibhāvaḥpratyādidyotyo vibhaktivācyaḥ | harisvāmikā lakṣmīriti bodhaḥ | 'vṛkṣaṃ vṛkṣaṃ prati siñcati'tyādau vīpsāviṣaye vyāpyatvaṃ dvirvacanadyotyaṃ, vṛkṣaśabdasya vyāpyatvaviśiṣṭavṛkṣaparatvaṃ, tasya ca vyāpakatvasaṃbandhenānvaye ttparyagrāhakaḥ pratyādiḥ | vyāpyatāvacchedaka- saṃbandhastādātmyaṃ, vyāpakatāvacchedakaśca secanasya dravadravyasaṃyogānukūla- vyāpārātmanaḥ svajanyasaṃyogaḥ | tādṛśasaṃyoge vyāpakatvabhāne tu samavāyastathā | vyāpakatāghaṭakābhāve pratiyogivaiyadhikaraṇyaṃ niravacchinnavṛttitvarūpaṃ, vyāpyavṛttitvaṃ vā niveśyam | tena kriyāyāḥ saṃyogasya cāvyāpyavṛttitayā tadabhāvasya tadvatyapi vṛkṣe sattve'pi na vyāpakatvahāniḥ | secanaviśeṣe jagatītalagatavṛkṣasāmānya- vyāpakatvasaṃbhave'pi na kṣatiḥ | vṛkṣapadasya buddhisthatvena vṛkṣaviśeṣaparatvāt | tathā ca tādṛśavṛkṣaniṣṭhapratiyogivyadhikaraṇābhāvapratiyogitānavacchedakaṃ yaccaitrakartṛka- secanatvādi tadvattvarūpaṃ vyāpakatvaṃ saṃbandha iti paryavasitam, na tu tādṛśābhāvapratiyogitāśūnyatvam | tādṛśasecanasya tādṛśavṛkṣaniṣṭhasvaprati- yogitāvacchedakāvacchinnavyadhikaraṇobhyābhāvādipratiyogitvād vyāpakatvāsaṃbhavā- patteriti dik | secanakriyāyāḥ sakarmakatayā karmaṇyeva dvitīyā, karmapravacanīyasaṃjñāphalaṃ tu gatyupasargasaṃjñābādhanadvārā patvābhāva eveti prāñca āhuḥ
| tanmate secanakarmatve tatkarmaṇi vā svarūpasaṃbandhena tādātmyena yathākramaṃ vyāpakatvaṃ prakṛtyarthasaṃbandhatayā bhāsate, na tu secanādau | vibhattayartha viśeṣaṇatayānvitasya prakṛtyarthasya secanādau viśeṣaṇatayānvayāyogāt | anye tu karmatvāvivakṣāyāṃ dvitīyāsiddhirapi karmapravacanīyasaṃjñāprayojanam | ata eva pratyartha śabdābhiniveśa ityādāvarthasya niveśanakriyānirūpitādhikaraṇatve'pi arthamartha pratītyeva vigrahapradarśanaṃ bhāṣyādau kṛtam | vṛkṣādeḥ kriyāyāḥ karmatvamupaganyaitasyāḥ saṃjñāyā dvitīyāsādhakatvāṅgīkāre tu saptamyantenaiva prakṛte vigrahasya samucitatayā tathāvidhabhāṣyasaṃgatyāpattiḥ | tādṛśasaṃjñāyā dvitīyāphalakatve tvarthasyādhikaraṇatva- vivakṣāyāṃ vyāpakatvasaṃbandhādhikā dvitīyeti nāsaṃgatiḥ | ata eva 'vṛkṣaṃ vṛkṣaṃ prati śyāmatā' 'ghaṭaṃ ghaṭaṃ prati gandha' ityādāvapi kriyājanitavyāpārakatvasaṃbandhāthikā dvitīyā sidhyati | dvirvacanena vyāpyatvadyotanāt secanārthakapadasamabhi- vyāhārāttattadvayāpakaṃ vyāpyatvarūpeṇa prakāratayā bhāsate | anugatavyāpyatvatvānaṅgīkāre tu vīpsāyaṃ dviruktividhānaṃ ca ? vyāpyatvasya vṛkṣādyarthakatve mānam | tathā hi vyāptipratīticchāprakṛte pyupasṛṣṭāpnotestatpratītiparatvād dviruktaprayogādicchāyā apratīterna tasya tadarthakatā | evaṃ ca pyāptipratīticchāyāṃ satyāṃ dvirvacanamiti vākyārthaḥ paryavasannaḥ | na ca dhātureva vyāpakatvārthako'stu astu ca sādhutvārthī vibhaktiriti vācyam | nāmārthadhātvarthayoḥ sākṣādbhedasaṃbandhasyāvyutpannatayā vṛkṣasya vyāpakatādau nirūpitātvādisaṃbandhenānvayāsaṃbhavāt | ata eva caitraḥ pacyata ityādau pākādau caitrādeḥ kartṛtānirūpakatvādisaṃbandhenānvayabodho na, śobhana pacatītyatra śobhanapadārthasya dhātvarthe bhedenānvayādbhedeneti | taṇḍulaṃ pacatītyādau taṇḍulādevibhattayarthadvārānvayāt sākṣāditi | 'māsamadhīte' atra 'kālādhvanoḥ-' iti sūtreṇa kālādhvānuyogike kriyādravyaguṇādibhāvapratiyogike'tyanta- saṃyoge'tyantasaṃbandhe nirantarasaṃbandhe satupasthitatvat kālādhvavācakebhyo dvitīyetyarthakena dvitīyā | antaṃ virāmamatikrāntaścāsau saṃyogaśceti vyutpattyā nirantarasaṃbandhalābhaḥ | upasthitāśabdasvarūpamātrasya na prakṛtitvaṃ 'kālāḥ' 'antantasaṃyoge ca' iti samāsavidhau bahuvacananirdaśālliṅgāt | vyāpakatvaṃ dvitīyārthaḥ | padārthatāvacchedakamāsatvanirūpitavyāpakatvabhānāṅgīkāre samavāyādirvyāpyatāghaṭakasaṃbandhastādātmyaṃ, tattvaṃ ca triśadahorātra tvarūpamāsatva-
vanniṣṭhapratiyogivyadhikaraṇābhāvapratiyogitānavacchedakadharmitvam | vyāpakatā- ghaṭakaśca kālikaviśeṣaṇatātmakaḥ saṃbandhaḥ | sāsapadaṃ cāvayavānatirikta- triśādahorātrasamuhaparaṃ, na tu tāvatparthantasthāyisthūlakālaparam | tathā satyekadinādimātrādhyayanakartaryapi māsamadhīte iti prayogāpatteḥ | tadīyādhyayanasyāpi triśadahorātrarūpatādṛśakālaniṣṭhapratiyogivyadhikaraṇābhāva- pratiyogitānāvacchedakatadīyādhyayanatvavattvena māsādivyāpakatvasaṃbhavāt tadīyādhya- yanābhāvasya pratiyogivyadhikaraṇatayā noktagoṣaḥ | tadghaṭakāhorātraśca sūryodayāvadhisūryodayaparyantasthāyyavayavātiriktaḥ kṣaṇasamūpādireva | tena triśaddine pratyahamadhyayanasthale'pi svāpādisamayāvacchedena māsatvādhikaraṇe kṣaṇādau tadīyādhyayanasya pratiyogivyadhikaraṇābhāvaniveśe uktarītyoktadoṣatādavasthyamiti tannirāsāya tanniveśaḥ | pratiyogivaidhikaraṇyaṃ ca vyāpakatāvacchedakatvenābhimata- saṃbandhāvacchinnapratiyogitāvacchedakāvacchinānādhikaraṇatāvadbhinnavṛttitvam | na tu tatsaṃbandhena priyogitāvacchedakāvacchinnādhikaraṇāvṛttitvamadhyayanādeḥ rālikāvyāpya- vṛttitayā svādhikaraṇakāle'pi kicitkṣaṇādyavacchedena svābhāvasya sattvena pratiyogyadhikaraṇāvṛttitvābhāvena māsāntargataikadinādhyayanaśālinyapi māsamadhīte iti prayogāpattiḥ | krośaikadeśamātre parvatādisattve'pi krośatvākrāntatadanyadeśa- niṣṭhasaṃyogasaṃbandhāvacchinnapratiyogitākatadabhāvasya krośatvākrānte tatra kālikasaṃbandhela parvarūpapratiyogyadhikaraṇatvasattvena pratiyogyanadhikaraṇa- vṛttitvavaiśiṣṭyābhāvenātītatadvayaktyabhāva eva krośe tādṛśapratiyogivaiyadhikaraṇya- viśiṣṭatatpratiyogitānāvacchedakatvasya parvatatve sattvāt 'krośaṃ parvata' iti prayogāpattiḥ | ato vyāpakatāghaṭakasaṃbandhena pratiyogyanadhikaraṇatvanivaśaḥ | yadā devadatte na kāśyānupavāsa ekāhastadadvayādikaṃ vā kriyate, māsantaḥ- pātidhvavaśiṣṭāhorātreṣu kāśīto bahirūpoṣyate tādṛśasthale devadattīyo bhojanābhāvo māsaṃ kāśyāniti prayogāpattiḥ | kāśyavacchinnatva- viśiṣṭadevadattīyabhojanābhāvasya kāśyanavacchinnatādṛśabhojanābhāvasya caikyena kāśyavacchinnasya tādṛśabhojanābhāvasyābhāvo dināntarātmakatādṛśamāsatvādhikaraṇe vartamāno'pi devadattīyabhojanābhāvātmakapratiyogyadhikaraṇatvena tanmāsatvākrānta- dināntarasya na pratiyogivyadhikaraṇo'pi tvanyābhāva eva, tatpratiyogitā- nāvacchedakatvasya kāśyavacchinnadevadattīyabhojanābhāvatve sattvāt | evaṃ
māsāntargatadinadvayaṃ devadatte na bahirūṣitaṃ tadanantaramaṣṭāviśatidināni kāśyāmuṣitaṃ, tatra devadattābhāvo māsaṃ kāśyāmiti prayogāpattiḥ | kāśyava- cchinnadevadattābhāvābhāvasya māsatvākrāntadinaniṣṭhatve'pi priyogivyadhikaraṇatvā- bhāvāt | deśāntarāvacchinnatvaviśiṣṭadevadattābhāvarūpapratiyogisamānādhikaraṇa- tvāt |viśiṣṭasya śuddhānatirekitvāt | ataḥ pratiyogitāvacchedakāvacchinnavaiyadhi- karaṇyaniveśaḥ | tathā sati kāśyavacchinnatvaviśiṣṭadevadattīyabhojanābhāvatvarūpa- pratiyogitāvacchedakāvacchinnānadhikaraṇatvasya māsatvādhikaraṇadināntare sattvāt kāśyavacchinnadevadattīyabhojanābhāvādirapi pratiyogivyadhikaraṇa iti noktātiprasaṅgaḥ | atredaṃ bodhyam dvitīyārtho na vyāpakatvaṃ, vyāpakatvasya vyapyaviśeṣāghaṭitasyākhaṇḍasya durvacatayā tadghaṭitavyāpakatvānāmānantyā- cchaktyānantyaprasaṅgāt | prakṛtyarthasya vibhattayarthe nirākāṅkṣatayānanvayāpatteśceti pratiyogivaiyadhikaraṇyaviśiṣṭābhāve pratiyogitāvacchedakatve abhāve ca khaṇḍaśaḥ vṛttiḥ | prathamābhāve prakṛtyarthamāsāderādheyatvasaṃbandhenānvaya | tadanvitābhāvaprati- yogitāvacchedakatvasya tathāvidhāvacchedakatvāvacchinnapratiyogitākatvasaṃbandhena dvitīyārthāntare'bhāve'nvayaḥ | tādṛśābhāvasya caitrakartṛkādhyayanatvarūpasvākṣaya- ghaṭitaparamparāsaṃbandhena caitrakartṛkādhyayane'nvayaḥ | tattatpratiyogitāvacchedakā- vacchinnānadhikaraṇatve'pi dvitīyāyāḥ śākti svīkṛtya tasya prakṛtyarthe vyutpattivaicivyādviśeṣaṇatvamiti vāstu | tatra śaktisvīkāre śattayānantyam | lakṣaṇāṅgīkāre vibhaktau na lakṣaṇeti siddhāntavirodho gauravaṃ ceti caidatrāhuḥ agatyā śaktayānantyādi svīkriyate | evaṃ tattadavyāpyāntarbhāveṇāpi śaktirabhyupeyate, viśiṣṭavyāpakatve eva nirupakatvasaṃbandhena prakṛtyarthasyānvayo na tu vyāpakatāghaṭakamāsatvādau | ato na māsatvavato māsatve ādheyatvenānvaye nirākāṅkṣatayānanvayaprasaktiriti dūṣaṇāvakāśaḥ | ata eva khaṇḍaśaḥ śaktisvīkāre vibhinnapadajanyopasthiterhetubhūtāyā abhāvānanvayabodhasaṃbhava iti dūṣaṇasyāpyanavakāśaḥ | kiṃ ca saṃbandhatvenaiva bodhasyānubhavasiddhatayā na śaktayānantyaprasaktirapīti | anye tu svaniṣṭhabhedapratiyogitāvacchedakaniṣṭha- pratiyogitākatvaviśiṣṭādheyatāsaṃbandhena prakṛtyarthasya vyāpakatāghaṭakābhāve'nvayo- pagamena sāmañjasyānna pratiyogivaiyādhikaraṇyasya vibhattayarthe'ntarbhāvaḥ | na vā vyāpyasyāpyantarbhāva iti na gauravamiti digityāhuḥ | māsatvaṃ ca tanmāsabhedena
bhinnamiti yatkicidekamāsamātradhikaraṇakādhyayanaśālina na tādṛśaprayogānupa- pattiriti spaṣṭaṃ vyutpattivādādau | 'divase svapitī'tyādau divasapadasya sūryodayāvadhisūryodayaryantakṣaṇasamūhaparatayā naikadaṇḍādimātrasvāpe tathāprayogaḥ | 'yojanaṃ parvata' ityatra dravyeṇa krośaṃ kuṭilā nadī krośaṃ ramaṇīyetyatra kauṭilyādiguṇena, māsaṃ bhojanābhāva ityatrābhāvenātyantasaṃbandhād yojanādipadottaraṃ dvitīyā | nanu 'māsamāste' godohamāste, māsa āsyate, godoha āsyata ityādāviva māsamadhīte ityatrāpi dhātorvyāparyantaṃ vṛttisvīkāreṇaiva 'karturīpsita 'iti karmatvena dvitīyāsiddhau 'kālādhvanoḥ 'iti sutraṃ vyartham | tathā coktaṃ vākyapadīye 
'kālabhāvādhvadeśānāmāntarbhūtakriyāntaraiḥ |
sarvairakarmakairyoge karmatvamupajāyate ||' iti |
tatrākarmakairityuktāvapi nyāyasāmyena sakarmakadhātorapi tadarthāntarbhāveṇa vṛttisvīkāre bādhakābhāvaḥ | ata evaitatsūtrasya vaiyarthyamuktābhiprāyeṇa 'prākṛtamevaitat krarme'ti prakṛtasūtrasthabhāṣyeṇāśaṅkitam | akarmake evoktarīterabhyupagame māsamadhīte ityādiprayoganirvāhārtha sūtrāvaśyakatve śaṅkā asaṃgatā syāt | na ca tādṛśaśaṅkāparabhāṣyāttādṛśaprayogo neṣyata eveti vācyam | 'apavarge tṛtīyā' iti sūtre anyathā māsamāste māsamadhīte iti dvividhaprayoganirvāhakasyāsya sūtrasya vaiyarthyaśaṅkānupapatteḥ | etatsūtre āsyate māsaḥ śayyate krośa ityakarmakadhātughaṭitaprayoga evāsti | na tu māsamadhīte ityādiḥ sakarmakadhātughaṭitaḥ prayogo yena tatrāpi vyāpanāntarbhāva āstheyaḥ | mānamadhīto'nuvāko na cānena gṛhīta iti prayogāt tādṛśaprayogāṇāmapīṣṭatvāt | ata eva māsamodanaṃ pacatīti prayogo'pi vyāpanakarmatvavivakṣayāpi bhavati | kālādikarmaṇo bahīraṅgatayā na tatra lādirapi tvantaraṅge dravyakarmaṇyedheti spaṣṭaṃ bhāṣyapradīpoddayote | anayaiva rītyātyantasaṃyoge karmavallādyarthamiti vacanamapi na kartavyamiti bhāṣye uktam | akarmakadhātubhiriti vacanaṃ tu bhāṣye nāstyeva | 'akathitaṃ ca' iti sūtrabhāṣye 
'kālabhāvādhvagantavyāḥ karmasaṃjñā hyakarmaṇām |'
ityapi siddhārthakathanaṃ na tvapūrva, hiśabdaprayogāditi spaṣṭaṃ śakharādau | tatra kālādikarma pradhānaṃ karma, dryamapradhānaṃ karma, kālāderādhāratvena dravyadvāraiva
kriyāsaṃbandhāt | tasmāt sūtraṃ vyarthamiti cenna | yatrākriyayā saṃyogastadarthamidaṃ vaktavyamiti bhāṣye eva samādhānāt | kathaṃ tarhi māsamadhīte ityudāharaṇamiti cet, saṃbandhasāmānyavivakṣāyāṃ ṣaṣṭhībādhanārthatvena kriyāyogārthamapyasyāvaśyakatvena yatra kriyākārakabhāvena nānvayavivakṣā tatrātyantasaṃyogārthamiti tadbhāṣya- vyākhyānasyocitatvamityāśayāt | yadyapi krośaṃ kuṭilā nadītyatrāstīi kriyādhyāhāraṃ vinā prathamāyāḥ sādhutvānirvāhāttadadhyāhārāvaśyakatā tathāpi kauṭilyādereva krośavyārakatvavivakṣāyāṃ dvitīyāsiddhayarthamidamiti na bhāṣyādigranthāsaṃgatiḥ | tatra kauṭilyādirūpe padārthaikadeśe'nvayo vyutpatti- vaicitryāt, tadviśiṣṭadharmiṇi vā, anye tu sarvatra tattaddhātorvyāptiparyantaṃ vṛttisvīkṛtya krośaṃ kuṭilā nadītyādāvapi dvitīyopapādane'pi māsi māsamāsitetyatra kṛdyogalakṣaṇā ṣaṣṭhyeva syānna dvitīyeti tadarthamida- māvaśyakabhityāhuḥ | iti dvitīyārthanirūpaṇam ||
dhūmena vahnimanuminoti, atra karaṇe tṛtīyā | dhūmādijñānasyānumiti- karaṇatāpakṣe dhūmādiśabdaḥ svajñāparaḥ | jñāyamānasya karaṇatāmate tu mukhyārthaka eva | vyāptiviśiṣṭavaiśiṣṭyāvagāhiparāmarśasya vyāpāratvād vyāptiviśiṣṭa- dhūmādijñānasya karaṇatānirvāhaḥ | anvayitāvacchedakavahnividheyakānumititvāva- cchedena dhūmādijñānakaraṇakatvānvayopagamādyatra vahnyanumitau na prayogaḥ | na vā dhūmena ghaṭamanuminotīti prayogaḥ | nanu tārkikanaye idaṃ saṃbhavati na śābdakanaye, tathā hi tannaye vidheyatā vidheyitādadvitīyārthaḥ | vahyanvitāyāstasyā nirūpakatvādisaṃbandhenānumitāvanvayaḥ | tādṛśavidheyatādiviśaṣṭāyāmanumitau dhūmajñānakaraṇakatvānvayādvahnividheyakatvādelanvayitāvacchedakatvasaṃbhavaḥ | śābdi- kamate tvanumityanukūlavyāpāro dhātvarthaḥ | nidheyatāsaṃbandhaḥ phalāvacchedakaḥ | itthaṃ ca vahnividheyakānumititvasyānvayitāvacchedakatvābhāvaḥ spaṣṭa eva | tathā ca pūrvāktātiprasaṅgo durvāra evaitanmata iti cenna, vidheyatādisthānīyavidheyatā- saṃbandhenānumitisaṃbaddhavahnyavacchedena dhūmādijñānakaraṇakatvānvayopagamenoktāti- prasaṅgāsaṃbhavāt | nanu tathāpi yatra dhūmaliṅgakavahnyanumitau ghaṭādijñānena dravyatvādyavagāhitvaṃ tatra ghaṭena na vahnimanuminotīti nañghaṭitaprayogānupapattiḥ | uktasamūhālambanavahnyanumatau ghaṭajñānakaraṇakatvābhāvabādhena tatprayuktaghaṭajñāna- karaṇatvaviśiṣṭoktānumitivirahabodhanāsaṃbhavāt | dhūmenedaṃ vahneranumānaṃ na ghaṭeneti
prayogasya tārkikamate'pyanupapattiḥ | pūrvoktasthale prathamāntārthe ghaṭajñānakaraṇa- kavahnyanumiterghaṭe vahnivyāpyatvabhramadāśāyāṃ prasiddhāyā abhāvabodhanasaṃbhave'pyatra tadasaṃbhavāditi cenna, uktasamūhālambanātmakānumitisattve dhūmakaraṇakaṃ vahneridamanumānaṃ na ghaṭakaraṇakamiti prayogasya saraṇeratra pradarśanīyatvād, yathā tādṛśaprayogo neṣyate tathāgatyā pūrvoktaprayogadvayamapi neṣyata ityāhuḥ | anye tu dhūmādipadaṃ jñānakaraṇatāmate'pi mukhyārthakameva 'hetau' iti sūtreṇa tṛtīyāsaṃbhavāt prayojakatvarūpaṃ hetutvaṃ janakatāvacchedakasādhāraṇamiti viṣayitayānumitajanaka- tāvacchedakadhūmādāvi tadakṣatam | prayojakatvaṃ janyatāvacchedakatārūpaṃ prayojyatvaṃ vā tṛtīyārthaḥ | tasya vahnimityādivibhattayantārthavahnividheyakatve nirūpakatādi- saṃbandhenānvayābhyupagamānnātaḥ ghaṭena vahnimityādiprayogāpattiḥ | vahnividheyakatve ghaṭaniṣṭhaprayojakatānirūpakatādyabhāvāt | tatra ghaṭaprayojyatvābhāvasyābādhita- tvānna ghaṭenetyādiprayogopapattiśca | dhūmena vahnimityādiprayogopapattihni- vidheyitāyā dhūmaniṣṭaprayojakatā nirūpakatvāt prayojyatvācca | śābdikamate vibhaktau lakṣaṇāviraheṇa vitheyitāyā vibhaktyarthatāvirahānnañdhaṭitoktaprayoga- nirvāhāyāgatyānupūrvakaminotereva vahnividheyatākānumitilākṣaṇikatvaṃ vahni- mityādivibhaktyantasya tātparyagrāhakatvaṃ cāṅgīkaraṇīyam | evaṃ ca nātrokta- samūhālambanānumititātparyeṇa ghaṭena vahnimanuminotīti prayogāpattiḥ | na ghaṭena vahnimanuminotīti prayogastu dhātvarthaikadeśavahnividheyakatvādau tṛtīyārthanvayā- bhyupagame eva saṃpādyavahnividheyakatvādau ghaṭādijñānaprayojyatvābhāvaprayukta- viśiṣṭānumityabhāvabodhanasaṃbhavena saṃpādya ityāhuḥ | 'putreṇa sahāgataḥ pite'tyatra 'sahayukte 'pradhāne' iti tṛtīyā | sahaśabdārthaḥ sāhityam | tacca samabhivyāhṛtapadosthāpyakriyādisamānakālikakriyādyanvayitvam | tadanvayitvaṃ ca samabhivyāhṛtakriyādibodhakapadena sāhityānvayinikriyāyā yādṛśaḥ saṃbandhaḥ pratīyate tādṛśaḥ saṃbandha eva | putreṇetyādau kartṛtvarūpaḥ saḥ | 'payasā sahaudanaṃ bhuṅke' 'payasā sahaudano bhūjyata' ityādau karmatvam | khaḍgena saha bāṇena hata ityatra karaṇatvam | śiṣyeṇa saha gurave dakṣiṇā dīyata ityatra saṃpradānatvam | prayāgeṇa saha kāśyāṃ devāḥ santītyatrādhikaraṇatvam | putreṇa saha sthūla ityādāvādhāratvādiḥ saṃbandhaḥ, putreṇetyādau tṛtīyārthe'pi kartṛtvādikameva | ādheyatāsaṃbandhena|putrānvitakartṛtvasyāgamānakālikāgamanakartatvarūpasahārthaghaṭakī-
bhūtāgamane nirūpakatāsaṃbandhenānvayaḥ | sahārthakartṛtvasya pitaryākṣayatayānvayaḥ | tadviśiṣṭakṛtpratyayāntārthasyāgamanakartṛrabhedenānvayaḥ | na coddeśyatāvacchedakavidheya- yoraikyānnirākāṅkṣatvamiti vācyam | karmakartṛtvādeḥ saṃbandhatvenaiva sahārthe'nta- rbhāveṇaikarūpoddeśyatāvacchedakavidheyayoraikye eva nirākāṅkṣatayā prakṛte tadabhāvāt | yadvā saṃbandhisamānakālikapadārthasaṃbandhitvameva sahaśabdārthaḥ | saṃbandhaśca kartṛtvādireva saṃbandhatvena pūrvavadantarbhāvyaḥ putrapadottaratṛtīyārthaḥ | kartṛtvaṃ sahaśabdārthānvayabalāt pratīyamānāgamanakriyānirūpitam | ata eva śiṣyeṇa sahāgata ityādau kartṛtṛtīyayaiva siddhamiti bhāṣyasya nāsaṃgatiḥ | pratīyamānakriyāsaṃbandhasyāpi kārakavibhaktiprayojakatvasya klaptatvāt putrasaṃbandhitādṛśapadārthasaṃbandhīyateti bodhe vṛtte āgamanasya putrasaṃbandhaṃ vinā pratītasya putrasya saṃbandhitādṛśapadārthasaṃbandhitvasyānupapattyā putre'pi na tatsaṃbandhasya manasā pratyāyyatvāt putrapadārthasya tṛtīyābodhitasvaniṣṭhakartṛtvādi- nirūpakatvasaṃbandhe sahārthaghaṭakasaṃbandhinyanvayaḥ | payasā sahaudanaṃ bhuṅke ityatra pratīyamānakriyānirūpitakarmatṛtīyāghaṭitasaṃbandhena putrasya saṃbandhinyanvayaḥ | athavā tādṛśapadārthānvayitvasamaniyatamakhaṇḍapadārthāntararūpaṃ sāhityam | śeṣaṃ pūrvavat | atra kalpe addeśyatāvacchedakavidheyayoraikyaśaṅkāpi na | tādṛśasāhitya- syaivoddeśyatāvacchedakatvāt | tathā ca putrasāhityavān pitāgamanakartṛbhinna ityādibodhaḥ | etanmate prakṛtyarthasya sāhitye nirūpitatvasaṃbandhenānvayaḥ | 'saputra' iti samāsaghaṭakasahaśabdasya tu dharmiparatvam | ata eva tasya pitrādipadena samānādhikaraṇyopapattiḥ | ata eva ca 'tena saha' iti bahuvrīhividhānaṃ caritārtham | sāhityavato'nyapadārthatvābhāvena anekam 'iti sūtrasyāprāpteḥ | sāhityamātrārthakatvāṅgīkāreṇa tu tatsūtrakhaṇḍanaṃ bhāṣyakṛtām | adhavā sahārtha sāhityam | tacca samabhivyāhṛtapadopasthāpitāgamanādipadasamāna- kālikapadatvādikameva, lāghavāt | ādipadena samānadeśatvaṃ tatsamaniyatama- khaṇḍopādhirūpaṃ vā sāhityaṃ tadarthaḥ | tasya cāgatādipadopasdhāpyāgamanādāvanvayaḥ | tṛtīyārthaśca kriyāsamabhivyāhāre kriyāyā yādṛśasaṃbandhaḥ pradhāne pratīyate sa eva, kartṛtvādiḥ sahārthānvayabalāt pratīyamānatattatkriyānirūpito dravyādisamabhivyāhāre sahārthanvayabalāt pratīyamānatattatpadārthanirūpitaḥ svasvāmibhāvādireva saṃbandhaḥ | tasya ca dhātvādyarthāgamanādāvanvayaḥ |
putreṇa saha sthūla ityādau sthūlapadārthaikadeśe'pisthaulyādau vyutpattivaicitryeṇa sahārthasthaulyasamāna- kālikatvasyānvayaḥ | tathāca putreṇa sahāmataḥ pitā, putreṇa saha piturgauḥ, putreṇa saha sthūla ityādau putrakartṛkāgamanasamānakālikāgamanāśrayaḥ, putrasvāmikatvasamānakālikapitṛsvāmikatvavatī gauḥ, putravṛttisthaulyasamānakālikasthaivyavānityādikrimeṇa bodhaḥ | saputra ityādau tu samudāyārthatādṛśakriyāderantarbhāvānnāsāmarthyam | vyapekṣāvāde samāsadhaṭakasahaśabdasyāgamanasamāna- kālikāgamanavān lakṣaṇayārthaḥ putrapadasya putrakartṛkatvamarthastasya cāgamane'bhedenānvayaḥ | putrasya svaniṣṭhakartṛtānirūpakatvena vā sahārthāgamane'nvayaḥ | putreṇa saha sthūla ityatra putrapadasya putrādheyatvavānarthaḥ | sahapadasya sthaulyasamānakālikasthaulyavān | pūrvapadārthasyottarapadārthe- 'ntargatasthaulye'bhadaḥ saṃsargaḥ, putrasyādheyatvena cānvayaḥ | evamanyatrāpi bodhyam | agatyā vyastasamāsavākyayorasamānākārabodhajanakatvaṃ saputra āgataḥ saputraḥ sthūla ityādau riyāyāḥ pitrādau dvedhāsaṃbandhabhānaṃ copagamyate | putreṇa sahāgataḥ patetyatra vyartha tasya dvedhābhānamityabhiprāyeṇottarapakṣo'ṅgīkṛto bṛddhaiḥ | vakṣyamāṇarītyā nañghaṭutavākye prakṛtadhātūpasthitakriyāyāmeva putrakartṛkāgamanādisamānakālikatvabhānasyāvaśyakatvācca | yatra kṣetre māṣavāpottaraṃ tilā upyante tatra tilaiḥ saha māṣānvaya iti bhāvaḥ | tatra samabhivyāhṛtapadārthasamānadeśatvaṃ tadarthaḥ | atarat pūrvavat | nanu kartṛtṛtīyayaiva gatārthatā sūtrasya | na ca putraṇa sahagamanaṃ devadattasyetyatra kṛdyogalakṣaṇaṣaṣṭhībādhanārtha taditi vācyam | itarasamabhivyāhāraṃ vināpi pratīyamāne kṛtpratyayārthakartrādereva tatra grahaṇāt | ktenāvidhānānna kartṛtṛtīyāprāptirityapi na etādṛśakartureva ktenābhidhānāditi cenna | uktarītyā putraṇa saha sthūla ityādyartha tasyāvaśyakatvāt | idaṃ tu bodhyam | pūrvamate putraṇa saha nāgacchatītyādau nañsamabhivyāhāre samabhivyāhṛtakriyādāveva sahaśahdārthasyāgamanādisamānakālikatvasyānvaya upagantavyaḥ | anyathā putrānāgamanasthale putrasāhityasya pitari bādhe'pi putrasāhityavat pitṛkartṛkāgamanābhāvasyāvādhena putreṇa saha nāgacchati pitetyādiprayogasyopapattāvapi putreṇa saha nāgataḥ pitā, putreṇa saha na sthūla ityāderanupapattiḥ | tatra putrasāhityavati pitari āgamanakartṛbhadasya sthūlabhedasya ca bodho vaktavyaḥ | sa ca tatra putrasāhityasyoktabhedasya ca bodhānna saṃbhavati | samabhivyāhṛtakriyādau putrakartṛkasamānakālikatvasyānvayābyupagame tu uktasthale putrakartṛkāgamanasamānakālikāgamanakartṛbhedasya putraniṣṭhasthaulyasamānakālikasthaulyavadbhedasya ca pitarthabādhenoktaprayoganirvāha iti samabhivyāhṛtaśabdopasthitisahakāreṇa tadarthakriyādyanvayitvābhāvo- 'trāprādhānyam | sa ca viśeṣaṇābhāvakṛto viśiṣṭābhāvaḥ putrādau vartate | 'sahaiva daśabhiḥ putrairbhāraṃ vahati gardabhī'tyādau vidyamānatāvācisahaśabdayoge kriyādyanyarupaviśeṣyābhāvakṛto'pi viśiṣṭābhāvaḥ | tādṛśasahaśabdayoge'pi tṛtīyā bhavati, vinigamanāvirahāt tādṛśasaṃkhyākaputrāṇāṃ vidyamānatve'pītyartha iti vadanti | teṣāmayamāśayaḥ sahaśabdo luptasaptamyantaḥ | sā ca satisaptamī | apiśabdādhyāhāraśca | tanoktārthalābha iti | anye tu vidyamānetyādyadhyāhāraḥ, evaṃ ca vidyatikriyāprayuktasāhityanirvāha iti pratyapīpadan | nanu 'prāsoṣṭa śatrughnamudāraceṣṭamekā sumivā saha lakṣmaṇena' ityādau śatrughnalakṣmaṇaprasavayoḥ samānakālatvaviraheṇa kriyādvayasamānakālikatvasya sahaśabdārthatvāsaṃbhava iti cenna | tatrāpi sthūlakālaghaṭitasamāna-
kālikatvasaṃbhavāt | evaṃ 
'śiśormātari gabhiṇyāṃ cūḍākarma na kārayet |
sahopanītyā kuryāñcettadā doṣo na vidyate ||' 
ityādi smṛtivākyeṣvapi bodhyam | tatra dinasya sahārthe'ntarbhāvaḥ | anyathā cūḍākarmopanayanayoḥ pūrvoparībhāvena ghaṭikādirūpaikakālatvābhāvenāsaṃgatyāpatteḥ | nanvevaṃ putrāgamanapūrvakāle āgantari āgato'yaṃ na putreṇa sahetyasyānāpattiḥ | tatrāpi divasādirūpasthūlakālamādaya samānakālikatva- sattvāditi cenna | tatra kṣaṇatvādinaiva kālasya saha śabdārthe'ntarbhāvanīyatvādityanyatra vistaraḥ |
daṇḍena ghaṭaḥ payaḥpānena sthūlaḥ puṇyena dṛṣṭo hari,-atra 'hetau' iti sūtreṇa tṛtīyā, na tu 'kartṛkaraṇayoḥ' iti karaṇe | daṇḍasya vyāpāravattve'pi kriyājanakatvābhāvena karaṇatvābhāvāt, vyā- pāravattve sati kriyājanakasyaiva karaṇatvāt | nanu astītyadhyāhārasyāvaśyakatayā kriyājanakatvamakṣatameveti cenna | kriyājanakatvābāvādityasya tajjanakatvavivakṣaṇābhāvādityartha- katvāt | daṇḍābhāve'pi dhaṭasattāyāḥ sattvena daṇḍasya tadajanakatvācca | payaḥpālasya śukavṛddhisaṃpādanarūpavyāpārasattve'pi sthaulyātmakaguṇajanakatvaṃ na kriyājanakatvam | puṇyasya paramāpūrvarūpādṛṣṭaviśeṣātmano darśanakriyājanakatve'pi vyāpārābhāvaḥ | puṇyapadasya dānādiparatve tu karaṇatṛtīyaiva | dravyaguṇakriyānyatamajanakasavyāpāranirvyāpārāntaravṛttidharmaviśeṣo hetutvam | tadaktaṃ vākyapadīye-
'dravyādiviṣayohetuḥ karaṇaṃ niyatakriyam |
anāśrite tu vyāpāre nimittaṃ heturiṣyate ||' iti |
tathā ca hetutvakaraṇatvayorbhedāttena rūpeṇa bodhanirvāhārthaṃ sūtradvayamāvaśyakamiti bruvanti sma vṛddhāḥ |
anye tu karaṇe tṛtīyānuśāsanavirahe śaraiḥ śātitapatra ityādau vṛttighaṭakaikadeśabhūtaśātanakriyāyāḥ śarahetutvānvaye sāpekṣatvenāsāmarthye samāsasya sādhutvānupapattiḥ| karaṇatṛtīyāyāstu kārakavibhaktitayā 
'pratiyogipadādanyadyadanyat kārakādapi |
vṛttiśabdekadeśena na tasyānvaya iṣyate ||'
-iti vyutpattau kārakaparyudāsena karaṇavibhakteḥ kārakavibhaktitvena karaṇatvasāpekṣatve'pinoktānupa- pattiḥ |
evaṃ dhanavatā ṛṇena baddha ityatra ṛṇapadāttṛtīyānupapattiḥ | hetutṛtīyāyā 'akartaryṛṇe 'iti sūtrānuśiṣṭapaḍhcamyāpavādabhūtayā bādhitāt | ataḥ karaṇe tṛtīyānuśāsanamāvaśyakameva, tatsattve tu karaṇatvavikṣāyāṃ tṛtīyā sidhyatītyāhuḥ | etanmate'pi vibhinnaprakārakabodhakatvaṃ tayoriti paryavasannameveti bodhyam | nanu yatraikaiva pākakriyā caitreṇa kāṣṭhairmaitreṇa tupapakṣepeṇa niṣpādyate tatra caitrastupairna pacati ki tu dārubhireveti prayogānupapattiḥ | tasyāṃ pākakriyāyāṃ caitrakartṛkatvasya tuṣahetukatvasya tatkaraṇasya ca sattvena tadviśiṣṭakriyābhāvabādhānnikhilaviśeṣeṇaviśiṣṭakriyāyā evābhāvasya nañā bodhanīyatvāt | nyāyamate tu naiṣa doṣaḥ | tuṣahetukatvasyoktapāke sattvena tadabhāvabodhe'pi samabhivyāhṛtakartṛvyāpārādhīnavyāpāradhaṭitakaraṇatvasya kartari vyāpāravattvasya sattvenātiprasaṅgavāraṇārtha vivakṣaṇīyasya tuṣeṣvasattvena tatkaraṇakatvābhāvasya tasmin pāke'bādhitasya
bodhanasaṃbhavāt | kṛtirūpakartṛvyāpārādhīnavyāpārāśraye kartaryatiprasaṅgavāraṇāya karti svabhedo niveśanīya iti cenna | kartṛbhedena pākakriyāyā api bhedena caitrakartṛkapāke tuṣakāraṇakatvābhāvabādhena tuṣakaraṇakacaitrakartṛkavartamānapākābhāvasya tatrākṣatatvenādoṣāt | tathā ca caitraḥ kāṣṭhaiḥ paci maitrastupaiḥ pacatīti vākyabhedastatreṣṭa evetyāhuḥ | 'adhyayanena vasati' atrādhyayanasya vāsaphalasyāpi svajñānadvārā vāsanimittatayā hetutvena vivakṣāyāṃ tṛtīyā | adhyayanābhinnahetuka ekakartṛo vāsa iti bodhaḥ | spaṣṭaṃ cedaṃ 'pratyayaḥ' 'tadartha vikṛteḥ prakṛtau' ityādisūtreṣu kaiyaṭe | nanvevaṃ kāśyāderapi vāsahetutayāpi hetutvavivakṣāyāṃ tṛtīyāprasaktirita cenna, adhikaraṇatvavivakṣāyāṃ parayā saptamyā bādhanāt, tadavivakṣāyāṃ tu karaṇatvavivakṣāyāmiveṣṭāpatteḥ | apare tu iha sūtre hetuśabdena phalamapi gṛhyate'ta evādhyayanarūpaphalajanako vāsa ityanubhavasiddhabodhanirvāhaḥ | na ca tatra lakṣaṇeti vācyam | vibhaktau lakṣaṇānaṅgīkārāt | tatra phalatvena phalaṃ vibhaktyarthaḥ | abhedena tatra prakṛtyarthasya tasya ca vāse janakatāsaṃvadhena viśeṣaṇatvam | etena hetutvaṃ jñānādvārakameva gṛhyate | ata eva dhūmādipadasya tajjñāne lakṣaṇāṅgīkriyate tāntrikaiḥ | evaṃ ca kathamatra tṛtīyeti deśyaṃ parāstamityāhuḥ | jñānadvārakanimittatvānāśrayaṇe'pi phasalya hetuśabdenāparigrahe'pi 'hetau' iti sūtreṇādhyayanapadāddhetutvāthikā hetvārthikā vā tṛtīyā sidhyati | hetutve tadarthe adhyayanasya nirūpitatvaṃ saṃbandhaḥ | vāse tasyāśrayatvaṃ, heturūpatadartheṣu svanirūpitahetutvaṃ saṃbandhaḥ | adhyayananiṣṭhahetutvanirūpito vāsa ityādyokārako bodha iti tu na śaṅkyam | hetutvādyarthe pratyāsattyā tadāśrayārthakād vibhaktirityarthyaiva lābhāvat, ata eva 'kartṛkaraṇayostṛtīyā' iti sūtravihitā tṛtīyā na kadācidapi kriyārthakādbhavi, api tu kartrādyarthakādeva | anyathā devadattena bhuktamityādau bhūktādipadādapi tṛtīyā syāt | na ca karmaktāntaraya 'sattvapradhānāni nāmāni' iti niruktāt karmapradhānakatayā pradhānārthenaiva pratyayārthānvayasya klṛptatvāttannirūpitakartṛkatvāsaṃbhavānna tatastṛtīyāpattiriti vācyam | evamapi bhuktikartā devadatta ityarthavivakṣāyāṃ bhuktipadāt 'kartṛkarmaṇoḥ kṛti' iti ṣaṣṭhīprasaktya bhukterdevadatta ityādiprayogāt durvāratvāt, devadattadasya bhuktirityasyai- veṣṭatvāt | na ca paratvāt prathamā bhaviṣyatīti noktāpattiriti vācyam | ṣaṣṭhyā antaraṅgatvāt prathamāyā evāpavādatvamityasya vaktuṃ śakyatvā | tādarthyavivakṣāyāmadhyayanāya vasatīti caturthyapi bhavati | upakāratvaṃ caturthyarthaḥ | tatra prakṛtyarthasya nirūpitatvaṃ saṃsargaḥ | adhyayananirūpitopakārakatāvān vāsa iti bodhaḥ | upakāryopakārakatvaṃ vā caturthyarthaḥ | upakārye bhedasaṃbandhena prakṛtyartho'nvetīti vopagantavyam | 'dāsyā saṃyacchate kāmukaḥ' atra caturthyarthe tṛtīyā | aśiṣṭavyavahāre ityādivacanāt | atra dhāturdānapūrvakopabhogārthaḥ | saṃpradānatvaśakrimāṃstṛtīyārthaḥ | dāsīsaṃpradānakadānapūrvakopabhogo vākyārthaḥ | 'dāṇaśca sā cet ' iti sūtreṇātmanepadavidhāyakena caturthyarthe tṛtīyā jñāpye cedityanena tadanityatvaṃ jñāpyate ityaśiṣṭavyavahāra ityaṃśasiddhiḥ | aśiṣṭānāṃ- niṣiddhācāraṇāṃ vyavahāraḥ kriyārūpa iti spaṣṭaṃ tatsūtre eva bhāṣye iti śivam ||
patye śete atra 'kriyayā yamabhipraiti ' iti saṃpradānasaṃjñā | saṃpradānatvaśaktimān vibhattayarthaḥ | patisaṃpradānakaṃ śayanamiti bodhaḥ | bhāṣyakāramate tu kriyāi kṛtrimaṃ karma saṃdarśana- prārthanavyavasāyajanyarambhakriyānirūpitaṃ tasyāḥ karturīpsitamiti karmatvam | evaṃ ca 'karmaṇā
yamabhi ' iti sūtreṇaiva saṃpradānasaṃjñāpattirityāhuḥ |
'saṃdarśanaṃ prārthanāyāṃ vyavasāye tvanantara |
vyavasāyastathārambhe sādhanatvāya kalpate ||
pūrvasmin yā kriyā saiva parasmin sādhanaṃ matā ||'
iti | saṃdarśanaṃ phalaviṣayakasaṃkalpaḥ, sa ca jñānamicchā vā | tat prārthanāyaṃ phalopāyātmikāyāṃ hetuḥ | vyavasāyo yārapākādervyāpārasya phalaviśeṣasādhanatvena viścayaḥ | tatrānantarā prārthanā hetuḥ | abhilāṣe sati tādṛṅniścayodayāt | vyavasāyaścārambhe pravṛttyāsmina hetustenāpyamānā prakṛtakriyā karmatvabhāgbhavati | pūrvasmin - pūrvakāle | parasmin - uttarakālabhāvini | etanmate ratisaṃpradānakarmārambhakarmarūpaṃ śayanamiti bodhaḥ | abhidānābhāvānna ghaṭaṃ karotītyādavatiprasaṅga ityāhuḥ | 'paśunā rudraṃ yajate'-atra phalāśaye karaṇatvaśakriruddeśye karmatvaśaktiḥ 'karmaṇaḥ karaṇasaṃjñā saṃpradānasya ca karmasaṃjñā' iti vacanāt | paśukaraṇako rudrakarmako yāga iti śābdo'rthaḥ | rudroddeśyakaḥ paśuniṣṭhasvatvanivṛttyanukūlo vyāpāra iti tvārthaḥ | ayamevānyamate śābdaḥ, evamevānyatrāpi bodhyam | haraye rocate bhaktiḥ prītyanukūlastatsamānādhikaraṇo vyāpāro rocaterarthaḥ | prītirantaḥ karaṇavṛttiviśeṣaḥ | sa ca saviṣayako madīyatvādiprakārakajñānarūpo 'yamiṣṭabhāgbhavatvityākārecchārūpo vā | hariniṣṭhaprītenityatve'pi viṣayasyānityatayā phalatāvacchedakaviṣayatāyā anityatvamitisaṃbandhena prītirjanyatvāropeṇa tadanukūlatvanirvāhaḥ | āropānaṅgīkartṛmate tu nirdhāyakatāsaṃbandhena tatphalaviśiṣṭajñānādikameva prītipadārthaḥ | evaṃ ca śikhī dhvasta itivadviśeṣaṇaphalamādāyānukūlatānirūpakatvanirvāhaḥ | viṣayatāyāḥ phalatāvacchedakatvādeva na rocatiḥ sakarmakaḥ | prīyamāṇa ityatra prīñ tarpaṇa ityasmāt karmaṇi laṭaḥ śānaca, na tu prīṅ prītāviti daivādikāt | tasyākarmakatvāt | ata eva prīyatāṃ bhagavānīśaḥ | evaṃ sati prīdhātvarthavyāpārāśrayasya saṃjñeti sūtrārtho'bhyupetavyaḥ | tathā satyapi prītisamānādhikaraṇatadanukūlavyāpārāśrayatvasya haryādau sattvena prakṛte saṃjñāsiddhestādṛśavyāpārā- nāśraye'tiprasaṅgābhāvācceti vācyam | prīdhātvarthakarmaṇaḥ saṃjñeti sakalagranthavirodhāt | jñīpsyamāna iti sāhacaryeṇa karmaṇyeva śānaca ucitatvācca | prīdhātvarthaphalatāvacchedakasaṃbandhastu samavāyaḥ | ata eva hari prīṇayatītyādiprayogāsiddhiḥ | samavāyasaṃbandhena prītyāśrayasya 'rucyarthānām-' iti sūtreṇa saṃpradānatvam | prīdhāto rucyarthatvābhāvānna tadyogesaṃpradānatvam | ata eva prīyamāṇa iti nirdeśopapattiḥ | etatsūtrābhāve hariśabdādeḥ ṣaṣṭhī prāpteti śaṣaṣaṣṭhyapavādatvaṃ caturthyāḥ | anye tu rucidhātuḥ prīdhātusamānārthaḥ | evaṃ ca karmasaṃjñāpavādikeyaṃ saṃjñā, prīyamāṇa iti nirdeśādeva prīdhātūpāttaprītyāśrayasya neyaṃ saṃjñeti vadanti | tanmate gauravam | prīdhātuyoge saṃjñāvāraṇārtha nirdeśāśrayaṇāt | abhilaṣyateśca prītivyadhikaraṇaprītyanukūlavyāpāro'rthaḥ | phalatāvacchedakasaṃbandho viṣayatā | ata eva na sa rucyartha iti haribhaktimabhilaṣyatītyatra harerna saṃpradānatvaṃ yena sukharūpaphalāśrayasya devadattādeḥ karmasaṃjñāyāṃ prāptāyāmiyaṃ saṃjñā tadapavādabhūtetyāhuḥ |
atrāpi mate pūrvoktaṃ gauravam | anye tu abhilāṣātmakaprīti rocaterarthaḥ, viṣayatvarūpaṃ kartṛtvamākhyātārtho lakṣaṇayā ṣaṣṭhyapavādikā caturthīti nāradasaṃbandhiruciviṣayatāvān kalaha iti nāradāya rocate kalaha iti vākyajanyabodha ityāhuḥ | apare tu tārkikāḥ 
prītijankatāprakṛtadhātvarthajanakatātmakadhātvarthavyāpāraśrayatayā kalahādeḥ kartṛtā āśrayatvamākhyātārtha āśritatvarūpaḥ saṃbandhaścaturthyartha ityāhuḥ | etanmate'pi ṣaṣṭhyapavādikā caturthī prīdhātustu naitadarthaka iti na pūrvoktādiprasaṅgaḥ | 'gopī smarāt kṛṣṇāya ślāghate hnute tiṣṭhate śapate vā' atra 'ślāghahnuṅsthāśapām-' iti sūtreṇa jñīpsyamānasya saṃpradānasaṃjñā | jñīpsyamānapadena ṇyantakarmaṇaḥ prakṛtyarthakarmaṇo vā grahaṇam | ṇyarthasya prādhānyāt tatkarmaṇo grahaṇam | sansvārasyāt prakṛtyarthakarmaṇaḥ | anyathā jñāpyamāna ityeva vadet, prakṛtyarthakarmaṇo'pi sannarthecchāviṣayavyāpāraprayojyaphalāśrayatvena sannantakarmatayā tatra lakārotpattiraviruddheti tādṛśanardeśopapattiḥ | ślādhyādeḥ stutyā prītipūrvako bodhanārthaḥ | stutyā kṛṣṇamātmānurāgaṃ bodhayati | evaṃ hnutyādīnā taṃ kṛṣṇaṃ bodhyatītyarthaḥ | hnutiritarādarśanayogyadeśasthāpanam | karmasaṃjñāpavādabhūteyaṃ saṃjñā, vādhiviṣayātmānugāgastu prakaraṇādigamyaḥ | kṛṣṇasaṃpradānaka ātmānurāgaviṣayakabodhānukūlaḥ smarahetukaḥ stutyādipūrvako vyāpāra iti śābdabodhaḥ | prakṛtyarthakarmaṇo grahaṇe stutyādinā kṛṣṇaṃ svasakhījanaṃ jñāpayatītyarthaḥ | devadattāya śataṃ dhārayatītyatra 'dhāraruttamarṇaḥ' iti sūtreṇottamarṇasya saṃpradānasaṃjñā | utkṛṣṭārthavṛtterudaratamap | dravyaprakarṣādām na | uttamamṛṇaṃ yasyeti bahuvrīhiḥ | ata eva nipātanānniṣṭhāntasya paranipātaḥ | uttamarṇo dhanatvāmī | dhṛṅ avasthāne divādiḥ | tasmāddhetumaṇṇici avasthityanukūlavyāpāro dhārayaterarthaḥ | śatasyāvasthitiradhamarṇe svakarmakādānajanyaduradṛṣṭaviśeṣasaṃbandhena | ata eva pariśodhanaparyantamadhamarṇe caitrādau saṃyogādyasattvadaśāyāṃ saṃyogānutpādādapi ca śataṃ dhriyate caitra iti prayoga upapadyate | evaṃ caitro devadattāya śataṃ dhārayatīti ca | na hi bhavati prayojyyāpāraśūnyatādaśāyāṃ pācayatīti prayogaḥ . spaṣṭaṃ cedaṃ 'hetumati ca' iti sūtre bhāṣye | tadanukūlo vyāpāra ṛṇāpariśodhanaṃ sajantīyadravyadānamaṅgīkṛtya paradattadravyagrahaṇaṃ vā | na ca grahaṇottarasamaye grahaṇasyāsattvādvārayatīti na syāditi vācyam | sūkṣmarūpeṇa svajanyādṛṣṭaviśeṣa- saṃbandhena pariśodhanaparyantaṃ tasya sattvāt | tasya vināśe'pi pravṛttāvirāmarūpavartamānatvavivakṣaṇādvā | atrottamarṇasya yadyapi dānakriyādvārā dhāraṇaprayojakatvaṃ tathāpi kriyāyā aśravaṇāddhetvādisaṃjñāyā aprāptyā śeṣaṣaṣṭhī prāptāḥ | sarvatra viśeṣarūpeṇāvivakṣaṇe kriyānimittatvamātreṇa vivakṣaṇe śeṣatvamiti bodhyam | taduktaṃ hariṇā 
'hetutve karmasaṃjñāyāṃ śeṣatve vāpi kārakam |
rucyarthādiṣu śāstroṇa saṃpradānākhyamucyate ||' iti |
sajātīyadravyādānābhyupagamapūrvakaṃ paradattaparakīyadravyādānaṃ tajjanyo'dhamarṇaniṣṭho'dṛṣṭaviśeṣo vā ṛṇam | ādīyamānadravye ṛṇavyavahārastannimittatvaprayukta aupacārikaḥ | ata eva gṛhītasyānnāderbhakṣaṇādikaraṇe'pi mama ṛṇamastīti vyavahāra upapadyate | tādṛśādānakarmaṇyeva mukhyastādṛśādṛṣṭa auhacāriko'stviti cenna | 'ṛṇamādhamarṇye' itisūtreṇādharamarṇaniṣṭhabhāvādāveva tasya nipātanāt, tatraiva śakterucitatvāt | amarakośe'pi 'satyānṛtaṃ tu vāṇijyaṃ syādṛṇaṃ paryudañcanam' ityuktam | sarvadhāpi mokṣasya na tathātvaṃ, tava tatra tādṛśaṃ duradṛṣṭamiti kathaṃ bhaktāya mokṣaṃ dhārayatītyudāharaṇamiti cedatrāhuḥ avaśyadeyatvena tatrāpi ṛṇatvavyahārānmokṣasthityanukūlo vyāpāro hariniṣṭhasevanakarmatvādirūpaḥ | sevanarūpopakārāśrayatayā bhakta uttamarṇaśabdavyavahāraviṣayaḥ |
vyutpattivādakṛtastu dravyāntaradānābhyupagamapūrvakaparadattadravyādānajanyo'dṛṣṭaviśeṣo dhārayaterarthaḥ | taddhaṭakadānānvayi kartṛtvaṃ caturthyarthaḥ | na ca ṛṇāpariśodhanameva duradṛṣṭajanakamupapātakamadhye tasyaiva parigaṇanāditi vācyam | ṛṇādānasya tajjanakatve'pi pariśodhanasya tannāśakatābodhanāyā- pariśodhanasya narakarūpaphalopadhānaprayojakatvābhiprāyeṇopapātakamadhye parigaṇanopapatteḥ | gṛhītarṇāpariśodhanapekṣayā lāghavena ṛṇādānasyaiva duradṛṣṭaṃ prati hetutvakalpanāyā ucitatvādityādi vistareṇāhuḥ | puṣpebhyaḥ spṛhayati atra svṛhayatiricchāmātravācī | īpsitapadena dhātvarthavyāpārajanyaphalāśrayatvena bodhanāt na tu phalasya dhātvarthatvāgrahaḥ | evaṃ ca ṣaṣṭhyapavāda etatsaṃjñāprayuktā caturthī | puṣpasaṃpradānikeccheti bodhaḥ | puṣpaviṣayiṇīti tvārthaḥ phalāvacchinnecchāvācī cāyaṃ dhātuḥ | icchānukūlamanaḥ saṃyogādirūpavyāpāravācī vā | icchāyāḥ phalaṃ viṣayatā, pravṛttirvā, tadyoge tadarthaphalāśrayatvena vivakṣāyāṃ paratvāt karmasaṃjñāyāṃ dvitīyetvādyanyatra vistaraḥ | haraye krudhyati druhyati īrṣyati asūyati vā |hananādyanukūlavyāpārajanyaḥ kaścidvṛttiviśeṣaḥ krodhaḥ krodherarthaḥ | druha jighāṃsāyāmityarthanirdeśād droho druheḥ | sa ca duḥkhajanakakriyāviśeṣajanakaścittavṛttiviśeṣaḥ | dhātvarthasaṃgṛhītakarmakatayaitāvakarmakau | ekadyoge ṣāṣṭhī prāptā | etaddhātvarthamūlabhūtakopasya viṣayatayā saṃbandhinaḥ 'krudhadruherṣyāsūyārthānām ' iti sūtreṇa saṃpradānasaṃjñā vidhīyate | harisaṃpradānakaḥ krodho droha iti bodhaḥ | utkarṣaviruddhadhārṇāropānukūlavyāpārajanakacittavṛttiviśeṣarūpākṣamerṣyāterarthaḥ | śaucācārādiguṇaviṣaye dambhādikṛtatvarūpadoṣāropānukūlaścittavṛttiviśeṣayo'sūyateḥ | cittavṛttiviśeṣātmakakopamūlakā ete | prarūḍhakopasyaiva krodhapadārthatayā tatrāpi kopamūlakatvamānapavādam | asūyādyarthakadhātuyoge mūlabhūtakopaviṣayatāviśiṣṭatattaddhātvarthaphalāśrayasya saṃpradānasaṃjñā, 'yaṃ prati kopaḥ' ityanenāi kopaviṣayasyetyeva labhyate | tathāpi prayogānurodhāt tādṛśaphalāśrayasyeti niveśitam | viśeṣaṇaprayojanaṃ tu bhāryāmīrṣyatītyatra bhāryāyāḥ saṃpradānatvavāraṇam | nātra bhāryāṃ prati kopo'pi tu parairdṛśyamānāyāstasyā asahanam | ekabhāryāyāṃ nerṣyatidhātvarthāropātmakaphalāśrayatvamityarthaḥ | harisaṃpradānakotkarṣavirodhidharmāropānukūlaścittavṛttiviśeṣa iti bodhaḥ | tārkikamate tu krodho dveṣaviśeṣo jñānaviśeṣo vā droho'pakāraviṣayecchāhiteccheti yāvat | īrṣyā parotkarṣagocara- dveṣātmākṣamā | guṇini doṣāviṣkāraṇarūpavyāpāraviśeṣā'sūyā | viṣayayitvādikaṃ catuthyartha iti bodhyam | hareḥ krodhadrohābhyāmicchāviṣayatvābhāvānna 'kriyayā yam' iti saṃjñāsiddhiḥ | mitrāya druhyati pratāpo'pītyādau cetanatvāropo bodhyaḥ | kupyati kasmaicidityādyasādhveva, 'kriyayā yamabhipraiti' iti saṃpradānatvaṃ vetyāhuḥ |
kṛṣṇāya rādhyati īkṣate vā | atra praśnaviṣayaśubhāśubhaparyālocanārthau rādhīkṣatī | tadarthapraśnaviṣayasaṃbandhino 'rādhīkṣyorthasya ' iti saṃpradānatvam | kṛṣṇasaṃpradānakaṃ śubhāśūbhaparyāvalocanamiti ccaturthī | tasmā idaṃ tadarthyā, tasya bhāvastādarthyamiti vyutpattyopakārakatvamupakāryopakārakabhāvo vā vibhaktyarthaḥ | ādye nirūpitatvaṃ prakṛtyarthasya nibhaktyarthe tasya ca dāruṇyāśrayatvaṃ saṃsargaḥ | antye prakṛtyarthasyādheyatvamupakāryatve saṃbandhaḥ | upakāryatvaṃ vā vibhattayarthaḥ | tatra prakṛtyarthasyābhedasaṃbandhaḥ | tasya svaniṣṭhopakāryatānirūpitopakāra- saṃbandhenānvayaḥ | 'svargāya puṇya'mityādi tviṣṭameva | ata eva 'pāpaṃ narakāya
bhavatī'tyādismārtādiprayogasaṃgatiḥ | yāgāya yātītyādau tumarthakadhañā tādarthyasyoktatvānnānena siddhiriti 'tumarthācca' iti sūtrārambhaḥ | brāhmaṇakartṛkabhojanopakāriṇi dadhni paraṃparayā brahmaṇopakāritā brāhmaṇopakārakatvasyoropaṇaṃ vā brahmaṇāya dhadhītyādau caturthīsiddhiḥ | yūpāya dāvityādau kāryavācakāduttaravirticaturthyeva kāraṇagatasaṃbandhasya bodhanāddhetutvasattve'pi na dārvādipadāt tṛtīyā | bhaktirjñānāya kalpate | atra 'klṛpi saṃpadyamāne ca' iti caturthī, klṛptyarthakadhātuyoge saṃpadyamāne'rthe vartamānāccaturthīti tadarthaḥ | sā ca prathamāpavādabhūtā bhaktyābhinnajñānakartṛkotpattiriti bodhaḥ, jñānātmanā pariṇamata ityatrātmaśabdo jñānavṛttidharmaparaḥ | evaṃ ca tasya na vikṛtivācakatvamiti na tataścaturthī ki tu prakṛtyāditvāt tṛtīyā | vaijñiṣṭhyaṃ tadarthaḥ, jñānatvaviśiṣṭarūpāntaraprāptirbhaktikartṛketi bodhaḥ | rūpāntaraprāptirdhātvarthaḥ | prakṛtivikṛtyorbheda- vivakṣāyāṃ prakṛtivikṛtibhāvāpratītyā na caturthī | api tu 'janikartuḥ ' ityapādānatvāt pañcamī | bhakterjñānaṃ kalpata iti jñānapadāt tādṛrthyacaturthyapekṣayā paratvāttasya kartṛkārakatayā upapadavibhakteriti vacanāñca prathamaiveti kaiyaṭe spaṣṭamiti mañjūṣākṛtaḥ |
anye tu bhedavivakṣāyāṃ vikṛtivācakāccaturthī | kalpate ityasya pariṇamata ityarthaḥ | yadyapi bhedavivakṣāyāṃ tādarthyacaturthaiva siddhaṃ, tathāpi saṃbandhatvena bodhanirvāhārthamidamāvaśyakam | anyathā tathāvivakṣāyāṃ ṣaṣṭhī syāt | abhedavivakṣāyāṃ tu paratvāt prathamaiva bhaktirjñānaṃ kalpata iti | caturthīghaṭitavākye bhaktikartṛko jñānābhinnaḥ pariṇāma iti bodhaḥ | prathamāghaṭite tu bhaktyabhinnajñānakartṛkottpattiriti bodhaḥ | apādānatvivakṣāyāṃ pañcamītyāhuḥ | phalebhyo yāti | atra 'kriyārthopapadasya-' iti caturthī | sthānino jñāyamānasya kriyārthopapadakasya tumunādyantasya karmaṇi - tumunādyantopasthitikriyākarmaṇi caturthīti tadarthaḥ | yathā brahmaṇāya dadhītyādau bhojanopakārakatvena dadhno brahmaṇopakārakatvāttādarthye caturthī bhavati, tathā phalāharaṇopakārakatvena phalopakārakatvasyāpi saiva caturthī yadyi prāpnoti tathāpi phalakarmakāharaṇaphalakaṃ yānamiti phalakarmatvaprakārakabodhe caturthīsādhutvārthamidaṃ sūtramāvaśyakamityanyatra vistaraḥ |
'vātāya kapilā vidyudātapāyātilohinī'tyādau jñāpyajñāpakabhāvāthikā caturthī | utpātena jñāpite ceti vacanāt, prāṇināṃ śubhāśubhasūcakabhūtavikārarūpotpātajñāpitārthavṛtteścaturthīti tadarthaḥ | vātajñāpikā kapilā vidyuditi bodhaḥ | 'brahmaṇāya hitaṃ' 'brahmaṇāya sukham' atra 'caturthī tadarthārtha ' iti samāsavidhānena hitasukhaśabdayoge caturthī | hitaśabdayoge caturthyā 'tasmai hitam' iti nirdeśo'pi jñāpako bodhyaḥ, iṣṭasādhanaṃ hitaśabdārthaḥ , taddhaṭakecchānvayādheyatvādikaṃ caturthyarthaḥ | iṣṭasādhanānvayīcchāghaṭitaḥ paraparāsaṃbandho vā | ata eva parakīyauṣadhāditātparyeṇa brāhmaṇāyedaṃ hitamiti saṃgacchate iti dik | haraye namaḥ | 'namaḥsvasti ' iti sūtreṇātra caturthī | apakṛṣṭatvajñānabodhānukūlo vyāpāraḥ svarādipaṭhitanamaḥśabdarthaḥ | namaḥśabdaprayoge svaniṣṭhaṃ yadapakṛṣṭatvajñānaṃ tajjñāpakasyānubhavasiddhatvāt | tādṛśānanubhave tu apakṛṣṭatvabodhānukūlo vyāpāra eva tadartho bodhyaḥ | ata eva bṛhacchabdenduśekhare tathaivābhyadhāyi | apakarṣaḥ svatantravaktṛniṣṭho namaskāryāvidhaka eva pratīyate | vyāpāro'pi vaktṛniṣṭha eva pratīyate, śabdaśaktisvabhāvāt | anyoccāritanamaḥpadādanyadīyanamaskārābodhāt | vyāpāraśca karamastakasaṃyogādiḥ namaḥpadaghaṭita- vākyaprayogaśca | caturthyartha uddeśyatvam, haryuddeśyakastādṛśo vyāpāra iti bodhaḥ | eṣo'rdhyaḥ śivāya
nama ityādau tyāgo namaḥpadārthaḥ | tyāgaśca svasvatvanivṛttyanukūlecchārūpo vyāpāraḥ | svatvabhāgi- tayecchāviṣayatārūpoddeśyatvaṃ caturthyarthaḥ | śivoddeśyakatvāgaviṣayo'rdhya iti bodhaḥ | svatvanivṛttiviṣayecchaiva parasvatvamapi viṣayīkarotīti manyeta tadoktavidhoddeśyatānirūpakatvarūpaṃ taduddeśyakatvaṃ bodhyam | yadi ca tyāgajanikānyecchaiva tadavagāhata iti svīkriyate tadoddeśyatārūpacaturthyasya paraṃparayā nirūpakatvarūpaṃ, svanirūpakecchādhīnecchāviṣayatvarūpaṃ vā saṃsargatayā bhāsamānaṃ tadbodhyam |
tyāgaśca vedabodhitoddeśyakatvasvakartṛkatvoparakta eva pratīyate, śabdaśaktisvabhāvāt | anyo- ccāritanamaḥpadenānyasaṃbandhityāgāpratīteḥ | vedabodhitetyādyuktyā na nīciddeśyakatyāge pūjābhinna- yādṛcchikecchayānnādityāge ca namaḥśabdaprayogeḥ | ṛtvijā ca yajamānadravye svīyatvaropapūrvaka eva tathāprayogaḥ kriyate | śivādīnāṃ dravye svatvabādhe'pi śivasvaṃ na gṛhṇiyādityasya nāsaṃgatiḥ | vivoddeśena tyaktamityarthakatvāt | namaskaroti devānityatra kṛdhātureva pūrvoktārthakaḥ | namaḥśabda ūrpāditvānnipātastadadyotakaḥ | upapadavibhakteriti nyāyāt, karmaṇi dvitīyā | yadavadhikamapakṛṣṭatvaṃ bodhyate tadvattitvaviśiṣṭaḥ samavāyātmakaḥ phalatāvacchedakasaṃbandhaḥ | atastena saṃbandhena tādṛśabodharūpaphalāśrayāṇāṃ devānāmeva karmatvaṃ na tu śuddhasamavāyenoktabodhāśrayasya taṭasthasyetyāhuḥ | namaḥśabdadyotyārthānvayamātreṇātra caturthīprāptiriti bodhyam | namaskurmo nṛsiṃhāyetyatra nṛsihamanukūlayitumiti bodhāt 'kriyārthopapadasya ' iti caturthīti spaṣṭaṃ kaumudyādau | atra namaḥśabdasya vācakatve 'namaḥsvasti ' iti caturthī | evaṃ dyotakatve'pi karmatvāvivakṣāyāṃ ṣaṣṭhī vādhitvā caturthīti pare vadanti | na tvāṃ tṛṇāya manye | atra 'manyakarmaṇi ' iti caturthī karmāthikā | atrānādaraśabdo nādarābhāvamātrāvedako'pi tu nañaḥ pratipakṣavācitayā'dharmādiva- dādaravirodhitaraskāsarthakaḥ | 'anādare' ityasya karmaviśeṣaṇatayānādaradyotakakarmārthakādeva caturthīvidhānānna yu macchabdāt sā | atra kutsita iti vaktavye'nādarapadopādānamanādarātiśaya- vivakṣāyāmeveyaṃ vibhaktiḥ pravartata iti bodhanāya | etadarthamevodāharaṇe nañupādānaṃ, tṛṇatulyamapi na manye iti bodhamena tadatiśayapratipādānāt | ata eva tṛṇāya matvā raghunandano'pītyādiprayohāṇāmasādhutvamabhāṇi nibandhakṛdbhi | manye iti śyannantasvarūpavivakṣayā tu na tadasādhutvaṃ yuktaṃ, viṅādyantataddhātuyoge caturthyanāpatteḥ | śyanā nirdeśastu tānādikavyāvartanāya | tena na tvāṃ manute ityatra caturthī neti spaṣṭameveti dik | brahmaṇebhyaḥ svasti | svastiśabdaḥ kuśalārtho maṅgalārtho vā, saṃbandhaścaturthyartha ityalaṃ vistareṇeti śivam |
sārthāddhīyate devadatta ityatra dhātūpātavibhāgāvadhitayā sārthasyāpādānatvam | nanu dhātūpātta- vyāpārāśrayatayā paratvāt kartṛtvāpattistasya tadanāśrayatve tu devadtasya tadāśrayatvāt kartṛtvāpattau tadupakarmaṇo ? lādinābhidhānānāpattiḥ | sārthasya karmatvāpattau devadattena sārtho hīyata ityasyā- ptiśceti cedatrāhuḥ 'apādāne cāhīyaruhoḥ' iti sūtreṇa kartṛtvaprāptāvapyapādānatvasya jñāpitatvānna doṣaḥ | anyathā sārthasyāpādānatvābhāvādena taseraprāptau kim 'ahīya' iti niṣedhena ? tajjñāpitamapādānatvaṃ karmapratyayāntajahātiviṣayakameva 'hīya' iti nirdeśāt | tena devadattaḥ sārtha jahāti ityādau na | 'karturīpsitatamam-' ityatra kartṛpadasya dhātūpāttapradhānībhūtavyāpārāśraya- lākṣaṇikatayā devadattasya karmatvasiddhiḥ | saṃpradānasaṃjñāsūtrasthakaiyaṭagranthasvaraso'pyatraiva | adharmājjugupsate-atra 'jugupsāvirāma-' iti vacanenāpādānatvam | yadiṣayatvena jugupsādayo vivakṣitāstadapādānamiti tadarthaḥ |
