aniṣṭasādhanatvajñānarūpā nindā jugupsaterarthaḥ | viṣayatvādi pañcamyarthaḥ | apādānatvaśaktirvā | tādṛśanindāpūrvakanivṛttau lakṣaṇāṃ dhātoḥ svīkṛtya 'dhruvamapāye-' iti sūtreṇaivāpādānatvam | evamevedaṃ vārtikamuttarasūtrāṇi ca na kartavyānīti bhāṣyakṛnmatam | dharmādviramati kṛtisaṃsargābhāvānukūlo vyāpāro viramaterarthaḥ | anukūlatā cātra yogakṣemasādhāraṇī | anukūla vyāpāraśca dharmaviṣayopekṣātmaka eva | taduktaṃ bhāṣye 'ya eṣa manuṣyo nāstikabuddhirbhavati sa paśyati vedaṃ kiciddharmo nāma nainaṃ kariṣyāmīti sa buddhayā saṃprāpya tato nivartate' iti | anena yaḥ kadācidi dharma na kṛtavāṃratatrāpyasau prayoga iṣṭa iti labhyate | etena karaṇānantaramakāraṇaṃ viramaterarthaḥ | tacca jīvanakālāvacchinnaḥ kṛtyasamānādhikaraṇakṛtidhvaṃsaḥ | ubhayatra kṛtirekaviṣayaiva | ato bhojanādikṛtisamānādhikaraṇatvājjīvanakālāvacchinnakṛtidhvaṃsa- sāmānyasyeti nāsaṃbhavaḥ | dhvaṃse jīvanakālāvacchinnatvaniveśāt maraṇādikālikakṛtidhvaṃsamādāya dharmādviramate ityādiprayogo na | jīvanaṃ kartṛsaṃbandhyevopādeyaṃ, tenānyadīyajīvanakālāvacchinnatvasya caitrīyamaraṇādikālikakṛtidhvaṃse sattvānna taddoṣatādavasthyam | pūrvaparakālayordharmācaraṇe ghaṭikādikāle tadanācaraṇepi dharmādviramatīti prayogāpattivāraṇāya kṛtyasamānādhikaraṇatvaṃ kṛtidhvaṃse viśeṣaṇam | tacca svāvacchedakaśarīrajātīyaśarīrāvacchedena kṛtisamānādhikaraṇatvaṃ yattadbhinnatvaṃ dharmaviṣaya | kṛtidhvaṃsasyāpi bhāvidehāntarāvacchinna kṛtisamānādhikaraṇatayā tādṛśaprayogā- saṃbhavāpattinirāsāya svāvacchedakaśarīrajātīyaśarīrāvacchinnatvakṛtisamānādhikaraṇye viśeṣaṇam | tādṛśarīrāvacchinnatvaniveśe śarīrāṇāmavasthābhedena bhedādyuvaśarīrāvacchedena dharma kurvāṇe'pi bālaśarīrāvacchinnakṛtidhvaṃsasya kṛtyavacchedakaśarīrāvacchinnatvābhāvena svāvacchedena kṛtyasamānādhi- karaṇatayā dharmādviramatītiprayogāpattestajjātīyaśarīrāvacchinnatvaniveśaḥ | sājātyaṃ caitrādi- padopasthāpitacaitratvādinaiva, nāto bhavāntarīyadehasyāpi pṛdhivītvamanuṣyatvādinā sajātīyatayā taddoṣatādavasthyam | tatra vartamānadehasya tādṛśakṛtyanavacchedakatayā na tatsāmānādhi- karaṇyāvacchedakatvagiti noktadoṣaḥ | nanu prācīnaśarīrāvacchinnakṛtisāmānādhikaraṇyāduktadoṣasta- davastha eveti cenna | svāvacchedakakālāvacchinnatvasyāpi sāmānādhikaraṇye niveśāttannirastam | kadācidapi dharmānācaraṇe iṣṭasya tādṛśaprayogasyānāpatteḥ | nanu neṣṭa eva tatra sa prayogaḥ | nikhilajanānāṃ dharmācaraṇānantaraṃ tadakaraṇa eva tathāvidhaprayogāt | uktabhaṣyasyāpi pūrva yo dharma kṛtavān paścādyo nāstikastatparatvasyāi saṃbhavāditi cenna, saṃkoce mānābhāvāt | dharmādvirato'pi
punaḥ pravṛtta ityāderanāpatteśca | nanu tava mate pūrva dharmaviṣayopekṣā tadanantaraṃ ca svāpādikriyā tatsamaye tādṛśaprayogāpattiḥ, kṛtyabhāvānukūlopekṣātmakavyāpārasya tadā sattvāt | tanmate tu nāpattistadānī tattatkṛtidhvaṃsasya tattatkṛtisamānādhikaraṇatvāditi cenna | yatkāle tathāvidhaprayogaḥ prāmāṇikastatkālaniṣṭhopekṣāyā eva dhātvarthatvāṅgīkārāt | etatsūjanāyeva mañjūṣāyāmupekṣāviśeṣa ityuktam | nanu karaṇānantaramakaraṇamityasya nokto'rtho'pi tvānantarthasāmānādhikaraṇyobhaya- saṃbandhābhyāṃ kṛtiviśiṣṭaṃ kṛtyabhāvānukūlavyāpārastadarthaḥ | vyāpāraśca pūrvokta eva | athavā kṛtidhvaṃsānukūloktavyāpāraḥ | evaṃ ca dharmādvirato'pītyādirnoktadoṣo'pariṣkārāpekṣayā lāghavaṃ bhavaduktārthāpekṣayaitasmin gauravābhāvaścāthaveti pakṣe iti cenna, kadāpīti pūrvoktadoṣatādavasthādutpattimattvaghaṭitadhvaṃsatvasyābhāvatvāpekṣayā gurutvācceti dik | dharmāt pramādyati iṣṭasādhanaviśeṣyakāniṣṭasādhanatvaprakārakajñānarūpānavadhānatā dhātvarthaḥ | viṣayitva- mapādānatvaṃ vā pañcamyarthaḥ | anye tvasmaraṇapūrvakapravṛttyabhāvo'smaraṇameva vānavadhānatā | sa eva dhātvarthaḥ, svaraṇaṃ ca kartavyādiprakārakam, viṣayitvaṃ pañcamyarthaḥ | dharmaviṣayakartavyātvādiprakārakasmaraṇābhāvaprayukto dharmaviṣayakapravṛttyabhāvastādṛśasmaraṇābhāvo vā vākyārthaḥ | corādvibheti trāyate vā-atra 'bhītrārthānāṃ bhayahetuḥ' ityapādānatm | aniṣṭasaṃbhāvanaṃ bhayam | aniṣṭanivṛttyanukūlo vyāpārastrāṇam | sūtre bhayaśabdena tadekadeśarūpamaniṣṭamucyate | tathā cāniṣṭaheturapādānasaṃjña ityarthaḥ | corāpādānakamaniṣṭajñānamityādyākārako bodho mañjūṣākṛnmate | anyamate corahetuka ityādirītyā bodhaḥ | tanmate prayojyatvasya pañcamyarthatvāt tasyāniṣṭe'nvayaḥ | aniṣṭaṃ ca pratyāsattyāniṣṭasaṃbhāvanaṃ kartṛsaṃbandhi | na caivaṃ yasya corādhīnamaniṣṭamaprasiddhamatha ca taddhetukatvena svasyāniṣṭaṃ saṃbhāvayati tatra corādayaṃ vibhetīti prayogo na syāt, tadīyāniṣṭe cora- hetukatvasya bādhāditi vācyam | taddhetukānyadīyāniṣṭasya tasmistadīyāniṣṭe vā corahetukatvasyāropeṇoktaprayogasya nirvāhāt | yajjanyaṃ bhayaṃ kasyāpyaprasiddhaṃ tatra tathāvidhaprayogasyeṣṭatve āropa eva śaraṇīkartavyaḥ | atrāpi corādeḥ saṃbandhitvena vivakṣāyāṃ ṣaṣṭhī bhavati | ata eva 'kasya vibhyati devāśca jātaroṣasya saṃyuge' iti rāmāyaṇaprayogasaṃgiḥ | kasya saṃyuge ityanvayo vā svīkārya ityāhuḥ | tārkikāstu prayojyatāprakārakatvaṃ paḍhcamyarthaḥ | tasya saṃbhāvanāyāmakṣatatvāt | evaṃ ca śatrubhrameṇa mitrādapi vibhetīti vākyasyāpi prāmāṇyopapattiḥ | evaṃ ca bhayārthakadhātuyoge bhayahetutvena saṃbhāvanamapādānamityeko vākyārthaḥ, trāṇārthakadhātuyoge'niṣṭa- prayojakaṃ tadityapara ityāhuḥ | adhyayanāt parājayate atra'parājerasoḍhaḥ' iti sūtreṇāpādānatvaṃ, kṛtyasādhyatvaprakārakajñānasamānādhikaraṇastadviṣayakotsāhābhāvo dhātvarthaḥ | tadviṣayatvamatrāpādānam | asoḍha ityasya tādṛśāsahanarūpadhātvarthaviṣayārthakatvāt | evaṃ cākarmakatve evodāharaṇatvamiti paryavasannam | utsāho harṣapadavyapadeśyaśicattavṛttiviśeṣaḥ, pravṛttiviśeṣo vā | adhyayamāpādāna- kastādṛśa utsāhābhāva iti bodhaḥ | viṣayatvaṃ pañcamyarthaḥ | adhyayanaviṣayatvasyotsāhe'nvaya ityanya āhuḥ | apare tu dveṣādhīnanirvṛtidharmātvarthaḥ | pañcamyartho viṣayitvaṃ tadarthadveṣe nivṛttau cānveti | adhyayanaviṣayakadveṣaprayuktatattadviṣayakanivṛttistadvān veti bodha ityāhuḥ | evaṃ ca samānantaroktamate etanmate ca sakarmaka iti labhyate | paraṃ tu pravṛttyabhāvādipratītirevānubhava- siddhetīdaṃ na yuktamiti pratibhāti | śatrūn parājayata ityatra tiraskārānukūlo vyāpāro dhātvarthaḥ |
tiraskārātmakaphalāśrayatvena śatrūṇāṃ karmatvam | 'asoḍha' ityukternātrāpādānatvam | 'gāṃ vāsyati' agrermāṇavakaṃ vārayati 'kūpādandhaṃ vārayati' atra 'vāraṇārthānāmīpsitiḥ' ityapādānatvam |vāraṇārthakadhātuyoga īpsitatattaddhātvarthaphalāśrayatvenecchāviṣayo'pādānasaṃjña iti tadarthaḥ | icchā ca vāryamāṇasya gavādeḥ | īpsita ityanena pradhānāpradhānānyataravyāpārāśrayakarturākṣepā | spaṣṭaṃ cedamatra sūtre kaiyaṭe | bhakṣaṇasaṃyogādyanukūlakriyābhāvānukūlavyāpāro vārayaterarthaḥ | yā kriyā yadā pratiṣidhyate tadā tatkriyābhāvānukūlakriyārthakatvaṃ tasyeti phalitam | yavādayo bhakṣaṇādi- karturgavādergalādhaḥsaṃyogānukūlavyāpārābhāvānukulavyāpāra iti bodhaḥ | yogakṣemasādhāraṇānukūlato- pādeyā | ataḥ prāgabhāvāderajanyatve'pi nāpravṛttavāraṇe uktaprayogāsiddhiḥ | 'yadīmā gāvastatra gacchanti dhruvaṃ sasyanāśastannāśe'dharmo rājabhayaṃ ca, sa buddhayā saṃprāpya nivartayati' iti bhāṣyātrāpi tasyeṣṭatvāvadhāraṇāt | svarūpayogyatārūpaiva cānukūlatā grāhyā na tu phalopahitatvarūpā, tena pratiṣadhānantaraṃ bhakṣayatyāpi gavādāvayaṃ nivārayati tathāpi na nivartate hati prayogopapattiḥ | bhāṣyakāramate tādṛśābhāvānukūlanivartanāyā dhātvarthatayā 'dhruvamapāye-' ityanenaivāpādānatvam | acetane'vyāpāropitanivṛtteḥ sattvānna puṣpebhya ātapaṃ vārayatītyasyāsiddhistanmate | kūpapata- namapyandhasyābhibhukhadeśagamanatvenecchāviṣayaḥ | evaṃ sati viṣayasya viṣayatvenecchāviṣayatvābhāvepi bhakṣaṇatvādinecchāviṣayatvābhattvānnāntarīyakaviṣabhojanaprasaktau tadvārayitari viṣādvārayati devadattaṃ yajñadatta iti prayogāpattiriṣṭāpattyā pariharaṇīyetyāhuḥ | māturnilīye kṛṣṇaḥ-'antardhau yena-' iti sūtreṇātrāpādānatvam | anyakartṛkasvaviṣayakadarśanābhāvānukūlā vyavahitadeśe sthitirantarghistaddhaṭakaṃ yatkartṛkadarśanābhāvamicchati tadapādānamiti sūtrārthaḥ | līṅdhātoruktāntarghirarthaḥ | udāharaṇe icchā ākṣepalabhyā, darśane mātṛkartṛkatvaṃ pratyāsattito labhyam | tathā ca mātrapādānakasva- karmakamarthānmātṛkartṛkaṃ yaddarśanaṃ tadabhāvānukūlastādṛśadeśāvasthitirūpo vyāpāraḥ kṛṣṇakartṛka iti bodhaḥ | anye tu kartṛtvaṃ pañcamyarthastasya dhātvarthaghaṭakadarśane nirūpakatvasaṃbandhenānvayaḥ | tathā ca mātṛkartṛkaṃ svakarmakaṃ yaddarśanaṃ tadabhāvānukūlastādṛśo vyāpāra iti bodhaḥ | sūtre 'yena' ityasābhāve yastādṛśadarśanābhāvamicchati tasyaiva syāditi 'yena' ityupāttam | nanu kartṛkarmaṇoḥ kṛti' iti ṣaṣṭhīprasaṅgād 'yena' iti nirdeśānupapattiḥ | na ca ātmana iti karmaṇo'dhyāhāreṇa 'ubhayaprāptau' iti niyamānna doṣa iti vācyam | evaṃ sati 'atha śabdānuśāsanam' ityatrāpyācāryasya karuḥ prasiddhatayādhyāhārāvaśyakatayā 'ubhayaprāptau-' iti niyamaprasattayā 'karmaṇi ca' iti samāsaniṣedhāpatteḥ | vastutastūbhayopādāne eva niyamapravṛttiriti 'ātmamāne-' iti sūtrabhāṣye spaṣṭam | evaṃ ca prakṛte ātmana ityasyādhyāhārānna doṣa iti sustham | 'upādhyāyādadhīte' 'paṇḍitāt purāṇaṃ śrṛṇoti' atra 'ākhyātopayoge' iti sūtreṇāpādānatvam | niyamapūrvakavidyā- svīkāre sati vaktāpādānamiti tadarthaḥ | uccāraṇottarakāliko brahmacaryādirūpaniyamapūrvakagrantha- svīkārānukūlo vyāpāra iṅdhātvarthaḥ | niyamapūrvakaśravaṇapratyakṣānukūlā vyāpāraḥ śrudhātvarthaḥ | yadi niyamapūrvakavidyāsvīkārarūpopayogasya svarūpasattāyāḥ saṃjñopayogitvaṃ svīkriyate na tu tasya śābdabodhe bhānamapi tadā dhātvarthe niyamapūrvākatvaṃ nāntarbhāvyam | upādhyāyāpādānakastādṛśo vyāpāra iti bodhaḥ | anye tu kartruccāraṇādhīnatvaṃ pañcamyarthaḥ, uccāraṇādhīnatvamātraṃ vā, ādye pañcamyarthaghaṭakakartari prakṛtyarthasyābhedenānvayaḥ, antye svakartṛkatvasaṃbandhena pañamyarthasyoccāraṇādi-
rūpe dhātvarthe āśrayatvasaṃbandhenetyāhuḥ | brahmaṇaḥ prajāḥ prajāyante-atra 'janikartuḥ-' iti sūtreṇāpādānatvam, jananaṃ janirutpattistasyāḥ karteti ṣaṣṭhītatpuruṣaḥ, tṛnnantatvānna samāsaniṣedhaḥ | na caivaṃ 'na loka-' iti ṣaṣṭhīniṣedhaprasaṅgaḥ, kārakaṣaṣṭhyā eva sa niṣedha iti siddhāntāccheṣa- ṣaṣṭhīpravṛttau bādhakābhāvāt |
vastutastu śeṣaṣaṣṭhyāstṛjantenāpi samāsaḥsulabha iti dhyeyam | spaṣṭaṃ cedaṃ manoramādau | evaṃ cotpattyāśrayasya prakṛtirūpādānakāraṇamapādānamiti sūtrārthaḥ | prakṛtiśabdasya tatraiva prasiddheḥ | māyāśabalasya brahmaṇo jagadupādānatvaṃ vedāntisiddhāntasiddham | ata eva brahmaṇo jagadupādānatve 'yato vā imāni siddhatvācca | putrāt pramodo'jāyata ityādāvupādānatvāropeṇa nirvāhaḥ | vṛttikṛtastadanuyāyino'nye ca putrāt pramoda ityādiprayogānurodhena prakṛtipadena hetumātraparigraha ityāhuḥ na ca dhūmādagrimānityādāviva prakṛte'pi 'vibhāṣā-' iti pañacamīsiddhau kimanena sūtreṇeti vācyam | apādānatvaprakārakabodhanirvāhārthametasyāvaśyakatvāt | anye tu hetutvātiriktasvarūpa- saṃbandhaviśeṣādhīnatvabodhe pañcamīsiddhaya idāmāvaśya, saṃbandhatvena rūpeṇa hetutvabodhanirvāhārtha cetyāhuḥ | himavato gaṅga prabhavati-atra 'bhuvaḥ prabhavaḥ' ityamenāpādānatvam | bhavanaṃ bhūḥ, saṃpadāditvāt bhāve kkip | samāsaikadeśabhūtamapi kartṛgrahaṇamanuvartate svaritatvāt | tathā ca bhavanakartuḥ prabhavaḥ, prabhavatyasmāditi prabhavaḥ-prathamaprakāśādhikaraṇamapādānamiti tadarthaḥ | 'prabhavo jalamūle syājjanmahetau parākrame | jñānasya cādimasthāne-' iti viśvaḥ | prabhavatītyatra prathamopalambharūpaḥ prathamaprakāśo dhātvarthaḥ | sūtre'pi bhūdhāturuktārthaka eva | prakāśapratyakṣātmaka- jñānaviṣayatvaṃ, tādṛśajñānamātraṃ vā viṣayatāsaṃbandhena | tadāśrayasya gaṅgādeḥ kartṛtvam | tadgataṃ prāthamyaṃ ca samānakālikatvasaṃbandhena svadhvaṃsāvijñiṣṭatvam, tādṛśaṃ jñānaṃ bhūmaṇḍalasthasya kasyacideva gaṅgāvirbhāvasamānakālikasya saṃbhavati |dakṣiṇadeśādāgatasya vindhyācale gaṅgāyāḥ prathamadarśane'pi na vindhyādito gaṅga prabhavatīti prayogaḥ, taddarśanasya tādṛśadhvaṃsāviśiṣṭatvābhāvāt | jñānaṃ ca tātparyavaśānmanuyalokasthaniṣṭhameva | ato devānāṃ tādṛśajñānaviṣayatvamādāya na vaikuṇṭhādgaṅga prabhavatīti prayogāpattiḥ | apādānatvaśaktimān pañcamyartha iti mañjūṣākṛtaḥ | adhikaraṇatvaṃ pañcamyartha ityapare | saṃbandhādhīnatvaṃ tadartha iti vyutpattivādakṛtaḥ | darśanayogyatvābhāvāvacchedakadeśāvyāvahitadeśāvacchedena darśanayogyatvaṃ dhātvarthaḥ | tādātmyaṃ pañmyarthaḥ | tasya dhātvarthaghaṭakadvitīyadeśe'nvayaḥ | tathā ca himavadabhinno yo darśanayogyatvābhāvāvacchedaka- deśāvyavahitadeśastadavacchinnadarśanayogyatvavatī gaṅgeti bodha iti tārkikaratnākarakṛtaḥ | kārakavādakakṛtastu-ādyabahiḥ saṃyogo dhātvarthaḥ | vahiḥ padārthaḥ prakṛte rasātalaṃ, syogadhvaṃsāvyavahitottarakṣaṇavṛttitvaṃ pañcamyarthaḥ | āśrayatvamākhyātārthaḥ |
tathā ca himālayānuyogikasaṃyogadhvaṃsāvyavahitottarakṣaṇavṛttitvaṃ pañcamyarthaḥ, āśrayatvamākhyā tārthaḥ | tathā ca himālayānuyogikasaṃyogadhvaṃsāvyavahitottarakṣaṇavṛttyādyapṛthvīsaṃyogāśrayatvavatī gaṅgeti bodhaḥ | vaikuṇṭhāt prabhavati gaṅga kāśīto veti prayogavāraṇāyāvyavahitatvādyatvayo- rupādānam | bhūsaṃyogasya vaikuṇṭhasaṃyogadhvaṃsāvyavahitottarakṣaṇavṛttitvābhāvāt, kāśīsaṃyogadhvaṃsā- vyavahitottarakṣaṇavṛttigaṅgābhūsaṃyogasyādyatvābhāvānna doṣa ityāhuḥ | atra prathamajñānātmakaprathama- prakāśa eva dhātvartha iti jñānasya cādimasthāna ityuktaviśvakośāllabhyate | upalabheteti
prabhavatītyasya vṛttisthavyākhyānācca | atra āsanāt prekṣate, 'lyablope karmaṇyadhikaraṇe ca' iti pañcamī | gamyamānārthakatayāprayogo lopaḥ | evaṃ ca vyabantasyājñāne pratyāsattyā lyabantadhātvarthakarmaṇītyādirarthaḥ | lyabantasyānuccāraṇe iti tu 'lyablope' ityasya nārthaḥ | darśanābhāvasyaiva lopasaṃjñakatvāt | dṛśeścoccāraṇakriyatvābhāvāditi spaṣṭaṃ bṛhacchabdaratnādau | āsane upaviśya prekṣata ityarthaḥ | prāsādāt prekṣate-prāsādamāruhya prekṣata ityarthaḥ | krameṇādhikaraṇakarmaṇī pañcamyarthau | upaveśanādikriyāyā vākyaikadeśanyāyena bhānamityanyatra vistaraḥ| āsalādhikaraṇakaṃ yadupaveśanaṃ tadavyavahitottarakālikaṃ tatsamānakartṛkaṃ prekṣaṇamityādiḥ śābdabodhaḥ | vanād grāmo yojane yojanaṃ vā atra 'yataścādhvakālanirmāṇaṃ tatra pañcamī, tadyuktādadhvanaḥ prathamāsaptamyau kālāt saptamī ca vaktavyā' iti vacanāt pañcamyādikaṃ vidhīyate | yadavadhitvenāśrityādhvakālānyatarasya nirmāṇaṃ paricchedaḥ pratipādyate tataḥ pañcamī, tadyuktāt - tena pañcamyantenārthadvārā yuktāt paricchedyādhvavācinaḥ prathamāsaptamyau bhavataḥ | kālādityatrāpi tadyuktādityasyānuṣaṅgaḥ | tathāvidhakālārthakāt stamīti tadarthaḥ | avyavahitottaratvaṃ pañcamyarthaḥ | pratiyogitvasaṃbandhena prakṛtyarthānvitasyāśrayatvasaṃbandhena yojane'nvayaḥ | prathamāsaptamyoravyahitottara- vṛttitvamarthaḥ | tasya grāme āśrayatayānvayaḥ |
evaṃ ca vanāvyavahitottarayojanāvyavahitottaradeśavṛttirgrāma iti bodhaḥ | kārtikyā āgrahāyaṇī māse ityatrāvayavaghaṭitatvaṃ pañcamyā arthaḥ, avyavahitottarakālavṛttitvaṃ saptamyāḥ, kārtikīrūpa- prakṛtyarthasya tādātmyasaṃbandhena pañcamyarthaghaṭakāvayave'nvayaḥ | kārtikyavayavaghaṭitamāsā- vyavahitottarakālavṛttirāgrahāyaṇīti bodhaḥ | pūrvatra prathamāsaptamyoratra ca saptamyā avyavahitottaratva- mātraṃ vārthaḥ | tasyāśrayatvasaṃbandhena grāme'nvayaḥ | yojanādhikavyavadhāne vanād grāma ityaprayogāttatra pañcamyarthe'vyavadhānāṃśaniveśaḥ | yojanīttaravibhattayarthe'pyata eya tanniveśaḥ | māse ityatra saptamyarthe tadantarbhāvo'pyetadarthameva | anyathā kārtikyāḥ pauṣī māse ityāderapyāpatteḥ | kārtikīghaṭita- māsottaravṛttitvasya pauṣyāmapi sattvāt | kārtikyā iti pañcamyā vanādityatrevāvyavahitottaratvaṃ nārthaḥ | kārtikyavyavahitotaramāsāvyavahitottaravṛttitvasyāgrahāyaṇyāmasaṃbhavāduktaprayogānupapatteḥ | ubhayatra pañcamyā avadhitvamarthaḥ | vanāvadhikayojanāvyahitottaradeśavṛttirgrāma ityākārako bodha iti śābdikaratmākarakṛtaḥ | yojanādyarthe jñāte niyamenāvadhyākāṅkṣāyāḥ sattvānnāsaṃbhava iti bhāvaḥ | kārtikyā ityādāvapi nārthāsaṃgatiḥ taddharmavato'pi taddharmāvadhitvāt, yathābhividhāviti bhāvaḥ | ghaṭādanya ityādau svapratiyogikabhedasaṃbandhaḥ pañcamyarthaḥ | tena saṃbandhena prakṛtyarthasyānyasminnanvayaḥ, ata eva nātraikadeśānvayaprasaktiḥ | ata eva devadattasya putra ityādau janyatvādeḥ ṣaṣṭhayarthatvamabhyupayanti śābdikāḥ | anyādiśabdena bhedādyavagatanirākāṅkṣatvaṃ na prakāratayā, tadbodhe eva nirākāṅkṣatvena saṃsargatayā bodhe nirākāṅkṣatvavirahāt | svapratiyogikatvamityasya prakṛtyarthatāvacchedakāvacchinnapratiyogitākatvamityarthaḥ | tena ghaṭe'pi ghaṭāntaramādāya ghaṭo ghaṭādanya iti prayogāpattiḥ | svapadena ghaṭatvādiviśiṣṭatattadvayaktyupādāne'pi dvitvādinā tatpratiyogika- bhedamādāya taddoṣastadavastha iti dūṣaṇaṃ prayuktam | anye tu pratiyogitvaṃ pratiyogimatt‌vaṃ vā pañcamyarthaḥ | pratiyogitve prakṛtyarthatāvacchedakāvacchinnādheyatvasaṃbandhena prakṛtyarthasya prakṛtyarthānvitasya nirūpakatvasaṃbandhena bhede taddhaṭitasaṃbandhena tadvati vānvayaḥ | dvitīyapakṣenuyogitā- svarūpe
pratiyogimattve prakṛtyarthatāvacchedakāvacchinnanirūpitatvasaṃbandhena prakṛtyarthasyānvayaḥ, tasya cāśrayatvasaṃbandhena bhede ekadeśe'pyanvayo vyutpattivaicivyād vispaṣṭapaṭurityatra pāṭave yathā vyutpattivaicivyādanvayaḥ | ata eva mukhyasāmānādhikaraṇyābhāvād, 'viśeṣaṇaṃ viśeṣyeṇa' iti karmadhārayo na ki tu supsupeti samāso vispaṣṭādīnīti jñāpakādveti spaṣṭaṃ 'prāk kaḍārāt samāsaḥ' ita sūtre bhāṣyakaiyaṭayoḥ | taddhaṭitasaṃbandhena bhedavati vānvaya ityāhuḥ | anyetyartha- grahaṇamata eva ghaṭādbhinna iti prayogasiddhiḥ | ghaṭasya bheda ityatra tu na pañcamīprasaktiḥ | bhedaviśiṣṭārthakasyaiva grahaṇāt anyaśabdasya viśiṣṭārthakatvāt | itaragrahaṇametadarthasūcakamevetyabhi- pretyaiva prapañcārthamityuktaṃ kaumudyām |
ata eva ghaṭo netyatra na tadāpattistasya dyotakatvāṅgīkārācceti prāñcaḥ | navyaśābdikamate tu nārthagrahaṇaṃ bhāṣyānuktatvāt | ghaṭādbhinna ityatra pañcamīnirvāhastu prāgukta evetyalam | kṛṣṇādṛte duḥkham, atra kṛṣṇaśabdastadbhaktilākṣaṇikaḥ | ṛteśabdārthaḥ saṃsargābhāvaḥ | pratiyogitāsaṃbandhena prakṛtyarthānvitasya tasya sāmānādhikaraṇyasaṃbandhena tatsthe duḥkhe'nvayaḥ | abhāve pratiyogivaiyadhikaraṇyaṃ niveśanīyamanyathārādhyatvaprakārakajñānātmakabhakteravyāpyavṛttitayā tadabhāva- sāmānādhikaraṇyasya sukhe'pi sattvena kṛṣṇādṛte sukhamiti prayogāpattiḥ | kkacit prayuktatvasaṃbandhenaivānvayaḥ | ata eva vṛṣṭeṛte sukhamiti na | anyathā vṛṣṭeḥ kṣetrādau sattve'pi tadabhāvasyātmani sattvena syāttathā prayogaḥ | svadeśāt śatroḥ prayāṇādṛte devadattasya duḥkhamityādau prayuktatvabodhasyānubhavasiddhatvādāvaśyakatvācca | anyathā śatroḥ prayāṇe satyapi tadabhāvasya devadateta sattvena tadīyavyādhyādiprayuktaduḥkhe tādṛśaprayāṇābhāvasāmānādhikaraṇyamādāya durvāratvāt | evaṃ daṇḍādṛte na ghaṭa itivadrāsabādṛte na ghaṭa ityādiprayogāpattirapyata eva na | daṇḍābhāvaprayuktatvasya ghaṭābhāve'bādhitatvavadrāsabhābhāvaprayuktatvasyābādhitatvavirahāt | sāmānādhikaraṇyasaṃbandhasya saṃsargatve tu syādevoktaprayogāpattiḥ | prayuktatvasaṃbandhenaiva tasyānvaya iti niyamastu na vahnoṛte parvatarūpābhāva ityādau tadasaṃbhavādityalam |
ṛte vātādvṛkṣaḥ patati, atrābhāvastadvānvā tadarthaḥ | vātābhāvaviśiṣṭavṛkṣasya patane saṃbandhāvagatau patane vā tajjanyatvābhāvalābha ārthaḥ | janyatvaśabdādanupasthitasyābhāvapratiyogitayā bhānāsaṃbhavājjanyatvasya vṛttyaniyāmakatayā tatsaṃbandhāvacchinnapratiyogitākavātābhāvabodho'pi na saṃbhavati | vātapadasya vātajanyatvalakṣaṇāyāṃ tu tadāśrayaṇameva doṣaḥ | bhedavānapi ṛteśabdārthaḥ | yathā rāmādṛte na dhanurdhara ityatra | rāmabhinno dhanurdharabhinna iti bodhaḥ | ghaṭādanya ityatroktaprakāraḥ sarvo'pyatrāvagantavyaḥ | etadyoge dvitīyāpi 'tato'nyatrāpi dṛśyate' ityatra dṛśigrahaṇāt | ata eva cāndrā 'ṛte dvitīyā ca' iti sūtrayanti | ata eva 'puruṣārādhanamṛte' iti prayogo'pi sādhurbhavati | tena ṛte vātaṃ vṛkṣaḥ patatītyapi bhavati | pūrvo grāmāttaḍāgaḥ atra 'anyarāt-' iti sūtreṇa pañcamī | atra pūrvaśabdasyādigvṛttitve'pi śabdagrahaṇasāmarthyāddiśi dṛṣṭamātrasya grahaṇāt | udayā- calasaṃnihitadeśaḥ pūrvapadārthaḥ | avadhitvaṃ pcamyarthaḥ | avadhitvasākāṅkṣo dharmāviśeṣaḥ pūrvatvam | sa cākhaṇḍopādhiḥ | evaṃ paratvādyapi | grāmāvadhitānirūpakapūrvatvavānityādikrameṇa bodhaḥ | caitrāt pūrvaḥ phālgunaḥ-atra pratiyogitaddhvaṃsānadhikaraṇakāla eva bhāsate tattadavadhibodhakapadasamabhivyāhāra- sācivyāt | caitrāvadhikapūrvatvavatkālabhavaḥ phālguna iti bodhaḥ | taddhvaṃsamātrānadhikaraṇabhavasya
pūrvatvena rūpeṇa vācyatve caitrāt pūrvaścaitra iti prayogāpattiḥ | tatpratiyogimātrānadhikaraṇabhavasya tathātve vaiśākhācaitrādirūpapratiyogyādyantarbhāveṇa śaktyaṅgīkāre śattayānantyam | pratiyogitvādirūpeṇevāntarbhāve'prasiddhirityādidoṣabhayāt tādṛśakālabhavasya pūrvatvenaiva śaktiraṅgīkṛtā | kālasya kriyāmātraparicchedakatvāt phālgunādinānvayo na syādatasta- dbhavamyāntarbhāvaḥ | pūrvādiśabdasya tatra lakṣaṇā | lakṣaṇābhāve tu caitrāt pūrva phālguna ityeva prayogaḥ | adhikaraṇaśaktipradhānamavyayam | astītvāderadhyāhāraḥ | anye tu grāmāt pūrva ityatrodayācalāvadhikāparatvāśrayaḥ | pūrvaśabdārthaḥ, pañcamyāḥ paratvanirūpakatvamarthaḥ | tathā ca grāmavṛttiparatvanirupakaṃ yadudayācalāvadhikamaparatvaṃ tadāśraya iti bodhaḥ | caitrādityādyudāharaṇe pūrvaśabdaḥ prāgagabhāvāśrayārthakaḥ | pratiyogitvārthikā pañcamī | prāgabhāvānaṅgīkāre tu dhvaṃsapratiyogo purvaśabdārthaḥ | ādheyatvaṃ pañcamyarthaḥ | tasya ca dhvaṃse'nvayaḥ | caitrapratiyogikaprāgabhāvāśrayaḥ phālguna ityādyākāro bodha ityāhuḥ | atra mate prāgabhāvādeḥ sāvadhikatvābhāvena tadarthakapūrvaśabdasyāvadhyākāṅkṣārthakatvaṃ na syāt, tathā ca vyavasthābhāve sarvanāmatā na syāt | na ca pratiyogyākaṅkṣāsattvādeva tathātvopapattiriti vācyam | prati- yogitvāvadhitvarthobhedāt, pūrvatvasya sāvadhikatvānmanmate nānupapattirityāhuḥ śābdikāḥ | caitrāt paro vaiśākhaḥ-caitradhvaṃsādhikaraṇakālabhavaḥ paratvena paraśabdārthaḥ | avadhitve pañcamī | 'gṛhādgaṅga mama parā kāśyāḥ saivāparā mama' ityādau viprakṛṣṭadeśasthasaṃnikṛṣṭadeśasthau paratvāparatvābhyāṃ parāparaśabdapratipādyau | gṛhāvadhikamatsaṃbandhiparatvavatī gaṅgetyādi krameṇa bodhaḥ | paratvādiniṣṭhaḥ svasaṃbandhaḥ svasaṃyuktasaṃyogaparaṃparāśrayāśritatvādyātmakaḥ madhyavati gṛhaṃ svasaṃbandhyanyasaṃbandhi vetyanyadetat | yasya tadarthe utthitākāṅkṣā pratyāsattyā tannimittakavibhaktestadvācakādeva svīkārāt | parādipadaṃ cāvadhāveva niyatākāṅkṣamiti spaṣṭameva |
prāgādayaḥ pūrvādinā vyākhyātāḥ | sadhyuṅ caitreṇetyādau tu pañcamīsāhaṭaryeṇa digvācakasyaivāñcūttarapadasya pañcamīnimittatvābhyupagamāt | grāmādvahniḥ samabhivyāhṛtapadārtha- tāvacchedakānāśrayastatsthaṃ ca bahiṣṭarūpopādhinocyate | anāvṛtadeśa ityanenāpyayamevārtho'bhipretaḥ | ata eva dravyādbahirbhūto guṇa ityādyupapadyate | kāśīto bahirbhūtamitaṃ gṛhamiti ca | bahiḥśabdena pañcamī samāsavidhānajñāpitātra pañcamī | jñāpakasiddhasyāsārvatrikatvena 'karasya karabho bahiḥ' iti siddhamityāhuḥ | apa hareḥ pari hareḥ saṃsāra ityatrāpaparī ṛteśabdasamānārthau | 'apaparī varjane' iti karmapravacanīyasaṃjñā | 'pañcamyapāṅparibhiḥ' iti pañcamī | hariśabdo haribhaktilākṣaṇikaḥ | haribhattayabhāvasamānādhikaraṇastatprayojyo vā saṃsāraḥ | saṃsāro janmādi | apare tu atyantābhāvo'nayorarthaḥ, pañcamyā ādheyatvam | tasya cātyantābhāve'nvayo'tyantābhāvasya pratiyāgitayā saṃsāre | harivṛttyatyantābhāvapratiyogī saṃsāra iti bodhaḥ | apa pṛthivyā gandhābhāva ityādau tathaivānubhavāditi vadanti | teṣāmayamāśayaḥ pṛthivyabhāvasamānādhikaraṇo gandhabhāva iti bodhābhyupagamo na saṃbhavi | apa pṛthivyāḥ snehābhāva ityāderapyāpatteḥ | atha pṛthivyabhāvaprayukto gandhābhāva iti bodhaḥ svīkriyate snehādyabhāvaśca na tatprayukta iti nātiprasaṅga iti cet, hetu- tāvacchedakasaṃbandhāvacchinnahetvabhāvasyaiva kāryābhāvaprayojakatayā saṃyogādisaṃbandhāvacchinnatatadabhāvasya tathātvāsaṃbhavāditi prathivyanyonyābhāvaprayuktatvabodhābhyupagame tu noktadoṣa iti bodhyam |
āmukteḥ saṃsāraḥ āṅo'tra maryādārthaḥ | kāladeśabhedena maryādā dvividhā | kālagataṃ maryādātvaṃ svasvottarakālobhayaprāgabhāvādhikaraṇayāvatkālabhavo yaḥ samabhivyāhṛtapadārthastada- nadhikaraṇatvam | muktipadaṃ tadadhikaraṇakālaparam, maryādāmaryādibhāvasaṃbandhaḥ pañcamyarthaḥ sa evāṅdyotyaḥ | muktimaryādakaḥ saṃsāra iti bodhaḥ | muktikālaprāgabhāvādinirūpitaṃ yanmuktikālatatprāgabhāvakālānyakālavṛttitvaviśiṣṭavyāpakatvaṃ saṃsāre tadbodhastvārthikaḥ | tādṛśakālavṛttitvasyārthikabodhanirvāhārthameva taduttarakālaprāgabhāvopādānaṃ kṛtam |tādṛśaprāgabhāvādhi- karaṇakāle samabhivyāhṛtakālaprāgabhāvānadhikaraṇatvamapi praveśyam, tena sāvitrī jaaidardhāstamite maṇḍale ā nakṣatradarśanāditi prayoge japaniṣṭhatathāvidhapragabhāvānirūpitavyāpakatvabhānānirvāhaḥ, anyathā tatparāgabhāvasyārdhāstamitapūrvakāle'pi sattvena tadavyāpakatvasya bādhena tadanirvāhāt | atra vastugatyā yastādṛśaviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭaḥ samabhivyāhṛtapadārthastadadhikaraṇabhedarūpaṃ tadanadhikaraṇatvaṃ samabhivyāhṛtapadārthabhedādhikaraṇabhedatvena rūpeṇocyate saṃbandhatvena veti na śattayānantyaṃ, maryāditvaṃ ca tathāvidhamaryādātvanirūpakatvamiti bodhayam | tārkikaratnākārakṛtastu samānakālikatvābhāvaḥ kālikamavyavadhānaṃ cāṅā pratyāyyate | tādṛgavyavadhānaṃ tatprāgabhāvādhikaraṇakṣaṇaprāgabhāvānadhikaraṇatve sati tatprāgabhāvādhikaraṇabhūto yaḥ kṣaṇastadvṛttitvam | prāghabhāvānabhyupagame tu tadadhikaraṇakṣaṇotpattikadhvaṃsapratiyogikṣaṇavṛttitvaṃ pratiyogitvaṃ pañcamyarthaḥ | tathā ca muktisamānakālikavābhāvavān muttavyavahitaḥ saṃsāra iti bodha ityāhuḥ | atra mate saṃsāre muttayuttarakālāvṛttitvasyārthika eva lābha iti bodhyam | na ca tadabhāne'pi na kṣatiriti vācyam | madhyāhnottaraṃ tatpūrva cādhyayane madhye'vasāne ā madhyāhnādadhīta iti prayogāpatteḥ | pratidinaṃ sāyaṃkālaparyantamadhyayanakartari ā sāyaṃkālādadhīte ityādi tu dhātostattadadhyayanaparatvena nirvāhyam | avyavahitottarakālaviśeṣo vottarakāletyanena vivakṣaṇīyaḥ | atra mate prātaḥkāle sāyaṃkālavyavahitapūrvakāle eva jape prātarārabhyā sāyaṃkālājjapatīti prayogāpattiriti tu draṣṭavyam | apare tu maryādātvaghaṭakaḥ prāgabhāva evāṅo'rthaḥ | muktipadaṃ muktikālaparaṃ pañcamyāḥ pratiyogitvaṃ vācyam | evaṃ ca muttayadhikaraṇakālapratiyogikaprāgabhāvalābhaḥ | tasya svasvottarakālāvṛttitva- sahitavyāpakatvasaṃbandhena saṃsāre'nvayaḥ ityāhuḥ | prayāgāt prabhṛtyā kāśyā vṛṣṭo deva ityādau deśarūpā maryādā | maryādātvaṃ ca svasvottaradeśayoganaṣṭhaṃ kāśīprayāgobhayāntarā- likayāvaddeśavṛttisamabhivyāhṛtapadārthavṛṣṭyādyanadhikaraṇatvam | uttaradeśāvṛttitādṛśasamabhivyāhṛta- padārthānadhikaraṇatvaṃ tanniṣṭhaṃ maryādātvamiti tu parasārthaḥ | uttaradeśe tadanadhikaraṇatvaniveśāt kāśītaḥ pūrvadeśe'pi vṛṣṭisadbhāve na tādṛśaprayogāpattiḥ | pāṭaliputrāt pūrvadeśe vṛṣṭisattve kāśīpāṭaliputrāntarāle tadasattve prayāgakāśīmadhyadeśe vṛṣṭisattvadaśāyāmuktaprayogasya prāmāṇikatve uttaraśabdenāvyavahitottaradeśo grāhyaḥ | vṛṣṭipadaṃ vā vṛṣiṭiviśeṣaparaṃ, prayāgapadottaraṃ pañcamī prabhṛtyarthayoge | tathā ca prayāgādikāśīmaryādakavṛṣṭikatā deva iti bodhaḥ | prayāgādhikaramakaprārambhikā kāśīprayāgobhayāntarālikayāvaddeśaniṣṭhaṃ yatkāśītaduttaradeśaliṣṭhābhāva- pratiyogyadhikaraṇatvaṃ tannirūpikā yā vṛṣṭistatkarteti yāvat | pañcamyarthamaryādātvaśarīre kāśyuttaradeśe samabhivyāhṛtapadārthānadhikaraṇatvāntarbhāvo'nanyagatikatayā svīkāryaḥ | evaṃ sati nānārthātve iṣṭāpattiriti bhāva | vastutastu ubhayavidhamaryādātvānugamakaṃ maryādātvaṃ dharmaviśeṣa- rūpam
| evaṃ ca na nānārthatvaprasaktiḥ, ata eva kāśīmaryādaketi bodhapradarśanamabhiyuktaiḥ kriyate | dharmaviśeṣarūpamaryādātvasyānaṅgīkāre tu pūrvoktarītiratrāpyanusaṃdheyā | prayāgādikāśīpaścimadeśa- nirūpitaṃ yatsubhikṣadeśaviśeṣāvṛttitvaviśiṣṭavyāpakatvaṃ tadbādhastvārthaḥ | tādṛśavyāpakatvasya śabdādanupasthiteḥ | kāśīpadottarapañcamyā vyāpakatvārthakatvāṅgīkāreṇa nirvāha iti tu na, vyāpakatvasya vyāpyaviśeṣāghaṭitasya durvacatvāt, tadghaṭakapadārtheṣu khaṇḍaśaḥ śaktisvīkāre gauravaṃ vyāpyasyānupasthitāviṣṭānirvāhaśca | vyāpyaviśeṣāntarbhāveṇa śaktau śatayānantyamityādikaṃ bodhyam | abhividhistatparyantamabhivyāptiḥ, tatpadenāvadhiḥ | sa cāṅā dyotyaḥ pañcamyarthaḥ | tatparyantābhivyāptiśca tadvayāpakatve sati taditaratra saṃbandhaḥ | sa ca kkacid vyāpakatvarūpaḥ kkacittādātmyasaṃyogādistātparyavaśādbhāsate | ktacidvayāpakatvamātraṃ yathā ā hareḥ prameyatvam | atra prameyatve hasivyāpakatve sati taditaravyāpakatvaṃ pratīyate | ā paramāṇoḥ pṛthivī, atra paramāmupadaṃ pārthivaparamāṇuparaṃ tādātyena tadavyāpakatvaṃ taditaratādātmyasaṃbandhaśca pratīyata ityādi bodhyam | ā sakalād brahma | atra vyāpakatvamātramarthaḥ | sakalavyāpakaṃ brahmeti bodhaḥ | abhividhirūpaḥ saṃbandho'pi dvividhaḥ kāliko daiśikaśca | ādyo kārtikyāścaitraṃ yāvacchītam | kārtikīprabhṛti caitrāntakālavyāpakaṃ śītamiti bodhaḥ, caitrapadaṃ caitrāntakālaparam | antyaṃ yathākāśītaḥ pāṭalīputraṃyāvadvṛṣṭo devaḥ | kāśīprabhṛtipāṭaliputrāntadeśavyāpakatvaṃ vṛṣṭau pratīyate | anayostātparyavaśāttadabhinnakālāvṛttitvaviśiṣṭaṃ tadabhinnadeśāvṛttitvaviśiṣṭaṃ ca vyāpakatvaṃ pratīyata ityuktamabhiyuktaiḥ | ayaṃ bhāvaḥ, vyāpakatvaṃ dvitīyārtho yāvacchabdo dyotakaḥ, vyutpatti- vaicitryāditarapadārthaviśiṣṭaprakṛtyartha caitrāntakāladenirūpitatvasaṃbandhena vyāpakatve'nvayaḥ | vyāpakatvaṃ ca tattadavyāpyaghaṭitamevopādeyamato nāprasiddham | athavā bhedapratiyogitāvacchedakatvama- bhāvaścetyetad dvayaṃ vyāpakatvaghaṭakaṃ vyāpyaghaṭitameva dvitīyārthaḥ | itarapadārthaviśiṣṭaprakṛtyarthā- syādheyatvasaṃbandhena bhede'nvayaḥ, taddhalādeva tadvayāpakatvalābhaḥ, athavā caitrapadasyeva kārtikīprabhṛticaitrāntakālārthakatvaṃ lakṣaṇayā, kārtikyā iti tātparyagrāhakaṃ dvitīyārthaḥ pūrvavat, kāśīta ityatrāpyevameva bodhyam, yāvacchabdasya vācakatvapakṣe tu ādheyatvaṃ dvitīyārthaḥ, tasya yāvacchabdārtha ghaṭakabhede'nvayaḥ, itarat pūrvavat |
athavā dvitīyārtho vyāpakatvaṃ taditarasaṃbandhaśca, taditaraśca pratyāsattyā kārtikīprabhṛtika- kāla eva saṃbandhaśca vyāpakatvarūpo bhāsate iti dik | maryādodāharaṇe'pi yadyapyevaṃrītyā vyāpakatvabodho nirvahati tathāpi maryādātvena rūpeṇa bodhanirvāha etādṛśakleśaṃ vinaiva saṃbhavatītyetadupekṣā tatra puraskṛtā | atra tvagatvā vyāpakatvabodha uktakrameṇāṅgīkāre kṛtaḥ | abhivyāptitātparyasattve kārtikīta ā caitryāḥ śītamityādyapi kālikābhividherudāhāraṇaṃ bodhyam | atrāpyuktaiva rītiranusartavyā | tatra yo dvitīyārthaḥ so'tra pañcamyartha āṅdyo ya iti viśeṣaḥ | apare tu kārtikītaścaitramityatrottarakālapūrvatvaṃ kāśītaḥ pāṭalīputramityatra pūrvadeśapaścimatvaṃ yāvacchabdārthaḥ | avadhitvaṃ dvitīyārthaḥ | tasya yāvacchabdārthe'nvayaḥ | uttarakālapūrvatvasya vyāpakatvasaṃbandhena śītabhavane'nvayaḥ | vyāpakatvaghaṭakottarakālapūrvatvādhikaraṇe samabhivyāhṛta- kārtikyādipūrvakālabhedo niveśyaḥ | dvitīye'pi pūrvadeśapaścimatvasya vyāpakatvasaṃbandhena vṛṣṭāvanvayaḥ | atrāpi vyāpakatvaghaṭakapūrvadeśapaścimatvādhikaraṇe kāśī paścimadeśabhedo niveśyaḥ |
etādṛśavyāpakatvabhāne kārtīkīpūrvakālādyavṛttitvamarthāt siddhaṃ bhavatītyalamativistareṇa | āṅghaṭitodāharaṇe'pīdaṃ sarva bodhyamityāhuḥ | kārtikyā iti pañcamī tu prabhṛtyarthayoganimittakā | ārabhyetyarthakamavyayaṃ prabhṛtīti | 'yataścādhvakāla ' iti vārtikapratyākhyānāya bhāṣye 'idamatra prayoktavyaṃ sanna prayumīti vacanasattvaṃ sūcitam | tena tadaprayo'pi pañcamīsiddhiḥ, avadhimādāyeti tadarthaḥ | avadhitvena svīkṛtyeti yāvat | ārabhyetyasya tadarthakatāgrahaṇamātrārthakatā ca | ādye pañcamī, dvitīye prātaḥkālamārabhyetyādau karmatvād dvitīyā | avadhitvaṃ ca bhavāt prabhṛti sevyo harirityatra samabhivyāhṛtapadārthamaraṇāntakālāpekṣamityanyatra vistaraḥ | avadhitvaṃ pañcamyarthaḥ | pradyumnaḥ kṛṣṇāt prati atra 'pratiḥ pratinidhipratidānayoḥ' iti prateḥ karmapravacanīyasaṃjñā | 'pratinidhipratidāne ca yasmāt' iti sūtreṇa pañcamī | yasmāditi ṣaṣṭhyarthe sūtre pañcamī, yatsaṃbandhī yatpratiyogī pratinidhiḥ pratidānaṃ vā karmapravacanīyayuktatadarthakāt pañcamīti sūtrārthaḥ, pratiyogitvarūpasaṃbandhaḥ sūtrasya pañcamyarthaḥ | priḥ sadṛśārthakaḥ | kṛṣṇāditi pañcamyāḥ pratiyogitvamarthaḥ, kṛṣṇapratiyogikasādṛśyavāniti bodhaḥ, tilebhyaḥ pratiyacchati māṣān | grahaṇapūrvakaṃ pratyarpaṇaṃ pratidānam, tatra gṛhītarṇajanyā dṛṣṭadhvaṃsaphalakadānātmakaṃ pratisamabivyāhṛtayacchaterarthaḥ | tilagrahaṇapūrvakaṃ māṣakarmakapratidānamiti bodhaḥ, karmatvā maḥ saṃbandhaḥ pañcamyarthaḥ, tasya dhātvarthaghaṭakagrahaṇe'nvayaḥ | tārkikaratnākarakṛtastu ṛṇatvadhvaṃsajanakatvaṃ praterarthaḥ, tasyāśrayatvenādāne'nvayaḥ, pañcamyā ādānajanyatvamartha ṛṇatānvayī ṛṇatvaṃ padārthāntaraṃ pāpaviśeṣo vā, tilādānajanyarṇatvadhvaṃsajanakamāṣadānakarmetyādyākāro bodha ityabhyupayanti sma | śatādbaddhaḥ 'akartṛryṛṇe pañcamī' iti sūtreṇātra pañcamī | kartṛbhinnaṃ hetubhūtaṃ yadṛṇaṃ tadvācakāt pañcamīti tadarthaḥ hetuhetumadbhāvo hetutvaṃ hetumattvaṃ vā pañcamyarthaḥ | śatahetukaṃ bandhanakarmeti bodhaḥ | ṛṇatvaṃ tu vastusannimita na tu tasya vācyakoṭāvantarbhāva ityāhuḥ | tasya vācyatvaṃ na yuktaṃ gauravāttadbodhasyānubhavādvetyāhuḥ | śatena bandhita iti pratyudāharaṇe hetumaṇṇya tāt karmaṇi ktaḥ | śatamiha ṛṇaṃ prayojakakartṛ 'tatprayojakaḥ ' iti hetusaṃjñaṃ ca | jāḍyādvaddha ityatra 'vibhāṣā guṇe ' iti vaikalpikī pañcamī | pakṣe hetutṛtīyā | jāḍyahetukabandhanakarmeti bodhaḥ, dhūmādagnimānityādo jñānaniṣṭhajanyajanakabhāvasya viṣaye āropāt pañcamītyāhurmañjūṣākṛtaḥ | etatpakṣe dhūmahetuko vahniriti bodhaḥ | etena bhūmādervahnayādyajanakatve'pi pañcamyanupapattinirastā | jñāpakasyāpi hetupadena grahaṇamiti tu na yuktam | 'heturnā kāraṇaṃ bījam' iti kośena hetupadasya kāraṇārthakatvaniścayāt | loke tatra prayogābhāvācca | dhūmapadaṃ dhūmajñānaparam | agnimatpadaṃ svajñānaviṣaye lākṣaṇikamiti laghuśekhare |
etanmate dhūmajñānahetukavahnimajjñānaviṣayaparvata iti bodhaḥ | maṇikṛtastu-vahnipadaṃ vahnijñānalākṣaṇikaṃ, dhūmapadaṃ dhūmajñānalākṣaṇikaṃ hetutvaṃ janakatvarūpaṃ pañcamyarthaḥ | prakṛtyartha dhūmajñānāndhitasya tasya nirūpakatāsaṃbandhena vahnipadārthahnijñāne'nvayaḥ | matuvartho viṣayatāvān iṣayatā vā vahnipadalakṣaṇā vā āśrayatvasaṃbandhī matuvarthaḥ | athavā vahnipadaṃ jñāyamānavahnilākṣaṇikaṃ matuvarthaḥ saṃyogīti svīcakuḥ | anayormañjūṣāśekharoktayoḥ pakṣayomadhye jñānagatajanyajanakabhāvasya viṣaya āropa ityeva pakṣaḥ samyagiti pratibhāti | dīdhitikārādayastu jñānajñāpyatvaṃ pañcamyartho dhūmapadaṃ mukhyārthakameva | ata evodeharaṇādivākye na dhūmasyaiva gamakatā-
prayojakavyāptyādikaṃ pradarśyate | evaṃ ca vahnipadamapi mukhyārthakameva | ata eva jijñāsitābhidhānaṃ bhavati yato vahnireva jijñāsito na tu vahnijñānamitāhurityalamativistareṇa | jñānaṃ ca pramātmakameva pañcamyādyarthaghaṭakaṃ tena vyāptyādibhramaviṣaye vahnayādiviṅgakadhūmānumatyutpattāvapi na vahninā vahnervā dhūmavāniti prayogāpattiḥ | pramātvaṃ ca yāvaddhūmabhinnatvaṃ vahnirdhūmavyāpya iti bhramasyāpi vahnityādyaṃśe pramātvamādāya noktaprayogāpattitādavasthyamiti bodhyam | dhūmāderaguṇatve'pi 'vibhāṣā' iti yogavibhāgāt pañcamī | ata eva nāsti ghaṭo'nupalabdherityatra svīliṅgatve'pi pañcamītyāhuḥ | pare tu 'bāhulakaṃ prakṛtestanudṛṣṭeḥ' iti 'uṇādayo bahulam' iti sūtrasthavārtikaprayogādvaśeṣaṇadvayaṃ prāyikamityāhuḥ | jñāpyajñāpakabhāvo'nvayitāvacchedaka- vahnitvadhūmatvādirūpeṇa pratīyata ityabhyupagamāt parvato vahnimān dravyavād dravyatvena vetyādirna prayogaḥ | anyathā tu prameyatvādinā vahnarapi dravyatvajñāpyatvāt, vahnitvena rūpeṇa viśiṣṭadravyatva- jñāpitatvāccoktaprayogāpattirdurvāreva | evaṃ jñāpyavahnimattvāderyadanvayitāvacchedakaṃ parvatatvādi tadavacchinnāṃśe eva jñāpyatvādikaṃ bhāsata iti niyamāṅgīkārāt parvato vahnimān mahānasatvānmahānasatvena vetyādernāpattiḥ | atha parvate vahnimān na dravyatvānnāpi mahānasatvādityādiprayogānupapattiḥ, sarvamate'pi vahnāvapi prameyatvādyavacchinnadravyatvanirūpita- jñāpyatvasya mahānase mahānasatvajñāpyatvasya ca sattvena tadabhāvabodhanasya tatprayuktasya dravyatvādi- jñānajñāpyatvasya parvate mahānasatvajñānajñāpyatvasya cāprasiddhayā tadabhāvabodhanāsaṃbhavācceti cenna | abhāvabodhane vāstavapratiryogiprasiddheranapekṣaṇāt, api tu pratiyogijñānamātrasya | taccāroparūpaṃ prakṛte'pi saṃbhavati, dravyatvajñānajñāpyatve vahnitvāvacchinnatvasyāropaḥ, parvatatvāvacchinnāṃśe mahānasatvasyetyādikaṃ bodhyam | ata eva nāntarikṣe cinvītetyādivākyādantarikṣādhikaraṇacayana- rūpakriyābhāvabodhanirvāhaḥ, ata eva ca yadgehādhikaraṇakaḥ kṣīparāko'prasiddhaḥ pākamātraṃ vāprasiddhaṃ caitrasya suṣuptidaśāyāṃ tādṛśagehatātparyakasya atra gehe kṣīraṃ na pacati caitra ityādernañghaṭitavākyasya nāyogyatā, tādṛśagehādhikaraṇakakṣīrapākānukūlakṛteravāstavatve'pyuktarītyā tadabhāvasya caitrasamabhi- vyāhṛtakārakaviśiṣṭapākābhāvasya ca bodhanasaṃbhavāt, tādṛśagehādhikaraṇakatvābhāvasyapākakriyāyāṃ bodhanasaṃbhave'pi tathāvidhābhāvaviśiṣṭapākānukūlavartamānakālikakṛteścaitre bādhitāyā bodhayitu- maśakyatvāt, etādṛśavākyānāmandha ākāśaṃ na paśyatītivadapramāṇateti cet, parvato vahnimān na dravyatvādityādivākyasyāpyapramāṇatorīkartavyā, navyatākrikāḥ punarevamuktaprayogopapattimāhuḥ | parvato vahnimān dhūmād dhūmena vetyādau pañcamyāderjñānaprayojyatvaṃ jñānajanyatāvacchedakatvarūpaṃ samabhi- vyāhṛtaparvatādiviśeṣyatānirūpitaṃ vidheyatve ca viśakalitamaryādvayam, dhūmāderviṣayatvasaṃbandhena jñāne'nvayaḥ, dhūmajñānaprayojyatvasya vidheyatve tasya vahnayādau, evaṃ coktasthale tathāvidhavidheyatve dravyatvamahānasatvajñānaprayojyatvābhāvaḥ pratyāyyate, sa cāvādhita eva, tatprayojyatvaṃ ca prameyatvādya- vacchinnavidheyatāyāṃ mahānasaniṣṭhaśeṣyatānirūpitavidheyatāyāṃ ca prasiddham, nanu parvato vahnimān na dhūmādityasya prayogasyāpattiḥ, dhūmajñānajanyatānavacchedikāyāstattadvahnitvāvacchinnāyāḥ parvatatvā- vacchinnaviśeṣyatānirūpitavidheyatāyā vahnāvapi sattvāditi cenna, tathāvidheyatātvāvacchedena dhūmādijñānaprayojyatvābhāvānvayabodhopagamāt, vidheyatāyāḥ sādhyatāvacchedakasaṃbandhāvacchinnavidheyatā-
tvena pañcamyādyarthatvamaṅgīkartavyam | tena jñānavānātmatvādityatra viṣayatāsaṃbandhāvacchinnapakṣaniṣṭha- viśeṣyatānirūpitajñānavṛttividheyatāyāṃ dravyatvajñānaprayojyatvasattvenāvacchedakāvacchedena tadabhāva- bodhanā'yogyatvāt,'na dravyatvā'dityādiprayogānupapattirnirastā | pañcamyādyarthaghaṭake jñāne hetutāvacchedakatattatsaṃbandhāvacchinnaprakārakatvaṃ praveśyam | tena samavāyena hetutvatātparyeṇāyaṃ vahnimān tadrūpānna dhūmāditi prayogasya nirvāha iti | pṛthagrāmādrāmeṇa rāmaṃ vā atra 'pṛthagvinā ' ityādinā vibhaktitrayaṃ vidhīyate | athavā tena tṛtīyāpañcamyau vidhīyete, dvitīyā tu ṛte vātamityatreva 'tato'nvayatrāpi dṛśyate' iti bhavati | atra pañcamyādeḥ pratiyogitvamarthaḥ | pṛthaktvaṃ guṇa iti mate'vadhitvamarthaḥ | vastutastu pṛthaktvaṃ na guṇaḥ, ki tu bheda eva | ata eva rūpaṃ rasāt pṛthagiti rūpādāvapi pṛthaktvapratyayo bhavati | na hi guṇe guṇaḥ saṃbhavati | na cāśrayagataṃ tatsāmānādhikaraṇyena rūpādau bhāsata iti vācyam | paṭāt pṛthagghaṭa ityatreva sātrātsaṃbandhenaitadbhānanyānubhavasiddhatvāt | tadrūpaṃ tadrūpāt pṛthagiti pratītyāpatteśca, tadrūpāvadhika- pṛthaktvasya tadāśraye sattvāt | api ca rūpatvaṃ rasatvāt pṛthagityasyānāpatteśca, tadāśraye pṛthaktvasya guṇarūpasaṃbhavādityanyatra vistāraḥ |
stokena stokādvāmuktaḥ atra karaṇaṃ karaṇatvaṃ vā vibhattayarthaḥ | stokaśabdasyālpatvarūpa- dharmaparatayā dravyāsāmānādhikaraṇyena sarvanāmaparāmarśāyogyatayā cāsattvarūpatvaṃ cādyupasthitasamu- ccayādivat | stokādiśabdopasthitadharmasya sarvanāmaparāmarśāyogyatā bodhyā viṅgādyaviśeṣyatvarūpa- mapyasattvabhūtatvaṃ tasya stokādiśabdāḥ śuklādiśabdavaddharmamātraparā api | dharmasya kāraṇatvamāśraya- dvārā 'karaṇe ca stokā-' iti sūtrabalādidṛśe'rthe eva sādhutā | tena hyatra tṛtīyāpañcamyāvanu- śiṣyete | spaṣṭaṃ caitat 'aluguttara-' iti sūtre kaiyaṭe | grāmasya dūrādadūreṇa vā gatam | atra 'dūrāntikārthebhyo dvitīyā ca' iti prātipadikārthamātre pañcamyādayo vidhīyante | vyākhyānād dūraśabdo dūradeśavṛttiparaḥ | grāmasaṃbandhidūradeśavṛttigamanamiti bodhaḥ | evaṃ grāmasyāntikamityā- dāvapi bodhyam | 'dūrāntikārthebhyaḥ saptamyadhikaraṇe ca' ityanena saptamī ceti catasro vibhaktayaḥ prātipadikārthe bhavanti | ki ca 'saptamyadhikaraṇe ca' ityatra 'dūrāntikārthebhyaḥ' iti sūtrasyānu- vṛttirii vibhaktitrayopetasyānuvartanādadhikaraṇe ebhyo vibhakticatuṣṭayaṃ vidhīyate iti jñeyam | duraḥ panthā ityatra tu na dvitīyādipravṛttiḥ | 'asattvavacanasya' ityasyānuvṛttyā dravyāsamānādhikaraṇebhya eva dūrāntikārthakebhyastādvidhānāt, 'dūrādāvasathāt mūtraṃ dūrāt pādāvasecanam' ityatra yatheti viśavam |
iti pañcamyarthavicāraḥ |
'ṣaṣṭhī śeṣe' iti sūtreṇa kārakaprātipadikārthabhinnasvasvāmabhāvādirūpasaṃbandhe ṣaṣṭhī vidhīyate, sa ca saṃbandhaḥ saṃbandhatvena, tattadrūpeṇa ca tadarthaḥ tātparyabalāt kkacittattadrūpeṇa kkacitsaṃbandhatvena pratīyate, saṃbandhatvena kriyākārakabhāvaśca tadarthaḥ, ata eva samānakālikatvādyanekasaṃbandhavatyapi vāsasi nedaṃ caitrasyeti prayoga upapadyate, anyathā tatra caitrasaṃbandhābhāvena tadvadbhedābhāvena ca tadanupapattiḥ spaṣṭaiva | tattadrūpeṇa saṃbandhasya ṣaṣṭhyārthatve tu svatvādimattvāvacchinnabhedasya cānyadīyavastrādau sattvānnoktaprayogānupapattiḥ | tattadrupeṇa vācyatvādevaikaśataṃ ṣaṣṭhyarthā iti 'ṣaṣṭhī
sthāne ' iti sūthasthabhāṣyamupapadyate | saṃbandhatvenaiva tasya ṣaṣṭhyarthatve tu saṃbandhatvāvacchinna- śakteraikyāttadanupapattiḥ | dvitīyāditaḥ kriyākārakabhāvasyānubhavānurodhāt karmatvakriyātvādyātmaka- viśeṣarūpeṇaiva bodhaḥ | mātuḥ smaratītyādau ca kriyākārakabhāvasya viṣaśerūpeṇa bodhābhyupagame bādhakadvitīyādyāpattyā saṃbandhatvenaiva tasya bodha upeyata iti navyāḥ śābdikāstārkikāśca miśrā- dikāḥ | prāñcastvatrāpi kriyākārakabhāvamūlakaṃ viśeṣaṇatvarūpaṃ, viṣayatvaṃ nimittatvaṃ vā saṃbandhatvena, viśeṣarūpeṇa vā pratīyate | ata eva 
'saṃbandhaḥ kārakebhyo'nyaḥ kriyākārakapūrvakaḥ |
śrutāyāmaśrutāyāṃ vā kriyāyāmabhidhīyate ||' 
ityanena saṃbandhasya kriyākārakabhāvamūlakatvamabhyadhāyi bhartaharibhiḥ | sarvatraiva hi saṃbandhasta- nmūlakaḥ | rājñaḥ pūruṣa ityatra svasvāmibhāvasaṃbandhaḥ kriyākārakabhāvapūrvakaḥ | 'yataḥ rājā pūruṣāya dravyaṃ dadāti tato'sau purūṣasya svāmībhavati' iti bhāṣyādau spaṣṭam | kriyā hi siddhasvābhāvānāṃ dravyāṇāṃ kriyāṃ vinā parasparasaṃbandhamabhajatāṃ niḥśrayaṇīva saṃbandhaṃ karotīti spaṣṭaṃ bhāṣyakaiyaṭādau | bhātuḥ smaratītyādau kriyā śrutā, rājñaḥ puruṣa ityādau tvaśrutā dānādikriyānumīyate ityāhuḥ | vistarastu mañjūṣāto'nusaṃdheyaḥ | svasvāmibhāvadisaṃbandhasya dviniṣṭhatve'pi viśeṣaṇavācakādeva rājādiśabdāt ṣaṣṭhī, na tu purūṣādiśabdāt, prakṛtyartha pri pratyayārthasya prādhānyānurodhāt, tathā hi rājādiḥ saṃbandhe viśeṣaṇaṃ, sa ca purūṣe viśeṣaṇam, rājasaṃbandho purūṣa iti bodhāt | ṣaṣṭhyarthasaṃbandhasya saṃsargatayaiva bhānāmiti bhāṣyamate'pi prakāratāyāḥ sāṃsargikaviṣayatāviśeṣyato- bhayanirūpyatayā prakāratānirūpakaviṣayatāśālitvarūpaprādhānyamakṣatameva | na hīdaṃ purūṣādiśabdāt ṣaṣṭhyāṃ satyāṃ nirvahati | kiṃ ca viśeṣyapurūṣādau kārakaprātipadikārthānyataratvasyāvaśyakatve nāśeṣatvānna tadvācakāt ṣaṣṭhītyāhuḥ | pare tu śabdaśaktisvabhāvādviśeṣaṇa eva saṃbandha udbhūtatayā pratīyata iti ṣaṣṭhī | taduktaṃ haribhiḥ 
'dviṣo'pyasau parārthatvād guṇeṣu vyatiricyate |
tatrābhidhīyamānaśca 'pradhāne'pyupayujyate ||' iti |
parārthatvād - guṇānāṃ paraṃ prati viśeṣaṇatayā vivakṣitatvāt | ayaṃ bhāvaḥ prakāratāyāabhedasya saṃbandhatvānaṅgīkartṛnaye nīlo ghaṭa ityādau vyabhicāra iti bhedasāhityānusaraṇam | bhedasahitāyāḥ saṃbandhaniyatatayā viśeṣaṇe eva tadākāṅkṣodayena tatra eva saṃbandha udbhūtatayā pratīyata iti tatraiva ṣaṣṭhī padamādadhāti | viśeṣyasya tu padāntarāsaṃnidhāne svaniṣṭhatvādeva na viśeṣyatāniyāmakasaṃbandhākāṅkṣā | svaniṣṭhatvaṃ ca svārthapravṛttinimittanirūpitā- viśeṣyatayā bhāsamānatvaṃ, tacchālitvaṃ vā | apradhāne ṣaṣṭhyādināṃśataḥ pratītipathāvatīrṇaḥ saṃbandhaḥ prādhānopayogyapi bhavati, tasya dviniṣṭhatvāt | rājādipadasāhitye pratīyamānarājādinirūpita- viśeṣyatāyā upakārako bhavatīti yāvat | ṣaṣṭhyāntarājapadasattve eva tadarthanirūpitaviśeṣyatā- śālī, tāda-śaviśeṣyatāvattvena bodhaviṣayo vā bhavati, rājña ityādestu puruṣādipadāsattve'pi adhyāha-tasaṃbandhisāmānyanirūpitaviśeṣaṇatvapratitiriti viśeṣaḥ | rājña ityetāvatā svāmitvamava- gamyamānamanyathānupapattyā puruṣe svatvaṃ gamayati | ataḥ saṃbandhasya bahiraṅgatvāt puruṣādantaraṅgatvāt prathamaiva, ata eva svatvasvāmitvādisamūhasyaiva saṃbandhatayā tasyaiva vibhattayarthatvaṃ,
na svatvādimātrasya, dviniṣṭhatvābhāvāt | svatvasya nirūpakatayā svāmivṛttitvena tattvamanapavādamiti tu na vācyam, nirūpakatvasya vṛttyaniyamākatayā svāminastadvattvāsaṃbhavāt, spaṣṭaṃ cedaṃ sarva prakṛtasūtrabhāṣyādāviti prāhuḥ |
puruṣasya viśeṣaṇatvavivakṣāyāṃ tato ṣaṣṭhī bhavatyevāṅgānāṃ rājetivat, śeṣapadasyāpradhānārtha- katvamabhyupagamya puruṣapadāt ṣaṣṭhīvāraṇaṃ tu na samyak, śuklaḥ paṭa ityādau śuklādestatprasaṅgāt | bhedasaṃbandhāvacchinnaviśeṣaṇatārūpaprādhānyapuraskāre tu nāyaṃ doṣa iti pratibhāti | atredaṃ bodhyam saṃyogasaṃbandhenādhikaraṇakārakasya saṃbandhatvena na vivakṣā, ata eva 'tadasyāstyasmin-' iti sūtre 'vṛkṣavān parvata' ityādiprayogasiddhaye saptamyupāttā, 'gomān devadatta' ityādyartha ca ṣaṣṭhīti tatsūtre bhāṣye uktam | nanvadhi brahmadatte pañcālā ityatrādhikaraṇasaptamī 'ādhṛtāste tatra bhavanti' ityādinā granthena 'yasmādadhikam-' iti bhāṣye svīkṛtā, tadvadatrāpi gāvaḥ santyasminniti saptamī bhaviṣyatīti kimartha ṣaṣṭhyupādānamiti cenna | svasvāmibhāvavivakṣāyāmapi gomānityādiprayogasiddhaye tadupādānāditi mañjūṣāyāṃ vistaraḥ | annasya hetorvasati | 'ṣaṣṭhī hetuprayoge' iti sūtreṇātra ṣaṣṭhī, hetuśabdaprayoge tasmāttatsamānādhikaraṇācca ṣaṣṭhīti tadarthaḥ | 'hetoḥ' itṣuktau hetuśabdādeva syānna tu viśeṣyānnavācakāditi tathā nāsūtri | hetuhetumadbhāvaḥ saṃbandhaḥ ṣaṣṭhyarthaḥ | annahetuko vāsa iti bodhaḥ | hetuśabdo'traiva tātparyagrāhakaḥ | evamannena hetunā vasatītyādāvapi bodhyam | vibhaktiḥ prayojanaṃ hetuṃ ca yathāyathamabhidhatte | prayojanatvādiprakārako bodha ityanye |
kṛṣṇasya kṛtiḥ, jagataḥ kartā kṛṣṇaḥ, atra 'kartṛkarmaṇoḥkṛti' iti ṣaṣṭhī | tadarthaścakartā karma | kṛṣṇakartṛkā kṛtiḥ, jagatkarmakakṛtyāśraya iti bodhaḥ | tārkikamate kartṛtvaṃ karmatvamarthaḥ | prakṛtyarthasya tatrādheyatvasaṃbandhenānvayaḥ | kartṛtvādinirūpakatāsaṃbandhena dhātvarthe'nvayaḥ | kṛṣṇa- vṛttikartṛtānirūpakakṛtiḥ jagadvṛttiviṣayatārūpakarmatānirūpakakṛtyāśraya iti bodhaḥ | idamanuśāsanaṃ śaktigrāhakameva na tu lakṣaṇāgrāhakam | lakṣaṇāgrāhakatvasya kutrāpyanuśāsane'bhāvāditi mañjūṣādau vistaraḥ | kṛtapūrvī kaṭamityādau tu na kaṭādipadādanena sūtreṇa ṣaṣṭhī | kṛdgrahaṇasāmarthyana kṛdantamātraśaktigrahaprayojyopasthitiviṣayakriyākartṛkarmaṇoreva ṣaṣṭhīvidhānāt | anyathā tiṅyoge 'na loka-' iti niṣedhena kṛdyoge eva ṣaṣṭhīsiddhau tadvaiyarthya spaṣṭameveti kaiyaṭādibhiḥ prapañcitam | prakṛte ca na kṛdantamātraśaktigrahaḥ kriyopasthitiprayojako'pi tu taddhitāntaśaktigrahaḥ, tathā hi kṛtetiktapratyayo na karmaṇi, kaṭamiti dvitīyānāpatteḥ, kiṃ tu karmaṇo'vivakṣayākarmakatvādbhāve ktaḥ | sa ca katapūrvī adhītītyādivṛttiviṣaya eva | taduktaṃ bhāṣye 'yadā vākyaṃ na tadā pratyayaḥ, yadā pratyayaḥ sāmānyena tadā vṛttiḥ' iti | 'na tadā pratyayaḥ' ityasya na tadā bhāve pratyayaḥ, sakarmakatvādityarthaḥ | 'yadā pratyayaḥ' bhāve iti śeṣaḥ, sarva vākyamiti nyāyena, 'tadā vṛttiḥ' ityasya tadā vṛttirevetyarthaḥ | sā ca na karmaktāntenetyāha 'sāmānyena' iti | upadaśitarītyā karmarahitārthakadhātuprakṛtikaktāntenetyarthaḥ | evaṃ caitādṛśaktāntasya vākye na prayoga iti spaṣṭamevoktam | yo'sau kṛtakaṭayorabhisaṃbandhaḥ sāmānādhikaraṇyarūpaḥ, sa utpanne taddhite nivartate iti ca bhāṣye uktam | tenākṛte taddhite kṛtaḥ kaṭa ityeva bhavatīti dhvanitam | ata eva kaṭaṃ kriyate iti na | tataḥ pūrva kṛtamanenetyarthe 'pūrvādiniḥ' ityanuvartamāne 'sapūrvācca' iti sūtreṇa taddhite kṛte tataḥ phalasyāśrayākāṅkṣāyāṃ kaṭādirūpakarmaviśeṣānvayaḥ | na caivamasāmarthyāttaddhito na
syāditi vācyam, tadutpattisamaye'sāmarthyavirahāt | evaṃ ca taddhitāntaśaktigrahaprayojyaiva prakṛtikriyeti spaṣṭam | kārakānvayo guṇībhūtākriyāyāmapīti spaṣṭameva |
'avigrahā gatādisthā yathā grāmādikarmabhiḥ |
kriyāsaṃbadhyate tadvat kṛtapūrvādiṣu sthitiḥ ||'
iti harikārikāyām | 'avigrahā' - kartṛviśeṣaṇatayaivopasthitiviṣayībhūtā | prakārāntaramapi tairabhyadhāyi 
'viśaṣakarmasaṃbandhe virbhakte'pi kṛtādibhiḥ |
viśeṣanirapekṣo'nyaḥ kṛtaśabdaḥ pravartate ||
akarmakatve satyevaṃ ktānte bhāvābhidhāyini |
tataḥ kriyāvatā kartrā yogo bhavati karmaṇām ||' iti |
nirbhāgaḥ - prāptiḥ, sā ca vākye ityavagantavyam | taṇḍulānāṃ pācakatara ityatra tu na ṣaṣṭhyanupapattiḥ | tatra pācaka ityasyāpi pṛthak prayogeṇa kṛdantamātraśaktijñānasyāpi kriyopasthitiprayojakatvāt | kṛtītyanena taddhitādhikyamātravyāvṛttyaṅgīkāre tūktabhāṣyarītyeṣṭasya
taṇḍulānāmityādiprayogasyānupapattirūpadoṣaḥ sphuṭa eva | taṇḍulān pācakatara iti pratyudāharaṇamanupanyasya kṛtapūrvītyādikliṣṭapratyudārahaṇopanyāsāt taṇḍulānāṃ pācakatara ityatra bhāṣyakṛtaḥ ṣaṣṭhīṣṭaiveti pratīyate | 'na loka-' iti niṣṭhāyoge neti niṣedhasya tu nātra prāptiḥ, niṣṭhāntamātraśaktigrahaprayojyopasthitiviṣayakriyānvayinoreva kartṛkarmaṇostena ṣaṣṭhīniṣedhāt | atra ca kadgrahaṇametatpratyudāharaṇaparaṃ bhāṣyaṃ ca mānyamityāhuḥ | etādṛśārthakalpanayā prakṛte niṣedhāprasaktyā kṛdgrahaṇasārthakyopapādanam | na caivaṃ kṛdgrahaṇaveyarthyam, kartṛvyapūrvī kaṭaṃ paktavyapūrvī ādanabhityādereva pratyudāharaṇasya saṃbhavāt |tatrāpyuktarītyā bhāve tavyapratyayasaṃbhavāt | na ca bhāṣye etatpratyudāraṇāttathākalpanā neti vācyam | prayojanadaurlabhyasya tathāpyanapāyādityapare āhuḥ | prayojanavirahe'pi bhāṣyasthatathāvidhodārahaṇānyathānupapattyā tathārthakalpaneti yuktamiti bhāti | kaṭaṃ kṛtavatputra ityādau tu niṣedhapravṛttirbhavatyeva, kriyāyā niṣṭhāntenopasthāpanāt, uktarītyā kartavyapūrvī kadabhityapi yadi pramitaṃ tarhi tadapi pratyudāharaṇaṃ bhavatu nāma | anye tu kṛtapūrvī kaṭamiti pratyudāharaṇaparabhāṣyaprāmāṇyādekārthībhāvānāpannaniṣṭhādiyoge niṣedho'bhyupeyate | ekārthībhāvānāpannatvaṃ cetarāśritaikārthībhāvānāśrayatvam, anyathāsaṃbhavāpatteḥ | evaṃ ca na prakṛte tena niṣedhaprasaktiḥ | etādṛśarītyā niṣedhasaṃkocaprayojanaṃ tu gata ivācarati gatati, ācārakkabantāllaṭ, gramasya gatatītyatra karmaṇi ṣaṣṭhīsiddhiḥ | lādeśayoge na iti niṣedhastu nātra śaṅkyaḥ | lādiprakṛtyupasthitimukhyaviśeṣyakriyākartṛkarmaṇoreva niṣedhāṅgīkārāt | evamācarakkabantagataśabdāṇṇvuli pacādyaci ca grāmasya gatako, grāmasya gata ityatra ca ṣaṣṭhīsiddhistatphalaṃ, ṇvulādiprayuktaṣaṣṭhīprāptiriti tu na śaṅkyam | niṣedhasya vidhyunmūlana- svabhāvatvena prāvaltyat | etacca sarva kṛtapūrvī kaṭamityādi niṣṭhāvaṭitabhāṣyodāharaṇādunnīyata ityāhuḥ | tarkaratnākare tu pakkapūrvī sūpān kṛtapūrvī kaṭamityādau pākādyanukūlabhāvanāvāvānini- pratyayasyaivārthaḥ, ktāntastu nirarthakaḥ, kṛtītyupādānasāmarthyāt | anyathā ktena karmam̐ṇo'bhihitatvenānabhihitādhikārīyatvena ṣaṣṭhyaprāptyā tadvaiyarthyasya spaṣṭatvāt | dvitīyāprasaktyā sūpānityādiprayogānupapatteśca | kṛtapūrvī kaṭamityatra kṛñaḥ sakarmakatayā bhāve ktasya daurlabhyam, anyathā gamyate grāmamityasyāpatteḥ | na caivaṃ ktāntaprayogavaiyarthyam, taddhitasyoktarthe
tātparyagrāhakatayā sārthakyāt | evaṃ ca karmaṇo'nabhidhānena ṣaṣṭhīprasaktiriti kṛdgrahaṇaṃ sārthakam, dvitīyāsiddhiśca, niṣṭhāprayuktaniṣedhaprasaktiśca na, pratyāsattyā niṣṭhāprakṛtyarthakriyānvayinoreva ṣaṣṭhīniṣedhādityuktam, tanmahābhāṣyānanuguṇamiti spaṣṭaṃ tadvidām | sūpādiśabdād dvitīyānupapattiśca, uktavyāpārasya dhātvarthatvābhāvena dhātvarthatāvacchedaphalaśālitvābhāvāt | bhāve ktapratyayastu prāgupapādita eva, bhāṣyādyanusāreṇātiprasaṅgo'pi vārita evetyalamanalpajalpanena | kaṭakarmakātītakālavṛttyutpattyanukūlavyāpārāśraya iti kṛtapūrvītyādivākyajanyo bodhaḥ | 'tadarham' iti nirdeśādanityo'yaṃ vidhiḥ | tena 'dhāyairāmodamuttamam' iti bhaṭṭiprayogopapattirityādyanyatra vistaraḥ | jagat kurvan, kurvāṇa ityādau na ṣaṣṭhī | 'na loka-' iti niṣedhāditi ṣaṣṭhyarthanirṇayaḥ |
adhītī vyākaraṇe, atra 'ktasyenaviṣaya ' iti saptamī | innanto viṣayo vartanabhūmiryasyetyarthaḥ | yadvā viṣayaśabdena prakṛtiḥ samarpyate | ino viṣaya iti ṣaṣṭhītatpuruṣo'tra pakṣe | atrāpi kṛtapūrvītivat bhāve ktaḥ | 'iṣṭādibhyaśca' iti kartari iniḥ | saptamyarthaḥ karmatvādiḥ | vyākaraṇakarmakādhyayamakarteti bodhaḥ | 'carmaṇi dvīpinaṃ hanti dantayorhanti kuñjaram' ityādau 'nimittāt karmayoge' iti saptamī | nimittaṃ - phalaṃ tadvācakāt saptamī bhavati, saṃyogasamavāyānyatararūpakarmasaṃbandhe satīti tadarthaḥ | atra karmasaṃbandhaḥ svarūpasannimitaṃ, na tu tasya saptamīvācyakoṭāvantarbhāvaḥ | itthaṃ ca phalaṃ saptamyarthaḥ, tasya svaniṣṭhaphalatānirūpakatvādisaṃbandhena 'dvopyādikarmakahananakriyāyāmanvayaḥ, tatra dvīpicarmaṇormṛgāṇḍakośayośca samavāya, itarayoḥ saṃyogaḥ | carmādīnāṃ hananaphalatvaṃ ca svaviṣayakecchāprayojakecchāviṣayatvam | hananecchāprajikecchā carmādilābheccheti carmādau tādṛśaphalatvamakṣatam | tārkikanaye phalatvaṃ, nibhittatvaṃ, tādarthya vā saptamyarthaḥ | lāghavādanuśāsanavirodhābhāvāccedameva yuktamityāhuḥ | brahmaṇeṣvadhīyāneṣu gataḥ, goṣu duhyamānāsu gataḥ, atra 'yasya ca bhāvena bhāvalakṣaṇam' iti saptamī | yasya bhāvena - kriyāyā kriyāntaraṃ lakṣyate - jñāyate tataḥ - tadvācakāt saptamīti tadarthaḥ | brāhmaṇādhīyānādi- padayorubhayorapi kriyāśrayavācakatvādubhābhyāmapi saptamī | nirjñātadeśakālā kriya'nirjñātadeśakālāyāḥ kriyāyā deśakālajñāpaladvārā lakṣaṇam | itthaṃ ca narjñātadeśādikakriyāśrayakartṛkarmavācakā jñāpyajñāpakabhāvasaṃbandhārthikā ṣaṣṭhyapavādikeyaṃ saptamī bhavati | atra kriyāpadena dhātvarthamātramiti karmaṇo'pi tadāśrayatvamavirūddham | tatra kācit kriyā svādhikaraṇakālena kriyāntaraṃ paricchinatti, yathoktodāharaṇayoḥ | kācit svādhikaraṇakālāvyavahitapūrvakālena yathā goṣu dhokṣyamāṇāsu gata ityādau | kācit svādhikaraṇakālāvyavahitottarakālena yathā goṣu dugdhāsu gata ityatra | jñāpyajñāpakabhāvaśca tattabdhabdabodhyatvena vivakṣita evaitacchāstrapravṛttau nimittaṃ, na tu mānāntareṇa grahaṇāpekṣeti na bhūyodarśanāpekṣā | ata evoktasthale saptamyupapattiḥ | anyathodite āditye tamo naṣṭamityādāveva syānnatvatra | kadācid godohanapuruṣaviśeṣakartṛkagamanayorekakālādau saṃbandho na tu miyameneti bodhyam | sadeśodāharaṇaṃ tu sati guṇe dravyatvamastītyādau | atrādhīyānabrahmaṇāderjñāpakatvaṃ saptamyā pratīyamānaṃ svaviśeṣaṇakriyākālādidvārake paryavasyati | goṣu dhokṣyamāṇāsvityādau bhaviṣyadgodo- gamāderapi buddhayupārūḍhasyoktaṃ jñāpakatvamaviruddhamiti bodhyam | mānāntarasiddhasyārthasya kāla-
viśeṣasaṃbandhādipratipipādayiṣayā yatra prayogastatraivedaṃ sūtraṃ pravartate, sūtrabhāṣyasvarasāt | evaṃ ca rāhūparāge strāyādityatra rāhūparāgapadena svārthāśrayakālo lakṣyate, adhikaraṇasaptamyeva | evaṃ vivāhe nāndīmukhaṃ kuryādityatra vivāhapadena tatprākkālaḥ, saptamī tu rāhūparāgapadavadityanyatra vistaraḥ | anye tūktasthale gamanādikriyādohanādisamānakālikatvena bodhyatayābhipreteti samānakālikatvaṃ saptamyarthaḥ | tato duhyādyupasthāpitadohanādernirūpakatayānvayena dohanasamānakālikatvasya gamanādau lābha iti na saṃbhavaduktikaṃ, dohanādikriyāyāḥ kṛdarthaviśeṣaṇatāpannāyāḥ samānakālikatve'nvayā- saṃbhavāt | saptamyā prakṛtyarthagavādyananvitasvārthabodhanāsaṃbhavācca | tataḥ samabhivyāhṛtadohanādi- kriyaiva saptamyarthaḥ | tatra kṛdantasya viśeṣaṇatve karmatvanirūpakatvasaṃbandhena prakṛtyarthasyānvayaḥ | kartṛkṛdantasya tathātve tvadhīyāneṣu brāhmaṇeṣvityādau kartṛtvanirūpakatveneti bodhyam | tasyāśca samānakālikatvādisaṃbandhena gamanādāvanvayaḥ | tatra vartamānārthakakṛtsamabhivyāhāre samāna- kālikatvaṃ saṃbandhaḥ | bhaviṣyadarthakakṛtaḥ sthale prākkālavṛttitvaṃ, bhūtārthakakṛtsthale uttarakālavṛttitvamityādyāhuḥ | atra sūtrabhāṣyādyānuguṇyāt pūrvoktameva mataṃ samyagiti pare āhuḥ | rudati rudato vā prāvrājīdityatra 'ṣaṣṭhī cānādare' iti sūtreṇa ṣaṣṭhīsaptamyau, jñāpyajñāpaka- bhāvastadarthaḥ | anādaraviśiṣṭaṃ pravrajanaṃ dhātvarthaḥ | tatra tātparya grāhayati ṣaṣṭhyādivibhaktiḥ | anādaraśca pratyāsattyā putrādiviṣayaka eva | svasamānādhikaraṇyasvottarakālikatva- saṃbandhābhyāmanādaravaiśiṣṭyam | itthaṃ ca rodanakartṛputrādijñāpyamanādaraviśiṣṭaṃ pravrajanamiti bodhaḥ | nṛṇāṃ nṛṣu vā dvijaḥ śreṣṭha ityādau 'yataśca nirdhāraṇam' iti sūtreṇa yataḥ - samudāyāt svetarasvaghaṭitasamudāyaghaṭakebhyo vyāvṛttena dharmeṇa jātiguṇakriyāsaṃjñāviśiṣṭasyaikadeśasya pṛthakakaraṇarūpaṃ nirdhāraṇaṃ tataḥ ṣaṣṭhīsaptamyau bhavata ityarthakena ṣaṣṭhīsaptamyau vidhīyete | pṛthakkaraṇaṃ ca vyāvṛttadharmaprakārakajñānaviṣayīkaraṇaṃ tādṛśaikadeśaviśeṣyakatādṛśadharmaprakārakajñānānukūlavyāpāre paryavasitam | svetaratvaṃ svaniṣṭhapadārthatāvacchedakadharmāvicchinnapratiyogitākabhedāśrayatvam | ata eva naraṇāṃ naraḥ śreṣṭha ityādinā dvijādyātmakanarasya dvijatvādyavacchinnabhedavacchūdrādyātmakanara- samudāyavyāvṛttaśreṣṭhatvakaraṇakapṛthakkaraṇasaṃbhave'pi naratvāvacchinnabhedavadvayāvṛttatvasya śreṣṭhatve bādhānnirūktanirdhāraṇaviṣayatvāsaṃbhavāt | dvijapratiyogikabhedasya dvije'pi saṃbhavena tathāvidhadvijaghaṭitasamudāyavyāvṛttatvasya śreṣṭhatve'saṃbhavena naraṇāṃ dvijaḥ śreṣṭha ityādiprayogāsaṃbhavo vā'ta eva na | samudāye svaghaṭitatvopādānaphalaṃ tu kṣatriyāṇāṃ brahmaṇaḥ śreṣṭha ityādiprayogavāraṇam | asti ca narāṇāṃ dvijaḥ śreṣṭha ityādau dvijatvāvacchinnabhedavān yo naraghaṭitasamudāyaghaṭakaśūdrādistadvayāvṛttaśreṣṭhatvaprakārakapratītidvijaviṣayiṇīti niruktārthavivakṣāyāṃ tathāvidhaprayoganirvāhaḥ | itthaṃ ca vyāvṛttatvarūpasaṃbandho nirdhāraṇavibhakterarthaḥ | narapadaṃ ca naratvarūpeṇa viśeṣarūpeṇa vā dvijetaranarasamudāyaparam | evaṃ ca tathāvidhanarasamudāyavyavṛttaśreṣṭhatvavān dvija iti bodhaḥ | narāṇāṃ kṣatriye śauryamityatra kṣatriye taranarasamudāyaparam | evaṃ ca tathāvidhanarasamudāyavyāvṛttaṃ kṣatriyavṛttiśauryamiti bodhaḥ | samudāyasyodbhūtāvayavabhedavivakṣayā etadviṣaye bahuvacanasyaiva sādhutvamiti 'kārake' iti sūtre 'nirdhāraṇe kārakeṣviti brūyāt' iti vadatā patañjalinā spaṣṭamevoktam | ata eva 'midaco'ntyāt-' iti sūtre 'acaḥ' ityekavacanasya sautratvāt sādhutvamuktaṃ nibandhakāraiḥ | vyāvṛttatvarūpavibhattayarthasya vyutpattivaicivyāt padārthaikadeśe'pi
śreṣṭhatvādāvanvayaḥ | athavā svāśrayapadārthatāvacchedakavatvasaṃbandhena dharmiṇyevānvayaḥ | etena dravyāṇāṃ kambugrīvādimān ghaṭa ityādau ghaṭatvādidharmasyānvayitāvacchedakaghaṭatvatvādirūpeṇānupasthityā tatra vyāvṛttatvānvayāsaṃbhava iti nodyaṃ pratyuktam | padārthānāṃ jātimati sattetyādau jātimadanya- nirūpitavṛttitvasāmānyābhāvasya sattādau bādhāttannarūpitasamavetatvāprasiddhayā tadabhāvasya cāprasiddhayā vyāvṛttatvabodho na saṃbhavatīti tu na śaṅkyam | sākṣātsaṃbandhāvacchinnavṛttitvābhāvasya tadarthatvāt | sākṣatsaṃbandhe kālikādyatiriktatvaṃ praveśyam | anyathā gandhasya pṛthivyatiriktajalādirūpadravye'pi kālikasaṃbandhena sattvād dravyāṇāṃ pṛthivī gandhavatītyādiprayogānupapattiḥ | athavā tādātmyenānvayividheyārthakapadasamabhivyāhārasthale tādātmyenetarasaṃbandhitvābhāvaḥ śreṣṭhādipadārthe evānveti bhedasaṃbandhenānvayividheyasthale pūrvoktasaṃbandhāvacchinnavṛttitvābhāvo narāṇāṃ dvije śraiṣṭhyamityādau śreṣṭhyādau pratyāyyate iti naikadeśānvayaprasaṅga ityāhuḥ | anye tu vyāvṛttatvaṃ bhedapratiyogitvarūpamabhedenānvayividheyārthakapadasamabhivyāhārasthale vibhaktayā pratipādyate | bhedānvayividheyasthale ca yena saṃbandhena tadanvayastatsaṃbandhāvacchinnātyantābhāvapratiyogitvaṃ vidheye pratyāyyate | tasya vidheyatāvacchedakāvacchedenānvaya upeyaḥ | ato devadatto narāṇāṃ śūratamo ghaṭaḥ pṛthivīnāṃ gandhavāniti prayogāpattirna | śūratamādiniṣṭhobhayādibhedapratiyogitvasya śūratamādau sattve'pi śūratamatvādyavacchedenāsattvāt | tasya tathāvidhabhedadharmitānavacchedakatvāt | athavā bhede pratiyogyavṛttitvarūpamatyantābhāve ca pratiyogivaiyadhikaraṇyādirūpaṃ viśeṣaṇamupādeya tena noktadoṣaḥ | bhedādau prakṛtyarthanarāderuddeśyatāvacchedakakṣatriyatvādyavacchinnabhedaviśiṣṭaprakṛtyarthatāvacchedakanaratvādi- vyāpakādhikaraṇatānirūpitādheyatvasaṃbandhenānvayaḥ | tāvataiva kṣatriyādītaranarādivyāvṛttervidheye lābhānnarādipadaṃ mukhyārthakameva | dravyāṇāṃ kṣitipāthasī rasavatītyādau dravyarūpaprakṛtyarthasya bhedātmakavibhattayarthe kṣititvāvacchinnabhedajalatvāvacchinnabhedadvayaviśiṣṭadravyatvavyāpakādhikaraṇatā- nirūpitādheyatvaṃ saṃbandhaḥ | vidheyasya śūratamādernirdhāraṇavibhaktiprakṛtyarthatāvacchedakanaratvādiviśiṣṭa- tādātmyādireva saṃbandhatayā bhāsate | ato narāṇāṃ rākṣasa ityādiprayogasya nāpattirityāhuḥ | pare tu vyāvṛttatvasya vibhaktyarthatve na bhagavataḥ sūtrakatastātparyam | 'na nirdhāraṇe' iti sūtreṇa nirdhāraṇasya vibhaktivācyakoṭipraveśasya bodhanāt | tatratyanirdhāraṇapadasya vyāvṛttatvārthakatve pramāṇābhāvāt | tasmājjātiguṇakriyāsaṃjñāviśiṣṭasyaikadeśasya yataḥ svasajātīyaghaṭitasamudāyāt svetarasamudāyaghaṭakavyāvṛttadharmakaraṇakaṃ pṛthakkaraṇaṃ tataḥ ṣaṣṭhīsaptamyābityarthaḥ sūtrasya, uktanirdhāraṇaviṣayatvamavayāvayavibhāvasaṃbandhaśca nirdhāryamāṇānvayī vibhaktyarthaḥ | nṛsamudāyāvadhika- dvijetaranaravyāvṛttaśreṣṭhatvakaraṇakanirdhāraṇaviṣayo nṛsamudāyāvayavo dvija iti bodhaḥ | nṛṇāṃ śūdraḥ śreṣṭha iti prayogavyāvartanāyādyasya saṃbandhasya, nṛṇāṃ rākṣasaḥ śūratama ityasya vyāvartanāya dvitīyasya vibhaktyarthatvāṅgīkāraḥ | śūdrepya'ntyajātyādyapekṣayā śreṣṭhatvasya vidyamānatayā tadbodhena tathāvidhaprayogāpatteravāraṇāt | saṃsargaghaṭakakaraṇaṃ ca pratyāsattyā śreṣṭhatvādirūpameva grāhyam | tena nṛṇāṃ kṣatriyādiḥ śreṣṭha iti prayogāpattirna | anyathā kṣatriyāderapi śūratamatvādikaraṇakapṛthakkaraṇa- viṣayatayā syādeva tādṛśaprayogaḥ | nṛṇāṃ madhye dvijaḥ śreṣṭha ityatra madhyayoge ṣaṣṭhī | tadyoge saptamī tu na sādhuḥ | na ca tatstve'pi madhyārthe'nvayāvivakṣayā nirdhāraṇasaptamī sādhureveti vācyam | tatsattve tadarthasya niyamenāvadhyākāṅkṣayā tatraivānvayasya yuktatvāt | nirdhāraṇavibhaktisthale śreṣṭhatamatvādau
dvijetaranarādivyāvṛttatvabodhastu devadatto vāmenākṣaṇetyādyuktau dakṣiṇanetrakaraṇa- kadarśanābhāvabodhavanmānasa eva, dvijamaṇḍale vartamāno'vaśiṣṭaḥ śreṣṭha ityādhārasaptamīghaṭitavākye'pi tathāpratītaruktarūpāvaśyakyeva | nirdhāraṇāvadhinirdhāryamāṇanirdhāraṇahetūnāṃ saṃnidhye eva nirdhāraṇa- pratītiratastatraiva nirdhāraṇavibhaktiḥ samāsaniṣedhaśca | ata eva puruṣāṇāmuttaṃma ityatra śeṣaṣaṣṭhīti puruṣottama iti samāsaḥ sādhuriti 'dvivacanavibhajya-' iti sūtre kaiyaṭa ityāhuḥ | māthurā pāṭaliputrakebhya āḍhyatarā ityatra tu nirdhāryamāṇanirdhāraṇāvadhyorbhedasyaiva sattvena 'pañcamī vibhakte' iti pañcamī | tatra vibhaktapadena bhedo, nirdhāryamāṇasya nirdhāraṇāvadhito yatra bheda eva tadavadhivācakāt pañcamīti tadarthaḥ | ibhaktagrahaṇasāmarthyādavadhāraṇalābhaḥ | tanopattena kenāpi rūpeṇābhede naitatpravṛttiḥ | atra nirdhāraṇāvadhivyāvṛttadharmakaraṇakanirdhāraṇaviṣayatvaṃ vibhaktyarthaḥ | pāṭaliputrakāvadhikatadvayāvṛttāḍhyataratvakaraṇakanirdhāraṇaviṣayā māthurā āḍhyatareti bodhaḥ | 'mūlenāvāhayeddevīṃ śravaṇena nisarjayet', mūle śravaṇe iti vā | atra 'nakṣatre ca lupi' iti sūtreṇa saptamī | lupśabdena lupsaṃjñayā luptapratyayārtho lakṣyate | tathā ca tādṛśapratyayārthe vartamānānnakṣatra- vācakādadhikaraṇe saptamī, tṛtīyā ceti tadarthaḥ | adhikaraṇa ityanuvṛttilabhyam | tatprayojanaṃ tvadya puṣya ityādau saptamyādivyāvṛttiḥ | anyathāntaraṅgatvāt prātipadikārthe eva syāt | mūlenetyatra 'nakṣatreṇa yuktaḥ kālaḥ' ityaṇ | tatra nakṣatraśabdena tatsamīpavartī candra ucyate | tasyāṇaḥ 'lubaviśeṣa' iti lup | lupi sati lupyamānapratyayārthaḥ prakṛtyaiva pratyāyayate, yaḥ śiṣyate iti nyāyāt | ādhārādikaṃ saptamyādyarthaḥ | itthaṃ ca mūlanakṣatrasamīpavarticandrāyuktakālādhikaraṇakaṃ devīkarmakamāvāhanamityādyākārako bodhaḥ | adya bhuktvāyaṃ dvayahe dvayahādvā bhoktā, atra 'saptamīpañcamyau kārakamadhye' iti sūtreṇa saptamīpañcamyāvanuśiṣyete | kārakapadena kārakartṛtvādi- śaktirucyate | 'kālādhvanoḥ-' iti sūtrāt 'kālādhvānoḥ' ityanuvartate | tathā ca śaktidvayamadhye yau kālādhvānau tadvācakāt saptamīpañcamyābiti tadarthaḥ | anantarakālavṛttitvaṃ vibhaktyarthaḥ | adyapadārthaitaddivasādhikaraṇakatvasya bhojanakriyāyāmanvayaḥ, tadanvitaktāntapratipādyabhojanakriyāyāḥ svottarakālavṛttitvasvasamānakartṛkatvobhayasaṃbandhena bhojane prakṛtavibhaktyarthasyāpi tatraiva | itthaṃ caitaddivasādhikaraṇakabhojanottarakālikaṃ tathāvidhabhojanasamānakartṛkametaddivasānantaradvayahānantara- kālavṛtti yad bhojanaṃ tadāśraya ityarthaḥ | dvayahe etaddivasānantaryalābhaḥ samabhivyāhārabalāt | so'pi na śābdaḥ kiṃ, tvārthaḥ | ihastho'yaṃ krośe krośādvā lakṣyaṃ vidhyet atrānantaradeśavṛttitvaṃ vibhaktyarthaḥ | ihaśabdapratipādyasyaitaddeśādhikaraṇaka vasya sthitāvanvayaḥ | tādṛśasthitiḥ kṛdarthāśraye, tasyākhyātārthakartari, tasya vyadhanakriyāyāṃ tatraiva krośānantaradeśasthitalakṣyakarmakatvasya | tathā caitaddeśādhikaraṇakasthitikartṛkartṛkametaddeśānantarakrośānantaradeśasthitalakṣyakarmakaṃ saṃbhāvanāviṣayībhūtaṃ vyadhanamiti bodhaḥ | evamanyatrāpyūhyam | iti saptamyarthanirṇayaḥ ||
evaṃ subvibhaktyarthānupavarṇya tadanurodhena saṃbandhasahitasakalakārakaghaṭitavākyārthabodhaṃ varṇayati-- evaṃ ceti | uktarītyā dharmaṇāṃ vibhaktyarthatve ca | tadabhinnāśrayaka iti | ākhyātārthāśraye gorabhedenānvaya iti bhāvaḥ | vibhāgajanaka iti | dadātervibhārapūrvakadāne vṛttiriti bhāvaḥ |
tathā tiṅāmiti | tiṅāmiti kartṛkarmaṇī saṃkhyā cārtha ityarthaḥ | niyama iti | arthaniyama
ityarthaḥ | evaṃ ca karmatvādiśūnyārthakāvyayādapi vibhaktisiddhiḥ | pratyayaniyamapakṣe'pi tatra vibhaktisiddhimāha--niyamaḥ prakṛteṣu ceti | prakṛtakartrādyarthāpekṣo niyamaḥ, karmaṇyeva dvitīyetyādiniyamena kartrādau dvitīyādayo vyāvacchidyante na tu karmatvādiśūnya ityarthaḥ | nanu 'svaujasamauṭ' ityādinā 'tiptasajhi-' ityādinā ca vihitānāṃ svādīnāṃ tibādīnāṃ ca 'karmaṇi dvitīyā' ityādiśāstrainiyamapakṣe na karmādau vācakatāgrāhakatvaṃ 'svaujas-' ityādiśāśtrasya labhyate, tatra 'karmaṇi-' ityāderabhāvāt, nāpi 'karmāṇi' ityādeḥ, niyāmakatvādityāśaṅkyāha-tathā hīti | ekavākyatayā - vākyaikavākyatayā, na padaikavakyatayā | tadabhyupagame karmatvādi- yogyārthakaprātipadikārthamātragate ekatve prathamaikavacanaṃ suḥ, karmatvādiśaktisamānādhikaraṇe ekatve dvitīyaikavacanamamityādikrameṇa bodhaḥ syādevaṃ cānyatrāvyayādeḥ prāptirupapādayituṃ naiva śakyeta | vākyaikavākyatāyāṃ tu prātipadikāt svādayo bhavanti, te ca prātipadikārthamātragate ekatvādau bhavantītyādibodhākāro bhavati | tathā sati tatra prathamavākyenāvyayādau prāptirupapādayituṃ śakyate | kiṃ caivamarthaniyamaprakṛtārthāpekṣapratyayaniyamaśuddhapratyayaniyāmakayorucchedaścaivaṃ syāt | ekavacanādi- śabde sūtraśāṭakavad bhāvisaṃjñāśrayaṇamanyonyāśrayaparihārāyeti gauravaṃ cetyekavākyatāpadena vākyaikavākyataiva vikṣitā |
nanvevaṃ pūrvapūrvavākyavihitānāṃ kathaṃ karmādyarthabodhanaṃ kartu śakyam | na ca sāmagrīsattve tat saṃbhavatyeveti vācyam | evaṃ satyekavākyatāvirahe'pi 'dvayekayoḥ--' ityādinā ekatve evaikavacanamityapi bodhane kṛte satyekatvādyarthakatvabodhanasaṃbhave vākyaikavākyatāpuraskāro nirarthaka iti cenna | ekavākyatāpakṣe svādivākyenaiva vākyaikavākyatoktahetubhiraṅgīkriyate, na tu 'karmaṇi dvitīyā' 'dvayekayoḥ-' ityādyoḥ, kiṃ tu padaikavākyatā | evaṃ ca karmagataikatve eva dvitīyaikavacanamam bhavatītyādyākāraniyamavalābho'tra bhavati | ekavākyatāvirahe tu karmaṇyeva dvitīyā, ekatve evaikavacanamamiti viruddhaniyamadvayaṃ syāt, taccāsaṃgatamityāśaya diti dik | ubhayathāpi siddhaniyamiruddhamiti | tatra karmaṇyeva dvitīyetyādirūpapratyayaniyamāvirodho viṣayatālakṣaṇāṅgīkāre spaṣṭa eva | viṣayatāyāḥ śeṣatvāccheṣe ṣaṣṭhyeveti niyamavirodho- 'rthaniyamapakṣe'pīti bodhyam | ukte'rthe prācīnanaiyāyikānāmapi saṃmatimāha ata evetyādinā | evaṃ ca navyanaiyāyikānāṃ na kevalaṃ vyākaraṇavirodho'pi tu svīyavṛddhasiddhāntavirodho'pīti taduktirupekṣyaiva | evaṃ mīmāṃsakīktirapīti sūcitamiti śivam ||
iti śrīkālopanāmakakeśavadīkṣitātmajaharirāmaviracitāyāṃ vaiyākaraṇabhūṣaṇasāra-
vyākhyāyāṃ subarthanirṇayaḥ samāptimagamat ||
<4>
sakalanāmamūlabhūtadhātvartha tatprakṛtikatiṅthāstatprasaṅgena subarthāśca nirūpyāvasarasaṃgatyā nāmārthānnirūpayatītyāśayenāha-nāmārthāniti | prātipadikārthānityarthaḥ | ekādiśabdānāṃ sādhāraṇa- tayānabhimatārthaparatvaśaṅkānivṛttaye prakṛte vivakṣitānārthānāha-ekaṃ jātirityādinā | gaurāditi | anekavyaktiṣu śaktikalpane, jātau ca tatkalpane tatra śakyatāvacchedakatvasya vā kalpane gauravādityarthaḥ | na ca jātiśaktimate'pi jātigatagotvatvādidharme śakyatāvacchedakatvamaṅgī- karaṇīyam, evaṃ ca tasya gavānyāsamavetatve sati sakalagosamavetatvarūpasya śakyatvamāvaśyakamiti viparītaṃ gauravamiti vācyam | atrālakṣyasyāpi lakṣyātāvacchedakatvavat aśakyasyāpi śakyatā-
vacchedakatvasaṃbhavād, gurubhūte'vacchedakatvasvīkāraprayuktaṃ gauravamityapi na, śabdārthayostādātmyā- cchabdagatajātereva śakyatāvacchedakatvāt, tadvṛttidharmasyā''vidyakadharmaviśeṣarūpatvādvā, gotvatva- mityādau tasyaiva tvādipratyayārthatvaṃ na tu gavetarāsamavetatvaghaṭitasya, gauravāditi spaṣṭamanyatra |
vyattayantare lakṣaṇāyāmiti | svīkriyamāṇāyāmiti śeṣaḥ, tatsvīkāraśca vyaktyantarabodhānya- thānupapattyāvaśyakaḥ | svasamaveteti | śakyatvenābhimatavyaktiviśeṣaḥ svapadārthaḥ | iti lāghavamiti | tathā coktalāghavaṃ vinigamakamiti na vinagamanāviraha iti bhāvaḥ | upajīvyatvādapi jātireva vācyeti yuktamityāha-nāgṛhīteti | 'nāgṛhītaviśeṣaṇā buddhiviśeṣyamavagāhata' iti nyāyaśarīraṃ, viśiṣṭabuddhi prati viśeṣaṇajñānasya hetutayāgṛhītaviśeṣaṇā etādṛśī buddhirna viśeṣaṇāviśiṣṭa- viśeṣyaviṣayiṇī bhavati | nañdvayena prathamato viśeṣaṇaviṣayakagrahasyāvaśyakatvaṃ sūcitam | tadidamuktaṃ bhaṭṭaiḥ 
'viśeṣyaṃ nābhidhā gacchet kṣīṇaśaktiviśeṣaṇe' | iti |
nanvevaṃ vyaktibodho na syāt | na cākṣepāt pīno devadatta ityatra rātribhojanavaditi vācyam, prakṛtivṛttyanupasthitatayā gāṃ dadyādityādau vyaktau karmatvādyanvayānupapatteḥ | pratyayānāṃ prakṛtyarthānvitetyādivyutpatteḥ | padārthantarānvayaśca tatra na syād vṛttigrahaviśeṣye eva tadanvayāt | anyathā samavāyenopasthitākāśe'pi tadanvayāpatteḥ | uktaṃ hi tadbhūtādhikaraṇe 
'gamyamānasya cārthasya naiva dṛṣṭaṃ viśeṣaṇam |
śabdāntaraivibhaktyā vā dhūmo'yaṃ jvalatītivat ||' iti |
viśeṣaṇaṃ, viśeṣaṇībhūtavyāvartanaṃ viśeṣaṇatākhyaviṣayatāvattayā pratipādanaṃ vā tadarthaḥ | kiṃ cāpattirūpa ākṣepo'pi na saṃbhavati | gāṃ dadyādityādito'nupapattijñānaṃ vināpi bodhadarśanāt | samānavittivedyatvamevākṣepa iti guravo menire | tathā coktaṃ taiḥ-yāvatī hi jātidhīrvyakti viṣayīkaroti, ato jātibhāsakasāmagryā eva vyaktibhāsakatvamaṅgīkāryam | vyaktipratyakṣaṃ vinā jātipratyakṣābhāvena tatraivaitanniyamāvadhāraṇāt, tathā ca jātiśaktijñānameva vyāktiviśeṣyakajāti- prakārakaśabdabodhaṃ prati kāraṇamiti paryavasannamiti, tadapi na | evaṃ sati gotvamastītyato gotva- tvaviśiṣṭabodhāpatteḥ | lakṣaṇaivākṣepapadārthaḥ | evaṃ ca tayopasthitavyaktau vibhaktyarthādyanvayaḥ saṃbhava- tyevetyanye, tadapare na kṣamante lakṣaṇāyāḥ śakyānvayānupapattijñānādhīnatayoktadoṣakavalitatvādityata āha vyaktibodhastviti | lakṣaṇayeti | nirūḍhalakṣaṇayetyarthaḥ | tatra ca nānvayādyanupapatti- jñānāpekṣeti bhāvaḥ | lakṣaṇayeti jātivyaktyorabhedasyāpyupalakṣaṇam | taduktaṃ bhaṭṭaiḥ 
'tena tallakṣitavyakteḥ kriyāsaṃbandhabodhanā |
vyaktyākṛtyorabhedo vā vākyārtheṣu vivakṣitaḥ ||' iti |
'taddhitārtha-' iti sūtre bhāṣye uktam-' athavā vyatirekād dravyākṛtyordravyānayanam' iti | gotvādipadaṃ tu gotyādivṛttidharmaśaktamiti na tato gotvaviśiṣṭaviṣayakabodhaḥ | nanvidamasaṃgataṃ ' śakyādanyena rūpeṇa jñāte bhavati lakṣaṇā |' 
iti niyamāllakṣaṇayā śakyagotvaprakārakavyaktiviśeṣyakabodhānirvāhāditi cenna, etanniyame mānābhāvāt | śuklamānayetyādito lakṣaṇayā śakyaśuklarūpādiprakārakavyaktiviśeṣyakabodhodayena vyabhicārāccetyabhipretyāha digiti | etadabhipretyaiva 'savarṇe'ṇgrahaṇamaparibhāṣyamākṛtigrahaṇāt' iti vārtikaṃ saṃgacchate | sarūpasūtre bhāṣye'pyuktam 'ākṛti vājapyāyanaḥ' iti | apare mīmāṃsakāstu yathā vyaktiśaktimate gotvādyaṃśe śaktistadavacchedakatā vā niravacchinnā, tathā jātiśaktimate'pi sā niravacchinnaiveti na gotvatvādiniveśaprayuktagauravaśaṅkāpi | ata evaitanmate'pi svarūpato gotvādibhānanirvāhaḥ śābdabodhe | nanu gotvatvādeḥ śakyatāvacchedakatva- virahe'pi śaktigrahakāraṇatāyāṃ gotvatvāderdharmitāvacchedakatākhyaviṣayatāpraveśād gauravaṃ durnivārameva, vyaktiśaktivādimate svarūpato gotvādiniṣṭhadharmitāvacchedakatvasyaiva niveśāditi cenna | nirūpitatvasaṃbandhena svarūpato gotvaprakārakasya gopadaviśeṣaṇatvāpannaśaktiviśeṣyakasya gopadaśaktirgaurityākārasya, śaktisaṃbandhena svarūpatastatprakārakagopadaviśeṣyakasya gopadaṃ gaurityākārakasya vā śaktijñānasyaiva kāraṇatvopagamāt | tatra ca gottvādiviṣayatāyā apraveśāt | samavāyenaiva svarūpataḥ prakāratvamiti niyamastu na, ghaṭaṃ jānāmityādyanuvyavasāye prakāritāsaṃbandhena ghaṭatvādīnāṃ svarūpato jñānādyaṃśe prakāropagamāditi digiti samūcuḥ | asmin mate dūṣaṇamudbhāvayan vyaktiśaktimate sādhakaṃ vadaṃścaikapadasya vyaktiparatvamāha yadveti | kevalavyaktireveti | evaśabdavyāvartyā jātiḥ | kevalaśabdenaiva tadvayāvṛttisiddhāvevaḥ spaṣṭārthaḥ | kevalavyaktipakṣa eveti | jātiviśiṣṭavyakteḥ padārthatvamate tu tajjātyavacchinnaviśeṣyatākaśakti- grahatvena tajjātiprakārakatadāśrayaviśeṣyakaśabdatvāvacchinnaṃ prati hetutayā atvādijātyāśrayasakala- vyaktigrahaṇasaṃbhavenāṇgrahaṇam 'aṇudit ' sūtre vyartha syāt | kevalajātiśaktimate tu tadvaiyarthya spaṣṭameva | 'aṇgrahaṇasya' ityasya 'ārambheṇa' ityatrānvayaḥ |
ekaśeṣasya ceti | 'sarūpāṇā'miti sūtrārambheṇetyarthaḥ | ekaśeṣārambhasya vyakteḥ padārthatāyāṃ mānatā, na tu kevalavyakteḥ padārthatāyāṃ, jātiviśiṣṭavyakteḥ padārthatve'pi tadārambhasyāvaśyakatvāt | yadvā tajjātyānugatīkṛtānekārthānāmekaśabdena pratipādanasya vakṣyamāṇatayā kevalavyakteḥ padārthatvasādhakateti bodhyam | na ca jāteḥ padārthatve'pyakṣādipadārthabhāṣapāśādisādhāraṇajāterabhāve- nānekamāṣatvapāśatvāditātparyeṇa akṣā ityādiprayoganirvāhāyaikaśeṣasūtrārambha āvaśyaka iti vācyam, aśnoterakṣaśabdasya vyutpādanāddhātvarthavyāptigatavyāptitvarūpajātiḥ svasamavāyi- samavāyātmakaparaṃparāsaṃbandhena māṣapāśādisaṃbaddhākṣapadavācyetyabhyupagamāt | tathā coktaṃ sarūpa- sūtrabhāṣye 'aśnoterakṣastatra kriyāsāmānyāt siddham' iti | kriyāgataṃ sāmānyamityarthaḥ | tathāvidhakriyāsamaniyataṃ sāmānyamakṣapadārthatrayasamavetamityartha ityapare | tathā ca tādṛśajāteḥ saṃbhavenoktadoṣānavakāśāt tasyāpi - kevalavyaktiśaktipakṣasyāpi śāstraṃ mānamuktvā yuktimapi tatra pramāṇayati yuktaṃ caitaditi | vyaktāveveti | ghaṭamānayeti vākyaprayogānantarakālikaprayojyakartṛ- kānayamādirūpavyavahāreṇa vyaktāveva śaktigrahādityarthaḥ | nanu vyaktau jāto'pi śaktigraho lāghavādagre bādhyate, bādho nāmāprāmāṇyagraha iti candramaṇḍalādipramāṇaṃ pūrva mindriyeṇa gṛhītamapi jyotiḥśāstreṇa bādhyate, na tu lāghavamātreṇeti na doṣaḥ | pūrva jāto'pi karmadhāraya- niścayo'gre'pūrvavidyākalpanabhiyā bādhyeteti niṣādasthapatyadhikaraṇavirodha iti cenna | pūrva jātasya
lāghavamātreṇa bādhāyogāt | anyathā niṣādasthapati yājayedityatra gāṃ dadyādityatrānupapattiśca, jātiśaktimate gotvāderdānādyasaṃbhavāt | lakṣitavyaktimādāya tadupapattirityapi na yuktam | vyaktyantare śakyasambandhā'grahāt, vyaktyantaraviṣayakasaṃbandhagrahasya taditaravyaktibodhājanakatvāt | anyathā hastitvadharmitāvacchedakakādekahastiviṣayakahastipakasaṃbandhagrahāt kālāntare hastipaka- darśane hastyantarasmaraṇāpatteḥ | gāṃ nayetyādivākyeṣu vṛttidvayakalpanāyāṃ gauravaṃ ca | ata eva 'jāterastitvanāstitve na hi kaścidvivakṣati |
nityatkāllakṣaṇīyāyā vyakteste hi viśeṣaṇe ||' 
iti maṇḍanimiśroktamapyapāstam | jātivyaktyorabhedāddānānvaya iti cettathā sati vyaktervācyatvaṃ svīkṛtameveti bhāvaḥ | nanu vyaktīnāmānantyācchaktyānantyaprasaṅgaḥ, kutracideva vyaktau śaktiriti noktadoṣa iti cedevaṃ satyagṛhītaśaktikavyaktibodhe'pyavyabhicāreṇa śaktigrahasya śābdabodhahetutvabhaṅgāpattiḥ | śaktigrahāviṣayatvāviśeṣād ghaṭapadāt taṭāderapi bodhāpattiśca | taduktaṃ mīmāṃsakādibhiḥ 'ānantyavyabhicārābhyāṃ śaktyanekatvadoṣataḥ |'
ityāśaṅkāmapākurvannāha-saṃbandhitāvacchedakasyeti | śakyatāvacchedakasyetyarthaḥ | jāti- rūpasya tasyeti jojanā | evaṃ ca saṃbandhitāvacchedakasyaiva viśeṣyatayā vivakṣaṇānnapuṃsakanirdeśo- papattiḥ | anyathā saṃbandhitāvacchedikāyā iti syāditi bodhyam | ekaiveti | śakyavyaktīnāmā- nantye'pyupalakṣaṇībhūtaghaṭatvādijāteraikyāttadaikyaṃ, kāraṇatāyāḥ nirūpakāśrayavyaktibāhulye'pyavaccheda- kadaṇḍatvāderaikyādaikyavaditi bhāvaḥ | taddharmāvacchinnadharmikaśaktigrahasya taddharmaprakārakataddharmāśraya- viśeṣyakaśābdatvāvacchinnaṃ prati kāraṇatvācchaktigrahasya yāvadvayaktiviṣayakatvāsaṃbhave'pi na vyabhicāraḥ | kāryatvakāraṇatvayoravacchedakakodau tattadvayaktiviṣayatāyā aniveśāt | tattaddharma- syāśakyatve'pi tadviṣayatāyāḥ kāryatāvacchedakakoṭipraveśādbhānopapattiśca | ghaṭapadāt paṭādibodha- prasaṅge dravyatvaprakārakaghaṭabhānaprasaṅgaśca neti bhāvaḥ | śaṅkate-na caivamiti | evaṃ - ghaṭatvādeḥ śakyatāvacchedakatve | śakyatāvacchedakatvāditi | śakyatve sati śaktigrahīyārthaniṣṭhadharmitāvacche- dakatvarūpatvācchakyatāvacchedakatvasya ghaṭatvāderapi vācyatvamāvaśyakamiti bhāvaḥ | tadeva vācyamiti | evaṃ ca kevalavyaktivācyatāpakṣo'tyantāsaṃbhavahrasta iti tātparyam | alakṣyatve'pīti | yathā gaṅgāyāṃ ghoṣa ityādau tīratvādyavacchinnadharmikapravāharūpaśakyasaṃbandhitāgrahādeva tīratvādiprakāra- kosthitiśābdabodhanarvāhaḥ, saṃbandhitāvacchedakatvagrahasyaivopasthityādiviṣayatāprayojakatvānna tu tadaṃśe'pi samuditatātparyārthaḥ | śakyatāvacchedakatve'pi vṛttyātmakasaṃbandhaśūnye tattatsaṃbandhitāvacche- dakatvamupeyata iti śaṅkā nirastā | ayaṃ ca dṛṣānto naiyāyikanaye | ālaṃkārikādinaye tu lakṣyatāvacchedake'pi lakṣaṇāstyeva | tathā coktam 
'mukhyārthabādhe tadyoge rūḍhito'tha prayojanāt |
anyo'rtho lakṣyate yat sā lakṣaṇā ||' iti |
lakṣyate - jñāyata ityarthaḥ | evaṃ ca nātmāśrayaḥ | evaṃ ca śakyādaśakyopasthiti- rūpāyāstasyā lakṣyatāvacchedake'pi viṣayatayā sattvamanapavādamevetyāhuriti dik | ukte'rtheruṇādhikaraṇasthabhaṭṭokti sādhakatayopanyasyati | ānantye'pīti | bhāvānāṃ - ghaṭādīnāṃ- vācyatvenābhimatānām | ekam - anekavyaktisaṃbaddhaṃ ghaṭatvādikam | sukarasambandhaḥ -
sukarasaṃbandhagrahaḥ, saṃbandhaḥ - śaktirūpaḥ | na ca vyabhicariṣyatītyasyokto'bhiprāyaḥ | ākṛti- padārthasyeti ākṛtiḥ padārtho yasyeti bhuvrīhiḥ | iti bhāṣyāditi | idaṃ ca sarūpasūtrasthaṃ bhāṣyam | viśiṣṭaṃ vācyamiti | jātiviśiṣṭaṃ gāvādikaṃ gāvādiśabdavācyamityarthaḥ | ata eva cakṣurādiśabdavācyamityarthapramāṇavacchadbarūpapramāṇasya svasaṃbaddhaviṣayasyaiva grāhitvamiti niyamabhaṅge na | pare tu "na hyākṛtipadārthasya dravyaṃ na padārthaḥ dravyapadārthakasya vākṛtirna padārthaḥ, ubhayorubhayaṃ padārthaḥ kasyacit kiṃcit pradhānabhūtaṃ kiṃcidguṇabhūta"mityādi bhāṣye dravyapadārthavāde ākṛte- viśeṣaṇatvoktyā tajjātyanugītakṛtānekavyāktibodhasyaikaśabdenaiva saṃbhavena tatpakṣe'pyekaśeṣo nārambhaṇīya iti sūcitam | jātivāde vyaktervyaktivāde jāteḥ śakyatāvacchedakatvamiti ca sūcita- mityāhuḥ | nanu viśiṣṭasya padārthatāpakṣe ekamiti viruddhaṃ syādata āha-ekamityasya ceti | castvarthaḥ | ayaṃ - vakṣyamāṇaḥ | jātireveti | śaktijñānaviṣṭhakāraṇatāyāṃ viṣayatāsaṃbandhena jātirevāvacchedikā na tu vyaktiḥ, tadviṣayakaśaktijñānatvena kāraṇatvābhāvāditi bhāvaḥ | ekaṃ nāmārtha ityasyaikaṃ - sāmānyameva tādṛśakāraṇatāvacchedakamityartha etanmate bodhyaḥ | etadeva viśadayati-tathā ceti | ghaṭatvāṃśe'nyāprakāraketi | tena ghaṭatvamastītyato na ghaṭatvaviśiṣṭa- bodhāpattiḥ | evaṃ jātirastītyato'pi | etena ghaṭatvamastītyatastathāvidhabodhavāraṇāya viśiṣṭaśaktijñānatvena kāraṇatāvaśyakīti dūṣaṇaṃ nirastam | ghaṭatvatvāvacchinne eva ghaṭatvapadāśaktarghaṭatvāṃśe tatprakārakaśaktidhiya eva saṃbhavāt | ayameva kubjaśaktivāda iti gīye mīmāṃsakaiḥ | yadviṣayakatvena śaktidhiyo hetutā tasyaiva śakyatvamiti niyame mānābhāvena vyakterapi śakyatvasiddhiḥ | nanu vinigamamāvirahād vyaktiviṣayakaśaktijñānatvenaiva tathāvidhabodhaṃ prati hetutāstviti cenna, vyaktināmānantyādanantavyaktiviṣayakatvaniveśe gauravāt | na ca yatkiṃcijjātyāśrayavyaktiviṣayakatvameva niveśyamiti na gauravamiti vācyam, dravyādipadāśakti- jñānasyāpi dvavyatvāśrayavyaktiviṣayakatvenaiva hetutāpattau ghaṭādipadādapi dravyatvaprakārakadhodhāpatteḥ | paryāyāntarād dravyatvaprakārakabodhānupapattyā dravyapadajñānatvena hetutvopagamāsaṃbhavādityādikaṃ bṛhadbhūṣaṇe draṣṭavyamityabhipretyāha prapañcitaṃ bhūṣaṇa iti | pūrvavat - viṣayatāsaṃbandhena tādṛśakāraṇatāvacchedakamityarthasvīkāreṇa pariharaṇīyaḥ | anyathā pūrvamate ekasyaiva nāmārthatvametanmate ca dvayostathātvamiti virodhaḥ spaṣṭa eva | pūrvatra viśiṣṭasya padārthatve virodhaḥ parihṛtaḥ, iha tu dvayostathātve sa parihṛtaḥ | nanu 'ratanakeśavatī strī syāt' ityādinā vivakṣitamavayavasaṃsthānaviśeṣarūpaṃ strītvādikaṃ naitacchāstrīyaprakriyā nirvāhakam | dārānityādau natvābhāvaprasaṅgāt | na cāropeṇa nirvāha iti yuktam | viśeṣadarśināntadasaṃbhavāt | na cāgāryaḥ sa saṃbhavatyeveti vācyam | tadasattve'pi tathāvidhaprayogāṇāmiṣṭatvāt | ata eva striyāmiti sūtre bhāṣyakārairuktānupapattyānyathā strītvādinirvacanaṃ kṛtamityāśaṅkya bhāṣyoktarītimanusarati sattveti | nanūpacayāpacayāderviruddhasyaikatra samāveśāyogāt taṭastaṭī taṭamityāderanupapattiḥ | guṇānāṃ kṣaṇikatvapuraskāreṇa tadupapattirityapi na yuktam, evamapi yugapattathāvyavahārānupapatterduṣparihāratvāt | avikāriṇyupacayādyasaṃbhavādbrahma ātmetyādibyavahārānupapatteśca | āropādare tu laukikaliṅgānādaro nirbīja ityata āha tattacchabdaniṣṭhamiti | nanvatrāpi pakṣe ekaśabde anekaviruddhaliṅgāsaṃbhavatādavasthyamata āha tameva viruddheti | bhidyanta iti | tathā ca
