noktadoṣāvakāśaḥ | nanvevaṃ 'taṭaṃ tri'ṣvityādivyavahārānupapattirata āha koṣāṃciditi | samāneti | ayaṃ cābhedārope hetuḥ | evaṃ ceti | viṅgasya śabdaniṣṭatve cetyarthaḥ | pare tu upacayādyavasthātrayasya sārvatrikatvādidaṃ liṅgaṃ kevalānvayi, idaṃ vastu iyaṃ vyaktirayaṃ padārtha ityādibyavaharāṇāmapratibaddhasakalajanasiddhaprasarāt | taccārthaniṣṭham | 'ekārthe śabdānyatvād dṛṣṭaṃ liṅgānyatvamavayavānyatvācca' iti bhāṣyācca | 'puṣyatārakā nakṣatramiti nānāśabdatvadarśanāt stanakeśādyatiriktameva liṅgamarthaniṣṭhaṃ, kuṭī kuṭira ityādau rephasyāvayavasyopajanane liṅgabhedadarśanāccetyartha' iti kaiyaṭopādhyāyāḥ | ata evopakramabhāṣye 'rūparasagandhasparśaśabdānāṃ styānaprasavau liṅgam' ityuktam | yasmādrūpādayona hi śabdagatāḥ |
nanvevaṃ 'puṃliṅgaḥ śabda' iti vyavahārānupapattiriti cenna | vācyavācakayorabhedopacārādarśa ādyajantatvādvā tadupapatteḥ | ātmā brahmetyādi vyavahārasiddhistu brahmaṇi sarvasyaivādhyāsena paraṃparayā saṃratyānādisattvena copapadyate | 'upacayāderviruddhasyaikatrāsamāveśāt taṭastaṭītyādyanupapatti'riti na vācyam, kośabhāṣyādiprāmāṇyena virodhasyaiva virahāt | kaścicchabda ekaliṅgaviśiṣṭārthe, kaściliṅgadvayaviśiṣṭa ityādikaṃ kośādito'vaseyam | tatra ṭighubhādivallaghusaṃjñākaraṇaucitye'pi gurusaṃjñākaraṇāt natvādiśāśtrapravṛttau laukikamapi liṅgaṃ nimittatvenāśrīyate | saṃkhyāsaṃjñāvat puṃsādisaṃketānāmadhikasaṃgrahārthatve'pi lokaprasiddhārthanirāsārthatvābhāvācca | laukikāsaṃbhave tu śāśtrīye eva natvādipravṛttiḥ | tatsaṃbhave tu śāśtrīyaviśiṣṭalaukika eva, na tu kevalaśāstrīye | natvādikāryeṇa lokikaliṅgasyārthagatasya bodhāt |
'āvirbhāvastirobhāvaḥ sthitiścetyapyapāyinaḥ |
dharmā mūrtiṣu sarvāsu liṅgatvena vyavasthitāḥ ||'
iti harikārikāto'pi tasyārthaniṣṭatvamevāvasīyate | atrāpinā sati saṃbhave laukikaliṅgasyāpi natvādipravṛttau nimittatvamiti sūcitam' ityādi mañjūṣāyāṃ pratyapīpadan | nanu tasya śabdaniṣṭatve śabdavācyatve'pyarthe tadanvayāsaṃbhavena paśunā yajeteti vihitayāgasya cchāgyāpi karaṇāpattirityata āha tacca liṅgamiti | arthaparicchedakatveneti | tadviśeṣaṇatve- netyarthaḥ | liṅgaviśeṣaviśiṣṭasyaivārthaviśeṣavācakatāyā liṅgānuśāsanaśāśtrasiddhatayā ca svāśrayavācyatvasaṃbandhenārthaviśeṣaṇatvopapattiḥ | evaṃ ca puṃstvaviśiṣṭapaśoreva vidhānācchagyā na tatkaraṇāpattiḥ, puṃliṅgasya paśuśabdasya puṃvyaktereva vācakatvamanyathā paraṃparayā tasyārtha- viśeṣaṇatvābhyupagamavaiyarthyāpatteriti bhāvaḥ | paśvādiśabdoktamiti | asya 'puṃstvaṃ paśuśabdārtha- paricchedakatvenānvetī'ti śeṣaḥ | paśu śtriyāṃ nāstīti | paśu yadi śtriyāṃ nāsti-paśunā yajeteti vākyāntargataḥ paśuśabdaḥ-nābhāvena puṃliṅgatvabodhanāt striyāṃ nāstīti hetoḥ paśunā yajetetyādividhiśchāgyādīnaṅgatvena na prayojayati - na grāhayatītyarthaḥ | nanu puṃliṅgaḥ paśuśabdaḥ puṃvyaktereva vācaka iti yadi niyamaḥ syāttadoktaṃ saṃbhavet, sa eva tu netyāśayena śaṅkate-na ceti | vyaktyādiśabdokteti | vyaktyādiśabdoktaliṅgasya yathā sādhāraṇyaṃ - svāśrayavācyatāsaṃbandhena pumādisaṃbaddhatvamata eva tattadarthavyāvartakaṃ, tathā paśvādiśabdoktaliṅgasyāpyuktaparaṃparāsaṃbandhena strīvyaktisaṃbaddhatvāttadapi na vyāvartakaṃ bhavet tanmātraniṣṭhasyaiva tadvayāvartakatvānna tu vyabhicāriṇa
iti samuditārthaḥ | paśvādiśabdoktasya-atra liṅgasyetyanuṣajyate | vyaktiśabdasya ktinnantatvena nityaśtrīliṅgatvāt sādhāraṇye'pi paśuśabdasya nityapuṃliṅgatve mānābhāvānna sādhāraṇyamiti pariharati-vyaktiśabdasyeti | vede darśanāditi | paśvetyādidarśanāt strīliṅge'pi paśuśabda iti bhāvaḥ . puliṅge nādeśapravṛtyoktarūpāsiddhiḥ spaṣṭaiva | tathā 'gherṅiti' iti guṇapravṛttyā paśva iti rūpāsiddhiśca | nanu strīliṅge'pi guṇapravṛttyoktarūpāsiddhistadavasthaiva | na ca 'ṅiti hrasvaśca' iti nadīsaṃjñāpravṛttyā na ghisaṃjñeti vācyam, tathā satyāṭpravṛttyoktadoṣatādavasthyādita cenna, chāntasatvādāḍapravṛtteḥ sūpapadatvamityāśayāt | 'jasādiṣu cchandasi vāvacanam' iti vacanena puṃliṅga'pyuktaprayogasiddhiriti tu na śaṅkyam, strīliṅge tadāśrayaṇaṃ vināpyuktarītyā nirvāhaḥ | āgamaśāśtrasya 'sāgaraṃ tarttukāmasye'tyādāvanityatvaṃ dṛṣṭamiti tatrāṅapravṛttyarthaṃ chāndasatvamanā- śrayaṇīyamityāśayāt | liṅgāntarasādhakahetvantaramapyāha-mīmāṃsāyāmiti | caturthe - caturthādhyāye | tatprathamacaraṇe etadavagantavyam | 'nanu niyatapuṃliṅgatve pramāṇābhāvā'dityasaṃgatam, 'upratyayāntāḥ puṃsī'ti vyākaraṇasyaiva mānatvādityāśaṅkya paśuśabdasya niyatapu'liṅgatve pramāṇasaddhāve'pi prakārāntareṇa cchāgīgrahaṇaprasakti vārayati vastutastviti | nābhāvābhāva ityukteriti | tathā ca paśvetyādiprayogānupapattirapi na paśuśabdasya niyatapuṃliṅgatve'pīti bhāvaḥ | nityapuṃstvanirṇayāditi | nityapuṃliṅgatvanirṇayādityarthaḥ | tathā ca puṃstvasya sādhāraṇyādvayāvartakatvānupapattiriti bhāvaḥ | tathā ca strīvyaktivyāvartikāyā uktayukterasaṃbhave'pi tadvayāvartakayuktyantaraṃ ddarśayati 'chāgo vā mantra-' iti-ṣaṣṭhādhyāyacaramādhikaraṇasiddhāntasūtram | tatra hi 'agnīṣomīyaṃ paśumālabhete'tyatra paśuśabdena nāśvādirgrāhyaḥ ki tvaja eva 'chāgasya vapāyāḥ' iti mantravarṇāditi nirṇītam, tadvat puṃstvasyāpi tata eva nirṇaya ityarthaḥ | yathā 'saṃdigdhe tu vākyaśeṣāt' ityadhikaraṇe vidhau saṃdehe vākyaśeṣānnirṇayaḥ saṃkhyādivat, yathā 'sārasvatau meṣau bhavata' ityatra taddhitaprakṛtiḥ śabdaḥ puṃliṅgaḥ strīliṅgo veti saṃdehe 'etadvai daivyaṃ mithunam' ityarthavādekaśeṣeṇobhayorgrahaṇamiti nirṇītam | nanvastūktasthale tāvadevaṃ tathāpi sarvanāmaniṣṭha- liṅgasya cetanācetanastrīpuṃsasādhāraṇatvāt 'prāsmā agri bharate'tyadhrigu ? praiṣasya striyāmapi pravṛttyāpattiḥ | na ceṣṭāpattiḥ | mīmāṃsakasiddhāntavirodhāt | laukikaliṅgasya vācyatvapakṣe tu na doṣaḥ, mukhyasaṃbhave gauṇaparigrahasyānyāyyatvenāropasyāpyasaṃbhavāditi cenna | pūrvopasthitatadrūpeṇopa- sthāpakādasmai enamityādi padācchāgatvādiviśiṣṭānāmiva puṃstvādiviśiṣṭānāmeva paśūnāmupasthityā meṣyāmapravṛtteḥ saṃbhavādityādiḥ-vistaro bṛhadbhūṣaṇe'nusaṃdheya ityāśayenāha-vistareṇa prapañcitaṃ bhūṣaṇe iti | liṅgasyārthaniṣṭhatvapakṣe'pi 'chāgo vā mantra-' ityādinyāyena puṃvyaktereva grahaṇamiti bodhyam | vaiśeṣikāstu puṃstvādayo jātayastathā ca haribhiruktam 
'tiśtro jātaya evaitāḥ keṣāṃcit samavasthitāḥ |
aviruddhā viruddhābhirgoṇahiṣyādijātibhiḥ ||' iti |
parasparaviruddhābhirgotvamahiṣatvādijātibhiḥ saha parasparamaviruddhā etāḥ samavasthitā ityarthaḥ | keṣāṃcid - vaiśeṣikāṇāmiti helārājaḥ | gotvādijātīnāṃ parasparamavirodhe mahiṣyādāvapi gavādivyavahārāpattirgavādāvapi mahiṣādivyavahārāpattistāsāṃ parasparavirodha- svīkāre'pi puṃstvādiriyaṃ vyaktirayaṃ padārtha ityādivyavahārasya sārvatrikatvādavirodha iti tātparyam
| tadvācyamiti | tat - niṅgāditrayam | tata eva - pratyayādeva | prakṛtereva vācakatvamita | na ca tatra prakṛterlakṣaṇeti vācyam, anupapattipratisaṃdhānaṃ vināpi bodhāt | anantaprakṛtiṣu śaktikalpanāgauravaṃ tu phalamukhatvānna doṣakṛt | prakṛtau gṛhītaśaktikasya prakṛtimātrādapi liṅgādibodhodayācca | liṅgānuśāsanasya prakṛtiviṣayasyaiva darśanācca | ata eva 'dvayekayoḥ-' ityādeṣcānirdiṣṭārthā iti nyāyena saṃkhyādyarthakatve siddhe niyāmakatvaṃ bhāṣyakṛdabhimataṃ saṃgacchate |
atiprasaṅgastu tātparyāderniyāmakasyāśravaṇāt pariharaṇīya ityāhuḥ | kecittu saṃkhyāmātraṃ vibhaktyarthaḥ, itaraccatuṣṭayaṃ prātipadikārtha ityāhuḥ | taduktam 
vācikā dyotikā vā syurdvitvādīnāṃ vibhaktayaḥ ||' iti |
atredaṃ bodhyam ghaṭādiśabde liṅgādinirūpitā pṛthak pṛthak śaktiḥ svīkāryā, ekapadopasthitānāmapyarthānāmākāṅkṣāvaicitryāt parasparamanvayaḥ | tatra liṅgasaṃkhyayoḥ prakṛtyarthe viśeṣaṇatvaṃ, kārakasya viśeṣyatvam | yadvā tasyāpi viśeṣaṇatvameva, dyotakatvāt, prātipadikasya liṅgasaṃkhyādiviśiṣṭārthakatve tvekatvaviśiṣṭe dvitvādiviśiṣṭe ca śaktikalpane gauravaṃ, gorūpamityādau saṃkhyādyanavacchinnabodhānupapattiśca, viśakalitapadārthatvapakṣe tātparyagrāhakābhāvāt tādṛśabodhanirvāhaḥ |
anye tu dhātuvadaviśiṣṭe eva śaktiḥ, kevalaghaṭatvādiviśiṣṭe'pi pṛthakśaktisvīkārāt saṃkhyādyaviṣayakabodhanirvāha ityāhuḥ | nanu pañcānāmapi prātipadikārthatve prātipadikārthaprakāraka- śābdabodhe subādijanyopasthitirheturityādervilayaprasaṅga iti cenna, vibhaktidyotyārthamādāya tadupapatterityeka āhuḥ | pare tu tadvilayepīṣṭāpattirdyotyārthasya viśeṣaṇatvena tādṛśabodhasya khapuṣpāyamāṇatvādityāhuḥ | vācakatāyā yuktatvāditi | prātipadikāpekṣayālpatarapratyeṣveva vācakatvakalpanāyā ucitatvamiti bhāvaḥ | 'śāstra' iti sāmānyapadaprayojanaṃ spaṣṭayati śāśtra iti | bahuṣviti || 25 ||
nanu śabdasya ghaṭo'stītyādivākyajanyaśābdabodhaviṣayatvānanubhavāt tattacchabdasya tattacchabda- vācyatvasādhanaparaṃ 'śabdo'pi yadi bhedena' ityādimūlamasaṃgatamityāśaṅkāṃ nirasayat śabdasya śābdabodhaviṣayatve'bhiyuktatamabhartṛharyanubhavaṃ pramāṇayati-na so'stīti | śabdānugamaḥ - śabdabhānam | anuviddhamiva - tādātmyena saṃbaddhamiva | jñāne āropitaṃ śabdatādātmyamiti bodhayitumivaśabdaḥ | pratyakṣādirūpasakalajñāne śabdabhānaṃ śabdārthayostādatmyādupapadyate | kecittu śabdārthayorbhedepyanu- bhavabalājjñānasāmānyasāmagrayā eva śabdabhāsakatvaṃ kalpyate'tastadupapattiḥ | arthajñānenodbodhaka- vidhayopasthāpitasya śabdasya pratyakṣādau arthāśe prakārībhūya bhānaṃ bādhakābhāvāt, anubhavācceti tu na yuktaṃ, śabdopasthāpakārthajñāne tadbhānāsaṃbhavāt | uktarītyānuvyavasāye eva tadbhānamupapādayituṃ śakyata ityāhuḥ | atra ghaṭādiśabdaṃ pakṣīkṛtya tattacchābdabodhaviṣayatvaṃ sādhyate tādṛśābhi- yuktoktyā sakalajātīyajñānaviṣayatvena bodhitatvarūpahetunā | yāvajjātīyajñānaviṣayatvaṃ tadantargatayatkiñcijjātīyajñānaviṣayatvaṃ vinā na saṃbhavatīti nāprayojakatvamiti bodhyam | ghaṭādiśabde viśiṣya tattacchābdabodhaviṣayatvasya sādhyatvapuraskāreṇa śabdostītyādivākyajanya-
śabdabodhaviṣayatvamādāya na siddhasādhanam ||
vināśabdaviṣayamiti | viṣṇvādiśabdarūpaviṣayaṃ vinetyarthaḥ | śabdaviṣayakatvaṃ vineti paryavasito'rthaḥ | śattayanatirekāt - śaktitulyatvāt | anāditātparyasyobhayatrāpyapekṣaṇāt svārasikalakṣaṇā na padamādadhāti, prayojanāsācivyāt | lakṣaṇāyā asaṃbhava eva yatra tādṛśaṃ sthalaṃ pradarśayati jabagaḍeti | śakyāgraheṇeti | ekākṣarakośādiprāmāṇyena pratyekavarṇasya śakyārthasatyepi tadagraheṇetyarthaḥ | nanu tatra lakṣaṇāyā agrahepi lakṣyārthabodhostu lakṣaṇarūpasāmagryā vidyamānatvā- dityāśaṅkyāha ajñātāyāśceti | castvarthe | anupayogāditi | tajjñānaṃ vinārthopasthitera- saṃbhavāt | sādhutāsaṃptipatteḥ - sādhutābhyupagamāt | spaṣṭaṃ cedamṛlṛk sūtre bhāṣye | nanu tatrāpi svajñāpyasaṃbandharūpā lakṣaṇā saṃbhavatyevetyāśaṅkyāha paranaya iti | naiyāyikanaye ityarthaḥ | evaṃ ñca svajñāpyasaṃbandho lakṣaṇeti svīkartṛmīmāṃsakamate tatsaṃbhave'pi tasya lakṣaṇātvāna- bhyupagantṛnaiyāyikanaye tatra śaktisvīkāra āvaśyakaḥ, anyathā bodho na syāt | vṛttyārthabodhajanakatvarūpārthavattvābhāvena prātipadikasaṃjñāpi na syāt, sādhutvaṃ ca na syāditi bhāvaḥ | kkacit - anukāryānukaraṇayorbhedavivakṣāyām | mūlasthatathetyasyārthamāha prātipadikārtha iti | siddha ityasya vivaraṇamupasthita iti | arthavaditi | arthe'vabhāsamāne śabdo bhāsate upasthita- tvāditi mūlārthaḥ, tatphalitārthaḥ - arthavadbhāsata iti | arthaśabdasyevaśabdena saha samāsastu na | tathā sati 'ivena samāsa' iti vibhaktiśravaṇāpatteḥ | nanu bhedābhedātmakapakṣadvaye'pyanukāryopasthitaye śabdāṃśe vṛttisvīkāravaśyakatayā mūle bhedapakṣe eva tadāvaśyakatākathanamayuktamatastadabhiprāyamāha vyākhyāyām ayaṃ bhāva iti | anukāryānukaraṇayorbhede iti | tayorbhedavivakṣāyāṃ paṭaditi kurvityādyanukaraṇānukāryayorbhedo dhvanimayatvavarṇamayatvādirūpaviruddhadharmasaṃbandhena siddha iti bhāvaḥ |
padānupasthitatvāditi | 'śaktyabhāve' ityādiḥ | tathā ca śaktyabhāvenukāryasya padādupasthityasaṃbhavādityarthaḥ | tatsiddhaye - upasthitisiddhaye | anyathā śābdabodhaviṣayatvamapi na syāt | vṛttyā padajanyapadārthopasthitereva śābdabodhahetutvāt | anukāryānukaraṇayorabhedapakṣetirikta- śakti vinaiva śābdabodhaviṣayatvaṃ bhavatītyāha abhedapakṣe pratyakṣe iti | hetutvāditi | ayamupasthiteḥ padajanyatve hetuḥ | atiprasaṅgeti | samavāyena ghaṭādiśabdajanyopasthitiviṣayasyā- kāśasya ghaṭostītyādivākyajanyaśābdabodhaviṣayatvāpattivāraṇāyetyarthaḥ | vṛttijanyapadopasthitireva heturiti sā ca na prakṛta iti na gavityayamāheti vākyajabodhaviṣayatānukāryasya saṃbhavatīti tātparyam | nanvāśrayatāsaṃbandhena śaktirūpavṛttimattvasya sattvepi nirūpakatāsaṃbandhenāsattvācchābda- bodhaviṣayatvānupapattistadavasthā,nirūpakatāsaṃbandhena tatsattvasyaiva śabdabhānaprayojakatvādityā- śaṅkyāhanirūpakateti | vṛttimata eveti | na tu nirūpakatayā vṛttimata evetyarthaḥ | kalpyata iti | anubhavānurodhād gauravasattvepi tathāvidhānyatarasaṃbandhena tatsatvasya śābdabhānaprayojakatvaṃ kalpayata iti bhāvaḥ | ubhayanirūpyatvāt - ubhayaniṣṭhatvāt | ubhayaspṛgeva hi saṃbandhaḥ | ata eva janyajanakabhāvaḥ avayavāvayavibhāvaḥ saṃbandha ityevaṃ rūpa eva tārkikādivṛddhānāṃ vyavahāraḥ | tadbodhakatvena - arthabodhakatvena | etena bodhyabodhakabhāvasaṃbandharūpā śaktirityāveditam, śabdasyāpi tadāśrayatvāt | evaṃ ca śabdasyāpi vṛttyā padajanyapadārthopasthiteḥ sattvānna bhānānupapattiriti bhāvaḥ | bhedapakṣe tu arthanirūpitaśaktyāśrayatvamanukārya eva nānukaraṇa ityanukaraṇenānukāryopasthityādyartha tatra
śaktyantarakalpanāvaśyakīti bhāvaḥ | 'svaṃ rūpaṃ śabdasya ' ityādisūtraṃ praṇayataḥ pāṇinerapyayamarthaḥ saṃmataḥ | svokte'rthe haryukti pramāṇayati uktaṃ ceti | tejasaḥ - dīpādeḥ | ete - grāhyatvagrāhakatvaśaktī ityarthaḥ | ayaṃ bhāvaḥ dravyacākṣuṣatvāvacchinnaṃ pratyālokasaṃyogo heturiti nirvivādam, anyathāndhakāre'pi ghaṭādipratyakṣāpatteścakṣusaṃyogadaśāyām | tathā ca yathā ghaṭadīpādyālokasaṃyoga eva ghaṭavat svasyāpi pratyakṣajanakastathārthagocaraśabdaniṣṭhaśaktisahakṛta eva śabdo'rthasya svasya ca pratyakṣajanakaḥ | yathā ca ghaṭacakṣuḥsaṃyogāsattve sa evālokasaṃyogaḥ svamātraviṣayakapratyakṣa- janakastathārthe tātparyābhāve śabde ca tātparye sati śabdastadvṛttyaiva svasyaiva bodhaṃ janayati | dravyatejobhedaniveśastu na, gauravāt prayojanavirahācca | evaṃ ca śrotreṇopasthite śaktigrahāt | etena gāmuccārayetyukte vikṛtasyaiva śrotropasthitatvāt uccāraṇāpattiḥ | śabdasyāprakṛtyarthatvāt tatra vibhaktyarthānvayānupapattiśca, svasyaiva pratyakṣeṇa śīghramupasthitatvād gaurastītyādāvapi śabdamātrabhānāpattirityādidūṣaṇābhāsā nirastāḥ | tātparyagrahasācivyād vṛttyaivāvikṛtasyaiva śabdādupasthiteḥ | anyathārthaparatvasyaiva nyāyyatvāt | tasmāduktatātparyanirṇaye sati śabdasyaiva prādhānyena bhānaṃ tadasattve tvarthaviśeṣaṇatvenaiveti siddhamiti vistaro bṛhadbhūṣaṇe'nusaṃdheyaḥ || 26 ||
ata ityasya vyākhyānamāha gavityayamityādinā | na bhidyanta iti | tattvena na vivakṣyante | evaṃ cānukaraṇaśabdānāmarthavattvābhāve'nukāryābhedavivakṣā hetuḥ | arthaṃvattvādītyādipadena kṛdantatvādisaṃgrahaḥ | ityādya pravṛttāviti | vṛttyārthabodhakatvarūpārthavattvābhāvāt | sādhutvamityupa- padyata iti | vakṣyamāṇarītyā'sādhutvābhāve śiṣṭaprayogādinā sādhutvamakṣatamevetyarthaḥ | nanvabhedābhāve'pi tasya sādhutvaṃ kuto netyaśaṅkyāha anyatheti | 'pratyayaḥ' 'paraśca' iti | pratyayavidhau pañcamīnirdeśāt ṣaṣṭhyaṃśavikalāyāḥ 'tasmādityuttarasya' iti paribhāṣāyāḥ pravṛttyaiva pratyayasya prakṛtiparatvalābhe siddhe 'paraśca' iti sūtraṃ niyamārtha, pratyayaparaiva prakṛtiḥ prayoktavyeti, tadetadabhipretyaiva paṭhyate na kevalā prakṛtiḥ prayoktavyā na kevalaḥ pratyayaḥ prayoktavya iti | tathā ca niyamaśāśtrāṇāṃ niṣedhamukhena pravṛttiritipakṣe niṣedhollaṅghanaṃ spaṣṭameva |vidhimukhena pravṛttiriti bhāṣyasiddhānte tvārthikaniṣedhollaṅghanamiti bodhyam | abhedapakṣe uktarītyā prātipadikatvābhāvena pratyayavidhābuddeśyatāvacchedakākrāntatvarūpaprakṛtitvābhāvena na kevaleti niṣedhasya padatvayogye eva padatvarahite 'apadaṃ ne'ti niṣedhapravṛttyā dvitīyaniṣedhasya cānavatāra iti bhāvaḥ | abhedapakṣe vṛttyā arthabodhakatve'pi śaktiviṣayabodhakatvābhāvānna prātipadikatvamiti bodhyam | pare tu sādṛśyamūlakā- bhedavivakṣāyāṃ sādṛśyasaṃbandhenaivānukaraṇenānukāryamupasthāpyate, vṛttijñānasyānukaraṇājanyaśābdatvameva kāryatāvacchedakamiti tathopasthitasyāpi śābdajñānaviṣayopapattiḥ | evaṃ ca vṛttyārthabodhakatvarūpārtha- vattvābhāvāt prātipadikatvaṃ na | apadaṃ na prayuñjītetyatrāpadamapariniṣṭhitamityarthaḥ, bhū sattāyāmityādinirdeśātpariniṣṭhatatvaṃ, evañcānukaraṇasyāpariniṣṭhitatvābhāvonnoktaniṣedhaviṣayatva- mityāhuḥ | imau dvau pakṣau jātivyaktipakṣavad vyavasthitau tena 'vārtaghnī paurṇamāsyāṃ kavatīṣu rathantaraṃ gāyati' ityādi śrutiprayogāḥ 'eruḥ' 'eco'yavāyāvaḥ' 'raṣābhyāṃ noṇaḥ ' ityādi sūtraprayogāścopapannāḥ, abhedapakṣasyaivāṅgīkāre subantāttaddhitotpatyā kavatīṣvityuktaśruti-
prayogānupapattiḥ syāt | evaṃ sūtraprayogānupapattirapi spaṣṭaiva | vyavasthitapakṣadvayāśrayaṇe sarveṣṭasiddhiḥ || 27 ||
iti vaiyākaraṇabhūṣaṇasāravyākhyāne nāmārthanirṇayaḥ samāptaḥ || 4 ||
<5>
samāsāniti | samāsatvaṃ ca pāṇinyādisaṃketasaṃbandhena samāsapadavattvam | samāsapadena ghaṭādirboddhavya ityākārakapuruṣāntarīyasaṃketasaṃbandhena samāsapadavattvasya ghaṭādāvativyaptiḥ syāditi pāṇinyādīti | vyākaraṇapraṇetṛmunītyarthaḥ | tathāvidhasya puruṣasya tathāvidhasaṃketastu na mānābhāvāt | teṣāmanyatra vyavahārābhāvādanyābhiyuktānāṃ vyavahārābhāvācca| ata eva yatkiñcitpuruṣīyasaṃketasaṃbandhena ghaṭādipadavattvaṃ na samāsatvam | abhiyuktānāṃ tathāvyavahārā- bhāvāt | arthabodhaphalakavibhattayavyavahitapūrvavartitvaprakārakajñānāviṣayapūrvapadaghaṭitanāmasamudāyatva samāsatvam | rājapuruṣa ityādāvapi rājādiśabde kasyacit puṃso vibhattayanusandhānasattve'pi tasyārthabodhaphalakatvābhāvānnāsambhavo'vyāptirvā | dadhisundaramastyānayetyādau tattadvibhattayanusaṃdhāna- sattve eva śābdabodhopagamānna tatrātiprasaṅgaḥ | samāsasthale tu pūrvapade vibhattayanusaṃdhānaṃ vinaiva tadupagamānna doṣa iti tu na yuktam | dadhi sundaramityādau vibhaktipadamajānato'pi bodhodayāt tatrātivyāpteḥ |
nanu vibhattayanusaṃdhānaṃ vinā bodhānaṅgīkāreṇa nātivyāptiriti cenna | vinagamanāviraheṇa samāse'pi tathā vaktuṃ śakyatayāsaṃbhavāpatteḥ | na ca samāse padayoravyahitapūrvāparībhāvasyā- kāṅkṣātvena tajjñānasya śābdabodhe hetutayā vibhaktyanusandhānaṃ vinaiva bodhopagama ucitaḥ, asamāse tu prātipadikasya vibhaktyavyavahitapūrvavartitvaprakārakajñānaviṣayasyaivākāṅkṣātvena tatra vibhaktyanu- sandhānamāvaśyakamiti vācyam, aluksamāse vyabhicāreṇa tathāvidhāvyavahitapūrvāparībhāvajñānasya hetutvāsaṃbhavāt, na ca tatra 'dāsyāḥ putre'tyādisamudāyasyaivākāṅkṣātvānna doṣa iti vācyam | evamapi 'dāsyāḥ putraḥ' 'kaṇṭhekāla' ityaluksamāse'vyāpteḥ, samudāyaghaṭakavibhaktijñānasyāpi bodha- janakatvāt, arthopasthitidvārā bodhajanakatvaṃ tatra niveśyamato na tatrāvyāptiḥ, tathā vibhakterarthopasthāpakatvābhāvāt | ata eva 'goṣucara' ityatra bahuvacanasattve'pi bahutvabodho na, spaṣṭaṃ cedaṃ samarthasūtrabhāṣye iti cedevamapi 'paryabhūṣat,' 'pacatabhṛjjate'tyādāvavyāptirdurvāraiva | alakṣyatvena parihārastu nocita iti digiti vadanti | anye tu tārkikā yādṛśamahāvākyottarastvatalādi- pratyayo yādṛśārthaviṣayatānirūpitasvārthaviṣayatāśālibodhajanakastādṛśavākye tādṛśortha samāsaḥ, tādṛśārthabodhakatādṛśavākyatvaṃ samāsatvamiti yāvat bhavati hi 'kṛṣṇaśrita' ityādau taduttaratvādipratyayena pūrvapadārthaviśiṣṭottarapadārthānvitasvārthabodhajananāt | pācakādikaṃ pākakriyā- kartrarthavākyamapi svaghaṭakānekanāmalabhyatādṛśārthapratipādakatvarūpamahatvābhāvānna mahāvākyamiti na tatrātiprasaṅgaḥ | nanūpakumbhādāvavyāptiḥ, kumbhapadāsyaiva lakṣaṇayā kumbhasamīpārthakatvāditi cenna, prakṛtyarthamātrāvacchinnapratyayārthānvayabodhāyogyatvarūpamahattvasya niveśāt, ata eva 'nīlaghaṭatva'- mityatra nīlaghaṭatvamitibhāge nātiprasaṅgaḥ, uktamahāvākyatvābhāvāt | na ca mātrapadānarthakyaṃ
'kṣīrapāyī' 'haritrāta' ityādāvavyāptiprasaṅgāt, tasya prakṛtibhūtadhātvarthaviśiṣṭaṇinyādirūpakṛdartha- viṣayakabodhajanakatve'pi na prakṛtyarthamātraviśiṣṭakṛdarthabodhahetutvamapi tu kṣīrādyanvitapānādi- viśiṣṭakṛdarthakartrādibodhajanakatvamiti tatsattve nātiprasaṅgaḥ | 'rājñaḥ puruṣatva'mityādito rājanyeva puruṣabhāvaḥ pratīyate, na tu rājasaṃbandhādyanvitapuruṣe tvapratyayārthaḥ | evaṃ 'tava putratvameṣyāmī'tyādāvapi putravṛttibhāve yuṣmadarthanirūpitatvasya ṣaṣṭhyantārthasyānvayastaddhitānāṃ prakṛtyarthamātrānvitasvārthabodhakatvavyutpatteḥ | atra tvādisthāne pratyayamātrasya, taddhitamātrasya vā niveśe 'rājñaḥ putrīya' ityādau rājasaṃbandhaviśiṣṭaputrasyaiva chapratyārthe'nvayād 'rājñaḥ putre'ti bhāge'tiprasaṅga iti viśiṣya tvādyupādānaṃ, nityasāpekṣatayā ekādeśānvaye'pi bādhakābhāvāt | taddhitānāṃ prakṛtyartheti vyutpattistaddhitaviśeṣaparatvena saṃkocanīyā | ata eva 'paramagārgyasyāpatya' mityarthe gārgyaśabdāt phagiti 'yena vidhiḥ-' iti sūtrasthabhāṣyasaṃgatiḥ, anyathā prakṛtyarthātiriktaparamānvitaprakṛtyarthānvitasvārthabodhakatvādasaṃgatiḥ spaṣṭaiva | sasaṃbandhikārthatirikta- prakṛtyarthaparā vā | yadi tatra sājasaṃbandhasya paraṃparayā prakṛtyarthaviśiṣṭapratyayārtha evānvayastadātaddhitasāmānyopādāne'pi na kṣatiriti bodhyam | 'rājñaḥ puruṣasya dhana'mityatra rājasaṃbandhānvitapuruṣasya ṣaṣṭhyarthasvāmitvādāvanvayādrājñaḥ puruṣeti bhāge'tiprasaṅga iti pratyayasāmānyopekṣetyāhuḥ | atra pakṣe 'pibatakhādatā' 'pacatabhṛjjate'tyādāvavyāptivirahe'pi dāsīsaṃbandhasya ṣaṣṭhyantārthasya 'dāsyāḥ putratva'mityatra putrānvitatvapratyayārthe evānvayāt na tu putrapadārthe |
ata eva rājñaḥ putratvamityetaddhaṭake rājñaḥ putreti bhāge'tiprasaṅgo nirākṛtaḥ, vyaste eva tathānvaya ityatra niyāmakābhāvaḥ | pradāyetyādeḥ kriyāyāmevānvayāttataḥ paryabhūṣadityādeśca tvādyabhāvāttatroktalakṣaṇanayamāsaṃbhavāt, tadarthasya prathamārthenaivānvayāt tasya prātipadikatve'pi ṣaṣṭhyantatvavirahāt, taduttaravibhakternirarthakatvācca | eteṣāmalakṣyatvena parihārastu na camatkārī tyavehi | pradāya prasthāya anuvyacalat paryabhūṣadityādāvavyāptiriti vadantyapare iti kṛtaṃ pallavitena | padadvayamapīti | apinā padatrayādiparigrahaḥ | tena 'dvayahnajātaḥ' 'citrājaradguḥ' 'hotupotṛneṣṭodgātāra' ityādisamāsasya nāsaṃgrahaḥ, tathā cānekasubantaghaṭitatvaṃ vibhājakatāva- cchedakamiti bhāvaḥ | rājeti | 'upapadamatiṅ' iti sūtrabhāṣyaprāmāṇyād 'dvitīyāśrita-' ityādāvapi 'gatikārakopapadānām-' iti paribhāṣāpravṛttyānekasubantatvāghaṭitatvāt prathamopa- sthitetyādinyāyasya nijāṃ trayāṇāmityatra trigrahaṇādatra śāstre'nāśrayaṇāt prasiddhatamatvācca kṛṣṇaśritādīnapahāya rājapuruṣa ityasya pradarśanamiti bodhyam |
paryabhūṣaditi | 'devān kratunā paryabhūṣa'diti ṛksaṃhitāyām | atra 'saha-' iti yogavibhāgāt samāsaḥ | atra 'samāsasya' ityantodāttatvaṃ bādhitvā tatpuruṣe tulyārtha-' ityavyaya- pūrvapadāprakṛtisvare prāpte tadapavādena 'gatikārakopa-' iti sūtreṇottarapadaprakṛtisvareṇāṭsvarasyā- vasthānaṃ bhavati, vārtikakāreṇa tatratyakṛdgrahaṇasya pratyākhyānāt | nanvatra samāsasya 'saha-' iti yogavibhāgena bhāṣye sādhanena tatpuriṣatvābhāvena tatpuruṣe vidhīyamānasvarasya kathamatra pravṛttiścedatrāhuḥ 'nityaṃ krīṅā-' iti sūtre 'nityam-' iti yogavibhāgena bhāṣye'tra samāsasya svīkāratroktadoṣa iti 'yatrā naro devayanto yugāni vitanvate' ityādau tu tricakrāditvakalpane-
nāntodāttatvaṃ bodhyam | atra kṛdgrahaṇābhāve prabhavanamityādāveva kriyāpradhāne syānna tu praṇī- rityādāviti kṛdgrahaṇaṃ kṛnmātraparigrahārthe kartavyameveti siddhāntapakṣe tu bāhalakādeva paryabhūṣadityādāvuttarapadādyudāttatvaṃ bodhyamiti kaiyaṭodyotādau vistaro'nusaṃdheyaḥ | nanu bhāṣye tiṅgrahaṇāt supsupetyubhayorapi nivṛtteruktatayā supāṃ nāmnetyasaṃgataṃ, padadvaye'pi subutpatteḥ prāgeva samāsapravṛtterityāśaṅkāpanodāyāha atrottarapade iti | ityarthaditi | asya 'supāṃ nāmneti nāsaṃgata'mīti śeṣaḥ | atiṅgrahaṇāt tṛtīyāntaṃ supetyetadeva nānuvartate, prathamāntaṃ subiti tvanuvartata eva | bhāṣyaṃ tu samudāyānanuvṛttitātparyakam | ekānuvṛttāvapi ubhayānuvṛtterabhāvasyā- kṣatatvānna tātparyānupapattiḥ | athavā subityanuvṛttyaivaidhānāhārako vrajatītyādervyāvṛttisiddhāva- tiṅgrahaṇādubhayorapi nivṛttirbhāṣyoktā yathāśrutaivāstu | evamappupapadamiti mahāsaṃjñākaraṇādupasamīpe uccāritaṃ padamityanvarthatāśrayaṇāt purvapade subutpattyanantarameva samāsaḥ | 'supyajātau-' iti ṇineḥ subutpattyanantarameva saṃbhavena taṃ vinopapadatvābhāvenādoṣācca | nirgata ityādāvapi na doṣaḥ, śabdāntarasaṃbandhāt pūrvamantaraṅgatvāt subutpattau suki ca padatvottarameva padāntarasaṃbandhāditi bhāvaḥ | uktavyākhyāyāḥ prayojanamāha anyatheti | anyathā - pūrvapade'pi subutpatteḥ prāgeva samāsapravṛttau | na lopānāpatteriti padatvābhāvāt | kaṭaprūriti | kaṭaṃ pravata iti kaṭaprūḥ | prūṅ gatāvityasmāt kartari kkip | āyatastūriti | āyataṃ syautītyarthaḥ | ubhayatrāpi 'kkibvaci-' iti dhātordīrghaḥ | vārtikāditi | vārtike nipātanāt samāsaḥ | pibateti | pibatakhādateti satataṃ yasyāṃ kriyāyāmabhidhīyate sā pibatakhādatetyarthaḥ | ākhyātamiti | kriyāyāḥ sātatye - nairantarthe gamye tiṅantaṃ tiṅantena samasyate ityarthaḥ | sūtrāditi | atra samāsa ityanuṣajyate |
jahistamba iti | stambādhātakartā samāsārthaḥ | jahikarmaṇeti | jahītyetat tiṅantaṃ karmārthakasubantena samasyate, āṅīkṣṇye gamye, kartrabhiprāyakaścet samāsastadetyarthaḥ ||
tadbhedaṃ - samāsabhedam | mūle-yoyaṃ pūrveti | yoyaṃ pūrvapadārthaprādhānyādaviṣayo vādaḥ - purvapadārthapradhāno'vyayībhāva ityādivādaḥ | so'pi prāyovādaḥ - prāyika ityarthaḥ | prācīnoktavibhāgasya prāyikatve hetumāha bhūtapūrva ityādinā | asaṃgrahāditi | teṣāṃ samāsānāmavyayībhāvādisaṃjñāvivirmuktatvāt | tatra bhūtapūrva ityatra 'sup supā' iti, āyatastūrityatra 'kkivvaci ' iti vārtike nipātanāt, vāgarthāvivetyatra 'ivena samāsaḥ ' iti samāsaḥ | pūrvapadārtheti | pūrvapadārthaḥ pradhānaṃ yatretyarthaḥ | tathā ca pūrvapadārthaniṣṭha- mukhyaviśeṣyatākabodhajanako'vyayībhāva iti paryavasito'rthaḥ | tena rājapuruṣa ityādāvapi rājatvādiniṣṭhaprākāratānirūpitarājādiniṣṭhaviśeṣyatākabodhajanakatve'pi na tatrātivyāptiḥ, rājādau tajjanyabodhīyamukhyaviśeṣyatāvirahāt | evamagre'pi bodhyam | upakumbhamityādyavyayībhāvasya kumbhasamīpamityākārakasya pūrvapadārthamukhyaviśeṣyakabodhasya janakatvāllakṣaṇasaṃgatiḥ | ubhayapadārtheti | anekapadārthapradhāna ityarthaḥ | tena tryādipadaghaṭitasamāsasya nāsaṃgrahaḥ | paraspareti | parasparasya vyabhicārāt - lakṣaṇalakṣitāvacchedakayoḥ parasparābhāvavaddhatitvādityarthaḥ | vyabhicāraṃ padarśayati-tathā hīti | avyāptiriti | avyayībhāvalakṣaṇasyāvyāptiḥ | anyapadārthaprādhānye gamakamāha 'anyapadārthe ca' iti |
samāsāditi ayamanyapadārthaprādhānye hetuḥ | uttarapadārthaprādhānyasattvāditi | supsaṃbandhileśa ityākārakottarapadārthaviśeṣyakabodhajanakatvādityarthaḥ | nanvavyayībhāvasamāsa- vidhāyake'vyayasya prathamānirdiṣṭatvāt kathamatrāvyayasyottarapadatvamityata āha 'sup pratinā' iti |
pūrvapadārtheti | pippalīsaṃbandhyarghamiti tato bodhodayāt | bhāṣyakāraistu samudāye dṛṣṭā iti nyāyena pippalī kāyādiśabdānāṃ pippalyādyavayavaparatārdhavippalī-pūrvakāya ityādiḥ karmadhārasya eva bhavati | evaṃ ca 'artha napusaṃkam' ityādi sūtratrayaṃ na kartavyamata evordhvakāyādisiddhāntitaṃ, tanmate tatrottarapadārthaprādhānyasattvena vyabhicārābhāve'pyanyo rājetyarthaka 'rājāntara'mityatra pūrvapadārthaprādhānya- bodhajanake mayūravyaṃsakādisamāse sarvamate vyabhicāro draṣṭavyaḥ | bhinnavācino'ntaraśabdasya napusaṃka- tayā samāsasya mayūkhyaṃsakādigaṇe nipātanāt paravalliṅgatā, etena 'paravalliṅgamiti sūtre bhāṣye ardhapippalītyādestatpuruṣodāharaṇasya karmadhāratvopagamenottarapadārthaprādhānyamāśrityānyathāsiddhi kṛtvā tatratyatatpuruṣagrahaṇapratyākhyānena kathamatra paravalliṅgateti dūṣaṇaṃ nirastam | atra pūrvapadārthatāvacchedakadharmāśrayabuddhisthayatkiñcitpratiyogikatvaṃ vyutpattibalalabhyam | evaṃ 'nṛsomaḥ' 'prasannena dṛgabjene'tyādāvapi vyabhicāro draṣṭhavyaḥ | dṛgabjenetyādau mayūravyaṃsākāditvāt samāsaḥ | tata eva paravalliṅgatā, pūrvapadārthapradhānatā ca | rājāntaramiti tadvihitasamāse tasyādṛṣṭatvāt | sāmānyavācakaprayogāt 'upamitaṃ vyāghrādibhiḥ ' iti samāsastu nātra padamādadhāti | upāttadharma eva sādṛśyaprayojakastadupādāna ityarthasya kāvyaprakāśe daśame sphuṭatvādanupāttadharmeṇaivātra sādṛśyamiti na yuktam | purenduḥ sakala ityādāvanupāttadharmeṇa sādṛśyāsaṃbhāvācca | kiṃ cedaṃ sūtraṃ samānaliṅgavacanatva eva pravartate, anyathā 'paravalliṅgam-' iti sūtre 'tatpuruṣaścāpi kaḥ prayojayati, yaḥ pūrvapadārthapradhānaḥ, ekadeśisamāsaḥ, ardhapippalīti,' iti bhāṣyasya dvandvaikadeśinoriti nyāsena tatpuruṣagrahaṇapratyākhyānaparabhāṣyasya cāsaṃgatvāpatteḥ | dṛgabjenetyāderapi pūrvapadārthapradhānasya dṛśyamānaparavalliṅgasya sattvāt | mukhacandra ityādāvapyanenaiva tasya pūrvapadārtha- pradhānatvāccandrarūpakavyavahāraḥ, paravalliṅgatā ca tata eveti mañjūṣāyāṃ spaṣṭam | ubhayapadārtha- prādhānyāditi, dvitrā ityato dvivyanyatara iti bodhotpatteḥ | dvandvātivyāptiriti | tallakṣaṇāti- vyāptirityarthaḥ | evamagre'pi | samāhārānyeti | samāhārarūpo yo'nyapadārthastatprādhānyādityarthaḥ | dvandvāvyāptiśceti | dhavakhadirāvityādītaretarayoge ubhayapadārthaviśeṣyakabodhajakatvādisaṃbhavo neti bhāvaḥ | vastutastu 'cārthe dvandvaḥ' iti sūtrabhāṣyaparyālocanayetaretarayoge'pi bahuvrīhāvanyapadārthasyeva cārthasamūhasyaiva prādhānyaṃ labhyate, tatra hi cārthasyāsattvabhūtatayā tatparatipādakasamāsasyāpya- liṅgāsaṃkhyatāpattimāśaṅkya svabhāvena parihāraṃ kṛtvā guṇavacanavadyaddravyāśritaḥ samāsārthastad- dravyaliṅgasaṃkhyāgrāhaka iti ca samādhānamuktam | ata evedaṃ bhāṣyaṃ na samāhāradvandvaparaṃ, tasya tadgrāhitvābhāvāt, tatretaretarayoga udbhūtāvayavabhedaḥ samūhaḥ, samāhāradvandve tvanudbhūtāvayavaḥ sa iti viśeṣaḥ, udbhūtāvayavabhedatvamavayavayagāropitasaṃkhyāvattvam, avayavagatasaṃkhyānārope- 'nudbhūtāvayavabhedatvam, idameva tirohitāvayavabhedatvamityādyanyatra vistaraḥ | etanmate tūktalakṣaṇāsaṃbhavo bahuvrīhilakṣaṇātivyāptiśca dvandve bodhyā | evaṃ prācīnoktānāṃ 'pūrvapadārthapradhāna' ityādīnāmavyayībhāvādilakṣaṇattvaṃ dūṣayitvā siddhāntimate tallakṣaṇānyāha siddhāntetviti |
avyayībhāvādhikāreti | avyayībhāvādhikārāntargatalakṣaṇavihitasamāsatvamityarthaḥ | tenopakumbhāde- stadadhikāre pāṭhābhāve'pi na kṣatiḥ, tattadaghikārapaṭhitādeśādau nātivyāptiḥ, teṣāṃ samāsatvābhāvāt, samāsatvaṃ prāguktameveti na doṣaḥ | pare tu pāṇinyādisaṃketasaṃbandhenāvyayī- bhāvādipadavattvameva tattallakṣaṇamiti pratipannāḥ | avyayībhāvādivyavahāraviṣayatvaṃ na tallakṣaṇamitya- pare jaguḥ | bhautapūrvyādita mūle pūrva bhūtaṃ bhūtapūrva, bhūtapūrvameva bhautapūrvya, 'svārthe ṣyañ' | vṛtteḥ pūrvakāle bhūtaṃ yat pūrvapadārthaviśeṣyakabodhajanakatvaṃ tasmāddhetoḥ so'pi - utsārgau'pi gavayādivadāsthitaḥ, - svīkṛta ityarthaḥ | bhautapūrvyādityanenoktisaṃbhava uktaḥ | na samāsalakṣatva- miti | rekhāgavayādeniṣṭhalāṅgūlādervāstavapaśugatatvābhāvānna vāstavapaśulakṣaṇatvaṃ yathā, tathā tādṛśatādṛśabodhajanakatvasya tattatsamāsaniṣṭhatvābhāvānna tattatsamāsalakṣaṇatvaṃ, tatra viśiṣṭa- śaktisvīkāreṇāvayavānāṃ nairarthakyāditi bhāvaḥ | bodhakatā tadvadeveti | rekhāgavayādiniṣṭalā- ṅgūlāderthathā sādṛśyena paśuniṣṭhalāṅgādibodhanadvārā vāstavapaśubodhakatvaṃ, tathā vṛtteḥ prāk tatta- tpadārthatvaṃ yatra dṛṣṭaṃ tattadarthaviśeṣyakabodhajanakatayā tattadāśraye ttatsamāsabodhakatvamiti bhāvaḥ ||
suvacamiti | tathā ca prācīnoktāni lakṣaṇāni nāsambhavaduktikānīti bhāvaḥ | jahatsvārtheti | jahatsvārthatvamevaikārthībhāvaḥ, ajahatsvārthatvameva vyapekṣeti bhāvaḥ | ekārthībhāvapakṣe samudāyaśaktyaiva viśiṣṭārthopasthitisaṃbhave'vayavā na svārthopasthiti kurvanti, vaiyarthyāt | ajahatsvārthatāṃ vinā parasparamanvayākāṅkṣārūpavyapekṣāyā asaṃbhavādvayapekṣāpakṣe'vayavāḥ svārtho- pasthāpakā eveti bhāvaḥ | uktavyavastheti | pūrvapārthapradhāno'vyayībhāva ityādivyavasthetyarthaḥ |
nāsaṃbhavinīti | upakumbhamityādyavyayībāvādau tasya saṃbhavāt | yathā sūpapatotyādāvavyā- ptyativyāptirūpadoṣasattve'pi samanantaramabhihito'saṃbhavo'saṃbhavītyarthaḥ | jahati padānīti | 'saptamī viśeṣaṇe ' iti sūtre saptamīgrahaṇājjñāpakādvayadhikaraṇānāmapi padānāṃ kkacidbahuvrīhi- rbhavatīti bhāvaḥ | jahati svāni - vṛttighaṭakībhūtapadāni yaṃsa jahatsvastādṛśā'rtho yasyāmiti samānādhikaraṇapadako'pi saṃbhavatīti pare | vṛttau padānāmānarthakyamiti | śaktapadaghaṭakavarṇānāmiva vṛttighaṭakapadānāmānarthakyānaṅgīkāre padaghaṭakavarṇānāmapyānarthakyaṃ na syāditi bhāvaḥ |
nanu padaghaṭakavarṇānāṃ kutrāpyarthābodhakatayānarthakye'pi rājādipadānāṃ vākye'rthabodhakatayā samāse vākyadṛṣṭārthabodhotpādena ca na nairarthakyasaṃbhava ityāśaṅkyāha aya bhāva iti | rājaviśiṣṭeti | svāmitvādisaṃbandhena rājaviśiṣṭapuruṣetyarthaḥ | rājñaḥ puruṣa ityatrāpi saṃbandhasya saṃsargatayaiva bhānādbṛttivākyayoḥ samānārthatvanirvāhaḥ | vākyajabodhe saṃbandhasya prakāratvopagame tu rājapuruṣādisamudāyasya rājasaṃbandhaviśiṣṭapuruṣabodhakatvamaṅgīkāryam | saṃbhave - saṃbhave sati | tadbodhakatvam - rājādibodhakatvam | kalpyamiti | na kalpyamityanvayaḥ, niṣprayojanatvāt bodhāvṛttiprasaṅgācceti bhāvaḥ | nanu saṃbhedenānyataravaiyarthyamiti nyāyena dvāvapūpāviva na bodhāvṛttiprasaṅgaḥ, tathā ca vṛttighaṭakapadānāmarthabodhakatve na kicidbādhakamityata āha-vṛṣabheti | vṛṣaśabdasya vṛṣabhasamānārthakatve'pi vṛṣabhasabdaghaṭakasya tasya na tadbodhakatvamityanubhavasiddhamevaṃ yāvakaghaṭakayāvaśabdasya, tathā ca śaktatāvacchedakānanupraviṣṭāyā evānupūrvyāḥ śaktatāvacchedakatva- mityavaśyamaṅgīkāryam | tathā ca kathaṃ vṛttighaṭakapadānāṃ bodhakatvasaṃbhava iti bhāvaḥ | nanu vṛṣavṛṣabhādiśabdānāṃ paryāyatayā vṛṣabhaśabdaghaṭakavṛṣaśabdasya bodhakatvābhāve'pi rājapuruṣaśabdayoḥ
samānārthatvābhāvāt samāsaghaṭakarājadipadasya nairarthakye sādhakābhāvaḥ, ekārthasthala eva śaktatāvacchedakāghaṭaketyādiniyama iti prakṛte na doṣaḥ, ki ca yogarūḍhe paṅkajādiśabde śaktatāvacchedakasamudāyaniṣṭānupūrvyanupraviṣṭhāyā avayavānupūrvyāḥ śaktatāvacchedakatayā tathā niyamāsaṃbhavaśca | evaṃ ca samāse nāyaṃ aniyamaḥ ki tu samāse evetyāśaṅkyāvayavānāṃ bodhakatve bādhakamāha-rājapadeneti | anyathā - samāsādyavayavasya svārthabodhakatve | ityasyāpyāpattiriti | jahatsvārthatve tu rājādyarthasya puruṣādiviśeṣaṇatayaivopasthityā na doṣaḥ |
na jahatīti | idaṃ śatrantaprakṛtikajasantam | atrāpi na jahati svāni yān te'jahatsvāstā dṛśā arthā yasyāmiti bahuvrīhigarbho bahuvrīhirbodhyaḥ | nanu samudāyaśaktyaiva rājādiviśiṣṭapuruṣādibodhasaṃbhave'vayavānāṃ pūrvoktarītyā na svārthopasthāpakatvaṃ saṃbhavatītya- jahatsvārthatvapakṣo'saṃbhavadiktika ityāśaṅkāṃ nirākaroti ayamabhiprāya iti | nanu rājāda- saṃbandhabodha eva tatkalpakaḥ, na ca saṃsargatayā bhānamanupasthitasyāpyaviruddhamiti vācyam, nāmārthayorbhedasaṃbandhena bodhasya vyutpattiviruddhatvādityata āha-klapteti | gauravaparāhatatvāditi | nāmārthayoriti vyutpattiḥ samāsavyatiriktaviṣayeti svatvādisaṃsargakabodho nānupapanna iti bhāvaḥ | vyutpattau vṛttighaṭaketaratvaviśeṣaṇaprakṣepe gauravamata āha-klaptaśaktīti | nanvayaṃ pakṣo na munisaṃmata ityata āha-ayameveti | rājapadasya rājasaṃbandhini lakṣaṇā, rājasaṃbandhinaḥ puruṣeṇābhedānvaya iti pakṣasyaivena vyavacchedaḥ | matāntaratveneti | tathā ca samarthasūtre bhāṣyaṃ 'parasparavyapekṣāṃ sāmarthyameke' iti | citragurityādibahuvrīhāvapi samudāyaśakye'nyapadārthe citrādyanvitāyā goḥ svasvāmi- bhāvādisaṃbandhenānvaya iti tatrāpi vyapekṣāsaṃbhavaḥ | dvandve'pi samudāyarthe samūhe vartipadārtha- yoravayavāvayavibhāvasaṃbandhenānvaya iti tatrāpi tatsaṃbhavaḥ | anyapadārthādeḥ samudāyaśakyatve mānaṃ tu-'anekamanyapadārthe' 'jārthe dvandva' iti ca sūtramityavagantavyam | vyapekṣāvirahādeva bhāryā rājñaḥ puruṣo dedadattasyetyādau rājapuruṣādipadayorna samāsaḥ | niśeṣaṇānvayāpattiriti | taddhetu- svātantryeṇopasthitisattvāt | vārtikāditi | samāsādau śaktyantarakalpanāpekṣayaitadvacanārambho laghīyāniti bhāvaḥ | vyavasthā sūpapādetīti | avyāptyādisattve'pyasaṃbhavo neti bhāvaḥ | āvaśyakaścāyaṃ pakṣaḥ | ekārthībāva rūpasāmarthyasya samarthasūtre parigrahe parāṅgavadbhāvasaṃgrahaḥ syāt, tatraikārthībhāvāsaṃbhavāt, na ceṣṭāpattiḥ, tathā sati 'yatte divo duḍitarmartabhojanam' ityatra diva ityasya parāṅgavadbhāvenāmantritanighāto yathā bhavati, tathā 'ṛtena mitrāvaruṇāvṛtāvṛdhāvṛtaspṛśā' ityatra ṛtenetyasyāpi paraṅgavadbhāvenāmantritādyudāttatāpattiḥ, padāt paratvābhāvena pādādisthitatvena cāṣṭamikanighātāprāpteḥ | vyapekṣārūpasāmarthyapakṣe tu samarthaparibāṣāpravṛttau satyāṃ ṛtenetyasya ānaśāthe ityarthakāśāthe iti padopattavyāptikriyāyāmevānvayena mitrāvaruṇābhyāmityanena vyapekṣābhāvānnoktadoṣaḥ, spaṣṭaṃ cedaṃ vedabhāṣyādāviti bodhyam | dve api vṛttīti | jahatsvārthājahatsvārtharūpe dve api vṛttī ityarthaḥ | bhadaśabdasya viśeṣe'pi prayogadarśanādāha-bhado- 'nyonyābhāva iti | nanu bhedasya vācyatve rājapadārthasya bhedaḥ puruṣe ? puruṣasya, rājaviśiṣṭapuruṣasya vā bhedo-rājñi pratīyate, nādyaḥ-puruṣaviśeṣaṇatāpannasya tasya bhede viśeṣaṇatayānvayāsaṃbhavāt, rājabhedasya rājasaṃbandhipuruṣāmātre sattvena tadbhedasyāvyāvartakatvācca, vakṣyamāṇaprayojanānarvāhācca | na dvitīyaḥ puruṣatvarūpānvayitāvacchedakāvacchinnapratiyoditāka-
bhedasya rājani bādhitatvāt, tatpratiyogikabhedasyāvyāvartakatvāt | rājadhanamityādau dhanatvādyavacchinnapratiyogikabhedasya pūrvapadārthe sattve'pi tadbodhasyānupayogācca | ata eva na tṛtīyo'pi, tadbodhasyānupayogādityāśaṅkāmapākartumāha-tathā ceti | bhedasya vācyāntarbhāve cetyarthaḥ | iti bodha iti | rājabhinnāsaṃbandhīti bodha ityarthaḥ | yathāśrutabodhākārastu na yuktaḥ, tādṛśabodhasya rājasaṃbandhibhinnabhedaprakārakatayā rājasaṃbandhibhinna ityākārakabodhaṃ pratyeva pratibandhakatvaṃ na tvitarasaṃbandhabodhaṃ pratīti rājapuruṣo devadattasya cetyādiprayogasyāvāraṇāt | rājapuruṣa iti samudāyasya rājabhinnasaṃbandhibhinne śaktiḥ, saṃbandhītyatra saṃbandho yathāyathaṃ svasvāmibhāvādirgrāhyaḥ, ata eva prītiviṣayatvādirūpasaṃbandhatātparyeṇa rājapuruṣo bhāryāyāśceti prayogo bhāvatyeva, tadbodhaṃ prati samāsajanyabodhasya rājabhinnāsvāmika ityākārakasyāpratibandha- katvāt, yadi sarvathā tādṛśaprayogo neṣyate, tatā saṃbandhasāmānyamevopādeyam | paraṃ tvasvāmike- 'pītyuttaragranthasvārasyaṃ pūrvakalpa eveti pratīyate | 'rājā puruṣaṃ nivartayatyanyebhyaḥ' iti samarthasūtrabhāṣya svārasyaṃ ca etatpakṣe | rājasaṃbandhī puruṣa iti bodhastu vyaktiviśeṣavṛttitvā- diviśaṣṭatādṛśabhedahetukānumitirūpapārṣṇika iti bodhyam | prayogāpatti ceti | asvāmike'pi rājabhinnasvāmikabhedasya sattvāditi bhāvaḥ | ubhayam - bhedaḥ saṃsargaścetyarthaḥ | pare tu jahatsvārthā nāma usasarjanapadānāṃ svārthānupasthāpakatvaṃ, tathā ca rājādipadasāṃnidhyāt puruṣapadameva rājasaṃbandhipuruṣabodhakam, puṭe jampakapuṣpādyasattve'pyāmodānvayamādāya campakapuṭo mallikāpuṭa iti vyavahāravadrājapadārthaḥ puuṣe viśeṣaṇamiti vyavahāra upapadyate | ata eva jahatsvārthāpadasya jahati upasarjanapadāni svārtha yasyāṃ setyartha ukto helārājīye | tathā coktaṃ samarthasūtre bhāṣye- 'athavānvayādviśeṣaṇaṃ bhavatyayaṃ mallikāpuṭo'yaṃ campakapuṭaḥ iti, niṣkīrṇāsvapi sumanassvanvayā- dviśeṣaṇaṃ bhavatyayaṃ mallikāpuṭo'yaṃ campakapuṭa iti' iti | yadvā bhedasaṃsargau sāmarthyam, tathā hi vṛttau rājapadasaṃnidhānāt puruṣapadameva rājabhinnāsvāmikarājasvāmikapuruṣameva bodhayati, ato na rājñaḥ svāntareṇa saṃbandhaḥ, rājapadasya svārthābhāvāt | ato na rājñaḥ puruṣo'śvaścetivadrājapuruṣo- 'śvaśceti prayogaḥ, nāpi puruṣasya svāmyantareṇa saṃbandhaḥ, puruṣatvarūpeṇāpi tādṛśavyaktiviśeṣajñānena virodhāt, tajjñānasyāpi devadattasvāmikaḥ puruṣa iti bodhavirodhitvamiti tātparyam | vedāntimate tattvamasyādivākyādakhaṇḍārthabodhavat prakṛte puruṣatvena rūpeṇa vyaktiviśeṣabodho bhedasya vācyatvaṃ tu na yuktamananubhavāt | vṛtterjahatsvārthatvapratipādanapareṇa bhedasaṃsargau vā sāmarthyamiti, kaḥ punarbhedaḥ saṃsargo vā, iha rājña ityukte sarva svaṃ prasaktaṃ, puruṣa ityukte sarvaḥ svāmī prasaktaḥ | ihedānīṃ rājapuruṣamānayetyukte rājā puruṣaṃ nivartayatyanyebhyaḥ svāmibhyaḥ, puruṣo'pi rājanamanyebhyaḥ svebhyaḥ, evamubhayato vyavacchinne yadi svārtha jahāti kāmaṃ jahātu' iti bhāṣyeṇāvācyatvasyaiva lābhāt | atra svāmyantarādityuktyā rājarūpasvāmino'nivṛttilābhena tatsaṃsarga uktaḥ, anena ca bhedasaṃsargayorbodhā- viṣayatvaṃ sūcyate | bodhaviṣayatve saṃsargabhedapratiyogitayānvayāya rājapadārthasyāvaśyakatayā tadanupa- sthitirūpattyāgakathanāsagatyāpatteḥ | na ca puruṣapadenaiva saṃsargabhedabodho'stu tathā ca na tatkathanāsaṃgatirati vācyam, athavānvayādviśeṣaṇaṃ bhavatīti pūrvakalpādaviśeṣāpatteḥ, puruṣo rājānaṃ svāntarebhyo nivartayatītyuktyā vā vācyatvameva tayorlabhyate, na hi rājani puruṣasaṃsargabodhaḥ puruṣānyasvāmibhedabodhaśca kasyacit saṃmataḥ | evaṃ dvandvabahuvrīhyoḥ tadbodhopapādanamaśakyameva | uktarītestu tatra nāsaṃbhavastathāvidhasyaivānyapadārthasya samūhasya ca tato bodha ityāśayāditi prāhuḥ | kṛṣṇaśrita ityādito'pi kṛṣṇamātraśritaḥ śritatvena rūpeṇa pratīyata ityāśaya iti vivaraṇādito
vistaro'nusaṃdheya ityalam | saṃbandhādanvaya iti pūrvakalpe atra kañpe ca mahādeva ityādau bhūtapūrvagatyārthavattvamādāyātvādipravṛttiḥ | yathā prakṛṣṭaprakāraścādra iti vākye kaścandra iti praśnottarabhūte vedāntimate prakṛṣṭaprakāśa iti padasya candratvarūpeṇa vastugatyā tādṛśaprakāśakabodhaparatvaṃ candraśabdasya grāhayitvā sārthakyaṃ, tathā prakṛte puruṣapadasya tādṛśavyakti- paratvagrāhakatayā rājādipadasya sārthakyaṃ bodhyam | vedāntimate hi 'prakṛṣṭaprakāśaścandra' ityaścandratvena, candrapadavattvena ca tathāvidhaprakāśavato bodho jāyate, na tu tādṛśaprakāśavattvena | tatraikārthī bhāvaḥ sāmarthya, paribhāṣā cetyevaṃ sūtramabhinnatarakaṃ bhavati' ityādisamarthasūtrasthabhāṣya- mityarthaḥ | dvividhasāmarthya parigrahe vākyabhedena sūtraṃ bhinnaṃ syādvayepekṣārūpaikavidhasāmarthyapakṣe'pi yadyapi sūtramabhinnaṃ bhavati, tathāpi tatpakṣe doṣāṇāṃ sattvāttadapekṣayaikārthībhāvapakṣa eva samīcīna iti sūcayituṃ tarabnirdeśa iti kaiyaṭaḥ | tanmūlaka iti | vyapekṣāvādamūlaka ityarthaḥ | nyūnatāṃ pariharttumāha samāsa iti | vṛttimātropeti | kṛdantādivṛttītyarthaḥ | ekopasthitijanakasamudāya- śaktirūpavṛttyāśrayatvātteṣāṃ vṛttivyavahāraḥ | samartha padaṃ vyācaṣṭe vigraheti . bhāṣyāllābhāditi |
kṛttaddhiteti | kṛtpadena kadantaṃ, taddhitapadena taddhaṭitaṃ gṛhyate, tena 'vahupaṭuḥ' 'sarvaka' ityanayornāsaṃgrahaḥ, kṛttaddhitayoḥ paribhāṣāpravṛttyā paṭaṃ karoti kumbhamānayetyatra na karmaṇyaṇ, ṛddhasyopagorapatyamityatra na taddhitaḥ, pṛthagarthānāmekārthībhāvaḥ sāmarthya, 'rājñaḥ puruṣa iti vākye pṛthakarthāni samāse ekārthānīti' samarthasūtrabhāṣyāt | tatra sāmarthyamasanna vacanaśatenāpi vidhātuṃ śakyata iti sāmarthyaviśiṣṭasya sādhutvaṃ vidhīyata iti bhāvaḥ | tathā ca yatkiṃcitpadajanyapṛthagupa- sthitiviṣayārthakatvena lokadṛṣṭaśabdānāṃ viśiṣṭaviṣayaikaśakyaikopasthitijanakatvamekārtībhāva iti paryavasitam | tādṛśaśabdānāntattvaṃ tu sādṛśyādanvagatavyam | kāryaśabdavāde tu vākyahetukaiva vṛttiniṣpattiriti, buddhervā | dṛṣṭānāmityantopādānaṃ vibhaktyante'tiprasaṅganirāsāya | vibhaktyarthasya yathā vibhaktyā pratipattistathā na kenāpyanyena śabdeneti vibhaktestathāvidhārthā- katvābhāvānna tadante'tiprasaṅgatādavasthyam | aupagavādisaṃgrahāya yatkiṃcittvaniveśaḥ, upagorapatyamityādāvapatyarūpārthasyāpatyapadajanyopasthitiviṣatvaṃ loke dṛṣṭamiti tatsaṃgrahaḥ | cvipratyayanirdeśo gauṇamekārthatvamiti sūcayitum | yathā padānāmasattvāttatsattāvyavahāro gauṇastathā teṣāmekārthavyavahāro'pīti bodhyamityanyatra vistaraḥ | samāsaikaśeṣeti | atraikaśeṣasya na vṛttitvaṃ, uktaikārthībhāvāsaṃbhāvāt parārthānvitasvārthopasthāpakatvābhāvāt, śiṣṭapadena svavṛttyaiva tattatsamūha- rūpārthasya bhānamiti bhāṣye spaṣṭamuktatvāditi śekharādau spaṣṭam | 'karmaṇyaṇ' ityāderapi paraṃparayā padoddeśyakatvānna tadasaṃgrahaḥ | vyākaraṇamadhīte vaiyākaraṇaḥ, pacatīti pācakaḥ, atra taddhitakṛdartha- bodhakākhyātasya kartrartha kaviśeṣyakabodhajanakatvamiti vigrahavākyārthābhidhanasāmarthyasya na kṣatiḥ | nanu paṅkajaśabdasyāpi samāsatayā tatrāpi paramate śaktirneṣyata eveti dṛṣṭāntatayā tadupādānama- saṃgatamityāśaṅkyāha śaktyasvīkartṛṇāmiti | asya 'vṛttā'vityādiḥ, tathā cedaṃ vakṣyamāṇarītyā paramatadūṣaṇavidhayā śaktisādhakameveti bhāvaḥ | yogādeveti | yogo'vayavaśaktistayetyarthaḥ | tatra vyutpattivaicitryeṇa nāmārtha syāpi paṅkasya janadhātvārthotpattāvadhikaraṇatānirūpakatvenānvayaḥ, tathāvidhotpatterḍapratyārtha kartaryāśrayatayā, evaṃ ca paṅkādhikaraṇakotpattyāśraya iti bodhopapattau satyāṃ samudāyaśaktirvyartheti bhāvaḥ | tatkalpyate - ekārthībhāvarūpaṃ sāmarthyam | pajhatvarūpeṇopasthitaya
iti | pajhatvāvacchinne śakti vinā tena rūpeṇopasthityasaṃbhavāt, taddharmaprakārakopasthiti prati taddharmāvacchinnavṛttijñānasya hetutvāditi bhāvaḥ |
svāmitvenopasthitaye iti | svāmitvena rūpeṇānyapadārthopasthitaya ityarthaḥ | nahi vṛttyantaraṃ vinā dharmiṇa upasthitiḥ kenāpi rūpeṇa sambhavatīti bodhyam | lakṣaṇayeti | samudāyalakṣaṇayā, ḍapratyayalakṣaṇayā vetyarthaḥ | paṅkajapadamanyasyāpi yogarūḍhasyopalakṣaṇamityāha evamiti | sthakārapadepīti | samudāyaśaktirna sidhyedityasyānuṣaṅgeṇātrānvayaḥ | nanu māstu tatra samudāyaśaktiḥ, rathakarmakotpādanakartṛrūpāvayavaśaktilabhyarūpeṇaiva hi tatra bodho'nubhūyate ityā- śaṅkyāha tathā ceti | brāhmaṇādiviṣayatayaiveti | rathaṃ karotīti vyutpattyā rathakārapade brāhmaṇādibodhakatvasyāpi saṃbhavāditi bhāvaḥ | upapattau - brāhmaṇāderadhyayanavidhisiddhijñānasattvenā- dhikāritayā tatkartṛkādhānavidhāyakatavā cāritārthye | tatkalpanām - samudāye jātiviśeṣā- vacchinnaśaktikalpanām | jātiviśeṣasya jātiviśeṣaviśiṣṭasya |
'kṣatriyāṃ māgadhaṃ sūte vaiśyāt kṣattārameva ca |' 
iti vājñavalkyavacanādvaiśyāyāṃ śūdrādutpanno'nulomajātīyo rathakāra iti bodhyam | apūrvavidyākalpanam 'mīmāṃsakānā'miti śeṣaḥ | atyayuktaṃ syāditi | rathakārapadasya yogarūḍhatvena na brāhmaṇādiviṣayakatvametadvākyasya, ki tvanulomajātīyaviṣayakatvam, tasya ca vedādhyayanāsaṃbhavādvedavihitādhānānyathānupapattyā tadupayuktavedādhyayanaṃ kalpanīyamiti mīmāṃsaka- siddhānto virudhyeteti bhāvaḥ | syādviśeṣaṇeti | tathā ca ṛddhasya rājñaḥ puruṣa iti prayogavadṛddhasya rājapuruṣa ityapi syāt | viśeṣaṇānvaye hetumāha rājapadeneti | svatantreti | 'ghaṭo nitya' ityādau ghaṭatvādau nityapadārthānvayabodhanivṛttaye viśeṣaṇānvayabuddhau mukhyaviśeṣyatāsaṃbandhena hetutayā kalpyamānopasthiteḥ sattvadityarthaḥ, samudāyaśaktikalpane tu tadvaśādrājādipadena svārthaviśiṣṭa evārtha upasthāpyata iti svamukhyaviśeṣyakopasthitevirahānna tatra viśeṣaṇānvaya iti bhāvaḥ | rājavaiśiṣṭyanaiva puruṣasyopasthityā rājapuruṣo bhāryāyāśceti na, ekasaṃbandhena yatkriṃcidvaiśiṣṭyeno- pasthitestatsajātīyasaṃbandhenetarānvayasyāvyutpannatvāt, ata eva ghaṭo dravyatvasyeti na, samavāyena ghaṭatvavaiśiṣṭyenopasthite tena saṃbandhena dravyatvānvayābhāvāt | rājapuruṣo'bhirūpa iti tu bhavatyeva, bodhakābhāvāt | rājapuruṣo'śvaśvetyapi na | ekatra viśeṣaṇatvenopasthitasyāparatra viśeṣaṇatvā- yogāt |
samāsaniyameti | avaśyaṃ vākyaprayoge prāpte ārabhyamāṇaḥ samāso nityaṃ vākyanivartakaḥ syāt, ekārthībāvapakṣe tu ekārthībhāve samāsasya, vyapekṣāyāṃ vākyasya siddhatayā vibhāṣādhikāro na kartavyaḥ | vibhinnaviṣayatvātsamāsena vākyabādhāprasakteḥ | evaṃ 'samarthānāṃ prathamādvā' ityatratyamapi veti na kartavyam, evaṃ sanvidhau vāgrahaṇamapi na kartavyam, taduktaṃ bhāṣye 'vāvacanānarthakyaṃ ca svabāvasiddhatvāt' iti | liṅgāyogaḥ kukkuṭāṇḍaṃ mṛgakṣīramityādau kkacid draṣṭavyaḥ, na tu sarvatra, kumārīputra ityādau darśanāt, pare tu jātyantaranivṛttipare vākyepi chāgasya māṃsamityādau liṅgaviśeṣādarśanānnedamekārthībhāvaprayuktamiti vadanti | evaṃ vṛttāvupasarjanapadārthe saṃkhyāviśeṣo na bhāsate ityuktaṃ bhāṣye samarthasūtre, saṃkhyātvena saṃkhyāviśeṣa ekatvādiḥ pratīye, na tu ekatvatvādi- viśeṣarūpeṇa, idamevābhedaikatvasaṃkhyā pratīyata ityanenocyate | bhedo - viśeṣarūpaṃ
tadabhāvarūpaikatvasaṃkhyeti tadarthaḥ | 'tadabhāvarūpe'tyasya tatpratītyabhāvarūpetyartho'vaseyaḥ | prātipadikopāttā pravṛttinimittabhūtā saṃkhyā tu 'dviputra' ityādau pratīyate, tatastadanupāttā sā na pratīyate, saṃkocena bhāṣyavyākhyāne mānābhāvāt, tvatputrastāvakīno māsajāta ityādau tattatpravṛttinimittvena rūpeṇaikā vyaktirbhāsate, yuphmatputro yauṣmākīṇa ityādau tu tena rūpeṇānekavyaktibhānam, ata eva kukkuṭendragopavācaka'goṣucaro' 'varṣāsujāta' ityādau bahuvacanasattve'pi na bahutvabhānamevaṃ cātra bahutvāropeṇa śabdasaṃrakāramātraṃ , na tu tasya prakṛtyarthe'nvayaḥ, ata eva 'rājapuruṣa' ityādau saṃkhyāpratītirna subabhāvakṛtā, atra tatsattve'pi tadapratīteriti prāhuḥ, vistaro bhāṣyapratīpodyotādāvanusandheyaḥ | ata eva - ekārthībhāvasya siddhāntisaṃmatatvenaiva || 32 ||
ekārthībāvasya prayojanāntaraṃ bhāṣyakaiyaṭādyuktaṃ svayamāha-tathā dhaveti | dhavakhadirāvityā- dīnāmitaretarayogādibhiḥ krameṇānvayaḥ, tathā ca dhavakhadirāvityādau dvandve 'cārthe dvandvaḥ' ityanenānekaṃ subantaṃ cārthe vtate, tacca dvandvasaṃjñakaṃ bhavatīti vākyabhedamaṅgīkṛtyarthabodhanaṃ kartavyaṃ, tathā ca gauravam, evaṃ 'niṣkauśāmvi'rityādāvapi 'nirādayaḥ krāntādyarthe dvitīyayā' ityanena nirādipūrvakadvitīyāntasamudāyaḥ krāntādyarthe vartate, sa ca samāsasaṃjñako bhavatīti vākyabheda- puraskāreṇārthādeśanaṃ bodhyam | gorathādiviṣayakaṃ tu tattadarthavidhāyakaṃ vacanaṃ kalpanīyamiti gauravam | tatra 'goratha' ityatra goyukto ratha iti yuktārthaḥ | 'ghṛtaghaṭa' ityatra ghṛtapūrṇo ghaṭa iti pūrṇārthaḥ | 'guḍadhānā' ityatra guḍamiśradhānā iti miśrārthaḥ | 'keśacūḍa' ityatra keśasaṃghātabhūtā cūḍā yasyeti saṃdhātārthaḥ | 'suvarṇālaṅkāra' ityatra suvarṇavikāro'laṃkāra iti vikārarūpārthaḥ | 'dvidāśā' ityatra dvirāvṛttā daśetyarthāt sujlopaḥ | 'saptaparṇa' ityatra sapta sapta parṇāni yasyetyartādvīpsā pratīyate |
pare tu krāntādyarthā nirādīnāṃ, na tu samudāyasyeti naikārthībhāvakṛto'yaṃ viśeṣaḥ, ata eva bhāṣye ekārthībhāvakṛteṣu viśeṣu nāsya gaṇanaṃ kṛtam, dvidaśā ityatrāpi śabdaśaktisvābāṣyena pūrvapadasya sujathantirbhāveṇa vṛttiḥ, na tu samudāyaśaktitaḥ dvitvasakhyotpattikāvṛttiviṣayā daśeti bodha iti vadanti | mūle cakārāditi | dhavakhadirāvityādau vākyavaccakāraprayogaḥ prāptastasya niṣedho - lopo vidheyaḥ | ekārthībhāvapakṣetu pṛthagupasthityabhāvāt pṛthagupasthitinibandhana samuccayāpratīterna caprayogaḥ prāpnotīti bhāvaḥ | ivādīnāmiti | atra lopa iti saṃbandhyate | pūrvoktānāmiti | niṣkauśāmbirityādau krāntādiśabdānāṃ lopa ityasya saṃgraha ityarthaḥ | 'haṃsagamana' ityasya haṃsa iva gamanaṃ yasyeti vigrahaḥ, ekārthībhāvapakṣe tu ivarthasya samudāyārthe'ntarbhūtatvādaprayogaḥ | siddha eveti bhāvaḥ | prathamata evodakapade udakasaṃbandhilakṣaṇāyāmudakasyaikadeśatayā prāptapadārthaprāptikarturanvayo na syādityata āha-prāptikartraiti | ityarthālābhāditi | saṃbandhilakṣaṇāyāṃ prāptikartrabhinnodaka- saṃbandhī grāma iti bodhasyaiva saṃbhavāt | yadyapi svakartṛkaprāptikarmatvameva saṃbandhitvamiti prāptikarmatvena bodho jātaḥ, evamūḍharatha ityādāvapi rathapadādau tatsaṃbandhilakṣaṇāyāmapi svakarmakavahanakartṛtvādirūpemeva saṃbandhitvamiti vivakṣitārthalābho jāta eva, tathāpi saṃbandhatvenaiva karmatvādibhānaṃ syānna tu karmatvatvādineti tātparya bodhyamiti bhāvaḥ |
prāptikarmeti-syāditi | prāptikarmābhinnamudakamiti bodhaḥ syādityarthaḥ | prāptapadasya
kriyāśabdatvenodakaśabdasya jātiśabdatvena codakasyaiva viśeṣyatāyā ucitatvāt udakasya prāptau kartṛtayeṣṭo'nvayo na syādapi tvabhedenaiva prāptikarmaṇeta sūcayituṃ tathābhihitaṃ, tathā ceṣṭānvayo na syādityatraiva tātparya, na tūktānvayabodhe'pi nirbhara iti bodhyam | anyatheti | kartṛtāsaṃbandhe- nodakasya prāptyanvayābhyupagame | samānādhikaraṇeti | samānavibhaktikaprātipadikārthayorityarthaḥ | anvayāsaṃvācceti | nāmārthadhātvarthayoḥ sākṣādabhedenaivānvaya iti vyutpatteriti bhāvaḥ | anyathā - tādṛśavyutptyanabhyupagame | ananvayānāpatteriti | devadattasya pāke kartṛtāsaṃbandhenānvayasaṃbhavena tādṛśavākye'nanvitārthakatvasyeṣṭasyānupapatterityarthaḥ | tatsambandhīti | prāptikarmetyarthaḥ | kartari viśeṣyatāyā iti | kartṛnirūpitaviśeṣyatāyā ityartha stathā coktabodhāsaṃbhava iti bhāvaḥ | asaṃbhave hetumāha-prakṛtīti | 'sattvapradhānāni nāmāni' iti vacanavirodho'pi draṣṭavyaḥ | nanu pacatītyādau klaptatyāgakasya pratyayaprādhānyasya vṛttāvapi tyago'stu, evaṃ 'sattvapradhānānī'tyasyāpi vṛttyaghaṭakanāmaparatvamastvityāśaṅkyāha-nāmārthatveneti | nāmajanyapratītiviśeṣyatvenetyarthaḥ | nāmajanyapratītiviśeṣyayorevānvayaḥ, sa cābhedenaiveti 'nāmārthayo'rityāderarthāditi bhāvaḥ | abhedānvayāpatteśceti | prāpterviśeṣyatve viśeṣaṇībhūtakartrā udakasyābhedānvayabodhānāpatterityarthaḥ | ayogyatāniścayasya śābdahetutvānaṅgīkāre yathāśrutamevāstu, na kevalaṃ dvitīyārtha bahuvrīhāveva lakṣaṇayā'nirvāho'pi tu tṛtīyādyarthabahuvrīhāvapītyāha-evamūḍharatha iti | ūḍho ratho yeneti vigrahavakyāt yatkartṛkavahanakarmasya iti bodhaḥ | upahṛtaḥ dattaḥ paśuryasmā iti vākyādyaduddeśya- kopaharaṇakarmapaśuriti, ud‌dhṛta odano yasyā iti vākyādyadavadhikoddharaṇakarmodana iti, bahavaḥ pācakā yasyāmiti vākyādyadadhikaraṇakabahṇabhinnāḥ pācanakartāra iti | atrāpi vahanakarmābhinno ratha iti śakyārthānvayabodhanantaraṃ tatsaṃbandhilakṣaṇāyāmapi na vahanakartetyādyakabodhanirvāhaḥ | vahanāderdhātvarthatayā pratyayārthakarmādikaṃ prati viśeṣyatvānupapattirityādidūṣaṇāni pūrvavad bodhyānīti samuditatātparyārthaḥ | saṃbandhilakṣaṇāyāḥ pūrvoktānekavyutpattibhaṅgābhāve'pi uktabodhānirvāhaḥ, evamagre'pi draṣṭavyamiti bhāvaḥ | bahupāciketyatrāpi saṃbandhilakṣaṇāyāṃ tatsaṃbandhitvenaiva bodhaḥ syānna tvadhikaraṇatveneti bhāvaḥ | adhikaraṇatvaviśiṣṭalakṣaṇāyāṃ tu naitad dūṣaṇamiti bodhyam | ghaṭapadasyeveti | yathā ghaṭapadasya ghaṭatvāviśiṣṭaktyā viśiṣṭopasthāpakatvaṃ tathā prāptoda- keti samudāyasyāpi vaśiṣṭaśaktyodakakartṛkaprāptikarmatvaviśiṣṭopasthāpakatayoktānupapattyabhāvaḥ, 'nāmārthayo'rityādivyutpattīnāṃ pṛthagupasthitiviṣayatvāditi bhāvaḥ || 33 ||
'aṣaṣṭhyārtha bahuvrīhā'vityatra ṣaṣṭhīpadaṃ saptamyā apyupalakṣaṇaṃ, ṣaṣṭhyarthabahuvrīhāviva saptamyarthabahuvrīhāvapi vyutpattyantarakalpanāyā aprasakteḥ | kalpayeriti | saṃpraśne 'vidhinimantraṇa-' iti sūtreṇa liṅ | lakṣaṇā ki pūrvapade ? uta uttarapade ? iti vikalpya nādya ityāha-sā hi na citreti | citrasvāmī gauritīti | gopadasāṃnidhyāccitrapadena citrarūpavattvena goreva bodhanirvāhe'pi citrānvitagotvaviśiṣṭāyāḥ svāmini vaiśiṣṭyabodhānupapattiḥ, gopadopasthitārthe citrasvāmino- 'bhedānvayāpattiḥ, evaṃ jaraccitraguḥ, śobhanacitragurityādāvekaikapadasya svārthasvāmilakṣakatve jaradādyabhinno yo gosvāmī tadabhinno gauriti bodhaḥ syānna tu jaradādyarthasya gavā saha, vyavadhānādityapi draṣṭavyam | citrapadaṃ citrasvāmilakṣakaṃ, gopadaṃ gosvāmilakṣakam, citratvena gotvenacaikavyaktereva bhānaṃ, vyaktivacanānāṃ saṃnihitavyaktiparatvamiti nyāyāditi tu tādṛśabodhānanu-
bhavācchobhanacitragurityādau śobhanānvayānupapatteścānupādeyam | śobhanapadasyāpi śobhanasvāmilakṣakatve atyantānubhavavirodha ityādi draṣṭavyam | ananvaya eva gopadārthasya syāditi bhāvaḥ | nāntya ityāha-nāpīti | goḥ svāmī citra iti pūrvavadvayākhyeyaṃ, tādṛśe'rthe tātparyādyabhāvagraho hetuḥ | nanu citrādipadalakṣyārthaikadeśacitrādinā gavāderabhadānvaye sati gavābhinnacitrasvāmī citrābhinnago- svāmīti vā bodho bhavati, tathā ca noktākārabodhāpattirityata āha- citrādimātrasyeti | mātrapadena svāmino vyavacchedaḥ | anvayāyogāditi | 'padārthaḥ padārthe ne'ti vyutpattivirodhāt | anvayabodhottaramiti | padārthaupasthityādighaṭitasāmagryā tayorevādāvanvayo bhavati pratyāsannatvāt tadanantaraṃ citrābhinnāyā gorbodhagrahe sati gopadena citrābhinnagosvāmitvena rūpeṇānyapadārtho lakṣyate | prāthamikatādṛśabodhaśca citratvādiviśiṣṭagosvāmitvopasthitaya āvaśyakaḥ | tādṛśabodhotpādakatayā ca citrapadasārthakyamiti bhāvaḥ | sākṣātsaṃbandha iti | tatra śakyasaṃbandhaḥ sattvanirūpitasvāmitvarūpaḥ sākṣātsaṃbandhaḥ, citrapadasya guṇavācakatayā svaśaktisamavāyiniṣṭhatvanirūpitasvāmitvarūpa āśrayadravyaghaṭitaḥ paraṃparāsaṃbandho lakṣaṇārūpo vācya iti gauravamiti bhāvaḥ, idaṃ tu pararītyoktam, svamate tu citrapadasyāpi guṇaviśiṣṭadravyavācakatvena tasyāpi sākṣātsaṃbandhasaṃbhavāditi dhyeyam | kṛtaviśva iti | kṛtaṃ viśvaṃ yeneti vyutpattyā viśvakarmakotpādakatvena viśvaviṣayakakṛtimattvena vānyapadārthaḥ pratīyate | atrāpi ṣaṣṭhīpadaṃ saptamyā apyupalakṣaṇaṃ, citragurgrāma iti saptamyarthabahuvrīhāvapi | kartrādyarthakatayeti | prāptodaka ityatra kartari ktaḥ | kṛtaviśva ityatra karmaṇi | snānīyacūrṇa pātramityādau karaṇādyarthakaḥ kṛtpratyaya ityarthaḥ, vinigamakasambhavādyau- gikānāmityuktiḥ, śuktāddhādirūḍhavyudāsārthām | idamupalakṣaṇam, pāṭhakaputrako devadattaḥ, vīrapuruṣako grāma, pācakapuruṣaka iti ṣaṣṭhyarthabahuvrīhau saptamyarthabahuvrīhāvapi kkacid vinigamanāvirahāt vīrapāṭhakādipadānāmapi dharmivācakatvena sākṣātsandho'viśeṣāt paraṃ tu dvitīyādyarthabahuvrīhau sarvatraiva vinigamanāviraho'nyatra tu kkaciditi sūcayutumaṣaṣṭhyarthetyuktamiti bodhyam | padadvaye lakṣaṇeti | citrapade, gopade ca citragosvāmilakṣaṇetyarthaḥ | naiyāyiketi | prācīnanaiyāyiketyarthaḥ | parasparamiti | citrapade citragosvāmilakṣaṇāyāṃ gopadasamabhivyāhāra- stādṛśārthātātparyagrāhakatayā niyāmakaḥ | gopade lakṣaṇāyāṃ citraśabdasamabhivyāhāro niyāmaka ityarthaḥ | vinigamanādhirahatādavasthyeneti | kadā kasminnekapade lakṣaṇetyatra vinigamanāviha- hatāvasthyenetyarthaḥ | lakṣaṇāyā asaṃbhavāditi | ekadā ekapade lakṣaṇāyā asaṃbhavādatyarthaḥ, evaṃ śobhanā citraguḥ, śobhanā jaratī citragurityādau śobhanādipadārthasyānanvayāpattiśca | citroderekadeśatvena tatrānvayāsaṃbhavāt, śobhanādipade'pi śobhanācitragosvāmilakṣaṇābhyupagame trayāṇāmapi padānāṃ tādṛśaviśiṣṭārthalakṣakatvāt samudāyaśaktyapekṣayā lāghavānakāśaḥ | vinigamanāviraheṇa padatrayajanyānāṃ tisṛṇāmapyupasthitīnāṃ śābdabodhahetutvābhyupagamaprayuktaṃ gauravaṃ caitatkalpe bodhyam |
navyanaiyāyikasiddhātaṃ dūṣayitumupanyasyati na ceti | sā - lakṣaṇā | saṃnihitapadārtheti | saṃnihitapūrvapadārthetyarthaḥ | anyathā rājñaḥ puruṣa ityatra puruṣapadārthe'pi ṣaṣṭhyarthasaṃbandhānvayāpattiḥ | tathā ca puruṣasaṃbandhirājetyapi pratīyeta | tathā ca prāptodaka ityādāvapyudakādipade evodakakartṛka- prāptikarmādilakṣaṇāṅgīkriyate iti bhāvaḥ | nanvanyapadārthapratipādakatvaṃ tatpratītyanukūlatvaṃ, taccara-
mapadasya svavṛttyā tadupasthāpakatvenetarapadasya tātparyagrāhakatayā tadbodhānukūlatvaṃ bhavatītyato dūṣaṇāntaramāha kiṃ ceti | evaṃ satīti | bahuvrīhau caramapade eva lakṣaṇāṅgīkāre sati | caramavarṇe eva vācakateti | caramavarṇasyaiva pratyayāvyavahitatvāt, lāghavācca tenaiva vācakatākalpanā syāt, na ceṣṭāpattirapasiddhāntāditi bhāvaḥ | kiṃ ca ghaṭapaṭādighaṭakācpratyayarūpacaramavarṇasya lakṣaṇayaiva ghaṭādibodhopapattirityapi draṣṭavyam | nanu kośādiprāmāṇyena ghaṭādipadasya tāvadvarṇasamudāyarūpasya vācakatvamabhyupeyate, prakṛte tu caramapadalakṣaṇayevopapattau na samudāyaśakti- siddhiḥ kośāderabhāvāt ananyalabhyasyaiva śabdārthatvāccetyāśaṅkyāha anyatra vistara iti | anyatra - vṛhadbhūṣaṇādau | vistara iti | pratyayānāṃ saṃnihitapūrvetyādivyutpattisvīkāro na saṃbhavati, sarvaka ityādau vyabhicārāt | dadhīyaddadātī'tyādau dadhni vatubarthānvayāpatteḥ | dadhi bahupaṭurdadāti ityatra dadhni bahujarthānvayāpatteḥ, paṭāvananvayāpatteśceti | kiṃ caivaṃ prātipadikāt karmatvādau svādaya iti śāstrāt pratyāsattilabdhāyā pratyayānāṃ prakṛtyarthānvitasvārthābodhakatvamiti vyutpattentallabdhasya paramparārthānvayenaiva prakṛtipratyayayoḥ sādhutvasya ca bhaṅga, uttarapadamātrasyā- prakṛtitvāt citragurityādau pūrvapade svavācyārthaghaṭitalakṣaṇāyāṃ tātparyaghāhakatvasya gaṅgāyāṃ ghoṣa ityādau klṛptasya ca bhaṅgapattiḥ | vyutpattyantarasvīkāre tu viśiṣṭaśaktirevociteti draṣṭavyamiti bhāvaḥ | uktayukteriti | prāptodakādāvanvayānupapattirūpayukterityarthaḥ | klṛpttyāga iti vyācaṣṭe klṛptaśaktyeti | vyutpattityāgaśceti | klṛptaśaktyaiva nirvāha iti niyamatyāgaścetyarthaḥ, anyathādakakartṛkaprāptikarmeti bodhānupapatteriti bhāvaḥ | tartki śaktimiti | pūraṇasaṃvalitaṃ vyācaṣṭe tatkiṃ sarvatra samāse śaktimiti |
iti bodhopapatteriti | tathā ca samudāyaśakti nivāpi rājapade rājasaṃbandhilakṣaṇayā saṃbandhabodhanirvāhe sati tattadbodhānyathānupapattyā samudāyaśaktyupagamaścintyaḥ, gauravāpatteḥ, na ca vṛttyārthabodhakatvarūpārthavattvābhāvāt, samāsatvādeva prātipadikatvasaṃbhavādveti bhāvaḥ | atra saṃbandhe lakṣaṇābhyupagame 'nāmārthayo'riti vyutpattivirodhena saṃbandhasyāśrayatāsaṃbandhena puruṣe'nvayānupapattiriti saṃbandhilakṣaṇānusaraṇaṃ kṛtam | ata eva - saṃbandhini lakṣaṇābhyupagamādevetyarthaḥ, 'eva'śabdo'pyarthaḥ | na śobanasyetyādiviśeṣaṇānvaya iti | 'śobhanāyāṃ gaṅgāyāṃ ghoṣa' ityādau tu gaṅgāpadameva śobhanagaṅgātīralakṣakamatastatra lakṣyārthaikadeśagaṅgāyāṃ śobhanānvayavadatrāpi lakṣyārthaikadeśe rājādāvapi śobhanānvayaḥ syāditi śaṅkānavasaraḥ | 'ghanaśyāma' ityatra ghanapadārthasya sādṛśyasaṃbandhenottarapadārthe'nvayaḥ, 'niphakauśābhbi'rityatra niṣkramaṇāvadhitvasaṃbandhena, 'goratha' ityatra svayuktatvasaṃbandhena pūrvapadārthasyottarapadārthe | evaṃ ca saṃsargatayevādyarthoktāvapi sādṛśyādiprakāraka- bodhārtha ivādiśabdaprayogo durvāra ityāśayena śaṅkate na ceti | uktārthakatayeti | samāse ghanādipadasya tatsadṛśādilakṣakatayoktārthakatvam, anyathā 'nāmārthayo'riti vyutpattivirodhena sādṛśyādisaṃbandhenānvayānupapatteriti bhāvaḥ | prayoganiyameti | vibhinnaviṣayakatvena samāsadinā vākyabādhāprasakteriti bhāvaḥ, nīlotpalādikarmadhāraye pravṛttinimittasāhitye lakṣaṇā ? nīlotpalayorvā sāhitye ? ādye nīlotpalatvasamūhavannīlamutpalamiti bodhaḥ, dvitīye tu nīlasamānādhikaraṇaṃ tadabhinnamutpalamiti |
śābdikamate samudāyāśakti retadarthamevopeyate, vākye tu cadvayena tatsāhityaṃ pratipādyate'to viviktaviṣayatvaṃ vṛttivākyayoḥ, evameva dvandve'pi bodhyam | anye tu nīlaṃ ca tadutpalaṃ ceti vākye nīlādipadottaravibhakterabhedo'rthaḥ, tasyāśrayatvasaṃbandhenottarapadārtho'nvayaḥ, samāse tu nīlādipadenaiva lakṣaṇayā nīlādyabhedaḥ pratipādyate, tathā ca lakṣaṇayā tādṛśārthavivakṣāyāṃ samāso, vibhaktyā tadvivakṣāyāṃ vākyamiti vyavasthāyāḥ saṃbhavaḥ, 'ekārthībāvapadenāpi vākyadṛṣṭapadārthaghaṭitārthe lakṣaṇaivocyata iti bhāva ityūcuḥ | evamaupagavādavapi upagvādiśabdasyopagvādisaṃbandhe lakṣaṇeti bodhyam . saktisādhiketi | vṛttimātre śaktisādhiketyarthaḥ | avayavaśaktyagrahasya lakṣaṇāgrahe kathaṃ bādhakatvamata āha na ca śaktyagraha iti | 'na ca saṃbhavatī'ta yojanā | na cetyasya na hītyarthaḥ | śaktigrahaṃ vinā śakyānupasthitau śakyasaṃbandharūpalakṣaṇāgrahavaidhuryeṇa pajhatvaviśiṣṭārtha- bodhāsaṃbhava iti bhāvaḥ | tathā cāvayavaśaktigrahavirahadaśāyamāpi paṅkajapadāt pajhatvaprakārakabodha- syānubhavasiddhasya saṃpattaye samudāyaśaktyāvaśyakatvamiti tātparyārthaḥ | evameva 'rathakāra'śabde'pi samudāyaśaktyabhyugama āvaśyaka iti na pūrvoktādhikaraṇavirodha iti bodhyam . nanvevaṃ rājādipadaśaktyagrahadaśāyāṃ rājasaṃbadhī puruṣa ita bodho na syādita tatrāpi samudāyaśaktirabhyu- pagantavyetyāśaṅkāmiṣṭāpattyā pariharati ata eveti | avayavaśaktijñānasya lakṣaṇayā viśiṣṭārthabodhaprayojakatvādevetyarthaḥ | na bodha iti | tathā ca tatrāvayavaśaktigrahavirahasamaye tādṛśabodhābhāva evānubhavasiddho na tu bodha iti bodhānudaya iṣṭāpattireveti bhāvaḥ | 'bahuvyutpattibhañjanamiti | mūle eva prāgetadvayākhyātam | uttarapada eva lakṣaṇāyāṃ vinigamakamāha pūrvapadasyeti | tallakṣaṇāyā iti | pūrvapadalakṣaṇāgrahasyetyarthaḥ | dhātupratyayeti | dhātupratyayayostadarthayośca jñānasādhyatayetyarthaḥ, dhātvādijñānaṃ vinā tadarthajñānānupapattyārthajñānadvārā dhātvādijñānasyāpi lakṣaṇāgrahaprayojakatvamiti bhāvaḥ | nanu 'prāpte'ti samudāye na lakṣaṇā, tasya śaktyabhāvena śakyasaṃbandharūpalakṣaṇāyā asaṃbhavāt, kiṃ tu tattaddhaṭakaktapratyaye eva, tathā ca tattadarthajñānamātrasādhyatvaṃ, na tu dhātutadarthajñānasādhyatvamapi, kartṛkaprāptikarmalakṣaṇāyāmapi udakaśabdatadarthajñānasādhyatayā sāmyameva | nanu ktapratyaye udaka kartṛkaprāptikarmalakṣaṇāyāṃ tādṛśadhātusamabhivyāhārajñānasya tātparyagrāhakatvaṃ vācyamanyathātiprasaṅgāt, evaṃ ca dhātutadarthajñānasādhyatvamakṣatameva, kiṃ ca 'svabodhyasaṃbandho lakṣaṇe'ti māmāṃsakamate 'prāpte'ti samudāye lakṣaṇāsaṃbhavāttattadarthajñānasādhyatvamanapavādamiti sphuṭameveti cet udakapade tādṛśārtha- lakṣaṇāyāmapa prāptapadasamabhivyāhārasya tātparyagrāhakatayā dhātupratyayatadarthajñānasādhyatvaṃ durapahnavameva, prāptikarmajñānaṃ vinā tatsaṃbandharūpalakṣaṇāgrahā'saṃbhavādityata uttarapadalakṣaṇāyāṃ vinigamakāntaramāha pratyayānāmiti | saṃnihiteti | saṃnihitaṃ yatpadaṃ tadarthagatetyarthaḥ | atra 'rājapuruṣamānaye'tyatra rājñi na pratyayārthakarmatvādyanvayaḥ, anyathā dvivacanāpatteḥ, puruṣe ānayanakarmatvavirahagrahadaśāyāṃ rājani ānayanakarmatvānvayatātparyeṇa 'rājapuruṣamānaye'ti prayogāpatteśca, tādṛśaśravaṇānantaraṃ tadarthabodhe sati rājānayanāpatteśca | etena rājani tadanvaye iṣṭāpattiniṃrastā | ghaṭādipadaghaṭakakacaramavarṇasyaiva vācakatvaṃ syāttu lyanyāyāditi śābdikoktapratibandīrūpaṃ dūṣaṇaṃ nirasyati ghaṭādipade ceti | atiriktā śaktiriti | taddhaṭakadhātvādiśaktyatiriktā, gṛhītaśaktikapadaśaktyatiriktā, bodhakatvātiriktā vetyarthaḥ |
viśiṣṭe kalpyate - viśiṣṭa eva kalpyate | saṃketitatvāt - kośādinā saṃketitatvena bodhanāt | nanu bodhakatvarūpā śakticaramavarṇe eva syāt, kiṃ ca tadanyathānupapattyā tadatiriktā kalpyamānāpi śaktistatraiva paryavasyodityāśaṅkyāha bodhakatvasyāpīti | bodhajanakatvaparyāptyadhi- karaṇatvasyapītyarthaḥ | tena bodhakatvāśrayatvasya tatra sattve'pi na kṣatiḥ | nanu prakṛte'pi 'citragusvāmī'ti bodhānyathānupapattyā kalpyamānā lakṣaṇāpi uttarapadavat pūrvapade'pi kalpyā'- viśeṣādityāśaṅkyāha prakṛte ceti . sā kalpyata iti | lakṣaṇā kalpya ityarthaḥ | viśeṣa iti | caramavarṇasyātyantaṃ pratyayasaṃnidhāne'pi tasya bodhakatvānanubhavātsamudāyasyaiva vācakatvaṃ kalpyata iti bhāvaḥ | svīkṛta ceti | tathā ca ghaṭādiśabde caramavarṇasyaiva vācakatve iṣṭāpattiriti bhāvaḥ |
mīmāṃsakammanyairiti | khaśi 'arurdviṣa'diti mum | mīmāṃsakamātmānaṃ manyanta ityarthaḥ | anenāsvarasaḥ sūcitaḥ, tanmūlaṃ tu kośādivirodhaḥ | saṃjñādikamiti | ādinā subluk, samudāyātpunaḥ subutpattiranudāttaṃ padamityasya pravṛttiḥ parigṛhyate | ākāṅkṣādisācivyenārtha- bodhakatvarūpārthavattvasyāpi prātipadikasaṃjñāprayojakatvasvīkāre gauravaṃ, 'citragu'rityādau tadasaṃbhavaśca, gośabdasyaiva lakṣaṇayā viśiṣṭārthabodhakatvāt, na cārthabodhajanakajñānaviṣaya- śabdatvamarthavattvaṃ grāhyam, taccaikasya padasya lakṣaṇayā,-parasya tātparyagrahakatayā cetyevaṃ samūhasya niṣpratyūhamiti vācyaṃ, pratyekaṃ vibhaktyutpattyāpatteḥ, 'ekācadvirvacana'nyāyena tu na tadvāraṇasaṃbhavaḥ, yatra samudāyakāryaṇāvayavānugrahastatraiva tatpravṛtteḥ, 'ekāco dve prathamasye'tyādinirdeśena tadvāraṇe tu gauravamiti bhāvaḥ | tadetaduktaṃ syānmahadgauravaṃ tasmāditi | samāsagrahaṇātseti . sā - prātipa- dikasaṃjñā | tathā ca samāsagrahaṇaṃ vidhyartha'miti bhāvaḥ | nanu tasya niyamārthatvābhāve'pi kṣatyabhāvaḥ, kiṃ cā'sūryapaśyāḥ' 'śaśaśṛṅga'mityādāvarthavattvābhāvena prātipadikasaṃjñāvidhāyakatayā niyamārthatvānupapattiścetyāśaṅkyāha bhūṣaṇe spaṣṭaṃ pratipāditatvāditi | 'asūryapaśyā' ityādāvapi 'asūrye'tyatra sūryakarmakadarśanābhāvavati śaktikalpanena 'arthavat' sūtreṇaiva sā sidhyati, uktaṃ ca samarthasūtre kaiyaṭe 'asamarthasamāse'pi kriyāyāmubhayoḥ saṃnipātādekārthībhāvastad‌dvārākostyeva' iti | ubhayoḥ - nañsūryapadayoḥ | śaśaśṛṅgādipade'pi viśakalitaṃ prasiddhamāropitasaṃbandhaṃ śakyamataḥ 'arthava'tsūtraprāptirastyeva, kiṃ ca samāsarūpadharmigrāhakāt 'asūryalalāṭayo'riti mānāda- sūryetvasya prātipadikasaṃjñā sidhyati, na ha prātipadikatvaṃ, subutpartti ca vinopapadatvaṃ samīpo- ccāritapadatvarūpaṃ nirvahatīti spaṣṭaṃ nibandheṣu | śaśaśṛṅgādervauddho'rtho vācya iti spaṣṭaṃ vivahaṇādau | evaṃ ca manmate samāsagrahaṇasya niyamārthatvaṃ susthameva, api ca 'kṛttaddhite'ti sūtre'pi tadantavidhilābhāya 'arthava'dityanuvartate iti spaṣṭaṃ bhāṣyādiṣu, tathā ca samāse śaktyanaṅgīkāre samāsagrahaṇenāpi na sā sidhyati | 'arthavatpadasya kṛttaddhitayoreva saṃbandho na samāsa' iti tu na yuktam, tathā sati dvandvasyāsādhutvāpatteranyatarasya pāṇḍitye 'devadatteyajñadattau paṇḍitā'viti prayogābhāvāt, dvandvaghaṭakayāvatpārthe itarapadārthānvaye eva dvandvasya sādhutvaṃ, na tu taddhaṭakayatkiṃcitpadārthe tadanvaye ityabhyupagamāt, sautratvāt sādhutvakalpane tu gauravameva | samāsagrahaṇasya vidhyarthatve'pi 'bahupaṭava' ityatrārthavat‌vābhāvenārthavatsūtreṇa prātipadikatvānāpattyā 'citaḥ saprakṛte'riti vacanena cittvaprayuktāntodāttatvaṃ vākrottaravartiṃno- 'kārasya syāt, iṣyate tu ṭakārottaravatyarthakārasyeti spaṣṭaṃ bhāṣyādau | siddhānte tu 'antaraṅgānapī'ti nyāyena prathamajaso
luguttarameva pravartate svara itīṣṭasiddhiḥ, 'bahucpūrvasye'ti vacanārambhe gauravam | na ca taddhitaśabdasya taddhiśiṣṭaparatayā 'kṛttaddhite'-ti sūtreṇeva tatra prātipadikatvamiti vācyaṃ, 'pacatakī'tyādāvatiprasaṅgāt, 'bahupaṭavastiṣṭhantīti vākye'ti- prasaṅgācca | yasmāttaddhito vihitastaddhitaviśiṣṭasya tasyaiva prakṛtipratyayasamudāyasya grahaṇe pramāṇābhāvāt, tādṛśavacanāntarakalpane gauravācceti bhāvaḥ |
etenāvyavahitapūrvatvāñyavahitottaratvānyatarasaṃbandhena taddhitaviśiṣṭasya grahaṇamiti na pacata- kītyādāvatiprasaṅga iti nirastam, uktavākyāvāraṇāt, 'pratyayagrahaṇe tadantagrahaṇa'miti paribhāṣā- viruddhārthakalpane mānābhāvācca | evaṃ 'mūlakenopadaṃśaṃ bhūṅkte' ityatra 'mūlakenopadaśa'mityasya kṛdgrahaṇaparibhāṣayā prātipadikatvāpattirmūlakasyopadaṃśanakarmatvaṃ, bhūjau karaṇatvaṃ cāsti, vibhaktistu pradhānakriyāṃ prati karaṇatvaprayuktā tṛtīyaiva kṛteti mūlakasyetyupadaṃśanakārakatvākṣateḥ | na ca 'tṛtīyāprabhṛtīnī'ti vikalpena samāsavidhānasāmarthyānna tādṛśavākyasya prātipadikatvaṃ, vibhakti- śraṇasyaiva tatphalatvāditi vācyaṃ,samāse uttarapadaprakṛtisvaraḥ asamāse tu udātadvayamiti svara- viśeṣasya tatphalasya saṃbhavāt, 'dadhi upadaṃśa'mityatra pakṣe 'iko'savarṇe' iti prakṛtibhāvapravṛtterapi phalasya saṃbhavācca | etenotarapadaprakṛtisvare vikalpayitavye samāsavikalpavidhānavaiyarthya nirastam | anibhidhānena tadvaiyarthyopapādanaṃ tu na samañjasam | na ca kṛd‌grahaṇe iti paribhāṣāyā jñāpakasiddhatayāsārvatrikatvena kṛttaddhitetyatrāvṛttyā na mūlakenetyādāvatiprasaṅga iti vācyam | agatikāśrayaṇasyāyuktatvāt | siddhānte tu samāsagrahaṇakṛtaniyamena paratvādbādha iti kṛttaddhita ityatra 'kṛd‌grahaṇe' iti paribhāṣāyā apravṛttereveti bhūṣaṇādito'vagantavyamityalamativistareṇa | samāsavākye | samāsātmake vākye ityarthaḥ | lākṣaṇikārthavattvasyāpyasaṃbhavāditi | idaṃ naiyāyikaṃ pratyucyate | svabodhyasaṃbandharūpalakṣaṇāvādimīmāṃsakamate tatsaṃbhave'pi nirūḍhalakṣaṇāyāḥ śaktitulyatayā śābdikābhimatasiddhiriti bhāvaḥ | bhāṣyasiddha iti | 'apratyaya iti cet tibekādeśe pratiṣedho'ntavattvāt' | 'apratyaya iti cet tibekādeśe pratiṣedho vaktavyaḥ | kāṇḍe kuḍye |' ityarthavat‌sūtrabhāṣyasiddha ityarthaḥ | kṛtamalam | arthavadādisūtradvayaṃ nārambhaṇīyaṃ kiṃ tvatip‌prātipadikaṃ samāsaśca iti sūtradvayaṃ racanīyamityarthaḥ | samāsasyāpīti | samāsasyāpi tibantabhinnatvāt | tipaḥ pratyayatvena pratyayagrahaṇe yasmāditi paribhāṣāyāḥ pravṛtteriti bhāvaḥ |
arthavattvāvaśyakatveneti | arthavaditi viśeṣaṇasyāvaśyakatvenetyarthaḥ | nanu saṃkhyākarmādera- bhāvādekaikasmādvarṇāt subutpattirna baviṣyati autsargikameva vacanamapi gamakasattva eva spaṣṭam | ata eva 'ṅyāp-' sūtre bhāṣye tatsūtrābhāve tiṅntebhyaḥ subutpattiśaṅkaikatvādīnāmuktatvānneti samāhitā | evaṃ ca phalābhāvāt prativarṇa prātipadikasaṃjñāpi na bhaviṣyatīti cet tathāpi daśa dāḍimāni ṣaḍapūpā ityādyananvitāthakasamudāyasya vāraṇāya 'arthavat' iti viśeṣasyāvaśyakatvam | tatra hi prātipadikatve sati 'sūpo dhātu-' iti luk syāditi bhāṣyādau spaṣṭam | niyamārthasamāsagrahaṇena tu na vāraṇaṃ kartuṃ śakyam, niyamasya sajātīyāpekṣatayā parasparānvitārthakasamudāyasyaiva tena vāraṇāt, ata eva 'gavityāhe'tyādau samudāyasya na prātipadikasaṃjñeti spaṣṭaṃ śabdenduśekharādau |
samāsāvyāptīti | samāsaghaṇasya vidhyarthatve tu pūrvoktadūṣaṇagaṇaprasaṅga iti bhāvaḥ | 'asamāsavākyasya ne'ti tṛtīyaṃ sūtramapi kartavyamiti ced gauravamiti bhāvaḥ | atra samādyantānāṃ
prātipādikatvāpādanaṃ tu na kṛtaṃ, tattve'pi tebhyaḥ subutpaterāpattyaṃsabhavāt, apādaistirbādibhirvādha- nāt | nanu samāsagrahaṇasya vidhyarthatve'pi 'mūlakene'tyatra pūrvoktakrameṇātiprasaṅgo vārita ityato doṣāntaramāha-kiṃ caivamiti | evam - samudāyaśaktyanaṅgīkāre | ardhapippalītyādāviti | ādinā-'pūrvakāya' ityāderitetaradvandvasya ca saṃgrahaḥ | dvandve vyavahitapadārthe'pi karmatvādyanvayena vyabhicāro draṣṭavyaḥ, avyayībhāvādibhedaṃ niveśya vyutpattestadatiriktaviṣayakatvasaṃpādane tvanekabhedaniveśena gauravamiti bhāvaḥ |
nanupakumbhamityatra kumbhapadasyaiva lakṣaṇayā kumbhasamīpārthakatvamevamarghapiplītyādāvapi pippalyādiśabdasyaiva viśiṣṭārthakatvamiti na tatra vyabhicāra iti cedviśiṣṭe'rthe lakṣaṇāṃ vanaivānvayopapattau tatsvīkāro'nucitaḥ, ata eva 'pūrvapadārthapradhāno'vyayībhāva' iti saṃgacchate, kiṃ ca dvandve vyabhicāro dṛḍha eveti bhāvaḥ | bhāṣyakāramate'rdhapippalyādeḥ karmadhārayatvenottarapadārthaprādhānyena tatra vyabhicārābhāve'pyupakumbhādau vyabhicāro niṣpratyūha eveti dhyeyam | samasyamānapadottarameveti | samasyamānaṃ padaṃ ca samāsaghaṭakībhūtapadaṃ pūrva paraṃ madhyamaṃ ca, tathā cāvyayībhāve'vyayam, ekadeśisamāse ekadeśavācipadaṃ, dvandve sarvapadānyapi samasyamānānīti tattadarthe vibhaktyarthānvayaḥ | avyayībhāvādisamāse'vyayaṃ subantena samasyate ityādināvyayādereva samasanakriyākarmatvaṃ bodhyate, natvasyeti bodhyam | evaṃ ca samāse kkacitpūrvapadārthe kkaciduttarapadārthe kkacidyāvatsamasyāmāna- padārthe'nvaya ityatra tadanuśāsanaṃyogyatādikaṃ niyāmakamiti bhāvaḥ | viśiṣṭottarameveti | samāsarūpasamudāyottaramevetyarthaḥ | evaśabdena samāsaghaṭakapadavyavacchedaḥ |
anuśāsanāt - prātipadikāt svādayo bhavantītyanuśāsanāt, tathā casamasyamānapadottaraṃ tadanuśāsanamasiddhameveti tātparyam, na ca pratilakṣyaṃ lakṣaṇabhedena samasyamānarūpaprātipadikatvāśrayāt svādaya ityevānuśāsanaṃ kalpyata iti vācyam, prātipadikatvaparyāptyadhikaraṇāt, svādaya ityanuśāsanasyaiva samarthasūtrabhāṣyasiddhatayātrānyathā tatkalpane mānābhāvāt, samudāyasyāṅgatvāna- patteravayavasya tadāpatteśca, tathā sati 'devasya puruṣo devapuruṣastasyedaṃ daivapuruṣa'mityādau samudāyādervṛddhirna syāduttarapadādereva syāt | 'avayavādṛto'rityāderjñāpakāt samudāyādereva bhaviṣyatīti cet, etādṛśakliṣṭakalpanāpekṣayā samudāyaśaktyanusaraṇameva yuktamiti bhāvaḥ | evamupakumbhamityādau samudāyottaraṃ vibhaktiśravaṇānāpattirapi draṣṭavyā ityeva | kalpyata iti | na tu prakṛtitvaparyāptyadhikaraṇārthe vibhaktyarthānvaya iti kalpyata ityarthaḥ | prakṛtitvāśrayatvaṃ cottarapade pūrvapade'pīti noktadoṣa iti bhāvaḥ | anvayaprasaṅga iti | ānayanādhikarmatvānvayaprasaṅga ityarthaḥ | iṣṭāpattistu na, satyapi tātparye vinā lakṣaṇāṃ tādṛśānvayodhānanubhavāt | paṅkānayana- prasaṅgācca | abhīṣṭāpattau sthalāntare'tiprasaṅgamāha-aghaṭamiti | pūrvoktasthale daṇḍādiviśiṣṭe kriyānvaye viśeṣaṇībhūtadaṇḍādāvapyanvaye kadācidiṣṭāpattiḥ syāt, atra tu na kasyāpi ghaṭe ānayanakarmatvānvayo'nubhavasiddha iti bhāvaḥ | viśeṣaṇatayā na tatreti | prakṛtijanyapratītiviśeṣye eva vibhaktyarthānvaya iti vyutpatteriti bhāvaḥ | citragusvāminastu prakṛtijanyapratītiviśeṣyatayā na vibhaktyarthānvayānupapattiḥ | ānayanādyanvayaḥ - ānayanakarmatvādyanvayaḥ | janyatāyāḥ - pañcamyarthabhūtāyāḥ | rūpasukhādāvananvayeti | nīlarūpasukhayornīladiśabdajanyapratītiviśeṣyatvā- bhāvāt, nīlaśabdasya nīlarūpaviśiṣṭaparatvāt, sukhiśabdasya tadviśiṣṭabodhakatvāt, athaikatra-
viśeṣaṇatayānvite itaratra viśeṣaṇatayānvaya evāvyutpanno, na tu viśeṣyatayānvaya iti na 'daṇḍinaṃ pūjaye'tyādau pūjanakarmatvādyanvayāpattiḥ, 'pākānnīla' ityādau nīlādau pākādijanyatvādyanvayānupa- pattiśceti cenna, evaṃ sati 'śūlī pūjya' ityādau 'śūlādau pūjyādirūpaviśeṣaṇānvayāpatteḥ, kiṃ ca śūlini dvitvāderbādhe śūle'pādhe 'śūlinau paśyata' ityādiprayogāpattiriti dik | jñānaviṣayatvamātramiti | na tu prakṛtivṛttyā tajjanyajñānaviṣayatvamityarthaḥ | atra - uttarapadādi- lakṣayacitragusvāmyādau | samavāyeneti | samavāyenopasthite ākāśe ghaṭapadottaravartidvitīyārtha- karmatvānvayavāraṇāyetyarthaḥ | vṛttyā prakṛtyarthatvasya - vṛttyā prakṛtijanyajñānaviṣayatvasya | āvaśyakatvāt - avaśyaṃ praveśanīthatvāt | 'ghaṭa'mityādau ghaṭatvādau karmatvādyanvayavāraṇāya viṣayatāsāmānyamupekṣya viśeṣyatvapraveśaḥ, ata eva 'daṇḍinaṃ paśye'tyādau daṇḍādau na tadanvayaḥ | ākāśe na tadanvayaḥ, pratyayaprāgvartipadavṛttyetyasyāpi niveśyātvāditi bhāvaḥ | kṛṣṇe tṛtīyārtheti | avyavadhānāṃśaniveśe tu 'upakumbha'mityādau pūrvapadārthe vibhaktyārthānvayāpattiriti bhāvaḥ | evaṃ 'bahupaṭaḥ', 'sarvaka' ityādāvavyaptiḥ, 'dadhīyaddadātī'tyādau dadhyādāvativyāptiśca draṣṭavyā, 'yaduttaraṃ yaḥ pratyayo vihitastattve satī'ti niveśe tu 'citragu'rityādābuttarapadasya tattvābhāyāduktadoṣatā- davasthyaṃ bodhyam | klaptavyutpattīti | 'pratyayānāṃ prakṛtyarthānvite'ti vyutpattītyarthaḥ | nanvanekasamāsarūpasamudāyeṣu śaktikalpanāmapekṣya vyutpattyantarakalpanā, klaptavyutpattityāgaśca na gururata āhadigiti | tadarthastu-'rājapuruṣa' ityādau rājasaṃbandhilakṣaṇāyāṃ rājña pakadeśatayā viśeṣaṇānvayavāraṇe'pi 'dāsyāḥ putra' ityādyaluk‌samāse lakṣaṇāyāṃ mānabhāvena dāsyādau śobhanā- diviśeṣaṇānvayo durvāraḥ, tatrāpi saṃbandhilakṣaṇāyāṃ dāsīsaṃbandhibodhaḥ syāt, iṣṭāpattistu na, sarvānubhavavirodhāt, alugviṣayībhūtavibhaktirna svārthopasthāpikā, ata evā'psucaro' 'goṣucara' ityatra na bahutvapratītiḥ, evaṃ ca dāsīśabdasya dāsīsaṃbandhilakṣaṇāyāmapi rājñaḥ puruṣa iti vākyādiva samāsādapi rājasaṃbandhaprakārakaḥ saṃbandhasaṃsargako rājaprakārakapuruṣaviśeṣyako bodho bhāṣyādisiddhaḥ saṃbandhilakṣaṇāyāṃ na nirvahatītyādirbodhyaḥ | tathātvābhāvāditi | vibhaktijanyopasthitiviṣayatvā- bhāvādityarthaḥ | nanu 'śubhrastaṇḍula' ityādau śubhrādirūpanāmārthaprakārakaśābdabodho viśeṣyatāsaṃbandhena taṇḍulādau jāyate, na ca tatra vibhaktijanyopastitirviśeṣyatayeti vyabhicārānnoktakāryakāraṇasaṃbhavaḥ, taṇḍulaḥ pacatī'tyatra uktabodhavāraṇāyāgatyā dhātujanyopasthiteḥ pratibandhakatvamāstheyaṃ, dhātubhinnajanyopasthiterhetutvaṃ vā kalpyam, tathā ca 'rājapuruṣa' ityādāvanvayabodhe bādhakabhāva ityāśaṅkyāha-taṇḍulaḥ śubhra ityādāviti | prātipadikārthakaprathameti | prātipadikasyārtho yasyāstādṛśī yā prathamā tadarthe ityārthaḥ | 'prātipadikārthamātre prathame'tyasya pratipadikārthamātre vācye prathametyarthaḥ, 'pāka' ityādau vyāpārasyeva prātipadikārthasyāpi dvedhā bhānaṃ, vibhaktyā'sattvarūpeṇa sa upasthāpyate iti virūpopasthiteḥ sattvenābhedānvayasaṃbhava iti bhāvaḥ, evaṃ ca nokta kāryakāraṇabhāve vyabhicāra iti bhāvaḥ |
nanvatra vyabhicāravāraṇe'pi 'śubhraṃ taṇḍulaṃ' 'śubhreṇa taṇḍulene'tyādau vyabhicāro durvāra eva, dvitīyādeḥ prātipadikārthe vihitatvābhāvādata āha śubhreṇa taṇḍulenetīti | abhedārtheti | karmādyabhedasya karmādirūpatayā'bhedasyānuśāsanaviṣayatvāditi bhāvaḥ | evameva 'stokaṃ pacatī'tyādau na vyabhicāraḥ | nanu viśeṣyavācakapadottaravibhaktyaiva karmādyarthasya gamitatayā viśeṣaṇavācakapadottara-
vibhaktiḥ sādhutvārthaiva, tathā ca śubhrapadārthasya taṇḍulenaiyānvayo vācya iti vyabhicārastadavastha evetyāśaṅkyāha-pārṣṇiko veti | ayaṃ bhāvaḥ-karmaṇyeva dvitīyotyādiniyamena sādhutvārthe vibhaktiriti na yuktaṃ, kiṃ tu karmādyayiṃkaiva, 'kaṭo'pi karma bhīṣmādayo'pi karma' iti bhāṣyādviśeṣaṇe'pi karmatvādisattvāt, evaṃ ca kārakāṇāṃ kriyāyaivānvaya iti niyamāt kriyāyāmanvayaḥ, tathā ca viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyayoḥ parasparamanvayābhāvānna vyabhicāraḥ, na ca kriyayānvayādeva vyabhicāraḥ, vibhaktyarthadvāraiva tadanvayasyāṅgīkārāt, parasparānvayābhāve viśeṣyasya vyavacchedo na syāt, tathā ca 'śubhreṇa taṇḍulena śivaṃ pūjaye'dityukte'pi śyāmāditaṇḍulena yena kenacicchubhreṇa pūjanaprasaktiḥ, evaṃ sāmarthyābhāvācchubhrataṇḍulo nīlotpalamityādiḥ samāso na syāditi doṣaparihārāya dvayoḥ pāṣṇikaḥ parasparamanvayabodho'ṅgīkṛtaḥ, sa ca śābdo, mānaso vā | na ca sāmarthyavirahe'pi 'viśeṣaṇaṃ viśeṣyeṇe'tyādinā vidhānasāmarthyātsamāsa iti vācyaṃ, tatsāmarthyāt pāṣṇiṃkabodhakalpanāyā eya yuktatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
pare tu prathamāyāḥ prātipadikārtho na vācyo bodhāvṛttiprasaṅgāt, ekarūpeṇopasthitayoranva- yāsaṃbhavācca | bhinnarūpeṇa bodhānanubhavācca, ata eva tattatpratipadikena tattadarthe vivakṣite taduttaraṃ prathametyeva vākyārthaḥ svīkriyate, evaṃ viśeṣaṇavibhakterabhedatvenābhedārthakatve mānābhāvaḥ, karmādyabhedasya karmābhedarūpatve'pi karmatvādinaiva vācyatāyā anuśāsanasiddhatvāt kathaṃ tarhi śubhrādipadād dvitīyeti cedatrāhuranabhihitasūtre bhāṣyakārāḥ 'viśiṣṭasya kriyayepsitatvāt karmatvādubhayatrāpi dvitīyā' iti | viśiṣṭaniṣṭhaṃ karmatvaṃ samānavibhaktikatvaṃ vinā na pratyāyayituṃ śakyata iti tadbhāvaḥ | atra pakṣe'pi viśeṣaṇapadāt karmatvādyanāliṅgatārthopasthitiḥ, viśeṣyāttu tadvaiśiṣṭyena pratīterviśiṣṭa- syaikakarmatvabodhaḥ, tato'thavā kaṭo'pi karma bhīṣmādayo'pīti' samadadhuḥ | atra pakṣe viśeṣaṇapadaṃ pṛthak‌tattacchaktimattayā pratipādakaṃ, dravyeṇābhedāttu tasyāḥ śaktervākyārthabodhena viśiṣya grahaḥ, atrāpi pakṣe viśeṣyaviśeṣaṇayoḥ parasparamanvayapūrvakameva kriyānvayaḥ, kārakāṇāṃ bhāvanānvayaniyabhaṅgastu na, sākṣāt kriyājanakānāṃ sākṣādeva bhāvanānvayaḥ, dravyadvārā tajjanakānāṃ tu dravyadvāraivānvaya iti niyamāṅgīkarāt | 'guṇānāṃ ca parārthatvā'diti nyāyasya tu nātrāvasaraḥ, yato dravyaguṇayoruktarītyā samatvābhāvaḥ, tato viśeṣyasyaiva karmatvaṃ, tatsāmānādhikaraṇyād bhīṣmādibhyo'pi dvitīyeti samādadhuḥ |
atra kaiyaṭopādhyāyāḥ yatheśvarasuhṛdaḥ nirdhanā api taddhanenaiva dhanaphalabhājastadvattadekayo- pakṣematvāditi, viśeṣyasamānaśakti vinā viśeṣyatayaikārthabodhaikatvarūpasāmānādhikaraṇyānupapattiriti viśeṣaṇasya viśeṣyasamānaśaktikatvameva, taccāropitameveti tato'pi vibhaktinirvāha iti bhāvaḥ | asminnapi pakṣe tayoreva parasparamanvayo labhyata" ityāhuḥ | vistarastu mañjūṣādito'nagantavyo granthavistarabhayānneha vitanyate | evaṃ ca 'śubhrastaṇḍula' ityādau vyabhicāro duṣpariharaḥ, tadvāraṇārtha bhedasaṃbandhāvacchinnanāmārthaprakāratākatvaṃ vibhaktijanyopasthiteḥ kāryatāvacchedake yadi niveśyate, tarhi-rājasaṃbandhino bhedena bodhe noktaṃ vādhakamiti pratibhāta, 'kiṃ ce'tyuttaragrantha ita evāsvarasādvokta iti bodhyam |
