nanu samāsasamānārthakatvaṃ samāsajanyapratītiviṣayārthapratītijanakatvaṃ, tacca prakṛte'kṣataṃ, samāsajanyapratītiviṣayarājasaṃbandhaviṣayakapratītijanakatvādrājñaḥ puruṣa ityasyetyata āha 
anyatheti | uktapratītimātrajanakatvasya virgaharūpatve | tasmāt-vigraharūpavivaraṇāt | śaktinirṇayo na syāditi | vivaraṇavivriyamāṇayoḥ samānārthakatvaniyame vivaraṇasya śaktinirṇākatvaṃ saṃbhavet, asāmānārthakasyāpyuktasya vivaraṇatvāṅgīkāre tu tādṛśaniyamabhaṅgena vivaraṇādabhiyuktoktaḥ śaktinirṇayo na syāt, tathā ca phalārthakakarotinā vivaraṇādākhyātasya yatne śaktirityādikaṃ vilīyeteti bhāvaḥ, athavā 'rājñaḥ puruṣa' iti vigrahasya samāsaniṣṭhārthabodhakatānirṇāyakatvamanu- bhavasiddhaṃ bhajyeteti bhāvaḥ | prasaṃgāditi | rājyasaṃbandhasya nāmārthatvenābhedasaṃbandhenaiva puruṣeṇānvayabodha syānna tvāśrayasaṃbandheneti bhāvaḥ | nanu samānavibhaktikatvaṃ nāmārthayorabhedabodhe tantraṃ, taccātra nāstīti kathaṃ rājasaṃbandhaḥ puruṣa iti bodhāpattirata āha virudvavibhaktīti | 'sundaraṃ dadhi' 'secakṛdvārī'tyādau vyabhicārāt samānavibhaktikatvasyoktabodhe prayojakatvāsaṃbhavā- dviruddhavibhaktirāhityasyaiva prayojakatvamurarīkriyata iti bhāvaḥ | idaṃ cāpādanaṃ yogyatājñānaṃ bhramātmakamayogyatāniścayābhāvo vā yadā vartate tadā bodhyam, vastutastu svamate yogyatājñānasya hetutvamayogyatāniścayasya pratibandhakatvaṃ ca nāstīti pūrva nirūpitaṃ na vismartavyam |
athoktavyutpattinirvāhako nāmārthaprakārakābhedānvayabodhe viruddhavibhaktirahitanāmajanyopa- sthitirheturiti kāryakāraṇabhāvo na saṃbhavati, 'caitrasya sutasya dhana'mityatrātiprasaṅgāditi cenna, vibhaktyarthamanantarbhāvya nāmārthanvayamātrabodhe uktakāryakāraṇabhāvāṅgīkārāt, caitramātraprakārakasyā- bhedasaṃsargakabodhasyeṣṭatvāditi bhāvaḥ | viruddhavibhaktiḥ - viruddhārthakavibhaktivibhinnārthakavibhaktiriti yāvat, tādṛśavibhaktirāhityamuktabodhe tantram | 'rājñaḥ sutasya dhana'mityatra tu vibhaktyorna samānārthakatvamekatra janyatvasyāparatra svatvasya tadarthatvāt, 'rājñaḥ puruṣasya dhana'mityatra ṣaṣṭhīdvayasya svatvārthakatve'pi tayoḥ svatvayorbhinnatvānna doṣa iti tu pare | samāse vyutpattyantaramaṅgīkurma iti cedatiriktaśaktisiddhirapratyūhaiva, tadetat sarvamabhipretyāha prapañcitaṃ vaiyākāraṇabhūṣaṇe iti |
ata eva - pṛthagupasthityabhāvaprayojakasamudāyaśaktaraṅgīkārādeva | bhakṣamuddiśyeti | ekarūpetyarthaḥ | bhaṅgāpatteriti | tathā sati sāmarthyavighātāt samāso na syāditi bhāvaḥ | samāse hi sarvapadārthānāmuddeśyatvaṃ vidheyatvaṃ vā bhavati, yathā 'dhavakhadirau sundarau' 'imau vṛkṣau dhavakhadirā'viti | na tu samāsaghaṭakaikapadārthamuddiśya tad‌ghaṭakāparapadārthasya vidheyatvamuktadoṣāditi prathamyaviśiṣṭabhakṣa iti mīmāṃsātṛtīyādhyāyasthastatratyasiddhānto bodhyaḥ | tryaṅgaiḥ sviṣṭakṛta- miti | 'paśau hṛdayasyāgre'vadyatī'tyādinā ekādaśāṅgānāṃ homasya vidhānena 'prakṛtivadvikṛti'riti nyāyenaikādāśabhiraṅgaihome prāpte idaṃ vākyamārabhyate, tatrāṅgānuvādena tritvaṃ vidhīyata iti pūrvapakṣaḥ, uktadoṣāt tritvaviśiṣṭāṅgāntaravidhiriti daśamādhyāye siddhāntitam, etacca viśiṣṭaśaktipakṣe ghaṭapadādghaṭaghaṭatvayoriva viśiṣṭaśaktyaiva prāthamyaviśiṣṭabhakṣasya tritvaviśiṣṭāṅgānāṃ copasthityā vākyārtharūpoddeśyavidheyabhāvenānvayāsaṃbhavāt saṃgacchate, samudāyaśaktyanaṅgīkāre tu 'prathamo bhakṣastribhiraṅgai'ritivat samāse'pi tathānvayaḥ syādityadhikaraṇavirodha iti samuditārthaḥ | na ca 'yad‌vṛttayogaḥ prāthamyamityādyuddeśyalakṣaṇa'mityādya- bhiyuktoktasyoddeśyatāvidheyatāniyāmakasya yad‌vṛttayogaprāthamyāderabhāvānna bhakṣārthānuvādena prāthamyādividhiriti vācyaṃ, 'prathamo bhakṣa' ityādāvapi tadanāpatteḥ, ata eve'tyādyuddeśya- lakṣaṇa'mityatrādipadaṃ yad‌vṛttāderaniyatatvasūcanārthamupāttam |
vidheyatāyā viśeṣyamātraniṣṭhatayā na prāthamyadervidheyatvamityapi na yuktam, ajñātasyaiva vidheyatvena tasya viśeṣyatvabad‌ viśeṣaṇatvasyāpi saṃbhavena tathā niyane mānābhāvāt, ata eva 'prathamo bhakṣa' ityatra prāthamyādervidheyatvānubhavaḥ, na ca samastabhinnapadajanyopasthitestādṛk‌śābdabodhe hetutayā na bhakṣādyanuvādena prāthamyādividhyāpattiriti vācyam, 'pītavāsāścaturbhujaḥ' 'prathamabhakṣaḥ kartavya' ityādau tathā bodhānāpatteḥ, na ca bhedapratiyogisamastapade uddeśyavidheyavācakobhayaghaṭitatvaṃ niveśyoktahetu- hetumadbhāvakalpanānna ko'pi doṣa iti vācyaṃ, tathāvidhagurutarakāryakāraṇabhāvakalpanāpekṣayaikārthī- bhāvakalpanāyāmeva samīcanatetyāśayāt | pare tu 'lohitoṣṇīṣā ṛtvijaḥ pracaranti' 'saptadaśāratnirvājapeyasya yūpo bhavatī'tyādau uṣṇīṣayūpāratnī anūdya lauhityasaptadaśatvayo- viṃdhiriti mīmāṃsakasiddhāntāttathānvayābhiprāyeṇa samāsaprayoge prāptatvādiparyālocanottaraṃ tathā bodha iṣṭa eva, ata eva 'apṛkta ekāl pratyayaḥ' 'abhyāsalopaśce'tyādiprayoge tathārtho'nubhūyate, ata evālaṃkārikaiḥ 'nyakkāro'yamatīva me yadaraya'ityādau uddeśyavācakedampadāprakṛte'pi vilambena tathāpratītiḥ, samāse tathāpratītyabhāva ityutsargaparityāgena sahṛdayodvego vā duṣṭibījaṃ, tadvadevāsyāpi nāsādhutoktasautraprayogāt, na cātra yūpāratnyuṣṇīṣayoratideśaprāptatayātideśādu- padeśasya balattvena saptadaśāratnirityādivākyairevādau viśiṣṭavidhiriti vācyaṃ, yūpaṛtvijāmapyatideśaprāptatayātideśaparyālocanottaramevāsya vidheḥ prakṛtteḥ, 'prathamabhakṣa' ityatra tu samākhyāpekṣayā vākyaśrutyorbalavattvena prathamato naiva bhakṣāntaravidhirityādi mañjūṣāyāṃ pratyapīpadan | prapañcitaṃ bhūṣaṇe iti | 'aruṇayaikahāyanyā piṅgākṣyā somaṃ krīṇātī'ti śrutam, atra hi kimaruṇimā vākyādbhittvā prākaraṇikeṣu sarvadravyeṣu niveśyaḥ ? kiṃ vā krīṇātinā saṃbandhamanubhūya tadaṅgaṃ sadekahāyanyām ? iti saṃdehe-amūrtatvena kriyāyāṃ karaṇakārakatvāsaṃbhavāttato vicchinnasya prakāraṇakalpitaśrutānumitaikadeśaniṣpannena vākyenāruṇimna prakṛtāpūrvasādhanībhūtadravyaparicchedaṃ bhāvayedityākārakeṇa prākaraṇikasarvadravyāṅgatvamiti pūrvapakṣe-yodyatājñānaṃ na śābdabodhakāraṇamapi tvayogyatāniścayasya pratibandhakatvamātraṃ, tataśca tādṛśaniścayābhāvasattve āruṇyasyāpi kriyānvayabodhopapattiḥ, pāṣṇiṃkastu dravyeṇānvaya iti na tato vicchedakaśaṅketyādi siddhāntitam | atra pūrvapakṣasya noktisaṃbhavaḥ, viśiṣṭaśaktyasvīkāre piṅgākṣyekahāyanīśabdayorapi piṅgatvākṣitvaikatvahāyanatvādervācyatayā teṣāmapyamūrtatvāt krayaṇakaraṇatvasaṃbhavena karaṇasyaitadvākyā- dalābhenāruṇyaprākaraṇikakaraṇānuvādāsaṃbhave vā vākyabhedaśaṅkāyā evāsaṃbhavāt, triṣvapi lakṣitadravyavidhyupapattau vākyabhedaśaṅkāyā nivarttitvācca, trayāṇāmapi lākṣaṇikatvāviśeṣe bahuvrīhyoreva dryavidhāyākatvaṃ nāruṇapadasyetyatra vinigamakāsaṃbhavādityādikaṃ bhūṣaṇe nirūpitam |
atha tṛtīyāyā niṣkṛṣṭaśaktivācakatvamiti mīmāṃsakasiddhāntāttayā māstu karaṇībhūtadravyānu- vādaḥ, 'aruṇe'ti prātipadikena guṇabācakenāpi lakṣaṇayā dravyapratipādakatvaṃ saṃbhavatyeva, pratipādyaṃ dravyaṃ ca tṛtīyāsaṃyogāt karaṇatvena bhāsate, tathā ca guṇaviśiṣṭakaraṇapratītirupapadyate, evaṃ ca karaṇatayāvagatāni dravyāṇi viśeṣyatā'nūdyataddhaṭitaviśeṣaṇarūpo guṇaḥ paricchedakatvena vidhīyate, yena dravyeṇa kicit kriyate tadaruṇaguṇakeneti pūrvapakṣaḥ saṃbhavaduktikaṃ eva | etena vākyabhedenāruṇyasya prakāraṇe niveśo na saṃbhavati, na tāvat prākaraṇikakriyābhiḥ saṃbandho mūrtatvāt, dravyaiḥ kārakatvāt, anuyātāni ca dravyāṇi kathaṃ guṇasaṃbandhaṃ bhajeranniti pūrvapakṣo'pi nirastaḥ,
tadetad dhvanayannāha āstāṃ vistara iti | atra jaiminīyānāṃ jaiminisūtravirodho'tyantamanucita iti dhvananārtha jaimimīyairavaśyābhyapeya ityuktamityalaṃ vistareṇa || 34 ||
nanu samudāyaśaktityavasthāpanānantaram 'ākhyātaṃ taddhitikṛtoḥ' ityādyabhihitaṃ, tanna pūrvagraṃthasaṃgatamityāśaṅkānirākaraṇāya saṃgatimāha rājapuruṣa ityādāvityādinā | rājā cāsāviti | rājapadaṃ vigrahasthamapi rājasaṃbandhiparamata eva vigrahasya samāsasamānārthatvāhāniriti bhāvaḥ | ityeva vigraha iti | 'eva' śabdena prasiddhasya rājñaḥ puruṣa ityasya nirāsaḥ | atha ṣaṣṭhyantena vigrahe'pi rājapadaṃ rājasaṃbandhilakṣakaṃ, ṣaṣṭhyabhedārthiketyabhyupagame nirvahatyeva samānārthatvamiti cet, ṣaṣṭyarthābhedasya vigrahe rājasaṃbandhinaṃ prati prādhānyena bhānāt samāse tathā'bhānāt samānārthatvahāneranuddhārāt, ṣaṣṭhyā abhedārthatve mānābhāvācca | atha sādhutvārthikā ṣaṣṭhīti na samāsasāmānārthatvahāniriti cenna, saṃbandhe eva ṣaṣṭhyā vidhānāt, sādhutvārthakatatprayogānupapatteḥ | gavāmayamityeveti | 'vigraha' ityanuṣajyate | 'śeṣo bahuvrīhirita sūtrāditi | yasya trikasya viśiṣya samāso noktaḥ sa śeṣaḥ, tādṛśaṃ ca svāditrikameveti bhāvaḥ | prathamāntam - prathamāntapadaghaṭitaṃ | pakṣe labhyata iti | samāsasya vaikalpikatvāditi bhāvaḥ | bhinnārthatvāditi | 'rājapuruṣa' iti samāsādrājasaṃbandhiprakārakasyābhedasaṃsargasya, 'citraguriti samāsāccitrābhinnagosvāmītyākārakasya vartipadārthaprakārakānyapadārthaviśeṣyakasya, citrā gāvo yasyeti vākyāt tatsvāmikāścitraḥ gāva ityākārakasyānyapadārthaprakārakasya vartipadārtha- viśeṣyakasya bodhasyotpadādbhinnārthatvamiti bhāvaḥ | ākṣika iti | 'tena dīvyatīti' sūtreṇa devanakartari ṭhak | kumbhakāra ityatra 'karmaṇyaṇiti sūtreṇāṇ | kumbhānukūleti kumbhakarmakotpattyanukūlavyāpāretyarthaḥ, utpattyānukūlavyāpāro dhātvarthaḥ | mīmāṃsakasyāpīti | asya 'saṃmati'riti śeṣaḥ | na vivaraṇatvabādhaka iti | taddhitakṛtorarthapradarśakavākye guṇapradhānyavaiparītye'pi vivaraṇatvavactitrā gāvo yasyeti vākye'pi vivaraṇatvasaṃbhavāditi bhāvaḥ | tathā ca vṛttijanyabodhasamānaviṣayakabodhajanakatvameva vigrahasya niyaṃtaṃ, na tu tajjanyabodha- samānaprakārakabodhajanakatvamapīti bhāvaḥ | kiṃ ca citrāṇāṃ gavāmayamiti vigrahasya matvarthe bahuvrīhiḥ, kartṛkarmavacanenāprathamāyāḥ aprathamāvibhaktyarthe bahuvrīhiḥ, śeṣo bahuvrīhistrikataḥ śeṣa ityādi munivacanaviruddhatvaṃ ca, na cālaukike prathamāntānāmeva praveśānna taddhirodha iti vācyam, alaukikake yadvibhaktyantānāṃ praveśastadvibhaktyantānāmeva pariniṣṭhitānāṃ laukike'rthabodhanāya sati saṃbhave praveśasyaucityāt, na tu ṣaṣṭhyantaghaṭitavigraho na yuktaḥ, aṣarṣṭhyabahuvrīhāvevamapyartha- sāmyāsaṃbhavāt, prāptasyodakasyāyamiti vigrahādudakakartṛkaprāptikarmetyākārakasya samāsajanyasya sarvānubhavasiddhasyānudayāt prāptikartrudakasaṃbandhītyākārakabodhasyaiva tata udayāditi dūṣaṇaṃ na samyak, udakakartṛkaprāptikarmatvādirūpasyaiva saṃbandhasyaiva bhānena samāsasamānārthakatva- nirvāhāditi " māmāṃsakanavyoktaṃ pratyuktam, uktamunivacanaviruddhatvāt |
pare tu 'tatra dṛṣṭo viparyaya' ityasya taddhitakṛdantayoḥ kartrādimukhyaviśeṣyakabodhajanakatvaṃ vigrahavākyasya, bhāvanāmamukhyaviśeṣyakabodhajanakatvaṃ taddhitāntāderthatprakārakayadviśeṣyakabodhajanakatvaṃ
tadviparītaviśeṣyaviśeṣaṇabhāvāpannārthaviṣayakabodhajanakatvaṃ paryavasitamityartho na ekādhikaraṇe, pūrvadṛṣṭaguṇatyāgapūrvakaguṇāntarasvīkārasyaiva viparyayapadārthatvāt, viśeṣyatādiviraryayasattve'pi tasyākhyātaniṣṭhatvābhāvāt, kiṃ tu loke prayuktatiḍantasya yathā bhāvanāmukhyaviśeṣyakabodhajanakatvaṃ na tathā bodhajanakatve, vigrahavākyasthasya tasyāpi tu taddhitāntādivat kartrādimukhyaviśeṣyaka- bodhajanakatvamityarthaḥ, evaṃ ca na vṛttivākyayoḥ samānārthatvabhaṅgaḥ | haripadyametatsamānārthakaṃ puñjarājenāpyevameva vyākhyātam, anena tulyanyāyāt sarvatra vṛttyarthapradarśakavākye evameveti sūcitam | tathā ca 'tadvahati' sūtrabhāṣye vahatyarthe rathādibhyaḥ pratyayavidhānamanarthakam, 'tasyedam' iti vihitatvādityuktvā voḍheti dravyapradhānaṃ vahatīti kriyāpradhānamityāśayenānyaḥ śabdo rathaṃ vahatyanyo rathasya voḍheti, tena na sidhyatītyāśaṅkyārthasāmānyāt siddhaṃ, ya evārtho rathaṃ vahati, sa eva rathasya voḍheti tadvaiyarthyadārḍhya kṛtvā 'dvirathya' ityādau yato luṅ mā bhūdityetadartha taditi siddhānta uktaḥ, idamarthe pratyaye tu tasya prāgdīvyatīyatvena 'dvigorluk' iti luk syāt, ayaṃ tu na prāgdīvyatīya iti na luk, ya evārtha ityādeḥ-rathyasya vigrahabhūte rathaṃ vahatītyatra sa eva tadvigrahabhūte rathasya voḍhetyatretyarthaḥ, vigrahāghaṭakatiḍante tukriyāprādhānyameva, vṛttivākyayoḥ samānārthatvānurodhāt, citrā gāvo yasyeti vigrahasyāpi citragosvāmītyākārabodhajanakatva- maṅgīkriyate, evaṃ 'prāptīdaka' iti samāsaḥ prāptikartrudakakarmetyākārakameva bodhaṃ janayati, udake viśeṣyabhūte karmatvanirupakatvasya bādhādvaśeṣaṇaprāptau 'śikhī dhvasta' itivatparyavasānam | ata eva prāptādipade viśeṣyaviśeṣaṇabhāvavyatvāsakalpanaprayuktagauravaṃ na | ata eva pūrvottarapadayoḥ samānādhikaraṇyasya bahuvrīhyupajīvyasya bhaṅgaḥ, puṃvadbhāvānāpattiśca neti vadantītyanyatra vistaraḥ || 35 ||
tathā vigrahaḥ - citrā gāvo yasyetyādyākāro vigrahaḥ | adhikaraṇasiddhānta iti | 'niṣādasthapati'śabdo lakṣaṇāviraheṇa lāghavāt karmadhārayo na tu ṣaṣṭhītatpupuṣa iti tatratyasiddhānta- statsiddhirviśiṣṭaśaktisvīkāre na syādubhayatrāpi śaktyaviśaṣādityarthaḥ | nanu śaktigrahe- 'ntaraṅgatvamityādyasaṃgatamubhayatrāpi śtigrahasya tulyakālatvādityata āha ayaṃ bhāva iti | niṣādasvāmike iti | nipādaḥ sthapattiḥ svāmī yasyeti bahuvrīhipakṣe ityarthaḥ, 'sthapati'śabdaḥ svāmiparyāyaḥ | sarvatra śaktatvāditi | tātparyasattve niṣādasaṃbandhisvāmyāderapi bodhotpattestatrāpi śaktirāvaśyakī, sati tātparye paramate'pi lakṣaṇayā tadbodhasyotpatteriṣṭatvāditi bhāvaḥ, etaccātraiva samanantaraṃ vakṣyati | tatkalpanāyāṃ - tātparyakalpanāyām | pūrvopasthitārtha eveti | niṣādarūpasthapatāveva niṣādasaṃbandhyapekṣayā tadupasthiteḥ prathamata evotpatteḥ | evaṃ ca śaktinirṇāya- katātparyagrahe'ntaraṅgatvādikaṃ paryavasyati, karmadhārayaśaktigrahāpekṣayā ṣaṣṭhītatpuruṣaśaktigrahasya ṣaṣṭhyartharūpādhikaviṣayatvena gauravamityapi bodhyam | upasthityādītyādipadena vā tad grāhyam | jaiminirapyāha 'sthapatinapādaḥ syācchabdasāmarthyā'diti | niṣādābhinnaḥ sthapatireva niṣādasthapitaśabdabodhyaḥ, antaraṅgatvena tathāvidhe'rthe śīghropasthitijanakatvānukūlaśaktirūpa- sāmarthāditi tadarthaḥ | nanu mīmāṃsakamate samāse śaktyabhāvāt kathaṃ tannānārthamata āha pareṣāmiti | sati tātparye iti | ṣaṣṭhītatpuruṣādyarthe tātparye satītyarthaḥ | yaṣṭīḥ praveśayeti- vaditi | tatra
yathā mukhyārthānvayānupapattyabāve'pi tātparyānupapattyā yaṣṭidharalākṣaṇikatvamevaṃ prakṛte'nvayānupapattyabhāve'pi ṣaṣṭhītatpuruṣādyarthe tātparyānupapattyā lakṣaṇā duvarityarthaḥ 'cintara'mityasya 'nāsaṃgata'miti śeṣaḥ | sthapatiśabdasya guṇaśabdatvaṃ, niṣādaśabdasya 'ambaṣṭhaḥ śūdrayāṃ niṣādo jātaḥ pāraśavo'pi ve'ti svaraṇādanulomajātiviśeṣavācitvam | tathā ca karma- dhārayasthapatiśabdasya pūrvanipātāpattistu na śaṅkyā, rājandantāditvena paranipātāt | atha saṃbandhyaṃśe nityasākāṅkṣatvena ṣaṣṭhītatpuru, eva yukta iti cenna, jaimininā 'liṅgadarśanācca' iti hetvantarābhidhānāt | liṅgaṃ ca dṛśyate kūṭaṃ dakṣiṇeti | kūṭaṃ hi niṣādānāmeva svamiti śābara- bhāṣyamiti dik || 36 ||
iti samāsaśaktinirūpaṇam ||
<6>
tasyāḥ - śakteḥ | 'anādityarthogyate'tyatra na ādiryasyāḥ setyartho boddhavyaḥ | saiva śaktiriti | bodhakāraṇatārūpaiva śaktirityarthaḥ | pare tu-"aryāsaṃbaddhasya śabdasyārthaviṣayakabodha- janakatvānvayathānupapattyākalpyamānaśaktirūpasaṃbandhasya bodhajanakatvarūpatvaṃ na saṃbhavati, ātmāśrayā- patteḥ | śābdabodharūpaphalopadhāyakatvānyathānupapattyā śābdabodhasvarūpayogyatātmakatatkāraṇatārūpā śaktiḥ kalpyata ityabhyupagame noktadoṣa iti cet, tathāpi pramāṇānāṃ janakatvataddhaṭitātirikta- saṃbandasyaiva pramitiprayojakatvasya klṛptatayeha janakatvasya tathātvakalpanamanucitam, anyathā vahnyādyanumitijanakatvameva dhūmādiniṣṭhaṃ vahnidhūmayoḥ saṃbandhaḥ syāt, viśiṣṭapratītiniyāmakasyaiva saṃbandhatayā janakatvasya tadaniyāmakatayā saṃbandhatvāsaṃbhavaśca, icchāyāstadviṣayatvasya ca viśiṣṭa- pratītiniyāmakatvābhāvena saṃbandhatvāsaṃbhavo bodhyaḥ, tasmāt padārthāntaraṃ śakti"rityāhuḥ | naiyāyikaḥ śaṅkate-nanviti | iyaṃ śaṅkā 'padārthāntaraṃ śakti'riti pakṣe'pi bodhyā | ādhunike - ādhunikasaṃketite | tadabhāvāt - anādiśakterabhāvāt, tathā ca tataḥ śābdabodho na syāt | nanu tatra śaktyabhāve mānābhāvo'ta āha-anyatheti | anyathā - tatra śaktisattve | pitrādisaṃketeti | yadi tatra śaktiḥ syāttarhi pitrādisaṃketajñānasya tad‌grāhakasāmagrīsattvena tad‌grahāpattiḥ, na ceṣṭāpattiḥ, tad‌graharūpakāraṇaghaṭitasāmagryā śābdabodhāpatterityarthaḥ | tathā ca yogyamānupalabdhireva tadabhāve mānamiti bhāvaḥ | lākṣaṇiketi | tasyāpi bodhajanakatayā tatrāpyanādiśaktiprasaṅga ityarthaḥ | saṃketajñānamiti | saṃketasyeśverecchārūpasya tīrādau lakṣye gaṅgāpadajanyabodhaviṣayatva- sattevapi na gaṅgāpadajanyabodhaviṣayatvatvena tīrāṃśe gaṅgāpadajanyabodhaviṣayatvaprakārakatvamīśva- recchāyā upeyate, kiṃ tu śuddhaviṣayatvatvenaiva, tāvataiva bhagavadicchāyāḥ sarvaviṣayakatvāvyādhātāt, tathā ca tatpadajanyabodhaviṣayatvatvāvacchinnaprakāratānirūpitaviśeṣyatāsaṃbandhena bhagavadicchārūpasaṃketa- prakārakajñānasya śaktigrahe hetutvāṅgīkārāllākṣaṇike tathāvidhasaṃketajñānābhāvena śaktijñānasaṃbhavena śābdabodhopapatteye lakṣaṇāṃgrahasya hetutvaṃ kalpyate, evaṃ ca śaktigrāhakābhāvena lākṣaṇike tatsadbhāve mānābhāvena na śaktiriti bhāvaḥ | ādhunike pitrāderiti | tatra viśiṣya 'cetrādipadajanyabodhaviṣayo jāyatā'miti bhagavatsaṃketābhāvāt-tādṛśasaṃketagrāhakakośādyabhāvāt | 'dvādaśe'hani pitā nāma kuryā'diti saṅketastu na viśiṣya tattatpadaviṣayaka iti so'kiṃcitkara iti saṅketamātraṃ
śaktiriti bhāvaḥ | svarupeṇa heturiti | svarūpasam heturityarthaḥ |
agṛhītaśaktikāditi-bahuvrīhiḥ,-padamanyapadārthaḥ, tatpuruṣeṇāgṛhītaśaktikādapi padā- tatpuruṣasya śābdabodhāpattiḥ, taddhetuśakteḥ sattvādityarthaḥ | sāmānyato jñātaḥ - yena kenacidrūpeṇa jñātaḥ | tajjñāne iti | īśvarādisaṅkete ityarthaḥ | laukiketi | laukikāśca mīmāṃsakādayaścetyarthaḥ, laukikapadena pāmarā nirīśvaravādino mīmāṃsakā atra bodhyāḥ, ādipadena bauddhādayaḥ | nanu toṣāmīśvarasaṅketatvena jñānasaṃbhave'pi saṃketatvena tajjñānaṃ saṃbhavatyevetyata āha tattadarthabodhajanakatvagrahavatāmeveti | evena-saṅketatvaprakārajñānavyudāsaḥ, tathā ca saṅketatvajñānāsattve'pyuktajñānācchabdabodhotpattyā vyabhicārātsaṃketatvena jñānamapi na heturiti bhāvaḥ | arthadhījanakateti | ayaṃ bhāvaḥ bodhajanakatvaṃ bodhajanakatvayogyatā, sā ca tadavacchedakadharmavattvaṃ, sa ca klṛptatvādīśvarādisaṃketa eva, tasya saṃketatvena jñānasya vyabicārāddhetutvāsaṃbhave'pi bodhajanakatāvacchedakatvena jñānasya hetutvaṃ saṃbhavatyeva, vyabhicārābhāvāditi | tajjñānaṃ - saṃketajñānam | lāghaveneti | janakatāvacchedakakoṭāvarthadhī- janakatāvacchedakatvasyāniveśena lāghavam | saṅketajñānādeveti | 'eva'śabdastadarthabodhajanakatva- grahavyāvartakaḥ | asya - tadarthabodhajanatāvacchedakatvagrahasya | avagāhaneneti | saviṣayakecchādijñānasya svaviṣayecchāderyadaṃśe yatprakārakatvaṃ tadaṃśe tatprakārakatvamiti niyamena tādṛśecchājñānasya pade'rthabodhajanakatvaprakārakatvasyākṣateriti bhāvaḥ | svātantryeṇeti | itarāviśeṣaṇatvenetyarthaḥ | atra tu prakāratvāṃśe tasya viśeṣaṇatvaṃ, bodhajanakatvaprakārakatvasyecchāyāṃ prakāratvāditi bhāvaḥ | anyathā - svatantrāsvatantrasādhāraṇatajjñānamātrasya hetutve | tad‌grahāpatteriti | arthagrahāpatterityarthaḥ, tatrāpi nañarthābhāve viśeṇatayā bodhajanakatvasyāva- gāhanāt | vādhena - pade vādhejanakatvābhāvaniścayena | pade pareti | 'idampadametadartha- bodhajanaka'miti jñānavānayamiti jñānavato'pi | tad‌graheti | pade'rithabodhajanakatva- grahānudayādityarthaḥ | anyathā - tadabhāvaniścayasattve'pi tatra tadvattāvagāhijñānābhyupagame | bhrāntatvāpatteriti | bhrāntijñasya 'rajatatvābhāvavāñśuktyādiḥ rajatatvena rūpeṇānena gṛhīta' iti jñānāvaśyakatve'pi rajatatvābhāvavacchuktiviśeṣyakarajatatvaprakārakajñānasaṃbhavāttasyāpi rajatatva- bhramāśrayatvaṃ syāditi bhāvaḥ | nanu gaṅgāpadepi tīrabodhakatvasya sattvena tīraśaktatvameva syāditi lakṣaṇocchedāpattistāmiṣṭāpattayā pariharati nāpīti | sādharaṇyāditi | evaṃ ca vyavahārāttatrāpi śaktigrahaeveti bhāvaḥ, etena tatra śaktigrāhakakośāderabhāvānna gaṅgāpadādau tīrādinirūpitā śaktirabhimateti pratyuktaṃ, śaktigrāhakaśiromaṇervyavahārasya sattvāt, prasiddhayaprasiddhibhyā mukhya- lakṣyavyavahāra upapādanīyaḥ | nanu vyavahārasya lakṣaṇāgrāhakatayopattau gaṅgādipade tīrādivāca- katvapramāṇikam, 'anyāyaścānekārthatva'miti nyāyaviruddhaṃ ca, ata eva gaṅgāpadasya tīra paryāyamadhye kośe na pāṭha ityata āha kiṃ ceti | śābdabodhānaṅgatvāt - tathopasthitisattve'pi agṛhītaśaktikasya pusaṃstena śabdena śābdabodhajananāttasyāstadanaṅgatvaṃ bodhyam |
śaktijanyeti | śaktijñānajanyetyarthaḥ, evamagre'pi bodhyaṃ | 'jñāyamānavṛtterupasthitahetutva'miti naye tu yathāśrutameva sādhu | gauravamiti | nanu tīrādau gaṅgādipadavācyatvavyavahārabhāvena tatra śaktisvīkāro'nucita iti lakṣaṇākhyaṃ vṛttyantaramavaśyamabhyupeyaṃ, tathā ca kāryakāraṇabhāvadvaya-
kalpanāgauravaṃ na doṣāyetyata āha api ceti | athavā tīrādibodhajanakatā gaṅgādipadaniṣṭhāpravahabodhajanakatāto bhinneti nirvivādameva, evaṃ ca tīrabodhajanakatāyāmapa śaktitvasaṃbandhakalpane tava mate'pi gauravaṃ, śakyasaṃbandharūpalakṣaṇā tu klṛptaiveti na tatkalpanamadhikaṃ, tajjanyopasthiteḥ śābdabodhahetutvamātraṃ mayā kalpyata iti nādhikaṃ gauravaṃ ; bodhajanakatātvaṃ tu na śaktitvam, anumānādividhayā bodhajanakatve'tiprasaṅgāt, tadatiriktajanakatātvasya śaktitvoktau tu gauravamanyāyaśceti nyāyatyāgo'pi netyāha-api ceti | vyabhicāraḥ vyatirekavyabhicāraḥ | tamupapādayati-śaktīti | avyeti | samavāyaghaṭitasāmānādhikaraṇyaviśiṣṭāvyavahitottaratvasaṃbandhe- netyarthaḥ | tena caitrīyaśaktijanyopasthityanantaraṃ jāyamānamaitrīyaśābdabodhasyāpyavyavahitottaratva- saṃbandhenopasthitimattvena tadupasthitermaitrātmanyasattve'pi na vyabhicāraḥ, maitrīyaśābdabodhasya caitrī- yopasthityasamānādhikaraṇatvena kāryatāvacchedakānākrāntatvāt, samānādhikaraṇyasaṃbandhamātreṇa tatta- dupasthitimattvasya kāryatāvacchedakakoṭau niveśe pūrvakāle, parakāle votpanne tatpuruṣīyalakṣaṇā- janyopasthitijanyaśābdabodhe-śaktijanyopasthiteravyavahitapūrvakṣaṇaḥ tadātmanyasattvena vyabhicāra itya- vyavahitottaratvā'nusaraṇam, avyavahitapūrvakālotpanne tathāvidhaśābdabodhe vyabhicāro mā prasāṅkṣīdityu'ttaratva' niveśanaṃ, vyavahitāttarālotpanne bodhe vyabhicāravyāvṛttaye'vyavadhānāṃśo- pādānam | ananteti | upasthitīnāmānantyena tadbhedena kāryakāraṇabhāvānantyāpatteriti bhāvaḥ | uktasaṃbandhena śaktijñānajanyopasthitiviśiṣṭaśābdatvāvacchinnaṃ prati tādṛśopasthititvena kāraṇatā, lakṣaṇājanyopasthitiviśiṣṭaśābdabodhaṃ prati tathāvidhopasthititvena kāraṇateti kāryakāraṇabhāvadvayenaivopapattau na tadānantyaprasaktiriti na śaṅkyam, upasthitestattadvayaktitve- nopādānamantārā'vyavahitottaratvasya nirvaktumaśakyatvāt, tathāhi svadhvaṃsādhikaraṇakṣaṇadhvaṃsā- nadhikaraṇatve sati, svādhikaraṇakṣaṇadhvaṃsādhikaraṇatvamavyavahitottaratvam, idaṃ ca dvitīyatṛtīyakṣaṇa- sādharaṇaṃ, kṣaṇadvayasya-svadhvaṃsādhikaraṇī bhūto yaḥ svatṛtīyakṣaṇastad‌dhvaṃsānadhikaraṇatvāt, svādhi- karaṇabhūto yaḥ kṣaṇaḥ prathamakṣaṇastad‌dhvaṃsādhikaraṇatvācca | caturthādikṣaṇavyāvṛttaye satyantasya, svapūrvakṣaṇavyāvṛttaye viśeṣyadalasyopādānam | atra svapadaṃ na śaktijanyopasthititvādinā tathāvidhopasthitisāmānyaparaṃ, sarvasyāpi kṣaṇasya svapūrvakālotpannatathāvidhopasthitidhvaṃsādhikaraṇa- kṣaṇadhvaṃsādhikaraṇatvena tattadavacchinnabhinnatvābhāvāt, kiṃ tu tattadupasthitivyaktiparam, evaṃ copasthitīnāmānantyena kāryakāraṇabhāvānantyaṃ sphuṭameva | vyabhicāraḥ ekaikābhāve satyapareṇopasthitijananāt | gauravaṃ kāryakāraṇabāvadvayakalpane gauravam | pūrvoktasaṃbandhena śaktijñānaviśiṣṭopasthiti prati śaktijñānatvena kāraṇatā, evaṃ lakṣaṇājñānaviśiṣṭopasthiti prati lakṣaṇājñānatvena kāraṇatopagamyate vyabhicāravāraṇāyeti cedanantakāryakāraṇabhāvakalpanāprayuktamati- gauravamityarthaḥ | tatsūcayannāha prāgvaditi | padatadartheti | padaṃ ca, tadarthaśca tayorbhedenetyarthaḥ | kalpane gauravamiti | ghaṭapadadharmikaghaṭarūpārthabodhakatvaprakārakajñānatvena, kalaśādipadadharmikatādṛśa- jñānatvena ca ghaṭaviṣayakopasthititvāvacchinnaṃ prati kāraṇatetivad ghaṭādipadātpaṭādyupasthiteḥ paramate lakṣaṇājñānajanyatvenābhimatāyā nirvāhāya ghaṭādipadadharmikasya paṭādyarthabodhakatvaprakāraka- jñānasya paṭādyupasthiti prati kāraṇatābhyupeyā, lakṣaṇāṅgīkartrā tu-ghaṭādipadadharmikapaṭādyartha- bodhakatvaprakārakajñānasya paṭādyupasthiti prati kāraṇatvaṃ na kalpyaṃ, śaktisahakāreṇa ghaṭādi-
padātpaṭādibodhajananāditi bhāvaḥ | tavāpi vaiyākaraṇasyāpi | vyabhicāreti | ghaṭapadadharmikapaṭa- bodhakatvajñānaṃ vināpi paṭapadadharmikapaṭabodhakatvaprakārakajñānāt, paṭapaṭadharmikapaṭabodhakatvajñānaṃ vināpi ghaṭapadadharmikapaṭabodhakatvajñānācca paṭaśābdabodhajananādityarthaḥ | avyavahiteti kārya- tāvacchedakasyāvyavahitottaratvaghaṭitatve cetyarthaḥ | tathā ca svasāmānādhikaraṇyaviśiṣṭasvā- vyavahitottaratvasaṃbandhenaikaikajñānaviśiṣṭopasthititvāvacchinnaṃ prati tattajjñānatvena hetutvaṃ svīkāryam, evaṃ tattadupasthitikāryatāvacchedakaśarīre'pi uktaviśiṣṭasaṃbandhena tattadupasthitivaiśiṣṭyaṃ niveśya- manyathā vyabhicārāpatteriti bhāvaḥ | sutārāmiti | tathāvidhajñānāmānantyena kāryakāraṇabhāvānantyā- pattyātiśayitaṃ gauravamiti sūcanāya prakarṣapratyayaḥ |
śaktibhrameti | sarvaśabdānāṃ sarvārthavācakatvamanabhyupagacchatāpi śaktibhramasahakṛtaghaṭapadenāpi paṭabodhāṅgīkārāttannirvāhāya ghaṭapadadharmikapaṭaśaktijñānatvena paṭopasthiti prati hetutāvaśyaṃ svīkāryeti tattatpadaṃ tattadarthamantarbhāvya kāryakāraṇabhāvānantyāpattestulyatvādityarthaḥ | lakṣaṇā- kāryeti | lakṣaṇājñānakāryakāraṇabhāvetyarthaḥ | vṛttijanyopasthititvenaiveti | vṛttijñānajanyopa- sthititvenaivetyarthaḥ, 'eva'śabdena śaktijñānajanyopasthititvena, lakṣaṇājñānajanyopasthititvena ca hetutvasya ca vyavacchedaḥ | mayā vācyamiti | tathā manmate'pi ekaika eva kāyakāraṇabhāva iti na gauravamiti bhāvaḥ | śābdabodhahetviti | śābdabodhahetuścāsau padārthopasthitiśceti karmadhārayaḥ, tathovidhopasthityanukūlo yaḥ padapadārthayoḥ saṃbandhastattvasyetyarthaḥ | gaganarūpārthe śabdasya samavāyasaṃbandhe'tiprasaṅgavāraṇāyānukūlāntaṃ saṃbandhaviśeṣaṇamupāttam, uktasamavāyasyāpyākāśopa- sthityanukūlatvāduktadoṣastadavastha iti 'hetvanta'viśeṣaṇamupasthiterupāttam | gauravāditi | śābdabodhajanakatātvarūpaśaktitvāpekṣayā śaktilakṣaṇābhinnabhedātmaka śaktilakṣaṇānyataratvarūpavṛtti- tvasya, śābdabodhetyādirūpasya ca guruśarīratvādityarthaḥ | padārthopasthitivṛtteriti | padārthopasthiti- niṣṭhasya śābdabodhahetutāvacchedakasya vṛttijñānajanyopasthititvarūpasya | asaṃbhavāditi | śābda bodhānukūlatvaṃ śābdabodhakāraṇatvaṃ, tasya ca kāraṇatāvacchedakarūpā'paricaye durgrahatvena, uktahetutāvacchedakasya durjñeyatvena ca kāryakāraṇabhāvagrahasyāsaṃbhavāditi bhāvaḥ | mamāpīti | asya 'mate'iti śeṣaḥ | śaktijñānatvenaiveti | na tu śaktijñānatvena, lakṣaṇājñānatvena cetyarthaḥ | hetuteti | śābdabodhaṃ prati, tadanukūlāpadārthopasthiti prati ca hetutetyarthaḥ | tathā ca naiyāyikādinaye'pyekaika eva hetuhetumadbhāva iti bhāvaḥ | 'śakyādaśakyopasthitirlakṣaṇe'ti pakṣe lakṣaṇā na śaktighaṭitā, pravāhādirūpo yo vastuto gaṅgāpadādiśakyārthastaddhetukatīrādyupasthitereva lakṣaṇātvāt, nacoktajñānasyopasthiti pratyupayoga ityata āha śakyasaṃbandharūpeti | atra śakterviśeṣaṇavidhayaiva praveśo, na tūpalakṣaṇatayā, evaṃ ca gaṅgāpadaśakyapravāhasaṃbandhitīramityādyā- kāraka eva lakṣaṇāgrahe heturna tu pravāhasaṃbandhitīramityādyākāraka iti bhāvaḥ | samānaviṣayatvasya - tatpraveśasya, tadviṣayakopasthitau tanniṣṭhaviṣayatānirūpitaśaktiniṣṭhaviṣayatākajñānahetutva- syāvaśyakatvāditi paryavasito'rthaḥ | gaṅgāpadaśakyasaṃbandhi tīramiti lakṣaṇājñānasya, śakyasaṃbandhasaṃsargakasya gaṅagāpadāttīramityākārasya vā lakṣaṇājñānasya tīraviṣayakatve sati śaktiviṣayakatve'pi na tīrāṃśe śaktiviṣayakatvamiti na śaktijñānavidhayā tīropasthitinirvāhakatā- saṃbhavaḥ | atra 'śaktijñānapadārthopasthityo'rityupalakṣaṇaṃ, śaktijñānaśābdabodhayorityapi bodhyam |
samānaviṣayakatvāpraveśe bādhakamāha anyatheti | saṃbandhāgraheti | tad‌grahadaśāyāṃ tu 'pravoho gaṅgāpadaśakya' iti jñānāttīrabodha iṣṭa eveti tad‌grahānusaraṇam | nanu tadviṣayakopasthityādikaṃ prati tadviṣayakaśaktijñānatvena hetutvamupeyaṃ, na tūktarūpeṇeti na lakṣaṇājñānādinirvāhaḥ, ? tasyāpi tīraviṣayakatvāt, uktaśaktijñānadāpattiśca na, tasya tīrāviṣayakatvādityāśaṅkyāha api ceti | prathamasmapaṇasya, ghaṭamānayeti vākyaviṣayakatvānusaraṇaṃ, vakṣyamāṇasmaraṇasya ghaṭaviṣaya- katvasya padajanyatvasya ca saṃpattye | tasya hastipakaviṣayakatvasaṃpattaye hastiviṣayakatvamanusasāra | gajavākyobhayaviṣayakapratyakṣasyāsaṃbhavāt-smarata iti | vastutastu smaraṇasyaikatvamityatra na nirbharaḥ, kramikānekavarṇaghaṭitānekapadaghaṭitasya pratyakṣaṃ na saṃbhavatīti smaraṇamanusṛtam | ata eva hastino'pi tadevādṛtaṃ, kalpanālāghavāt | vṛttijanyatveti | śaktijñānajanyatvasattvādityarthaḥ | vṛttijanyatvotkīrtanaṃ tu vṛttijanyopasthititvena hetutvapakṣe'pi dūṣaṇamāviṣkartumiti bodhyam | eteneti | uktasthale hastipakasya śābdabodhāpattirūpadūṣaṇena |
nanu śaktijñānaprayojyatāvacchedakaviṣayatāsaṃbandhena tathāvidhopasthiterviṣayatāsaṃbandhena śābda- bodhe hetutvamabhyupeyate'to noktasamūhālambanasmaraṇāddhastipakaśābdabodhāpattiḥ, hastipakaniṣṭhaviṣaya- tāyā ghaṭapadaśaktijñānaprayojyatāvacchedakatvābhāvāt, ghaṭaniṣṭhaviṣayatāyāstu ghaṭapadaśaktijñāna- prayojyatāvacchedakatvādupapadyate ghaṭasya śābdabodhaviṣayatvaṃ, tīrādiniṣṭhaviṣayatāyā api gaṅgapadīyalakṣaṇātmakaśaktijñānaprayojyatāvacchedakatvasyākṣatatayā gaṅgāpadāttīropasthityā tīrasya śābdabodhaviṣayatvopapattiriti cenna, hastipakaniṣṭhaviṣayatāyā api hastipakapadadharmikaśaktijñāna- prayojyatāvacchedakatvenoktasthale tasya śābdabodhāpatterduvāratvāt |
nanu tattacchabdaviṣayakaśaktigrahaprayojyatāvacchedakatvaṃ viṣayatāyāṃ niveśyamato noktadoṣo'ta āha prayojyatvasyeti | anatīti | 'ghaṭapadaṃ ghaṭaśakta'miti śaktigrahavataḥ puṃso ghaṭāddhaṭopasthitau tena svasamavāyyupasthitākāśasya ghaṭapadajanyaśābdabodhaviṣayatvāpattiḥ, tatrāpi ghaṭapadaśaktigraha- prayojyatāvacchedakaviṣayatāsaṃbandhena tathāvidhopasthiteḥ sattvāditi bhāvaḥ |
na ca śaktitvāvacchinnasāṃsargikaviṣayatānirūpitapadaniṣṭhaprakāratānirūpitārthaniṣṭhaviśeṣyatā- kajñānatvena śābdabodhahetutvaṃ svīkriyate, śaktisaṃbandhena śakyasaṃbandhena vā padaprakārakārthaviśeṣyaka- jñāne śakteḥ saṃsargatvāt, śaktitvaniṣṭhāvacchedakatāparyāpteraniveśācchakyasaṃbandhena padaprakārakajñāna- syāpi saṃgrahaḥ, śaktisaṃbandhāvacchinnapratiyogitākapadābhāvaprakārakaniścayapratibadhyatvaṃ tu-śakya- saṃbandhasaṃsargakapadaprakārakajñānasya na, padaprakāratānirūpitaśaktiniṣṭhasāṃsargikaviṣayatāyā artha- niṣṭhaviśeṣyatānirūpitatvābhāvāt, tanniṣṭhaviśeṣyātānirūpitatatsaṃbandhaniṣṭhasāṃsargikaviṣayatānirū- pitatanniṣṭhaprakāratākabuddhi pratyeva saddharmikatatsaṃbandhāvacchinnapratiyogitākatadabhāvaniścayasya prati- bandhakatvāt, anyathā saṃyuktasamavāyasaṃbandhena ghaṭavattābuddhirapi saṃyogana ghaṭābhāvaniścaye sati na syāt | itthaṃ ca na lakṣaṇājñānasya śābdabodhaṃ prati pṛthak‌kāraṇatvakalpanāpattiḥ, na voktasamūhālambanasmaraṇāddhastipakasya śābdabodhāpattiḥ, na vākāśasyoktasthale śābdabodhāpattiśceti vācyaṃ, śaktisamānakālikapaṭapadasamavāyasaṃbandhena ghaṭapaṭaprakārakākāśādiviśeṣyakajñānādākāśādeḥ śābdabodhāpatterduvāratvāt, na ceṣṭāpattiḥ, ananubhavāt, paramparāsaṃbandhastu na lakṣaṇātvena vṛddhasaṃmataḥ, ata eva kāvyaprakāśakṛdbhiḥ 'bādho yogaḥ phalena no' ityuktam, anyathā śaityapāvanatvādāvapi tīrasya svasaṃyuktasamavetatvarūpaparamparasaṃbandhasya sattvena tadasaṃgatiḥ spaṣṭaiva | nanu śakyasaṃbandha- jñānādeva yatra tīrapasthitistatra sarvānubhavasiddhabodhanirvāhāya lakṣaṇājñānakāraṇatvakalpanaṃ śābdikasyāvaśyakamiti paramate'pi na prāktanagauravāvakāśa iti cenna, tatrāpi gaṅgāpadaṃ tīrabodhakamiti jñānāntarameva tīraśābdabodhāṅgīkārāt | na ca tādṛśajñānavyaktikalpanāgauravam, phalamukhatvena tasyādoṣatvāt | anyetu vastutastu suṣuptyapagamakṣaṇamārabhya punastadutpattikṣaṇaparyantaṃ pratyakṣātmaguṇo'vaśyaṃ vācyaḥ, ata eva suṣuptikālabhinnasya tathāvidhaguṇaśūnyakālasya mūrcchātvamucyate, tathā ca tādṛśakāle jñānādyāvaśyakatayā jñānādyantarasthāne idameva kalpyate iti kka gauravamiti vadantīti saṃkṣepaḥ, tadetadibhipretyāha vistareṇa prapañcitaṃ bhūṣaṇe iti || 37 ||
evaṃ - bīdhajanakasya śaktitve | 'īśvarecchā śakti'riti mate tu tattatpadapadārthasaṃbandhatvaviśe-
ṣitecchāyā eva śaktitvam | evaṃ ca gagarīśabdād‌bodhotpattyā gagarīśabdāddhaṭabodho jāyatāmitīcchāyā āvaśyakatve'pi tasyā na gagarīśabdasaṃbandhatvaṃ kalpyate, tadanugrāhakakośādya- bhāvānnāpabhraṃśe śaktiprasaṅgaḥ | evaṃ 'padārthāntaraṃ śakti'riti mate'pi na tatra sā kalpyata iti bhāvaḥ | dvidhā - prakāradvayena, apabhraṃśānāṃ sākṣād‌bodhakatvānabhyupagama ekaḥ prakāraḥ, vācakatve iṣṭāpattirityaparaḥ, tatrādyaṃ samādhānaṃ naiyāyikādimatena, dvitīyaṃ svamateneti vivekaḥ | anumāya - smṛtvā | ata evāgre'teṣāṃ sādhusmaraṇa evopayoga' iti vakṣyati | nanvevaṃ tasya vācakatmanupapannaṃ, sākṣādbodhakatvasyaiva vācakatvarūpatvādata āha vācako bodhaka iti |
sākṣāttadvācakatvamiti | sākṣāttadbodhakatvarūpaṃ tadvācakatvamityarthaḥ | tādātmyamupagamye- veti | arthatādātmyaṃ gagaryādiśabde gṛhītvevetyarthaḥ | grahasya bhramatvasūcanāya 'ive'ti | tena teṣāmavācakatvaṃ dhvanitam, tadvācaka eva hi tattādātmyābhyupagamāt | etena śaktibhramāttato bodha iti ca sūcitam, na ca pāmarāṇāṃ śaktyagrahe kathaṃ śaktibhrama iti vācyaṃ, gagarīśabdaṃ śrṛṇvataḥ puṃsaḥ sādhusmaraṇādbodhe'pi taṭasthasya gagarīśabdajanya evāsya bodha iti bhrameṇā''dyasya śaktibhramo'nyeṣāṃ tanmūlakasya tadbhramasya saṃbhavāt | na śiṣṭairiti | 'śiṣṭaiḥ-sādhavaḥ paryāyā ivāpabhraṃśā nānugamyanta' iti yojanā, paryāyasamudāye na saṃgṛhyanta ityarthaḥ | nayataḥ - artha bodhayataḥ sādhuśabdasya, smṛtimātreṇa asya 'arthabodhopapatti'riti śeṣaḥ | baṃbaṃbeti | śikṣyamāṇo bālo 'baṃ baṃ be'tyavyaktaṃ yadā prabhāṣate, tadvidāṃ - sādhuśabdaṃ jānatāṃ, vyakte - sādhusvaraṇe sati, niścayaḥ - arthaniścayo bhavatītyarthaḥ | asādhoḥ sādhuprakṛtikatvāt sādṛśyena tatsmaraṇamiti bhāvaḥ | atra 'baṃ baṃ be'ti pāṭha eva yukto, na tva'mbāmbe'ti , tasya sādhutvāt |
pūrvakārikāṃ vyācaṣṭe evaṃ sādhāviti | sādhuvyavahitaḥ - sādhusmaraṇapūrvakaḥ | 'vyavahārāde'rityādipadādaptavākyasaṃgrahaḥ | paryāyatulyatā-yathā sarve'pyanekaparyāyā ekasyārthasya vācakā aviśeṣāttathāpabhraṃśā apītyarthaḥ | dṛṣṭāntadārṣṭāntikayorvaiṣamyamāha teṣāmiti | paryāyāṇāmityarthaḥ | ekatvāditi | ata eva vinigamanāvirahaḥ, anyathā ya eva paryāyaḥ sarvadeśeṣu prasiddhastatraiva śaktiḥ sidhyediti bhāvaḥ | tathā - sarvadeśeṣu ekatā, teṣāṃ deśabhedena bhinnatvāt, ata eva 'vāco yatra vibhidyante taddeśāntaramiṣyate' iti smaryate | anyatheti | apabhraṃśānāmavācakatve cetyarthaḥ | teṣāṃ vācakatve tu tadvayāvartanāyānyavidhameva tadvaktavyamiti bhāvaḥ | na ca lākṣaṇikāsaṃgrahaḥ, teṣāmapi kkacicchaktatvāt, vṛttimattvaṃ vā tadartho bodhyaḥ | aviduṣāmiti | tattadarthabodhakatvena grahaśūnyānāmityarthaḥ, evaṃ ca tattacchabdatvādinā pūrvamanubhave'pi neṣṭasiddhiḥ, tathānubhavavatāṃ tathāvidhasmaraṇasaṃbhave'pi tasya śābdabodhānupayuktatvāt, tattadarthabodhakatvena smaraṇasyaivopayogitvāditi bhāvaḥ | etadabhiprāyeṇaivāha teṣāmiti | pāmarāṇāmityarthaḥ, apabhraṃśa- mātravyutpannāḥ pāmarāḥ | kiṃ bodhakatvarūpaśakterbhramo'bhita utecchārūpāyāḥ ? tatra nādya ityata āha bodhakatvasyeti | tattadarthabodhajanakatvasyetyarthaḥ | abādheneti | 'nedaṃ tadarthabodhaka'miti bādhaniścayābhāvenetyarthaḥ | bādhābhāvāditi | gagaryādiśabdādapi ghaṭādirūpārthabodhajananena 'ghaṭo gagarīśabdajanyabodhaviṣayo jāyatā'mitīśvarecchāvaśyakī, anyathā ghaṭaniṣṭhagagarīśabdajanyabodha- viṣayatvasyeśvarecchā'viṣayatayā tasyāḥ sarvaviṣayakatvānupapattiḥ | na ca tasya suddhaviṣayatātvenaiva ghaṭāṃśe gagarīśabdajanyabodhaviṣayatvaprakārakatvamicchāyā upagamyate, na tu tacchabdajanyabodha-
viṣayatātvena, tāvatāpi tasyāḥ sarvaviṣayakatvānupapattiriti vācyaṃ, ghaṭaniṣṭhaviśeṣyatānirūpita- prakāratāvacchedakaghaṭakatayā tadanavagāhitve'pi svātantryeṇa bodhāṃśe tadavagāhitvopagamāt | tatta- cchabdajanyabodhaviṣayatātvāvacchinnaprakāratānirūpitaviśeṣyatāsaṃbandhena saṃbaddheśvarecchā śaktiḥ, sā caivaṃ sati tatra na saṃbhavati, athavā padapadārthasaṃbandhatvaviśiṣṭā tathāvidheśvarecchā śaktiḥ gagarīśabdasaṃbandhatvavaiśiṣṭyaṃ tu tasyā nāṅgīkriyata iti na tatra śaktisaṃbhava iti vācyaṃ, tato'pi bodhotpādānubhavena tatrāpi gagarīśabdajanyabodhaviṣayatātvena rūpeṇa tatprakārakatvasyecchāyā avaśyābhyupeyatvāt, gagarīśabdaghaṭaitadubhayasaṃbandhatvavaiśiṣṭyasya vāvaśyaṃ svīkāryatvāditi tātparyam |
padārthapadaviśeṣaghaṭitāyā iti | tattatpadajanyabodhaviṣayatvaprakāratānirūpitārthaniṣṭha- viśeṣyatākecchāyā eva śaktitvena gagarīśabdajanyabodhaviṣayatvaprakārikā ghaṭaviśeṣyikecchaiva śaktiḥ, prakṛte vācyā, sā yadyasti, na tarhi tadbhramaḥ, yadi nāsti, tarhi sutarāṃ na tadbhramaḥ sidhyati, kkacitprasiddhasya svavirahiṇi jñānasyaiva bhramatvāt | atha tattacchabdaviśeṣyikā tattadarthabodhajanakatvaprakārikecchāpi śaktivinigamanāvirahāt, tathā ca gagarīśabde ghaṭabodhajanakatva- prakārikāyā icchāyā viṣayatayā ghaṭaśabde prasiddhāyā bhramaḥ saṃbhavatīti cenna, ghaṭabodhakatvaprakārikecchāmātrasya śaktitvābhāvena tadbhramasyānupayuktatvāt | viguṇeṣu - sādhuśabde vyutpattividhureṣu | abhidhātṛṣu - vaktṛṣu | vyavakīrṇā - anyathākṛtā, apabhraṃśīkṛteti yāvat | anityadirśināṃ tviti | 'kāryāḥ śabdā' iti mate śāstramātrasamadhigamyamarthabodhakatvamityapabhraṃśeṣu śāstreṣu tad‌grāhakasyābhāvātsādhava eva vācakā nāpabhraṃśā iti bhavati bhrama iti bhāvaḥ |
vācakatvasya puruṣabhedaniyantritatvābhāvā'tteṣāṃ sādhuravācaka' ityayuktamata āha avācako- 'bodhaka iti | nanu tatpadajanyabodhaviṣayatvaprakārikecchaiva śaktiḥ, evaṃ tadarthabodhajanakatva- prakārikecchā, ādyā śabdaghaṭitā na tvarthaghaṭitā, caramā tvarthaghaṭitā, na tu śabdaghaṭitā, tathā ca caramāyā vilakṣaṇaviṣayatāsaṃbandhena gagaryādiśabde bhramaḥ saṃbhavatyevetyanuśayana āha-kiṃ ceti | sarvatraikatveneti | mahārāṣṭrasaṃcārayogyeṣvekarūpatvenetyarthaḥ, tena yāvanādadeśeṣu tadasattve'pi na kṣatiḥ | saṃskṛtaśabdasyāpi tatrāsattvācca | paryāyeṣviveti | nānāparyāyaniṣṭhā nānānupūrvyo yathā śaktitāvacchedikā aṅgīkriyante, tathāpabhraṃśaniṣṭhā api tāḥ śaktatāvacchedakatvena svīkriyantāma- viśeṣādityarthaḥ | sādhūnāmeva | na tu vācakamātrasyetyarthaḥ | ata eva "samānāyāmarthāvagatau śabdaiścāpaśabdaiśca śāstreṇa dharmaniyamaḥ" iti paspaśāhnike bhāṣye uktaṃ, tatra samānatvaṃ-śabdāntara- smaraṇāpūrvakatvena, anyathā'vyāvartakatadupādānasya nairarthakyāpatteḥ | kiṃ ca yādṛśaṃ sādhuśabdānāṃ hetutvaṃ tādṛśamevāpaśabdānāmapi svarasataḥ pratīyate, na tu vyavahitaṃ, tadupamṛdyetāsādhoravācakatvābhyu- pagame | na caivamāryamlecchādhikaraṇavirodhaḥ-tatra hi pīluśabdaṃ vṛkṣaviśeṣe āryāḥ prayuñjate, tameva budhyante, anāryarūpamlecchā gaje prayuñjate, tameva pratiyanti, evaṃ yavaśabdo dīrghaśūke, tadbhinnastu kaṅgau, tatrāryaprasiddhervalavattvāt pīluśabdo vṛkṣe eva śakto, na tu gaje, yavaśabdo dīrghaśūka eva na tu kaṅgāviti siddhāntitaṃ-tadvirudhyata iti vācyam, āryaprasiddhervalavattvādvede yavapadasya dīrghaśūkaparatvameva na tu śaktisattve'pi priyaṅguparatvamiti siddhāntāśayasya varṇanīyatvāt | 'śavatirgatikarmā kambojeṣveva bhāṣyate, vikāra eva āryā bhāṣyante' ityādibhāṣyānniruktācca yathā śavatyādīnāṃ niyataviṣayatvaṃ tathā vede tatparatvameva yavaśabdasyeti kalpanāyā ucitatvāt, śaktiḥ
punarubhayatra jāgartyeva, anārye prasiddhe'rthe aśaktatve'sādhutve vā tātparye mānābhāvāt | jaiminirapi sūtrayāvabhūva 'teṣvadarśanādvirodhasya samā vipratipratiḥ syā'diti | yavaśabde ubhayathā śabdārthāvagamādvikalpaḥ syāditi tadarthaḥ | evaṃ pūrvapakṣayitvā siddhāntasūtramāha-'śāstrasthānāṃ tannimittatvāt' iti | śāstrasthānāṃ - śāstraniṣṇātānāṃ śiṣṭānāṃ,śabdārthe, vede iti śeṣaḥ, teṣāṃ smṛtiravicchinnā smṛtiḥ, śabdeṣu vedeṣu ca te śiṣṭā hi śrutismṛtidhāraṇe nimittamityarthaḥ | apabhraṃśānāṃ vācakatvādeva vācakasya vyañjakatve 'māedharova araṇa'mityādi bhāṣodāharaṇaṃ kāvyaprakāśakṛtāmāñjasyena saṃgacchate ityalaṃ vistareṇa | puṇyajananeta | taduktamekaḥ pūrvaparayoriti sūtre bhāṣye-' ekaḥ śabdaḥ samyag jñātaḥ suprayuktaḥ śāstrānvitaḥ svarge loke kāmadhugbhavatī'ti | iyaṃ śrutiriti prāñcaḥ | smṛtirityanye | pāpajananeti | pāpajanako'sādhuśabdaprayoga iti bodhanāya'na mlecchitavā' iti niṣedhaḥ, viṣedhena niṣedhye'niṣṭabodhanāditi bhāvaḥ, ayañca niṣedho yājñe karmaṇyeveti paspaśāhnike bhāṣyādau spaṣṭaṃ, nirūpitaṃ caitatprāgākhyātārthavicāre | puṇyajananeti | tattvarūpayogyatvarūpaṃ puṇyajanakatvamityarthaḥ, evamagre'pi | kośādīti | ādipadamāptavākyādi- saṃgrahāya | vyakaraṇādītyādipadamupamānasaṃgrahāya, kośādeḥ sākṣātprātipadikaśaktigrāhakatayā prathamatastadupādānaṃ, prāyaḥ kośādita eva śaktigrahācca | brāhmaṇe phaleti | 'rājā rājasūyena yajete'tyatra rājapadasya rūḍhyā kṣatriyārthakatayā brāhmaṇasya tadanadhikāritvānttena kṛte tatra phalājanakatvamityarthaḥ | nāśvādau sādhutvamiti | arthaviśeṣanibandhanatvātsādhutvasyeti | paryavasitamiti | apabhraṃśāstu na tadarthe vyutpannā iti na sādhava iti bhāvaḥ | gauṇānāmiti | 'siho māṇavaka' ityādau siṃhādiśabdānāṃ krauryādiguṇayogāt puruṣe āgatānāṃ - pravṛttānāmityarthaḥ, 'tatra āgataḥ' ityaṇ | guṇe - gotvādidharme | tatpuraskāreṇa - tattaddharmapuraskāreṇa, śakyatāvacchedakadharmāropeṇeti yāvat | ārope nimittaṃ tu krauryādiguṇayogaḥ, 'siho māṇavaka' ityādau siṃhatvādirūpeṇa māṇavakāderbhānamiti bhāvaḥ, 'gaṅgāyāṃ ghoṣa' ityādāvapyevam | spaṣṭaṃ cedaṃ kāvyapradīpodyote | 'krūro māṇavaka' ityākārakabodhabhyupagantṛnaye tu idaṃ na saṃgacchate, siṃhādiśabdānāṃ krauryādyavācakatvāt | guṇavācakaśuklādiśabdaparatvena prakṛtagranthayojane tu 'gaṅgāyāṃ ghoṣaḥ' 'siṃho māṇavaka' ityādyasaṃgrahāpattiḥ | ādhuniketi | śakyatāvacchedakātirikta- rūpeṇa bodhakatātparyakāṇāṃ lākṣaṇikānāṃ tvityarthaḥ | lakṣaṇayāpyasādhuriti | ata eva karmādau ṣaṣṭhyāderanuśāsanaṃ caritārthanityanyatra vistaraḥ, apabhraṃśānāṃ vācakatve'pi na prātipadikasaṃjñā, sādhutvasyāpyuddeśyatāvacchedakatvāt, ata eva 'siddhe śabdārthasaṃbandhe lokato'rthaprayukte śabdaprayoge śāstreṇa dharmaniyamaḥ' iti vārtikakṛtoktam, 'sādhvanuśāsane'smiñśāstre' iti ca bhāṣye uktam | evaṃ sādhutvajñānasya śābdabodhe hetutvamasādhutvaniścayasya pratibandhakatvaṃ veti kathaṃ tebhyo sādhutvagrahavatāṃ bodha iti na śaṅkyaṃ, sādhutvajñānavirahiṇāmasādhutvaniścayavatāmapi śābdānubhavāt, sādhutvajñānasya hetutvādasādhutvaniścayasyāpratibandhakatvācca, tadetadabhipretyāha-vistareṇeti || 38 ||
atirikteti | icchāviśeṣātiriktetyarthaḥ, sā punarbodhajanakatārūpā, padārthāntararūpā vā
bhavatu | saṃbandho viṣaya iti | tajjanyapratipattiviṣayaḥ | tathā ca 'saṃbandhaśabde saṃbandha' ityasya saṃbandhaśabdavācyaḥ saṃbandha ityarthaḥ, evaṃ yogyatāṃ pratītyāderartho bodhyaḥ, 'saṃbandha' śabdasya 'yogyatā'śabdasya cārthapradarśanasya prakṛtopayogamāha-ghaṭapadamityādinā | atra yogyaṃ - ghaṭarūpārthe yogyaṃ, tadviṣayakabodhajananayogyamiti yāvat | etatsaṃbandhīti | ghaṭasaṃbandhītyarthaḥ sā - śaktiḥ | tathā ca prāthamikaśaktigraha uktavyavahārādbhavati, tatra ca saṃbandhādiśabdaśaktigraho'pekṣito- 'tastadarthapradarśanaṃ kṛtaṃ, tatra dṛṣṭānto-māteti | 'yogyava'diti pāṭhe mātrādiyogyaḥ - tatpratiyogikatvādaviśiṣṭaḥ, saṃbandho, yathā lokavyavahād gṛhyate tathetyarthaḥ, 'yogava'diti pāṭhastu samyageva | pare tu -saṃbandhaśabde [sambandho] yogyatāṃ prati bodhajanakatāvacchedakadharmarūpayogyatā- nirūpito yastādātmyalakṣaṇaḥ saṃbandhaḥ sa eva yogyatā, sā ca svābhāvikyevānādavṛddhavyavahāra- paramparāparaparyāyo yo'smadādisamayastena gṛhitenokteśvarasamayānniścīyate, yathā mātāputrayorjanya- janakabhāvalakṣaṇasaṃbandho vyavahārānniścīye tathetyarthaḥ, na tu janakatvameva yogyatā, 'etajjanane'yaṃ yogya' iti vyavahārādityāhuḥ | atra kalpe-'saṃbandhaśabda' ityasya-saṃbandhyata iti saṃbandhaḥ, karmaṇi ghañū tattadarthe saṃbaddho ghaṭādiśabda ityartho bodhyaḥ, saṃbandhaśabdo yogyatāṃ prati saṃbaddho yogyateti kārikāyāṃ yojanā bodhyā | athavā 'idaṃ padametadarthasaṃbandhī'tyatratyasaṃbandhaśabda ityarthaḥ, itthaṃ ca saṃbandhaśabdo-bhāvaghañanta eva, saṃbandho yogyatetyasya yogyatāviṣaya ityartha iti dik ||
iti śaktinirṇayaḥ || 6 || 
<7>
'āropitatvaṃ nañdyotya'mityanena nañartha eva pradarśyate na tu samāsārtha iti tātparyeṇāha nañarthamāheti | prasaṅgasaṃgatiratra bodhyā | aparaśabdārthamāha-uttarapadārthasyeti | ata eva - uttarapadārthaprādhānyānurodhādeva | āropitatvamiti | āropitabrāhmaṇatvādi brāhmaṇādipadapravṛtti- nimittamityabrāhmaṇādipadaghaṭakanañā dyotyata ityarthaḥ | anyathā - uttarapadārthaprādhānyavirahe | atisarva ityatreveti | sarvamatikrānta ityarthe 'atyādayaḥ krāntādyarthe dvitīyaye'ti samāse pūrvapadārthapradhāne sarvaśabdasyopasarjanatayā sarvāditvābhāvena yathā na sarvanāmatvaṃ, tathātrāpi naño bhedārthakatve sarvabhinna ityarthe'sarva ityāderna syāt, tathā ca-'asarve' 'asarvasmai' ityādiprayogānu- papattiḥ | nanvanyonyābhāvārthakatvapakṣe tasya pratiyogitāsaṃbandhena pratiyogini viśeṣaṇatve uttara- padārthaprādhānyaṃ nirvahedityāśaṅkyāha-ghaṭo nāstītyādāviti | tasya - abhāvasya | viśeṣyatāyā eveti | 'ghaṭābhāvo'stitvāśraya' ityākārakasya, 'ghaṭakartṛkasattābhāva' ityākārakasya vā bodhasyānubhavasiddhatvādityarthaḥ | nanvevamaghaṭa ityādau ghaṭabhinna iti bodhasya sārvajanīnasyāpalāpo na yogya ddatyāśaṅkyāha-asmadrītyā ceti | ghaṭabhinne eva dhaṭatvasyāropattadbhinna iti bodho'rthasiddho bhavatīti na tadapalāpaḥ, tatra śābdatvapratītistu śabdaprayojyatvādbhramarūpaiveti bhāvaḥ |
āropaviṣayatvamiti | tasya cottarapadārthe brāhmaṇādāvanvayaḥ, āropaśca pratyāsattyā pravṛtti- nimittaprakāraka eva pratīyate, evaṃ cā'brāhmaṇa' ityata āropaviṣayatvavān brāhmaṇa iti bodhaḥ, tathāvidhaśca kṣatriyādirevābrāhmaṇaśabdena pratipādyate nañsamabhivyāhāre, samavāyasaṃbandhasaṃsargakāropa- saṃbandhena brāhmaṇatvādike pratīyate ityāropaviṣayo brāhmaṇa ityākārakatā bodhasya nānupapannā, āropastu kasyacit tadānīmapi saṃbhavatyeveti nāsaṃgatiḥ | spaṣṭaṃ cedamatraiva sūtrabhāṣye saṃdehā-
ddurupadeśādvā' ityādigranthe | athavā'ropaviṣayatva'mityasyāropaviṣayapravṛttinimittakatvamityarthaḥ, vyavahitakāle āropasattve'pi na kṣatiḥ, āropitatātkālitvasyāniveśāt | āropaviṣayatvasya prakāratayā sasargatayā ca bhānam rājñaḥ puruṣa ityatra ṣaṣṭhyarthasvatvāderiva bhānamaviruddham, abrāhmaṇa ityādita āropitabrāhmaṇatvavānita bodhaḥ, āropitatvaviśiṣṭabrāhmaṇyavattvaṃ brāhmaṇabhinne eveti na brāhmaṇe'brāhmaṇaśabdaprayogaḥ | laghubhūtamarthamāha āropamātramartha iti | naño dyotyo vācyo vārthaḥ, sa cottaparadārthe viṣayatvasaṃbandhena viśeṣaṇamityāha viṣayatvaṃ tu saṃsarga iti | tathā caitatpakṣe niṣkarṣa iti pūrvapakṣāpekṣayātra pakṣe lāghavādityāśayaḥ | bhāṣyasaṃmatastu 'athave'tyādyuktaḥ pūrva eva kalpaḥ, yuktaścādyo'ntyastu na, āropasya nañvācyatve'pi brāhmaṇādipadānāṃ sukhyārthatyāgaḥ śabādārthasaṃbandhasyānityatāpatteḥ, madhyamakalpe tu na doṣaḥ, mukhyasaṃbandhena brāhmaṇatvavata ivāropitasaṃbandhena tadvato'pi tadarthatvāt, nañ tvantyārthe tātparyanirṇāyakaḥ, idameva naño dyotakatvaṃ nāma, etāvataivātraikārthībhāvavyavahāro'pi, arthavattvamapi samāsasyopapadyate dyotyārthāntarbhaveṇa viśiṣṭānupūrvyāḥ śaktatāvacchedakatvadityāhurityanyatra vistaraḥ | anye tvabrāhmaṇa ityādau brāhmaṇādipadaṃ tādātmyasaṃbandhena brāhmaṇādyāropaviṣayārthakaṃ ca, nañ tātparyanirṇāyakhaḥ, evaṃ cā'brāhmaṇo'yamityatra brāhmaṇāropaviṣayo'yamiti bodhaḥ, āropito brāhmaṇa ityanenāpyukta eva bodha ityucyate ityāhuḥ | atrāropo brāhmaṇābhinnamātraviśeṣyako grāhyaḥ, tene'mau brāhmaṇā'viti samūhālambanāropaviṣayatvasya brāhmaṇe sattve'pi na brāhmaṇapadaprayogaḥ | nanu naña uktārthavācakatvasvīkāre'pi na kṣatiḥ, āropaviṣayatvarūpasyāropitatvarūpasyāropitatvasyāropasya vā viṣayatāsaṃsargeṇottarapadārthe viśeṣaṇatvasaṃbhavenābhimatottarapadārthaprādhānyasiddherata āha dyotya- tvoktiriti | nipātamātrasya dyotakatvamati siddhāntānurodhāt tathoktirna tu prakṛte vācakatva- svīkāre kācit kṣatiriti bhāvaḥ | vastuta uktārthavācakatvapakṣe doṣa ukta eveti prāguktārtha- dyotakatvameva yuktamiti vadanti | 'āropitabrāhmaṇatvavā'miti śābdabodhottaraṃ jāyamānabrāhmaṇabhinnaḥ kṣatriya iti bodhāpattiḥ, evaṃ 'ghaṭo bhūtale nāstī'ti vākyādāropitā ghaṭakartṛkā bhūtalādhikaraṇikā satteti śābdabodhottaraṃ tādṛk sattābhāvabodho mānaso bhavati, tena ghaṭakartṛkabhūtalādhikaraṇaka- sattābodhaḥ pratibadhyate, abhāvājñānasya pūrvavartina eva pratiyogimato buddhipratibandhakatvamiti na niyamo'pi tu tatkāryasahabhāvenāpīti nābhāvabud‌dhyutpattisamaye'pi pratiyogibuddhayutpattiḥ 'vāyau rūpaṃ nāsti' 'śaṅakhaḥ pīto nāstī'tyādāvapi vāyvadhikaraṇikā rūpakartṛkā sattā āropitā, pītaśaṅkhakartṛkāropiteti bodhaḥ, viśiṣṭaprasiddhistvāhāryaiva, rūpakartṛkasattāyāṃ vāyvadhikaraṇakatvā- ropaḥ, pītakartṛkasattāyāṃ śaṅkhakartṛkatvāropaśca paryavasyati, āropitā tathāvidhasattetyevaṃ bodhavarṇanaṃ tu kriyāyāḥ prādhānyatayaiva nañarthānvaya ityabhipretyeti bodhyam || 40 ||
tadāśayāditi | tasmin - abhāvasya nañarthatve, āśayāt - tātparyāt | sādhāraṇyeneti | nañvācyasyāpyabhāvasya tattadanubhāvanurodhena viśeṣaṇatvaṃ viśeṣyatvaṃ vā kalpyate | dyotyārthasyāpi vā tata eva viśeṣyatvaṃ ca, athavā vācakatvapakṣe viśeṣyatvaṃ dyotyatvapakṣe viśeṣaṇatvam, ata eva dyotyārthānāṃ viśeṣaṇatvameveti 'hetumati ca'ti sūtrabhāṣyasiddhaniyamabhaṅge neti bodhyam |
nivṛttaśabdārthamāha abāvārthaka itīti | adhyāropiteti . adhyāropitaṃ brāhmaṇyaṃ yeṣu ta ityarthaḥ | nañvācyatvāditi | lāghavāllokaprasiddhayā ca nañobhāva eva vācyaḥ, 'abrāhmaṇo devadatta'ityādau devadattādinānubhūyamānasāmānādhikaraṇyopapattistvāśraye lakṣaṇayābodhyeti bhāvaḥ | anyathā - anubhavasyānyathopapattāvapyadhyāropitabrāhmaṇyakasya nañvācyatve |
sāddaśyāderapīti | sādṛśyāderapyabrāhmaṇa ityādau pratīyamānatvādvācyatāpatterityarthaḥ | apāpamiti | atra pāpātyantābhāva iti bodhaḥ | 'anaśva'ityato'śvānya iti bodhaḥ | 'abrāhmaṇa' ityatra sādṛśyaghaṭakatayānyatvabodho na kevalānyatvabodha iti viśeṣaḥ, sādṛśyaṃ padārthāntaramiti'mate bhedasya tatrābhāvācca | 'anudarā kanye'tyatrālpodarā kanyeti bodhaḥ | 'apaśavo vā'ityatrāpraśastāḥ paśava iti bodhaḥ | 'adharma'ityatra dharmavirodhī adṛṣṭaviśeṣaḥ pratīyate, na tu tadbhinnamātram | ārthikārthamiti | āropito brāhmaṇa ityādiśābdabodhottaraṃ sādṛśyādermānasabodho bhavati, anudaretyādovalpatvādihetukodarābhāvāropeṇa nañsācivyādāropitodarabuddhayā tanmūlālpatvādau viśrāntiḥ, evamanyatrāpi bodhyam | nañarthābhāvasya pratiyogyatirikte viśeṣaṇatve sāmarthyādabrāhmaṇa ityādau samāsānāpattiḥ, brāhmaṇāderanyonyābhāvapratyayānāpattiśceti śaṅkāmapaninīṣurāhapratīti | brāhmaṇatvādyavacchinnapratiyogitāsaṃbandhena bhedasya viśeṣaṇatvaṃ, tasya prakāratvānubhave tu tadantarbhāveṇa vṛttiraṅgīkāryā, bhedapratiyogitvamātrasya kevalānvaritve'pi na tathāvidhabhedapratiyogitvasya tathātvaṃ, tathā cābrāhmaṇa ityatra bhedapratiyogī brāhmaṇa iti śābdabodhe brāhmaṇapratiyogikabhedabodha ārthikaḥ, 'aghaṭaḥ paṭa' ityatra paṭavṛttibhedapratiyogī ghaṭa iti bodhaḥ, naño nipātatvāttadarthe paṭasya nāmārthasyādheyatvasaṃbandhenānvayo na virudhyate, 'aghaṭamānaye'tyukte ghaṭasyānayanaṃ tu na bhavati, nañupādānasāmarthyāt, śābdabodhottarakāle pratīyamānasya ghaṭapratiyogikabhedavata eva tatsaṃbhavāditi bhāvaḥ, 'abrāhmaṇo devadatta' ityādau 'kathaṃ nāma' ita evāsvarasādvā viśeṣyo veti pakṣāntaraparigraha iti bodhyam | uttarapadārthapradhānasya prayojanamāha anekamiti | idamevottarapadārthapradhāna iti pakṣasya mukhyaṃ prayojanam | tathā ca bhāṣyam 'idaṃ khalvapi bhūya uttarapadārthaprādhānyesaṃgṛhītam, kim ? anekamiti, kimatra saṃgṛhītam ? ekavacanam |' iti | ekavacananiyama iti | anyethaikabhinnasyānekatvād dvivacanabahuvacane eva syātāṃ, na tvekavacanamita bhāvaḥ, 'dvayekayo'riti sūtraṃ niyamārthamityabhipretya 'niyama' ityuktam | nanu padānniyamenaikavacanamityartho na boddhavyaḥ, tathā sati pakṣāntare tadaniyamaḥ pratīyeta, tacca na yuktaṃ, pūrvapadārthaprādhānye'nyapadārthaprādhānye caikavacanāsiddheḥ | tatpuruṣatvamiti | sati saṃbhave prāyikasyāpyuttarapadārthaprādhānyasya tatpuruṣe aucityamiti bhāvaḥ | pūrvapadārthaprādhānyasvīkāre puruṣādivyavasthā nopapadyate ityāha-anyatheti | itivaditi | 'tvadabhāvo' 'madabhāvo'stī'tyatra yathā yuṣmadasmatsānādhikaraṇyābhāvānna madhyamottamapuruṣau bhavato'pi tu prathamastadekavacanameva tadvadatvaṃ bhavasi, anahaṃ bhavāmītyatra yuṣmadādyarthasya nañarthānyonyābhāve viśeṣaṇatvābhyupagame syāt, evamayuvāṃ bavathaḥ, ayūyaṃ bhavatheti dvivacanādikamapa na syāt, karturabāvasyaikatvādityarthaḥ | svamate puruṣādivyavasthāmupapādayati-asmanmate ceti | castvarthe | sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ - yuṣmadādisāmānādhikaraṇyam | yuṣmadādyarthagatasaṃkhyābhidhāyitvameva sāmānādhikaraṇyam, tacca pūrvapadārthaprādhānyapakṣe'pi saṃbhavatyeveti tu na yuktam | sāmānādhikaraṇyapadasyoktārthabodhane- 'sāmarthyāt, evamapi vacanavyavasthāyā anupapādanācca . pratiyoginītīti | tathā cāvrāhyaṇaḥ kṣatriya ityato brāhmaṇabhinnaḥ kṣatriya iti bodhaḥ | nanvemasa ityādyasiddhirityāśaṅkyāha ayaṃ bhāva iti | gauṇatve'pi - upasarjanatve'pi | nanūpasarjanatve'pyaneṣo dadāti asaḥ śiva' ityatraitatta- cchabdasattvena lopo mā prasāṅkṣīditi 'anañ‌samāse' iti caritārtham, na caitattadarthagatasaṃkhyā- bhidhāyinaḥ sostena lopo vidhīyate, pūrvapadārthaprādhānye ca na tathābhidhaḥ suḥ saṃbhavatīti tadvaiyarthyamiti vācyaṃ, ṣaṣṭhyāstādṛśasaṃbandhārthakatve mānābhāvāt, anantarapadādhyāhāraṃ kṛtvā etattadoranantarasya sorlopa ityarthasyaivocitatvāt, anyatra tathā vyākhyātatvāccetyata āha akoriti | akasahitaparyudāsenākajyogyayoḥ sarvanābhroreva tayorgrahaṇamucitaṃ, saṃsargavadviprayogasyāpyaśaṅkhacakro harirityādau viśeṣāvagatihetutvasya klṛptatvāditi bhāvaḥ | tathā ca - uktarītyā sarvanāmnoreva tayorgrahaṇe ca, idamupalakṣaṇam-etattacchabdamātragrahaṇe'pi atitadityādābupasarjane atvābhāvena hal‌ṅayādilopasya durvāratayā tadvaiyarthyamityapi bodhyam | jñāpakamiti | pūrvapadārthaprādhānye'pyuttara- padārthaprādhānyaprayuktakāryapravṛttau jñāpakamityarthaḥ, tena puruṣavacanavyasthāpi sidhyatīti bhāvaḥ | viśeṣyānurodhāditi | bhinnasaṃkhyaviśeṣyāsaṃnidhāne eva samasyamānapradhānapadārthagatasaṃkhyābhidhāyakatvaṃ supra iti-tathāvidhaviśeṣyasaṃnidhāne vānekapadasya dvivacanabahuvacanāntateti bhāvaḥ | nanvevama'varṣā hemanta' ityatrāpi hemantarūpaviśeṣyānurodhādekavacanāntatvaprasaṅga iti cenna, 'guṇavacanānāmāśrayato liṅgavacanānī'ti bhāṣye eva samādhānāt, tatra viśeṣyarūpāśrayasyaiva pratiyogitayāśrayasya pratiyogino'pi grahaṇāt, evaṃ ca nañsamāsasya kkacidanuyogigate liṅgasaṃkhye kkacitpratiyogigate bhavata iti svīkriyata iti na doṣa iti tātparyāt | nanu 'pratyayaḥ' ityasmin 'tavyattavyānīyaraḥ' ityādāvanuvartamāne vacanavipariṇāmavad 'dhātoḥ' ityasmin 'nandigrahipacādibhyaḥ' ityādāvanuvṛtte vacanavipariṇāmavacca subutu śabde'pi so'stvityāśaṅkyāha-kiṃceti | bahūnāmiti | bahūnāṃ bahuvrīhirna sidhyediti yojanā | autsargikaṃ veti | ekatvadvitvādyavivakṣā 'ekavacanaṃ ce'ti vibhaktasūtreṇaikavacanamiti bhāvaḥ | uttarapadārtheti | tathā ca 'sevyate'nekayā' iti prayogānurodhāduttarapadārthaprādhānyaṃ nāstheyamekavacanāntatvasyānyathāsiddhatvāditi bhāvaḥ | ata eva - pūrvapadārthaprādhānyādeva | sūpapādamiti | sukhenopapādayituṃ śakyamityarthe ṇyantāt padeḥ karmaṇi khal, uttarapadārthaprādhānye tvatrānekaśabdasya bahuvacanāntatvānupapattiḥ, nanvaneko ratho'śvo gaja ityarthādekaśeṣeṇa tadupapattiriti śakyamekaśovānekaśabdasya sakalārthapratyāyakatvasaṃbhavenāne- kānekaśabdāprāptyaikaśeṣāvṛtteḥ pṛthagvibhaktyantarathādiśabde prayoge ekasyaivānekapadasyāvṛttyānvaya- saṃbhenaikaśeṣānupayogācca, teṣāṃ dvandvanirdeśe'pi samudite ekasyaivānvayasaṃbhavācceti bhāvaḥ | nanu 'etattado'riti sūtre'nañgrahaṇaṃ pūrvapadārthaprādhānye'pi sarvanāmasaṃjñāyā jñāpakamuktamevaṃ cāsa ityādeḥ siddhāvapi 'atvaṃ bhavasī'tyādau puruṣavyavasthānupapannaivetyāśaṅkāṃ pariharati-atvaṃ bhavasītyā- dāviti | tathā ca - naño dyotakatve ca | bhinnena yuṣmadarthena - tvadbhinnarūpayuṣmacchabdārthena, pūrvapadārthāprādhānyepyuttarapadārthāprādhānyaprayuktakāryajñāpakatā nañgrahaṇasyeti lakṣaṇaṃ vināpi
yuṣmacchabdasya puruṣavyavasthā sidhyati, ata eva dyotyārthasya viśeṣaṇatvameveti niyamasyāpi na bhaṅga iti pare | bhāvanābhāva iti | asyeti | asya-'iti rītyā bodha' ityatrānvayaḥ | nanu sakalakārakaviśiṣṭakriyāyā abhāvabodhopagamena tasya bhūtalaṃ ghaṭavaditi jñāne pratibadhyatvaṃ prativandhakatvaṃ ca na syāt, evamāropitā tathāvidhasatteti bodhāṅgākāre'pi ādyasya ghaṭābhāvā- viṣayakatvādantyasyābhāvamātrāviṣayakatvāditi ced bhūtalavṛttitvābhāvavān ghaṭa iti bodhasvīkāra- paśre'pyetaddoṣasattvāt, yadi vānubhavabalāttasya bodhasyoktajñānapratibadhyatvapratibandhakatve aṅgīkriyete tarhi tayāpyuktabodhayoste aṅgīkriyete | yadi bhūtalavṛttitvābhāvabodhottaraṃ bhūtalādhikaraṇakaghaṭā- bhāvabodho mānaso'bhyupagamyate tenoktajñānapratibandhādinirvāha ityucyeta tarhi sa eva panthā avalambyate ityalamativistareṇa | kriyāyāmeveti | kriyāgrahaṇaṃ guṇasyāpyupalakṣaṇam, ata eva nañsūtre bhāṣye 'prasajyakriyāguṇau tataḥ paścānnivṛttiṃ karotiṃ' itayuktam | prasajya - vidhāya, tasya pratiṣedha iti tadarthaḥ | tena guṇābhāvabodhe'pi prasajyapratiṣedha iti labhyate, guṇodāharaṇaṃ tu 'na naḥ-ekaṃ priya'miti | na iti ṣaṣṭhābahuvacanāntam | atra hyekapriyapratiṣedhena bahupriyapratītirbhavati | 'na saṃdeha' ityādau ca saṃdehātyantā bhāvaḥ pratīyate, saṃdehasya guṇatvāt | pūrvodāharaṇe ekatvaṃ saṃkhyārūpo guṇastatpratiṣedhaḥ, no'smākamekaṃ priyaṃ neti tadarthāt, evaṃ caikatvasaṃkhyāpratiṣedhaḥ paryavasyati | taduktaṃ kaiyaṭena 'atraikapratiṣadhasāmarthyādeva saṃkhyāntarayuktavastupratītiḥ' iti | na cāstītyadhyāhārādekaviṣayakartṛkasattā niṣidhyate iti kriyodāharaṇamevaitaditi vācyam, uktabhāṣya- prāmāṇyād‌guṇasyaiva tatra niṣedhānubhava ityāśayāt, etanmate 'kriyayā saha yatra nañ' iti prasajya- pratiṣedhalakṣaṇe'pi kriyāgrahaṇaṃ guṇopalakṣaṇamiti bodhyam | bhāṣyānusāriṇastvanañ‌samāgrahaṇamuttara- padārthaprādhānye eva lāghavājjñāpakaṃ nivṛttapadārthaka iti bhāṣyasthakaiyaṭoktaṃ vyākhyānameva samyak, yatra uttarapadārthaprādhānyapakṣe nañā ceduttarapadārthābhāvo bodhyate tadottarapadārthaprādhānyabhaṅga eva syāditi nañastatrānarthakatvamupeyamabhāvapratiyogitvādikaṃ tvavyāvartakatayā na viśeṣaṇaṃ saṃbhavatītyāśayena abrāhmaṇamānayetyukte brāhmaṇamātrasyānayanaṃ na prāptotīti śaṅkāyā apanayanārtha 'naiṣa doṣaḥ' ityādinā yathā 'rājapuruṣamānaye'tyatra rājaviśiṣṭa eva puruṣaḥ pratīyate, tathehāpi nañarthaviśiṣṭa eva vrāhyaṇo na kevala ityarthakaṃ 'nañviśiṣṭasyānayanaṃ bhaviṣyati' ityuktam, tataḥ 'kaḥ punarasau nivṛttapadārthakaḥ iti bhāṣyamupādāya 'nivṛttaḥ padārtho brāhmaṇyaṃ yasmin' ityasyābhidhānāt, tadagrimabhāṣyagranthenāpyaropitabrāhmaṇyavān kṣatriyādireva abrāhmaṇa ityatra brāhmaṇapadārtho nañdyotya ityasyaiva lābhācca, pūrvapadārthaprādhānyapakṣe tu tābhyāṃ bhinno'stītyarthe'tau sta iti dvivacanasyā- nāpattiḥ, tebhyo bhinna ityarthe te santītyatra bahuvacanāntasya cottarapadārthaprādhānyamiti bhāṣyoktapakṣe iṣṭasyaikavacanāpattiśca, na ceṣṭāpattiḥ, pakṣayoḥ phalabhedāpattyeṣṭāpatterayogāt, 'aneka'mityatraika- vacanasyeva prakṛtasthale dvivacanāderapyuttarapadārthaprādhānyaphalatvasyoktaprāyatayoktaprayogādabhinnāśrayaṇa- syāyuktatvāt | yadyapi 'etattado'riti sūtre'nañyamāsagrahaṇena pūrvapadārthaprādhānye'pyuttaraprādhānya- prayuktakāryātideśenātau sta ityādau dvivacanādiranekarmityatraikavacanasya ca siddhistathāpyuktā- tideśakalpanāpekṣayā lāghavāduttarapadārthaprādhānyakalpanaiva jyāyasīti bhāvaḥ | 'aneka'mityatraika- vacanāsiddhiḥ, subiti viśeṣyānurodhādekavacanamati tu na yuktam | svasminnanyasaṃkhyāsattve'pi viśeṣyānurodhi vacanamityādyanuśāsanābhāvāt, na cāvarṣā hemanta ityatrevāśrayato viṅgetyanena nirvāhaḥ,
pratiyogirūpāśrayasyātra grahaṇāt, pratiyogijñānasyābhāvajñānārthamāśrayaṇāt, ata evānya- padārthaprādhānyapakṣavat pūrvapadārthaprādhānyapakṣe'varṣā hemanta etyatra hemantarūpāśrayagataliṅgasaṃkhyāpatti- rūpo doṣo bhāṣye noktaḥ, 'guṇavacanānāmāśrayato liṅgādivat samāsārthaḥ pratiyogitayā yad dravyaṃ śritastasya liṅgādi samāsasye'tyaṅgīkārāditi vācyam, guṇavacanānā'mityāderatideśasya svīkāre gauravāt, dṛṣṭāntapadārṣṭāntiṃkayorāśrayavailakṣaṇyaprayuktavaiṣamyāpatteśceti dik |
anyonyābhāvatvādītyādipadena dhvaṃsatvaprāgabhāvatvasaṃgrahaḥ | nanu sakalābhāvānugatābhāvatvā- vacchinne śaktirucitā lāghavāt, na tvatyantābāvatvādirūpanānādharmāvacchinneṣu gauravādityā- śaṅkyāha tattadrūṣeṇa bodhāditi | tattadrūṣeṇa bodhasyānubhavasiddhatvādityarthaḥ | ghaṭaprāgabhāvadhvaṃsa- samaye 'atra ghaṭo nāstī'ti pratītyā ghaṭaprāgabhāvatvādinā ghaṭaprāgabhāvo ghaṭadhvaṃsaśca viṣayīkriyate, na tu tadatyantābhāvatvāvacchinnaḥ, prāgabhāvadhvaṃsayoratyantābhāvena prācīnatākrikamate virodhena tadā tatra tadatyantābhāvāsattvāditi bhāvaḥ | navyatārkikāstu naño'tyantābhāvatvāvacchinne'nyonyābhāvatvā- vacchinne eva śaktirna tu prāgabhāvatvādyavacchinne'pi, mānābhāvāt, prāgabhāvāt, prāgabhāvādisamaye 'ghaṭo nāstī'ti dhīstadatyantābhāvamevāvagāhate, prāgabhāvādyoratyantābhāvena saha virodhe mānābhāvāditi vadanti | nanvatyantābhāvatvaṃ nityasaṃsargābhāvatvaṃ, tādātmyasaṃbandhāvacchinna- pratiyogitākābhāvatvamanyonyābhāvatvamevaṃ cātyantābhāvatvāpekṣayānyonyābhāvatvaṃ, ladhviti tadavacchinne eva śaktikalpanamucitaṃ, na tvatyantābhāvatvāvacchinne | evamutpattimadabhāvatvotpattiśūnyanāśa- pratiyogyabhāvatvarūpadhvaṃsaprāgabhāvatvayorapi anyonyābhāvatvāpekṣayā gurutayā tadavacchinne'piṃ śaktikalpanamanucitamiti cenna, 'nāstī'tyādipratītisākṣiko'nuyogitāviśeṣa evātyantābāvatvādirna tūktarūpaḥ, tathā sati saṃsargābhāvatvasya durvacana tayā tad‌ghaṭitātyantābhāvatvasyāpi tathātvāpattiḥ, tathā hi na tāvat saṃsargoropajanyapratītiviṣayābhāvatvaṃ saṃsargābhāvatvaṃ, pratiyogisaṃsargāropasyā- bhāvalaukikapratyakṣaṃ pratyeva hetutayātīndriyābhāve'vyāpteḥ, nāpi saṃsargāvacchinnapratiyogitākābhāvatvaṃ tat, anyonyābhāvasyāpi tādātmyasaṃbandhāpratiyogitākatayā tatrātivyapteḥ, na ca tādātmyātiriktatvaṃ saṃsarge viśeṣaṇaṃ praveśyamiti noktadoṣa iti vācyaṃ, tathāpi saṃyogasaṃbandhā- vacchinnapratiyogitāke saṃyogino'tyantābāve'vyāpteḥ, saṃyogarūpasaṃyogitādātmyarūpatayā tadati- riktatvābhāvāt, evamanyonyābhāvatvaṃ na tādātmyasaṃbandhāvacchinnapratiyogitākābhāvatvaṃ, saṃyogasaṃbandhāvacchinnapratiyogitākasaṃyogyatyantābhāve, samavāyasaṃbandhāvacchinnapratiyogitāke samavāyyatyantābhāve cātivyāptiriti dik, tad dhvanayannāha ityādyanyatra vistara iti | dīdhityādigrantheṣu vistaro draṣṭavya ityarthaḥ ||
iti nañarthanirṇayaḥ ||
īśvaramanubhavatītyādāviti | 'ādi'padāt prajayītyādeḥ parigrahaḥ | 'anubhavādi'rityādipadaṃ prakṛṣṭajayādisaṃgrāhakam | na dhātvarthaḥ - na bhvādidhātupratipādyaḥ | tatra hetumāha bhavatītyatreti | tathā ca na tattaddhātuvācyaḥ sa ityarthaḥ | anvayavyatirekābhyāṃ lāghavādupasargavācya eva so'stvityāśaṅkāṃ pariharati nopasargārtha iti | nopasargavācya ityarthaḥ | nanu pratyayānāṃ prakṛtitatsaṃbhivyāhṛtānyatarārthānvitasvārthabodhakatvamityeva vyutpattiḥ kalpyatāṃ, na caivaṃ gauravaṃ, dhātau
tattadarthavācakatvasyopasarge dyotakatvakalpanāpekṣayā lāghavānapāyādityato dūṣaṇāntaramāha anu- gacchatītyādāviti | pratyayāpatteśceti | anubhavavācakatvenābhimatasyānoḥ sattvāt, tattaddhātusamabhivyāhārāsyānubhādestattadupasargeṇa bodhe niyāmakatvaṃ kalpyata iti cet, sattādyarthavācakatvena klṛptasya dhātorevānubhavādibodhakatvamupasargasya dyotakatvaṃ kalpayituṃ yuktam, evaṃ sati 'pratyayānā'miti vyutpattirapi na saṃkocyeti bhāvaḥ | na viśiṣṭārtha iti | nopasarga- viṣiṣṭatattaddhātuvācya ityarthaḥ | gauravāditi | viśiṣṭasyobhayarūpatvenobhayatra vācakatvakalpanāyāṃ gauravāt | nanu 'prajayatī'tyatra jayo dhātuvācyaḥ, prakarṣa upasargavācya ityeva kiṃ nopayata iti cecchṛṇu prakārṣasya nāmārthatayā dhātvarthajayenānvayānupapatteḥ, nāmārthadhātvarthayoḥ sākṣādbhedasaṃbandhe- nānvayasyāvyutpannatvāt, 'upasargātiriktatva'sya nāmani praveśe tu gauravamiti bhāvaḥ | nanu vṛttiṃ vinā kathaṃ tatrānubhavapratītirata āha tathā ceti | uktarītyā dhātorupasargasya cānubhavācakatvāsaṃbhave cetyarthaḥ | vidyamānatvādivācakasyeti | idaṃ dhātorhetugarbhaviśeṣaṇaṃ, yato dhāturvidyamānatvādivācī 'ghaṭo bhavatī'tyādau tadarthapratīterato dhātoreva śakyasaṃbandharūpalakṣaṇā saṃbavati, upasargasya kevalasyāprayogāt kasyāpyarthasyāvācakatvena śakyasaṃbandharūpā lakṣaṇā na saṃbhavatītyarthaḥ | nanvevamupasargāṇāṃ dyotakatvapravādavirodha ityāśaṅkyāha tātparyeti | evaṃ 'pratiṣṭhate' ityatra gamanatvenānubhavasiddhabodho dhātulakṣaṇayaiva nirvāhyaḥ, upasargasyābhāve śaktisvīkāre tu 'nāmārthadhātvarthayo'riti vyutpattisaṃkoce'pi gamananivṛttyabhāvatvarūpeṇaiva bodhaḥ syānna tu gamanatveneti bodhyam | anye tu jaye klṛptaśakterdhātoreva prakṛṣṭajaye śaktiḥ, prottarajitvaṃ śaktatāvacchedakam, iyamevaupasaṃdānikī śaktirūpasargāṇāmiti gīyate, śaktatāvacchedakānupraveśa eva hi tatropasaṃdānapadārthaḥ, evamevānubhavatītyādāvityāhuḥ | svārtheti | āsadhātorupāsanārūpaphala- vācakatvābhāvena 'svārthe'tyādirūpasya sakarmakatvasyānāpatteriti yojanā | na ca phalavyadhikaraṇa- vyāpārāvācitvameva sakarmakatvamastu, māstu ca tatra svārthatvaniveśanaṃ, tathā copasanāyā upasargavācyatve'pi tadātmakaphalavyadhikaraṇavyāpāravācitvaṃ dhātorniṣpratyūhameveti vācyaṃ, yatkiñci- tphalavyadhikaraṇavyāpāravācitvasyātiprasañjakatayā phale svārthatvapraveśasyāvaśyakatvāt | gauravā- diti | idamupalakṣaṇaṃ-viśiṣṭasya bhvādigaṇe pāṭhābhāvāddhātutvānāpattiḥ, pāṭha kalpyata iti cet, tathāpi dvirvacanāḍādivyavasthānāpattirityapi bodhyam | uktaṃ ca vākyapadīye 
'aḍādīnāṃ vyavasthārtha pṛthak‌tvena prakalpanam |
dhātūpasargayoḥ śāstre dhātureva tu tādṛśaḥ ||' iti |
idamanubhavatītyādāvapi viśiṣṭasya vācakatvakalpane veditavyam | dhātoḥ sakarmakatvābhāve'pyupāsyata iti bhāve lakāra utpadyatāmityāśaṅkyāha ddaśyata ityatra karmaṇīti śeṣa iti | bhāve lakāre 'hariharau upāsyete' itiprayogānupapattiriti bhāvaḥ || 42 ||
tattadarthavācakatvamiti | laukikaviṣayatāśālipratyakṣānukūlavyāpārādyarthavācakatvamityarthaḥ, lakṣaṇāsvīkāre 'vācakatva'mityasya bodhakatvamityartho'vaseyaḥ, śaktikalpane tu yathāśrutameva sādhu | kkacittu 'bodhakatva'mityeva pāṭhaḥ | dhātūnāmanekārthatvādatra śaktikalpane tulyanyāyādanubhavatī-
tyādāvapyanubhavādivācakatvakalpanaṃ yuktam, ata eva paspaśāhnike kaustubhe 'upāsyate guru'ranu- 'bhūyate sukha'mityādau dhātoḥ sakarmakakriyāparatvaṃ vinā karmaṇi lakārāyogena vācakatve sthite ityuktam | 'alaṃkriyata' ityatra śobhānukūlavyāpāraviśeṣo dhātvarthaḥ | anyathā - yatkiṃcidarthe sakarmakatvamāśrityārthāntare'pi sakarmakatve | ityatrāpi syāditi | tatrāpi 'karoti ghaṭa'mityatra kṛdhātoḥ sakarmakatvasya dṛṣṭatayā 'vāyurvikuruta' ityādāvapi sakarmakatvāpattiḥ, na ceṣṭāpattiḥ, 'vāyunā vikriyate' 'saindhavairvikriyata' iti bhāve lakāro na syāditi bhāvaḥ | kiṃ ca sākṣātkārā- dikarmatvena gurvāderabhidhānaṃ lakāreṇa na syāt, sākṣātkārāderdhātvarthatāvirahe dhātvarthaphalāśrayasyaiva karmatvāt, na ca karotikarmatvasaṃbhāvanāpi, siddhe nitye tasya bādhitatvāt | arthabhede'pi śabdābhedena, śabdabhede'pi sakarmakadhāturūpaśabdasādṛśyamūlakābhedāropanibandhanā vā śaṅkā, arthabhedācchabdabheda evetyuttaram | kośasvarasāditi | yadyapyupāsanādāvupādeḥ kośo na śaktigrāhakastathāpi 'sākṣātpratyakṣatulyayoḥ' 'alaṃ bhūṣaṇaparyāptiśaktivāraṇavācakaḥ' 'vā syādvi- kalpopamayorevārthe'pi samuccaye' ityādi kośaprāmāṇyāt 'atha nipātāḥ uccāvaceṣvartheṣu nipatantī'tyārabhya nirukte nipātānāṃ tadattarthakatvasyokteścopāsanādakamapi tadarthatvena kalpyata iti bhāvaḥ | dhātorvyāpāramātravācitve pūrvoktarītyā sakarmakatvaṃ na na syādityāśaṅkāṃ nirasyati svasveti | svaḥ - dhātuḥ, sa ca tadyuktanipātaśca tayoranyatarasyārthabhūtaṃ tadvayadhikaraṇavyāpāra- vācakatvamityarthaḥ | ddaṣṭānteti | ubhayatrāpi nipātasya dyotakatāṃ vinaiva gurvādeḥ karmatvanirvāhāt | nedaṃ sādhakamiti | uktasthale gurvāderlakāreṇa sākṣātkārādikarmatvenābhidhānaṃ sādhakaṃ - dhāto- viśiṣṭārthakatvasya, nipātasya dyotakatvasya ca sādhakam | anvayasyaiveti | anukūlatāsaṃbandhenā- nvayasyaivetyarthaḥ, prāguktakośādikaṃ tu dyotyārthamādāyopapannamiti bhāvaḥ | tadanvayaḥ - kīdṛśādi- padārthānvayaḥ | tulyasamādheyatvādita | tulyena - prāditulyena nirarthakatvahetunā, samādhātuṃ śakyatvādityarthaḥ | nanvabhedena nāmārthaprakārabodhaṃ prati nipātabhinnajanyopasthitiḥ kāraṇamiti kāryakāraṇabhāvāṅgīkārānna dṛṣṭāntadārṣṭāntikayordoṣaṃ ityata āha api ceti | bhedenānvayā- saṃbhava iti | 'nāmārthayo'riti vyutpattivirodhāt, bhedasaṃsargakanāmārthaprakārakahetubhūtapratyayajanyo- pasthiterabhāvācca, dyotakatāpakṣe tu tattatprātipadikasyaiva viśiṣṭārthakatvenvayābhāvādeva na doṣaḥ | 'vinā ṣaṣṭhyādika'mityuktistu-'tatsattve tadarthadvārā nāmārthayorapi bhedenānvayaḥ saṃbhavati, vibhaktyartha- saṃsargadvārā vetyarthasya bodhanāya | 'ṣaṣṭhyādika'mityādipadaṃ dvitīyādisaṃgrāhakam | anyathā - ṣaṣṭhyādikaṃ vināpi nāmārthayorbhedasaṃbandhenānvayāṅgīkāre, 'nāmārthayo'riti vyutpattyānaṅgīkāre iti yāvat | cetyeveti | na tu dhavaśca khadiraśceti bhāvaḥ || 43 ||
saddaśeti | 'vācā' 'niśe'tivadābantamiti bhramavāraṇāyāha-saddaśeneti | yena kenacidrūpeṇa sādṛśyasyāsamānādhikaraṇe'pi saṃbhavādāha samānādhikaraṇeneti | svapratipādakaśabdasamānādhi- karaṇaśabdapratipādyena, yadvā-svavṛttipadārthatāvacchedakasamānādhikaraṇapadārthatāvacchedakādhikaraṇena, tādātmyasaṃbandhena svasaṃbandhinā vā | etādṛśapadārthayorabhedasaṃbandhenānvayasaṃbhava ityāśayenāha- abhedeneti | śeṣeti | samānādhikaraṇeti | samānavibhaktikayorviruddhavibhaktirahitayorvā
prātipadikayorarthayorityarthaḥ | anvaya eva nāstīti | pṛthagupasthitayoreva saṃsargasyānvayarūpatvāditi bhāvaḥ | nāyaṃ doṣa iti | 'dhavasya khadirasya cetyeva syā'dityukto doṣo na, vyutpattervibhinnopa- sthitaviṣayatvāt, prakate ca khadirapadenaiva khadirasamuccayopasthāpanāditi bhāvaḥ | ata eva - nāmārthayo'riti vyutpattesaṃkocādeva | lākṣaṇikamiti | saṃkoce tu lakṣaṇākalpanaṃ nirarthakameva syāt, ghaṭasya ghaṭatvāvacchinnapratiyogitākatvasaṃbandhena nañarthābhāvenvayasaṃbhavāt | nanu ghaṭādipadasya ghaṭādipratiyogike lakṣaṇāmaṅgīkṛtyābhedānvayopapādanaṃ naiyāyikānāṃ na yuktaṃ, tathā satyapi 'bhūtale na ghaṭa' ityatra tātparyasattve bhūtalavṛttitvābhāvasya ghaṭe'nvayabodhaśca 'dīdhitikārā'dibhirabhyupeyate, tathā ca tatra 'nāmārthayo'riti vyutpattibhaṅgaḥ | evaṃ 'caitrona pacatī'tyatra pākānukūlakṛtyabāvasya caitrenvayāttatrāpi | evaṃ 'ghaṭaḥ paṭo ne'tyatra ghaṭapratiyogikabhedasyāśrayatāsaṃbanyena bodhāttatrāpi | naño'bhāvavallākṣaṇikatve tasya mukhyārtha paratvadaurlabhyāpatti, abāvasya bhedānvayabodhopagame'pi kāryakāraṇabhāvādhikyavirahādbhedānvayabodhopagama eva samucitaḥ, spaṣṭaṃ cedaṃ vyutpattivāde | kiṃ cānekaśabde samuccayaśaktikalpanāpekṣayā katipayanipāteṣveva tatkalpanā yuktetyāśaṅkyāha api ceti | rasasaddaśāniti | eteṣāmu'dicyā'nityatra viśeṣaṇatvam | atra karaṇamiti | atra - udagdeśīyarājoddharaṇakriyāyām | saddaśasya karaṇatvepīti | usrasadṛśasyābhedena śaraviśeṣaṇatayā prakṛtakriyāyāṃ karaṇatvasaṃbhave'pi | tasya - sadṛśasya | anena - usrapadottaravartitṛtīyāpratyayena | aprakṛtyeti | atra 'sadṛśasye'tyanuṣajyate | tathā ca sadṛśe-ivārthe usrapadottaravartitṛtīyayā karaṇatvaṃ pratipādayituṃ na śakyate, 'pratyayānāṃ prakṛtyarthānvite'tyādivyutpattivirodhāditi samuditārthaḥ | tṛtīyā'saṃbhavāditi | karaṇatvārthakatṛtīyāyā asaṃbhavāt, avyayottaraṃ sarvavibhakti- luk‌saṃbhave'pi tasyā nirarthakatvāt, vibhaktyarthapradhānanipātārthasya kārakatvenānvayāt | nanu tadarthasyāpi kārakatvenānvayo'stu, ata eva 'dīdhitau' 'pṛthivyāmeva gandha' ityatraivakārārtha- syānyasya luptasaptamyarthādheyatve'nvayaṃ svīkṛtyāparasminnevaśabdārthe'bhāve tadanvayamupagamya-pṛthivī- samavetaḥ pṛthivyanyāsamaveto gandha iti bodho varṇitaḥ, tathā cevāśabdottaravartitṛtīyā karaṇatvārthikā saṃbhavatyevetyāśaṅkyāha-saṃbhaveveti | kṣavaṇeti | kārakatvena tadarthasyānvaye- 'sattvārthakatvābhāvena nipātatvābhāvenāvyayatvāprāptyā 'avyayā'diti luganāpattyā sup‌kṣavaṇaṃ syāditi bhāvaḥ, ata eva dīdhitikārasyātra na nirbhara ityuktaṃ prāmāṇikaiḥ | evaṃ ca evaśabdasyādheyatvārthakatāṃ svīkṛtya pūrvoktabodha upapādanīyaḥ | 'supāṃ ca śravaṇa'mityatra cakāro bhinnakama ityāha-supāṃ śravaṇaṃ cetyartha iti | nanu tasya sattvārthakatve'pi svarādipāṭhādavyayatvopapattiḥ, tathā ca na supaḥ śravaṇaprasaṅga ityato mūle caśabdaḥ upāttastatsamucceyamāha-cakārāditi | tathā ca tṛtīyāyā asādhutvamapi syāt' liṅgasaṃkhyāvibhedepi hyupamānopameyatā, vibhaktiḥ punarekaiva, 'upamānopameyayo'rityanuśāsanaṃ tu munipraṇītaṃ nāstyeveti bhāvaḥ | sādhutvamātrārtheti | 'na kevalā prakṛti'riti niyamena kriyamāṇā vibhaktirviśeṣyavācakapadaprakṛtikasajātīyaiva kartumucitā, anyathā bhedānvayabodho na syāt, viruddhavibhaktirāhityādestatra tantratvāditi bhāvaḥ | 'usrasadṛśaśarāṇā'miti tripado dvandvaḥ | bhedenānvayeti | usrapadārthasya pratiyogitvasaṃbandhena sadṛśapadārthekadeśe sādṛśye, sadṛśasya svaniṣṭhasādṛśyānuyogitvasaṃbandhenanvayo vādhita ityarthaḥ | evaṃ ca 'ghanaśyāma' ityādau ghanādipadaṃ ghanādisadṛśalākṣaṇikam, ghana iva śyāma iti vigrahe'pi tattathā, ata egha
sāmānādhikaraṇyātsamāsapuṃvadbhāvāvāñjasyena siddhau, anyathā vacanasāmarthyātsāmānādhi- karaṇyavirahe'pi samāsāṅgīkāre prakaraṇavādhaḥ syāt, evamapi puṃvadbhāvānāpattiḥ, mṛgakṣīravajjāti- mātravivakṣayā prayogamirvāhastu-gatyantarasattve na yukta iti spaṣṭamatraiva sūtre kaiyaṭe | upamānaparatvaṃ tu 'ghana' iti taṭasthaprayogamādāya bhūtapūrvagatyāśrayeṇa, ghanasadṛśārthakatve'pi viśeṣaṇavidhayā ghanārthakatvasattvādvā, evaṃ ca ghanasadṛśaḥ śyāma ityubhayatra bodhaḥ, dyotyasyāpi sadṛśasyānubhavabalā- diśeṣyatvāṅgīkāre kṣativirahaḥ, dyotyavācyayoryatra pṛthagupasthitistatraiva dyotyasya vācye viśeṣaṇatva- miti niyama iti svīkārāt, sādṛśyasya prayojakadharmakāṅkṣāyāmusthitaśyāmatvaṃ prayojakatvena mānasa bodhaviṣayo bhavatītyanyatra vistaraḥ | usrābhinneti | usrasya sadṛśe'bhedenānvaye usrābhinna- sadṛśasya śare'bhedasya vādhaḥ, sadṛśasya pūrvaśareṇābhedānvaye sadṛśābhinnaśare usrasyānvaye ityarthaḥ | pare tu keṣāṃcinnipātānāmanubhavānurodhena vācakatvamapi, ata eva netthato'bhāvabodho'nubhūyate, kasyeti jijñāsā ca dṛśyate, abhāvaśabdavat, api ca 'ā no divo vṛhataḥ parvatādā' ityādau parvatādarvāgatyādyarthabodhāttatra vāvakatvamāvaśyakamiti nirukte prathamādhyāye tṛtīyakhaṇḍe 'upasargā arthān nirāhuḥ' ityupakrame-' arthavikaraṇam ā ityavargarthe' ityatra spaṣṭam | dyotakatve hi digyogalakṣaṇapañcamyanupapattistasya viśeṣaṇatā ca syāt, ata eva 'striyā'miti sūtre strītvasya prādhānye vacanānupapattirdyotakatāpakṣamavalambaya parihṛtā, ata eva 'hetumati ce'ti sūtrabhāṣye dyātakatāpakṣe 'hetumatī'tyasya prakṛtyarthaviśeṣaṇatvamuktam | hariṇāpyuktam 
'nipātā dyotakāḥ, kecit pṛthagarthābhidhāyinaḥ |
āgamā iva kepi syuḥ saṃbhūyārthasya vācakāḥ || iti |
kecidityubhayānvayī, kecit pṛthagarthābhidhāyino vācakāḥ, kecinnirarthakā ityāha āgamā iveti tadarthaḥ | 'nāmākhyātamupasargau nipāta' ityādiprātiśākhyāmapyatra mānaṃ bodhyamiti mañjūṣāyāṃ nirūpitam | evaṃ-'śarairusrarive'tyādau dyotakatāpakṣepyusrasadṛśaiḥ śarairityartho na yuktaḥ, uktarītyā dyotyārthakasya viśeṣaṇatvaniyamāt, kiṃ caivaṃ 'ghanaśyāma' ityādau vigrahe samāse ca ghanaśabdasya ghanasadṛśaparatvenopamānatvena bodhakāni sāmānyadharmaviśiṣṭopameyavacanaiḥ samānādhikaraṇaiḥ saha samasyante ityarthakena 'upamānāni sāmānyavacanaiḥ' iti samāsānāpattiḥ,tasyopameyaparatvenopamāna- paratvābhāvāt | bhūtapūrvagatirapi prakṛte na nirvāhikā, vigrahavākyepi tasyopameyaparatvāt, ghanena tulya ityatropamānaparatvepi tasya vigrahatvābhāvād vṛttivigrahayorupamānavācakatvagrahaṇamucitaṃ pratyāsatteḥ, uktavākyaṃ tu na vigrahastathā sati 'mṛgacapale'tyatra puvattvānāpatteḥ | 'darśanīyā māte'tyatra puṃvattvavāraṇāya vṛttau vigrahe ca sāmānādhikaraṇyasya puṃvattvaprayojakatvāt, kevalaghanādiśabdaprayoge upamānatvaviśiṣṭavācakatvaṃ durupapādameva, evaṃ sādṛśyasaṃbandhena pūrvapadārthasyottarapadārthe'nvaye'pi puṃvattvānupapattiḥ, kiṃ ca śyāmaśabdasyopameyaparatvena ghane tadanvayābhāvena tasya sādhāraṇadharmavaiśiṣṭyenaitāditaraparicchedakatvarūpopamānatvānupapattiḥ | taduktaṃ bhāṣye 'yadi śastrīva śyāmā devadatteti vagrahastadā śastryāṃ śyāmetyetadapekṣyaṃ syāt, yadi 'yathā śastrī śyāmā tadvaddevadatte'ti vigrahastadā devadattāyāṃ śyāmetyetadapekṣyaṃ syādi'ti |
kaiyaṭopādhyāyā api 'na ca śabdasyāvṛttirasti yenobhayagataṃ śyāmatvaṃ pratipādaye'diti, tataḥ-' evaṃ tarhi tasyāmevobhayaṃ vartate' iti bhāṣye siddhāntitam | tasyām - upameyāyāmityarthaḥ | sādṛśyamūlakābhedādhyavasāyādupamānaśabdasyāpyupameyaparatvasaṃbhavaḥ, sādṛśyanirvāhāyopāttaśyāmādereva tantrādinopamānepi saṃbandhoṅgīkāryaḥ, tātparyavaśāt piyadarśanatvarūponupāttadharmopi candramukhī- tyādāvupamānimittaṃ bhavati, evaṃ copameye śrutaḥ kathamupamānaṃ na saṃspṛkṣyatīti, spaṣṭaṃ cedamatraiva kaiyaṭe | āvṛttisattve'pi sāmānādhikaraṇyānurodhādayameva pakṣo bhāṣye siddhāntitaḥ | śyāmatvā- derubhayatra saṃbandhe etaduttaraṃ bhāṣye-śyāmatvasyobhābhyāmeva saṃbandhāt, bahubhiḥ sambandhābhāvāt, samānānāṃ bhāvaḥ sāmānyaṃ, tadvacanatvaṃ sāmānyavacanatvaṃ, bahvanvitadharmavacanatvamiti yāvat | tadabhāvamāśaṅkya siddhāntinoktaṃ 'śyāmatvasyobhayatra bhāvādubhayataguṇavācakatvācca śyāmāśabdasya, sāmānyamāha sopi sāmānyavacanaḥ' iti | atro'bhayagataguṇavācakatvā'dityanena sādhāraṇadharmasyo- bhayatra śābdonvaya iti sūcitam | vastugatyobhayaguṇavācakatvārthakatve śyāmatvasya bahuṣvapi sattvena 'nāvaśyaṃ sa eva sāmānyavacanaḥ' ityetad‌grantho'saṃgata eva syāditi bodhyam, evaṃ copamānaśyāmadhanābhinnaḥ śyāmo devadatta iti bodhaḥ, vigrahe ivaśabda upamānadyotakaḥ, samāse tvekārthībhāvāttadbhānam | kāvyaprakāśakṛtāmapyupamānatvaprakārakabodhonumato, yataḥ 'yathevavā- śabdāstasyaivopamānatāpratītiriti yadyapyupamānaviśeṣaṇāni te' ityādyuktaṃ taiḥ | vastugatyā yadupamānaṃ tadvācakaṃ samasyata iti sūtrārthābhyupagame uktabhāṣyagranthasaṃgatiḥ, prakāśagranthasaṃgatiśca syāditi sudhībhiruhyam |
'yathā haristathā hara' ityādau tu bhedakadharmavaddhācakatāyāḥ 'prakāravacane thāl', iti sūtra- sthākaragranthasaṃmatatayā,yatprakāravān haristatprakāravān hara iti bodhānantaraṃ, yattacchabdabhyāṃ tayordharmayorekatvāvagamanameva prakārabhūtaṃ dharma nimittokṛtyopamānaharyabhinna upameyahara iti bodhaḥ, tasyāpi śābdatvam, ālaṃkārikamate lakṣaṇāmūlavyañjanāsthale tatprayojyaśaityapāvanatvādipratyaya- syeti bodhyam | spaṣṭaṃ cedaṃ tiṅarthavicāre tiṅantārthakriyāyā asattvanirūpaṇāvasare mañjūṣāyāṃ, 'yathā sādṛśye' iti sūtre vivaraṇādau ca | prāthamika eva bodhe upamānatvaprakārakatvaṃ prāthamika- bodhenābhyupagataṃ, tathā sati 'yathā vai śyeno nipatyādatte evamayaṃ dviṣantaṃ bhrātṛvyaṃ nipatyādatte' ityatrāpi prāthamikabodhe tathātvasvīkārāpattyā 'na vai tiṅantenopamānamasti' iti 'sūn'sūtra- bhāṣyavirodhaprasaṅgādityalamativistareṇa |
na caivaṃ 'puruṣo vyāghra iva śūra' ityatrāpyuktākārabodhe viśeṣaṇavyāghrasyāpi śūrātmaka- viśeṣaṇasāpekṣatayā samāsānupapattirita vācyaṃ, sādhāraṇadharmavattvenetaraparicchedakatvarūpopamānatvasya sādhāraṇadharmāśe nityasāpekṣatvena doṣāsaṃsparśāta, pradhānasya sāpekṣatve bhavatyeva samāsaḥ, pradhānenekaviśeṣaṇasaṃbandhasaṃbhavāt, 'devadatto dhanī sundaraśce'tivat | etena sādhāraṇadharmavattvena paricchedyatvarūpopametvasyāpi vityasāpekṣatayā pradhānasāpekṣatvepi samāso bhavatītyarthe 'sāmānyāprayoge' ityasya jñāpakatāparabhāṣyāsaṃgatirityapāstam, kaiyaṭoktanyāyasiddhārthaparatvena bhāṣyasyādoṣāt, athavā nityasāpekṣatvaṃ na samāsaprayojakaṃ, kiṃ tu gamakatvameveti samarthasūtre bhāṣye spaṣṭam, nirūpitaṃ ca samāsavāde mañajūṣāyām | tathā ca sāpekṣapradhānakasya samāsasya gamakatvaṃ 'rājapuruṣaḥ sundara' ityādau dṛṣṭamiti 'puruṣo vyāghra iva śūra' ityatrāpi samāsaprasaktiriti- tadvāraṇāya
'sāmānyāprayoge' iti jñāpakaparabhāṣyasyāpyatraiva tātparyamiti, prāhurityanyatra vistaraḥ | 45 ||
lakṣaṇāvyāptyāpattiriti | uttarapadārthasyopasarjanatvābhāvenāsa ityādyasiddhirnāpāditā | prādhānyābhāvāditi | prādhānyasya viśeṣaṇanirūpitatvena pūrvapadasya nirarkatve tadasaṃbhavāditi bhāvaḥ | upasargasyeti | 'upasarga'padaṃ nipātamātropalakṣaṇam, ata evātra 'upasargādibhyaḥ' iti vṛhad‌bhūṣaṇe uktam | yadvā-nipātānāṃ prāguktarītyā na sarveṣāṃ dyotakatvamapi tu keṣāṃcideveti sūcayituṃ 'upasargasya' ityevoktam | dyotyamarthamādāyaiveti | uttarapadārthaprādhānyaṃ nāmetarapadārthaniṣṭha- viśeṣaṇatānirūpitaviśeṣyatāśālibodhajanakatvam, tatretarapadārthaśabdena vācyalakṣyadyotyātmakastri- vidhopyartho gṛhyata iti nottarapadārthaprādhānyajñāniriti bhāvaḥ | tameva -
