dyotyameva | arthavattvādati | arthabodhajanakajñānaviṣayatvameva hyarthavattvamarthavatsūtre niviṣṭaṃ, na tu vṛttyārthabodhajanakatvarūpamato nānupapattiriti bhāvaḥ | nanvetadayuktaṃ 'dhanaṃ' 'vana'mityādau prativarṇaṃ saṃjñāpatteḥ, teṣāmapyarthabodhajanakajñānaviṣayatvāt, na ca satyapi prātipadikatve saṃkhayākarmāderabhāvānna subutpattiriti vācyaṃ, tathā sati nipātādapi subutpattyanupapatteḥ, na ceṣṭāpattiḥ, 'pragacchatī'tyādau padātparatvābhāvena 'tiṅṅatiṅa'iti nighātānāpatteḥ | 'ud‌gacchatyuccai'rityādau jaśtvarutvādyanāpatteścetyata āha vastuta iti | jñāpakātsubutpattiriti | prātipadikatvābhāve'pi jñāpakātsutpattirityarthaḥ | nipātasyeti | atra pakṣe sarveṣāṃ nipātānāṃ vṛtyārthabodhakatvarūpārthavattvā- bhāvāt prakṛtavārtikaprāptiḥ, 'anarthakagrahaṇaṃ na kariṣyate' iti bhūvādisūtrabhāṣyoktyā tatpadarahitasya tasya prāptirityanye | kriyāyogābhāvād gatyupasargasaṃjñāprāptireva nāstīti 'adhiparī anarthakau' ityanenādhiparyorgatyupasargasaṃjñānivṛttaye karmapravacanīyasaṃjñāvidhānasyānarthakanipātānāmārthavatprayukta- kāryajñāpakatāmāśrityaitadvārtikapratyākhyāne tu tasyaiva sāmānyato nipātānāmarthavatprayuktakāryajñāpaka- tāmāśritya prātipadikatvaṃ sidhyatīti bhāvaḥ | sūtreṇaiva siddhau vārtakamapa māstvityāha-'kṛttaddhi'teti | pare tu-nipātānāṃ vyajjanāparaparyāyadyotakatārūpavṛttyārthabodhakatvarūpārthavattvāt prātipadikatva- siddhiḥ, ata eva vārtikamanarthakapadaghaṭitaṃ saṃgacchate, ata eva ca yeṣāṃ dyotyo'pyartho nāsti 'tu ha sma vai' ityādestadarthamidamityarthasūcanārthamanarthakasyetyuktaṃ kaiyaṭīye iti vadanti | dyotakatāyāṃ mīmāṃsāvārtakakṛtsaṃmatimāha uktaṃ ceti | caturvidhe iti | nāmākhyātopasarganipātātmake | tathā coktaṃ nirukte-'catvāri padajātāni nāmākhyāte copasarganipātaśce'ti | saṃśayānutpattau hetumāha tayoriti | nanvevamupasargeṇetyādavṛddhoktāvupasargasyaiva dyotakatoktirviruddhetyāśaṅkyāha upasargeṇeti | anya iti | upātnirdiṣṭarūpātiriktarūpāvacchinnaḥ | tena 'prajayatī'tyatra pratīyamānaprakṛṣṭajayasya śuddhajayābhedepi na kṣatiḥ || 46 ||
nanu teṣāṃ vācakatve tatra śaktikalpanāprayuktagauravaprasaṅgo'ta āha-bodhakateti | 'tiḍāde'- rityādinā kṛtsanādiparigrahaḥ | kṛtpratyayasya dyotakatve tadarthasya viśeṣaṇatvāpattirita na
śaṅkyām, anubavānurodhāt, sattvapradhānāni nāmāni' ita vacanād dyotsyāpi kṛdarthasya
viśeṣyatvamityāśayāt, evaṃ sanādāvyūhyaṃ | syāditi | na ceṣṭāpattiḥ, siddhāntavirodhādita bhāvaḥ | pare tusuptiṅāṃ dyotakatve iṣṭapattiḥ, ata eva tadarthakālakartrāderviśeṣaṇatvam, kālasya dyotyatā tu kaiyaṭādibhirapyaṅgīkriyata eva, ata eva pacetyāditaḥ kartṛpratītiḥ | kṛtpratyayādestu na dyotakatā, tadarthasya viśeṣaṇatvāpatteḥ na ca dyotyatvepi tadarthasya viśeṣyatvam, uktarītyā tatra vācakatāpakṣasyaiva svīkartumucitatvādityāhaḥ | anvayavyatirekayoḥ - tayoḥ satorityarthaḥ | tathā cānvayavyatirekābhyāṃ tiṅādīnāṃ vācakatvavannipātānāmapi vācakatvamiti bhāvaḥ | 'pareṣāṃ yanmataṃ tadeva no'smākaṃ na yukta'miti mūle yojanā | evaṃ ca - nipātānāṃ vācakatve ca | tathetyagatyeti | tathā - nipātātiriktaviṣayamityarthaḥ, itaradoṣā api prāgud‌dhṛtā eva | idamupalakṣaṇaṃ sakarmakatvaṃ ' svasvayuktanipāte'tyādikrameṇa pūrvoktaṃ bodhyaṃ, tenānubhavatītyādau na doṣaḥ, 'pratyayayānā'miti vyutpattāvapi prakṛtyarthasamabhivyāhṛtanipātārthaprakṛtyarthānyatarānvitatvaṃ niveśyam | ata eva 'pratiṣṭhata' ityatropasargasya gativācakatve'pi na kṣatiḥ | upasargasya phalamātravācakatvaṃ dhātorvyāpāravācitva- mityabhyupagame tu prakṛtyarthe eva pratyayārthānvayādidaṃ nādaraṇīyam | evaṃ nāmārthaprakārakabhedasaṃsargaka- śābdabodhaṃ prati pratyayanipātānyatarajanyopasthititvena hetutvamaṅgīkāryamityapi bodhyam, na caivaṃ gauravam, uktayuktyā teṣāṃ tattadarthavācakatve sthite'gatyā gauravasya svīkaraṇīyatvāt | ata eva netyetāvanmātrādabhāvabodhanirvāhaḥ, tatra padāntarasamabhivyāhāraṃ vinā dyotakatvāsaṃbhava ityāhuḥ | nanu teṣāṃ vācakatve kevalānāmapi prayogāpattiriti cenna, keṣāṃcit 'te prāgdhātoḥ' iti niyamanāt, keṣāṃcinnetyādīnāṃ tathātvasyeṣṭatvāt, anyeṣāṃ vācakatvepi kevalaprakṛtipratyayavadupapatteḥ, uktaṃ hi vākyapadīye 
'pratyayasya paratve niyāmakavadatra tadabhāve'pi prakarṣādervinā sambandhinamanva- yānupapatetarna kevalānāṃ prayogaḥ, 'śobhanaḥ samuccya' itinat 'śobhanaśce'tyasya, 'ghaṭasya samuccaya' itivacca 'ghaṭasya ce'tyasya cāpattarityapi na śaṅakyam, śabdaśaktisvābhāvyena nityaparatantratayetaraviśeṣaṇatvenaiva svārthopasthāpanenāpattidvayasyābhāvāt, ṣaṣṭhyarthasambandhabodhe itara- viśeṣaṇānvayabodhe ca samuccayādiśabdajanyopasthitereva hetutvakalpanādvā | athavā caśabdasya samuccaye śaktikalpanānna doṣaḥ, dharmyaśe śaktikalpanāprayuktagauravaṃ prāmāṇikatvādaṅgīkriyate, taduktaṃ 'samuccitābhidhāne hi vyatireko na vidyate' iti bodhyam | vyarthamiti | dyotakatāpakṣe sarvaṣāmeva teṣāṃ nirarthakatayā vyāvartyābhāvāt | spaṣṭatvāditi | tatrānarthakasyeti tu pādapūrāṇārthānāṃ sarvathā nārthavattvaṃ sūcayitum | pratyākhyātatvāditi | 'adhiparī anarthakau' ityasya anarthakasyāpyarthavatkṛtaṃ bhavatītyarthe jñāpakatāmākṣitya pratyākhyātatvāt | bahuvacanaṃ mīmāṃsaketi | mīmāṃsakānāṃ bhāṣyakārādi bhedena nānātvād bahuvacanamiti bhāvaḥ | mīmāṃsakamataṃ pradarśayati kevaleti | tātparyagrāhakatveneti | tathā cānvayavyatarekayoranyathāsiddhatayā na śaktigrāhakateti bhāvaḥ | lakṣaṇāgraheti | dhātoḥ prakṛṣṭajayādau lakṣaṇāgrahe ityarthaḥ | kāryeti | lakṣaṇāgrahaśābdabodhādyoḥ kāryakāraṇabhāva ityarthaḥ | śaktigrahasyāpīti | atra 'kāryakāraṇabhāva āvaśyaka' ityanuṣajyate | ubhayamapi - vācatvadyotakatvobhayamapītyarthaḥ | ata eva - vācakatvadyotakatvobhayasvīkārādeva || 47 ||
nanu prādīnāṃ cādīnāṃ ca vācakatve upasargatvanipātātvayoḥ śaktatāvacchedakatve gauravamata āha
mūle-nipātatvamiti | tathā ca nipātatvaṃ prādicādisādhāraṇameveti noktakrameṇa gauravamityarthaḥ | athabopasargeṣu dyotakatvagrāhaścādiṣu vācakatāgrahaścobhayasādharaṇadharmābhāvādupasargatvāvacchedena nipātatvāvacchedena ca jāyata ityupasargatvanipātatvayorubhayoḥ saṃbandhatve kimiti nipātatvā- vacchedenaiva dyotakatvādi kalpyate, nopasargatvāvacchedenetyatra kiṃ vinigamakamityāśaṅkā- mapanudati-paraṃ tvityādinā | upasargatvepi svasamānādhikaraṇānyonyābhāvapratiyogitānava- cchedakatvādirūpavyāpakatvasattvādvayāpakatvaṃ na sāmānyatvamata āha-vyāpakatvātsāmānyatvāditi | tathā ca vyāpakatvaṃ prakṛte uktānavacchedakatvaviśiṣṭaṃ svābhāvavad‌vṛttitvātmakamadhikadeśavṛttatvarupaṃ vivakṣitamiti bhāvaḥ | nyūnatāṃ pariharati śaktatāyā ityupalakṣaṇamiti || 48 ||
iti nipātopasargadyotakatvādinirṇayaḥ || 8 ||
<8>
bhāvapratyayārthaṃ - tvādapratyayārtham, vārtakavacanaṃ - vārtikarūpaṃ vacanamityarthaḥ | hariṇeta | evaṃ ca haryuktau vārtikatvabhramasteṣāmiti bhāvaḥ | tvataloriti viśeṣopādānenemanijāderanyārthaka- tvaśaṅkā syādata āha tvatalorita | bhāveti | ayaṃ - vakṣyamāṇaḥ | arthaḥ -tātparyārthaḥ | vadati - bodhatītyarthaḥ | na saṃbhavatīti | tatpakṣe rājapuruṣādisamāsajanyabodhe saṃsargasyāprakāra- tvāt tatpakṣe rājapuruṣatvamityatra tvapratyayasya bheda evārtha ityaśayaḥ | evaṃ puruṣarūpeṇa vastugatyā rājasaṃbandhavān yaḥ puruṣaḥ sa samāsārtha iti pakṣe-tvapratyayārthaḥ puraṣatvameva, tadvayaktamātranirūpatā- dheyatvasaṃbandhena prakṛtyarthasya tatrānvayaḥ, ata eva tatpakṣe kṛttaddhitetyādivacanamāvaśyakamiti śaṅkānavakāśaḥ | ityanvayabodhaḥ - iti śābdabodhaḥ | taddhitānte'piśaktisattvena pṛthagupasthitiviṣayārthayoḥ saṃsargasyaivānvayatvena yathāśrutāsaṃgatyāpatteḥ, śābdatvaṃ tu tatrāpa 'śabdayāmī'tyanubhavādityanyatra vistaraḥ | 'upasthitayoḥ saṃbandho'nvaya' iti mate tu yathāśrutamevāstu | avyabhicaritasaṃbandhe tu-apatyāpatyavatsaṃbandhasyāvyabhicaritasaṃbandhatve tu | yathā sat sattayoḥ saṃbandhaḥ kadāpi na vyabhicaritastathānayorapi, sata utpattiprabhṛti-āvināśāt saṃbandhasya vidyamānatvādapatyasyotpattiprabhṛti-āvināśāduktasaṃbandhasyāpi tathātvāditi bhāvaḥ | 'gotraṃ ca caraṇaiḥ sahe'tyanenāpatyavasyāpi jātitvabodhanājjātyābhedasaṃbandhasya tirodhānāt jātireva tatra prakāra iti saiva tvādyartha iti tātparyārthaḥ | jātiprayuktakāryamevātidiśyate, na jātitvaṃ tasyeti pakṣāntarasūcanāya tuśabdaḥ | arthāntaravṛttiriti | 'aindratva'mityādau devatādyarthakataddhita udāhārya ityarthaḥ | taddhitāntatvepīti | ata eva saṃbandhārthakatvaprasaktiḥ | abhedapratyayāditi | matupo lugvidhānaṃ tu 'paṭasya śukrala' iti guṇaguṇinorbhedavyavahārasyāpi sattvācchuklavānityaviṣṭaprayoga- nivṛttyartham | jātāveveti | lokanirūḍhena 'so'ya'miti pratyayena bhedasaṃbandhasya tirodhānānna saṃbandhe pratyayaḥ, evaṃ 'śukla' ityādāvapi | pācakādau tu kriyayābhedasyaiva pratītervyabhicārācca krayāsaṃbandhājñānamūlikaiva śabdasya dravye pravṛttiḥ, evaṃ rājapuruṣādāvapīti tattatsaṃbandha eva
tattaduttarabāvapratyayārthaḥ | 'nīlaghaṭatva'matyādau nīlaghaṭādivṛttarghaṭatvādikameva tvādivācyatve- nāśrayati, na tu saṃbandhiviśeṣaṇā'bhinnarūpatvena, pūrvottarapadārthayorviśeṣyaviśeṣaṇabhāvātirikta- saṃbandhasyābhāvāt | ghaṭatvameva nīlaghaṭaśabdapravṛttinamittaṃ, nīlādiviśeṣaṇabalāttu ghaṭatvarūpeṇa
nīlādivyaktibhāne nīlasamānādhikaraṇaghaṭatvādikameva pravṛttinivṛttinimittatvena bhāsate, upādhibhedād ghaṭatvāderapi bhedaḥ, ato ghaṭatvasyātiprasaktatayā kathaṃ nīlaghaṭādiśabda- pravṛttinamittatvamiti śaṅgānavakāśaḥ | 'dhavakhadiratva'mityādau jātisamudāyastadvācyaḥ, samudāyasyānud‌bhūtāvayavatve ekavacanam, udbhūtāvayavatve punardvivacanādakamapīti bodhyamiti vadanti, tad dhvanayannāha digiti | tārkikāstu karmadhārayottarabāvapratyayaḥ padārthatāvacchedakayonī'latva- ghaṭatvādyoḥ sāmānādhikaraṇyavacana ityūcuḥ ||
prakṛtyarthaviśiṣṭeti | saṃyogādisaṃbandhena vastugatyā daṇḍādiviśiṣṭetyarthaḥ, tatra syogādiḥ saṃsagamaryādayā bhāsata iti tatra na śaktiḥ, kiṃ tu prakṛterdaṇḍe, āśraye ca pratyayasya | mātrapadena saṃyogādirūpasaṃbandhavyāvṛttiḥ, ata eva 'pācaka' ityādāvapi saṃbandho na śakyaḥ | uktaṃ ca bhaṭṭaiḥ 
'pākaṃ tu pacirevāha kartāraṃ pratyayopyakaḥ |
pākayuktaḥ punaḥ kartā vācyo naikasya kasyacit ||' iti |
na cānantadravyeṣu śaktigrahāsaṃbhavaḥ, paramparayā ekaṃ daṇḍatvādikamupalakṣaṇīkṛtya tatsaṃbhavādityaruṇādhikaraṇoktarītyā draṣṭavyamiti bhāvaḥ | 'mīmāṃsakānā'mityasya 'mata' iti śeṣaḥ | tadabhidhānam - saṃbandhābidhānam | nanu 'dāmodara'tvamityādau bhāvapadena dāmodarādiniṣṭho dharmaviśeṣo gṛhyata iti tadanabhidhānaṃ, 'daṇḍitva'mityādau punarbhāvaḥ saṃyogādirevātastadabhidhāne kiṃ bādhakamityāśaṅkāpanuttyai haitumāha prakṛtīti | hi - yasmāddhetoruktatvarūpo bhāvastaduttaratvādi- pratyayenābhidhīyate'to 'daṇḍitva'mityatra saṃyogasya tava mate daṇḍipadajanyabodhīyaprakāratvābhāvena- tvādipratyayenābhidhānaṃ na syādityarthaḥ | padayoḥ krameṇa vyavṛttimāha anyatheti | anyathā - dharmamātrasya bāvapadārthatve | dravyatvāderiti | tasyāpi ghaṭasamavetatvāt, ghaṭapadajanyabodhe prākaratvaṃ tu neti na ghaṭapadottaratvādinā tadabhidhānam | daṇḍādeśceti | daṇḍipadajanyabodhe tasya prakāratvāt, daṇḍasaṃyuktadevadattādau samavetatvābhāvāttu na 'daṇḍitva'mityatra tvādinā daṇḍādirabhidhīyate, atra samavetatvaṃ na samavāyena vartamānatvaṃ, ghaṭatvatvamabhāvatvamityādau ghaṭatvatvāderasamavetatvena tvādavācyatānupatteḥ, api tu prakāratāvacchedakasaṃbandhena tatra vartamānatvarūpam | saṃbandhaḥ prakāra iti | tathā ca dāmodarādipadajanyabodhīyaprakāratvābhāvāt taduttaratvādipratyayena saṃbandhābhidhānavaddaṇḍitvamityādāvapi saṃbandhasya tenānabhidhānaṃ syāditi samuditārthaḥ | 'daṇḍitva'mityādau saṃbandhavācyatve mīmāṃsakoktāṃ yuktimanūdya dūṣayati yadeti | svasamavetaḥ - prakṛtijanyabodhaviśeṣyasamavetaḥ | vācyaḥ - prakṛtivācyaḥ, prakṛtijanyabodhaprakāra iti yāvat, yadvā tvādi pratyayavācyaḥ, tadabhidhānayogya iti yāvat | prakṛtijanyabodhīyaprakāratāśrayo hi tadabhidhānayogyaḥ | sambandho vācya iti | prakṛtijabodhaprakāro'pi svasamavetaḥ sambandhastvādivācya āśrita ityarthaḥ | prapañcitaṃ caitaditi | ayaṃ bhāva-'daṇḍi'tyādau sambandhāvācyatve
daṇḍitvamityatrāpi sambandhasya vācyatvaṃ na syāt, pratītestadvadevopapatteḥ, na ca tvādairvaiyarthyāpattyā sambandhe vācyatvaṃ kalpya iti cenna, 'ghaṭatva'mityādau klaptapadārthatāvacchedakaśaktyaiva nirvāhāt | 'kṛttaddhite'tvādivacanaṃ tu harerna muneryad‌balātkalpanā syāt | anubhavānurodhātsambandhe tvādiśaktikalpanamiti cenna, śaktyantarakalpanāmapekṣyenādīnāṃ klṛptaśaktau dharmīvatsambandha-
viṣayatvenopapattau pṛthak‌śaktikalpanā yogāt, tathā sati klṛptapadārthatāvacchedakaśaktyaiva tadbhānasambhava ityādi tatra prapañcitamiti | idaṃ vaiṣamyam - dāmodaratvamatyādau sambandho na vācyo daṇḍitvamityādau tu vācya iti vaiṣamyamityarthaḥ | vakṣyamāṇeti | vakṣyamāṇaścāsau śepaścetyarthaḥ | pare tu inādeḥ sambandhāvācakateva'pi daṇḍyadipadasya sambandhajñānādeva pravṛttiriti saṃbandhasyaiva pravṛttinimittatayā tādivācyatvasambhava iti pratipannāḥ | ityatra - ityākārakajijñāsāyāṃ satyāt ||
mūle 'prakṛtyarthaprakaratā'mityasyā'panna'miti śeṣaḥ ityamipretyāha-prakāratayā bhāsa- mānamiti | prakāratvāt - ghaṭatvatvasya prakāratvāt ghaṭatvapadottaratvapratyayavācyatvaṃ syādityarthaḥ | dharmamātramiti | dharmatvena vācyatā, na tu tattadrūpeṇa, śattayānantyaprasaṅgāditi vadanti | na ca gurudharme lakṣaṇeti vācyatvaṃ, śaktāveva lāghavādaro na lakṣaṇāyāmityatra vinigamanāviraha iti bhāvāt | vastutastu gauravaṃ nāstyevetyāha vdyaktīti | apare tu 'ghaṭatvatva'mityātrāvidyako dharmaviśeṣastadartho na tu ghaṭetarāsamavetatvetyādirūpo gauravādityāhuḥ | niruktabāvasya tvādyarthatve vārtikakṛtsaṃmatimāha uktaṃ cetyādinā | 'yasya guṇasye'tyatra guṇaśabda upakārakapara ityabhipretyāha viśeṣaṇatayeti | uktaṃ ca kaiyaṭīye 'guṇaśabdena yāvān kaścit parāśrayo bhedako jātyādiḥ sarva iha gṛhyate' iti | 'bhāvā'dityetad vyācaṣṭe āśrayatvādita | 'dravye' ityetad vyācaṣṭe viśeṣya iti | guṇe iti | guṇiparaśuklādibhyo guṇa, guṇaparebhyastu tebhyastad‌vṛtti- jātāvata bodhyam, aṇvādiśabdā nityaṃ guṇiparā iti-tebhyaḥ parimāṇarūpo guṇa eva pratyayaḥ, 'parimāṇaṃ caturvadhamaṇu mahaddīrghaṃ hrasvaṃ ce'tyasya tvaṇvādivṛttirityarthovaseyaḥ |
ghaṭatvatvādaśabde vyaktaya eva tvādavācyā ita pakṣāntaramāha jātivācakānāmiti | ghaṭa- tvādiśabdanāmityarthaḥ | vyaktya eveta | svavṛttitvasaṃbandhena tīsāṃ vācyatāvacchedakatvamiti bodhyam | evaṃ tatra tatra tvādyarthamuktvā śābdabodhamāha tathā ceti | 'ghaṭatva'mityatra prakṛtyarthasya svetarāsamavetatvaviśiṣṭanikhilasvasamavetatvasaṃbandhena prakāratā, pratyayārthe dharme etāvatā sādharaṇa- dharmalābhaḥ, etadabhiprāyeṇaivā'sādhāraṇo dharma' bodha ityavādīt, na tvasādhāraṇadharmatvena śaktistasyerāvṛttitvaviśiṣṭasakalaprakṛtyarthavṛttitvarūpatvena gauravāt | athavā vastuto yo'sādhāraṇo dharmastanniṣṭhādheyatvasaṃbandhena prakṛtyarthānvayastena ghaṭatvamityatra na dravyatvādibhānam | tatsūcanagrayojanako'pīti | tasya - vārtikasyasūcanāyetyarthaḥ | 'api'śabdaḥ pakṣāntaratsa- sūcanāya, yadvetiśabda ityarthasya samuccayāya, 'vārtikasūcanaprayojanaka' ityanenāyaṃ pakṣo vārtikokta ityaprāmāṇyaśaṅkā nirastā | vārtikavyākhyānaṃ bṛhad‌bhūṣaṇoktamāha bavantīti | vācakatvaneti | vācakatāsaṃbandhenetyarthaḥ | pravartante asyārtheṣviti śeṣaḥ | dhātūnāmanekārthatvād bhūdhātuḥ pravṛttyarthakaḥ,
pravṛttiśca prakṛte prayogarūpā | evaṃ ca bhāvaśabdaḥ śabdapratipādaka ityāha bhāvāḥ iti | atra 'sva'śabda ātmavācītyāha svena rūpeṇeti | sa - śabdātmā, teṣāṃ - śabdānām | vārtikasthacaramabhāvapadārthamāha bhāvaḥ pravṛttinimittamiti | nanu śabdasya pravṛttinimittataṃ kathaṃ ? śakye prakārasyaiva tattvādatyāśaṅkāṃ nirasitumāha ayaṃ bhāva ityādinā | tattadvācyaḥ kaścidita | viśeṣarūpeṇānupasthityā nalatvādarūpaviśeṣadharmāvacchinne śktigrahāsaṃbhavena sāmānyarūpeṇaiva
bodho'bhyupeyaḥ, 'idaṃ padaṃ kkacicchaktaṃ padatvāt sādhupadatvā'diti sāmānyato'numityātmaka- śaktigrahasattvāt | nāgarikān pratyabodhakatvāpatteriti | teṣāṃ prāyo viśeṣarūpājñānena, viśeṣarūpeṇa śaktigrahasaṃbhavāt, gāramoṇānāṃ tu viśeṣato jñānasaṃbhavānnābodhakatvāmiti sūcayituṃ 'nāgarikā' nityuktam | tacca viśeṣarūpeṇānabhijñaparam, tena kasyacinnāgarikasya viśeṣarūpeṇa bodhe'pi nāsaṃgatiḥ | evameveti | etatpadavāc ityākāraka eva, na tu tattajjātyādīti, 'anubhava' ityanupajyate | ata eva janmāntarīṇasaṃskāravāśādvaśeṣarūpeṇa śaktigrahe sati viśeṣarūpeṇa bhānaṃ nalādīnāmityapāstam | viśeṣarūpeṇānubhavābhāvenānantasaṃskārakalpane mānābhāvāt, tattadvācaka- padaśravaṇaṃ vinā teṣāṃ viśeṣarūpeṇa smaraṇāpatteśca | ud‌bodhakābhāvānna smaraṇamiti tu na sāmprataṃ, tata eva śābdabodhanirvāhe saṃskārasmaraṇādyorhetutvāsiddhayāpatetarityāhuḥ | ubhayamavacchedakamiti | jātyādi, śabdaścetyubhayaṃ śakyatāvacchedakamityarthaḥ | tatra śabdasya śaktisaṃbandhena śakyatāvaccheda- katvaṃ, jātyādestu yathāyathaṃ samavāyādisaṃbandhena | tathā śaktigrahaḥ - jātyādirūpeṇa śaktigrahaḥ | tathaiva - padaprakāraka eva | śaktagrahavailakṣaṇyādbodhavailakṣaṇyamiti bhāvaḥ | tathā ca - śabdasya śābdabodhaprakāratve ca | śabdopi - apiśabdo jātyādisamuccāyakaḥ | pare tu yeṣāṃ mate śabdaḥ pravṛttinimittaṃ teṣāṃ mate śabdastvādyarthaḥ, yeṣāṃ mate jātyādarūpo'rthaḥ pravṛttinimittaṃ, teṣāṃ mate sa tadartha iti vadanti | prakṛterlakṣaṇayā śabdaparatvaṃ, jātyadiḥ śābdo vā tvādyarthaḥ, janyabodhaprakāratva- saṃbandhena prakṛtyarthasya śabdasya pratyayārtha'nvayaḥ, ata eva śabdaparatvaṃ prakṛteḥ svīkriyate, anyathā ghaṭādeḥ prakṛtyarthasya śabdarūpe pratyayārthe'nvayāsaṃbhavāt, na hi tatra samavāyādeḥ saṃbhavaḥ | 'janyabodhe'tyādyuktasaṃbandhasyāpyasaṃbhavaḥ | śaktisaṃbandhasya saṃbhave'pi paṭatvādau tadasaṃbhava iti śabda- paratvamevādṛtamiti bodhyam | pare tu bhāṣye 'tasya bhāvaḥ ' ityatratyabhāvaśabdasyābhiprāyādyarthaka- tayābhiprāyādiṣvatiprasaṅgaśhakānirāso 'yasya guṇasya bhāvādi'ti vārtikena kṛtaḥ, 'bhāva'śabdena prakṛtasūtre pravṛttinimittabhūto dharmo gṛhyate, na tvabhiprāyādirityabhiprāyāt | tadanantaraṃ ḍitthatvamityādau tvādyanupapattiḥ, ḍitthādiśabdena vyaktereva bodhanādityāśaṅkāṃ vyaktereva viśeṣyatāprakāratvābhyāṃ bhāvasya prakāratāviśiṣṭavyaktirūpatvādyarthasya copagamena samādhāya 'yadvā sarve bhāvāḥ svena 'ityādivārtikena samādhānāntaraṃ kṛtam | tasyāyamarthaḥ-svaśabdo'trātmīyaparaḥ, śabdasya dvividho'rtho vācyo ghaṭādiḥ, pravṛttinimittirapaśca, tadanyatarābhidhāne tvādiḥ | tatrāntyo ghaṭatvamityādau | tatra prakṛtirarthaparaiva, arthasya ghaṭādeḥ pratyayārthe jātyādirūpe'nvayasaṃbhavāt | 'kutvaṃ' ḍitthatva'mityādāvādyaḥ, tatra pratyayārthānupapattyā śabdaparaiva prakṛtiḥ, śabdaparāt kkādiśabdāt vyaktirūpe'rthe pratyayaḥ, evaṃ ca ḍitthatvaḍitthaśabdayoḥ, kutkuśabadayoḥ, padapadaśabdādyoḥ, paryāyatā | ata eva vṛddhisūtre bhāṣye 'kutvaṃ kasmānna bhavati 'coḥkuḥ' 'padasya'
iti, bhatvāt, kathaṃ bhasaṃjñā, ayasmayādīni chandasi' ityuktam | evaṃ ca kutvādipadasya kavargādarevārthaḥ, evaṃ sati 'tasya bhāva' iti sūtrasthatacchabdasya śabdaparatvamāstheyaṃ, tasya ca 'guṇavacane'ti sūtre'nuvṛttasyārthaparatvamāśraṇīyam, evaṃ cārthasya tyāgaupādānaprayuktagauravāpattiśca na | na ha śabdasya karma saṃbhavati, pratīyate vā, 'tasyāpatyam' ityādau tacchabdasyārthaparatāyā eva dṛṣṭatvācceti samūcire | prakṛtyarthapratyayārthayoḥ saṃbandhastvādyantasamudāyaśakyaḥ ||
iti tvādipratyayārthanirṇayaḥ samāptaḥ
prasaṅgasaṃgatyā devatādyarthakataddhitaparatyayārthamāha sāsya devatetyatreti | prathamopāttasyāpya- patyārthakataddhitopekṣā tu tatra pratyayārthasyāpatyarūpasyaikatvāt, tatra ca prakṛtyarthasya janyajanakabhāva- saṃbandhādirūpabhedasaṃbandhena prakāratāyāḥ sugamatvena | devatārthakataddhite tu nānāpakṣasaṃbhavena durūhatvena tadarthanirṇaye ādara iti bodhyam | devatāviśiṣṭamiti | svasvāmibhāvasaṃbandhena svoddeśyakatyāga- karmatvasaṃbandhena vā devatāviśiṣṭamityarthaḥ | ata eva - prakṛtataddhitasyoktārthakatvādeva | tasyaiva - taddhitasyaiva | viṣayārpaṇamiti | asyeti sarvanāmno viśeṣabodhakatayā tadarthe vihatasya taddhitasyāpi tathātvena ko'sau viśeṣa iti jijñāsāyāmāmikṣāpadasāṃnidhyādāmikṣārūpo viśeṣa iti pratīyata ityarthaḥ | kevalāditi | 'kevalādāgreśabdāddevatāvācī taddhitaḥ samuccharan - prādurbhavannanyayuktāgnidaivatyaṃ pratipādayituṃ na kṣama' iti yojanā, itarasāpekṣasya devatātve'sāmarthyā- ttaddhitavṛttireva na syāditi bhāvaḥ | ata evāgniṣomīyādāvāgneyapadavyavahārābhāvād 'āgneyaṃ caturdhā karoti' iti vākyavihitacaturdhākaraṇasya na pravṛttiḥ, dvandve itarasāpekṣasyaiva vyāsajyavṛtti- devatātvāditi sthitaṃ pūrvatantre tṛtīyasya prathamacaraṇe | 'nānyayuktāgnidaivatya'mityādinā kevalāgni- daivatyaṃ pratipādayituṃ kṣama iti sūcitamiti bhavatyupaṣṭambhasaṃgatiḥ | devatāyāmitīti | bṛhad‌bhū- ṣaṇakārāstu 'āgneya' ityādau prakṛtyaiva labdhasya prakṛtyarthasyoddeśyatvamātraṃ yathā caturthyā pratapādyate, evamatrāpi pratyayena devatātvaṃ pradeyaṃ ca pratipādyate, devatātvasya nirūpakatvaṃ pradeye saṃsargaḥ, prakṛtyarthasya tatrādheyatvaṃ saṃsargaḥ, pradeyatvaṃ saṃbandhitvenaiva pratyayārtho na tu dadhitvādyātmaka- viśeṣarūpeṇa, ata evendraṃ dadhītyādi prayogasaṃgatiḥ, ābhikṣāpadena viśeṣākāṅkṣā pūryate, evaṃ cāgreyami yatrāgrivṛttidevatātvanirūpakamiti bodhaḥ | nanvevaṃ 
'taddhitena, caturthyā vā, mantravarṇena vā punaḥ |
devatāsaṃgatistatra durbalaṃ tu paraṃ param ||' iti 
mīmāṃsakoktadaurbalyānupapattiriti cenna, caturthyā vākyāddevatātvasaṃbandhalābhaḥ, pṛthakapado- pasthitahavīrūpasaṃbandhini taddhite tu samānapadaśrutyaiveti taddhitasya valavattvopapatteḥ | athavā pradeyameva pratyayārthaḥ, svavṛttadevatātvanirūpakatvasaṃbandhena prakṛtyarthasya pratyayārthe'nvayaḥ, tāvataivendrāde- rdevatātvalābhaḥ, devatātvaghaṭitasaṃbandhena bodha evānuśāsanatātparyamiti nānuśāsanavirodhaḥ, devatā- tvabodhe eva hi tattātparya, na tu tatprakāraka eva | uktasaṃsargakabodho'pi devatātvaviṣayako bhavatyeveti bhāva ityāhuḥ | devatātvaṃ ca-diveraiśvaryakarmaṇo devateti 'devāttal' iti sūtrabhāṣyā- nmantrakaraṇakahavistyāgajanyasvargādiphalātiriktaphalāśrayatvam, mantrastutyatvaṃ ca anukramaṇikāyā-
mapyuktam yasya vākyaṃ sa ṛṣiryānenocyate sā devateti, ata evaindro mantra endraṃ haviriti dvividhaprayogasiddhiḥ | devatāniṣṭhaṃ phalaṃ cāsyedaṃ phalaṃ bhavatvitīcchātmakapratītireva, ka idaṃ kasmā adādityādimantraprayojyasvāmitvabhāja pratigrahītaryataprasaṅganivṛttiphalakaṃ 'mantrakaraṇake'ti havistvāgaviśeṣaṇam | darśādiśrāddhe mṛtapatnīnāṃ devatātvavāraṇāyoddeśyatvapraveśaḥ | na ca tāsāṃ ttatphalasaṃbandhitvamiti vācyaṃ, 'na yopid‌bhyaḥ pṛthagdadyā'datyādinā tāsāṃ phalasaṃbandhitvabodhanāt, śūdrakartṛkaśrāddhādāvapi namaskārarūpamantrasattvānna tadīyapitrādīnāṃ devatātvānupapattiḥ, 'anumato'sya namaskāro mantra' iti gautamasmṛteriti prāhurityādyanyatra vistaraḥ |
abhedenāpyanvaya iti | bhedasaṃbandhenānvayo na saṃbhavati, tasya bādhitatvāditi sūcanā- yāpiśabdaḥ | athavā svavṛttadevatātvarūpadharmavatvasaṃbandhenānvayanirāsāyāpirupāttaḥ || 53 ||
lakṣamayaiveti | anyādiśabdognyādidevatāyāṃ lākṣaṇika ityarthaḥ | pradeye eveti | 'eva'kāro devatāvyāvṛttaye | nirūḍheti | anāditātparyavatī nirūḍhalakṣaṇetyarthaḥ | tena sarvadā devatātvarūpeṇa pratītinirvāhaḥ | sthitāḥ - mīmāṃsakādigrantheṣu sthitāḥ | tatra śaktiriti | tatra - deye dravye | dravye śaktyabhāve'pi dadhyādipadalabhye tasmin pratyayalabhyāyā devatāyāḥ svaniṣṭhadevatātva- nirūpakatvādisaṃbandhenānvayādviśiṣṭalābhasaṃbhavaḥ | tatpratīteḥ - dravyapratīteḥ | sāmānādhi- karaṇyācceti | tathā cābhedānvayānurodhāttatra pratyayaśaktirāvaśyakīta bhāvaḥ | uktasaṃbandhena devatāyā anvayastu na yukto'nāmārthayo'riti vyutpattivirodhāt, na ca devatāvaśiṣṭadravye prakṛte- nirūḍhalakṣaṇaivāstu kiṃ pradeye pratyayaśaktikalpanayeti vācyam, tathā sati pradeyasya prakṛtyartha- viśeṣyatvānāpatteḥ, dyotyārthasya viśeṣaṇatvaniyamāt, anyathaupagavādāvapi pratyayasya dyotakatvāpatteḥ | anyathā - sāmānādhikaraṇyānurodhāt pradeye śaktyanabhyupagame | na sidhyediti | asya vaiyākaraṇamate' ityādiḥ | devatātvameveti | devatāyāṃ śaktisvīkārāpekṣayāpi lāghavāddevatātve eva śaktikalpanamevocitaṃ, devatātvasya svanirūpakatvasaṃbandhenānvayo bhaviṣyatīti bhāvaḥ | kartṛvadvācya- tvamiti | yathā mīmāṃsakanaye ākhyātasya kartā na vācyo'pi tu lāghavādbhāvanaiva, āśrayasya padāntareṇa lābhāt, kartṛtvaviśiṣṭabodhasya tu bhāvanārūpakartṛtvasya tatrānvayenaiva saṃbhavāt, tathātra pradeye padāntaralabdhe pratyayārdhadevatātvasyānvayenaiva viśiṣṭalābhambhabhavāt pratyayasya pradeye śaktirna sidhyet | na ceṣṭāpattiḥ, 'āmikṣāṃ devatāyuktā'mityanena pradeye taiḥ śaktabodhanāt | atra cet sāmānādhikaraṇyānurodhāt pradeye śaktikalpanaṃ, tarhi ākhyāte'pi kartṛvācakatvaṃ syāddevadattaḥ pacatītyatra kartṛdevadattayoranubhūyamānasāmānādhikaraṇyānurodhāditi-sihāvalokananyāyenedaṃ dūṣaṇaṃ draṣṭavyam | nanvagtyādipadena śaktyaivāgtyādidevatāyā lābhe kiṃ lakṣaṇayetya āha-devatātveti | nirūḍhapadārthamāha-anupapattīti | anvayānupapattijñānābhāve'pi tātparyānupapattijñānasaṃbhavāda- saṃgatibhāśaṅkyāha anādīti | lakṣye yo'nādaprayogastadavacchinnatvamityarthaḥ, prayogasyāvacche- dakatvaṃ ca-karmatāsaṃbandhena ||
mūle eṣaiva digati-eṣaiva rītirityarthaḥ | ityatreti | etatsūtrānuśiṣṭaṇapratthayāntadāṇḍādi-
śabde | praharaṇaviśiṣṭeti | asyārtha ityatrānvayaḥ | arthaḥ - ṇapratyayārthaḥ | tatra prakṛtyarthadaṇḍasya praharaṇaviśiṣṭakrīḍārūpaṇapratyayārthaikadeśapraharaṇe'bhedenānvayaḥ | ekadeśe'nvayamasahamānaḥ khaṇḍaśaḥ praharaṇe krīḍāyāṃ ca śaktimāha-praharaṇakrīḍe iti | atra pakṣe'bhedena daṇḍānvitipraharaṇasya krīḍāyāmāśrayatvasaṃbandhenānvayaḥ | lāghavādāha krīḍāmātramiti | 'mātra'padena praharaṇavyavacchedaḥ | prakṛtyarthadaṇḍādeḥ svaniṣṭhapraharaṇatvanirūpakatvasaṃbandhenānvayaḥ | athavā daṇḍapadasyadaṇḍapraharaṇe nirūḍhalakṣaṇāsnin pakṣe bodhyā | pare tu-praharaṇatvaṃ pratyayārthaḥ, krīḍā ca, praharaṇatveprakṛtyartha- syādheyatvaṃ saṃsargaḥ tasya tu krīḍayāṃ nirūpakatvamityāhuḥ | anena saṃbandhenānvaye tātparyagrāhakaṃ sūtrasthaṃ praharaṇapadamiti tadbhāvaḥ | 'so'sya nivāsa' iti | sughro nivāso - nivāsādhikaraṇa-
bhūto'sya straughnaḥ | atrāpa pūrvavat pakṣatrayamunneyam, strughnābhinnanivāsādhikaraṇasaṃbandhītyādyakāro bodhaḥ | sāsmin paurṇamāsīti | prathamāntādasmin paurṇamāsītyarthe pratyayo bhavatīti tadarthaḥ | itiśabdaḥ saṃjñāyāṃ yathā syādityetadarthaḥ, prakṛtipratyayasamudāyaścet saṃjñārūḍhastadeti yāvat | 'caitro māsa' ityādyudāharaṇam |
prasaṅgāt katipayānuktataddhitārtho nirūpyate | caitryādiśabdo 'nakṣatreṇa yuktaḥ kālaḥ' iti sūtravahitāṇantaprakṛtikaḍībantaḥ, tatra citrādiśabdaḥ svārthatattannakṣatrayuktacandraparaḥ, anyathā sarveṣāṃ nakṣatrāṇāṃ sarvadā sattvena sarvadaivādya caitramaharityādiprayogāpatteḥ | atra yukta eva pratyayārthaḥ na tu kālo'pi, lāghavena vastusata eva tasya pratyayotpattinimittatvaucityāt, aharādipadena tallābha- saṃbhavācca | tathā ca citrāyuktacandrayukteti caivyādiśabdajabodhaḥ, caitrādiśabdottarataddhitasya pūrṇimādhikaraṇādipratyayārthaḥ, atrāpi pūrvavat pakṣatrayamunneyam, tādṛśapaurṇamāsyādhikaraṇamiti bodhaḥ | tattatpaurṇamāsyāṃ citrādiyogāniyame'pi-'caitrī paurṇamāsī'tyādau tattannakṣatrayogayogyataiva vivakṣitā | tattannakṣatrayogābhāve'pi 'caitrī' paurṇamāsī'tyādiprayogāt, 'caitro' 'vaiśākha' ityādiprayogācca | yogyatā ca svasvasamīpanakṣatrānyatarayuktatvarūpā, caitrādiśrāvaṇāntānāṃ māsānāṃ citrādidvandvamadhye kasyacidyogastathāvidhavyavahāre prayojakaḥ, bhādrapadāśvayujostu śatabhiṣagrevatyāditrikanyatamayogaḥ, kārtikādimāghāntānāṃ kṛttikādidvandvānyatarayogaḥ, phālgunasya tu pūrvāphālgunyāditrayānyatamayoga iti vivekaḥ | tathā coktaṃ saṃkarṣaṇakāṇḍe 
'dvandvaiścitrāditārāṇāṃ paripūrṇendusaṃgame |
māsāścaitrādayo jñeyāstrikaiḥ ṣaṣṭhyantasapramāḥ ||' iti
anyatrāpi 'antyopāntyau tribhau' jñeyau phālgunaśca tribho mataḥ |
śeṣā māsā dvibhā jñeyāḥ kṛttikādivyavasthayā ||' iti |
antyopāntyau - bhādrapadāśvayujau | caitrādipadasya śuklapratipadādidarśāntasamudāye rūḍhiḥ svīkiyate'to na yatkiṃcattithiprabhṛtikatriṃśaddinasamudāye caitrapaurṇimāsīghaṭite caitrādipadaprayo- gātaprasaṅgaḥ | kṛṣṇapratipadādipaurṇamāsyantasamudāye tu na rūḍhiḥ, tatra pracuraprasiddhayabhāvāt, paurṇa- māsīghaṭitamāsasya cāndratvena tasya ca 'cāndraḥ śuklādidarśāntaḥ' ityādismṛtibhyaḥ śiṣṭācāra- bāhulyācca śuklādidarśāntasyaiva grahītumucitatvāt | kṛṣṇapratipadādipūrṇimānte tu lākṣaṇiko vyavahāra ityāhuḥ |
māghavācāryāstu cāndrasya śrutismṛtilaṅgadarśanād darśāntatvapūrṇimāntatvayoḥ samo vikalpaḥ, ātharvaṇikāḥ sṛṣṭiprakaraṇe saṃvatsaradakṣiṇottarāyāṇasṛṣṭimāmnāyamāsapakṣasṛṣṭi- mevāmananti | māso vai prajāpatiḥ, tasya kṛṣṇapakṣa eva ratiḥ, śuklaḥ prāṇa iti | tatra kṛṣṇapakṣasya prathamapāṭho māsasya kṛṣṇapratipadāditve liṅgam |
'māsi bhādrapade'ṣṭarmyā kṛṣṇapakṣerddharātrake |
bhavet prajāpaterṛkṣaṃ jayanto nāma sā smṛtā' || iti ||
jayantīprakaraṇasthā smṛtirapi liṅgam | 'aśvayuk‌kṛṣṇapakṣe tu śrāddhaṃ kuryāddine dine' iti smṛtirapi mahālayaprakaraṇasthā śuklapratipadāditve liṅgam | anyathā tasya pakṣasya bhādrapadāntargatatvenāśvayuk‌saṃbandhitvābhāvenāsaṃgatyāpatteḥ, śiṣṭācāraviśeṣo'pi liṅgaṃ, yatidharma-
prakaraṇe' ṛtusandhiṣu vāpayanti' iti smaryate | paraṃ tu yatayaḥ pūrṇimāyāmeva vāpayanti, na darśe, anyathā tatraiva vapanaṃ kuryuḥ, anuṣṭhāne tattadvacanaviśeṣācchiṣṭācārācca vyavasthetyāhuḥ | etanmate darśānte pūṇiṃmānte ca caitrādiśabdarūḍhiriti labhyate ityalamativistareṇa |
gomānityādau 'tadasyāstyasminniti matu'biti matup | tatra saṃbandhī saṃbandha āśrayaṃ cetyubhayaṃ vā pratyayārthaḥ, atrāstīti vartamānakālo vivakṣitaḥ, anyathā gavāmatītatve bhāvitve vā matubantasya sādhutvāpatteḥ, vartamānasattāvatsvārthaparo gavādiśabdaḥ, evaṃ ca vartamānasattāvad‌gosaṃbandhīti bodhaḥ | 'gomānasī'tyādau tu gomatpadasya tatpadavyahāraviṣaye lakṣaṇeti nānupapattiriti kaiyaṭādigrantheṣu sthitam | pāṇininā proktaṃ pāṇinīyam | atra proktatvaṃ nādhyāpanena prakāśitatvam, suśarmaṇādhyā- pite kāṭhake sauśarmaṇamiti prayogāpatteḥ, nāpi kṛtatvaṃ 'kṛte grathe' ityanenaiva siddhāvetasya vaiyarthyāpatteḥ |
nanu vedasya nityatvena tatra kṛtatvābhāvena taittirīyaṃ kāṭhakamityādaprayogānupapattiriti cenna, 'yadyapyartho nityo yā tvasau varṇānupūrvīṃ sā'nityā' iti bhāṣye eva samādhānāt | pralayeṣūccāra- yitṛpuruṣābhāvādānupūrvīvināśena punarutpadya ṛṣayaḥ saṃskārātiśayādvedārthaṃ smṛtvā śabdāracanāṃ vidadhati, arthābhede'pi ānupūrvībhedanibandhana eva keṣucit kāṭhakādivyavahārastataśca 'kaṭhādayo vedānupūrvyāḥ kartāraḥ pravaktāraśca' iti kaiyaṭokteśceti cenna, proktatvaṃ - prathamaprakāśitatvaṃ, tacca svakṛtaparakṛtasādhāraṇam, evaṃ ca mādhureṇa prathamaprakāśitāyāṃ tenākṛtāyāṃ vṛttau mādhu[thu ?]rīti prayogopapataye 'tena proktam' ityasyāvaśyakatvāt | suśarmaṇā kāṭhakantu na prathamaṃ prakāśitamiti nātiprasaṅgaḥ | 'tena proktam' ita sūtrabāṣye eva spaṣṭam, bhāṣye varṇā'nupūrvītyatra varṇaḥ śabdaparastena padānāmameda iti sūcitam | tathā ca smṛtiḥ 
'pratimanvantaraṃ caiṣā śrutiranyā vidhīyate' | iti |
'svayaṃbhūrepa bhagavān vedo gītastvayā purā |
śivādyā ṛṣiparyantāḥ smartāro'sya na kārakāḥ ||
yugānte'ntārhitān vedān setihāsān maharpayaḥ |
lebhire tapasā pūrvamanujñātāḥ svayaṃbhuvā ||' iti 
smṛtidvayaṃ tvarthānityatvādupapannamityanyatra vistaraḥ | pañcānāṃ pūraṇaḥ pañcamaḥ,-atra 'tasya
pūraṇe ḍaṭ' iti ḍaṭ, tasya 'nāntādasaṃkhyādeḥ-' iti maḍāgamaḥ, svaniṣṭhapañcatvasaṃkhyāpūraṇatvaṃ saṃbandhaḥ prakṛtyarthasya pratyayārthe, svaniṣṭhapañcatvasaṃkhyāpūraṇa iti bodhaḥ, pūraṇatvaṃ svāśritatatta- saṃkhyāpūrtinimittatvam, dvitīyādau tṛtīyādivyavahārāpattistu na tasyeṣṭatvāt, ata eva 'samarthaḥ padavidhiḥ' ityadhyāyasyāṣṭama iti vyavahāraḥ kṛtaḥ buddhisthāvadhisāpekṣo'yaṃ vyavahāra iti nātiprasaṅgaḥ | yathā haristathā haraḥ | atra 'prakāravacane thāl' iti thāl‌pratyayaḥ prakāravadarthakaḥ, sāmānādhikaraṇyānurodhādvacanagrahaṇācca | jātīyarādesthālā bādhastu na, 'tathājātīyakaḥ' iti bhāṣyaprayogāt | prakāraśca prakṛte sāmānyasya bhedako viśeṣaḥ, evaṃ ca yadviśeṣadharmavān harista- dviśeṣadharmavān hara iti bodhaḥ, yattad‌bhyāṃ dharmayoraikyāvagatau harisādṛśyaṃ hare paryavasyatī | nanu sādṛśyameva tadartho'stu, evaṃ ca yatsadṛśo haristatsadṛśo hara ityanubhavasiddhaḥ sādṛśyaprakārakaḥ śābdabodha upapadyata iti cenna, 'anyathetarathe'tyādau tadapratīteḥ | 'guḍakalpā drākṣā' 'bahuguḍo'
drākṣā' ityatra 'īṣadasamāptau kalpabdeśyadeśīyaraḥ' 'vibhāṣā supo bahuc purastāttu' iti sūtrābhyāmī- padasamāptaviśiṣṭārthakādete pratyayā bhavanti, bahuc etādṛśasubantāt purastādvibhāṣā bhavatītyarthakābhyāṃ kalpabvāhucau staḥ, vibhāṣāgrahaṇaṃ tiṅante sāvakāśānāṃ kalpabādīnāṃ pravṛttyartham, guḍapadaṃ-guḍasadṛśaparam, pratyayastu dyotakaḥ, ata eva 'guḍakavpā drākṣe'ti strīliṅgaṃ 'bahuguḍo drākṣe'tyatra puṃliṅgaṃ ca nirvahati | svārthikānāṃ prakṛtiliṅgavacanātikramānatikramayoḥ svabhāva- siddhatvāt, sādṛśyasya pratyayārthatve tu bahuguḍo drākṣe'tyatra viśeṣyagatastrīliṅgāpattiḥ, atrepada- samāptatvaṃ sādṛśyaprayojakaṃ mādhuryādi pratīyate, teneṣatsādṛśye pratyayānutpattiḥ, guḍasadṛśī drākṣeti bodhaḥ, upamā gamyate |
pare tu-sādṛśyād drākṣāyāṃ guḍatvāropād‌guḍatvajātyabhāvācceṣadasamāptaṃ guḍatvaṃ drākṣāyāmiti 'guḍa iyaṃ drākṣā' 'guḍakalpā drākṣe'ti prayogaḥ | evaṃ ca guḍatvaprakāska eva bodha ityāhuḥ | mañjūṣākṛnmate tu-upamānaguḍābhinnā drākṣeti bodhaḥ | anye tu-sādṛśyarūpepadasamāptiḥ pratyayārthaḥ, guṇaguṇinorāropitābhedācca drākṣādinā'bedānvayopapattiḥ, athavā sādṛśyavān pratyayārthaḥ, sādṛśyāśrayau vā tadarthau, bahuguḍo drākṣeti prayogānupapattistu na, vibhāṣāsūtre purastādityā- vṛttyāvadhāraṇārthakataśabdena purastādeva sarvaṃ bhavatīti vyākhyānena 'pratayayotpatteḥ prāgdṛṣṭe ye liṅgasaṃkhyete eva staḥ, prayogaśca prāk prakṛte'rityarthāṅgīkāreṇāpādanena pratyayaparatvavāraṇārthastu na saḥ, apavādakatayaiva tatsiddheḥ, na ca purastā'dityatra vibhāṣāpadasaṃbandhāt pūrvatvavikalpe sati paratvamapi syāt, tannivṛttaye'vadhāraṇārthastuśabda iti vācyam, 'udaśvito'nyatarasyām' ityatra paratvamivātra purvatvaṃ na vikalpyate, guṇānāṃ ca parārthatvāditi nyāyāt, kiṃ tu prādhānyāt, pratyaya eva vakalpyata iti bhāṣye evoktatvādatyāhuḥ | pācakaka ityādau 'kutsate' iti sūtreṇa kutsāviśiṣṭārthakāt pācakaśabdāt kan, kutsitapākatvavāniti bodhaḥ, pravṛttinimittakutsāyā asaṃbhave tu-aśvaka ityādau tatsahacaritadharmāntaragatakutsānimittakaḥ pratyayaḥ, 'kutsitaka' ityādau kutsitatvakutsānimittakaḥ pratyayaḥ, asya kutsitatvaṃ na samyak, ki tu śatrubhirdveṣeṇāropitamiti tasya kutsā, athavā niyamena kutsāsaṃbandhapratītyarthaṃ pravṛttinimittakutsāyāmeva pratyayaḥ, kiṃ ca svārthe pūrṇasyaiva kutsānādiyogāt subantādeva kan iti suvantena pratipāditārthasvārthasvārtha-
dravyaliṅgādigatā kutsā kanā pratyāyyate, tatra-'paṇḍitaka' ityatra pravṛttinimittakutsā | 'śuklkaṃ rūpa'mityatra dravyakutsā, 'paśya gāṇḍīvadhanvānaṃ viddhi koravakān striya' ityatra liṅgakutsā | 'putrakaḥ śata'matraikatvasaṃkhyākutsā, śatabharaṇe yad‌duḥkhaṃ tadekasyaiva bharaṇe iti pratīteriti dik | aśva iva aśvako'yam, 'ive pratikṛtau' iti kan, aśvasadṛśābhinno'yam, upamānāśvābhinno'yamiti vā bodhaḥ, antye upamānāśva iti pratyayāntād‌bodhaḥ, tasyedapadārthopameyena sādṛśyamūlakāropitābhedenānvayaḥ' pūjyapratimācitrapaṭadhvajapratimāsu śivādyabhedabuddhireva na tvivārthapratītiriti na kan | spaṣṭaṃ cedaṃ rasanirūpaṇe kāvyaprakāśe-tatra hi 'rāmo'.mita buddhyā gṛhīte naṭe' ityādyuktam, kākatālīya-kāntāmelanākākāgamanaparamuttarapadaṃ tūktapūrvapadārthasamānādhi- karaṇopamānatālapatanaparam uttapadārthe pūrvapadārthasya nāntarbhāvaḥ, sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ pūrvapadārthapratiyogikamiti bodhanāya paraṃ tathoktirato na nairā kāṅkṣyaṃ | kākāgamanarūpapūrvapadārthasyottarapadārthaikadeśasāmānādhikaraṇye antye sāmānādhikaraṇyaghaṭitasaṃbandho
dharmiṇi vā | ata eva sāmarthyasattvāt samāsopapattiḥ, uttarapadamapa svārthasamavetapatanādimātraparaṃ, na tu samānādhikaraṇasyāpi tadarthe'ntarbhāvaḥ, pūrvapadottarapadārthayoḥ sāmānādhikaraṇyarūpa- saṃbandhasaṃbhavāt sāmarthyopapatterita tu na yuktam, sāmānādhikaraṇyasya saṃbandhatvābhāvāt | ata eva 'caitrasya bhrāte'tyādau ṣaṣṭhyarthasaṃbandho na saṃbhavati, ekodaraśāyitvasya tathātve pānthayorekadeśe rātrāvupatiyopari saṃbandhaḥ syāt, tathā ca tatrāpi 'yajñadattasya devadatta' iti prayogaḥ syādityāśaṅkya kālpaniko'vayavāvayavibhāvaḥ ṣaṣṭhyartha iti samāhitam 'anekamanyapadārthe' iti sūtre bhāṣyakaiyadayoḥ | pānthayostu na tādṛśasaṃbandhakalpanā, tādṛśaprayogābhāvāt, etenāta eva jñāpakādasāmarthye'pi samāsa iti nirastam | uktarītyā sāmarthyasaṃbhavāt | kākatālamiti samudāya upameyaparaḥ, evaṃ copamānakākāgamanasamānādhikaraṇopamānatālapatanābhinnaṃ kāntāgamana- samānādhikaraṇo devadattasamāgama iti samāsajanyo bodhaḥ | viśiṣṭayorupameyopamānabhāvabodhe viśeṣaṇayorviśeṣyayośca sa paryavasyati, tattadupameyavācakasamabhivyāhāro niyāmakaḥ, tatastaddhite sati samāsārthastadupamānaṃ kākāgamanasamānādhikaraṇatālanipatanaṃ tatprayojyopamānabhūtakākakartṛka- dalitatālaphalopabhogābhinnaḥ samāsārtho, yadupameyaṃ kāntāgamanasamānādhikaraṇadevadattasamāgamarūpaṃ tatprayojyo devadattakartṛkaḥ kāntāsaṃbhoga iti bodhaḥ | ubhayatrāpyupamānopameyayoḥ sādṛśyamūlako'bhedo'dhyavasitaḥ, 'ghanaśyāma' ityatra yathā ghanaśyāmapadārthaśrīkṛṣṇayoḥ prayojya- prayodakabhāvaḥ samudāyaśaktigamyaḥ | anye tu-pūrvaṃpadaṃ kākāgamanasadṛśalākṣaṇikam, lakṣyārthayośca sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ saṃbandhaḥ, pūrvapadameva vā kākāgamanasadṛśasamānādhikaraṇalākṣaṇikam, evaṃ ca tayorabheda eva saṃbandhaḥ, kākāgamanasadṛśasamānādhikaraṇaṃ kākāgamanasamānādhikaraṇatālapatana- sadṛśamiti samāsajanyo bodhaḥ, tata ivārthe chaḥ, chapratyayāntavṛttivaṣaye kākatālaśabdasya kākatālasamāgamaprayojye nirūḍhalakṣaṇā, prayojyaṃ ca kkacit kākakṛtatālaphalopabhogarūpaṃ, kkacitālakṛtakākavadharūpamupameyavācakatatsamabhivyāhāravaśād‌budhyate prayojyasya sādṛśye pratiyogitvenānvayaḥ, kākatālasamāgamaprayojyasadṛśo devadattakartṛkaḥ kāntāsaṃbhoga itibodhaḥ, upamānopameyabhāvaścākasmādupanatatvenopameyavācakatattatpadasamabhivyāhāreṇa, upamānatāvacchedaka-
tādṛśakākakṛtatālaphalopabhogatādṛśavadhatvādirūpeṇābodhapratyayopamāyāmupamānalopamālaṃkārikāḥsvīkurvanti, vākyalakṣaṇānaṅgīkāre tu tālaśabdasyaivoktaparayojye nirūḍhalakṣaṇetarat pūrvavadityāhuḥ | evamanyatrāpyūhyam | vistastu mañjūṣādito'nusaṃdheya ityalaṃ vistareṇa |
samarthaḥ padavidhirita sūtrādita | tatsaṃbandhyapatyamityarthe pratyaya iti tasyāpatyamityāderarthād 'vastramupagorapatyaṃ caitrasye'tyarthavivakṣāyāmupaguśabdāt pratyayāprāptāvapi 'ṛddhasyaupagavo' 'vajradhara aindra' ityādiprayogavāraṇāya samarthyaparibhāṣopasthitastaddhite'pyāvaśyakīta bhāvaḥ | viśiṣṭa- śaktyaiveti tattatprakṛtyarthatatsaṃbandhaviśiṣṭe samudāyaśaktyaivetyarthaḥ ||
iti taddhitapratyayārthanirṇayaḥ 
avibhāgeneti | saṃkhyātvena rupeṇa sarvasaṃkhyābhānamityarthaḥ | parityaktaviśeṣamiti | asmin pakṣe saṃkhyāttvena saṃkhyāsāmānyasyaiva bhānaṃ na viśeṣasyeti pūrvasmādviśeṣaḥ | yadyapi viśeṣānāliṅgitasāmānyāsattvādidamasaṃbhavi, tathāpi 'vandhyāsuto' 'rāhoḥ śira' itivadvikalpātmā- kasya saṃbhavānna doṣaḥ | taduktam 
'atyantāsatyapi hyarthe jñānaṃ śabdaḥkaroti hi' iti |
matadvaye'pi 'rājapuruṣa' ityatra saṃkhyāvadrājasaṃbandhīti bodhaḥ | jātibhāgasya - saṃkhyātvasya | vyāpārastenaiva rūpeṇa bhānam | bhedāpohena - bhedarūpo yo'poho'tadvyāvṛttistena vartate - tena rūpeṇa bhāsata ityarthaḥ, atadvayāvṛttireva jātitātparyam | agṛhīteti | yathā dūrādrūpatvena rūpa gṛhyate, na tu śaklatvādinā, tathā saṃkhyātvena sāmānyameva bhāsata iti yathākathaṃcidṛṣṭāntaḥ | 'na śuklādi'rityasya śuklatvādineti śeṣaḥ | ata eva rūpatvena rūpeṇa tadbhāne'pi nāsaṃgatiḥ | yadvā 'śuklādi'rityasya śuklatvādiviśiṣṭa ityarthaḥ | itinādhyāhārāpekṣā | pūrvaṃ saṃkhyāviśeṣānugataṃ bhedāpoha rūpaṃ tadityuktam | idānīṃ samastabhedasaṃbhargarūpaṃ taditi viśeṣaḥ, tasyāḥ - abhedaikatvarūpasaṃkhyāyāḥ | vigalitabhedāyāḥ saṃkhyāyā abhedaikatvasaṃkhyāśabdena vyatavahāraḥ | tasyā ekatvena pratīriti matāntaramāha-tasyā iti | ekatvena - ekavatvena | kapiñjaletīti | 'kapiñjalānālabhete'tyatra bahuvacanena traya eva pratipādyante, gaṇanāyāṃ prathamopasthitatvāt, tathā prakṛte abhedaikatvarūpamekatvamekatvena bhāsata ityarthaḥ | tadrūpeṇeti | tritvarūpeṇaivetyarthaḥ | tritvādirūpameva bahutvamityarthaḥ | abhedaikatvasaṃkhyāyā bhānaṃ vṛttau iti mata dūṣayati-vastutastviti | ekatvatvena rūpeṇa tadbhānamatyantānucitameva, kiṃ tu saṃkhyātvena, anyathā 'rājapuruṣa' ityādāvukta- jijñāsānāpatteḥ, iṣṭāpattistu na 'vṛddhimicchato mūlamapi te naṣṭa'miti nyāyāpātāt, 'rājapuruṣa' ityādau tattatpadārthatāvacchedakarūpeṇa ekānekavyaktirvṛttau bhāsate, na tu saṃkhyāvattveneti navyamataṃ samāsaprakaraṇa uktam | brāhmaṇādiśabdādviśeṣadharmaprakārakajijñāsodayo'pi na tasya tacchabda- vācyatānaṅgīkārādidamasaṃgatamiti vācyam | tatra niyamena na jijñāsodayaḥ, atra niyamena tadudayaḥ, evaṃ ca tadupasthāpakāntarāsattve tadupasthitaye vācyatā syādatyāśayāt | vastutastādṛśa- jijñāsābhāve eva nirbharo nātra dūṣaṇe iti bodhyam | vistarastu vṛhad‌bhūṣaṇādāvanusaṃdheyaḥ || 56 ||
iti abhedaikatvasaṅkhyānirūpaṇam
ghaṃ saṃmārṣṭīti | grahaṃ saṃmārṣṭī'tyādau grahān vākyāntaravihatānuddiśya saṃmārgo vidhīyate, tatroddeśyagataṃ viśeṣaṇamavivakṣitamiti sarvagrahasaṃmārgāsiddhiriti mīmāṃsakasiddhāntaḥ | sa vaiyākaraṇairnāśrayituṃ śakyate, 'dhāto'rityādau vyabhicārādityarthaḥ, tatraikatvāvivakṣāyāṃ dhātusamudāyādapi pratyayāpattirita bhāvaḥ | dhātorityatraikatvavivakṣā mīmāṃsāvārtikakṛtāmapi saṃmatetyāha-utpadyeteti | dvandvotpanneti | dvandvasamāsādutpannasubarthasaṃkhyādinā yathā sarve dvandvaghaṭakapadārthā viśeṣyante, evaṃ dhātusamudāyādyadi pratyaya utpadyeta tadā tāvanto dhātvarthāḥ pratyayārthena saṃbadhyerannityarthaḥ | idamupalakṣaṇam samudāyāsyāṅgasaṃjñāyāmaḍādikaṃ tasya syādityapi bodhyam | nanūktavādhakabalena pratyayopāttānuvādyaviśeṣaṇavivakṣāyāmapi pratipādikopāttasya tasyāvavakṣā bādhakābhāvāt syādityāśaṅkāṃ nirasyati ārdhadhātukasyeti | yadvā-'dhātutvaparyāptyadhikaraṇātpratyaya' ityarthāṅgīkāre tatsamudāyasya dhātutvaparyāptyadhi- karaṇatvābhāvānna dātusamudāyātpratyayāpattiriti naikatvavivakṣāyāstatropayoga ityatra āha 'ārddha- dhātukasye'ti | lābho na syāditi | na ca vākyabhedāttallābhaḥ, tathā sati gauravāpatteḥ, evameva
cakārākaraṇe lāghavātiśayācca | paśunā yajetetivadita | yathā vākyāntareṇa prāptaṃ yāgamanūdya paśogatamekatvaṃ vivakṣitamata eva tatrānekapaśukaraṇakayāgānuṣṭānānnādṛṣṭasiddhirevaṃ vidheyagataṃ viśeṣaṇaṃ vivakṣitameveti niyamo mīmāṃsāyāṃ sthitaḥ, so'pi śābdikamate nāstītyarthaḥ | tatra hetumāha radābhyāmiti | nanu tatra cakārakaraṇādvākyabheda eva sūtrakṛto'bhimata iti labhyate iti nakāradvayalābho nirābādha evetyata āha ād‌guṇa ityeti | vaiyarthyāpatteriti | yadi vidheyaviśeṣaṇaṃ niyamena vivakṣitaṃ syāt, tadā pūrvaparayoḥ sthāninorekasyaiva guṇādyādeśasya siddhyā ekagrahaṇavaiyarthyaṃ spaṣṭam, tasmāt pāṇinorvivakṣaiveti niyamo nābhimata iti bhāvaḥ | evamavivakṣaivetyapi na niyamaḥ, 'upeyivā'nityatra sūtra upetyasyāvivakṣāyā nañprabhṛteśca vivakṣāyā darśanādityapi bodhyam | tantrātantre - vivakṣāvivakṣe | 'tātparyātātparye'iti yāvat | pratipādanecchā vivakṣā, saṃkhyāvivakṣāvivakṣayorhetuḥ lakṣyānurodhādita | parśvekatvādhikaraṇeti | tatra hi samānapratya- yopāttatvapratyāsattyā vibhaktyarthe saṃkhyāyāstadarthakārake evānvayaḥ, tatrāruṇādhikaraṇanyāyena vibhaktyarthasya prathamataḥ kriyānvayaḥ, paśvāt prakṛtyarthena, itthaṃ ca paśvekatvobhayakaraṇako yāga iti bodhaḥ, kiṃ gatamekatvamityekatvasyāśrayākāṅkṣāyāmekaḥ paśuriti pāṣṇiko mānaso bodha ityuktam | grahaikatvavivakṣāyāṃ saṃmārgoddaśena grahaikatvayorvidhāne vākyabhedāpattiḥ | tathāhi 'prājāpatyā na grahā' ityādivākyavihitagrahānuvādena saṃmārgavidhāne grahaṃ saṃmāṣṭiṃ taṃ caikamiti vacanavyaktau vākyabhedaḥ, spaṣṭa eva | uktaṃ hi vākyapadīye 
'grahāstvanyatra vihitā bhinnasaṃkhyāḥ pṛthak pṛthak |
prājāpatyā navetyevamādibhedasamanvitāḥ ||
aṅgatvena pratītānāṃ saṃmārge tvaṅgināṃ punaḥ |
nirdeśaṃ prati yā saṃkhyā sā kathaṃ syādvivakṣitā ||' iti |
pare tu utpattivākye navatvādisaṃkhyāśravaṇādvidhevagatatvena navādiśabdopāttatvena tasyāḥ
saṃkhyāyā vivakṣaṇāt tatsaṃkhyāvaiśiṣṭyenaivānubhūtagrahāṇāṃ grahaśabdena tat‌saṃkhyāviśiṣṭānāmeva smṛtau teṣu vibhaktyaikatvabodhane upakramasthasaṃkhyābādhalakṣaṇo vākyabhedaḥ syāt | mīmāṃsakoktavākya- bhedaścāyameveti spaṣṭametaddharikārikāvyākhyāne helārājīye |
yadvotpattivākyagatasaṃkhyayaitadvākyagatasaṃkhyāyā virodhādvākyadvayasya bhedāpattiḥ, na ca grahānu- vādena saṃmārgo vidheyaḥ, saṃmṛjyamānagrahānuvādenaikatvaṃ vidhīyatāmiti vācyam, ekapadopasthāpita- yoruddeśyavidheyabhāvenānvayāsaṃbhavāt, saṃmārgasyoddeśyakoṭau praveśāsaṃbhavācca | vākyabhedatā- davasthyāccetyāhuḥ || 57 ||
anantayakārādyāpatteriti | 'sudhyupāsya' ityādau 'iko yaṇavī'ti sūtreṇānantayakārādikaṃ syādityarthaḥ | pūrvoktāmekagrahaṇavaiyarthyāpattimuddharati ekaḥ pūrveta | ādeśabhedeti | idaṃ sūtra- kārāśayavarṇanamātram | vastutatvanayoḥ pūlayoḥ kaṭaṃ kurvatyatra dvitvaviśiṣṭaphalasaṃbandhye- kakaṭakaraṇapratītariva 'pūrvaparayo'riti dvandvanirdeśenobhayoḥ sthāne ekavākyatayaikaguṇādividhānānnā- nekaguṇādiprasaṅgaḥ, vidheyagatasaṃkhyāyā avivakṣāyāṃ hetvābhāvāditi pratyākhyātameva 'ekaḥ pūrvaparayoḥ' iti sūtrabhāṣye vistareṇeti spaṣṭaṃ bhāṣyakaiyaṭādigranthavidām | vākyabhedādiriti | vākyabhedastu
varṇitaḥ | ādipadena 'graha'miti dvitīyayā grahasya saṃskāryatvalakṣaṇaṃ prādhānyaṃ pratīyate, evaṃ ca 'pratipradhānaṃ guṇāvṛtti'riti nyāyena, vinigamanābhāvena ca somāvasekanirasanaprayojana- kasaṃmārgasya sakalagrahairapekṣaṇācca sarvatra saṃmārgasidvyā vivakṣāphalābhāvarūpahetuparigrahaḥ, paśvādi- viśeṣaṇavat, kriyāṅgāvābhāvena tadvaikalyaprayuktakriyāvaikalyaprasaktirapi neti bhāvaḥ | jaimi yācāryeṇāpi 'ekatvayuktamekasya śrutisaṃyogāt' iti pūrvapakṣānantaraṃ 'sarveṣāṃ lakṣaṇatvādaviśiṣṭaṃ lakṣaṇa'miti siddhāntasūtraṃ vyaraci | tadarthastu-sarveṣāṃ saṃmārgaḥ kāryaḥ grahatvajāterdravyalakṣaṇatvād‌- dravyabodhakatvād dravyasya lakṣaṇaṃ dravyabodhanaṃ yata iti śabdena bodhyamānajātyā vyaktirākṣiṣyata ityaviśeṣāt sarvavyaktibodhanamiti paryavasito'rthaḥ | anākaram - ākaragranthāsiddhaṃ, tadviruddhaṃ vā | nanu grahaikatvavaddhātorityatraikatvasya 'ārdhadhātukasyeṅvalāde'rityādau valāditvāderapyavivakṣā syādityāśaṅkyāha ekatvaviśiṣṭamiti | coddiśyeti | tathā ca tatraikatvādiviśiṣṭānuvāda- sāmarthyādekatvāvivakṣāsaṃbhava iti bhāvaḥ || 58 ||
nanvekatvavivakṣāyāmekasmādeva dhātoḥ pratyayaḥ syānna tu dhātvantarādita cenna, sarveṣāṃ pratyekamekatvādvinigamanāviraheṇa vyaktyantarebhyo'pi pratyayotpattisiddhisaṃbhava ityāśayāt, ukta- rītyā grahaikatvavivakṣāyā yathā saṃbhavastathoktam |
pare tu dhātutvaparyāptyadhikaraṇāt pratyaya ityarthenaiveṣṭasiddhisaṃbhave ekatvavivakṣāyā na prayojanam, kiṃ caivamekasmādeva kasmācciddhātereva pratyayaḥ syānna dhātvantarāt, 'sarveṣā'mityādikaṃ tu na yuktam, tathā satyekaṃ brahmaṇaṃ bhojayedityādāvapyanekabrahmaṇabhojanāpatterityāhuḥ | vistaratu samarthasūtrādisthavivaraṇādāvanusaṃdheyaḥ | nanu vidheyagatasaṃkhyāvivakṣāyāṃ kiṃ mānamiti cedabhāhu- rmīmāṃsakāḥ saṃkhyāyāḥ samānapratyayopāttatvapratyāsattyā kārake evānvayaḥ, na tu prakṛtyarthe,
vibhaktyarthasya cāruṇādhikaraṇanyāyena kriyāviśeṣaṇatvam | evaṃ ca paśvekatvobhayakaraṇako yāga iti bodhaḥ, 'ekaḥ paśu'riti tu pāṣṇiṃko mānasaḥ saḥ, evaṃ caikatvasya śabdenaiva yāgāṅgatvāvagamāt tadvivakṣāvaśyakīti | tadapare na kṣamante yataḥ pratyayārthasya prakṛtyarthe evānvaye sādhutva na tvanyatrānvaye, kiṃ ca svādisūtrasya 'karmaṇi dvitīyā' ityādibhiḥ 'dvayekayoḥ 'ityādibhiścaika- vākyatve ekasmin karmaṇi dvitīyaikavacanamityādyarthe'nyatraikatvasyānvaye'sādhutvaṃ spaṣṭameva | api ca bhinnapadopātakriyānvayāpekṣayā samānapadopāttatvena prakṛtyarthe evānvayasyaucityaṃ, tadanvitasyaiva kriyākāṅkṣādarśanāt | nanu 'svaujasamauṭ ' ityādyuktasūtratrayasyaikavākyatve'pi naikatvādeḥ karmādau viśeṣaṇatvamucitaṃ, kiṃ tu vidheyatayā pradhāne eva, guṇānāṃ ca parārthatvāditi nyāyāt, tathā ca prātipadikāt karmaṇi ekatve ca dvitīyaikavacanamityādyākāraka eva vākyārthabodho na tadākāra iti karmatvasya kārakānvaye na kiṃcid‌bādhakamiti cenna | evamapa pratyāsattinyāyena parasparasaṃbaddhārthabodha eva tattadanuśāsanena tayīḥ sādhutvabodhanaṃ, vibhaktyarthasya ca prakṛtyarthe viśeṣaṇatvameveti sthitaṃ mañjīṣādau | tasmāt paśunetāyadāvekatvaviśiṣṭapaśuvidhānādekatvavivakṣeti yuktaṃ | viśiṣṭavidhānādeva vākyabhedāprasakteḥ | viśiṣṭasya śrutyāṅgatvabodhanāt paśutvādi- viśeṣaṇavaikalye ivaikatvavaikalye'pi viśiṣṭānanuṣṭhānādyāgavaiguṇyamapi siddham |
jaiminināpi 'tatraikatvamayajñāṅgamarthasya guṇabhūtatvāt' iti pūrvapakṣe 'śabdavadupalabhyate
tadāgame hi dṛśyate tasya jñānaṃ yathānyeṣām' iti siddhāntasūtraṃ praṇītam, śabdavat - śabdābodhyaṃ yathā bhavati tathopalabhyate, ekatvaṃ hi yatastadāgame - ekavacanāgame, dṛśyate tasyāṅgatvajñānaṃ yathānyeṣāṃ - viśeṣaṇānāṃ śabdopasthitānāṃ paśutvalauhityādināmiti tadarthaḥ | evaṃ ca jaiminerapi saṃkhyāviśiṣṭabodha eva tātparyam | harirapyāha 
'yajeta paśune'tyatra saṃskārasyāpi saṃbhave |
yayā jātistathaikatvaṃ sādhanatvena gamyate ||' iti |
'na kevalā prakṛti'riti nyāyādvibhakteḥ śabdasaṃskārārthatvasaṃbhave'pi jāteriva tasyāḥ kriyā- sādhanatvaṃ, viśiṣṭasyaivopasthiterityartha iti helārājaḥ | api ca saṃkhyāyā amūrtatvāt sākṣāt kriyāsādhanatvāsaṃbhavāt paramparayā kriyānvayātsādhanatvakalpanāpekṣayoktayuktibhiḥ prakṛtyarthānvayasyaiva kalpanocitā, āruṇyasya tu prakṛtyarthatayāgatyā paraṃparayā krayākaraṇatvamucitam, evaṃ ca vidheyaviśeṣaṇaṃ sarvaṃ vivakṣitam,avidhavakṣānimittavirahāt, kiṃ caikenaiva paśunā yāgasiddhau paśvantarālambhanaṃ vyarthaṃ tathāpi madhye paśvantarālambhane prayogavidhyavagataprayogaprāśubhāvabādhaḥ syāt, uktaṃ hi vākyapadīye 
'pratyāśrayaṃ samāptāyāṃ jātāvekena cetkriya |
paśunā na prakalpeta na syādetatprakalpanā ||
ekena tu prasiddhāyāṃ kriyāyāṃ yadi saṃbhavāt |
pasvantaramupādeyamupādānamanarthakam ||
yathaivāhitagarbhāyāṃ garbhādhānamanarthakam |
tathaikena prasiddhāyāṃ paśvantaramanarthakam ||' iti |
vidheyagatasaṃkhyāvivakṣāṃ varṇayantītyatra vistaraḥ ||
iti saṃkyāvivakṣānirūpaṇaṃ

ktvāpratyayāderiti | yadyapi mūle 'tumādaya' ityuktaṃ tathāpi ktvāpratyaye bahuvaktavyatvena prādhānyadyotanāya 'ktvāpratyayāde'rityuktam | vārtikādityartha iti | bhāṣyakārāḥ-'tumarthe' iti sūtre 'tumartho bhāva' iti vyavasthāpyāgre 'tumartha' ityasyānuvṛttimabhyupagamya--'avyayakṛto bhāve' iti pratyācakṣāṇāḥ-ktvādīn bhāva evābhyupayanti | paraṃ tu ktvātumunāderasattvabhūta eva bhāvo'rthaḥ, pāka ityādāviva liṅgasaṃkhyānanubhavāt, tathā ca dhātvarthānuvādaka eva tumunādiriti paryavasitam | kā gatiriti | ktvāpratyayasya bhāvāmātrārthakatvāṅgīkāre samānakartṛkatvādestadarthatvāsaṃbhavāttadupādānavaiyarthyāpattiratastatkriyākartṛtvāśrayaḥ ktvāpratyayārtho vācyaḥ, ata eva 'roṭikāḥ paktvāhaṃ bhokṣye' ityatrāsmacchabdāttṛtīyā na, ktvāpratyayena pākakarturabhidhānāt | pākakarturbhojanādau svaniṣṭhakartṛtānirūpakatāsaṃbandhenānvayāt samānakartṛtvalābhaḥ | athavākartṛkatvaṃ tadartho'stvityāśayenāyaṃ praśnaḥ, samānakartṛkātvāderdyotyatvaṃ vyarthaṃ, tasya prakṛtyarthe viśeṣaṇatvasya viśeṣyatvasyacānanubhavāt | samānakartṛkatvādikaṃ tu vastusat ktvādeḥ sādhutvaniyāmakaṃ syādata āha-ayaṃ bhāva iti | ayaṃ - vakṣyamāṇaḥ | sarvasiddhā - sarvānubhavasiddhā | anyathā - viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyabhāvamantarāpyekavākyatve | bhuṅkte vrajatīti | 'ekakartṛkā vartamānakālikī bhujikriyā' 'ekakartṛkā vartamānakālikī vrajanakriye'ti kriyādvayamukhyaviśeṣyatāko bodho'nubhavasiddho, na tu tatraikamukhyaviśeṣyatāśālitvarūpamekavākyatvamanubhavasiddham | 'bhuktvā vrajatī'ttrāpi bhojanamekakartṛkaṃ vrajanaṃ ceti samūhālambanātmakabodhāṅgīkāre ekavākyatvānupapattiḥ, karturvācyatvāṅgīkāre bhojanakartuḥ ktvārthasyākhyātārthakartaryabhedānvayasaṃbhavena 'pacati bhavatī'tivadekavākyatā yadyapi saṃbhavati 'caitreṇa bhuktvā gamyate' ityatrāpi bhojanakartrabhinnacaitrakartṛgamanakarmeti bodhābhyupagamāt | ata eva samānakartṛtvaghaṭakābhedāṃśe na śaktiḥ, niṣphavalatvāt, uktarītyāpi tatraikavākyatvasaṃbhavo'sti, tathāpi katurvācyatvaṃ sūtrānna labhyate iti granthakṛtaiva vakṣyata iti saṃtoṣṭavyam |
samānakartṛkatvapūrvottarakālikatvavadanyadapi saṃsargatayā bhāsamānaṃ ktvādyotyaṃ, samānakartṛkatvayorityāderupalakṣaṇatvādityāśayenāha-janyatvamityādi | pare tu--upalakṣaṇatve mānābhāvāt tanmānāntaragamyatvānna ktvādyotyamityāhuḥ | bhojanajaniketi | 'bhoktuṃ pacatī'tyatra bhojanapacikriyayorjanyajanakabhāvasaṃbandhastumundyotyaḥ, tumunṇvulau kriyāyāṃ kriyārthāyā'mityukteḥ | tādarthya ca janyajanakabhāvarūpamiti bhāvaḥ | tādarthyavat samānakratṛkatvamapi kriyayoḥ saṃsargatayā bhāsate, śabdaśaktisvabhāvādata eva vibhinnakartṛkakriyādvayatātparyako bhoktuṃ pacatīti na prayogaḥ | pare tūddeśyatvanirūpakatvarūpaṃ tādarthyaṃ tumunādidyotyaṃ saṃsargatayā bhāsate, tacca tadicchādhīnecchāviṣayatvam, 'tumunṇvulau-' ityatra 'bhaviṣyati' ityanuvṛtterbhaviṣyattvamapi tad‌ dyotyam, tathā ca bhaviṣyadbhojanoddeśyakā bhojanakartṛkaḥ pāka iti bodhaḥ, tumuṃstu na kartari, 'avyayakṛto bhāve' ityukteḥ--'kartari kṛ'dityasyātrāpravṛtteḥ | anirdiṣṭārthakeṣvetatpravṛtteḥ, etena-'tumunākhyātārthakartroḥ parasparamabhedānvayenaiva vibhinnakartṛkakriyādvayatātparyakatādṛśaprayogāpattyabhāvena samānakartṛkatvaṃ na saṃbandha' ityapāstam | bhoktuṃ pacyate bhoktuṃ sthīyata ityādau tadasaṃbhavācca | na ca tatrāpi 'devadattene'tyadhyāhārāt tṛtīyārthakartaryabhedānvayānnānupapattiriti vācyam, adhyāhāra vināpi vākyārthabodhasyānubhavasiddhatvāditi dik | tumunṇvulau-' iti sūtravihito ṇvul tu kartari kṛt' iti kartaryeva, saṃbandhaśca kriyayoḥ pūrvokta eva, evaṃ ca 'kṛṣṇaṃ darśako yātī'ti vākyāt kṛṣṇakarmakabhaviṣyaddarśanoddeśyakaṃ darśanakarttṛkaṃ vartamānakālikaṃ yānamiti bodha iti vadanti | yuktaṃ caitat,--kṛṣṇadarśanoddeśyakayānena mārgasthajanāntaradarśanotpāde'pi 'janaṃ draṣṭuṃyātī'ti prayogavirahāt, 'kṛṣṇaṃ draṣṭuṃ yāti na yavanādika'miti prayogācca | ata eva navyatārkikāḥ--kartṛkartṛkatvaviśiṣṭecchāviṣayatvasya tumunvācyatāmanumanyante | ata eva caturthīkārake vyutpattivāda'tra cāhartumiti tumunantārthaḥ, svasyamabhivyāhṛtakriyānvayisvārthaprayojanakatvānvayyāharaṇamityuktam | svārthaprayojanakatvasya vācyatāṃ vinānvayotyuktarasaṃgatiḥ | anyathā tasya vṛttyaniyāmakatayā tatsaṃbandhāvacchinnapratiyogitāyā aprasiddhyā tathāvidhapratiyogitākayavalādigocaradarśanābhāvasya bodhayitumaśaktatvāt | uktasaṃbandhena yavānādigocaradarśanaviśiṣṭagamanāśrayatvābhāvaevaprathamāntārtho bodhyata iti tu na | 'kṛṣṇaṃ draṣṭuṃ sthīyate na yavalādika'mityādau tadasaṃbhavāditi dik |
śābdikanaye tu sakalaviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭamukhyaviśeṣyakriyāyā eva sarvatra bodhasvīkārānnānupapattiriti dhyeyam | 'bhoktumicchati'tyādau 'samānakartṛkeṣu' iti tumun, icchārtheṣu tumunprakṛtibhūtadhātvarthasamānakartṛkārthapratipādakeṣūpapadeṣu dhātostumunniti tadarthaḥ | kriyārthakriyopapadābhāvārthamidam, tumunnarthaviṣayitvaviśiṣṭakarttṛkartṛkatvasaṃbandhena prakṛtyarthasyecchāyāmanvaye tāt‌paryagrāhakastumun, paramate tu tasya tadvācyaṃ parakartṛkabhojanecchāyāṃ bhoktumicchatīti prayogavāraṇāya samānakartṛkatvasyāpi saṃbandhamadhyaniveśaḥ | 'śakroti bhoktuṃ' 'dhṛṣṇoti paṭhituṃ' 'jānāti paṭhitu'mityādau 'śakadhṛpajñāglāghaṭarabhalabhakramasahārhāstyartheṣu tumun'-iti tumun | ādye sāmarthyāparaparyāśaktirūpadhātvarthe prakṛtyarthasya prayojakatvasaṃbandhastumunūdyotyaḥ, dvitīye śaṅkārāhityarūpaprāgalbhyaghaṭakaśaṅkāyā viṣayitvaṃ saṃbandhaḥ, prayojakatvaṃ voktaprāgalbhye saṃbandhaḥ, tṛtīye viṣayitvaṃ saṃbandhaḥ spaṭa eva | atrāpi sarvatra samānakartṛkatvamapi saṃbandhe niveśyam, atra yaḥ saṃbandhaḥ sa paramate tadvācyaḥ, atra śakrāterarthe samabhivyāhṛtatattadvātvartho'ntarbhāvanīyaḥ, tathā hi galādhaḥsaṃyogānukūlakriyāyāṃ vyāpāre śaktau ca śaknātervṛttiḥ | tatra śaktau śaktyākhyā, itaratra lakṣaṇā, tatra tumun‌prakṛtirbhujyādistātparyagrāhakaḥ | etādṛśe'rthe vṛttiḥ karmapratyayasthale eva na tu kartṛptyayasthale, prayojanavirahāt, tatra prayojyatvasambandhena śaktyanvitavyāpārasya janyatvasambandhenoktakriyāyāmanvayaḥ, tathāvidhakriyārupaphalaśālitvādodanādeḥ karmatvaṃ, evañca'śakyate odano bhoktu'mityataḥ śaktiprayojyavyāpārajanyagalādhaḥsaṃyogānukūlaudanāśrayikā kriyeti bodhaḥ | tārkikanaye tu-tathāvidhakriyāśrayatāvānodana iti bodhaḥ | śaktiśca mīmāṃsakanaye padārthāntaraṃ, nyāyanaye tattatkāraṇānukūlāsādhāraṇakāraṇakalāpaḥ, yathā prakṛte pradīptajaṭharānalasaṃyogādiḥ |
asti bhavati vidyate vā bhoktum | atrānnirūpakarttṛkarttṛkasattāyāṃ janakatvasaṃbandhena tumun‌prakṛtyarthasyānvayaḥ, saṃbhavarūpe vāstyādyarthe viṣayitvasaṃbandhenānvayaḥ | paryāpto bhoktuṃ pravīṇo bhoktuṃ kuśalo bhoktum | atra 'paryāptivacaneṣvalamartheṣu' iti tumun | paryāptiḥ - pūrṇatā tadvāciṣu sāmarthyāpratipādakeṣūpapadeṣu tumun bhavatīti tadarthaḥ | atra prayojakatvaṃ, viṣayitvaṃ vā prakṛtyarthasya kauśalyādiniṣṭhaṃ tumun‌dyotyam | paryāpto bhoktumityādau paryāptādiśabdaḥ samarthārthaḥ | atra prathamapadānupādāne alaṃ bhuktvetyatrātiprasaṅgaḥ | atrālaṃśabdo''laṃ kaṃsaṃ hantuṃ kṛṣṇaḥ' ityatra 'paryāptivacana iti prāptiḥ, dvitīyapadānupādāne paryāptaṃ bhuṅkte ityatrātiprasaṅgaḥ, tadupādāne tu naiva doṣaḥ, adanīyagataprabhūtatārūpaparyāptereva tatra gamyamānatvāt na tu sāmarthyasyetyalam |
kālaḥ samayo velā vā bhoktum | atra 'kālasamayavelāsu tumun' iti tumun | atra kālaniṣṭho yogyatālakṣaṇo bhojanasya saṃbandhastumun‌dyotyaḥ, atra praiṣādigrahaṇānuvartanāt praiṣādivivakṣāyāmeva tumun, nānuvāde,--bhūtāni kālaḥ pacatī'tyādāvityalamativistareṇa | prakṛtamanusarāmi--'bhuktvā tṛpta' iti vākyajaṃ bodhamāha--bhojanajanyeti | bhojanajanyā yā tṛptistadāśraya ityarthaḥ, 'gatyarthākarmake'ti sūtreṇa kartari ktpratyayavidhānāt | nanvānantaryasamānakartṛkatvayoreva tatra saṃsargatayā bhāvamastu, kiṃ guruśarīrajanyatvasya saṃsargatayā bhālasvīkāreṇetyata āha-ata eveti | janyatvasya saṃsargatayā bhānasvīkārādevetyarthaḥ | na prayoga iti | bhojanajanyatṛptau jalapānajanyatvābhāvāditi bhāvaḥ | samānakartṛkatvamapīti | 'bhuktvā vrajatī'tyatra vrajane bhojanādhikaraṇavṛttitvasya bhāne kriyayāradhikaraṇaniṣṭhaikyasyāpyarthasiddhatvāt, samānādhikaraṇyasya tadaikyaṃ vinānupapadyamānatvāt | 'matprasūtimanārādhya prajeti tvāṃ śaśāpa sā' ityatra 'sthitasye'tyadhyāhārāt sthitikriyāsamānakartṛkatvādikamastīti ktvāpratyayasya nānupapattiḥ |
pūrvottarabhāva iti | pūrvakālavṛttitvottarakālavṛttitvasamudāya ityarthaḥ, etasya saṃsargatayā bhānānabhyupagame vrajanasya pūrvakālavṛttitve'pi bhuktvā vrajatīti prayogāpattiḥ, sāmānādhikaraṇyasya tathātvānabhyupagame bhinnakartṛkatathāvidhakriyāyugalābhiprāyeṇa tadāpattirityubhayoḥ saṃsargatvānusaraṇam | pūrvottarabhāve'vyavadhānāṃśo'pi niveśyaḥ | anyathā pūrvavarṣotpannaṃ śvaśuragṛhe bhojanabhetadvarṣādhikaraṇakaṃ vrajanaṃ cādāya 'śvaśuragṛhe bhuktvā vrajatī'ti prayogāpattiḥ | avyavadhānaṃ tu tātparyavasāt kkacid daṇḍena kkacinmuhūrtena kkacit praharādinā yathāyartha grāhyam | daṇḍādinā vyavadhānaṃ ca svaprāgabhāvādhikaraṇadaṇḍādiprāgabhāvanādhikaraṇatvaṃ, svaṃ - samabhivyāhṛtavrajanādikriyāviśeṣaḥ | tathā cāvyavahitapūrvatvaṃ kālaniṣṭhaṃ tādṛśāvyavadhānaviśiṣṭatatkriyāprāgabhāvādhikaraṇatvaṃ, bhojanottaraṃ daṇḍottaraṃ gamanasthale tu bhojanādhikaraṇakṣaṇādestatra vrajanapāgabhāvādhikaraṇadaṇḍaprāgabhāvādhikaraṇatayā na tathāvidhaprayogaprahaṅgaḥ | viśeṣyadalaniveśād‌brajanottaraṃ bhojanatātparyeṇa na 'bhokte' iti prayogaprasaṅgaḥ | vastutastad‌vrajanakāle bhojalādhikaraṇasamaya dhvaṃsādhikaraṇatvarūpottaratvavirahānnoktātiprasaṅga iti tannaniveśyam | 'adya bhuktvā śvo gantā' 'mārgaśīrṣe udvāhya paupe kāśīṃ gata' ityādirathale dinamāsādinoktavyavadhānābhāvo vivakṣita iti nānupapattiḥ | prāgabhāvānabhyupagame tu--svādhikaraṇakṣaṇotpattikadhvaṃsapratiyogidaṇḍādhikaraṇakotpattikadhvaṃsāpratiyogitvavadhānaṃ, tadadhikaraṇasamayotpattikadhvaṃsapratiyogitvaṃ tatpūrvaṃtvam | paraṃ tu prakṛte etanniveśānāvaśyakatā pūrvavadavaseyā | kālaviśeṣeti | tatkālāvacchinnasvādhikaraṇaniṣṭhātyantābhāvapratiyogitānavacchedakamukhavyādānatvādimat sāmānādhikaraṇyarūpaṃ vyāpyatvamityarthaḥ | kālaviśeṣāvacchinneti | atra--kālaḥ kṣaṇādirūpaḥ sūkṣmakālo grāhyaḥ, nātaḥ sthūlakālāvacchinnavyāpyatvamādāya mukhavyādānottaraṃ mukhavyādānaśūnyakālikasvāpavyattayabhiprāyeṇa tatkāle mukhaṃ vyādāya svapitīti prayogāpattiḥ | ata eva nirantarādhyayānaśālinyevāyamadhītya tiṣṭhatīti prayogo, na tu kadācidadhyayanakartarīti vyavasthopapadyate | tatrāpyuktavyāpyatvasya saṃbandhatayā bhānānnirantarādhyayanavirahipuruṣasthitau adhyayanavyāpyatvābhāvāt tādṛśaprayogāprasaṅgāt | paraṃ tu svasamānādhikaraṇetyatra svaśabdena tatpuruṣoyanidrādiśūnyakālikasthitisānānyaṃ grāhyaṃ,-nāta uktadoṣatādavasthyam | 'mukhaṃvyādāye'tyatra tu svapadena tu tattat‌svāpavyaktirgrāhyā, nāto vyādānaśūnyakāle tādṛśaprayogāpattirvyādānakāle tādṛśaprayogopapattiśceti pratibhāti | sthādidhāturapi tādṛśasthititvādyavacchinnaparaḥ, nāta uddeśyatāvacchedakāvacchedena tādṛśavyāpyatvānvayānupapattiḥ | yadyapyetādṛśavyāpyatvabhānenaivātiprasaṅganirāse sāmānādhikaraṇyasya saṃbandhatābhyupagamo nirarthakastathāpi sāmānādhikaraṇyaghaṭitāvyāptiḥ saṃbandhatayā svīkāryeti sūcanāya--'sāmānadhikaraṇyaṃ ca saṃsarga' ityuktam | athavoktavyāptau sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ na praveśyamiti yathāśrutameva ramaṇīyam | athavā kālikasaṃbandhāvacchinnaṃ vyāpyatvaṃ saṃbandha ityeva kālaviśeṣāvacchinnetyādigranthatātparyārthaḥ | evaṃ ca kādācitkādhyayanakartṛpuruṣasthiterapi puruṣāntarīyādhyayananirūpitoktavyāpyatāśrayatayā 'dhītya tiṣṭhatī'ti prayogāpattirataḥ sāmānādhikaraṇyasyāpi saṃbandhatvapuraskāraḥ | svasamānādhikaraṇeti-svaśabdaḥ pūrvoktārthaka evato na pūrvoktadoṣāvakāśaḥ, tattatkālaviśeṣagarbhasvasamānakālikatvena sāmānādhikaraṇyasahitena tādṛśasthititvādyavacchedenānvayo lāghavāt svīkartumucita iti cet, yadīyasvāpakāle bahudhā mukhavyādānaṃ yadīyastitikāle tathādhyayanaṃ tatraiva tādṛśaprayoga iṣyate, na tu kādācitkaṃyadīyasvāpādisamaye mukhavyādānādikaṃ tatretyato'tiprasaṅgavāraṇāyoktavyāpyatvabhānānusaraṇamiti pratibhāti |
yadi tūktasthale'pi sa prayogaḥ prāmāṇikastadedamevocitaṃ, prakṛtagranthasyāpyatraiva tātparyamiti vāstu | adhyayanamukhavyādānottaramapi svāpasthitasattvena pūrvakālatvādeḥ saṃbandhasya sattvānna ktvaḥ sādhutvānupapattiḥ | spaṣṭaṃ ceda' prakṛtasūtrabhāṣye | na ca 'bhuñjāne bhuṅkte' iti prayogavāraṇāya samabhivyāhṛtatatphalaviśeṣānukūlayāvatkriyāvadhikapūrvatvasyaiva grāhyatayā kathamuktarītyā 'mukhaṃvyādāya svapitī'ti prayoga iti vācyam, tatra phalabhedakalpanayā mukhavyādānottarasukhaviśeṣādiphalakavyāpārasāmānyapūrvakatvasya mukhavyādāne saṃbhavenādoṣāt | 'bhuktvā bhuṅkte' iti prayogasyanusāritvāt 'madhyāhna bhuktvā rātrau bhuṅkte' iti prayogastviṣyata evoktarītyopapadyate ca | tātparyasattve ekabhojanasthale'pi 'odanaṃ bhuktvā roṭikāṃ bhuṅkte' ityāhuḥ | atra kecana naiyāyikāḥ-prāgabhāvāvacchinnakālavṛttitvarūpaṃ pūrvakālikatvaṃ kartā ca ktvārthaḥ, tatra pūrvakālavṛttitve pūrvakāle taddhaṭakaprāgabhāve eva śaktirvā, kartari tu śaktinirūḍhalakṣaṇā vā | prathamapakṣe pūrvakālavṛttitvasyāśrayatvasaṃbandhena prakṛtyarthabhojanādāvanvayaḥ | dvitīyapakṣe-pūrvakālasya kālikasaṃbandhavacchinnādheyatvaṃ saṃsargaḥ,tṛtīyakalpe-pratiyogitvasaṃbandhena prāgabhāve prakāratā, prakṛtyarthabhojanādeḥ ktvārthe kartari svaniṣṭhakartṛtvanirūpakatvasaṃbandhena viśeṣaṇatvam | tadarthakartari vrajatītyākhyātopāttakartṛtvasyātrprayatayā viśeṣaṇatvamiti vrajanaprāgabhāvavācchinnakālavṛttibhojanānukūlakṛtimān vrajanakartṛtvāśraya iti bodhaḥ | 'pakttvā bhujyate odana' ityatra tūktavidhāpākakartuḥ ktvāntārthasya bhujadhātvarthavyāpāre svaniṣṭhakartṛkatānirūpakatvasaṃbandhenānvayaḥ | tathā ca bhojanaprāgabhāvavāvacchinnakālavṛttipākakartṛkartṛkavyāpārajanyagalādhaḥsaṃyogānukūlakriyāśraya odana iti bodhaḥ | yatkriyāvyaktau ktvārthakartuḥ sākṣāt kartṛtvādvārā vānvayastatkriyāvyaktereva ktvārthaprāgabhāve'nvaya iti vyutpat‌tyaṅgīkārānnānyakartṛkavrajanapūrvakālavṛttikasvakartṛkavrajanottarakālikabhojanakartari bhuktvā vrajatīti prayogaprasaṅgaḥ | 'caitro bhuktvā vrajatī'tyatra tu caitre ākhyātārthānvitaktvārthakarturabhedena viśeṣaṇatvaṃ viśeṣyatvaṃ vā | 'caitrasya bhuktvā vrajana'mityatra caitrakartṛkaṃ yadbajanapūrvakālikabhojanaṃ tatkartṛ kartṛkaṃ vrajanamiti bodhaḥ | vrajane tathāvidhabhojanakartuḥ svaniṣṭhakartṛtānirūpakatvaṃ saṃbandhaḥ | 'caitro na bhuktvā vrajatī'tyatra nañā vartamānabrajanakartṛtvaviśiṣṭavrajanapūrvakālikabhojanakartṛbhedaścaitre pratyāyyate | tena yadā ca tādṛśaprayoganirvāhaḥ, yena puruṣeṇa kadāpi bhojanottaraṃ gamanādikaṃ na kṛtaṃ tattātparyako'yaṃ bhuktvā na vrajatīti prayogo'pyupapadyate | etena-caitrābhinna uktavidhabhojanakartari vartamānavrajanakartṛtvābhāvabodho'bhyupeyata iti nirastam | uktasthale'nirvāhāditi digityāhuḥ |
aparaṃ punastārkikā evaṃ varṇayāṃcakruḥ--prāgabhāvaśakyaḥ karttā lakṣyaḥ, prāgabhāve prakṛtyarthasya svādhikaraṇakālavṛttitvasaṃbandhena kālikasambandhāvacchinnavṛttitvasaṃbandhena vānvayaḥ | kartrekadeśakṛtautvamukūlatvaviṣayitvarūpasaṃbandhena prakṛtyarthānvitaprāgabhāvasyottarakriyāyāṃ pratiyogitāviśeṣaṇatvam | bhojanakartuḥ svaniṣṭhakartṛtānirūpakatvasaṃbandha uttarakriyāyāṃ viśeṣaṇatāghaṭakaḥ, tāvataiva samānakartṛkatvalābhaḥ | itthaṃ ca caitro bhuktvā vrajatīti vākyāt caitrābhinnabhojanakartṛkartṛkabhojanādhikaraṇakālavṛttirbhojanavṛttirvā yaḥ prāgabhāvastatpratiyogi vrajanaṃ tatkartṛtvavāṃścetra iti bodhaḥ | prathamāntapadāsamabhivyāhāre tu vrajanakartṛtvamukhyaviśeṣyaka eva bodhaḥ | caitreṇa bhuktvā vrajanamityādau tu vrajanādimukhyaviśeṣyaka eva saḥ | ktvārthadvayasyaikakriyāvyaktāvevānvaya iti niyamāttat puruṣāntarakartṛkavrajanaprākṛkālikasvakartṛkavrajanottarakālikabhojanakartari tādṛk‌prayogāpattiḥ | caitro na bhuktvā vrajatītyatra tathāvidhavrajanakartṛtvābhāvavāṃścaitra iti bodha iti | ityādimatāni nāśrayituṃ yogyānītyāha--evañceti | evaṃ - pūrvakālatvādīnāṃ saṃsargatvapuraskāre | anyalabhyatvād‌vṛttiṃ vinaiva saṃsargavidhayā bhānasaṃbhavāt | anyathānvayamātre śaktisvīkārāpatteriti bhāvaḥ | ādyapakṣe vrajanapūrvakālikabhojanakartetyādikarmeṇa bodhasvīkāre'nubhavasiddhasya pūrvakriyāprakārakottarakriyāviśeṣyakabodhasyānupapattirūpamapi dūṣaṇaṃ draṣṭvyam | ata eva maṇikāraiḥ--pūrvakālatvasya vācyatvapakṣe pūrvakriyāviśeṣyaka eva bodhaḥ syānna tūttarakriyāviśeṣyakastathā cānubhavaviredha iti dūṣaṇamabhidhāyānantaramasya ktvāvācyatvamaṅgīkṛtam | na ca pūrvakālatvasya vācyatve'pi tasya nirūpakatāsaṃbandhenottarakriyāyāmanvayopagamenānubhavasiddho bodho nirvahatyevetyāśaṅkya na bhuktvā vrajatītyatra nirūpakatvasya bodhayitumaśkyatvam | āśrayatvasaṃbandhāvacchinnapratiyogitākābhāvabodhābhyupagame bhojanottaraṃ gamanakartaryapi nañghaṭitaprayogāpattiḥ | ānantaryasya tu āśrayatvasaṃbandhāvacchinnapratiyogitākābhāvaḥ pratyāyayitu śakyata evetyapi tairabhyadhāyi | anyalabhyatvādevottarakālatvādestatsaṃbandhatayaiva bhānamaṅgīkṛtaṃ navyatārkikairapītyabhāṇi--gurucaraṇaiścaṣake ityalaṃ pallavitena | nanvevaṃ 'darśapūrṇamāsābhyāmiṣṭvā somena yajete'ti vākyena darśapūrṇamāsottarakālaḥ somo vidhīyata iti mīmāṃsakasiddhāntāsaṃgatiḥ, kālasyāvācyatvāditi cenna | uttarakālatvasaṃndhena darśapūrmamāsavaiśiṣṭyaṃ vidhīyata iti svīkāreṇākṣateḥ | tāvatāpi tatsiddhāntaphalasya darśapūrṇamāsābhyāṃ prāk somānuṣṭhāne pratyavāyasya siddheḥ darśādhikaraṇe somākaraṇe ca tatsiddheḥ | prakṛtyartheti | prakṛtyarthaśca kriyā ceti dvandvaḥ | athavā ktvāpratyayasya samabhivyāhṛtapratyayasya ca prakṛtirgrāhyeti nāsaṃgatiḥ | svaśabdenopātteti | iha kasmānna bhavati pūrvaṃ bhuṅkte paścād‌vrajatīti pūrvapakṣasya samādānamuktaṃ--svaśabdenetyādi | dyotye iti | anena pūrvakālatvottarakālatvāderdyotakatvameva ktvādīnāṃ na tu vācakatvamiti spaṣṭameva sūcyate tena saṃsargatayaiva kaiyaṭābhimatamiti bhāvaḥ | parimalakārādimataṃ dūṣayati--yatviti | sūtrāditi | samānakartṛkayoriti dharmaparaṃ, dvivacanaṃ tu kriyādvayarūpāśrayagatadvitvāropanimittakamiti bhāvaḥ | athavā dharmiparameva tat, kriyārūpadharmiṇo dhātuta eva lābhāt, samānakartṛkatve kartaryeva vā śaktiḥ sūtrabalāt kalpyata iti bhāvaḥ | astuvānubhavānurodhāt kartṛkartṛkatveśaktiḥ, bhuktavāghrajatītyatra bhojanasya kartṛtve'nvayaḥ | tasyāvācyatve bādhakamāha--anyatheti | tṛtīyeti | ktvāpratyayena karturanabhidhānāditi bhāvaḥ | abhihite tṛtīyā na bhavatīti prasajyapratiṣedhamabhyupetyāha--na ceti | kriyākartṛrabidhānepīti | kriyākartṛtvaviśi,ṭasyābhidhāne'pītyarthaḥ | kriyākartṛtvaśakteriti paryavasito'rthaḥ | kriyādvayasyaika eva karteti yathāśrutasyāsaṃgatyāpatteḥ | evamagranimasyāpi kriyākarturityasyārthaḥ | paryudāsāśrayaṇāt | prasajyapratiṣedhe tu abhihite na bhavatītyarthāt tiṅā kartṛtvaśakterabhidhānānna mayeti tṛtīyāpattiḥ | paryudāsāśrayaṇe tu abhihitabhinne bhavatītyarthādanabhihatabhujikriyānirūpitakartṛtvaśaktimādāya tṛtīyāpattirdṛḍhaiveti bhāvaḥ | lāghavāt paryudāsāśrayaṇameva yuktam | prasajyapratiṣedhe hi asamarthasamāso vākyabhedaścāśrayaṇīyaḥ syāt | tadāśrayaṇe phalamapyāha--kriyādhikaraṇasyeta?| prasīdatyasminnityadhikaraṇe 'halaśca' iti ghañ pratyayaḥ | kriyādhikaraṇasyeti karturitivad‌ vyākhyeyam | bhāṣye spaṣṭamiti | asyānabhihite ityādiḥ | vastutastu śakteḥ sādhanatvamiti siddhāntapakṣe ghañā prakṛtyarthanirūpitaśakterabidhānena tasyāṃ vācyāyāṃ saptamīniṣedhepi āsanakriyānirūpitādhikaraṇatvaśaktau vācyāyāṃ niṣethābhāvena prāsāde āste ityatra saptamīṃsiddhirniṣpratyūhaiva | spaṣṭaṃ cedaṃ kaiyaṭodyote | evaṃ cātra lāghavādeva paryudāsāśrayaṇamiti tattvam | upasaṃharati--tasmāditi | sāmānakartṛkayoriti | samāna ekaḥ kartā yayoḥ kriyayoriti kriyārūpe'nyapadārthe bahuvrīhiḥ | pūrvakāla ityādi | pūrvaḥ kālo'syeti bahuvrīhiḥ | tathā ca samānakartṛkayoḥ kriyārūpārthayormadhye pūrvakālavṛttyarthābhidhāyino dhātoḥ ktvāpratyayo bhavatītyeva sūtrārthaḥ | evaṃ ca karturvācyatvaṃ kathamapi na labhyata iti bhāvaḥ | nanu pūrvoktarītyā tallabhyata evetyāśaṅkyāha--anyatheti | pūrvoktakrameṇa karturvācyatve vivakṣite | samānakartari iti samānapadopādānaṃ ktvārthakartuḥ samabhivyāhṛtakriyākartaryabhedānvayaḥ pūrvoktarītyeti bodhanāyeti bodhyam | nyāsaḥ syāt - nyāsaḥ kṛtaḥ syāt | api ca pūrvoktarītyā vākyārthavarṇanaṃ na mabhañjasamiti bodhyam | nanu tṛtīyādarśanameva pūrvoktārthakalpanadvārā karturvācyatve mānaṃbhaviṣyatītyāśaṅkya tṛtīyādarśanasyānyathāsiddhamāha--tṛtīyāpādanaṃ tvityādinā | pradhānetarayoriti--kārikādvayaṃ prāgvyākhyātam | ime ca harikārike 'svādumi ṇamul' iti sūtrasthabhāṣyārthānuvādinyāviti spaṣṭaṃ śekharādau | nanu krimityevaṃ varṇyate tṛtīyāvāraṇārtha ktvādeḥ kartari śaktirevābhyupagamyatāmata eva 'samānakartṛkayoḥ' iti sūtrakāroktiḥ saprayojanā | anyathā ḍajajanyatvādisaṃbandhavadetasyāpi saṃbandhasyānubhavabalādeva lābhā bhaviṣyatīti vyarthā soktirityāśaṅkyāha--anyatheti | prakārāntareṇeti | kartari śaktyaṅgīkāre'pi karmaṇā'nabhidhānādodanapadād dvītīyāpattirdurvāraiva | karmaṇyapi śaktisvīkāre tu gauravaṃ mānābhāvaśca | uktarītyanusaraṇe tu tiṅntopāttapradhānabhujikriyānirūpitakarmaśaktestiṅābhidhānād‌guṇabhūtapacikriyānirūpitāyāstasyā anabhidhāne'pi na dvitīyāpattiriti samuditatātparyārthaḥ | api ca vācakatvapakṣe ktvādīnāṃ janyatvavyāpyatvādyanekārthakatvāpattiḥ | janyatvādīnāṃ vācyatve pratyayārthatayāḥ teṣāṃ viśeṣyatvāpattyā 'bhuktvā bhuktvā vrajatī'tyatra 'ābhīkṣṇye dve vācye' iti dvitvānāpattiḥ | kriyāprādhānyavihāt | viśeṣaṇībhūtakriyāmādāya tatpravṛttyaṅgīkāre tu pācaka ityādāvapi tadāpattiḥ | evaṃ dhātusaṃbandhādhikārabādhāpattiśca | na ca pratyayārthadvārakaḥ saṃbandho'kṣata eveti bodhyam | 'lunīhi-lunīhītyevāyaṃ lunātī'tyādau dhātvārthayoḥ sākṣāt saṃbandhadarśanena 'yathāvidhyanuprayoga' ityādau saṃbandhapadā'nuvṛtteḥ sākṣāt saṃbandhatātparyakatāyā eva nirṇayena parasparasaṃbandhasya tadarthakatvābhāvaniścayāt | gauravādapi na parasparāsaṃbandhasya tadarthakatvamiti tad dhvanayannāha-āstāṃ vistara iti | pare tu samānakartṛkayordhātvarthayormadhye pūrvakālaviśiṣṭārthabodhakād dhāto katveti sūtrārthāt pūrvakālaḥ ktvāprakṛtyarthe prakāraḥ ānantaryaṃ ca dhātvantarārthe tādṛśaprakṛtyarthasya prakāratāghaṭakaḥ saṃbandhaḥ | ata eva bhāṣye--pūrvaṃ bhuṅkte paścādvajatītyatra ktvāpratyayaśaṅkā--pūrvaśabdo'tra pūrvakālārtho na tu dhātostatra vṛttiratyarthakena svaśabdonoktatvādityanena samāhitā | uktārthānāmiti nyāyaparatvaṃ tu na tasya, tathā satīha kasmānna bhavati--āsyate bhoktumiti śaṅkāparabhāṣyasyāsaṃgatyāpatteḥ | tatrāpi tumunā taduddeśyakatvarūpasaṃbandhaṃ bodhayatā''sane pūrvakālatvasya dyotitatvena tadaprāpteḥ | tatra pūrvakālānavagane tu kathaṃ śaṅkā padaṃ nidadhyāt | vāsarūpanyāyena laṭā sādhanamityanyat | 'yadā tvāseḥ ktvā bhavati tadā bhujestumunna bhaviti anabhidhānā'diti kaiyaṭagranthaḥ | 'pūrvabhuktvā paścād‌vrajatī'tyatra tva'nyebhyo bhoktubhyaḥ pūrvaṃ bhuktvānyebhyo vrajitṛbhyaḥ paścād‌vrajatītyartha' iti sādhanāntarakriyāvadhikameva pūrvakālatvaṃ pratīyata iti nāsya sūtrasya viṣayaḥ, kintu 'vibhāṣāgre prathame'tyasyeti spaṣṭamatra bhāṣye | 'darśapūrṇamāsābhyā'mityatra tvānantaryasaṃbandhena pūrvakālikadarśādivaiśiṣṭyaṃ some pratīyata iti na tatra doṣaḥ | evaṃ ca 'pūrvakāla' iti karmadhārayaḥ, evaṃ ca ṣaṣṭhīvat ktvādirapi samānakartṛkatvādisaṃbandhavācaka iti vaktuṃ śakyamiti prāhuḥ | atra pūrvakālatvaṃ prāgabhāvavacchinnakālatvaṃ, prāgabhāve vrajanakriyāyāstattadvayktitvāvacchinnapratiyogitākatvasaṃbandhenānvayaḥ | ānantarya ca-tadadhikaraṇasamayadhvaṃsādhikaraṇamuhūrtādidhvaṃsānadhikaraṇatvaviśiṣṭatadadhikaraṇasamayadhvaṃsādhikaraṇakṣaṇavṛttitvam, tena na pūrvoktadoṣaḥ, 'īśvaro jñātvā tiṣṭhatī'tyatreśvarajñānadhvaṃsāprasiddhayā tad‌dhvaṃso nānantarye niveśitaḥ | idamatra bodyam--'snātvā bhuktvā pītvā vrajatī'tyatra vrajanakriyāvadhikapūrvakālatvamādāyaiva sarvatra ktvā, na tu bhojanādikriyāvadhikaṃ tadādāya | tathā sati 'tasyāpatyam' ityādāvekatvāderiva prakṛte 'samānakartṛrakayo'riti dvivacanopāttasya dvitvasyāvivakṣāyāmapyamīṣāṃ brahmaṇānāṃ pūrvaṃ ānīyatāmityukte sarvapūrva eva ānīyate, na tu yatkiñcitpūrvo madhyamastathātrāpi snātereva ktvāpratyayaḥ syānna tu bhujyādeḥ | evaṃ ca-snānakriyāyāḥ pānakriyāyāśca vrajanakriyāyāmevānvayo, na tu snānādikriyāyā bhojanādau, ata eva tatra 'bhuktāvā pītvā vrajatī'ti viparītaprayogo'pyupapadyata iti spaṣṭaṃ bhāṣye iti śivam || 60 ||
|| iti tumunktvādyarthanirṇayaḥ || 
siddhānta iti | prāguktaprakṛtipratyayādyarthavicārasyāvāstavatvaṃ dhvanayituṃ sphoṭasyaiva vācakatvamiti siddhāntaniṣkarṣamāhetyarthaḥ | sphuṭati prakāśate'rtho'smāt sphoṭa iti vyutpattyārthabodhakaśabdaḥ sphoṭa iti paryavasannam | anarthakamiti | itarasphoṭānāmapi svīkṛtatayā'vyāvartakatvāt | durarthakam - anabhihitavarṇasphoṭādivyāvṛttipratipādakam | vicārāṅgabhūtāṃ vipratipattimāha--sādhuśabdeti | saṃśayīyayāvatkoṭyupasthāpakaṃ vākyaṃ vipratipattiḥ na tu saṃśayajanakavākyam, śabdasya navyatārkikaiḥ saṃśayajanakatvānaṅgīkārāt, tadaṅgīkartṛmate tu tadeva vipratipattipadapratipādyamastu, sādhuśabdānantargatānāṃ ghakāradīnāṃ ghaṭātiśabdaghaṭakānāṃ parairapi vācakatvāṅgīkārāt sādhuśabdānrgatatvāvacchedena bodhakatvasādhane na vaiyākaraṇamarte'śataḥ siddhasādhanam, arthāntaraṃ vā'niṣedhasādhane'śatobādhaḥ, ataḥ sādhuśabdāntargatatvaṃ pakṣatāvacchedakamanusṛtam | anyeṣāṃ - prācīnanaiyāyikādīnām | nanu sādhuśabdāghaṭakeṣu varṇeṣu bodhakatvavyāvṛttisiddhāvapi vācakatvavyāvṛttirna siddhetyata āha-bodhakā vācakā iti | bodhakapadādvācakatvalābhaḥ | kathamityāśaṅkāyāāha--tasyaiveti | bodhakatvasyaivetyarthaḥ | 'śaktiḥ padārthāntara'miti mate'pi bodhakatvānyathānupapattyā kalpyamānā śaktibodhakatvasamaniyateti sādhuśabdānantargateṣu bodhakatvābhāve siddhe śaktyabhāvo'pyarthāt sidhyatīti bodhyam | paramataṃ dūṣayitumupanyasyati-yattviti | teṣāṃ - tivādyādeśānāṃ, bahutvena - lādirūpādeśyapekṣayā bahutvena | śattyānantyeti | śaktatāvacchedakatvamapi bahvīṣvādeśaniṣṭhānupūrvīṣu kalpanīyamityapi dūṣaṇamatra pakṣe bodhyam | sthānino vācakatvapakṣe'pi śābdabodhapūrvamanekasmṛtikalpanāyāṃ lāghavānakāśa ityato dūṣaṇāntaramāha--edhāṃcakre iti | atra 'āmaḥ' ityanena liṭo luk | abhāvarūpasyeti,-hetugarbhaviśeṣaṇaṃ, yato'bhāvarūpatvamatastasya bādhakatvāsaṃbhavaḥ, tasyāḥ bhāvaniṣṭhatvādabhāvena kkacidapi bodhajananāditi bhāvaḥ | 'edhāṃcakra' ityatrānuprayujyamānadhātūttaraśrūyamāṇatiḍādeśenaiva kālakārakādyanugama iti spaṣṭaṃ nivandheṣu | tadarthakālānvayasya yadyapi anuprayujyamānakṛñādyarthe eva tathāpyabhedena tadarthānvyyedyarthe'pi tasyāthikaṃ bhānaṃ saṃbhavatyeva | spaṣṭaṃ cedaṃ prakṛtasūtrabhāṣye | ata eva 'kṛñ cānuprayujyate' iti sūtrekaumudyāṃ--'teṣāṃ kriyāsāmānyavācitvādām‌prakṛtīnāṃ viśeṣavācitvāttadarthayorabhedānvaya' ityuktam | abhedānvayaścettham ,--edhādayastattatphalānukūlavyāpārārthakāḥ, kṛñ-utpattyanukūlavyāpārārthaḥ | bhvasto--sattānukūlavyāpārābhidhāyinau | utpattyādikamāmprakṛtyarthaphalaniṣṭhameva, pratyāsatteḥ | itthaṃ caidhāṃcakra ityata ekakartṛkavṛddhyānukūlavyāpārābhinnaparokṣānadyatanabhūtotpattyanukūlavyāpāra iti bodha ityāhuḥ | evamādeśānāṃ vācakatvapakṣe'pi nātra doṣa ityato 'brahme'tyasyānusaraṇam | ādipadena 'vāri' 'paca' 'yā' 'apācī' tyādeḥ saṃgrahaḥ, na ca tatrāpi'yaḥ śiṣyata' iti nyāyena prakṛtireva luptapratyayārthiketi vācyam, prakṛtestatra śattayādikalpanāpekṣayā'nyatrabodhakatvena klṛptasya pratyayasya ramaraṇakalpanaiva yuktetyāśayāt | rathānino vācakatāpakṣe śaktatāvacchedakalāghavamapyastītyāha--latvasyeti | jātirūpatayeti | dantavāyusaṃyogajanyatāvacchedakatayānugatapratyayanirvāhakatayā ca tasya jātitvamiti bhāvaḥ | paramate tvānupūrvīṇāṃ śaktatāvacchedikānāṃ nānātvaṃ spaṣṭameva |
yuttyantaramapyāha-avyabhīti | kartrādyarthajñānādhikaraṇe tajjñānapūrvasamaye niyamena lakārajñānasattvādityarthaḥ | tathā ca na svamate vyatirekavyabhicāraḥ | paramate tu sostītyāha--ādeśānāmiti | bhinnatayā - parasparapratiyogikabhedavattayā,-anekatayeti yāvat | na caivaṃ 'bhū-la' ityato'pi bodhaḥ syāditi vācyam, samabhivyāhāraviśeṣasya kāraṇatvābhyupagamāt | anyathā tava mate'pi bhū-ati ityāderapi bodhapattiḥ | ādeśijñānavidhurāṇāṃ bodhastu śaktibhramādupapadyate, apabhraṃśeṣu tathākalpanāditi bhāvaḥ | athaivaṃ śatrādyantasthale kartṛkarmabodho na syāt, lakārasya tava mate kṛtikarmatvamātravācakatvāditi cenna | lakārasya kṛtyādimātravācakatvepi śatrādeḥ kartrādivācakatvābhyupagamāt, 'kartari kṛ'dityanuśāsanāt | nāmārthayopabhedānvayānurodhāttatra lakārasyaiva kartrādyartha iti na ko'pi doṣaḥ | evaṃ ca śrūyamāṇamātravṛttivācakatāvirahād varṇayanti sma-ādeśānāṃ vācakatve 'rāma' ityādo visarga ekatvasya vācaka iti vyavasthā bhavati, smṛtānāṃ vācakatve'vyavasthā syāt | tathā hi 'rāma' ityatra visargeṇa kiṃ suḥ ? kiṃ vā ruḥ ? kiṃ vā siḥ smartavyaḥ ? pāṇinīyaiḥ suḥ, anyai ruḥ, kālāpatantrābhijñaiḥ siḥ, yastu sakalatantrābhijñastasya vinigamanāviraheṇa pratibandhāt kasyāpi smaraṇaṃ na syāditi | tattu na yuktam | anekalipijñānasattve'pi yathā nam śabde ekakāle na śabdopasthitipratibandhastathā sakalavyākaraṇābhijñasya nādeśismṛtibandhaḥ | api tu sarveṣāṃ smṛtistayācārthopasthitiravilakṣaṇaivetyabhyupagamamācca | ata eva ghaṭakalaśādyanekaparyāyebhyopyavilakṣaṇabodha upapadyate'tonyathā vyācaṣṭe-tathāhītyādinā | bodhāditi | tathā ca bodhānāpattirevātrāvyavasthāpadārtha iti bhāvaḥ | mānābhāvāditi | tato bodhodayena tatra vācakatvasyābādhitatvāditi bhāvaḥ | vinigamanāvirahātsarveṣāmapi sthānināṃ vācakatvaṃ bhavatītyāśaṅkāyāmāha-sarveṣāmiti | vyabhicāraścāstyeveti | tathā ca yadbhayādādeśānāṃ vācakatvapakṣaparityāgastau doṣau sthānino vācakatvapakṣepi duṣpariharāvevetyarthaḥ | ādeśānāṃ tivādīnāmādhikyāttatpakṣāpekṣayātra pakṣe bahu gauravamityanuśayāna āha-tathā ceti | mukhya iti | śaktigrahe jananīye śaktigrāhakāntarānapekṣatvarūpamukyātvam | vyākaraṇādīnāṃ tu na tathātvam,-tathāha-'tasyāpatya'mityādināṇādipratyayasya śaktigrahe'patyādiśabdaśaktigraho'pekṣyate, sa cecchabdāntareṇa, tasyāpi śabdāntareṇaiva śaktigraha ityabhyupagame'navasthāprasaṅgo'to vyavahārasyāpatyādiśabdaśaktigrahasaṃpādanadvārā'pekṣaṇāditi bhāvaḥ | ta eva - tiṅādaya eva | evakāreṇa syānibhūtalakārādivyavacchedaḥ | nanu vyavahārāttiḍaṣu gṛhyamāṇāpi śaktirlāghavādādeśini vyavasthāpyate, na tu tiṅṣu, gauravāt, yathā vyavahārādvākye gṛhītāpi śaktirlāghavāttattatpade kalpyate tadvat | anekasmaraṇakalpanāgauravaṃ tu phalamukhatvānna doṣāyetyāśaṅkamāno mūle sādhakāntaramāha-taddhetunyāyata iti | taddhetunyāyapadayoḥ karmadhārayaḥ | vyākhyāyāṃ-kiṃ ceti | asya 'sādhakāntaramāhe'ti śeṣaḥ | pūrvaphakkikāsthasyānuṣaṅge vā bodhyaḥ | nyāyaṃ vyutpādayati-lakārasyeti | tādṛśe bodhe - ekakartṛkavartamānakālikaviklityanukūlo vyāpāra iti bodha ityarthaḥ | pacatīti samabhivyāhāropīti | avyavahitottaratvasaṃbandhena pac‌dhātuviśiṣṭa ativiśiṣṭa pacatiśabdarūpaḥ samabhivyāhāra ityarthaḥ | vācakatvaśaktiḥ - vācakatvarūpā śaktirityarthaḥ | samabhivyāhārasya vācakatve siddhepi sādhuśabdāntargatavarṇatvāvacchedena na tatsiddhamityāśaṅkāmapākartumāha--tathā cetyādinā | sidhyatīti | vinigamanāvirahātsamabhivyāhṛtavarṇānāmapi vācakatvaṃ sidhyatītyarthaḥ | anye tu-vinigamanāvirahātpratyekāvṛtteḥ samudāyāvṛttitvācca samabhivyāhṛtavarṇānāmeva vācakatvam | na ca dvitvavat pratyekāvṛttitve'pi samudāyavṛttitvasaṃbhavaḥ, dvitvasyāpi pratyekaṃ vṛttitvasvīkārādityāhuḥ | 'peṭākṛtastu rathāṅgeṣu pratyekaṃ gamanānukūlaśaktidhirahe'pi tatsamudāyarūpe rathe tatsadbhāva ityarthavatsūtre-'dṛṣṭo hyatadarthena guṇena guṇino'rthabhāvaḥ, 'yathā tarhi rathāṅgāni vihṛtāni pratyekaṃta vrajikriyāṃ pratyasamarthāni tatsamudāyaśca rathaḥ samartha iti rathāṅgavaditi bhāṣyakārai rabhihitaṃ, tadvat prakṛte'pi saṃbhava iti vinigamanaḥviraheṇa samabhivyāhṛtavarṇānāmapi vācakatvamityeva yuktamityāhuḥ | samabhivyāhṛtatāvadvarṇavṛttirvācakatetyabhyupagamānna taddhaṭakaikaikādivarṇāt tattadarthabodhapattiriti bodhyam | kartṛbhāvaneti | kṛtaḥ kartṛvācakatvaṃ, tiṅo bhāvanāvācakatvamiti vibhāgo na syādityarthaḥ | bhavanmate na syāditi | śatrādirūpe kṛti kartṛvācakatvasyāsaṃbhavāt tasyāpi lādeśatvāviśeṣeṇavācakatvāsaṃbhavāt, sthānibhūtalakārasya tu bhāvanāmātravācakatvāditi bhāvaḥ | nirākāṅkṣatayeti | 'arthāṃśe' iti śeṣaḥ | kartarītyasyeti | 'kartari kṛ'dityasyetyarthaḥ | apravṛtteriti | ākāṅkṣitavidhānasyaivocitatvāditi bhāvaḥ || 62 ||
