ityādisāmānādhikaraṇyeti | ityādau sāmānādhikaraṇyānurodhāt, abhedānvayānurodhādityarthaḥ | yadi kartā śānajartho na syāt tadā caitraḥ pacamāna ityādāvananvayaḥ syādabhedasya bādhitatvāt | ādhārādheyabhāvasaṃbandhenānvayasya nāmārthayoriti vyutpattivirodhenāsaṃbhavāt | kartṛvācakatā syāditi | asya 'tiṅā'mityādiḥ | 'pacatitarāṃ maitra' ityātrātiśayitaviklittyanukūlakriyānukūlabhāvanāyā nāmārthatvena maitreṇa sahābhedānvayāsaṃbhavāt kartrākhyātasya kartṛvācakatvaṃ syāt, tacca paramate'niṣṭamiti bhāvaḥ | ityatrāpyāpatteriti | śānaco'nyatra śaktyanirṇayena śakyaśambandha rūpā lakṣaṇā na saṃbhavatīti na śaṅkyam | prathamāntapadāsamabhivyāhṛtākhyātavaditarāsamabhivyāhṛtapacamāna iti prayoge śānaco'pi bhāvanāyāṃ śaktinirṇayasaṃbhavāt | athavā tatsthāninolakārasya tatra lakṣaṇāpattirbodhyā | na ca 'nāmārthayo'riti vyutpattau nāmapadaṃ sattvapradhānaparaṃ, tiṅantaprakṛtikatarabādyantapratipādyā bhāvanā tvasattvabhūtaiveti na doṣaḥ, kiṃ ca 'nāmārthayo'rityādivyutpatternāmajanyapratītiviśeṣyayorevānvayaḥ sa cābhedenaiveti śarīram, evaṃ ca bhavanmate'pi 'pacatitarāṃ devadattaḥ' 'pacatikalpaṃ devadatta' ityatra kathaṃ devadattasya viśeṣaṇībhūtena tiṅarthakartrā sahānvayaḥ | tava mate vyutpattestatra saṃkoca iti cenmayāpi so'nusriyata iti vācyam | nāmapadena prātipadikamātrasya, suvantamātrasya vā grahaṇaṃ, nāmajanyapratītiviśeṣyayorevetyādiniyame mānābhāvaścetyāśayāt | na ca tathāvidhaniyamānabhyupagame 'nityo ghaṭa' ityasya vākyasya ghaṭatve nityapadārthānvayasaṃbhavāt prāmāṇyāpattiriti vācyam | ghaṭatvasya padārthaikadeśatayā 'padārthaḥ padārthenānvetī'tyādivyutpattivirodhena tatra tadanvayāsaṃbhavenoktavākyasya prāmāṇyāpatterabhāvāt | kartuḥ padārthaikadeśatvābhāvena 'pacatikalpaṃ devadatta' ityādāvanvayopapattistvakṣataiva | tasmāt samabhivyāhṛtavarṇānāmeva vācakatvamiti varṇasphoṭo niṣpratyūhaḥ | nanu tivādyādeśānāṃ vācakatve 'laḥ karmaṇi--ityādyanuśāsanavirodhastena sthānināmeva vācakatvabodhanādityāśaṅkyāha--'laḥ karmaṇo'tyanuśāsanamiti || iti varṇasphoṭanirūpaṇam 
samabhivyāhṛtavarṇānām--tattatpadaghaṭakayāvatāṃ samabhivyāhṛtavarṇānāmityarthaḥ | vācakatve siddhe iti | tāvatāṃ varṇānāṃ vācakatvaṃ vinā vakṣyamāṇasvarūpapadasya vācakatvaṃ nopapadyate, ataḥ padasya vācakatvasiddhistāvatāṃ varṇānāṃ vācakatvasiddhyadhoneti bhāvaḥ | evaṃ ca pūrvagranthena sahāsya granthasyopajīvyopajīvakabhāvasaṃgatiḥ sūcitā | nanu tathāpi vācakatā prativarṇaṃ viśrāmyatu nāma, naitāvatā 'padaṃ vācaka'mityabhimatasiddhirityāśaṅkyāha--prativarṇamiti | samabhivyāhārarūpeti | samudāyarūpatyarthaḥ | nalopādyāpatteścati | supaḥ śravaṇaṃ tu nāpāditaṃ, samudāyātmakaprātipadikāvayavattevana 'supo dhātvi'ti luk‌saṃbhavāt | samāsagrahaṇasya-'bhede sati padārthayoḥ parasparaṃ saṃsargastathāvidhārthavatsamudāyasya cedbha vati tarhi samāsasyaive'ti niyamārthatayā prakṛte samudāye prātipadikatvasya niṣpratyūhatvāt | pratīpodyotakṛtaḥ-prativarṇaṃ sakhyārthakarpetvābhāvānna tad‌dyotakavibhaktyutpattiḥ | etādṛśasthale eva saṃkhyotpatteḥ | saṃkhyā ca samudāyasyaivārtho na pratyekavarṇānām, 'ekatvaṃ saṃdhātasyārtha'iti 'aiu'ṇasūtrasthabhāṣyasvarasāt | tathā ca tatra bhāṣyaṃ--saṃghātasyaikārthyāt subabhāvo varṇā'dityādi | prakṛtyaṃśe varṇavyatyayopajanayoḥ sattve'pi saṃkhyāntarānavagamāt saṃkhyā samudāyasyaivārtha iti tadāśayaḥ | na cāvyayebhya ivaikavacanaṃ durvāramiti vācyaṃ, saṃkhyāśūnyārthake eva bhāṣyaprāmāṇyāttathākalpane'pi na tadyuktārthake'nabhidhāyakasvādyutpattiḥ kalpanārhā, mānābhāvāt | gamakasattve eva tatkalpanācca | evaṃ caiṣāṃ pratyayod‌deśyatvarūpaṃ prakṛtitvameva neti 'na kevale'ti nyāyasyāpi nāvatāraḥ | evaṃ ca na nalopādyāpattiriti-prāhuḥ | evaṃ cānubhavavirūddhārthasmaraṇāpattyā varṇavyatyayopajanādisattve'pi 'tarku' 'rajju'rityādau dhātvarthāntarānavagatyā gauraveṇa ca na varṇānāṃ pratyekamarthavattvamityeva jyāyaḥ | spaṣṭaṃ cedam 'aiuṇ' sūtrabhāṣye | etacceti | samabhivyāhāravācakatvasamarthanaṃ cetyarthaḥ | caramavarṇa eveti | prakṛtyādighaṭakacaramarṇa evetyarthaḥ | prathamādivarṇe tu na śaktiḥ, tanmātroccāraṇādarthapratyayāpatteḥ, uttaravarṇānubhavajanyasaṃskārasya tadānutpādena prathamavarṇāṃnubhavasyottarottaravarṇānubhavajanyasaṃskāraṇāṃ sahakāritvāsaṃbhavāt | uttarottaravarṇānubhavajanitasaṃskārasahakṛtaprathamādivarṇānubhavajanyasaṃskarasya hetutve tu vinigamanāviraheṇa tāvadvarṇasamudāye śaktisiddhayāpattiriti bhāvaḥ | vyāsajyavṛttitve--anekavarṇaparyāptatve | mānābhāvāditi | caramavarṇānubhavottaramevārthabodhodayena tatraiva vācakatvaviśrāntiriti bhāvaḥ |
nanvevaṃ caramavarṇamātroccāraṇepyarthabodhāpattirata āha-pūrvepūrveti | ata eva netaravarṇanairarthakyam | pūrvoktadoṣaparāhatatvādevaitatpakṣadūṣaṇasyaivoddeśyatayā,--dūṣaṇāyaivetyarthaḥ | ānupūrvyā eveti | na tu caramavarṇamātravṛttitadvyaktitvādeḥ | nanu 'ghaṭa' ityādau ghottarāvarṇottaraṭakārottarāvarṇatvādarūpānupūrvyāścarame varṇe eva vṛtterna tāvadvarṇasamudāyavṛttitvamiti kathaṃ padasya vācakatvarūpabhavadabhimatasiddhiriti cenna | ānupūrvyāṃ padārthāntaratvavādimīmāṃsakamate etaddopāsaṃsparśāt | 'pūrvoktarūpanupūrvīṃ'ti mate'pi ghāvyavahitottarāvarṇatvasyāvarṇe, tadavyavahitapūrvaghatvādirūpānupūrvāyastatpadaghaṭakasakalavarṇeṣu viśrānterakṣateḥ | etāsāṃ nānātvena gauravānna śaktatāvacchedakatvamiti cenna, agatyā svīkārāt | 'avyavahitottaratva'saṃbandhena pūrvapūrvavarṇasaṃśliṣṭottarottaravarṇasamudāyatvasyānupūrvīṃrūpatvādvā | athavā caramavarṇamātraviśrāntānupūrvīṃviṣayitāvyāpakaviṣayatāpratiyogitkarūpānupūrvīṃghaṭakanvaṃ, tena saṃbandhenānupūrvī vā śaktatāvacchediketyaṅgīkriyatāṃ, padabhedena ghaṭakavarṇānāṃ bhede'pi ghatvādyavacchinnaviṣayitātvena varṇaviṣayitānāṃ tādṛśānupūrvī viṣayitāvyāpakatvaṃ vivakṣaṇīyamiti nāvyāpakatvāsaṃbhavādidoṣa ityalaṃ pallavitena |
nanu tādṛśānupūrvyāḥ sahakāritvādevīktatiprasaṅgavāraṇe'laṃ tasyāḥ śaktatāvacchedakatvakalpaneyetyata āha--digiti | tadarthastu--samudāyasyārthavattvābhāvena prādipadikatvānāpattiḥ, arthabodhajanane sahakāritvamātreṇa samudāyasya prātipadikatvas‌vīkāre'pi caramavarṇamāvrasyāpi prātipadikatvāttato'pi pratyayotpattyāpattiḥ | evaṃ caramavarṇasyaiva vācakteva tadarthe vibhattayarthānvayo na syāt, tasyāprakṛtitvāt | samudāyasya prakṛtitve'pi prakṛtārthasya tadvṛttyānupasthityā tadarthatvābhāvāt | kiṃ ca--paṅkajādipade samudāye śaktyabhāve yaugikayogarūḍhādivibhāgocchedāpattiḥ, 'pācaka'iti vaktavye 'pāc' ityanantara ghaṭikādivyavadhānenāka ityuccāraṇe'pi pākakartteti bodhāpattyā sarvatra samudāyānuparvyā eva yatra sahakāritvaṃ tatra yaugika'mityādervaktumaśakytvāt | nanu yatsvaghaṭaprakṛtyādicaramavarṇaśaktyaivārthopasthāpakaṃ tadyaugikaṃ,yathā pācakādipadaṃ | yat svacaramavarṇaśaktyaivārthopasthāpakaṃ tat 'kevalarūḍhaṃ'--yathā-ḍitthādipadaṃ | yatra svaghaṭakaprakṛtyādicaramavarṇaśaktyā, svīyacaramavarṇaśaktyā ca pṛthagupasthitayorarthayoḥ parasparamanvayastadyogarūḍhaṃ, yathā-paṅkajādipadamityevaṃ vibhāgaḥ saṃbhavatyeveti cenna | pācakādipadaghaṭakapratyayacaramavarṇe svacaramavarṇatvasyāpi sattvena niruktyaugikatvasyāsaṃbhavāpatteḥ | evaṃ gopade kevalarūḍhatvaṃ na syāt, taccaramavarṇasya taddhaṭakapratyayacaramavarṇābhinnatayā svacaramavarṇaśaktyaivopasthityabhāvādityādirbodhyaḥ || 64 ||
paribhāṣitapadasyeti | tasyaivetyarthaḥ | arthavatpadatvenābhimataprakṛtipratyayavyudāptaḥ | subantādereva prayogeṇa vyavahārāttatraiva śaktigrahāditi bhāvaḥ | anye tu-paribhāṣitapadasyāpottharthaḥ ityāhuḥ | tādṛśapadasya vācakatve'nyathānupapattimapi pramaṇayati--mūle ghaṭenetyādaṣviti | tadvyācaṣṭe-ghaṭenetyādāviti | neti pratyaya iti | asye'ti vibhāgasye'tyatrānvayaḥ | iti vibhāgasya jñānāsaṃbhavāditi yojanā | nirṇayāsaṃbhavādityarthaḥ | viśiṣya jñānasaṃbhavāditi | 'varṇayorādeśa'iti pakṣe-ekārarūpa ādeśaḥ pūrvāvayavo vā pasavayavo veti saṃśayāḍitastatsaṃśayaḥ saṃbhavati | 'sarve sarvapadādeśā' iti pakṣe tu --vasnasādāviva saṃpramohādvibhāgajñānameva na saṃbhavatīti bhāvaḥ | kaścidatra prakṛtibhāgaḥ syāditi jñānaṃ saṃbhavatyevetyato 'viśiṣye'tyuktam | ayaṃ prakṛtibhāgo'yaṃ pratyayabhāgaḥ iti jñānāsaṃbhavādityarthaḥ | vibhāgaḥ saṃbhavatīti | tathā ca yathā vaiyākaraṇānāmapi vatnasādau vibhāgajñānaṃ na saṃbhavati tathā 'sarve sarvapadādeśāḥ' iti pakṣe 'rāmeṇe'tyādāvapīti bhāvaḥ | atra pakṣe ṭāsthāne nādeśaṃ vidadhato bhāṣyakāramate'pi vaiyākaraṇānāmapi vibhāgajñānaṃ na suśakamiti bodhyam | evaṃ satyapi 'rāmeṇe'tyato rāmābhinnaḥ kartetyādirūpabodhānyathānupapattyā suvantādyātmakapadasya vācakatvamāstheyamiti bhāvaḥ | yatra prakṛtyādivibhāgakalpanā na saṃbhavati tasyaiva padasya vācakatvaṃ, yatra tu tathā kalpanāsaṃbhavastatra prakṛtipratyayoreva vācakatvamityāhuḥ | bhāṣyattvavidastu-etādṛśasthale padasya vācakatve klṛpte sarvatraiva padasya vācakatvamanyathārdhajaratīyatāpattervyāvahārāttatraiva śaktigrahāccetyāhuḥ | evaṃ vākyasphoṭe'pi matadvayaṃ bodhyam || 65 ||
tadāha - vācakatvamāhetyarthaḥ | padayoreṅa iti | varṇayorādeśapakṣe padāvayavavarṇayorityarthaḥ | 'sarve sarvapadādeśāḥ' iti pakṣe tu yathāśrutameva sādhu | tadvibhāgaḥ - pūrvapadottarapadavibhāgaḥ | harevetyādāvityādipadād--'viṣṇo'va' 'kṛṣṇeve'tyādisaṃgrahaḥ | ādeśenāpahāro na prakṛtipratyayabhāgayoryathā--'lihaṃ lihau liha' ityādau | tatrāpi samudāyaśaktiraśyābhyupeyetyāha--evaṃ prakṛtīti | mūle--vākyaphoṭamiti | vākyaṃ - vācakamityarthaḥ | nanu yatra vibhāgajñānaṃ na saṃbhavati tasya vākyasya vācakatvasiddhāvapi na sarvavākyeṣu tatsiddhirata āha-vastuta ityādināḥ padaiḥ-ghaṭādiprātipadikaiḥ, subantairvā--yathā ghaṭādirūportho bodhyate, tathā 'ghaṭo'stī'tyādivākyairvākyārthaḥ pratyāyyate, paraṃ tu prāptāprāptavivekena saṃsarge sā śaktiḥ paryavasyatīti bodhyam |
peṭikākṛtastu-vibhaktyaiva kriyākārakabhāvasaṃbandhasyābhihitatayā tadaśe vākyaśakterasaṃbhave'pi viśeṣyaviśeṣaṇabhāve vākyaśaktiḥ | vibhaktiprakṛtyarthagataviśeṣaṇatvasya vibhaktiśakyatve'pi viṣeṣyaniṣṭhamitarapadārthaviśeṣaṇatāpratiyogikaṃ viśeṣyatvaṃ tacchakyaṃ bodhyaṃ, viṣayatāyā api śābdabodhe bhānamāvaśyakamiti 'arthavat--'sūtre bhāṣye spaṣṭam | tathā prātipadikārthasūtre ',ṣaṣṭhī śeṣe' iti sūtre ca bhāṣye | tatra hi--'vīraḥ puruṣa' ityādau 'viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyabhāvarūpaṃ yadādhikyaṃ sa vākyārtha' ityuktam | 'ādhikya'mityatra svārthe ṣyañiti prāhuḥ | vākye śaktyakalpane bādhakamāha--anyatheti | tādṛśavyutpattīti | 'ghaṭaḥ karmatva'mityādivākyaṃ ghaṭakarmakānayanabodhajanakamiti jñānarahitasyetyarthaḥ | bodhaprasaṅga iti | ghaṭakarmakamānayanamiti bodhaprasaṅgaḥ | 'ghaṭādipadaśaktigrahavata' iti śeṣaḥ | tathā ca 'ghaṭamānaye'tyatrevātrāpi padārthopasthatyādighaṭitasāmagrīsattvāt tathābidhabodhaprasaṅga ityarthaḥ | tādṛśavyutpattimato bodha iṣṭa eveti tādṛśavyutpattirāhityānusaraṇam | nanu 'ghaṭaḥ kamatva'mityādivākyaṃ svarūpāyogyameveti na tato bodhāpattiḥ,ghaṭe'vyavahitapūrvavartitvasaṃbandhenām‌padamattvasyāmpade'vyavahitottaravartitvasaṃbandhena ghaṭādipadavattvasyā'kāre 'ānay'padavattvasyānayūpade'kāravattvasya vākāṅkṣātvād--'ghaṭaḥ karmatvaṃ'mityādau ca tādṛśākāṅkṣābhāvāt | ghaṭapade karmatvapadavattvāderākāṅkṣātvābhāvāt, śābdabodhajanakasamabhivyāhārasyaivākāṅkṣātvādityata āha--ghaṭamānayetyatreveti | ātrāpi 'tādṛśavyutpattirahitasye'tyanupajyate | bodhābhāvācceti | vākyaśaktyanaṅgīkāre tu tajjñānetarasakalakāraṇakalāpasattvācchābdabodhāpattirityarthaḥ | tatraiva - ghaṭamānayetyatraiva | 'iti jñānavato bodhā'diti yojanā | ghaṭārthakaprātipadiketi | tena kalaśādipadasaṃgrahaḥ | karmatvavācaketi | etattu spaṣṭārtham | ghaṭādipadottarāmpadasya karmatvavācakatvāvyabhicārāt | kkacit 'karmatvavācakavibhakte'rityeva pāṭhaḥ | karmatvavācakavibhaktitvenauśasoḥ saṃgrahaḥ | tathā ca ghaṭakalaśādipadabhedenā'mādivacanabhedena ca na kāryakāraṇabhāvabāhulyamiti bhāvaḥ | 'dhāto'rityanantaraṃ vikāraṇasyeti kkacid‌bodhyam | parantu vikaraṇasya sarvatra na niveśaḥ, 'odanaṃ papāce'tyādau tadasattvāt | yadyapyānayetyādāvākhyātasyāpi na śrūyamāṇatvam, anusaṃdhānaṃ cobhayatra samānameva, tathāpyākhyātasyārthopasthityarthamanusaṃdhānamāvaśyakaṃ, vikaraṇasya tvanarthakatayā tadanāvaśyakamityaśayena dhātorityanantaraṃ vikaraṇasyeti nābhihitam | athavā vikaraṇasya nirarthakatvarūpamaprādhānyaṃ sūcayituṃ vikaraṇānabhidhānam | tajjñānamapīti | tathāvidhakāryakāraṇabāvajñānamapi | heturiti | tathā cānvayavyatirekābhyāmetādṛśākāṅkṣājñānahetutvenaivopapattau vākye śaktisadbhāve mānābhāva iti bhāvaḥ | siddho vākyasphoṭa iti | vākyasya vācakatā siddhetyarthaḥ | viśiṣṭavākyārthabodhe - vākyārthaviśeṣabodhe | 'ghaṭakarmakamānayana'mityādyākārakabodhe iti yāvat | padasamabhivyāhāreti | uktapūrvāparībhāvāpannapadasamudāyarūpaṃ ca tadvākyaṃ cetyarthaḥ | idaṃ ca 'ghaṭamānaye'ti vākyādeva bodha ityabhipretya | yadi tu 'ānaya-ghaṭa'miti viparyastādapi vākyāttathāvidhabodho'nubhavasiddhaḥ, ata eva samarthasūtre bhāṣye uktam--'yatheṣṭamanyatareṇābisaṃbandho bhavati vākye--rājñaḥ puruṣaḥ, puruṣo rājña itī'tyucyate--tadoktasamabhivyāhāramiva viparītatiṅantasamabhivyāhāramādāya tanniṣṭhaprakṛtavākyārthabodhakatvagrahasyāpi tathāvidhabodhe kāraṇatvaṃ bodhyam | nanu tādṛśakāryakāraṇabhāvajñānamabyupagantavyamanyathā ghaṭaviṣayakavṛttijñānāt paṭādiviṣayakabodhāpattiḥ | evaṃ viṣayabhedena kāryakāraṇabhāvabhedāvaśyakatve hetutvakalpane'pi vākye śaktiḥ kathaṃ siddhetya āha--arthopasthāpaketi | arthopasthāpakaṃ-yajjñānaṃ tadviṣaye yaḥ śabdastadvṛti yajjñānanirūpitakāraṇatvaṃ tasyetyarthaḥ | 'ghaṭamānaye'tivākyaniṣṭhaghaṭakarmakānayanarūpavākyārthajñānakāraṇatvajñānasya śaktitvavāraṇāyā'rthopasthāpake'ti | prakṛtārthopasthāpaketyarthaḥ | tena na tattaddoṣatāvasthyam | tathā ca paramate yā ākāṅkṣā saivāsmākaṃ vākyaśaktiriti nādhikyamiti bhāvaḥ | nanu tadviṣayakaśābdabodhe tadviṣayakavṛttijñānasya hetutve prayojanābhāvena saṃbandharūpaviṣayaṃ niveśya kāryakāraṇabhāvakalpanāttadaśe śaktyanabhyupagame'pi tad‌bodhopapattisaṃbhava ityāśaṅkyāha--yuktaṃ caitadityādinā | viṣayatāsaṃbandheneti | tathā ca na viṣayedena kāryakāraṇabhāvavāhulyamiti saṃbandhāṃśe śaktyanabhyupagame tad‌bodhānirvāhānna śaktisvīkāra āvaśyaka iti bhāvaḥ | nanu 'ghaṭamānaya' 'paṭamānaye'tyādisamabhivyāhārānantyācchaktyānantyaṃ syādati cet, tavāpi mate tattatsamabhivyāhārabhedena kāryakāraṇabhāvānantyaṃ syāditi gauravasya tulyatvāt | yadi ghaṭaprakāraśābdabodhavāvacchinnaṃ prati ghaṭārthakapadottaravibhaktyādijanyopasthitirhetuḥ, kāryatākāraṇatayoravacchedakaḥ saṃbandho viśeṣyatā, eve viśeṣyatāsambandhena karmatvaprakāraśābdatvāvacchinnaṃ prati karmatvārthakavibhaktiprāgvartipadajanyopasthitirviśeṣyatāsaṃbandhena hetuḥ | idaṃ ca karmatvasya dvitīyādyarthasya prakṛtyarthe viśeṣaṇatvābhyupagame | viśeṣyatvābhyupagame tu prakāratāsaṃbandhena karmatvaviśeṣyakaśābde sāmānyata eva kāryakāraṇabhāvaḥ sarvatrābhyupeyate,--tadā vākyaśaktisvīkāre tadānantyāpattiratiricyate iti cettadā mamāpi mate 'ghaṭamānaya' 'paṭamānaye'tyādisakalavākyaniṣṭhaikaiva śaktirabhyupeyate | vākyārrthaviṣayiṇyāstu nirūpitatvādeḥ karmatvādisaṃbandhasyaikyāt | 'ghaṭamānaye'tyatra paṭasya padādupasthityabhāvādeva na paṭakarmakabodhāpattiḥ | vastutastu ghaṭaprakārakaśābdabodhe ghaṭapadottarāmpadajanyopasthitirheturiti viśiṣyaiva tattapadamantarbhāvya hetuhetumadbhāvakalpanamāvaśyakam | anyathā nirākāṅkṣaghaṭapadād ghaṭopasthitau kalaśapade'gṛhītaśaktikapuṃsaḥ 'kalaśamānaye'ti vākyād‌bodhāpatteriti bodhyam | etatsūvanāyaiva bhūṣaṇe--'yadi ghaṭaprakārake'tyatra 'yadī'tyuktam | tattatpadamantarbhāvya taduktau tu noktāpattiḥ | tatpadaghaṭitākāṅkṣājñānasahakṛtatattatpadajanyopasthitereva sāmagrīmadhye niveśāt |
nanu tathāpi vākyaniṣṭhabodhakatāyāḥ śaktitve kiṃ mānamiti cecchābdabodhe saṃbandhabhānānyathānupapattiriti gṛhāṇa | śābdabodhīyaviṣayatāyā vṛttyā padajanyopastitiviṣayatatvāt viṣayatāsaṃbandhena śābdabodhapadārthopasthityorviṣayamaniveśya sāmānyata eva hetuhetumadbhāvakalpanāt | tatpuruṣīyatvaṃ tvavacchedakakoṭo niveśyam | anyathā puruṣāntarasyopasthitisattve tacchūnyasyāpi puruṣasya śābdabodhaprasaṅgaḥ | viṣayaṃ niveśyātmaniṣṭhapratyāsattyā tayohatuhetumadbhāvakalpanāyāṃ puruṣāpekṣyā viṣayāṇāmadhikyādadhikakāryakāraṇabhāvāpattiranyathāgṛhītaśaktikaghaṭādipadāccakṣurādyupasthitaghaṭāderapi śābdabodhāpattiḥ | nanu sāṃsargikaviṣayatātiriktaviṣayatāsaṃbandhenaiva kāryakāraṇabāvaūrīkriyate'to na saṃbandhe śaktisvīkārāvaśyakateti cedatrāhuḥ,--jñānarūpāyā viṣayatāyā viśeṣyāditrayasādhāraṇyā ekyāttathā niveśanāsaṃbhavāt | atiriktaviṣayatāpakṣepi tasyā ekyenaivopapattau tattraividhyānabhyupagamāt viṣayasvarūpā viṣayateti pakṣe traividhyasaṃbhavepi sāṃsargikaviṣayatābhedaniveśe gauravādityādikaṃ bhūṣaṇato'nusaṃdheyam | tadetadabhipretyāha--vivecitaṃ caitad bhūṣaṇe iti |
pare tvanvayāṃśe vākyaśaktyanaṅgīkāre 'taṃ śābdayāmī tyanuvyavasāyānudayāpattiḥ, śabdasaṃbaddhabodhasyaiva tathānuvyavasāyaviṣayatvāt | śabdaprayojyatvasyaiva tathāvidhānuvyavasāyaniyāmakatve dhūmapadopasthitadhūmaliṅgikāyā vahnyanumiterapi śabdaprayojyatayā tādṛśānuvyasāyāpattiḥ | nanu śābdatvajātireva tathāvidhānuvyavasāyahetuḥ, sā ca jātirākāṅkṣājñānadighaṭitasāmagrījanyaśābdabodha eva, na tvanumitāviti noktāpattiriti cet, tathāpī'me vākye parasparaṃ saṃbaddhe'iti vyavahāro vilīyeta, śabdānāmarthadvārakasaṃbandhasyaivāṅgīkāreṇa vākyasyostadvirahāt | na ca prayojanarūpabodhaviṣayadvārako vākyayorapi saṃbandhaḥ saṃbhavatyeveti vācyam, īdṛśasaṃbandhasya saṃbaddhavyavahāraniyāmakatvābhāvāt | anyathā janyajanakabhāvādisaṃbandhāsattve māthurapāṭaliputrakyorapi saṃyuktasaṃyogādirūpaparamparāsaṃbandhasya, svaniṣṭhadravyatvādimattvasya ca sattvene'mau saṃbaddha'viti vyavahārāpatteritpalamiti prāhurityalaṃ pallavitena |
idaṃ tu bodhyam--bahuṣu sthaleṣu padasphoṭavākyasphoṭayorvyavasthitatvepi yatra vākyasyaiva śāstreṇa saṃskārastatra vākyādevārthabodho, na padāt, yathā 'ṣaṭtsanta' ityādau | ata eva 'ḍaḥ si dhuṭ' iti sūtre bhāṣye dhuḍādayaḥ pūrvāntā eva kiṃ na kṛtā ityāśaṅkā ṣṭutvāpādanena samāhitā | anyathā śaktatāvacchedakānupūrvībhedāpattyā 'santa' ityataḥ svārthabodhānupapattiḥ syāditi parāditvasyāvaśyakatvena sa vicāro'saṃgatimāpadyeteti vadanti | sarvatra kṣūyamāṇānupūrvyeva śaktatāvacchedikā, na tu smaryamāṇānupūrvī, śrūyamāṇādeva bodhānubhavāt, sthānyādeśabhāvaprahavidhurāṇāṃ tatsmaraṇāsaṃbhavācca | etena 'ṣaṭtsanta' ityatrāgamarahitānupūrvyeva tattadarthaśaktatāvacchedikā, ata eva 'santaḥ pūjyā' ityādivākyaghaṭaka--'santa' ityataḥ svārthopasthityādikaṃ bhavatyato na bhāṣyasthavicārāsaṃgatiriti dūṣaṇaṃ nirastam | evameva 'ghaṭene'tyādau ṭānusandhānapūrvaka eva śābdabodhaḥ | tasyaiva 'matyā' 'goduhe'tyanurodhena kalpanāt | evaṃ ca sthāninaḥ kkacidapi na śrūyamāṇatā, yathā lakārādestasyaiva na vācakatvamityapi nirasanīyam | sthānyādeśabhāvajñānavidhurasya śrūyamāṇādeva bodhodayena tasya vācakatvāvaśyakatvāditi dik | apūrvatvāt - sarvatra śābdabodhāt prāgajñānāt | tena kkacidindriyeṇa tajjñānasaṃbhave'pi nāsaṃgatiḥ | tad‌grahāsaṃbhavaḥ - anvaye padaśaktigrahāsaṃbhavaḥ | vādābhyupagama iti | ajñātaśakteranupayoga ityato''bhyupagama' ityaktam | tathā cātra pakṣe'nirbharaḥ sūcitaḥ | ata eva pakṣāntaraṃ mūle uktam | tadavatārayati-nanvityādinā | padārthavaditi--saptamyantādvatirūpameyavākyārthe iti saptamīdarśanāt | vākyārthepi - anvaye'pi | tad‌grahaḥ - padaśaktigrahaḥ | 'pade'rthe'pya'stvityatra vākyārthapadottaravartināpiśabdena padārthasamuccaye'saṃgatyāpattiḥ, padārthe śaktigrahasyāpratikṣepādata āha--apiḥ padapadottaramiti | tadagrahaścediti | vākyārthe śaktigrahaścedityarthaḥ | etāvatā granthena pūrvaṃ padārthe śaktigrahastatprayuktapadārthopasthitau satyāṃ paścānmanasopasthitasaṃbandhe śaktigraha ityuktam | adhunā tu vyavahāradarśanasahakṛtamanasā vākyārthe eva śaktigrahaḥ, paścādevāvāpodvāpābhyāṃ tattatpadasya tattadarthe śaktigraha ityāha--vastustviti | atrāpi 'vṛddhavyavahāraṃ paśyata' ityanuṣañjanāyam | tena kośādito vākyaśaktigrahāt pūrvaṃ padaśaktigrahe'pi na kṣatiḥ | kkacidupadeśena vākye viśiṣṭavākyārthanirūpitaśaktigrahaḥ pūrvaṃ bhavati paścāttad‌ghaṭakapadaśaktigrahaḥ | etādṛśasthale, 'ṣaṭa-santa' ityādau ca padārthajñānapūrvaka eva vākyārthabodha ityasya bhaṅga ityāhuḥ | kkacit padebhyaḥ padārthānāmupasthitau satyāmākāṅkṣāsācivyāddhākyārthopasthitāṃ tadaiva śaktigraho bodhaśca, mūlabhūtavākyārthopasthitau vṛttijanyatvābhāvācchābdabodhādaraḥ | na ca padānāmevānvitasvārthe śaktayaivopapattau vākye śaktikalpanaṃ vyarthamiti vācyam, anvaye śaktisvīkāre'pyanvayaviśeṣabodhanirvāhāya samabhivyāhāre tad‌bodhakāraṇatvamabhyupeyam | tathā kāraṇatvāgrahavato bodhavāraṇāya tad‌grahasyāpi hetutvamupeyamiti vākyaśakterniṣpratyūhatvāt, padānāmanvaye śaktikalpanāyāṃ gauravāpatteśvetyādyanyatra vistaraḥ |
nanvevaṃ padārthavadvākyārthasya smṛtireva syānna tvanubhava iti cedatrāhuḥ--vākyārthabodhasyānubhavarūpatvābhāvāt svargādikaṃ śrṛṇvato'pi 'svargādikamanubhavāmī'tyapratīteḥ, kiṃ tu śābdatvaṃ jñānatvavyāpyo jātiviśeṣa iti || 66 ||
uktapakṣadvayam - padaṃ vācakaṃ, vākyaṃ vācakamiti pakṣadvayam | anupapattiṃ spaṣṭayati--utpatteriti | varṇānāṃ janyatvamate--utpatteriti, nityatvamate tvabhivyākteriti | sarvadāsaṃbhaveneti | yadyekadotpattiḥ syāttadā yugapadutpannarūparasādīnāmiva pratyakṣaṃ syāt | evamabhivyāktirnānāvarṇānāṃ yugapadbhavettadā padapratyakṣavākyapratyakṣopapattirbhavet, na caikadā tatsaṃbhavaḥ, abhivyañjaka tattatsthānānuyogikavāyusaṃyogādeḥ kramikatvena āśutaravināśināṃ kramikāṇāṃ melanāsaṃbhavāt tāvadvarṇasamūharūpapadavākyapratyakṣānupapattiriti samuditārthaḥ | tasmin - uttaravarṇe | upasthiteti | smṛtetyarthaḥ | pūrvavarṇavattvamiti | asya 'sugraha'mityanenānvayaḥ | upasthitaviśiṣṭeti | upasthito yaḥ prathamavarṇaviśiṣṭo dvitīyo varṇa ityarthaḥ | evaṃ caturthādivarṇapratyakṣasamaye upasthitaprathamavarṇaviśiṣṭadvitīyaviśiṣṭatṛtīyavaiśiṣṭyaṃ sugrahamiti rītyā caramavarṇapratyakṣaṃ padavākyaghaṭakayāvadvarṇaviṣayakaṃ saṃpadyate iti na padārthabodhavākyārthabodhayoranupapattiriti praghaṭṭakārthaḥ | padatvasyeveti | vākyaśaktyanabhyupagamepi padasya vācakatvamabhyupagamyata eva, tannirvāhopayogipadatvaprakārakapratyakṣopapattiruktarīt‌yaivābhyupetavyā | evaṃ na vākyaśaktyabhyupagamapakṣe vākyatvaprakārakaṃ pratyakṣamapi tayaiva rītyopapadyata ityarthaḥ | ekasmṛtyupārūḍhānāṃ varṇānāmarthabodhakatve saṃbhavatīyaṃ gurubhūtarītirnopasyeti na śaṅkyam, ānupūrvījñānābhāvenārthabodhānupapatteranubhavakrameṇaiva smaraṇamiti niyamābhāvena 'saro rasa' ityādavavilakṣaṇabodhāpatteśca || 67 ||
idānīm - sakhaṇḍapadasphoṭanirūpaṇānantarasamaye | tathā ca prasaṅgo'vasaro vāsaṅgatiriti sūcitam | akhaṇḍapakṣamāheti | 'lāghavā'diti śeṣaḥ | nanu 'varṇeṣvavayavā na ce'ti prakṛtānupayuktamityāśaṅkāṃ pariharttumāha--dṛṣṭānteti | dṛṣṭāntamiṣeṇetyarthaḥ | pratīyamānā api yathāneti | sā pratītistu tattaduccāraṇaviśeṣavyaṅgyatattadvarṇasamānākāradhvaniviśeṣorāgaupādhikībrāntirevāto nārthasādhikā, ekārādāvavayavakalpane gauravāpatteḥ | kvacidivetyeveti | 'varṇeṣvavayavā' iti pāṭha ityarthaḥ | asmin pāṭhe dṛṣṭāntatā sphuṭaiva | 'praviveka'padārtamāha--bheda iti | 'vicir pṛthagbhāve' iti dhātvarthānusārāt--pravivekapadaṃ bhedārthakamiti bhāvaḥ | kakārādipratatītivailakṣaṇyānyathānupapattireva mānamityāśaṅkāmapākaroti--tattadvarṇotpādeti | tattadvarṇotpādakatvenāmito yo vāyusaṃyogastanniṣṭhamityarthaḥ | janakatāyā iti | janakatānirūpitaṃ vyajjakatānirūpitaṃ vā yadavacchedakatvaṃ tadvadityarthaḥ | pratītivailakṣaṇyeti | vāyusaṃyogagataṃ yat katvādi tena rūpeṇa sphoṭo vibhāsata iti 'kakāraḥ' 'khakāra' ityādivilakṣaṇapratītinirvāha ityarthaḥ | bāmatyām - vācaspatigranthe | varṇeṣvāropyata iti | ata eva 'tāro' 'manda' iti ekavraṇaviṣayapratītivyavahārayorupapattiḥ, anyathā viruddhadharmadvayasyaikatrānutpattyā tattadvarṇabhedāpatteḥ | ekasminnapi varṇe kiṃcidapekṣayā tāraḥ, kiṃcidapekṣayā manda iti vyavahārastu sakalajanasiddha eva | āropābhyupagame tu nāyaṃ doṣaḥ | anāropitasyaiva viruddhadharmadvayasaṃbandhasyāśrayabhedaprayojakatvāt | pratyakṣopalabhyamāneti | pratyakṣeṇopalabhyamāno yaḥ kakārādiḥ katvādiviśiṣṭaḥ, katvādiprakārakapratyakṣaviṣayasyetyarthaḥ | evaṃ 'śrṛṇomī'tyanubhavānurodhena ca sphoṭa āvaśyakaḥ | saṃyogasya śravaṇendriyagrāhatvābhāvāt | tadavedamiti | ityāditrayasya pratītye'tyatrānvayaḥ | 'tadevedaṃ pada'mityādyākārakapratītyetyarthaḥ | na ca vyaktabhede'pi 'tadevedamaupadhaṃ' 'tadevedaṃ nakhaṃ' 'ta evene keśā' ityādipratītivat sājātyāvalambanoktapratītiriti vācyam | 'tajjātīyamidaṃ pada'mityādi vyavahārasyāpyāpatteḥ | na ceṣṭāpattiḥ 'tadevedaṃ pada'mityādivyavahārasyaiveṣṭatvāt | evamekaṃ vākyamiti buddhirapi tatsādhikā bodhyā | na caikasamudāyaviṣayatvāt senāvanādivadeko dhānyarāśiritivaccopapattiriti vācyam, vanarāśyāderapyatiriktasyāṅgīkārāt | api caikadeśasthatvādirūpopādherekatvenaiko dhānyarāśirityādipratītestādṛgupādhiviṣayatvenopapattāvapi prakṛte copādhyasaṃbhavena tadasaṃbhavāt |
nacaikārthamukhyaviśeṣyakabodhahetutvaṃ tathā, vyāsasamāsasamudāye'tiprasaṅgāt | cārthe kiṃcitpādarthaviśiṣṭatvaṃ niveśyam. samāse'vyāpteḥ | 'ghaṭa' iti prakṛtyaṃśe'tivyāpteśca | ekārthabodhahetutvenaikatvasiddhāvekatvenaikārthabodhahetutvasiddhirityanyonyāśrayāpatteśca | arthabodhābhāve'pi aprasiddhārthakapadaśravaṇe 'ekaṃ pada'miti pratīteśca | na caikabuddhiviṣayatvālambanaikakartṛkaṃ padamityādipratītiriti vācyam, anekakartṛkoccāraṇaviṣaye'pīdamekaṃ ghaṭapadamiti pratyayasyeṣṭhasyānupapatteḥ | na ca tatraikabuddhiviṣayatvābhāvāt tatra neṣyata eveti vācyam, tasyai'ka evendraśabdo'nekakratuṣvāvirbhavatī'tyādisarūpasūtrabhāṣyasiddhatvāt | na caikajātīyabuddhiviṣayatvaṃ tatra nimittamiti vācyam, padādirūpaviṣayaikyādhīnaṃ buddhīnāmekajātīyatvamekajātīyabuddhiviṣayatvanibandhanaṃ ca viṣayaiktamityanyonyāśrayāt | na caikajātīyatvamekākāratvaṃ, tacca viṣayaikyaṃ vināpyupapannamiti nānyonyāśraya iti cet, paṭādirūpāvayavino'pyatiriktasya siddhayanāpatteḥ | 'ekaḥ paṭa'iti vyavahārasyāpyekabuddhiviṣayatvantu samūhenaivopapatteryadi tatraikabuddhiviṣayatvasyāgraheṣyeka iti vyavahārātsa vyavahāro'tiriktāvayavisādhaka ityupeyate tarhi prakṛte sa na pāṇipihitaḥ | na caika indraśabda ityasyaikajātīya indraśabda ityartha iti vācyam, anekaśabdakalpanāpekṣā tadekatvasyaivocitatvāt | ekajātīyatvālambanāyāḥ pañcādighaṭeṣvapi 'eko ghaṭa' iti pratīterāpatteśca | ekajātīyaindraśandra ityavyavahārācceti kṛtaṃ pallavitena | varṇānāmeveti | tāvadvarṇaviṣayakapratyakṣaṃ tu prāgupapāditameveti bhāvaḥ | teṣāṃ - varṇānāṃ | sphoṭātiriktatvābhāvāditi | vyajjakavāyusaṃyogagatagatvādinā sphoṭasyaiva bhānāditi bhāvaḥ | varṇānāṃ pratyekaṃ vācakatvaṃ ? tatsamudāyasya veti vikalpya nādya ityāha--varṇānāṃ pratyekamiti | nāntya ityāha--samudāyasyatviti | etasya jñānamityanenānvayaḥ | āśutarotpannānāmiti | 'varṇānā'mitnuṣajyate tasya ca samudāyasyetyanvayaḥ | 'āśutarotpannānā'miti āśutaravināśināmityasyāpyupakṣaṇam | etenāśutarotpannānāmapi kramavatāṃ cirasthāyināṃ padārthānāṃ jñānaṃ saṃbhavatyevetya'saṃbhavyeve'tyuktirasaṃgatetyapāstam | tathaiva - krameṇaiva | tathā ca varṇānāṃ janyatvapakṣe tatsamudāyasyaivāsaṃbhavāttajjñānāsaṃbhavaḥ | nityatvapakṣe samudāyasaṃbhave'pi tajjñānāsaṃbhava iti bhāvaḥ | saṃskāreṇeti | etatsahakāraḥ kṣotrendriye caraṇavarṇānubhave vā bodhyaḥ | tathā ca caramavarṇaviṣayakānubhavastathāvidhasaṃskāravaśāttajjanyasmṛtivaśādvā tāvadvarṇaviṣayakaḥ | tathāvidhasaṃskārasahakṛtacaramavarṇānubhavena svajanyasaṃskāradvārā tāvadvarṇaviṣayakaṃ samūhālambanasya samaniyataviṣayatākatvāt | na ca yena krameṇānubhave viṣayabhānaṃ tenaiva krameṇa smaraṇe'pīti nāviśeṣa iti vācyam, tādṛśaniyamāsiddheḥ, yena krameṇānubhavastadviparītakrameṇāpi smaraṇasyānubhavasiddhatvāt | na ca varṇānubhavasmaraṇayoreva tathā niyama iti vācyam, mānābhāvāt | 'saro rasa' ityādāvarthabodhavailakṣaṇyānurodhāttathā kalpyata iti cenna, atiriktasphoṭakalpanayāpi tadupapatteḥ | tricaturādipadādyanubhave tadviparītakrameṇa smaraṇānubhavācca | vikalpeti | yadi pratyekaṃ vyañjakatvaṃ tarhi prathamavarṇenaiva vyañjanasaṃbhave itaravarṇavaiyartyam, samuditānāṃ vyañjakatvaṃ tvasaṃbhavi, caramavarṇasya vyañjakatvepi taditaravarṇavaiyarthyam | pūrvapūrvavarṇānubhavajasaṃskārasahitacaramavarṇānubhavasya vyañdakatve tasyaivārthapratyākatvamastvalamatiriktasphoṭāṅgīkāreṇeti bhāvaḥ |
pare tu--uktarītyā padaśānasaṃbhavopapādanaṃ na yuktam, 'ayaṃ pūrvo'yaṃ para' ityabhilāpāsaṃbhavāt, tādṛśābilāpābhāve--'iko yaṇacī'tyādiśāstrāṇāmasaṃgatyāpattyeṣṭāpatterayogāt | manmate tu nānārañjanasādhanadravyāhitanānāvarṇoparāgeṇa paṭāderbhānavaduccāritavarṇakrameṇaiva tattadvarṇagatakatvādinā sphoṭābhivyaktistatraiva buddhiviṣaye paur‌vāparyavyavahārasaṃbhavaḥ | ata eva 'saro rasa' ityatra vilakṣaṇa-vilakṣaṇārthabodhopapatiḥ | naṣṭavidyamānayoravyavahitottaratvasaṃbandhasya kālikasaṃbandhavadvaktumaśaktyatvācca | etena naṣṭavidyamānapuruṣayorayaṃ pūrvo'yaṃ para iti vyavahāravadannāpi paurvakālikatvādinibandhanaḥ sa vyavahāra upapadyata iti parāstam | tathāpi tayoḥ saṃbandhānupapatterjāgarūkatvāt, vaktumaśakytvācca kālikasaṃbandhavat | evaṃ padapratyokṣopapādane'pi tatra vṛttyāśrayatvajñānāsaṃbhavāccaca padasyāvidyamānatvāt, avidyamāne'pyāśrayatvopagame''tītaghaṭo jalavā'miti pratyayāpattiḥ, abādhitārthakatayā tajjñānasya pramātvāt | nanvidānīmatīte vṛttyāśrayatvābhāvepi pūrvakālāvacchedena vṛttyāśrayatvaṃ tatra sughaṭamevātītaghaṭe rūpādyāśrayatvavaditi cenna, varṇasamudāyātmakapadasya pūrvamapyasattvāt | ghaṭasya pūrvaṃ sattvena tatra tadā rūpādyāśrayatvamupapadyate eveti spaṣṭam | nanvatītaghaṭo javavāniti pratyayo nāpādayituṃ śakyaḥ, matupā vartamānasaṃbandhasyaivābhidhānāt, 'tadasyāstyasmi'nniti sūtreṇa tathaiva bodhanāt | evaṃ ca buddhiviṣayavarṇasamudāyatmakapade vṛttyāśrayatvāṅgīkāre na kiṃcidbādhakam | ata evā'tītaghaṭaṃ jānāmī'ti prayoga upapadyate | anyathātītaghaṭe idānīntanajñānādyāśrayatvābhāvāttadanupapattiḥ spaṣṭaiveti cenna, evamapi padavākyayorabhāvena śabdārthasaṃbandhasyaiva hānyāpattiḥ taduktaṃ vākyapadīye--
'aśābdo yadi vākyārthaḥ padārthopi tathā bhavet' iti |
padasamudāyarūpavākyavadvarṇasamudāyarūpapadasyāpi virahāt, arthasaṃbandhasyobhayatrāpyasattvāditi tadbhāvaḥ | evaṃ tadevedaṃ kṛṣṇapadamekaṃ vākyamiti buddheranupapattiśca | upapāditaṃ tattvametat | kiṃ caivamuccāraṇabhedabhinnaśabdeṣu śaktigrahāsaṃbhavaḥ, vyaktivādānantyavyabhicāraprasarāt | gṛhītaśaktikādeva bodha iti niyame vyabhicāro'vagantavya iti dik | evaṃ ca kaiyaṭoktameva samyagiti prāhuḥ | sphoṭaśca parāpaśyantīmadhyamāvaikharīticaturvidhavāṅmadhye yā madhyamā vāk tasyā yo nādāṃśastadātmakaḥ | vistarastu harigrandhādavagantavya iti mañjūṣāyāṃ spaṣṭam |
sa cākhaṇḍa ekavarṇasvīkārapakṣe yadyepyekavarṇenābivyajyate tathāpi netaravarmavaiyarthyamantyavarṇābhivyaktasyaiva tasya bodhahetutvāt | na ca tathāpi taditaravarmavaiyarthyaṃ durvārameveti vācyam | pūrvapūrvavarṇānubhavajanitasaṃskārāṇāmapi sahakāritvāt, saṃskārāṇāṃ ca sthiratayā noktopapattyasaṃbhavaḥ | anubhavaśca tadānīntana upādeyaḥ, nāto devadattādipade dakārādivarṇānāṃ sārthakyaṃ durupapādam, kālāntarīṇatāvadvarṇānubhavajanitasaṃskārāṇāṃ sattvāditi dūṣaṇāvakāśaḥ | saṃskārod‌bodhanārthaṃ cāritārthyācca | ud‌buddhasaṃskārāṇāmeva sahakāritvāt | na caivaṃ sphoṭavaiyarthyam, dattottaratvāt | 'paraḥ saṃnikarṣaḥ-' iti sūtrabhāṣye'pyuccaritapradhvastānāṃ varṇānāṃ saṃnikarṣaḥ paurvāparyaṃ cānupapannamiti śaṅkāpanodāyoktam--
'buddhau kṛtvā sarvāśceṣṭāḥ kartā dhīrastattvannītiḥ' |
śabdenārthān vācyān dṛṣṭ‌vā buddhau kuryāt paurvāparyam ||
buddhiviṣayameva śabdānāṃ paurvāparyam | iha ya eṣa manuṣyaḥ prekṣāpūrvakārī bhavati sa paśyatyasminnarthe'yaṃ śabdaḥ prayoktavyo'smistāvacchabdo'yaṃ tāvadvarṇastato'yaṃ tato'yamiti | tattvannītiḥ - sakalaviṣayakajñānavān | dhīraḥ - pāṇḍityabhāk, buddhāvantaḥ karaṇe, ceṣṭāḥ - kaṇṭhādyabhighātavyāpārajanyaśabdān pratibimbitān kṛtvā, 'śabdene'ti sāhitye tṛtīyā, tathā ca tatsahitatadvācyārthān buddhāvupadeśe dṛṣṭvā tatraiva paurvāparyavyavahāraṃ kuryāditi tadarthaḥ | 'buddhiviṣaya'mityasya buddhisthaśabdaviṣayamityarthaḥ | 'ya eṣa' ityādinā vaktṛbuddhisthatvopapādanaṃ tu śāstrasya vaktrudeśenaiva pravṛttestasyaiva dharmaphalaśravaṇācceti bodhyam | buddhigatapaurvāparyasya varṇetvāropaparamidaṃ bhāṣyamiti tu na yuktam | buddhīnamapi kramikotpannāśuvināśitayā paurvāparyāsaṃbhavāt | evamayamiti pratkṣaviṣayamātraparāmarśakedamābhilāpāsaṃbhavo'pi sphoṭānaṅgīkāre bodhyaḥ | tadaṅgīkāre tu svāmātraśravyasūkṣmoccāraṇavyaṅgyatvena tadupapattiriti bhāvaḥ | eva doṣa iti | vikalpātmiko doṣa ityarthaḥ | tamupapādayati--tattadvarṇeti | pratyekaṃ vyañjakatvamiti | tadhātve ca taditaravāyusaṃyogopādānanairarthakyāpattiriti bhāvaḥ | nābhipradeśādud‌gatavāyostattatsthāne krameṇaiva saṃyogotpattyā samuditānāṃ vāyusaṃyogānāmasaṃbhavo bodhyaḥ | pratyekaṃ saṃyogonāmabhivyañjakatvepi kaścana gatvena kaścanautvena kaścidvisargatvena rūpeṇa sphoṭamabhivyanakti, tathā ca tāvadrūpavaiśiṣṭenābhivyaktasyaiva tasyārthabodhakatvamiti na kasyāpi vāyusaṃyogasya vaiyarthyamityāśayena samādhatte--pratyekameveti | ata eva - vāyusaṃyogānāṃ svavṛttigatvādinābhivyañjakatvādeva | tadatirekāsvīkāropi - sphoṭātiriktāsvīkīropītyarthaḥ | vāyusaṃyogasya gatvādinābhivyañjakatvānupagame tu tathābhivyaktayeriktavarṇasvīkāra āvaśyako bhavediti bhāvaḥ | nanu vāyusaṃyogānāṃ pūrvokta diśā yaugapadyāsaṃbhavena kathaṃ yugapattattadrūpeṇa sphoṭābhivyāktisaṃbhava iti cenna,nābipradeśādud‌gatavāyoḥ sthūlatayā yugapadevānekasthāneṣu nānāsaṃyogotpattisaṃbhavenoktadoṣānavakāśāt | nanvevamapi padābhāvena padajñānasyārthadhīkāraṇatā'saṃbhavaduktikaivetyāśaṅkāmapanudati--evaṃ ceti | vāyusaṃyogagatatatadrūpeṇa sphoṭābhivyaktau cetyarthaḥ | 'gṛhyat' ityasya 'yatre'ti śeṣaḥ | tathā ca yatra jñāne ghaṭatvaṃ ṭakāre bhāsate tādṛśaṃ yatpadajñānaṃ tatkāraṇatayā ityarthaḥ | avivādāditi | sarvamatatvādityarthaḥ | nanu ghaṭatvādinā bhāsamānasya sphoṭasya nityatayā svādhikaraṇakṣaṇadhvaṃsādhikaraṇakṣaṇadhvaṃsānadhikaraṇatvaviśiṣṭasvādhikaraṇakṣaṇadhvasādhikaraṇatvavān yaḥ kṣaṇastadutpattikatvarūpamavyavahitottaratvamasaṃbhavītyata āha-paraṃ tvavyavahitottaratvamiti | svajñāneti | svaṃ - ghaṭatvādinā bhāsamānaḥ sphoṭaḥ | etādṛśāvyavahitottaratvaniveśo na dṛṣṭacara ityāśaṅkyāha--ata eveti | parimaloktaṃ sphoṭakhaṇḍanaṃ dūṣayati--eteneti | uktavakṣyamāṇahetubhirityarthaḥ | ghaṭapadaeveti | ghaṭapadābivyakte evetyarthaḥ | kalaśapadāditi | kalaśapadābhivyaktādityarthaḥ | bodhaprasaṅgāditi | sphoṭasyaikyena ghaṭapadābhivyakte tatra śaktigrahasya vṛttatvāt--kalasapadābhivyaktādapi tato'rthabodhaprasaṅga ityarthaḥ | varṇasamudāyarūpapadasya vācakatvamate tu nāyaṃ doṣaḥ, tena śabdena bodhe jananīye tacchaktigrahasya hetutvāt | arthadhīheturiti | tatparyāyaśravaṇajābhivyaktistatparyāyābhivyāktaśaktigrahasahakṛtārthadhīheturityarthaḥ | tatparyāyaśravaṇena sphoṭabhivyaktidvārārthabodhe jananīye tatparyāyābhivyaktiśaktigrahaḥ sahakāri kāraṇamiti vārthaḥ | tattatparyāyagataśaktigrahahetutāyā ucitatve hetumāha--tathā satīti | śaktigrahatvenaiveti | tattatparyāyagataśaktigrahatvenaivetyarthaḥ | athavā upadhāyakatāsaṃbandhena śābdabuddhitvāvachinnaṃ prati viṣayatāsaṃbandhena śaktigrahatvenaivetyarthaḥ | lāghavācceti | co hetau | kāraṇatāvacchedakalāghavādityarthaḥ | tattve--hetutve | parimaloktamiti | 'parimalā'dyuktamityarthaḥ | ādinā 'nyāyarakṣāmaṇi'parigrahaḥ | sarvasiddhatvāditi | sphoṭānabhyupagantṛmate'pi paryāyabhedena śaktibhedasya siddhatvādityarthaḥ | nanu sphoṭasya nānātve gauravamevatve kathaṃ śaktibheda ityāśaṅkyāha--tadavacchedaketi | tathā ca sphoṭasyaikye'pi śaktitāvacchedakānupūrvībhedācchaktibhedaḥ, śakyatāvacchedakabhedena tadbhedavaditi bhāvaḥ | sphoṭatiraktavarṇānāmabhāvena 'ghaṭapade eva gṛhītaśaktikasye'tyādidoṣānavakāśaḥ | nanvatiriktavarṇānabhyupagame'pi ghatvaṭatvādinābhivyakta eva gṛhītatattacchaktikasya puṃsaḥ kalaśatvādinābivyāktādapi sphoṭācchābdabodhāpattirūpo doṣasyadavastha iti cenna | tattadānu'pūrvyavacchinne śaktigrahasya tattadānupūrvīprakārakajñānena bodhe jananīye sahakāritvāt | varṇānāmatiriktānāmabhyupagamapakṣe'pi tattadvarṇagatarūparūṣitasyaivārthe saṃketagrahānna ghaṭaśabde eva gṛhītaśaktikasya puṃsaḥ kalaśādipadaśravaṇe bodhāpattirityāhuḥ | sa ca sphoṭātmakaḥ śabdo vastuta eko'pi tattadvarṇaviṣayakasaṃskāraiḥ pratibimbitatattadrūpo'nantarūpatāmivāpanna iti sarvapadarūpa iti bodhyam | 'sphoṭa eva katvādinā vyajyate'iti 'eoṅ' sūtrabhāṣye ṣvanitam | tatra hi narasihavajjātyantaratayā varṇaikadeśā varṇagrahaṇena na gṛhanta iti mate klṛpta ityatra 'kṛpo rolaḥ' iti latvaṃ na prāpnotītyāśaṅkya--'athavobhayataḥ sphoṭamātraṃ nirdiśyate raśruterlaśrutirbhavati' ityuktam | atredamākūtaṃ bhāṣyakṛtām--vastuto yad‌gataṃ ratvaṃ tādṛśo na kaścidvarṇaḥ kiṃ tu tadrūpeṇa sphoṭasyāvabhāsamātramato ratvāvabhāsamānasphoṭaprasaṅge latvāvabhāsamānasphoṭo bhavati ityartho varṇanīyaḥ | anyathā kalpata ityādāvapi na pravarteta | evaṃ kṛpāvapi rephāvabhāsāt klṛpta ityādāvapi latvaṃ siddham | evaṃ dīrghākārādauhrasvaprayuktakāryātiprasaṅgastu na tatra hrasvāvabhāsavirahāditi | antarbhūtānantarbhūtasādhāraṇajāti--nirdeśa ityetatparamidaṃ bhāṣyamiti tu na peśalam | antargate ratvajātisattve māna bhāvāt | narasiṃhavadvarṇāntara'miti svīktivirodhācceti vistareṇa nirūpitaṃ vivaraṇādau sūrimiḥ | kiṃ caivaṃ sphoṭānaṅgīkāre drutamadhyamavilamvitavṛttiṣu prayatnabhedena cirācirakāloccāraṇajanyatvād‌hrasvākārasyāpi kālabhedena yatkiṃcidekavṛttau taparakaraṇe tadanyavṛttāvato bhisa aisanāpattiḥ | tadaṅgīkāre tu na kṣatiḥ | tadabhivyāktyanantaraṃ jāyamāno yo vaikṛtadhvanistena cirakālaṃ ciratarakālaṃ madhyamāyāṃ vilamvitāyāmupalabdhirbhavati | evaṃ copalabdhereva kālabhedo na tu sphoṭe iti vaktuṃ śakyatvāt,'tamevāyaṃ drutamuccāritavānanyo vilamvita'miti pratyabhijñāsattvāt | hrasvadīrvādau tvevaṃ na pratyabhijñeti taparakaraṇepi na hrasvaprayuktasya kāryasya dīrghādāvāpattiḥ | janyatve tu varṇānāmāśutaravināśitvābhyupagamācciramupalabdhirdurghaṭaiva syāditi bodhyam | taduktaṃ taparasūtre bhāṣyakṛdbhirvṛttyantare aiso'nāpattirityāśaṅkāyā apanuttaye--"evaṃ tarhiṃ sphoṭaḥ śabdo, dhvaniḥ śabdaguṇaḥ, katham ? yathā bheryāhantā bherīmāhatya kaścidviśatipadāni gacchati kaścitriṃśat kaściccatvāriṃśat sphoṭastāvāneva bhavati, dhvanikṛtā vṛddhiḥ-- dhvaniḥ sphoṭaśca śabdānāṃ dhvanistu khalu lakṣyate |
alpo mahāṃśca keṣāṃcidubhayaṃ tatsvabhāvataḥ ||" iti |
sphoṭaḥ śabda iti | śrotrendriyagrāhyaḥ sphoṭanāmakaḥ padārthaḥ, sphoṭābivyattaraṃ jāyamānavaikṛtadhvanirdhvanipadenātra vivakṣitaḥ | sa ca sphoṭātmakasya śabdasyopakārakatvād‌guṇaḥ | 'bhairīmāhatye'tyasya-'taṃ śabdamupalabhamāna' iti śeṣaḥ | 'dhavanikṛte'tyatrāpi dhvanipadaṃ vaikṛtadhvaniparam | dhvaniḥ sphoṭaścetyasya 'sta' iti śeṣaḥ | śabdānāṃ - śabdasaṃbandho dhvaniḥ | vaikṛtadhvanistu keṣāṃcidalpo'lpakālamupalabdhijanakaḥ, keṣāṃcinmahān - bahukālamupalabdhijanako lakṣyate | tatra pramāṇamāvedayati-'ubhayaṃ tatsvabhāvataḥ' iti | 'siddhi'miti śeṣaḥ | harirapyāha--
"sphoṭasyābhinnakālasya dhvanikālānupātinaḥ |
svabhāvatastu nityatvād hrasvadīrghaplutādiṣu ||
prākṛtasya dhvaneḥ kālaḥ śabdasyetyupacaryate ||"
--ityādyanyatra vistaraḥ | atiriktasphoṭasādhikāṃ kaustubhoktayuktimāha--kaustubhetviti | varṇamālāyāṃ - varṇasamūhe | padamiti pratīteḥ | idamupalakṣaṇaṃ,-vākyamiti pratīterityasya | atirikta eva - varṇātirikta eva | samudāyasya samudāyyatiriktatvāt | anyathā 'pada'mityādipratītyanupapatteḥ, varṇānāṃ pratyekapadarūpatvābhāvāt | uktarītyā 'ekaṃ pada'miti pratītyanupapatteśca | anyathā - atiriktasphoṭānaṅgīkāre | kapālātirikteti | 'pada'miti pratīterbuddhisthatāvadvarmaviṣayakatvenopapādane 'ghaṭa' iti pratīterapi ghaṭasamavāyitvenābhimatatāvatkapālairevopapādanasaṃbhavāt kapālādyatiriktaghaṭādeḥ parābhimatasyāsiddhiprasaṅga ityarthaḥ | paramāṇvatiriktaghaṭāsiddhiprasaṅgāstu noktaḥ, tathā sati mahattvābhāvena pratyakṣānupapatteḥ | idamupalakṣaṇaṃ--kapālāderapi tryaṇukādyatiriktasyāsiddhiprasaṅgasyetyalamativistarema ||
evam - atiriktasphoṭasyaiva vācakasyāhgīkāre | śāstrāpramāṇyeti | vyākaraṇaśāstrā prāmāṇyetyarthaḥ | upeyapratipattyarthā ityanteneti | eṣā kalpanā - prakṛtipratyayādikalpanā, upeyapratipattyarthe'tyatyatra vacanavipariṇāmo liṅgavipariṇāmaśca bodhyaḥ | ityetadrūpo yo'ntastenetyarthaḥ | uttaratra yata ityadhyāhāryam | yayato hetorupeyapratipattyarthā upāyā anavasthitā bhavantītyanvayaḥ | upeyasya - prāpyasya, pratipattiḥ, pratipattiḥ - prāptiḥ, jñānaṃ vārthaḥ | 'pañcakośādivadityādyupapādati--ayaṃ bhāva ityādinā | bhṛguvallyām - upaniṣadviśeṣe | ānandaballyām - upaniṣadviśeṣe | annaprāṇeti--ānandaśabdānto dvandvaḥ, tataḥ prācuryādyarthe mayaṭ | tasya pratyekaṃ saṃbandhaḥ, 'dvandvānte śrūyagāṇa'miti nyāyāt | tathā cānnamayaprāṇamayamanomayavijñānamayānandamayātmakā ye kośāstatpañcakavyutpādanamityarthaḥ | vāstaveti | vāstavaṃ - kālatrayābādhitaṃ yat pucchaṃ - jagadādhārabhūtaṃ brahma tad‌bodhanāyetyarthaḥ | etasya vāstavatvottayā pañcakośānāmavāstavatvamāveditam, tacca śārīrakabhāṣyādau sphaṣṭameva | atra keciditthaṃ vyācakṣate--'tathā hi bhṛgurvāruṇirvaruṇaṃ pitaraṃ brahya papraccha | varuṇa uvāca--'anna'miti | asyotpattyādikaṃ samīkṣya punaḥ papraccha | tata uttarayāṃbabhūva--'prāṇo brahme'ti | tasyātathātvāvadhāraṇānantaraṃ punaḥ praśne punarāha--'mana'iti | tasyāpi 'aśitamannaṃ tredhā bhavati, yat sthūlaṃ tatpurīṣaṃ yanmadhyamaṃ tanmāsaṃ, yadaṇīyastanmanaḥ' iti śrutāvutpattiśravaṇān punaḥ praśne āha--'vijñānamaya' iti | tasyāpi vṛttyupahitatvenātadhātvaṃ bud‌dhvā punaḥ pṛṣṭavān | tata 'ānando brahme'tyuttarite vastutattvaṃ prāpya sthito bhṛguriti | tanna peśalam | pañcamasya tatrānupāyatvāt | akośatvācca | tasmād‌bhṛguvallī nodāharaṇaṃ, kiṃtvānandavallī, tatratyānāṃ pañcānāmapyupāyatvāt | ānandamayapadaṃ hi tatratyena mukyābrahmasamarpakam | ata eva jagadādhāratvārthaka'puccha'śabdopapattiḥ | lāṅgūlātmaka mukhyārthasya tatra bādhāt | "ānandamayo'bhyāsāt" iti vyāsasūtraṃ tu tatratyānandamayaśabdasya tadvākyaśeṣe śrūyamāṇabrahmapade tātparyāt saṃgamanīyamityādi bhagavacchaṅkarapādaprabhṛtibhiḥ prapañcitam | etat sarvamabhipretyehānandavallyevodāhṛtā | kkacid‌bhūṣaṇasārapustake--bhṛguvallyāmityādigrantho dṛśyate, sa tu na yuktaḥ, uktahetubhyām | athavoktāsvarasādeva bhṛguvallyupanyāsānantaramānandavallyupanyāsa iti yojanā vidheyā | nanu vyākaraṇajñānaṃ vinotpannasphoṭajñāne vyabhicāro'ta āha--tathā ca byākaraṇeti | vaijātyaṃ kalpyata iti | tathā ca kāryatāvacchedakabhedānna vyabhicāraḥ | mantreti | mantreṇārthasmṛtiḥ prakārāntareṇāpīti vyabhicāravāraṇāya mantrajanyārthasmṛtau vaijātyaṃ mīmāṃsakaiḥ kalpyate tadbadatrāpīti bhāvaḥ | 'mantrajanya'mityasya mantrajanyatāvacchedakamityarthaḥ | vedānteti | tacchruvaṇajanyatāvacchedakam | anyathā bhāṣāgranthaśravaṇenāpi brahmajñānasyotpattervyabhicārāpattiriti vedāntibhistatra vaijātyaṃ yathā kalpyate tatheti bhāvaḥ | tasya ca jñānasyeti | śāstrābhyāsajanitasphoṭajñānasyetyarthaḥ | śarīrādiśuddhāvupayoga iti | evaṃ ca na śāstravaiyarthyamiti bhāvaḥ | paramparayā veti | yajñādyanuṣṭhānadvārā vetyarthaḥ |
uktārthe vākyapadīyaṃ pramāṇayati--taduktamiti | cikitsā saṃjītā yatretyarthaḥ | vāṅmūlānāmapanayaheturiti paryavasito'rthaḥ | adhividyamiti-vibhaktyarthe'vyayībhāvaḥi | sarvavidyāvyāpakamiti paryavasito'rthaḥ | atīteti | atīto viparyāso bhramo yasyetyarthaḥ | kevalām - parākhyāṃ vācam | kathaṃ vāstaveti | alīkajalodervāstavatṛḍādinivṛttijananādarśanādalīkaprakṛtyādikalpanayā vāstavasphoṭapratītirna saṃbhavatīti bhāvaḥ | vakṣyamāṇatvāditi | 'anekavyaktyabhivyaṅgye'tyādigranthena pañcakośādivat sadrasadvailakṣaṇyasya sūcayiṣyamāṇatvāt | ādinā - 'pañcakośādī'tyādipadena || 66 ||
varṇajātīnām - varṇavṛttitvenābimatakatvādijātīnām | utpanna iti na syāditi | iti pratītirna syādityarthaḥ | jātīnāṃ nityatvābidhānaṃ--'śikhī dhvasta'iti vadviśeṣaṇībhūtajātiviṣayakatayaivo'tpannaḥ kakāra' ityādipratītinirvāha--ityasya nirākaraṇāya | idamupalakṣaṇaṃ 'vinaṣṭaḥ kakāra'iti pratīteriti bodhyam | āvidyako dharnaviśeṣo jātiriti mate noktapratītyanupapattirityata āha--vāyusaṃyogeti | parairapīti | mūle--'para'śabdo mīmāṃsakaparaḥ | mūle--'svaradairdhyādyapi hyanye' ityatrādyo'nyasabdo'pi tatparaḥ | 'svaradairdhyādī'tyatra svaraśabdenodāttatvādisvaro grāhyaḥ | 'vināśādī'tyādipadena prāgabāvasaṃgrahaḥ | tasyaikatvāt - sphoṭātmakavarṇasyaikatvādityarthaḥ | tathā ca viruddhodāttatvādyanekadharmasamāveśānupapattiḥ | na cedāttādīnāmavirodha eveti vācyam, 'agni'mityākāro'nudāttaḥ, 'yajñasya'tyādisthalīyo'kāra udātta ityādipratītyanāpatteḥ | nanu tasyānityatvamaṅgīkriyata iti noktapratītyanupapattirityāśaṅkyāha--tasya nityatvācceti | na ca gatvāvacchinneti | tathā ca pūrvaṃkālaitatkālabhavagakārayoḥ parasparaṃ tadvyaktitvāvacchinnapratiyogitākabhede'pi gatvāvacchinnapratiyogitākabhedo na gatvāvacchinne gakāre iti tādṛśabhedābhāvo'kṣata iti bhāvaḥ | tadviṣayaḥ - sa evāyaṃ gakāra iti pratyabhijñāviṣayaḥ | evaṃ ca varṇānāmanekatve na kṣatiriti nodāttādipratītyanupapattiriti bhāvaḥ | vyaktyāśābhedasyeti | etatkālavṛttivyaktāvatītakālavṛttivyaktestadvyaktitvāvacchinnapratiyogitākabhedābhāvasyetyarthaḥ | tyāgāyogāditi | anyathā padārthamātrasya kṣaṇikatvāpattiḥ, pratyabhijñāyā uktarītyaivopapattiriti bhāvaḥ | anubhaveti | 'utpanno gakāra'ityasya tvabhivyakto gakāra ityartha iti tātparyam | nanū'tpanno ghaṭa'itivadu'tpanno gakāra'iti pratīteḥ prāgasattvaviśiṣṭasattvarūpotpattireva viṣayo vācyaḥ, samānākārapratītyorvibhinnaviṣayatvasyānucitatvādityāśaṅkyāha--kiṃ ceti | uccaritatvaṃ tālvoṣṭhapuṭasaṃyogajanyatvamevetyutpattipratītirjāgartyevaityāśaṅkāyāmāha--uccaritatvaṃ ceti | paramparayā - svāśrayavyaṅgyatvarūpaparāsaṃbandhena | utpattyāde'rityādipadena nāśaparigrahaḥ | tasya tu svapratiyogivyaṅgyatvarūpaḥ saṃbandho yodhyaḥ | varṇaniṣṭhatveti | nityavarṇaniṣṭhatvābhyupagamādityarthaḥ | itthaṃ ca pūrvānūbhūtenedānīṃtanānubhūtasyābhedasiddhau tāvatkālastiraṃ cainaṃ kaḥ paścānnāśayiṣyati" iti nyāyena varṇānāṃ nityatvaṃ siddhameveti bhāvaḥ | sākṣāt saṃbandhāṃśe iti | utpattyādeḥ sākṣātsaṃbandhenotpanno gakāra iti sākṣāt‌saṃbandhāṃśe yā pratītistasyā bhratvarūpaṃ dūṣaṇamavaśiṣyata ityarthaḥ | vyaktyabhedāṃśe iti | vyaktayabhedāvagāhinī yā 'sa evāyaṃ gakāra'iti pratītistasyā abhedāśe ityarthaḥ | tava bhramatvavaditi | tasyāḥ pratīteryathā tava mate bhramatvaṃ tathā sākṣātsaṃbandhenotpattyādipratītemama mate bhramatvaṃ tulyamityarthaḥ | ekapratīterbhramatvāpattirūpaṃ dūṣaṇaṃ matadvayena samānamiti paryavasito'rthaḥ | nanu pakṣadvaye dūṣaṇasāmye nityanānāvarṇapakṣopekṣā nirvījetyāśaṅkyāha--parantviti | mama - nityavarmavādinaḥ | lāghavamatiricyata iti | paramate tūktagauravāt tatpakṣopekṣeti bhāvaḥ | varṇasthale dhvaninaiyatyaṃ yadi syāt tadā paraṃparāsaṃbandhenotpattyā pratītinirvāhastatra dhvanisattve eva tu na mānamityāśayena śaṅkate--na ceti | nanu gakāra utpanna ityādipratītyathānupapattireva tatra mānaṃ bhaviṣyatītyāśaṅkyāha--tadutpādaketi | dhvanyutpādakasāmagryabhāvena tadasaṃbhavaḥ | na hyanyathānupapattireva kāryotpādikā, sāmagrīṃ vinānyathānupapattiśatamapi kāryotpādanasamarthaṃ na dṛṣṭacaramiti bhāvaḥ |
samādhatte--kakārādyuccāraṇeti | kakārādyuccāraṇasvarūpayogyayatnādisattva ityarthaḥ | īṣadantareti | īṣadviśleṣe satītyarthaḥ | jihveti | jihvātattatsthānayoryo'bhighātākhyasaṃyogastajjanyo yo vāyukaṇṭhasaṃyogādistasyetyarthaḥ | dhvanijanakatveti | anvayavyatirekābhyāmuktavidhāvāyutatsthānasaṃyogāderdhvanijanakatvakalpanādityarthaḥ | tasya - tādṛśavāyusaṃyogādeḥ | sattvāditi | tathā ca sāmagrīsattvena varṇotpattispale'pi dhvanyutpādo'kṣata eveti bhāvaḥ | varṇotpādakasāmagryāḥ pratibandhakatvānna varṇotpattisthale dhvanyutpādasaṃbhava ityāśaṅkate--tavaiveti | evam - uktarītyā varṇānāṃ nityaikatvarūpatve | 'dīrghatvādikamapī'tyapinotpattyādiparigrahaḥ | tathā cotpattyādivaddīrghatvādikamapi na varṇaniṣṭhaṃ virodhāditi bhāvaḥ | teṣāṃ - mīmāṃsakānāṃ | pramādādipūrvakoccāritaśabdāt prayoktṛniṣṭhapramādajanyatvānumānānupapattistu na śaṅkanīyā, etanmate tadanumāpakasya tattaduccāritaśabde vaijātyasyāsambhavepi pramādādijanyatāvacchedakatālvoṣṭhasaṃyogādigatavaijātyasya barṇeṣvāropeṇa pramādādijanyatvānumānopapatteḥ | dhūmatvena jñāyamānapadārthāntareṇāpi vahnyanumiterdṛṣṭatvāt | na ca 'seyaṃ gurjarī' 'seyaṃ dīpakalike'ti pratyabhiṃjñāvaśāt teṣāmapi nityatvaṃ syāt | gurjarī rāgaviśeṣa iti vācyam | tatra mānāntareṇa nāśapratiyogitvasiddhau agatyā pratyatrijñāyā bhramatvasya sājātyāvalambasya vāṅgīkārāt | varṇānāmekatve 'ghaṭika' ityatra dvyajlakṣaṇaṣṭhān ghaṭena taratītyarthe āvṛttigatadvitvasaṃkhyāmāropya nirvāhyaḥ | evaṃ 'ghaṭene'tyādau 'sāvekācaḥ--' iti vibhakt‌yudāttatvamapi āvṛttigatadvitvasaṃkhyayaikatvasya bādhādvāraṇīyam | spaṣṭaṃ cedaṃ sarvaṃ pratyāhārahnike bhāṣyadau ityāstāṃ vistaraḥ | pratibandyevottaramiti | kkacit pratibanderapyuttaratvamiti bhāvaḥ | etādṛśasthale pratibanderanuttaratve parasiddhāntasyaiva bhaṅgāpatteḥ | pañceti | varṇapadavākyasphoṭāstrayaḥ, caramayoḥ sakhaṇḍatvākhaṇḍatvābhyāṃ dvaividhyamiti vyaktisphoṭānāṃ pañcavidhatvamiti bhāvaḥ || 70 ||
iti vyaktisphoṭanirūpaṇam
'bopadeva'rītimavalambavya jātisphoṭamāha--śakyatva iveti | nanu akhaṇḍasphoṭabādinā varṇānāmanahgīkārādāśrayaṃ vinā jāterasaṃbhavājjātervācakatvamasaṃbhavaduktikamityāśaṅkāmapākartumāha--ayaṃ bhāva ityādinā | uktarītyeti | varṇānaṅgīkārasahitaḥ sphoṭāhgīkāra ityuktarītyetyarthaḥ | nanvetanmate 'yo'yaṃ gakāra' ityādipratītiriṣṭaiva | advaitavādimate 'yo'yaṃ devadattaḥ sa yajñadatta' itivaditya āha-gakāro'yaṃ neti | nanvāsepitabhedāvalambano 'gakāro'yaṃ na hakāra' iti pratyaya upapadyate, vedāntinaye parasparaṃ jīvabhedapratyayavadityanuśayāno dūṣaṇāntaramāha-kiṃ ceti | gatvādīti | anāropitaṃ gatvādyabhyupeyaṃ na vetyarthaḥ | āropitasva pareṇāpyaṅgīkārādyathāśrutāsaṃgateḥ | tadeva - gatvādyeva | gatvādergakārādirūpatve hetumāha--varṇanityateti | atirikteti | varṇātiriktetyarthaḥ | gatvānaṅgīkārāditi | gatvādyanaṅgīkārādityarthaḥ | anekavyaktīnāmānugatapratītaye hi atiriktajātyabhyupagamaḥ | varṇānāṃ nityatvamate tu gakārādivyakteraikyenānanugamāprasaktyā gakārādyatiriktagatvāṅgīkārasya niṣprayojanatvāditi bhāvaḥ | tathā ca - sphoṭe gatvādyabhyupagame ca | athavā gakārādergatvādirūpatve cetyarthaḥ | atiriktasphoṭeti | gatvādyatiriktasphoṭakalpane ityarthaḥ | gauravam - atiriktasphoṭātmakadharmikalpanāgauravamityarthaḥ | antye - sphoṭe gatvādyanabhyupagamapakṣe | pratītivirodha iti | gatvādyabhāve gatvādiprakārakoktapratītyanupapattirityarthaḥ | āropya tathā pratyaya iti | gatvādeḥ sphoṭāvṛttitve'pi vāyusaṃyogādivṛttigatvādiprakārakārope bādhakābhāva iti bhāvaḥ | āropyetyasya sthitasyetyadhyāhāreṇopapattiḥ | anyathā pratītibhavanasamānakartṛkatvābhāvena ktvāpratyayānupapatteḥ | paurtrakālikāropānusaraṇaṃ tu tathāvidhapratyayamūlakasya vyavahārasyopapatteḥ | evaṃ ca pratyayorvyavahāra ityarthaḥ | yadvā samānakālikatvaṃ pratyayabhavane saṃbandhatayā bhāsata iti yathāśrutārthakameva pratyayapadamastu | 'vāyusaṃyoga eve'tyevena sphoṭavyavacchedaḥ | gakāro'pīti | tathāvidhapratyayaviṣayo'pītyarthaḥ | evaṃ ca pratīterna bhramatvaprasaktiḥ | apinā--'kakāra' ityādipratyayaviṣayasaṃgrahaḥ | tasya - vāyusaṃyogasya | atīndriyatvaṃ doṣa iti | saṃyogapratyakṣe yāvadāśrayapratyakṣasya hetutvamabhyupeyam, anyathā ghaṭākāśasaṃyogapratyakṣāpatteḥ | tathā ca vāyorapratyakṣatvena vāyutattatsthānasaṃyogasyātīndriyatayā gakāra ityādipratyakṣānupapattirityarthaḥ | dharmavaditi | vāyudharmasya sparśavadityarthaḥ | yathā vāyoratīndriyatve'pi vāyusparśastvācapratyakṣaviṣayastathā tālvādyanuyogikavāyusaṃyogā aindriyiko bhaviṣyati bahirdravyagrāhakendriyajanyasaṃyogapratyakṣe eva yāvadāśrayapratyakṣasya hetutvāṅgīkārāditi bhāvaḥ | athavā 'gagane śabda' itivadvāyusaṃyoge gatvāmiti saṃyogaviśeṣaṇakaṃ saṃyogopanītameva gatvādiviśeṣyakaṃ tadaśe laukikapratyakṣamastu, saṃyogalaukikapratyakṣe eva yāvadāśrayapratyakṣasya hetutvāt | tadeka pratyakṣaṃ 'gakāra' iti śabdenābhilapyata iti bhāvaḥ | etatpakṣe dharmavat - atīndriyagaganādidharmavaditi vyākhyeyam | kṛtaṃ sphoṭena - alaṃ sphoṭena tattadvāyusaṃyogaireva tattadarthabodhanirvāhāditi bhāvaḥ | tattatsaṃyogasamudāya eva idamekapadamityādipratītiviṣaya iti hṛdayam | vastutastvetatpratītyanyathānupapattyātiriktasphoṭāṅgīkāra āvaśyaka ityāhuḥ | nirūpitaṃ caitat pūrvam |
ākṛtyadhikaraṇeti | tatra hi yathā ānantyavyabicārābhyāṃ vyaktirna śabdavācyā kiṃ tu jātirityuktam | tathā śabdavatā jātireva vāciketi svīkāraḥ, samucitastulyahetīrityarthaḥ | nanu varṇavṛttijātīnāmapi nānātvāt kathaṃ vācakatvanirvāha ityāśaṅkyāha--idaṃ harīti | nanu nānugatapratītyā jātisiddhiḥ saṃmatā tantrikāṇāmapi tu kāraṇatāvacchedakatayā, kāryatāvacchedakatavā vetyata āha--hartupastitīti | tadavacchevakatayā - hetutāvacchedakakoṭipraviṣṭatayā | varṇānupūrvyaivānugatapratītinirvāhaḥ, saiva ca hetutāvacchedikā bhaviṣyīti kṛtamatiriktajātisvīkāreṇetyāśaṅkate--na ceti | anyathāsiddhyāpatteriti | saṃyogaviśeṣaviśiṣṭamṛdādibhireva ghaṭa ityādyanugatapratīterdaṇḍādikāryatāvacchedakatvasya ca nirvāhād ghaṭatvādijātirapi na sidhyet | saṃyogaviśeṣāvacchinnamṛdādikaṃ prati daṇḍādeḥ kāraṇatvasya vaktuṃ śakyatvāditi bhāvaḥ evaṃ ghaṭādiravayavyapi na sidhyet, ghaṭa ityādipratīteḥ-saṃyogaviśeṣaviśiṣṭamṛdādiviṣayakatvenaivāpapatterityarthaḥ | ākāraḥ - anugatasaṃsthānaviśeṣaḥ | yadyavayavasaṃyogānāmananugamād ghaṭatvādijātyabhyupagama āvaśyakaḥ, evaṃ saṃyogaviśeṣaviśiṣṭānāmavayavānāṃ nānātvāt 'ghaṭa' iti pratītestadviṣayakatve gauravaneko ghaṭa iti pratītyanupapattiśceti ghaṭaghaṭatvādyāśrayaṇamiti cet, prakṛte'pi tulyamityāśayenāha--tasmāditi | uktarītyā jātisvīkārādityarthaḥ | nanu padādivṛttijātereva vācakatve tad‌vṛttidharmasya sakalaghaṭādipadasamavetatvaviśiṣṭaghaṭapadādyariktāsamavetatvarūpasya vācakatāvacchedakatvamurarīkatavyaṃ, tathā cātigauravamityata āha--tādātmyeneti | avacchedikā - vācakatāvacchedikā, tādātmyatya vṛttyaniyāmakatve'pyavacchedakatāvacchedakatvasya nānupapattiḥ, svādhikaraṇasambaddhatvasyavāvacchedakatāyāṃ tantratvāditi bhāvaḥ | jātyoḥ sattvāditi | padadvaye'pi āśrayībhūtānāṃ me pāmeva varṇānāṃ sattvādvinigamanāviraheṇaikasmin jātidvayasattvāviśeṣādityarthaḥ | vāśabdasya vikalpārthakatve'thosaṃgatirata āha--vā tvarthe iti | tvarthaśca prakṛte'vadhāraṇamityāśayenāha--saiveti | bhedaḥ kāraṇībhūteti | tathā cobhayatra varṇānāmabhede'pyānupūrvībhedasya jāgarūkatayā tattadānupūrvīviśeṣeṇa ekaiva jātirabhivyajyate ekatra, anyatrānyaiva jātirabhivyajyata iti tattajjātijñānarūpakāraṇabhedāt 'saro' 'rasa' ityatra vilakṣaṇabodharūpakāryopapattirityarthaḥ | vyañjiketīti | iti - iti hetoḥ | kāraṇībhūtajñānasya bheda iti yojanā | atiprasaṅgaḥ - avilakṣaṇabodhasyātiprasaṅgaḥ | upādhiprayukteti | balavaducvāritatvādikaṃ tatropādhiḥ | tena yathā 'tāro' 'manda' iti pratītivailakṣaṇyaṃ tadvadityarthaḥ || 71 ||
jāteriti | ghaṭapadādivṛttitvenābhimatajāteḥ | sphuṭati bodhaviṣayo bhavatyasmādartha iti vyutpattyā sphoṭapadaṃ bodhakaparamityāha--yogārthatayeti | apāstamiti | jāteḥ sarvadā sattve'pyabhivyaktiviśiṣṭāyā asattvena noktadoṣaḥ, abhivyaktiviśiṣṭāyā eva jāterbodhakatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
nanvevamapi padaghaṭakadvyādivarṇebhyo'rthabodho durvāra evābivyaktiviśiṣṭajāteḥ sattvāt | na ca padaghaṭakayāvadvarṇaireva jāterabhivyaktirityabhyupagamānna doṣa iti vācyam | tathā sati padasphoṭevākyasphoṭe vā paryasāmenaitatpakṣe varṇasphoṭocchedāpattestyiāśaṅkāmapākartumāha--ayaṃ bhāva iti | kathatadoṣaḥ - yat‌kiñcid‌varṇādarthabodhāpattirūpo doṣaḥ | pakṣayornetyatra 'kathita doṣaḥ' ityanukṛṣyate | tatra vyāsajyavṛttitvasya - padādighaṭakayāvadvarṇasamudāyavṛttitvasya | varṇasphoṭapakṣe tu naivaṃ vaktuṃ śakyam | pratyekāvṛttestadanatiriktatāvadvarṇasamudāyavṛttitvasyāsambhavāt | samudāyasyātiriktatve tu padasphoṭo vākyasphoṭa eva vā sidhyenna varṇasphoṭa iti bhāvaḥ | padavākyapakṣayoḥ - tayoḥ dhārmigrāhaketi | dharmo - jātirūpastad‌grāhakaṃ mānam - idaṃ haripadamityādyanugatapratītyādirūpaṃ, tatsiddhatvāt | tathā ca varṇaṃsphoṭo'smin pakṣe duṣṭatvānnābhyupeyata iti bhāvaḥ | mūle vyaktya evāsyā iti | asyāḥ - jātervyañjiketi śeṣaḥ | śeṣārthaḥ - 'kaiścidvktaya' ityādyurārdhasyārthaḥ | varṇavyaktīnāṃ sphoṭavyañjakatayā tatra dhvaniriti vyavahāraḥkṛtai tyapekṣāyāmāha--uktaṃ hityādinā | sphoṭavyañjakasyeti | varṇavyaktīnāṃ sphoṭavyañjakatayā tatra dhvaniriti vyavahāraḥ kṛta ityarthaḥ || 72 ||
advaitasiddhānte "ātmaivedaṃ sarvam" "neha nānāsti kiṃcana" ityādiśrutibhyo brahmātiriktapadārthamātrasyāsatyatayā kathaṃ jāteḥ satyatvamityāśaṅkyāha--tattadvayaktiviśiṣṭaṃ brahmaiveti | ādheyatāsaṃbandhena tattadvayaktyupahitaṃ brahmaiva jātirūpamiti tasyā nityatvaṃ nānupapannamiti bhāvaḥ | viśeṣyānvayividheyānanvayī vyāvartaka upādhiḥ | viśeṣyānvayividheyānvayī vyāvartako viśeṣaṇam | 'karṇaśaṣkulyavacchinnaṃ nabhaḥ śrotraka'mityatra karṇaśaṣkulyupādhiḥ | śrotratvarūpavidheyānanvayāt | vidheyānvayācca rūpaviśiṣṭo ghaṭo'nitya ityatra rūpaṃ viśeṣaṇam | prakṛte ca tattadvayakterupādhitvameva | jātitvarūpavidheyānanvayāditi bodhyam | etacca--
"saṃbandhibhedāt sattaiva bhidyamānā gavādiṣu |
jātirityucyate tasyāṃ sarve śabdā vyavasthitāḥ ||
tāṃ prātipadikārthaṃ ca dhātvarthaṃ ca pracakṣate |
sā nityā sā mahānātmā tāmāhustvatalādayaḥ ||"--
--iti vākyapadīye uktam | asatyeti | paspaśāhnike--"dravyaṃ nityam'iti bāṣyamupādāyedaṃ kaithaṭe uktam | brahmatattvameveti | pratyāhārāhnike "candratārakavat pratimaṇḍito veditavyo brahrāśiḥ" ityatrāyaṃ kaiyaṭagranthaḥ | iti kaiyaṭa iti | ayamapi kaiyaṭagrantha "ākṛtiranityā" iti paspaśāsthabhāṣyastho draṣṭavyaḥ | avidyeti | ñatra dharmaviśeṣaḥ ityatra jātirikta ityanupajyate | spaṣṭaṃ cedaṃ kaustubhe || 73 ||
mūle--nirañjanam - ajñānarahitamityarthaḥ | samārambha iti bhāvānāṃ - dṛśyamānaghaṭādipadārthānāṃ, samārambhaḥ - utpattirityarthaḥ | anādīti | prāgabhāvapratiyogītyarthaḥ | 'śāśvatamityanena dhvaṃsāpratiyogitvamuktam | tathā ca prāgabhāvāpratiyogitvaviśiṣṭaṃ dhvaṃsāpratiyogitvarūpaṃ nityatvaṃ labdham | pracayānte maṅgalamiti | 'kārikāvalī'rūpagranthānte ityartheḥ | śiṣṭācārānugṛhītād "granthādau granthamadhye ca granthānte maṅgalaṃ caret" ityabhiyuktavacanād granthānte maṅgalamāhetyarthaḥ | stutinatīti | 'namaḥ' ityanena natiḥ, saṃpūrṇātmane' ityanena stutiḥ pratipādyate | pūrṇatvamapratihatecchatvaṃ, samāptakāmatvena niricchatvaṃ vā | so'kāmayata vahu syāṃ prajāyeya' ityanāropitaiva tatreccheti na virothaḥ | svīyasukhecchāvadanyatvaṃ vā pūrṇatvam | spaṣṭaṃ cedaṃ " pūrṇāya paramātmane" iti dīdhitivyākhyāne gadādharīye ityalamativistareṇeti śivam || 74 ||
yo'bhūtpaṇḍitamaṇḍalīpariṇataḥ śrotābdhisārajñatāmāpto vedavidāṃ varaḥ parivṛḍhairbhūmeḥ sadā vanditaḥ |
śrīmadbhairavaśaṅkarīpadarajaḥsevāptapūrṇārthakaḥ śrīmatkeśavadīkṣitaḥ samabhavattasyātmajo'bhūddhariḥ ||
rāmāntaḥśivapālite śivapure śrīkāśiśabdābhidhe teneyaṃ khalu nirmitā kṛtivarā śrībhūṣaṇe kāśikā |
śrīmadbhairavapādasevanavapuḥ śrīkaṇṭhapāde'pritā modaṃ santanutāṃ sadāptaviduṣāṃ sadyuktibhājāṃ ciram ||
dhanarājānujaḥ śrīmān sakhīdevyāstu garbhajaḥ |
vyākhyāṃcakre bhūṣaṇasya yathāmati śivāptaye ||
yugabhūtadigīśātmasaṃmite vatsare gate |
mārgaśīrṣaśuklapakṣe paurṇamāsyāṃ vidhordine ||
rohiṇīsthe candramasi vṛścikasthe divākare |
samāptimagamad granthastena tuṣyatu naḥ śivaḥ ||
